#CH 51
[ZH] 一桌子菜，林觉一口没动。他最后的提议搞得千里寻也毫无胃口，对门外喊道：“你们两个进来吧！”
[EN] Not a single dish touched by Lin Jue—and his parting demand killed Qianli Xun's appetite cold too! Calling toward the doorway: "You two come inside!"
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果进屋后，千里寻让她们坐下跟自己一起吃。两人高兴地坐下，拿起筷子吃起来。
[EN] Stepping in, Kuqiao and Xiaguo gladly obeyed her gesture to sit and eat alongside her, picking up chopsticks right away.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧着夏果，不知如何开口，总觉得用一个丫鬟交换自己的幸福太不仁义。
[EN] Eyeing Xiaguo, Qianli Xun wrestled with how to broach it—trading a maid for her own freedom felt awfully shady!
[ZH] 又一想，林觉大小也是练兵官，林家在临安也算有些地位，像夏果这等乡下丫头能给林觉做妾室应该不错。
[EN] Still, Lin Jue was Lin'an's Training Officer from a respectable house; for a poor country girl like Xiaguo, becoming his concubine might mean security for life.
[ZH] 于是大胆说：“夏果，有件事我需征求你的意思……”
[EN] Biting the bullet, she spoke gentle: "Xiaguo, need to ask your honest opinion on something..."
[ZH] “小姐请说。”夏果放下筷子。
[EN] Setting down her chopsticks, Xiaguo answered attentive: "Speak, Miss."
[ZH] “嗯……我刚与林觉聊得不错，他答应与我和离，但提出一个要求，让我把你送给他……”
[EN] "Well... just talked things over with Lin Jue—he agreed to grant me a mutual divorce, on one condition: that I give you to him..."
[ZH] 夏果垂下眼帘：“可是给主君做妾室？”
[EN] Lowering her lashes, Xiaguo asked quiet: "As Lord's concubine?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“我跟他和离后，他身边就你一人，说不定将来会把你扶正。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded: "Once I leave, you'd be the only woman beside him—who knows, he might even promote you to primary wife someday!"
[ZH] 夏果依然垂着眼帘：“小姐不要哄我，我是乡下丫头，除长得好看些，其他都上不了台面，给主君当妾岂有被扶正的机会？”
[EN] Eyes still downcast, Xiaguo murmured: "Don't sugarcoat it, Miss—I'm a country girl with nothing going for me except my face. How could a concubine ever get promoted to primary wife?"
[ZH] 夏果跪在地上：“小姐，我爷爷说过，宁可嫁给穷人做妻，也不嫁给富人当妾，还望小姐不要把我送给主君。”
[EN] Dropping straight to her knees on the floorboards, she pleaded: "My grandfather always taught me: rather be a poor man's wife than a rich man's concubine! Please don't hand me over to Lord, Miss!"
[ZH] 夏果这么一跪，千里寻更为难：“你起来吧，坐下边吃边说。”
[EN] Seeing her kneel tied Qianli Xun in tighter knots: "Get up—sit back down and talk while we eat."
[ZH] 夏果很坚决：“小姐若不答应，我便长跪不起。”
[EN] Iron-firm: "Until Miss promises, I'm staying on my knees!"
[ZH] 苦荞劝道：“你都说自己是乡下丫头，能嫁给姑爷当妾已不错，就别再为难小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao jumped in nudging her: "You called yourself a country girl—becoming Son-in-law's concubine is a huge step up! Stop putting Miss in a bind!"
[ZH] 夏果反问道：“苦荞姐姐既觉得当妾室不错，你为何不去？”
[EN] Xiaguo fired right back sharp: "If Sister Kuqiao thinks being a concubine is so grand, why don't YOU go?!"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“可惜姑爷没瞧上我，如若姑爷瞧上我，我义不容辞，不为别的，只为小姐能顺利与姑爷和离。”
[EN] "Too bad Son-in-law didn't pick me! Had he asked for me, I'd say yes in a heartbeat—just to buy Miss her mutual divorce!" Kuqiao shot back.
[ZH] 夏果冷笑道：“苦荞姐姐没摊上事儿，只管说大话，如若主君选中你，我不相信你会拿自己的婚姻去赌？”
[EN] Xiaguo let out a bitter laugh: "Easy talking big when it isn't your neck on the block! If Lord really picked you, bet you wouldn't gamble your whole life away!"
[ZH] 夏果态度坚决，千里寻不忍心将她强行送给林觉，怕她跟了林觉会成为第二个朱淑真，到时再被林觉家暴，她千里寻会一辈子内疚。
[EN] Seeing Xiaguo dead set against it, Qianli Xun couldn't bear forcing her—what if Xiaguo ended up Zhu Shuzhen number two, beaten behind closed doors? Qianli Xun's conscience would eat her alive forever!
[ZH] 不会把夏果送给林觉的话还未出口，听到韦嬷嬷的声音：“如若苦荞遇到同样的事，为了姑娘，她绝对会答应！”
[EN] Just as she opened her mouth to promise she'd never hand her over, Matron Wei's voice cut in from the doorway: "Were Kuqiao in your shoes, she WOULD say yes for our young lady—without blinking!"
[ZH] 千里寻招呼韦嬷嬷：“嬷嬷坐下一起吃。”
[EN] "Sit and eat with us, Matron," Qianli Xun invited.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷挑位置坐下：“我已在屋里用过饭，出来时碰到姑爷，与他聊了两句，得知他的想法，便过来瞧瞧。”
[EN] Taking a chair, Matron Wei said: "Already ate in my room—bumped into Son-in-law walking out, chatted two words, heard his demand, and came straight over."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷对夏果说：“姑娘对你有恩，你报答的机会就在眼前，给姑爷当妾，你一点儿都不亏，大不了我替姑娘做主，多给你些嫁妆，让你当个有钱的妾室。”
[EN] Turning stern eyes onto Xiaguo: "Miss saved you from ruin—here's your chance to repay her! Being Son-in-law's concubine won't shortchange you one bit; on Miss's behalf, I'll even pack you a generous dowry so you walk in a rich concubine!"
[ZH] 夏果心想，小姐想要甩开的人却硬塞给自己，说不定主君有什么无法对外人提起的隐疾，她决不能将个人幸福随意葬送，凭她的容貌能嫁个更好的。再说她刚进城里，什么都没见识过，也没体验过，人生就被定格，她心有不甘。
[EN] Xiaguo thought suspicious: Miss can't run fast enough from this husband and wants to dump him on ME? Bet he's got some nasty hidden illness! No way am I tossing my whole future away—with my looks I can aim way higher! Plus I just hit the big city—haven't even tasted life yet!
[ZH] “正因感恩小姐救我，才下定决心一辈子不嫁，陪在小姐身边伺候她。韦嬷嬷说的嫁妆我不稀罕，跟着小姐不愁吃喝，当丫鬟我心满意足。”
[EN] "Precisely out of gratitude for Miss saving me, I swore never to marry and stay serving Miss for life! Don't want any dowry, Matron Wei—well fed and clothed by Miss's side as a maid is all I ever want!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷还打算继续批评夏果，被千里寻拦住：“既然夏果不愿意，不必为难她。你起来吧，跟苦荞把酒菜端到丫鬟房慢慢吃，我想静一静，想些事情。”
[EN] Before Matron Wei could scold further, Qianli Xun stepped in: "If Xiaguo says no, we don't force her! Get up now—you and Kuqiao pack up this food and eat at your leisure in the maids' quarters. Need quiet to think."
[ZH] 夏果感谢完小姐，起身与苦荞一起将酒菜收拾到两个食盒中，提去丫鬟房。
[EN] Thanking Miss profusely, Xiaguo rose and helped Kuqiao pack every dish into two food carriers for the maids' quarters.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷劝道：“姑娘就是心软，看不得他人委屈。可姑娘也不应被夏果蒙蔽，她嘴上说想陪姑娘一辈子，心里可不一定这么想，说不定还想嫁给更好的人家当正室。”
[EN] Alone again, Matron Wei sighed: "Too softhearted, young lady—can't stand seeing anyone unhappy! Still, don't let Xiaguo fool you: claims she wants to serve you forever, yet deep down I bet she's angling to marry primary wife into an even richer house!"
[ZH] “她有这想法倒也正常，如若心不甘情不愿嫁给林觉，像我一样遭遇家暴，岂不是令我一辈子不安心？”
[EN] "Can't blame her for wanting better! Force her into Lin Jue's bed unwilling and watch him beat her like he beat me? I'd never sleep at peace!"
[ZH] “按说丫鬟的人生大事掌握在主子手中，主子想把她嫁给谁，她不能说任何反对的话，主要是姑娘心善，才会处处替丫鬟们考虑。唉，姑娘无论做什么决定，我都支持，我这就去找姑爷说说此事。”
[EN] "By rights a maid's marriage rests wholly in her mistress's hands—not her place to protest! Only because you're so kind do you weigh her wishes. Fine—whatever you choose, I back you! I'll go give Son-in-law our answer right now."
[ZH] “有劳嬷嬷。”
[EN] "Thank you, Matron."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走后，千里寻陷入沉思，和离之事刚有眉目，又出了夏果这等事。如果没有收夏果当丫鬟，林觉就没有机会以此为条件再次为难她，真应了韦嬷嬷在竹林说过的话。
[EN] Once Matron Wei left, Qianli Xun sat brooding—mutual divorce inches away, only for Xiaguo to snag the whole deal! Had she never hired Xiaguo, Lin Jue wouldn't have this card to block her—just like Matron Wei warned back in the bamboo grove!
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷来到主屋拜访林觉，林觉倒是对韦嬷嬷敬重，只因这位嬷嬷说话做事令他佩服，看人的眼光也独到，比很多男子都厉害。他家暴朱淑真时，每次都选韦嬷嬷不在才敢动手，韦嬷嬷那双眼睛太过犀利，只要被她看一眼，他的拳头都打不下去。
[EN] Walking over to the main hall to call on Lin Jue, Matron Wei commanded quiet respect even from him—sharp-tongued, razor-sighted, sharper than most men; back when he beat Zhu Shuzhen, he always checked Matron Wei was out first, because one piercing glare from her shriveled his nerve!
[ZH] 林觉请韦嬷嬷坐下，不敢接触她的眼睛，怕那双眼睛看穿他心中所想：“不知嬷嬷前来有何指教？”
[EN] Inviting her to sit while dodging direct eye contact lest she read straight through him, Lin Jue asked: "What brings you over, Matron?"
[ZH] “指教谈不上，我是来替姑娘回个话，夏果那丫头想跟着姑娘一辈子，不愿嫁人，还请姑爷另觅妾室。”
[EN] "Just bringing our young lady's reply—Xiaguo wants to serve Miss lifelong and refuses to marry, so please look elsewhere for a concubine."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的话如同当头一棒，他林觉的感情之路为何如此不顺？看上朱淑真，娶了她，日子过得糟心不说，还闹到和离。现在好不容易有个心仪的丫鬟，结果又被拒绝，让他情何以堪？
[EN] The reply hit Lin Jue like a brick—why was his love life cursed?! Fell for Zhu Shuzhen, wed her, miserable every day till she demanded divorce; finally spotted a gorgeous maid who lit the same spark, and rejected flat again! How humiliating!
[ZH] 一生气，也不顾及韦嬷嬷那双眼睛，大胆说：“既如此，和离之事免谈。”
[EN] Tempers flaring past his usual fear of Matron Wei's eyes, he snapped: "Then mutual divorce is off the table!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷猜到林觉会如此说，早已准备好应对之词：“我来也是向姑爷招呼一声，和离不和离无所谓，我在外看好宅子，也付了定金，不管姑爷同不同意和离，我家姑娘很快会搬出去。”
[EN] Having seen this coming miles away, Matron Wei rolled out her counter smooth: "Also came to give you fair warning, Son-in-law—mutual divorce or not makes zero difference! I've already picked out a fine courtyard house outside and put down deposit. Agree or refuse, our young lady moves out soon!"
[ZH] 林觉更像是被泼了一盆冷水，从外到里冰得透心凉：“这么快宅子都买好了？岳父大人可知此事？你们这么做，朱府的颜面何存？”
[EN] Ice water poured straight down Lin Jue's spine: "Bought a house already?! Does Father-in-law know?! Pull that stunt and what happens to Zhu Manor's honor?!"
[ZH] “既然朱府已将女儿嫁给林家，朱家老爷知不知道此事不重要。姑娘已是林家人，搬出去住与朱家颜面无关，丢得只会是林家的人。”
[EN] "Once Zhu Manor wed their daughter into Lin Manor, Master Zhu's opinion stopped mattering! She's a Lin wife now—moving out doesn't shame the Zhu house, it shames the LIN house!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷句句戳中林觉的心窝，可林觉不敢大发雷霆：“我只是想要个丫鬟，朱淑真为何不能满足我？只要把夏果给我，大家都高兴，嬷嬷您说是也不是？您再帮我劝劝她，就让她把夏果给我得了。”
[EN] Every syllable hit dead-center in Lin Jue's chest—yet daring not explode at Matron Wei, he pleaded tight: "All I ask is one maid—why won't Zhu Shuzhen humor me?! Hand over Xiaguo and everyone wins—isn't that right, Matron? Talk her round—just hand Xiaguo over!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷叹口气：“唉！姑娘当然愿意，夏果不乐意，姑娘心又善，不愿强人所难。我给姑爷出个主意，不如私下多与夏果接触接触，说不定能培养出感情来，有了感情，她自然乐意。”
[EN] Matron Wei sighed: "Our young lady would gladly agree—Xiaguo's the one balking, and Miss's kind heart refuses to force anyone! Tell you what, Son-in-law—why not spend some time getting close to Xiaguo on your own? Spark some affection between you two, and she'll say yes on her own!"
[ZH] 林觉立刻乐道：“嬷嬷的主意甚好，那我就试试，不过可不能这么快搬出去，否则我与那丫头怎么培养感情？”
[EN] Lin Jue's face lit up at once: "Brilliant idea, Matron! I'll try that! Only—don't move out so fast, or how do I get time to warm that girl up?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷笑道：“那是自然。”
[EN] "Naturally!" Matron Wei smiled smooth.
[ZH] 千里寻独自想了一会儿，品着净光法师说的四个字“正思正念”，她决定不能将个人的幸福凌驾于他人不幸之上，尊重夏果的想法，才是正确的选择。想通之后，她到书桌前坐下，开始提笔写话本。
[EN] Back in Zhulan Residence, mulling over Master Jingguang's four words—Right Thought and Right Mindfulness—Qianli Xun felt peace settle back: building her own freedom on another girl's misery was wrong; honoring Xiaguo's choice felt clean. Mind clear, she sat at her desk and resumed writing huaben scripts.
[ZH] 苦荞拿着请帖进来：“小姐，乔公子已从汴梁回来，听说您从乡下回到林府，差南双送来请帖，邀请您参加诗会。”
[EN] Kuqiao walked in holding an invitation card: "Miss, Young Master Qiao is back from Bianliang! Heard you returned from the countryside and sent Nan Shuang over with an invitation to a poetry salon!"
[ZH] 千里寻放下笔，接过请帖打开，看到地点是在西湖边的观景亭，落款写着乔文仙。既是朱淑真的知己邀请，她必然要替朱淑真前去赴约，看看时间，是明日午时。
[EN] Setting down her brush and opening the card, Qianli Xun read: Sightseeing Pavilion by West Lake, signed Qiao Wenxian. Zhu Shuzhen's prized confidant—of course she had to attend! Date: tomorrow at noon.
[ZH] “你去告诉送请帖之人，明日我会按时参加。”
[EN] "Tell the messenger I'll be there on time tomorrow."
[ZH] “我已替小姐应下。”
[EN] "Already told him yes for you, Miss!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞅一眼苦荞：“什么时候轮到你替我做主？”
[EN] Qianli Xun arched a brow: "Since when do you make my schedule?"
[ZH] “乔公子相邀，小姐从来不会拒绝，必然参加，有时我在想，如果小姐能和乔公子在一起那该多好。”
[EN] "Whenever Young Master Qiao invites, you never say no—always go! Sometimes I catch myself wishing you and Young Master Qiao ended up together..."
[ZH] “你不是说我以前心里有徐公子，怎么又希望我跟乔公子在一起？”
[EN] "Didn't you say my heart belonged to Young Master Xu? Now shipping me with Young Master Qiao?!"
[ZH] “以前虽希望您跟徐公子，可他娶了别人，所以就想到乔公子。”
[EN] "Rooted for Young Master Xu before—he married someone else, so Young Master Qiao's next up!"
[ZH] “别瞎想，你家小姐我有婚姻挡在面前，再想其他男子会被人说成有失妇德，你竟然还敢说希望我与乔公子在一起的话？该打！”
[EN] "Quit daydreaming! Still got a husband blocking the way—fantasizing over other men gets you labeled lacking wifely virtue, and you dare say that aloud? Asking for a swat!"
[ZH] 苦荞乐道：“我宁可被打，也要说出希望小姐好的话，反正小姐跟姑爷和离是迟早的事，在我心里失忆后的小姐已是自由身，所以替您畅想一下未来。”
[EN] Kuqiao giggled fearless: "Swat me all you like, still wishing you the best! Divorce is only a matter of time—in my book, reborn Miss is already a free woman, so I'm dreaming ahead for you!"
[ZH] 别说苦荞这么认为，千里寻穿越成为朱淑真，从来没把这段婚姻当回事，在她心里又何尝不认为自己是自由之身？
[EN] Not just Kuqiao—ever since transmigrating into Zhu Shuzhen, Qianli Xun had never treated this bogus marriage as real either; in her own heart she felt free as air!
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷走后，林觉思忖着如何能与夏果培养感情，让她心甘情愿给他做妾。思来想去，只觉良家女子实在麻烦，不像商妓，无需费心费力。可想起夏果的身段与容貌不比朱淑真差，费些心思倒也值得。
[EN] Meanwhile, once Matron Wei left, Lin Jue plotted how to warm Xiaguo up until she willingly became his concubine. Respectable girls took such tedious effort—nothing like easy courtesans! Still, remembering Xiaguo's figure and face matching Zhu Shuzhen's beauty, putting in work felt worth every ounce!
[ZH] 于是叫进一名下人：“去，把夫人新收的丫鬟夏果给我找来，就说我找她问些事情。”
[EN] Calling a servant in: "Fetch Madam's new maid Xiaguo—say I have questions for her."
[ZH] 不一会儿夏果前来，已猜到主君之意，不如趁此机会表明想法，让他死了这份心思。
[EN] Moments later Xiaguo arrived, already guessing his angle and ready to shut him down cold for good.
[ZH] 夏果走到主屋门口，向里问：“主君找我？”
[EN] Stopping outside the main hall doorway, she called in: "You sent for me, Lord?"
[ZH] 传来林觉的声音：“进来吧。”
[EN] "Come inside," Lin Jue answered.
[ZH] 夏果走进去，不敢抬头，一直盯着自己的脚尖。
[EN] Stepping across the threshold, Xiaguo kept her eyes pinned to her toes.
[ZH] 林觉越看越喜欢，站起来，绕着夏果将她上下打量好几遍。
[EN] Liking her more by the second, Lin Jue stood and circled around her eyeing every inch top to bottom.
[ZH] 夏果心里发毛，担心主君非礼她：“主君找我什么事？”
[EN] Skin crawling with dread that he might get handsy, Xiaguo asked stiff: "What did Lord wish to ask?"
[ZH] 林觉在夏果面前站定，直接问：“你为何不愿跟我？”
[EN] Halting right in front of her, Lin Jue cut straight to the chase: "Why won't you come to me?"
[ZH] 果然是为此事，夏果回道：“我身份卑微，配不上主君……”
[EN] Just as she expected! Xiaguo answered flat: "My humble birth isn't worthy of Lord..."
[ZH] “我不嫌弃。”
[EN] "Doesn't bother me."
[ZH] “我不想给人当妾室。”
[EN] "I refuse to be anyone's concubine."
[ZH] 林觉想了一会儿，说道：“我很快与朱淑真和离，身边没有正室，你若当妾室把我伺候得好，我一高兴，没准儿会将你扶正。”
[EN] Pausing a beat, Lin Jue pitched: "I'm divorcing Zhu Shuzhen soon—no primary wife left! Serve me well as concubine, keep me happy, and maybe I promote you to primary wife!"
[ZH] 夏果说：“我要是嫁人，必须是明媒正娶的当家主母，才不愿意先当妾室，慢慢等着被扶正，再说主君的话能有几分可信？”
[EN] "If I marry, I enter legally wed as head matriarch—never starting as concubine waiting on scraps! Besides, how much weight does Lord's word even carry?"
[ZH] 这丫头的果断倒令林觉佩服，但林觉不可能直接让她成为正室。夏果家世不好，身份卑贱，要不是模样长得好，他又怎会注意她？若跟朱淑真一和离，就娶她做正室，不仅族人会反对，他林觉都会觉得面上无光。耐心劝道：“不如这样，我答应你，三年后将你扶正。”
[EN] Impressed by her grit yet refusing to wed a peasant girl as primary wife—zero background, sheer pretty face; marrying her straight after divorcing Zhu Shuzhen would ignite clan uproar and cost him face—he tried bargaining patient: "Tell you what—I promise to promote you to primary wife in three years!"
[ZH] 夏果被三年惊住，抬起头，盯着林觉：“三年！主君是哄我吧？三年的时间主君身边不知会有多少女人，到时还能记得承诺？我虽年轻，可我也不是三岁小孩儿，让主君这般哄骗。”
[EN] Startled by "three years," Xiaguo snapped her head up glaring straight at him: "Three years?! Playing me for a fool, Lord?! Who knows how many women pass through your bed in three years—think you'll remember one promise? Young as I am, I'm not a three-year-old buying fairy tales!"
[ZH] 夏果的眼睛实在太美，五官极其耐看，盯得林觉心里直痒，忍不住一把抱住夏果：“你就跟了我吧，我决不会亏待你，现在就去里屋，让你体验一下我的好。”
[EN] Her flashing eyes and striking features lit a fire straight in Lin Jue's gut; losing every ounce of restraint, he swept her tight into his arms: "Just be mine—I'll never mistreat you! Step into the inner room right now and feel how good I am to you!"
[ZH] 林觉毕竟是练兵官，劲大，把夏果抱住，她不得挣脱，还被对方在脸上亲了两口。
[EN] Strong as a Training Officer was, his grip locked Xiaguo helpless as he planted two sloppy kisses across her cheek!
[ZH] 林觉什么都顾不上，使劲儿把夏果抱起来，向里屋走去。
[EN] Throwing all caution aside, he hoisted her off her feet and carried her toward the inner chamber!
[ZH] 夏果在林觉身上又捶又打：“你放开我！快放开我！……”
[EN] Pounding and clawing at his chest, Xiaguo screamed: "Let me go! Let me go right now!..."
[ZH] 林觉哪里肯放？一心只想从夏果的身上体验从未在朱淑真身上体验过的感觉。
[EN] Fat chance Lin Jue let go—burning to claim from Xiaguo every pleasure Zhu Shuzhen had denied him!
[ZH] 朱淑真嫁给他时，又美又冷漠，还以死相逼，加之她的父亲是临安知府，林觉不敢轻举妄动。被他打晕后醒来，朱淑真竟然会拳法，林觉根本打不过，更不敢随意轻薄于她。
[EN] When Zhu Shuzhen wed him, she stayed glacial, threatened suicide, and had the Prefect of Lin'an backing her, keeping Lin Jue in check; after knocking her out, she woke knowing lethal boxing he couldn't match, putting her way out of reach!
[ZH] 可夏果身后没靠山，人也不冷漠，还柔柔弱弱，重点是长得跟朱淑真一样貌美，激起林觉霸占的欲望。
[EN] Xiaguo had zero backing, soft and fragile—and best of all, blessed with beauty matching Zhu Shuzhen's, igniting pure possessive frenzy in him!
[ZH] 他把夏果放到床上，重重压在她的身上，又朝她脸上亲去，却被夏果一脚踢在裤裆，疼得难受，翻身而下，捂着裤裆直喊疼：“哎吆！哎吆！……”
[EN] Dropping Xiaguo onto the bed, he pinned her flat beneath his weight and leaned in for another kiss—when Xiaguo drove a vicious kick straight into his groin! Blinding agony doubled him off the bed clutching himself howling: "Aargh! Aargh!..."
[ZH] 夏果趁机逃了出去。
[EN] Seizing the split second, Xiaguo bolted straight out the door!

#CH 52
[ZH] 夏果往竹兰居跑去，路上将有些乱的发髻再弄乱些，衣裳的纽绊解开一些，让自己显得惊慌错乱。
[EN] Xiaguo ran toward Zhulan Residence. On the way, she messed up her already disheveled hair even more and loosened some of the buttons on her clothes to make herself look flustered and distraught.
[ZH] 一进竹兰居，小姐正好站在院内欣赏竹子。
[EN] As soon as she entered Zhulan Residence, she found Young Lady standing in the courtyard admiring the bamboo.
[ZH] 夏果扑上前，跪下大声哭：“呜呜……小姐！您一定要替我做主，主君轻薄于我，我没脸见人，我……我……我现在就一头撞死算了！”夏果起身向院墙上撞去。
[EN] Xiaguo threw herself forward, knelt down, and wailed loudly: "Wuwu... Young Lady! You must... you must stand up for me! The Lord molested me! I have no face to see anyone anymore. I... I... I might as well smash my head against the wall and die right now!" Xiaguo got up and charged toward the courtyard wall.
[ZH] 千里寻一把拽住夏果，注意到她凌乱的头发和解开的纽绊，很是担心：“到底怎么回事？说清楚！”
[EN] Qianli Xun grabbed Xiaguo and noticed her disheveled hair and loosened buttons. She was very worried: "What exactly happened? Explain clearly!"
[ZH] 夏果又跪下：“呜呜……主君找我问话，没问几句，把我抱到床上，他劲儿大，我怎么反抗都没用，呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo knelt down again: "Wuwu... The Lord summoned me for questioning. After just a few questions, he carried me to the bed. He's so strong, no matter how I resisted, it was useless. Wuwu...!"
[ZH] “林觉可得手？”
[EN] "Did Lin Jue succeed?"
[ZH] “呜呜……！那倒没有，我踹了主君一脚，趁机逃走，呜呜……！”
[EN] "Wuwu...! No, he didn't. I kicked the Lord and took the chance to escape. Wuwu...!"
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，气愤道：“竟然敢欺负我的人，看我怎么削他！起来，跟我一起找林觉算账！”
[EN] Qianli Xun breathed a sigh of relief, then said angrily: "He actually dares to bully my people. Let me teach him a lesson! Get up, come with me to settle accounts with Lin Jue!"
[ZH] 苦荞拉住小姐：“您去必然与姑爷打起来，只怕和离之事遥遥无期！”
[EN] Kuqiao held back Young Lady: "If you go, you'll definitely fight with the Young Master. I'm afraid the mutual divorce will be postponed indefinitely!"
[ZH] “林觉欺人太甚，顾不了那么多。”
[EN] "Lin Jue has gone too far. I can't worry about that now."
[ZH] 千里寻执意找林觉算账，不管在哪个时代，她身边的人都不能随意被人欺负。
[EN] Qianli Xun was determined to settle accounts with Lin Jue. No matter which era she was in, the people around her couldn't be bullied at will.
[ZH] 她气冲冲来到主屋门外，大喊道：“林觉！给我滚出来！”
[EN] She stormed to the door of the main hall and shouted: "Lin Jue! Get your ass out here!"
[ZH] 林觉在屋内坐着，听到朱淑真的声音，还是有些胆怯，犹豫要不要出去，毕竟是丑事，闹到大庭广众之下，脸上挂不住，决定还是不出去得好。
[EN] Lin Jue was sitting inside. Hearing Zhu Shuzhen's voice, he was still somewhat timid. He hesitated whether to go out. After all, it was a scandalous matter. If it became public, he'd lose face. He decided it was better not to go out.
[ZH] 没任何反应，千里寻干脆直接冲进屋内。
[EN] There was no response. Qianli Xun simply charged into the room.
[ZH] 林觉正坐在桌前拿本书，一副道貌岸然的样子。
[EN] Lin Jue was sitting at the table holding a book, looking hypocritically dignified.
[ZH] 她上前把林觉手中的书抢走，放到一边：“你这种人还有脸看王右丞的诗集！起来！给夏果道歉！”
[EN] She stepped forward, snatched the book from Lin Jue's hands, and put it aside: "Someone like you still has the face to read Wang Wei's poetry collection! Get up! Apologize to Xiaguo!"
[ZH] 林觉质问道：“凭什么给一个下人道歉？”
[EN] Lin Jue challenged: "Why should I apologize to a servant?"
[ZH] “她是我的丫鬟，你欺负她就是欺负我。”
[EN] "She's my maid. Bullying her is bullying me."
[ZH] “我是主君，欺负个把丫鬟你又能把我怎样？”
[EN] "I'm the Lord. Even if I bully a maid or two, what can you do to me?"
[ZH] “给你两个选择，要么被我打一顿，要么乖乖道歉！”
[EN] "I'll give you two choices: either take a beating from me, or apologize like a good boy!"
[ZH] 林觉装作和颜悦色的样子：“朱淑真，不要为一个丫鬟伤你我和气，小心我不同意和离，耗着你。”
[EN] Lin Jue pretended to be amiable: "Zhu Shuzhen, don't damage our relationship over a maid. Be careful, or I won't agree to the divorce and keep you waiting."
[ZH] 千里寻说：“哼！我已经决定，不会把夏果送给你，至于不同意和离那是你的事，在我心里早已与你和离。耗着我是吧？我也耗着你！本来打算早些搬出林府，此时我改变主意，偏偏要住在林府西院，以后你别再想把任何商妓带回林府，带一个我打一个；更别想纳妾，我会闹得没人敢给你当妾室；你要是出去逛青楼，我会随时派人盯着，你去哪里我就打到哪里，看哪个青楼还敢接待你！”
[EN] Qianli Xun said: "Hmph! I've already decided I won't give Xiaguo to you. As for not agreeing to the divorce, that's your business. In my heart, I've already divorced you. You want to stall? I'll stall you too! I originally planned to move out of the Lin residence soon, but now I've changed my mind. I'll stay in the west courtyard on purpose. From now on, don't even think about bringing any merchant prostitutes back. Bring one, and I'll beat her. Don't even think about taking a concubine. I'll make such a scene that no one will dare. If you go out to visit brothels, I'll send someone to watch you. Wherever you go, I'll go and beat you up. Let's see which brothel will still dare to serve you!"
[ZH] 林觉被激怒：“你……你……！陈浩宇说得没错，你就是个母老虎！小心我休了你！”
[EN] Lin Jue was provoked: "You... you...! Chen Haoyu was right. You're a shrew! Be careful, or I'll divorce you!"
[ZH] “休我？求之不得！如果不想挨打，老老实实给夏果道歉！”
[EN] "Divorce me? I'd love that! If you don't want to get beaten, obediently apologize to Xiaguo!"
[ZH] 打又打不过，至于道歉，林觉一百个不乐意。可总不能这么僵着，得有人低头，目前的情形只有他低头的份儿，毕竟他非礼了夏果，有错在先。
[EN] He couldn't beat her anyway. As for apologizing, Lin Jue was extremely unwilling. But they couldn't stay in this deadlock forever. Someone had to bow down. In the current situation, only he could bow down. After all, he had molested Xiaguo and was in the wrong first.
[ZH] 林觉忍住怒气，好言相劝：“我是主君，怎么能向一个丫鬟道歉？大不了我当着你的面发誓，以后不再动她，不再对她有非分之想，你觉得如何？”
[EN] Lin Jue suppressed his anger and tried to persuade her gently: "I'm the Lord. How can I apologize to a maid? At most, I can swear in front of you that I won't touch her again and won't have any improper thoughts about her. How does that sound?"
[ZH] 千里寻历来最讨厌好色之徒，见一个打一个，绝不会轻易饶了林觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun had always hated lecherous men the most. She'd beat every one she saw and would never let Lin Jue off easily.
[ZH] “不行！必须道歉！”
[EN] "No! You must apologize!"
[ZH] “朱淑真！来劲了是吧！”
[EN] "Zhu Shuzhen! You're really pushing it!"
[ZH] “你说呢？”千里寻举起拳头在林觉眼前晃晃。
[EN] "What do you think?" Qianli Xun raised her fist and waved it in front of Lin Jue's eyes.
[ZH] 林觉逼不得已：“好！你厉害！我道歉，行了吧。不过你得答应我，以后不能闹到青楼去。”
[EN] Lin Jue had no choice: "Fine! You win! I'll apologize, okay? But you have to promise me not to cause a scene at the brothels in the future."
[ZH] 千里寻在心里冷笑道：“一句假话，还真把他吓住。”口中说道：“我答应你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sneered in her heart: "One lie and he's actually scared." She said out loud: "I promise you."
[ZH] 林觉走到夏果跟前，很不情愿地吐出三个字：“对不住。”
[EN] Lin Jue walked up to Xiaguo and reluctantly spat out three words: "I'm sorry."
[ZH] 夏果没有理会。
[EN] Xiaguo ignored him.
[ZH] 林觉瞧着她的头发比刚才乱了些：“把头发弄那么乱干什么？好像我得手一样，我也没落上什么好，被你踹得生疼。”
[EN] Lin Jue noticed her hair was more disheveled than before: "Why did you mess up your hair so much? It's like I succeeded. I didn't get anything good out of it either. You kicked me so hard it still hurts."
[ZH] 千里寻见好就收，不愿与林觉计较太多，走之前好心叮嘱：“你我耗着对谁都不好，建议你认真考虑一下，尽快与我和离。”
[EN] Qianli Xun knew when to stop. She didn't want to argue with Lin Jue too much. Before leaving, she kindly reminded him: "Stalling isn't good for either of us. I suggest you think seriously about it and divorce me as soon as possible."
[ZH] 说完带着苦荞和夏果离开。
[EN] With that, she left with Kuqiao and Xiaguo.
[ZH] 林觉被逼道歉，心里很不爽，对着朱淑真的背影说：“我就不同意和离，耗死你！”
[EN] Lin Jue was forced to apologize and was very unhappy. He said to Zhu Shuzhen's back: "I won't agree to the divorce. I'll stall you to death!"
[ZH] 夏果之事让期待的和离遥遥无期，千里寻的内心感到很无助，难道竹林之行带来的运气很快被消耗殆尽？
[EN] The matter with Xiaguo made the anticipated divorce seem indefinitely distant. Qianli Xun felt very helpless inside. Had the luck brought by the bamboo forest trip been quickly exhausted?
[ZH] 她想起净光法师说的改变观念和行为之事，她的观念和行为与朱淑真不同，只有身体是她的，为何还不能过上属于千里寻的生活？
[EN] She remembered what Master Jingguang had said about changing one's mindset and behavior. Her mindset and behavior were different from Zhu Shuzhen's. Only her body belonged to Zhu Shuzhen. Why couldn't she live a life that belonged to Qianli Xun?
[ZH] 又仔细一想，如若是朱淑真，只会被林觉欺负；而现在，林觉只有被千里寻欺负的份儿，不就证明已过上千里寻的生活。
[EN] Thinking carefully, if it were Zhu Shuzhen, she'd only be bullied by Lin Jue. But now, Lin Jue was the one being bullied by Qianli Xun. Didn't that prove she was already living Qianli Xun's life?
[ZH] 想到这些，又有一丝快意，剩下婚姻这堵墙需慢慢攻克。与净光法师聊天儿的过程中，她已猜到终将会结束这段婚姻，还是耐心等待时机到来。
[EN] Thinking of this, she felt a bit satisfied. The remaining wall of marriage needed to be slowly dismantled. During her chat with Master Jingguang, she had already guessed that this marriage would eventually end. She just needed to patiently wait for the right moment.
[ZH] 第二日午时，千里寻来到观景亭。西湖的观景亭位于水色天光之中，她再次被西湖美景吸引。
[EN] The next day at noon, Qianli Xun arrived at the Sightseeing Pavilion. The pavilion at West Lake was situated amidst the water's color and sky's light. She was once again captivated by the beautiful scenery of West Lake.
[ZH] 亭中有位男子的身影，远远看去，身姿挺拔，那背影优雅中不失力度，散发出的气息与天地融为一体，仿佛周围的空气都弥漫着他身上的魅力一般。
[EN] There was a man's figure in the pavilion. From afar, his posture was upright. His back view was elegant yet powerful. The aura he emitted seemed to merge with heaven and earth, as if the surrounding air was filled with his charm.
[ZH] 千里寻再仔细观察周围，亭中除那背影再无一人，心中纳闷：“不是诗会吗？东方尘泽他们为何不见人影？”
[EN] Qianli Xun observed the surroundings more carefully. There was no one else in the pavilion except that back view. She was puzzled: "Isn't this a poetry gathering? Why aren't Dongfang Chenze and the others here?"
[ZH] 怀疑归怀疑，还是向苦荞询问：“他可是乔文仙乔公子？”
[EN] Despite her doubts, she still asked Kuqiao: "Is that Young Master Qiao Wenxian?"
[ZH] 苦荞笑道：“正是，没想到乔公子是借诗会之名约小姐出来。”
[EN] Kuqiao smiled: "Yes. I didn't expect Young Master Qiao to use the poetry gathering as an excuse to invite Young Lady out."
[ZH] 夏果心里想道：“唉！一个逛青楼，一个出来私会外男，难怪闹到和离。”
[EN] Xiaguo thought to herself: "Sigh! One visits brothels, the other meets men in secret. No wonder it came to divorce."
[ZH] 千里寻向亭子靠近，边走边在心里想着如何与乔文仙开口。到达亭子跟前，谨慎地唤了声：“乔公子。”
[EN] Qianli Xun approached the pavilion, thinking along the way how to start a conversation with Qiao Wenxian. Reaching the pavilion, she cautiously called out: "Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 乔文仙转过身，面带笑容，快一年了，他的心里一直装着淑真，此刻真真切切地见到，她丝毫未变，还像以前一样美……
[EN] Qiao Wenxian turned around with a smile. For almost a year, his heart had been filled with Shuzhen. Now seeing her in person, she hadn't changed at all. She was still as beautiful as before...
[ZH] 当乔文仙转过身时，千里寻惊呆了，世上竟有如此好看的男子！不管是身姿还是五官，比例恰到好处，无论视觉触及到他身上哪一处都觉得无比舒畅。
[EN] When Qiao Wenxian turned around, Qianli Xun was stunned. How could there be such a handsome man in the world! Whether it was his posture or his features, the proportions were perfect. Wherever her gaze landed on him, it felt incredibly pleasant.
[ZH] 她实在想不通有这么一位尤物当知己，为何朱淑真会挂念徐公子？反正她千里寻已心跳加快，脸色微红，想要恋爱的想法愈加强烈。不行，得尽快和离，否则岂不是辜负了眼前的美男子？
[EN] She really couldn't understand. With such a stunning man as a confidant, why would Zhu Shuzhen still think of Young Master Xu? Anyway, Qianli Xun's heart was beating faster, her face slightly flushed, and her desire to be in a relationship grew stronger. No, she had to divorce as soon as possible. Otherwise, wouldn't she be letting down the handsome man before her?
[ZH] 夏果也被乔文仙吸引，她多次在梦中梦到的人不就是这个样子吗？眼前的公子满足了她对男子外形所有的美好想象。
[EN] Xiaguo was also attracted to Qiao Wenxian. Wasn't this exactly the person she had dreamed of many times? The young man before her satisfied all her beautiful imaginations of what a man should look like.
[ZH] 朱淑真称他“乔公子”，乔文仙想起失忆之事，看来她真的忘记自己，连平日对他的称呼都不记得。他强调道：“你还是像以前一样称呼我文渊吧。”
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen called him "Young Master Qiao." Qiao Wenxian remembered her amnesia. It seemed she had really forgotten him, even forgetting how she usually addressed him. He emphasized: "You can still call me Wenyuan like before."
[ZH] 千里寻回过神来：“哦，是该称呼文渊。”
[EN] Qianli Xun came to her senses: "Oh, I should call you Wenyuan."
[ZH] 她带着羞涩和期待走进亭内。
[EN] She walked into the pavilion with shyness and anticipation.
[ZH] 夏果想要跟进去，被苦荞拉住，拽到远处。
[EN] Xiaguo wanted to follow them in, but Kuqiao held her back and pulled her away.
[ZH] 夏果很不满：“为何不能跟小姐进去？”
[EN] Xiaguo was very dissatisfied: "Why can't I go in with Young Lady?"
[ZH] 苦荞解释道：“主人说话，我们当丫鬟的还是少听为妙。”
[EN] Kuqiao explained: "When the master is talking, it's better for us maids not to listen."
[ZH] 夏果立刻笑着说：“苦荞姐姐，问你个事。”
[EN] Xiaguo immediately smiled and said: "Sister Kuqiao, let me ask you something."
[ZH] “问呗。”
[EN] "Ask away."
[ZH] “乔公子的家世如何？”
[EN] "What's Young Master Qiao's family background?"
[ZH] “问这做什么？”
[EN] "Why ask that?"
[ZH] “我刚给小姐当丫鬟，总得了解小姐身边的人，以后碰见就知道该如何应对。”
[EN] "I just became Young Lady's maid. I should know about the people around her. That way, when I meet them in the future, I'll know how to respond."
[ZH] “给你说说也无妨，乔家是皇亲国戚，家世当然好。”
[EN] "It's fine to tell you. The Qiao family are imperial relatives. Their family background is naturally excellent."
[ZH] “啊！皇亲国戚！”夏果更对乔文仙充满向往。
[EN] "Ah! Imperial relatives!" Xiaguo was even more filled with longing for Qiao Wenxian.
[ZH] 观景亭中，千里寻忍住花痴的表情问道：“不是诗会吗？怎么只来你一人？”尽管苦荞已给出答案，可千里寻还想问，她想听乔文仙如何回答。
[EN] In the Sightseeing Pavilion, Qianli Xun suppressed her smitten expression and asked: "Isn't this a poetry gathering? Why are you the only one here?" Although Kuqiao had already given the answer, Qianli Xun still wanted to ask. She wanted to hear how Qiao Wenxian would answer.
[ZH] “我很想光明正大单独约你出来，可你已成亲，总得顾及你的名声，只能以诗会为由。”
[EN] "I really wanted to invite you out openly and alone, but you're married. I have to consider your reputation, so I could only use the poetry gathering as an excuse."
[ZH] 千里寻故意装作严肃的样子：“以诗会为由约我出来，难道就能保全我的名声？”
[EN] Qianli Xun deliberately pretended to be serious: “Using the poetry gathering as an excuse to invite me out—does that really protect my reputation？”
[ZH] “我顾不了那么多，只想见到你。你知道吗？我离开临安是因为你成婚我太过伤心，以为离开后会渐渐把你忘记，可在汴梁没有一日不想你，听说你被夫君毒打，我更是痛心疾首，想回来将林觉打一顿。后来又听说你打算和离，我已无心在汴梁待下去，便匆忙赶回来。”
[EN] "I couldn't care about that anymore. I just wanted to see you. Do you know? I left Lin'an because I was too heartbroken over your marriage. I thought after leaving, I'd gradually forget you. But in Bianliang, there wasn't a single day I didn't think of you. Not a single day. When I heard your husband was beating you, I was even more heartbroken and wanted to come back and beat Lin Jue myself. Later, when I heard you were planning to divorce, I had no heart to stay in Bianliang anymore, so I hurried back."
[ZH] 千里寻第一次对异性的表白动心。男女之间哪有真正的友情？所谓的“知己”，不过是一方想要接近另一方的由头。
[EN] This was the first time Qianli Xun was moved by a man's confession. Where was there true friendship between men and women? The so-called "confidant" was just an excuse for one party to get closer to the other.
[ZH] 朱淑真心里没有乔文仙，她千里寻却在瞬间把乔文仙装进心里，也许是她太想谈恋爱的缘故，怪只怪乔文仙出现得恰到好处。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen didn't have Qiao Wenxian in her heart, but Qianli Xun had instantly put Qiao Wenxian in hers. Perhaps it was because she wanted to be in a relationship so badly. She could only blame Qiao Wenxian for appearing at just the right moment.
[ZH] “没想到你对我是这等心思。”
[EN] "I didn't expect you to have such feelings for me."
[ZH] “你心里一直有徐公子，我不敢向你表露心迹，后来你又无缘无故嫁人，我更不敢向你说明。发生这么多事，我不想再错失机会，才忍不住把你请出来，向你表明一切。……听东方兄说你失忆，可否还记得……”
[EN] "You always had Young Master Xu in your heart. I didn't dare reveal my feelings to you. Later, you married for no reason, and I dared even less to explain. So much has happened. I didn't want to miss this opportunity again, so I couldn't help but invite you out and reveal everything to you... I heard from Brother Dongfang that you have amnesia. Do you still remember..."
[ZH] 千里寻猜出乔文仙的意思，红着脸说：“我已不记得徐公子，连他长什么样都不知道，估计在大街上遇见只会把他当成陌生人。可我却记得文渊，你说奇怪不奇怪？”
[EN] Qianli Xun guessed Qiao Wenxian's meaning. Blushing, she said: "I don't remember Young Master Xu anymore. I don't even know what he looks like. If I met him on the street, I'd probably treat him like a stranger. But I remember Wenyuan. Isn't that strange?"
[ZH] 朱淑真隐晦的表白令乔文仙开心，终于听到期待已久的话，他想上前抓住她的手，或是抱住她，考虑到她的婚姻，还是控制住冲动：“和离之事是不是很难解决？”
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen's subtle confession made Qiao Wenxian happy. Finally hearing the words he had longed for, he wanted to step forward and hold her hand, or even hug her. But considering her marriage, he controlled his impulse: "Is the divorce matter hard to resolve?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“是很麻烦，不过我会想办法。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded: "It's very troublesome, but I'll find a way."
[ZH] “需要我做什么？”
[EN] "What do you need me to do?"
[ZH] “你只需等着我的好消息。”
[EN] "Just wait for my good news."
[ZH] “淑真，你能接受我的感情我真的很开心。”
[EN] "Shuzhen, I'm really happy you can accept my feelings."
[ZH] “我也开心，在陌生的时代碰到自己喜欢的人不容易，我很珍惜。”
[EN] "I'm happy too. It's not easy to meet someone you like in a strange era. I cherish it very much."
[ZH] 乔文仙沉浸在表白得到肯定的喜悦中，没有心思追问“陌生的时代”背后的含义：“我想找知府大人，劝说他同意和离之事……”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was immersed in the joy of having his confession accepted. He had no mind to pursue the meaning behind "a strange era": "I want to find the prefect and persuade him to agree to the divorce..."
[ZH] “千万不要！你去找他只能将事情搞得愈加复杂。你有这份心思，我已心满意足。我有一个小小的要求，希望你能答应。”
[EN] "Absolutely not! If you go to him, it'll only make things more complicated. Having this intention from you is enough for me. I have a small request, and I hope you'll agree."
[ZH] “不管你有什么要求，我无不答应。”
[EN] "No matter what your request is, I'll agree to all of it."
[ZH] “能不能在我和离之前，我们尽量不要见面？我怕林觉抓住什么把柄，再来刁难我。”
[EN] "Can we try not to meet before I get divorced? I'm afraid Lin Jue will catch some evidence and use it to trouble me again."
[ZH] “虽然我很不情愿，还是会答应你，放心，我会控制好自己的感情，等着你。”
[EN] "Although I'm very reluctant, I'll still agree to you. Don't worry, I'll control my feelings and wait for you."
[ZH] 千里寻行礼道：“文渊，就此别过，定要等着我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun bowed and said: "Wenyuan, let's part here for now. You must wait for me."
[ZH] 望着淑真离去的背影，乔文仙兴奋不已，终于得到心仪女子肯定的答复，他要赶回去把好消息告诉仪王，也让他替自己高兴高兴。
[EN] Watching Shuzhen's departing figure, Qiao Wenxian was extremely excited. Finally getting a positive response from the woman he loved, he wanted to hurry back and tell Prince Yi the good news, letting him be happy for him too.
[ZH] 临行前，仪王想要跟着一起来。乔文仙没有同意，只说出去与朋友见一面，仪王在一旁不太方便。
[EN] Before leaving, Prince Yi wanted to come along. Qiao Wenxian didn't agree, saying he was just going out to meet a friend. It wouldn't be convenient with Prince Yi there.
[ZH] 仪王一下子猜中，问他该不会去见朱淑真。乔文仙并未承认，推脱着迅速出了幽林别居。因怕被朱淑真拒绝，当着更多人面子上挂不住，他甚至连南双都未带在身边。
[EN] Prince Yi guessed immediately and asked if he was going to see Zhu Shuzhen. Qiao Wenxian didn't admit it and made excuses to quickly leave Youlin Villa. Afraid of being rejected by Zhu Shuzhen and losing face in front of more people, he didn't even bring Nan Shuang with him.
[ZH] 没成想一切顺利，乔文仙从心里感谢淑真失忆，要不是失忆，她又怎会接受他的感情？
[EN] He didn't expect everything to go so smoothly. Qiao Wenxian was truly grateful for Shuzhen's amnesia in his heart. If not for the amnesia, how could she have accepted his feelings?

#CH 53
[ZH] 千里寻像个初恋少女，脸色微红，想起文渊说的话，时不时露出羞涩的笑容。
[EN] Qianli Xun was like a young girl in her first love, a faint blush on her cheeks. Thinking of what Wenyuan had said, she couldn't help but smile shyly from time to time.
[ZH] 苦荞猜小姐定是喜欢上乔公子，她从心里替小姐感到高兴，也慨叹，如若早些如此那该多好。
[EN] Kuqiao guessed that Young Lady must have fallen for Young Master Qiao. She was genuinely happy for her and sighed, wishing things had worked out this way sooner.
[ZH] 夏果则十分羡慕，都怪苦荞，硬是把她拉走，如若她也走进观景亭，说不定会被乔公子注意到。忍不住直白地问：“小姐，乔公子该不会是喜欢您？”
[EN] Xiaguo, on the other hand, was quite envious. She blamed Kuqiao for dragging her away. If she had also gone into the Sightseeing Pavilion, Young Master Qiao might have noticed her. She couldn't help but ask bluntly: "Young Lady, could it be that Young Master Qiao likes you?"
[ZH] 千里寻掩饰道：“别瞎说，乔公子是江南才子，向来爱好品鉴诗词，之所以请我来，不过是对我的一首词不甚理解，专门向我请教而已。”
[EN] Qianli Xun covered up: "Don't talk nonsense. Young Master Qiao is a renowned talent from Jiangnan who has always enjoyed appreciating poetry. The reason he invited me was simply because he didn't quite understand one of my ci poems and wanted to ask me about it."
[ZH] 夏果回道：“是，我以后不会再瞎说。”心里却想，难怪乔公子会喜欢小姐，只因她会写诗作词。自己的容貌虽不比小姐差，可连字都还没认全，更别说写诗作词。
[EN] Xiaguo replied: “Yes, I won't talk nonsense anymore.” But in her heart she thought, no wonder Young Master Qiao likes Young Lady—it's because she can write poetry. Though her looks were no worse than Young Lady's, she hadn't even learned all her characters yet, let alone how to write poetry.
[ZH] “小姐，能不能等我把字认全，您也教我写诗作词？”
[EN] "Young Lady, after I've learned all my characters, could you teach me to write poetry too?"
[ZH] 千里寻感到为难，写小说她没问题，写宋代的诗词她并不会。得益于脑容量够大，将朱淑真的诗词记得多，有事没事来那么一首，可要教人，实在为难：“你先把字认全，以后再说。”
[EN] Qianli Xun found it difficult. Writing novels was no problem for her, but writing Song Dynasty poetry—she couldn't do that. Thanks to her excellent memory, she had memorized most of Zhu Shuzhen's poems and could recite one now and then, but teaching someone else was truly beyond her: “First learn all your characters. We'll talk about it later.”
[ZH] 夏果回道：“是，小姐。”
[EN] Xiaguo replied: "Yes, Young Lady."
[ZH] 苦荞十分不解：“夏果，你我是丫鬟，能学会认字已经不错，写诗作词不是一般人能学会，就别想了。”
[EN] Kuqiao was quite puzzled: "Xiaguo, you and I are maidservants. Being able to learn to read is already good enough. Writing poetry isn't something just anyone can learn. Don't even think about it."
[ZH] 夏果辩解道：“小姐是女词人，我们是她身边的丫鬟，如果也学会一些，别人见丫鬟都如此厉害，会认为小姐更厉害。”
[EN] Xiaguo argued: "Young Lady is a famous woman ci poet. We're her maidservants. If we also learned some, people would think, 'If even the maidservants are this talented, Young Lady must be even more impressive.'"
[ZH] 苦荞反对道：“你说得不对，我一直跟着小姐，不会写诗作词，大家照样认为小姐厉害。所以小姐厉害跟丫鬟一点儿关系都没有。”
[EN] Kuqiao disagreed: "You've got it wrong. I've been with Young Lady all along and can't write poetry, yet everyone still thinks Young Lady is amazing. So Young Lady's talent has absolutely nothing to do with her maidservants."
[ZH] 夏果被苦荞怼得一时无语。
[EN] Xiaguo was left speechless by Kuqiao's rebuttal.
[ZH] 突入其来的爱情让千里寻感到幸福，她内心的感情终于被填满，也找回久违的踏实与归属感。
[EN] This love that came out of nowhere filled Qianli Xun with happiness. Her heart was finally full, and she had found a long-lost sense of security and belonging.
[ZH] 从观景亭回来，穿越而来的陌生感荡然无存，这便是爱情力量。千里寻想要在文渊身上体验爱情的美好，想要与文渊步入琴瑟和鸣的婚姻生活。
[EN] After returning from the Sightseeing Pavilion, the sense of strangeness from traveling through time had completely vanished—such was the power of love. Qianli Xun wanted to experience the beauty of love with Wenyuan, to enter into a marriage of perfect harmony with him.
[ZH] 马车到达林府门口，她急急匆匆进了林府，来到西院，直奔韦嬷嬷居住的院子，吩咐两个丫鬟在外等候，她要与韦嬷嬷说些心里话。一进屋，迅速把门关上，立刻跪在韦嬷嬷面前。
[EN] The carriage arrived at the Lin residence. She hurried inside and went straight to the west courtyard. Then she rushed to Aunt Wei's quarters. She ordered the two maidservants to wait outside—she needed to have a heart-to-heart talk with Aunt Wei. As soon as she entered the room, she quickly closed the door and immediately knelt before Aunt Wei.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷感到惊讶，上前去扶：“姑娘怎能跪我？快起来！”
[EN] Aunt Wei was surprised and stepped forward to help her up: "How can Young Lady kneel before me? Please, get up!"
[ZH] 千里寻执意不起：“求嬷嬷帮我尽快促成和离之事，我一刻也不想再等下去。”
[EN] Qianli Xun insisted on staying kneeling: "I beg you, Auntie, to help me expedite the mutual divorce. I don't want to wait another moment."
[ZH] “我也希望姑娘尽快与姑爷和离，可只要姑爷不同意，我们是一点儿办法都没有，你先起来，坐下仔细说。”韦嬷嬷扶起姑娘，拉她坐在榻上。
[EN] "I also hope Young Lady can divorce the Young Master as soon as possible. But as long as he doesn't agree, there's nothing we can do. Get up first, sit down, and tell me everything." Aunt Wei helped her up and pulled her to sit on the platform bed.
[ZH] 榻上放一张小方桌，千里寻脱掉鞋子，学着韦嬷嬷盘腿而坐。
[EN] A small square table sat on the platform bed. Qianli Xun took off her shoes and, imitating Aunt Wei, sat cross-legged.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷吩咐丫鬟为姑娘上茶，待茶放好后，问道：“我听说姑娘昨日还对姑爷说要耗着他，为何今日又如此着急？”
[EN] Aunt Wei ordered the maidservant to serve tea. Once the tea was set down, she asked: "I heard that just yesterday Young Lady told the Young Master you'd outlast him. Why are you in such a rush today?"
[ZH] 千里寻不愿瞒着韦嬷嬷：“我已有喜欢之人，想尽快结束不如意的婚姻。”
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't want to hide anything from Aunt Wei: "I have someone I like. I want to end this unhappy marriage as soon as possible."
[ZH] “可是徐公子？”
[EN] "Is it Young Master Xu?"
[ZH] “不是，我已不记得他。”
[EN] "No, I don't remember him anymore."
[ZH] “不记得甚好，在我看来徐公子根本配不上姑娘。姑娘出去一趟就有喜欢之人，不知是哪家公子？”
[EN] "Forgetting is for the best. In my opinion, Young Master Xu was never worthy of Young Lady anyway. You went out once and already found someone you like. I wonder which young master it is?"
[ZH] “乔家公子乔文仙。”
[EN] “Young Master Qiao from the Qiao family—Qiao Wenxian.”
[ZH] “确实不错，我虽没见过乔公子本人，但总听苦荞提起，跟姑娘倒是志同道合，既如此，我定会帮姑娘。要想和离，除了说服姑爷，还有一位关键人物……”
[EN] "That's indeed a good match. Though I've never met Young Master Qiao in person, I've often heard Kuqiao mention him. He and Young Lady share the same interests. Since that's the case, I'll definitely help you. But for the divorce to happen, besides persuading the Young Master, there's another key person..."
[ZH] “我爹？”
[EN] "My father?"
[ZH] “对！只要朱家老爷同意，姑爷不敢说半个不字。他之所以能耗着，就是仗着他的岳丈大人撑腰。”
[EN] "Exactly! As long as Master Zhu agrees, the Young Master won't dare say no. The only reason he can drag his feet is because he has his father-in-law backing him up."
[ZH] 千里寻为难道：“可我爹根本不可能同意。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said with difficulty: "But my father would never agree to it."
[ZH] “所以得想办法让他同意。”
[EN] "That's why we need to find a way to make him agree."
[ZH] “什么办法？”
[EN] "What way?"
[ZH] “唉，现在我还想不出来，只能走一步看一步，正好有个机会姑娘必须回朱府一趟。”韦嬷嬷拿出请帖，“这是今日朱府派人送来的，老爷要举行宴会，邀请临安有头有脸的人参加，也邀请你跟姑爷同去，我正好跟着姑娘回去看看夫人，顺便找机会当面与老爷说说和离之事，希望能说服他。”
[EN] “Sigh, I can't think of anything right now. We'll have to take it one step at a time. Fortunately, there's an opportunity—Young Lady must return to the Zhu residence.” Aunt Wei took out an invitation. “This was delivered by the Zhu family today. The Master is hosting a banquet and has invited all the big shots in Lin'an. He's also invited Young Lady and the Young Master to attend together. I'll take this chance to go back with Young Lady to see Madam, and find an opportunity to speak with the Master about the divorce face-to-face. I hope to persuade him.”
[ZH] “有劳嬷嬷。”
[EN] "Thank you, Auntie."
[ZH] “老爷十分固执，只怕难以说服，大不了我拼了这条老命也要让老爷同意你与姑爷和离。”
[EN] "The Master is very stubborn. I'm afraid it won't be easy to persuade him. But if it comes to it, I'll risk my old life to make him agree to the divorce between you and the Young Master."
[ZH] “嬷嬷，千万不要。”
[EN] "Auntie, please don't."
[ZH] “姑娘放心，我不会做傻事。”韦嬷嬷暗自决定，如若朱晞颜不同意，她便以性命胁迫。
[EN] "Don't worry, Young Lady. I won't do anything foolish." But secretly, Aunt Wei made up her mind: if Zhu Xiyan didn't agree, she would threaten him with her own life.
[ZH] 乔文仙回到幽林别居，直奔主屋。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian returned to Youlin Villa and headed straight for the main hall.
[ZH] 申简辰正在院中教白风练习拳法，见文渊前来，脸上有掩饰不住的笑容，猜到他此次出去密约佳人，定是满载而归。笑道：“文渊有什么喜事？如此开心！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was in the courtyard teaching Bai Feng boxing techniques. Seeing Wenyuan arrive with an unmistakable smile on his face, he guessed that this secret rendezvous with his beloved must have been a complete success. He smiled: "What happy news, Wenyuan? Why so cheerful?"
[ZH] 乔文仙拉起仪王走进屋内，白风并不理会他们，继续练习拳法。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian pulled Prince Yi into the room. Bai Feng paid them no mind and continued practicing his boxing.
[ZH] 进屋后，二人还未坐下，乔文仙已忍不住，兴奋地说：“你肯定想不到，淑真竟然接受我的表白，我一路上都不敢相信这是真的，可的的确确真实发生。”
[EN] Once inside, before either of them had sat down, Qiao Wenxian couldn't contain himself and said excitedly: “You'll never guess—Shuzhen actually accepted my confession! I couldn't believe it was true the whole way back, but it really, truly happened.”
[ZH] 他一把抱住仪王：“我太激动！怎么办？她竟然答应我！其实我想这样抱着她，可我不敢，只能抱抱你，把你当成她……”
[EN] He threw his arms around Prince Yi: "I'm so excited! What do I do? She actually said yes! I wanted to hold her like this, but I didn't dare. I can only hold you instead and pretend you're her..."
[ZH] 申简辰觉得十分可笑，扶开文渊：“冷静，冷静，不就是表白得到回应，何须如此激动？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen found it quite ridiculous and pushed Wenyuan away: “Calm down, calm down. So your confession got a response—why be this excited？”
[ZH] “你不知道，我等这一天等了多久！”
[EN] "You don't know how long I've waited for this day!"
[ZH] “不管你等了多久，都不应该如此激动，要知道你现在喜欢的可是有夫之妇。”
[EN] "No matter how long you've waited, you shouldn't be this excited. Remember, the woman you like now is married."
[ZH] 乔文仙立刻清醒：“是啊，有婚姻挡在前面，我与她该怎么办？她说让我耐心等待和离的好消息，可我不想等，你说我该怎么办？总不能干着急，得帮她做些什么。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian immediately sobered up: “Right. There's a marriage standing in the way. What should she and I do? She told me to wait patiently for good news about the divorce, but I don't want to wait. What should I do? I can't just sit here worrying—I need to do something to help her.”
[ZH] 申简辰郑重地问：“你真的很爱她？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked solemnly: "Do you really love her that much?"
[ZH] “那还用问！”
[EN] "Is that even a question!"
[ZH] “非她不娶？”
[EN] "Would you marry no one else?"
[ZH] “当然！”
[EN] "Of course!"
[ZH] “既如此，我帮你。”
[EN] "In that case, I'll help you."
[ZH] “你帮我？”乔文仙愣了一下，很快醒悟过来，又抱住仪王，“太好了！你是仪王，又是临安节度使，林觉还不得听你的？知府大人也不敢驳你的面子，由你出面，和离之事一定能成。”
[EN] "You'll help me?" Qiao Wenxian was stunned for a moment, then quickly realized what this meant and hugged Prince Yi again. "That's wonderful! You're Prince Yi and the Regional Governor of Lin'an. Lin Jue would have to listen to you, wouldn't he? Even Prefect Zhu wouldn't dare refuse your face. With you stepping in, the divorce is as good as done."
[ZH] “别再抱着我，好热。”
[EN] "Stop hugging me. It's hot."
[ZH] 乔文仙放开仪王：“我现在终于体会到作为皇亲国戚的好处，遇到难处有王爷撑腰，真好！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian let go of Prince Yi: “I finally understand the benefits of being imperial relatives. Having a prince backing you up when you're in trouble—it's truly wonderful！”
[ZH] 申简辰叹道：“唉，我是仪王，又是临安节度使，来到临安一件大事没办，倒先帮人离婚，传出去会不会被人笑话？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen sighed: "Sigh. I'm Prince Yi and the Regional Governor of Lin'an, yet since arriving in Lin'an I haven't accomplished a single important matter. Instead, I'm helping someone get a divorce first. Won't people laugh if this gets out?"
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“你是官家的儿子，要替官家关心黎民百姓。我与淑真均为草民，是你的百姓，百姓之事无小事，你帮我们只会被人竖大拇指夸赞，怎会笑话？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said: “You are the Emperor's son. You should care for the common people on His Majesty's behalf. Shuzhen and I are both commoners—your people. There are no small matters when it comes to the people. Helping us would only earn you thumbs-up praise, not ridicule.”
[ZH] 申简辰说：“有些道理！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said: "That makes sense!"
[ZH] 李管家手里拿着请帖来到院中，见白风练习拳法十分起劲，夸道：“真厉害，不愧是仪王的贴身侍卫。我家公子和仪王可在？”
[EN] Butler Li arrived in the courtyard holding an invitation. Seeing Bai Feng practicing boxing with great vigor, he praised: “Truly impressive—worthy of being Prince Yi's personal guard. Are our young master and Prince Yi here？”
[ZH] 白风边练边回：“在里面说话。”
[EN] Bai Feng replied while continuing his practice: "They're inside talking."
[ZH] 李管家走到门口，向里说：“仪王，公子，乔府派人送来请帖，知府大人明日举办宴会邀请公子前去。”
[EN] Butler Li walked to the doorway and said inside: "Prince Yi, Young Master, the Qiao family has sent an invitation. Prefect Zhu is hosting a banquet tomorrow and invites Young Master to attend."
[ZH] 乔文仙到门口接过请帖：“我爹可还说别的？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian went to the door and took the invitation: "Did my father say anything else?"
[ZH] 李管家回道：“来人只说请帖之事，其他一概未提。”
[EN] Butler Li replied: "The messenger only mentioned the invitation. Nothing else was said."
[ZH] “好的，我知道了，你去忙吧。”乔文仙拿着帖子对仪王说，“你刚说帮我说服知府大人，他的帖子就到，真是天助我也，我预感到此事必成。”
[EN] "Very well, I understand. You may go attend to your duties." Qiao Wenxian held the invitation and said to Prince Yi: "You just said you'd help me persuade Prefect Zhu, and his invitation arrives right away. Heaven is truly helping me. I have a feeling this matter will surely succeed."
[ZH] 申简辰从文渊手中拿过请帖，打开浏览一遍：“临安的官员也喜欢搞宴会。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen took the invitation from Wenyuan's hands, opened it, and glanced through it: "The officials in Lin'an also love hosting banquets."
[ZH] “官家喜欢，大家必然争先效仿，知府大人每年举办一次，对临安有头有脸的人来讲，也算是大事。”
[EN] "Since the Emperor enjoys them, everyone naturally competes to follow suit. Prefect Zhu holds one every year. For the prominent people of Lin'an, it's quite a significant event."
[ZH] 申简辰心想：“真是上梁不正下梁歪！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought to himself: "The fish really does stink from the head down!"
[ZH] “林觉与朱淑真可去？”
[EN] "Will Lin Jue and Zhu Shuzhen be attending?"
[ZH] “淑真是幽栖居士，名闻江南，知府大人是她的父亲，宴会怎么能少得了她？至于林觉，既然还是知府大人的女婿，应该也会给个面子让他参加。”
[EN] “Shuzhen is Youqi Jushi, famous throughout Jiangnan. The Prefect is her father—how could the banquet be complete without her? As for Lin Jue, since he's still the Prefect's son-in-law, they'll likely give him face and let him attend.”
[ZH] “既如此，我不妨趁此宴会帮你解决难题，只是从此仪王的身份就得公开，一想到会有很多公务缠身，我浑身不自在。”
[EN] "In that case, I might as well use this banquet to help solve your problem. But from then on, my identity as Prince Yi would have to be revealed. Just thinking about all the official duties that would follow makes me uncomfortable all over."
[ZH] “公开是迟早的事，早些公开也好。先谢过仪王，我会永远记着你这份情。”乔文仙拱手致谢。
[EN] "Going public is only a matter of time. It might as well be sooner rather than later. Thank you, Prince Yi. I'll remember this favor forever." Qiao Wenxian cupped his hands in salute.
[ZH] 两人又聊一会儿，乔文仙才离开。
[EN] They chatted a while longer before Qiao Wenxian took his leave.
[ZH] 剩下申简辰独自一人，听着白风在外面练拳的声音，他更加觉得内心孤单，走到门口说：“不必练了，去休息吧。”
[EN] Left alone, Shen Jianchen listened to the sound of Bai Feng practicing boxing outside. He felt even more lonely inside. He walked to the doorway and said: "No need to practice anymore. Go rest."
[ZH] 白风问：“我能不能去城里逛逛？”
[EN] Bai Feng asked: "Can I go into town and look around?"
[ZH] 申简辰依然没有放弃寻找千里寻，人既已离开竹林，不知所踪，目前唯一能抓住的机会还是说话艺人那里：“可以，继续到西三胡同守着。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen still hadn't given up searching for Qianli Xun. Since she had left the bamboo forest and vanished without a trace, the only lead he could still hold onto was the storytelling artists: "Sure. Continue keeping watch at West Third Alley."
[ZH] “啊！还守着？那个叫千里寻的才子已经不知所踪，王爷为何还要找他？”
[EN] "Huh? Still watching? That talented scholar named Qianli Xun has already disappeared without a trace. Why does Your Highness still want to find him?"
[ZH] “不必多问，守着便是。”
[EN] "Don't ask so many questions. Just keep watch."
[ZH] “是。”白风摸摸腰包，“王爷，没银子了，能不能再给我些？”
[EN] "Yes." Bai Feng patted his money pouch. "Your Highness, I'm out of silver. Could you give me some more?"
[ZH] 申简辰掏出二两银子扔给白风，他迅速接住：“谢谢王爷！”一溜烟往出跑。
[EN] Shen Jianchen took out two taels of silver and tossed it to Bai Feng, who caught it quickly: "Thank you, Your Highness!" Then he dashed off like a streak.
[ZH] 在院门外遇到佳和郡主，打声招呼：“郡主来了，王爷在里面，我先失陪，忙着去逛街。”没等佳和郡主问话，“呲溜”跑掉。
[EN] Outside the courtyard gate, he ran into County Princess Jiahe and greeted her: “County Princess, you're here. Prince Yi is inside. Please excuse me—I'm in a hurry to go shopping.” Before County Princess Jiahe could say anything, he whooshed away.
[ZH] 喜兰怨道：“这白风，太不懂礼数，也不知王爷为何喜欢他？”
[EN] Xilan complained: "That Bai Feng is so rude. I don't know why Prince Yi likes him."
[ZH] 乔梦容批评道：“哥哥喜欢自有他的道理，什么时候轮到你瞎议论？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong scolded: "Brother has his reasons for liking him. When did it become your turn to gossip?"
[ZH] 喜兰很快承认错误：“郡主批评得是，奴婢以后不会再瞎议论。”
[EN] Xilan quickly admitted her mistake: "County Princess is right to scold me. This servant won't gossip anymore."
[ZH] 乔梦容进入院内，见哥哥站在门口，似乎想着什么，上前说道：“我刚过来，路上碰见乔公子，他看上去很开心，说你们明日要参加临安知府举办的宴会，不知哥哥可愿意带上我？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong entered the courtyard and saw her brother standing at the doorway, seemingly lost in thought. She stepped forward and said: "I just came over. On the way, I ran into Young Master Qiao. He looked very happy and said you're attending the banquet hosted by the Lin'an Prefect tomorrow. I wonder if Brother would be willing to take me along?"
[ZH] 文渊获得爱情的喜悦，搞得申简辰越发思念千里寻。哪怕她穿越成为男子也要找到他，找到后亲眼目睹男儿之身，他才会死心，否则，她在他心里永远是那个阳光靓丽的女子，他又怎能轻易放下，接受眼前温柔的妹妹？
[EN] Wenyuan's joy of finding love only made Shen Jianchen miss Qianli Xun even more. Even if she had traveled through time and become a man, he would still find him. Only after seeing his male form with his own eyes would he give up. Otherwise, she would forever remain that radiant, beautiful woman in his heart. How could he easily let go and accept the gentle sister before him?
[ZH] “既然想去，哥哥带上你，宴会中会见到你喜欢的女词人幽栖居士。”
[EN] “Since you want to go, Brother will take you. At the banquet, you'll meet the woman ci poet you admire—Youqi Jushi.”
[ZH] “太好了！哥哥参加宴会难道还要隐瞒身份？”
[EN] "That's wonderful! Will Brother still hide his identity at the banquet?"
[ZH] “先瞒着吧，该公开的时候自会公开。”
[EN] "Let's keep it hidden for now. When it's time to go public, it will happen naturally."
[ZH] “哥哥想怎样便怎样，我听哥哥的。”
[EN] "Whatever Brother wants is fine. I'll listen to Brother."
[ZH] 申简辰向院外走去，没有要带佳和郡主的意思，她急切地问：“哥哥要去哪里？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked toward the courtyard gate with no intention of bringing County Princess Jiahe along. She asked anxiously: "Where is Brother going?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回头：“我想出去走走。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn't look back: "I want to go for a walk."
[ZH] “我陪哥哥一起。”
[EN] "I'll accompany Brother."
[ZH] “那就跟上吧。”
[EN] "Then keep up."
[ZH] 得到允许，乔梦容跟在哥哥身后。他们走出幽林别居，在林间的小径中穿梭。
[EN] Having received permission, Qiao Mengrong followed behind her brother. They left Youlin Villa and wound through the forest paths.
[ZH] 乔梦容不知道哥哥要去哪里，又不敢多问，哥哥看上去心情沉重，怕问了他会不高兴。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong didn't know where her brother was going and didn't dare ask too many questions. He looked heavy-hearted, and she was afraid asking would upset him.
[ZH] 终于穿过密林，来到池塘边，竟然也有荷花绽放，景致还不错。
[EN] Finally passing through the dense forest, they arrived at a pond. Lotus flowers were blooming there too—the scenery was quite pleasant.
[ZH] 继续尾随哥哥绕着池塘边行走，一直走到对岸的柳树旁。
[EN] She continued following her brother around the pond until they reached the willow trees on the opposite bank.
[ZH] 乔梦容第一次见如此粗壮的柳树，一树的红绸迎风飘动，有对情侣正在树下抓住一根柳枝一起系着红绸。
[EN] It was Qiao Mengrong's first time seeing such a thick, sturdy willow tree. Red silk ribbons fluttered in the wind throughout its branches. A couple was under the tree, holding a willow branch together and tying a red silk ribbon onto it.
[ZH] 乔梦容上前问：“你们在做什么？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong stepped forward and asked: "What are you doing?"
[ZH] 女子以问作答：“你们也是来向神柳祈求二人的感情？”
[EN] The woman answered with a question: "Are you also here to pray to the Sacred Willow for your relationship?"
[ZH] 申简辰并未理会，一味盯着神柳发呆，在内心向神柳祈求，让他尽快见到千里寻。
[EN] Shen Jianchen paid no attention. He simply stared at the Sacred Willow in a daze, silently praying in his heart to the willow to let him see Qianli Xun as soon as possible.
[ZH] 乔梦容没有直接回答，继续问：“这样祈求有用吗？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong didn't answer directly and continued asking: "Does praying like this actually work?"
[ZH] 女子回道：“当然有用！不过须到山上的尼姑庵请一条红绸，写下两人的名字，再一起挂在神柳上，爱情才会长长久久，两个相爱的人也才能走到一起。”
[EN] The woman replied: "Of course it works! But you need to go to the nunnery on the mountain to get a red silk ribbon, write both your names on it, and then hang it on the Sacred Willow together. Only then will your love last forever, and only then will two people in love be able to be together."
[ZH] 乔梦容望望高山：“尼姑庵就在这座山上吗？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong looked up at the tall mountain: "Is the nunnery on this mountain?"
[ZH] 女子回道：“是，顺着那条路上去是尼姑庵的后门，只有拿到红绸后才允许从后门下山，你们可以绕着山下走，到山那边，会看到上山的路，顺着路往上走就能看到尼姑庵。”
[EN] The woman replied: "Yes. Follow that road up and you'll reach the nunnery's back gate. You're only allowed to come down from the back gate after getting the red silk ribbon. You can walk around the base of the mountain to the other side, where you'll see the path going up. Follow that path up and you'll see the nunnery."
[ZH] “多谢姑娘告知。”
[EN] "Thank you for telling us, miss."
[ZH] 乔梦容目睹这对情人将红绸挂好，互相牵着手幸福地离开。她很羡慕，什么时候哥哥也能牵起她的手？
[EN] Qiao Mengrong watched the couple finish tying the red silk ribbon and leave happily, holding hands. She felt envious. When would Brother take her hand like that?

#CH 54
[ZH] 申简辰走到神柳跟前，在一块石头上坐下，背靠着神柳粗壮的树干，闭上眼睛，用心与神柳沟通，希望它能了解自己的心意。
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked up to the Sacred Willow, sat down on a stone, and leaned his back against its thick trunk. He closed his eyes and reached out to the Sacred Willow with his heart, hoping it could understand his feelings.
[ZH] 乔梦容也找块石头坐下，学着哥哥背靠神柳，也闭上眼睛用心感受。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong also found a stone to sit on, imitating her brother by leaning against the Sacred Willow and closing her eyes to feel it with her heart.
[ZH] 喜兰瞧向二位，甚觉奇怪，不知王爷与郡主到底在做什么。
[EN] Xilan watched the two of them, finding it quite strange. She had no idea what the Prince and the County Princess were doing.
[ZH] 乔梦容感受了一会儿，只觉神柳的树干冰冰凉凉，也许哥哥嫌热，想在这里乘凉吧。
[EN] After feeling it for a while, Qiao Mengrong only noticed that the willow trunk was cold and cool. Maybe Brother felt hot and wanted to cool off here.
[ZH] 一股凉气袭来，她不由打了个喷嚏：“阿嚏！”
[EN] A gust of cool air swept over, and she couldn't help but sneeze: "Achoo!"
[ZH] 她睁开眼睛，挪开身体，瞧向哥哥，他依然紧靠神柳，闭着双眼。
[EN] She opened her eyes, shifted her body, and looked at her brother. He was still pressed against the Sacred Willow, eyes closed tight.
[ZH] 乔梦容想起那对情侣系红绸的情景，试着问：“哥哥，有时间能不能陪我去尼姑庵请一条红绸？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong remembered the couple tying the red silk ribbon and asked tentatively: "Brother, when you have time, could you go with me to the nunnery to get a red silk ribbon?"
[ZH] “好的。”申简辰依然紧闭双目。
[EN] "Sure." Shen Jianchen kept his eyes shut.
[ZH] “我要在上面写下哥哥和我的名字，到时哥哥要陪我一起把红绸系在神柳上。”
[EN] "I want to write both our names on it, and then Brother has to go with me to tie it on the Sacred Willow."
[ZH] 申简辰后悔刚才不假思索的应答，他睁开眼睛：“回去吧。”他站起身来，就要离开。
[EN] Shen Jianchen regretted answering so thoughtlessly. He opened his eyes: "Let's go back." He stood up, about to leave.
[ZH] 乔梦容也起身，看着哥哥的背影：“难道哥哥不愿与我一起系红绸？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong also stood up and looked at her brother's back: "Does Brother not want to tie the red silk ribbon with me?"
[ZH] 申简辰转过身，乔梦容双眼中闪着泪光，他不忍伤害。心想，不如替赵朴满足她的心愿：“我愿意。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around. Qiao Mengrong's eyes glistened with tears. He couldn't bear to hurt her. He thought, might as well fulfill her wish on Zhao Pu's behalf: "I'm willing."
[ZH] 乔梦容又露出笑容，走到哥哥身边：“哥哥，谢谢你答应我。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong smiled again and walked to her brother's side: "Brother, thank you for saying yes."
[ZH] “走吧，太阳快落山，凉气起来，哥哥担心你会着凉。”
[EN] "Let's go. The sun is about to set and the air is getting cool. Brother is worried you'll catch a chill."
[ZH] 乔梦容感到无比幸福，以前总是她关心哥哥，现在换成哥哥关心她，这种感觉实在美好。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong felt incredibly happy. Before, it was always her caring for Brother. Now it was him caring for her. The feeling was truly wonderful.
[ZH] 哥哥已转身往来的方向走去，乔梦容紧跟上，一直瞅着哥哥宽大的手掌。自从哥哥身体恢复正常，从来没有将她的手抓在那手掌之中。
[EN] Brother had already turned and walked back the way they came. Qiao Mengrong followed closely, staring at Brother's large palm. Ever since Brother recovered, he had never held her hand in that palm of his.
[ZH] 离开池塘，踏上林间小径，乔梦容快走几步，用她纤细的手指抓住哥哥宽阔的手掌。
[EN] Leaving the pond and stepping onto the forest path, Qiao Mengrong hurried a few steps forward and grabbed Brother's broad palm with her slender fingers.
[ZH] 申简辰立刻站住：“梦容，这是做什么？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stopped immediately: "Mengrong, what are you doing?"
[ZH] “我想和哥哥拉着手一起走。”
[EN] "I want to hold Brother's hand and walk together."
[ZH] “林间小径太过狭窄，拉着手实在不方便。”
[EN] "The forest path is too narrow. Holding hands would be really inconvenient."
[ZH] 乔梦容只得放开，试探地问：“如果这条路能容纳两人，哥哥可愿牵着我一起走？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong had to let go and asked tentatively: "If this path were wide enough for two, would Brother be willing to hold my hand and walk together?"
[ZH] “如果是赵朴他一定愿意。”
[EN] "If it were Zhao Pu, he'd certainly be willing."
[ZH] 听到这话，乔梦容不知是该伤心还是该高兴，强调道：“在我眼中，哥哥就是赵朴，赵朴就是哥哥。”
[EN] Hearing this, Qiao Mengrong didn't know whether to be sad or happy. She insisted: "In my eyes, Brother is Zhao Pu, and Zhao Pu is Brother."
[ZH] “可我什么都不记得……”
[EN] "But I don't remember anything..."
[ZH] 乔梦容听出来，哥哥是想告诉她，他已不记得他们之间的感情。这一点她一直都知道，听哥哥亲口说出，还是被伤到，一滴泪掉落下来。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong could tell—Brother was trying to tell her that he didn't remember the feelings between them. She had always known this, but hearing it from his own lips still hurt. A tear fell.
[ZH] “哥哥不记得没关系，只要我记得哥哥爱着我，我也爱着哥哥便好，我会等着哥哥明年中秋节娶我过门。”
[EN] "It's okay if Brother doesn't remember. As long as I remember that Brother loves me and I love Brother, that's enough. I'll wait for Brother to marry me next Mid-Autumn Festival."
[ZH] 乔梦容的表白以及提醒，申简辰感到不安，他不想辜负乔梦容，更不想违背自己的感情，还是尽快找到千里寻，再做决定。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's confession and reminder made Shen Jianchen uneasy. He didn't want to let Qiao Mengrong down, nor did he want to betray his own feelings. He needed to find Qianli Xun as soon as possible before making any decision.
[ZH] “别站在这里说话，走吧。”
[EN] "Don't stand here talking. Let's go."
[ZH] 申简辰向前走去，乔梦容继续跟上。她虽伤心，却自信哥哥只会属于她，毕竟有官家赐婚，新生的哥哥即使把她忘记，也不能违背官家金口玉言，她含着泪露出笑容。
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked forward and Qiao Mengrong followed. Though sad, she was confident that Brother would only belong to her. After all, there was the Emperor's arranged marriage. Even if the reborn Brother had forgotten her, he couldn't go against the Emperor's own words. She smiled through her tears.
[ZH] 喜兰观察二人，替郡主担忧不已，自从仪王起死回生后，像变了个人，对郡主没有以前亲密，仅像哥哥对待妹妹一般。
[EN] Xilan observed the two and worried deeply for the County Princess. Ever since Prince Yi was brought back to life, he'd been like a different person—no longer as close to the County Princess as before, treating her only like a brother treats a sister.
[ZH] 朱晞颜举办宴会，一来效仿官家；二来沟通官员之间的感情；三来，为江南才子提供一个展示自我的机会。说白了第三个原因还是效仿官家，毕竟官家喜文不喜武，极力推崇文道，作为朝廷封的官员，定要紧跟官家，方可平步青云。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan hosted the banquet for three reasons: first, to follow the Emperor's example; second, to strengthen bonds among officials; third, to provide a stage for Jiangnan's talented scholars. To be honest, the third reason was also about following the Emperor. After all, His Majesty valued letters over arms and championed the way of the scholar. As court-appointed officials, they had to follow the Emperor's lead if they wanted rapid promotion.
[ZH] 官家举办宴会一般要提前一个月准备，朱晞颜不敢与官家媲美，怎么说也得提前半个月准备。
[EN] The Emperor's banquets usually required a month of preparation. Zhu Xiyan didn't dare compare himself to the Emperor, but even so, he needed at least half a month to prepare.
[ZH] 朱府上上下下的人为了每年的宴会煞费苦心，不仅有人负责出点子，使得每年的宴会各具特色，动用四司六局更不在话下。
[EN] Everyone in the Zhu household worked hard for the annual banquet. There were people in charge of coming up with ideas to make each year's banquet unique. Deploying the Four Departments and Six Bureaus went without saying.
[ZH] 所谓“四司”即帐设司、厨司、茶酒司、台盘司；“六局”则是果子局、蜜煎局、菜蔬局、油烛局、香药局、排办局。还要提前让歌伎和舞伎编排好节目。
[EN] The "Four Departments" were the Office of Setup, the Office of Cuisine, the Office of Refreshments, and the Office of Tableware. The "Six Bureaus" were the Bureau of Fruit, the Bureau of Confections, the Bureau of Greens, the Bureau of Lighting, the Bureau of Incense, and the Bureau of Logistics. The female singers and dancers also had to rehearse their performances in advance.
[ZH] 宴会设在朱府的花园内，花园在建造时就是为举办宴会而设。分为两个大的区域，分别是男女宾客宴饮的区域，均竖着一个木牌，上面写着李白的一句诗。男宾区是：“金樽清酒斗十千”，女宾区是：“玉盘珍羞直万钱”。
[EN] The banquet was held in the Zhu Family Garden, which had been built specifically for hosting banquets. It was divided into two large areas—one for male guests and one for female guests. Each area had a wooden plaque displaying a line from Li Bai's poetry. The men's area read: “Clear wine in golden cups, ten thousand coins per vat.” The women's area read: “Rare delicacies on jade plates, worth a fortune.”
[ZH] 两个区域内摆着几个方形的长桌，按照参加宴会的人数，放好鼓凳。周围种植着低矮的花卉，既可赏心悦目，亦可不阻挡大家的视线。花园中还设置了一座不高不矮的木台，朱家的歌伎和舞伎要在此为宴饮之人表演节目。
[EN] Several square long tables were set up in both areas, with drum stools arranged according to the number of guests. Low flowers were planted around the tables—pleasing to the eye without blocking anyone's view. A wooden stage of moderate height was also set up in the garden, where the Zhu family's performers would entertain the guests.
[ZH] 千里寻回朱府并未与林觉同行，只带着两个贴身丫鬟与韦嬷嬷向朱府而去。
[EN] Qianli Xun did not travel with Lin Jue back to the Zhu residence. She brought only her two personal maidservants and Aunt Wei.
[ZH] 林觉甚是不满，又无可奈何，想到只要朱淑真在，他以后连个女人都带不进来，不如今日找机会向岳父大人表明和离之心，希望岳父大人不要因此对他有任何看法。一想到要放了朱淑真，多少还有些舍不得。
[EN] Lin Jue was quite displeased but helpless. He thought that as long as Zhu Shuzhen was around, he wouldn't even be able to bring another woman home. He might as well take the opportunity today to tell his father-in-law about the divorce, and hope the old man wouldn't think any less of him. But thinking about letting Zhu Shuzhen go, he felt somewhat reluctant.
[ZH] 到达朱府，千里寻直奔向母亲房中。
[EN] Arriving at the Zhu residence, Qianli Xun headed straight for her mother's quarters.
[ZH] 夏果第一次见这么大的宅子，心里既羡慕又嫉妒，更觉自己是红颜薄命。
[EN] It was Xiaguo's first time seeing such a grand estate. She felt both envious and jealous, and more than ever convinced that beauty was ill-fated.
[ZH] 宴会有专人负责，无需向夫人太过操持，再说已布置完毕，只等宾客们前来。
[EN] The banquet had dedicated staff managing it, so Madam Xiang didn't need to worry too much. Besides, everything was already set up—just waiting for the guests to arrive.
[ZH] 她坐在房内休息，也期待女儿归来。上次和离连老祖宗都请出来，结果还是没能成功，等女儿回来，她想多安慰几句。
[EN] She sat in her room resting, also looking forward to her daughter's return. Last time, they'd even brought out the Elder to help with the divorce, and it still failed. When her daughter came back, she wanted to offer more comfort.
[ZH] 正想着，外面传来银杏的声音：“夫人，小姐回来了。”
[EN] Just as she was thinking this, Yinxing's voice came from outside: "Madam, Young Lady is back."
[ZH] 向夫人等不及，起身出去迎接，见到女儿，双眼不由湿润：“淑真，娘见到你打心里高兴。”
[EN] Madam Xiang couldn't wait. She got up and went out to welcome her daughter. Seeing her, her eyes moistened: "Shuzhen, Mother is truly happy to see you."
[ZH] 千里寻故作轻松地说：“娘既见到女儿高兴，为何每次都红着眼睛跟我说话？”
[EN] Qianli Xun said lightly: "If Mother is so happy to see me, why do you always talk to me with red eyes?"
[ZH] 向夫人闪着泪光强调道：“娘是高兴。”
[EN] Madam Xiang insisted through tears: "Mother is happy."
[ZH] 见韦嬷嬷也回来，向夫人问：“嬷嬷回来，可是有要事给老爷反映？”
[EN] Seeing Aunt Wei had also returned, Madam Xiang asked: "Has Auntie come back with something important to tell the Master?"
[ZH] “夫人，咱们进屋说。”韦嬷嬷向跟着的下人吩咐，“在外面候着，没叫不准进屋，有话在外面回。苦荞，你跟着。”
[EN] "Madam, let's talk inside." Aunt Wei ordered the maidservants following them: "Wait outside. Don't enter the room unless called. If you have anything to report, do it outside. Kuqiao, you come in."
[ZH] 苦荞回声：“是。”
[EN] Kuqiao replied: "Yes."
[ZH] 夏果很不爽，跟着的下人只有她和苦荞，韦嬷嬷明显是针对她。哼！仗着主子器重，整日在下人面前作威作福，等哪一日她混成主子，定要杀杀韦嬷嬷的威风。
[EN] Xiaguo was quite annoyed. Of the maidservants following, only she and Kuqiao were here, and Aunt Wei was clearly targeting her. Humph! Relying on the Young Lady's favor, she threw her weight around in front of the servants every day. Just wait until she became the mistress—she'd take Aunt Wei down a peg.
[ZH] 进入屋内，待大家坐定后，向夫人寒暄道：“自淑真嫁到林家，嬷嬷是头一次回来，应该不是因为宴会之事吧？”
[EN] Once inside and everyone was seated, Madam Xiang made small talk: "Since Shuzhen married into the Lin family, this is Auntie's first time coming back. It shouldn't be about the banquet, should it?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“一是回来看望夫人，与您唠唠嗑；二是向您说说姑娘在林家的状况，夫人和老爷将姑娘交给我，我总得对她负责，要让夫人和老爷清楚姑娘在林家的真实处境；三是想见见老爷，跟他聊聊姑娘与姑爷和离之事。”
[EN] Aunt Wei said: “First, I've come back to visit Madam and have a chat. Second, I need to tell you about Young Lady's situation in the Lin family. Madam and the Master entrusted Young Lady to me—I'm responsible for her and need to make sure you both know what she's really going through. Third, I want to see the Master and talk to him about Young Lady's divorce.”
[ZH] 向夫人说：“让你费心，我与老爷把你放在淑真身边，就是想让你代替我们照顾她。”
[EN] Madam Xiang said: "Thank you for all your trouble. The Master and I placed you by Shuzhen's side specifically so you could look after her on our behalf."
[ZH] “唉！我没把姑娘照顾好，姑爷面对我倒老实，一旦我外出办事，他就对姑娘大打出手，还把商妓带回家里，这次我外出收租，他竟然把姑娘打得失忆，这日子没法过下去。”
[EN] "Sigh! I failed to take good care of Young Lady. The Young Master behaves himself when I'm around, but the moment I go out on business, he beats Young Lady. He even brought a courtesan home. This time while I was out collecting rent, he actually beat her so badly she lost her memory. This marriage can't go on."
[ZH] 向夫人又开始抹眼泪：“你说的这些我已知，上次淑真回来全都告诉我，我听后痛心不已，也支持她和离，只是老爷不同意，搞得朱家所有人不敢替淑真出面。”
[EN] Madam Xiang started wiping tears again: "I already know what you've described. Shuzhen told me everything last time she came home. I was heartbroken and supported her divorce, but the Master wouldn't agree. It's gotten to the point where no one in the Zhu family dares to speak up for Shuzhen."
[ZH] “所以，我打算跟老爷好好谈谈，夫人怕老爷，我可不怕，大不了老爷把我赶回老家。”
[EN] "That's why I plan to have a serious talk with the Master. Madam may be afraid of him, but I'm not. Worst case, he'll send me back to my hometown."
[ZH] 向夫人说：“有你这句话我就放心，等宴会结束后，我带你见老爷。”
[EN] Madam Xiang said: "Hearing you say that puts my mind at ease. After the banquet, I'll take you to see the Master."
[ZH] 千里寻没参加过这等宴会，问道：“娘，你可去宴会？”
[EN] Qianli Xun had never attended such a banquet and asked: "Mother, are you going to the banquet?"
[ZH] 向夫人一阵心疼，淑真可是宴会的常客，竟然问出这等无知的问题。她耐心解释：“娘必须去，要替你爹接待官宦家眷，你与文心也要跟着。”
[EN] Madam Xiang felt a pang of sadness. Shuzhen was a regular at banquets, yet she was asking such an ignorant question. She explained patiently: "Mother must go. I need to help your father host the officials' households. You and Wenxin need to come along too."
[ZH] 千里寻继续问：“嬷嬷可去？”
[EN] Qianli Xun continued: "Is Auntie going?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷回道：“这种宴会我没有资格参加，正好趁此机会歇歇，想想见到老爷时的说辞。不过宴会的美食少不了我的，是不是？夫人？呵呵……！”
[EN] Aunt Wei replied: "I'm not qualified to attend such banquets. I'll take this chance to rest and think over what to say to the Master. But I'm sure the banquet food won't skip me, right, Madam? Hehe..."
[ZH] “是，少不了你的。”向夫人终于露出笑容，对外喊道，“银杏！”
[EN] "Yes, you won't be missed." Madam Xiang finally smiled and called out: "Yinxing!"
[ZH] 银杏进来后，向夫人吩咐她去给负责宴会的人打声招呼，将酒菜备一份，送到这里。
[EN] Yinxing came in, and Madam Xiang told her to notify the banquet staff to prepare a portion of food and wine and deliver it here.
[ZH] 银杏答应着出去安排。
[EN] Yinxing agreed and went out to arrange it.
[ZH] 安排好后，赶回来回道：“夫人，已安排好。路上碰到老爷身边的人，说时辰差不多，让夫人尽快去花园。”
[EN] After arranging everything, she hurried back and reported: "Madam, it's all set. On the way, I ran into one of the Master's attendants. He said it's almost time and asked Madam to come to the garden as soon as possible."
[ZH] 向夫人起身，拉起女儿的手：“淑真，与娘一起过去。”
[EN] Madam Xiang stood up and took her daughter's hand: "Shuzhen, come with Mother."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷送至门口，吩咐苦荞跟着小姐贴身伺候，见夏果也要跟上，再次阻止：“夏果，你不用跟着姑娘，随我在此处待着。”
[EN] Aunt Wei saw them to the door and told Kuqiao to attend closely to Young Lady. Seeing Xiaguo about to follow, she stopped her again: "Xiaguo, you don't need to follow Young Lady. Stay here with me."
[ZH] 夏果很不情愿地应声：“是。”眼睁睁看着苦荞跟随小姐参加宴会，心中更对韦嬷嬷不满。
[EN] Xiaguo replied reluctantly: "Yes." She watched helplessly as Kuqiao followed Young Lady to the banquet, growing even more resentful toward Aunt Wei.
[ZH] 千里寻与向夫人往花园去的路上正好碰到陈文心，她已安顿好莱宝，赶来与婆婆一起接待女眷。
[EN] On the way to the garden, Qianli Xun and Madam Xiang happened to run into Chen Wenxin, who had settled Laibao and hurried over to help her mother-in-law host the female guests.
[ZH] 拜见婆婆后，与淑真打声招呼：“妹妹好。”
[EN] After greeting her mother-in-law, she turned to Shuzhen: "Sister-in-law, hello."
[ZH] 千里寻也行礼：“嫂嫂好。”
[EN] Qianli Xun returned the greeting: "Sister-in-law, hello."
[ZH] 三人一起朝花园走去。
[EN] The three of them walked toward the garden together.
[ZH] 向夫人边走边问：“陈家来了何人？”
[EN] Madam Xiang asked as they walked: "Who came from the Chen family?"
[ZH] 陈文心回道：“我娘病着，我爹不放心，他们二人没法前来参加宴会，派我弟弟浩宇前来。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin replied: "My mother is ill, and my father is worried about her, so the two of them couldn't attend. They sent my brother Haoyu instead."
[ZH] 向夫人说：“陈家最令我羡慕的不是钱财，而是你父母伉俪情深，像你爹那样的男子实在少有，你娘嫁给你爹不知令多少女人羡慕。”
[EN] Madam Xiang said: “What I envy most about the Chen family isn't their wealth—it's how deeply devoted your parents are to each other. Men like your father are truly rare. Your mother's marriage to him has made countless women envious.”
[ZH] 陈文心说：“婆婆过誉，我爹娘也经常吵架，没有外人传得那么好。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin said: "Mother-in-law flatters them. My parents argue often too. It's not as perfect as outsiders make it seem."
[ZH] 向夫人站住批评道：“哪有这么说自己爹娘的？”
[EN] Madam Xiang stopped and scolded: "How can you speak of your own parents like that?"
[ZH] “我说的是实情。”陈文心清楚，平日公爹对婆婆冷漠，如果过分夸赞自家父母之间的感情，婆婆听了心里会更羡慕，也因羡慕会更加难过。
[EN] “I'm telling the truth.” Chen Wenxin knew that her father-in-law was usually cold toward her mother-in-law. If she praised her own parents' relationship too much, Mother-in-law would feel even more envious—and even sadder because of it.
[ZH] 千里寻想不通，陈家父母感情深厚，嫂嫂陈文心又知书达礼，陈家怎么会有陈浩宇那样的人渣。
[EN] Qianli Xun couldn't figure it out. The Chen parents had a deep relationship, and Sister-in-law Chen Wenxin was educated and refined. How could the Chen family produce someone like that scumbag Chen Haoyu?
[ZH] “嫂嫂，陈浩宇为何跟你一点儿都不像？”
[EN] "Sister-in-law, why isn't Chen Haoyu anything like you?"
[ZH] “哪里不像？”
[EN] "How is he not like me?"
[ZH] “他的做派，绝对是纨绔子弟，我碰见他两次，每次都在欺负良家妇女，他在外口碑可不怎么好。”
[EN] “His behavior—he's a total playboy. I've run into him twice, and both times he was bullying respectable women. His reputation out there isn't great at all.”
[ZH] 向夫人批评道：“淑真，你怎么能这么说陈家公子！”
[EN] Madam Xiang scolded: "Shuzhen, how can you speak of the Young Master of the Chen family like that!"
[ZH] 陈文心倒不介意：“没事，我弟弟那样子谁见了都不喜欢。不过，他并不坏，只是被奶奶惯坏，以后你接触多了，自然会觉出他的好。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin didn't mind: “It's fine. No one likes my brother the way he is. But he's not bad—he was just spoiled by his grandmother. Once you get to know him better, you'll naturally see his good side.”
[ZH] 千里寻直接傻眼，心想，这么个纨绔子弟，竟然还有人说他并不坏！如果连陈浩宇这样的人都不坏，天底下哪还有什么坏人？
[EN] Qianli Xun was dumbfounded. She thought, here's this total playboy, and someone's telling me he's not bad! If someone like Chen Haoyu isn't bad, then who in this world is?
[ZH] 三人边走边聊，来到宴会处。到处花团锦簇，不时美味香飘，朱家丫鬟们忙碌着，个个穿梭在席间布置瓜果和美酒佳肴。
[EN] The three chatted as they walked and arrived at the banquet grounds. Flowers bloomed everywhere, and the air was filled with the scent of delicacies. The Zhu family's maidservants bustled about, weaving between the tables arranging fruit, fine wine, and exquisite dishes.

#CH 55
[ZH] 千里寻跟随母亲和嫂嫂走向迎宾处。
[EN] Qianli Xun followed her mother and sister-in-law toward the Reception Area.
[ZH] 迎宾处设在花园的长廊入口，长廊由几十根柱子撑着，既结实又宽敞，中间一个亭子，将长廊分为两部分。一部分摆上一排桌案，上面放置笔墨纸砚；另一部分正是迎宾处，铺有红色毯子，一直铺到亭中。亭子两侧也安排有数名丫鬟，恭敬地站立着，随时等待主人或宾客吩咐。
[EN] The Reception Area was set up at the entrance of the Long Corridor in the garden. The corridor, supported by dozens of pillars, was both sturdy and spacious. A pavilion in the middle divided it into two sections. One section had a row of tables with the Four Treasures of the Study—brush, ink, paper, and inkstone. The other section was the Reception Area itself, covered with a red carpet that extended all the way into the pavilion. Several maidservants stood respectfully on both sides of the pavilion, ready to serve the host or guests at any moment.
[ZH] 千里寻悄悄问陈文心：“嫂嫂，爹举办宴会用的都是朱家的银子吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun whispered to Chen Wenxin: "Sister-in-law, does Father use the Zhu family's silver for all these banquets?"
[ZH] “要用朱家的银子，朱家岂不是亏死？当然是府衙给拨的银两，只不过借朱府举办而已。”
[EN] “If the Zhu family had to pay for it, they'd be ruined! Of course it's the prefecture's funds—they just use the Zhu residence as the venue.”
[ZH] 陈文心讲完，已有人前来，三人赶紧笑脸相迎。
[EN] After Chen Wenxin finished explaining, guests began to arrive. The three of them quickly put on welcoming smiles.
[ZH] 首先到来的是苗知瑜与母亲秦夫人；接着是东方尘泽，身后跟着化雨；再接着，曹班跟随父亲曹经略使而来，安明轩则跟随父亲安提举和母亲前来。然后又有许多官员和家眷面带笑容走来。
[EN] First to arrive were Miao Zhiyu and her mother Madam Qin. Then came Dongfang Chenze, followed by Huayu. Next, Cao Ban arrived with his father Military Commissioner Cao, while An Mingxuan came with his father Intendant An and his mother. After that, many more officials and their families approached with smiles on their faces.
[ZH] 大家一一与向夫人、陈文心和幽栖居士行礼打招呼后，穿过长廊向宴饮之处而去，每个人都一派喜气。
[EN] Everyone greeted Madam Xiang, Young Madam Chen, and Youqi Jushi one by one, then passed through the corridor toward the banquet area, each person radiating joy.
[ZH] 千里寻又小声问嫂嫂：“父亲可请了乔家？”
[EN] Qianli Xun whispered to her sister-in-law again: "Did Father invite the Qiao family?"
[ZH] 陈文心悄悄回道：“当然要请，乔家可是皇亲国戚。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin whispered back: "Of course! The Qiao family is imperial relatives, after all."
[ZH] 千里寻心中偷乐，昨日与文渊见过一面就爱上他，没想到今日又能见到。即使不说话，只看对方一眼便心满意足，喜欢一个人的感觉竟然如此美好。
[EN] Qianli Xun secretly rejoiced. She had fallen in love with Wenyuan after meeting him just once yesterday, and she never expected to see him again today. Even without speaking, just one glance at him was enough to satisfy her. The feeling of liking someone was truly wonderful.
[ZH] 千里寻忽然觉得自己在另一个时代不谈恋爱实在是白白浪费了三十年的光阴。
[EN] Qianli Xun suddenly felt that not falling in love in her previous life had been a waste of thirty years.
[ZH] 正想着，心中所念之人向她走来。文渊在人群中如此出挑，让人不得不将目光聚焦在他的身上。
[EN] Just as she was thinking this, the person she was thinking of walked toward her. Wenyuan stood out so much in the crowd that people couldn't help but focus their attention on him.
[ZH] 千里寻既紧张又娇羞，她怨自己太没出息，有什么好害羞的？在另一个时代活了三十年，在这个时代连婚姻都有，怎么还跟个怀春少女一般？可依然忍不住红了脸。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt both nervous and shy. She scolded herself for being so useless—what was there to be embarrassed about? She had lived thirty years in another era, and in this era she was even married, so why was she still like a lovesick young girl? Yet she couldn't help blushing.
[ZH] 传来文渊温暖而充满磁性的声音：“向夫人、少夫人、幽栖居士，文渊这厢有礼。”
[EN] Wenyuan's warm, magnetic voice reached them: “Madam Xiang, Young Madam, Youqi Jushi—Wenyuan offers his greetings.”
[ZH] 千里寻跟着嫂嫂还礼，抬头的一刻正好与文渊四目相对，她的内心竟然怦怦乱跳。
[EN] Qianli Xun followed her sister-in-law in returning the greeting. The moment she looked up, her eyes met Wenyuan's, and her heart began to pound wildly.
[ZH] 向夫人问：“乔老爷和夫人怎么没来？”
[EN] Madam Xiang asked: "Why didn't Master Qiao and Madam Qiao come?"
[ZH] 乔文仙收回充满爱意的眼神：“今日家中有人上门提亲，家父与家母无法前来。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian withdrew his loving gaze: "Someone came to seek a bride from our family today, so my father and mother couldn't make it."
[ZH] 听到提亲，千里寻一阵担心，难道是向文渊提亲？又一想，不对，这个时代一般都是男方到女方家里提亲，估计文渊有什么妹妹或姐姐之类。
[EN] Hearing about the marriage proposal, Qianli Xun felt a wave of worry—was someone proposing to Wenyuan? Then she thought again: no, in this era, it was usually the man who went to the woman's family to propose. Wenyuan probably had a younger sister or older sister or something like that.
[ZH] 向夫人继续问：“可是你妹妹的亲事？”
[EN] Madam Xiang continued to ask: "Is it for your sister's marriage?"
[ZH] 文渊回道：“正是。”
[EN] Wenyuan replied: "Yes, exactly."
[ZH] 千里寻松口气。
[EN] Qianli Xun breathed a sigh of relief.
[ZH] 向夫人瞧向文渊身旁：“这位是？”
[EN] Madam Xiang looked at the person beside Wenyuan: "And who is this?"
[ZH] 现在还不是表明身份的最佳时机，乔文仙介绍道：“这位是我在汴梁认识的朋友申公子，这位是我的远亲乔姑娘。”
[EN] Now was not the best time to reveal his identity, so Qiao Wenxian introduced: "This is Young Master Shen, a friend I met in Bianliang. And this is Young Lady Qiao, my distant relative."
[ZH] 千里寻听到“申公子”的介绍并未有任何怀疑，她一心关注文渊，哪有闲暇思索他人？只认出申公子正是在竹林遇到的人。
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't suspect anything when she heard "Young Master Shen" introduced. She was so focused on Wenyuan that she had no time to think about others. She only recognized Young Master Shen as the person she had met in the bamboo forest.
[ZH] 申简辰走过来时注意到幽栖居士，心想，原来正是在竹林与他有一面之缘的姑娘。
[EN] When Shen Jianchen walked over, he noticed Youqi Jushi and thought to himself: so this is the young lady I met briefly in the bamboo forest.
[ZH] 向夫人笑道：“哦，是从汴梁来的朋友，里边请！”
[EN] Madam Xiang smiled: “Oh, a friend from Bianliang—please come in！”
[ZH] 乔文仙经过淑真身边，向她露出充满爱意的笑容。即使一个字未说，千里寻已感受他满满的爱意。她在心里默默祈祷，希望今日韦嬷嬷与父亲谈判能顺利促成和离。
[EN] As Qiao Wenxian passed by Shuzhen, he gave her a loving smile. Even without saying a word, Qianli Xun could feel his love overflowing. She silently prayed in her heart, hoping that today Weimomo's negotiation with her father would successfully bring about the mutual divorce.
[ZH] 申简辰经过时，也瞟了一眼朱淑真，见她娇羞的样子，觉得好笑，心想：“恋爱中的女子真是可笑，哪怕经历过一场婚姻，遇见爱情，也是一幅无脑的样子。”
