[EN] When Shen Jianchen passed by, he also glanced at Zhu Shuzhen. Seeing her shy expression, he found it amusing and thought: "Women in love are truly ridiculous. Even after going through one marriage, when they meet love, they still act brainless."
[ZH] 乔梦容第一次见幽栖居士，很开心，默默赞叹天下竟然真有才貌双全的女子。此时与幽栖居士搭话甚不方便，还是等宴饮之时再找机会与她聊两句，因而仅是礼貌性地从她身边走过。
[EN] This was Qiao Mengrong's first time meeting Youqi Jushi. She was very happy and silently marveled that there really was a woman in the world who was both talented and beautiful. It wasn't convenient to talk to Youqi Jushi at the moment, so she'd wait for the banquet to find a chance to chat with her. Thus, she merely passed by politely.
[ZH] 千里寻依然舍不得将目光从文渊身上挪开，哪怕盯着他的背影，都会有种心满意足的感觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun still couldn't bear to take her eyes off Wenyuan. Even just staring at his back gave her a sense of satisfaction.
[ZH] 正当沉浸在爱情的体验中，却听到最讨厌的声音：“伯母，姐姐。”
[EN] Just as she was immersed in the experience of love, she heard the most annoying voice: "Aunt, Sister."
[ZH] 千里寻扭过头，陈浩宇一副嬉皮笑脸的样子，身边一个人都没带。仔细想来，他平常身边不是一群听差遣的小弟，就是地痞流氓，这些人怎么可能进得了朱府？没带人倒十分正常。
[EN] Qianli Xun turned her head. Chen Haoyu was grinning from ear to ear, without a single person beside him. Thinking carefully, he was usually surrounded by either a group of lackeys or hooligans. How could such people enter the Zhu residence? It was perfectly normal that he came alone.
[ZH] 当陈浩宇礼貌性地唤了声“幽栖居士”，结果看到朱淑真露出拳头，想起她的第三个条件：“第三，以后见我要问好行礼，然后赶紧从我面前滚开。”
[EN] When Chen Haoyu politely called out "Youqi Jushi," he saw Zhu Shuzhen clench her fist and remembered her third condition: "Third, from now on, when you see me, greet me and then get lost quickly."
[ZH] 陈浩宇没等向夫人问话，急忙说：“我得赶紧过去，你们忙，你们忙。”说完迅速离开。
[EN] Before Madam Xiang could ask anything, Chen Haoyu hurriedly said: "I need to get going. You're busy, you're busy." Then he quickly left.
[ZH] 陈文心说：“这孩子，为何如此着急？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin said: "This child, why is he in such a hurry?"
[ZH] 向夫人很是理解：“估计是看到朋友，随他去吧。”
[EN] Madam Xiang was very understanding: "He probably saw a friend. Let him go."
[ZH] 千里寻心中训斥道：“这就是欺负老娘的下场，以后见到老娘能滚多快就滚多快！”
[EN] Qianli Xun scolded in her heart: "This is what happens when you mess with me! From now on, when you see me, roll away as fast as you can!"
[ZH] 林觉跟着族叔林子孝前来，林子孝先问好：“向夫人，少夫人，幽栖居士有劳了。”
[EN] Lin Jue came with his clan uncle Lin Zixiao. Lin Zixiao greeted first: “Madam Xiang, Young Madam, Youqi Jushi—much obliged.”
[ZH] 千里寻跟着嫂嫂还礼。
[EN] Qianli Xun followed her sister-in-law in returning the greeting.
[ZH] 向夫人笑道：“林家二叔来了，老爷等着跟你一起品鉴诗词。”
[EN] Madam Xiang smiled: "Second Uncle Lin is here. The master is waiting to appreciate poetry with you."
[ZH] 林子孝说：“承蒙知府大人厚爱。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao said: "I'm honored by the prefect's kindness."
[ZH] 林觉行礼道：“岳母，嫂嫂。”
[EN] Lin Jue bowed and said: "Mother-in-law, Sister-in-law."
[ZH] 向夫人因女儿之事，对这位姑爷着实不满，收起笑容：“姑爷既然来了，进去吧。”
[EN] Because of her daughter's situation, Madam Xiang was quite dissatisfied with this son-in-law. She put away her smile: "Since you're here, Young Master, go in."
[ZH] 林觉跟着林子孝向里走，经过朱淑真身边时，努力装道：“娘子走时也不给我说一声，我好跟你一起前来帮忙。”
[EN] Lin Jue followed Lin Zixiao inward. As he passed by Zhu Shuzhen, he tried to act concerned: "You didn't even tell me when you were leaving, wife. I could have come with you to help."
[ZH] 千里寻怼道：“林觉，装什么装？你我都这样，我不叫你才正常，再说朱家举行宴会，哪有你帮忙的份儿？”
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped back: “Lin Jue, stop pretending! We're like this now. It's normal that I didn't call you. Besides, this is the Zhu family's banquet—what business do you have helping？”
[ZH] 林觉气不过，想要当众说两句，被陈文心一句“姑爷慢走”给打住。
[EN] Lin Jue couldn't stand it and wanted to say something in public, but Chen Wenxin stopped him with a sentence: "Young Master, take care."
[ZH] 林子孝见情势不妙，拉起侄儿：“还不快跟我走！”
[EN] Seeing the situation was bad, Lin Zixiao pulled his nephew: "Hurry up and come with me!"
[ZH] 林觉只好悻悻地离开。
[EN] Lin Jue had no choice but to leave resentfully.
[ZH] 千里寻跟母亲和嫂嫂又接待了一会儿，才算结束，三人走到宴会之地，在女宾区专门为她们预留的位置坐下。
[EN] Qianli Xun continued receiving guests with her mother and sister-in-law for a while before it was finally over. The three of them walked to the banquet area and sat in the seats specially reserved for them in the female guest section.
[ZH] 所有人还未动筷，大家需等待知府大人讲完话方可享用美食，欣赏节目。
[EN] No one had picked up their chopsticks yet. Everyone had to wait for the prefect to finish his speech before they could enjoy the food and performances.
[ZH] 朱晞颜见大家已落坐，从男宾区起身，来到木台之上，环顾台下之人，冷冰冰的面孔上挂着少有的笑容。
[EN] Seeing everyone seated, Zhu Xiyan rose from the male guest section and went up to the wooden stage. He looked around at the people below, a rare smile on his usually cold face.
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“我爹真是奇怪，面对自己女儿连个笑脸都没有，对着大家倒是笑容可掬。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought: "My father is so strange. He can't even smile at his own daughter, but he's beaming at everyone else."
[ZH] 只听朱晞颜讲道：“诸位同僚及其家眷，还有临安的文人才子们，大家每年能享有盛宴，首先要感谢官家，自官家即位，历经建中靖国、崇宁、大观、政和、重和，现至宣和，官家励精图治，为大宋创下这等丰亨豫大之象，真是国泰民安，上下一片祥和……”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan spoke: "Esteemed colleagues, your families, and the literary talents of Lin'an. Every year we can enjoy this grand banquet. First, we must thank His Majesty. Since his ascension, through the eras of Jianzhong Jingguo (1101), Chongning (1102-1106), Daguan (1107-1110), Zhenghe (1111-1118), Chonghe (1118-1119), and now Xuanhe (1119-1125), His Majesty has governed with diligence, creating such prosperity and grandeur for the Great Song. The country is prosperous and the people are at peace, with harmony throughout..."
[ZH] 申简辰心中很不屑：“好好吹吧，北宋很快就没了，到时哭都来不及。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was disdainful in his heart: "Keep boasting. The Northern Song will be gone soon, and by then you won't have time to cry."
[ZH] 千里寻也想道：“现在是宣和四年，还有三年金军南下，汴梁陷落，我爹竟然意识不到，还在这里夸赞昏君，实在可笑。再看看周围这些人，都是一副无知的样子，还以为太平永随。要不告诉他们？……还是算了，说了也没人信，再说对这个时代的人来讲这是天机，常言道天机不可露，我还是少言语，不如仔细筹谋在即将动乱的年代如何过好自己的日子。”
[EN] Qianli Xun also thought: "It's now the fourth year of Xuanhe. In three years, the Jin army will march south, and Bianliang will fall. My father doesn't even realize it and is still here praising the incompetent emperor. It's ridiculous. Look at all these people around, all ignorant, thinking peace will last forever. Should I tell them? ...Forget it, no one would believe me anyway. Besides, for people in this era, this is a heavenly secret. As the saying goes, heavenly secrets must not be revealed. I'd better keep quiet and carefully plan how to live well in the coming turbulent times."
[ZH] 千里寻想完，向乔文仙看去，他也看过来，他们互相以微笑示意。
[EN] After thinking this, Qianli Xun looked at Qiao Wenxian. He was also looking at her. They smiled at each other.
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续讲道：“本官建议，先举起酒杯，面向汴梁，与官家同饮！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan continued: "I suggest we first raise our cups, face Bianliang, and drink with His Majesty!"
[ZH] 一个下人用盘子端着酒杯呈给知府大人，朱晞颜拿起酒杯，先面向汴梁方向。所有人端起酒杯，站起来，学着知府大人，将酒杯双手举到头顶，片刻之后，一饮而下。
[EN] A servant presented a cup of wine to the prefect on a tray. Zhu Xiyan picked up the cup and first faced the direction of Bianliang. Everyone picked up their cups and stood up, following the prefect's example. They held their cups with both hands above their heads, and after a moment, drank it all in one gulp.
[ZH] 千里寻与申简辰心知肚明，又不能不随大流，也装模作样端起酒杯。
[EN] Qianli Xun and Shen Jianchen knew exactly what was going on, but they couldn't not follow the crowd, so they also put on an act and picked up their cups.
[ZH] 千里寻在心中说：“敬这群无知的人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said in her heart: "Here's to this ignorant bunch."
[ZH] 申简辰则想道：“我还是敬神柳吧，希望它能帮我早日找到千里寻。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought: "I'd rather toast the Sacred Willow. I hope it can help me find Qianli Xun soon."
[ZH] 所有人饮完酒，坐回原位。
[EN] After everyone finished their wine, they sat back down.
[ZH] 朱晞颜把酒杯放在下人端的盘中：“其次，感谢各位同僚，本府任职临安，承蒙各位支持，临安的百姓才能有好日子过，百姓有好日子过，诸位也就有好日子过；最后，感谢各位文人才子，你们虽不参政，却以各自的才华装点临安，使得临安多次被官家夸赞，说临安人杰地灵，人才辈出。希望你们再接再厉，让临安的诗词书画更上一层楼！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan put his cup on the tray held by the servant: "Second, I thank all my colleagues. I serve as prefect in Lin'an, and with your support, the people of Lin'an can live well. When the people live well, you all live well. Finally, I thank all the literary talents. Although you don't participate in politics, you adorn Lin'an with your respective talents, making Lin'an a place praised by His Majesty many times, saying Lin'an is renowned for its talented people, with talents emerging in large numbers. I hope you will continue to strive and take Lin'an's poetry, calligraphy, and painting to the next level!"
[ZH] 男宾区的官员们都鼓起掌：“知府大人说得好！”
[EN] The officials in the male guest section all applauded: "Well said, Prefect!"
[ZH] 父亲每次讲话，朱景元都会认真聆听，每次听了都佩服不已，大家喊好，他也跟着喊，声音很大。
[EN] Every time his father spoke, Zhu Jingyuan would listen attentively. Each time, he was filled with admiration. When everyone cheered, he joined in, his voice very loud.
[ZH] 女宾区的家眷们也都鼓起掌来。
[EN] The family members in the female guest section also applauded.
[ZH] 掌声结束后，朱晞颜发话：“宴会开始！”
[EN] After the applause ended, Zhu Xiyan announced: "The banquet begins!"
[ZH] 大家才正式享用美味佳肴。
[EN] Only then did everyone formally enjoy the delicious food.
[ZH] 朱晞颜坐回位置，与各位官员觥筹交错，不断示意朱景元向官员敬酒。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan sat back in his seat, exchanging toasts with the officials, constantly signaling Zhu Jingyuan to toast the officials.
[ZH] 台上的乐手演奏音乐，舞伎们上台跳起一曲《桃夭》，歌声从一旁传出：“桃之夭夭，灼灼其华。之子于归，宜其室家。……”
[EN] On the stage, musicians played music. The female dancers went on stage and performed "Peach Blossoms." A singing voice came from the side: "Peach blossoms so fair, glowing bright and clear. This lady goes to her new home, bringing harmony to her household..."
[ZH] 千里寻仔细瞧去，原来在木台的一侧坐着一位弹琵琶的女子，边弹边唱，琵琶声与其他乐器之音完美融合在一起，配上绝美的嗓音，令千里寻着迷……
[EN] Qianli Xun looked carefully. On one side of the wooden stage sat a woman playing the pipa, singing as she played. The sound of the pipa blended perfectly with the other instruments, accompanied by a beautiful voice, which fascinated Qianli Xun...
[ZH] 乔文仙顾不上欣赏歌舞，他的双眼一直没有离开淑真。男宾区和女宾区只隔着两排低矮的花卉，加之二人坐的位置较便利，只要稍微努力一下，可轻易瞧见对方。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian didn't have the mind to appreciate the singing and dancing. His eyes never left Shuzhen. The male and female guest sections were separated by only two rows of low flowers. Plus, their seats were conveniently located, so with just a little effort, they could easily see each other.
[ZH] 申简辰边吃菜边观察文渊，凑到耳边轻声说：“你的魂儿快被幽栖居士勾走。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen ate his dishes while observing Wenyuan. He leaned close to his ear and whispered: "Your soul is about to be stolen by Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 乔文仙小声说：“你不觉得她很美吗？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian whispered: "Don't you think she's beautiful?"
[ZH] “是不错，不过那个丫鬟也挺美。”
[EN] "She's not bad, but that maidservant is also quite beautiful."
[ZH] 不远处的丫鬟正是夏果。
[EN] The maidservant not far away was Xia Guo.
[ZH] 夏果趁韦嬷嬷不注意，从房中溜出来，顺着歌舞声寻找而来，见苦荞等贴身丫鬟和小厮们挤在树下专为下人设置的用餐之处，便过去在苦荞和南双之间挤出一个位置，望向男宾区寻找着乔文仙的身影。
[EN] Xia Guo took advantage of Weimomo's inattention and slipped out of the room. Following the sound of singing and dancing, she came to where the personal maidservants and young servants were squeezed under the tree in the area specially set up for servants. She squeezed in between Kuqiao and Nanshuang and looked toward the male guest section, searching for Qiao Wenxian's figure.
[ZH] 乔文仙顺着仪王示意的方向瞧去，一眼看到夏果：“她是淑真的贴身丫鬟，是不错，可在我眼里，谁都没法与淑真相比。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian looked in the direction the Prince indicated and saw Xia Guo at a glance: "She's Shuzhen's personal maidservant. She's not bad, but in my eyes, no one can compare to Shuzhen."
[ZH] 当乔文仙瞅到夏果时，夏果也一眼看到乔公子，见他看向自己，脸不由一红，低头装作吃菜的样子。
[EN] When Qiao Wenxian saw Xia Guo, Xia Guo also saw Young Master Qiao at a glance. Seeing him looking at her, she couldn't help but blush and lowered her head, pretending to eat.
[ZH] 苦荞奇怪道：“夏果，你脸红什么？”
[EN] Kuqiao asked strangely: "Xia Guo, why are you blushing?"
[ZH] 夏果掩饰道：“没……没什么。”
[EN] Xia Guo covered up: "No... nothing."
[ZH] 苦荞又说：“既然来了，与我在这里边吃边注意小姐，一旦小姐招手，定是有事，你我要随时过去。”
[EN] Kuqiao said again: "Since we're here, eat with me and keep an eye on the young lady. Once she waves, it must be for something. We need to be ready to go over at any time."
[ZH] “小姐要是招手，我过去就行，苦荞姐姐只管放心享用美食。”夏果很乐意被小姐招呼，那样她就有理由光明正大站起来走到女宾区，让乔公子更加清楚地看到她。
[EN] "If the young lady waves, I'll go over. Sister Kuqiao, just enjoy the food in peace." Xia Guo was very happy to be called by the young lady. That way, she'd have a legitimate reason to stand up and walk to the female guest section, letting Young Master Qiao see her more clearly.
[ZH] 乔文仙的话更让申简辰意识到文渊对朱淑真的感情深厚，他想起千里寻。申简辰对千里寻的感情也是如此：“女子外表美又有何用？关键要有一个有趣的灵魂？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian's words made Shen Jianchen realize even more how deep Wenyuan's feelings were for Zhu Shuzhen. He thought of Qianli Xun. His feelings for Qianli Xun were the same: "What's the use of a woman's outward beauty? The key is to have a fascinating spirit?"
[ZH] 乔文仙不解：“有趣的灵魂？什么意思？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was puzzled: "An interesting soul? What does that mean?"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我曾喜欢一位姑娘，向她表白，她却对我说'除非猪能上树，老娘才会嫁给你！'你不觉得很有趣吗？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said: "I once liked a young lady. I confessed to her, but she said to me, 'I'll marry you when pigs learn to climb trees!' Don't you find that interesting?"
[ZH] 乔文仙诧异：“有趣吗？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was surprised: "Interesting?"
[ZH] 申简辰肯定道：“当然。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen affirmed: "Of course."
[ZH] “你的爱好真特别。”乔文仙进一步靠向仪王耳边，“难道佳和郡主有一个有趣的灵魂？”
[EN] "Your taste is truly special." Qiao Wenxian leaned closer to the Prince's ear. "Does County Princess Jiahe have an interesting soul?"
[ZH] 申简辰端起一杯酒饮下，叹道：“只可惜世间美女无数，有趣的灵魂并不多。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen picked up a cup of wine and drank it, then sighed: "It's just a pity that while there are countless beautiful women in the world, there aren't many interesting souls."

#CH 56
[ZH] 申简辰放下酒杯看向朱淑真，她又向文渊投来充满暧昧的目光。
[EN] Shen Jianchen put down his wine cup and looked at Zhu Shuzhen. She was casting ambiguous glances at Wenyuan again.
[ZH] “文渊，你们两个眉来眼去，我看我还是躲开一会儿。”
[EN] "Wenyuan, you two are exchanging flirtatious glances. I think I'd better step away for a while."
[ZH] 申简辰起身要走，被乔文仙拉住：“你瞧，也有人看你。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen got up to leave, but Qiao Wenxian held him back: "Look, someone is looking at you too."
[ZH] 申简辰瞧去，乔梦容向他举起酒杯。
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked over. Qiao Mengrong was raising her wine cup toward him.
[ZH] 申简辰露出笑意，既满意又失落，拿起酒壶将酒杯斟满，端起来，隔空向乔梦容做出碰杯的动作，然后喝下。
[EN] Shen Jianchen smiled, a mix of satisfaction and disappointment, picked up the wine pot to fill his cup, raised it, made a toasting gesture toward Qiao Mengrong from afar, then drank it down.
[ZH] 得到哥哥的回应，乔梦容也把酒喝完，放下酒杯时，发现哥哥已起身离开宴饮之处，她猜哥哥应该是去方便。
[EN] Getting her brother's response, Qiao Mengrong also finished her wine. When she put down her cup, she noticed her brother had already gotten up and left the banquet area. She guessed he had probably gone to relieve himself.
[ZH] 朱景元端着酒杯过去与乔文仙对饮，没有哥哥在，乔梦容懒得观察。想起幽栖居士，她倒好少许酒，端起酒杯，来到幽栖居士身边。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan took his wine cup over to drink with Qiao Wenxian. With her brother gone, Qiao Mengrong didn't bother observing. Remembering Youqi Jushi, she poured a little wine, picked up her cup, and went to sit beside Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 千里寻身边本坐着嫂嫂，陈文心见到好友，过去打招呼聊天儿，正好空出位置，乔梦容趁机坐下：“幽栖居士为何一直看乔公子？”
[EN] Qianli Xun's sister-in-law had been sitting beside her. When Chen Wenxin saw a friend, she went over to greet her and chat, leaving the seat empty. Qiao Mengrong took the opportunity to sit down: "Why does Youqi Jushi keep looking at Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 被人发现，千里寻很不好意思，辩解道：“别误会，我在看哥哥给人敬酒，他正好与乔公子对饮。”
[EN] Being discovered, Qianli Xun felt embarrassed and defended herself: "Don't misunderstand. I was watching my brother toast guests. He happened to be drinking with Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] “幽栖居士不用解释，乔公子对你的想法我尽知。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi doesn't need to explain. I know everything about Young Master Qiao's feelings for you."
[ZH] 千里寻面向乔梦容：“乔姑娘是文渊的远亲，经常来临安吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned to Qiao Mengrong: "Young Lady Qiao is Wenyuan's distant relative. Do you come to Lin'an often?"
[ZH] “不，我住在汴梁，第一次来临安。我在汴梁时，常听人提起幽栖居士的大名，也读过你的诗词，甚是倾佩，今日得以相见，实乃荣幸，我敬你一杯。”
[EN] "No, I live in Bianliang. This is my first time in Lin'an. When I was in Bianliang, I often heard people mention Youqi Jushi's name. I've also read your poems and ci, and I admire them greatly. To meet you today is truly an honor. I toast you."
[ZH] 千里寻端起酒杯：“能认识乔姑娘也是我的荣幸。”
[EN] Qianli Xun raised her cup: "It's also my honor to meet Young Lady Qiao."
[ZH] 二人一起将酒饮下。
[EN] The two drank their wine together.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“你与申公子都来自汴梁，该不会早就认识？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked: "You and Young Master Shen are both from Bianliang. You wouldn't happen to know each other already, would you?"
[ZH] “他是我哥哥。”
[EN] "He's my brother."
[ZH] “原来是兄妹，为何一个姓申？一个姓乔？”
[EN] "So you're brother and sister. Why does one have the surname Shen and the other Qiao?"
[ZH] “我们不是亲兄妹，而且我与他有婚约。”
[EN] "We're not biological siblings, and I have a marriage engagement with him."
[ZH] 刚认识乔姑娘，千里寻不好追问其中的弯弯绕绕，想起在竹林遇到申公子之事：“你哥哥为何要找一位叫千里寻的才子？”
[EN] Having just met Young Lady Qiao, Qianli Xun didn't feel it appropriate to press for the complicated details. Instead, she remembered meeting Young Master Shen in the bamboo forest: "Why is your brother looking for a talented scholar named Qianli Xun?"
[ZH] “没想到幽栖居士也知此事？”
[EN] "I didn't expect Youqi Jushi to know about this?"
[ZH] “哦，我在乡下竹林住过一段时日，你哥哥带人找到竹林，我与他偶遇，有过一面之缘。”
[EN] "Oh, I lived in a bamboo forest in the countryside for a while. Your brother led people to find the bamboo forest. I happened to meet him there. We had a brief encounter."
[ZH] “原来如此，其实也没什么，我听白风说，哥哥听了一回说书，觉得这位叫千里寻的才子故事写得甚好，想结识而已。”
[EN] "I see. Actually, it's nothing much. I heard from Bai Feng that my brother attended a storytelling session and thought the stories written by this talented scholar named Qianli Xun were excellent. He just wants to make his acquaintance."
[ZH] 如此看来与苦荞的猜测完全一致，不如告诉申公子自己就是千里寻？很快，她否认了这一想法，说了也没人相信，反而会让事情变得更加麻烦。
[EN] It seemed completely consistent with Kuqiao's guess. Why not tell Young Master Shen that she was Qianli Xun? She quickly rejected the idea. Even if she told him, no one would believe her, and it would only make things more complicated.
[ZH] “幽栖居士在想什么？”
[EN] "What is Youqi Jushi thinking about?"
[ZH] “没什么。”
[EN] "Nothing."
[ZH] “我与哥哥刚到临安，有幸参加东方公子组织的诗会，品鉴的正是《断肠谜》，大家都无不赞叹幽栖居士心思巧妙，词技高超。”
[EN] "My brother and I just arrived in Lin'an. We were fortunate to attend Young Master Dongfang's poetry gathering. The piece we appreciated was exactly 'Heartbreak Riddle.' Everyone praised Youqi Jushi's clever mind and superb ci skills."
[ZH] 面对他人夸赞，千里寻不知该如何回应，况且这词并非她所写，更加不知该说些什么。
[EN] Faced with others' praise, Qianli Xun didn't know how to respond. Besides, the ci wasn't written by her, so she didn't know what to say even more.
[ZH] 苗知瑜与母亲秦夫人坐在一处，秦夫人跑去与向夫人等人聊天儿，留下她一人，边吃边观察乔公子，自从乔公子入席，目光就没离开过幽栖居士。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu sat with her mother Madam Qin. Madam Qin ran off to chat with Madam Xiang and others, leaving her alone. She ate while observing Young Master Qiao. Ever since Young Master Qiao took his seat, his gaze hadn't left Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 乔文仙对幽栖居士的心思，苗知瑜向来知道。幽栖居士心有徐公子，苗知瑜觉得她与乔文仙兴许还有机会。徐公子举家搬迁，听说也娶了妻，苗知瑜开始紧张，直到幽栖居士嫁给林觉，她才感到轻松。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu had always known about Qiao Wenxian's feelings for Youqi Jushi. Since Youqi Jushi had Young Master Xu in her heart, Miao Zhiyu felt she and Qiao Wenxian might still have a chance. When Young Master Xu moved his entire family and reportedly married, Miao Zhiyu grew nervous. It wasn't until Youqi Jushi married Lin Jue that she felt relieved.
[ZH] 尽管她时常为幽栖居士惋惜，认为不该嫁给胸无点墨之人，可只要她嫁人，乔公子便没有任何机会。现下幽栖居士打算和离，苗知瑜为她高兴的同时，又为自己担心不已。
[EN] Although she often felt it was a pity for Youqi Jushi, thinking she shouldn't have married someone without literary talent, as long as she was married, Young Master Qiao wouldn't have any chance. Now that Youqi Jushi was planning a mutual divorce, Miao Zhiyu was happy for her while also worried for herself.
[ZH] 宴会上，苗知瑜发现幽栖居士看乔公子的表情与往常很不一样，难道幽栖居士也喜欢上乔公子？
[EN] At the banquet, Miao Zhiyu noticed that the way Youqi Jushi looked at Young Master Qiao was very different from usual. Could it be that Youqi Jushi had also fallen for Young Master Qiao?
[ZH] 木台上，舞伎已换了新舞，苗知瑜无心欣赏，她注意到很多人将美食享用得差不多，三三两两聚在一起，有的站着，有的坐着，有的甚至结伴逛朱府，毕竟朱府景致甚好。
[EN] On the wooden stage, the female dancers had changed to a new dance. Miao Zhiyu had no mind to appreciate it. She noticed that many people had finished most of their food and gathered in groups of two or three. Some stood, some sat, and some even went together to tour the Zhu residence. After all, the scenery of the Zhu residence was quite beautiful.
[ZH] 苗知瑜吃得差不多，起身来到朱淑真跟前，打断她与乔梦容的对话：“幽栖居士，可否赏脸与我走走？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu had eaten her fill. She got up and went to Zhu Shuzhen, interrupting her conversation with Qiao Mengrong: "Youqi Jushi, would you do me the honor of taking a walk with me?"
[ZH] 乔梦容明显感受到苗知瑜想要单独邀约幽栖居士的意思，起身礼貌地说：“改日再聊，失陪。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong clearly sensed Miao Zhiyu's intention to invite Youqi Jushi alone. She got up and said politely: "Let's chat another day. Excuse me."
[ZH] 乔梦容起身，向苗知瑜微笑点头后离开。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong got up, smiled and nodded at Miao Zhiyu, then left.
[ZH] 千里寻站起来：“坐这么久，也乏了，不如陪你走走。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stood up: "Sitting for so long, I'm tired too. I might as well take a walk with you."
[ZH] 苗知瑜瞟向乔文仙，他依然向此处看来，而幽栖居士也向乔公子回看一眼，脸上露出甜蜜的笑容。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu glanced at Qiao Wenxian. He was still looking in this direction, and Youqi Jushi also glanced back at Young Master Qiao, a sweet smile appearing on her face.
[ZH] “幽栖居士看上去很开心，不知有何喜事？”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi looks very happy. I wonder what happy event there is?"
[ZH] 被这么一问，千里寻不好意思起来：“今日宴会大家都喜气洋洋，我当然开心，苗姑娘也应开心些。”
[EN] Being asked this, Qianli Xun felt embarrassed: "Today's banquet has everyone in high spirits. Of course I'm happy. Young Lady Miao should also be happier."
[ZH] 二人并肩走出女宾区，随便挑个方向，边走边看风景。
[EN] The two walked side by side out of the female guest area, picked a random direction, and walked while enjoying the scenery.
[ZH] 夏果见小姐离开女宾区，对苦荞说：“小姐离开了。”
[EN] Xia Guo saw her young lady leave the female guest area and said to Kuqiao: "The young lady has left."
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“小姐应该是吃得有些多，在朱府逛逛消消食，不用理会，你我正好可以安心享用美食。”
[EN] Kuqiao replied: "The young lady probably ate a bit too much and is touring the Zhu residence to digest. No need to pay attention. You and I can just enjoy the food in peace."
[ZH] 夏果落得轻松，更将心思放在乔公子身上，她扭头向乔公子看一眼。东方尘泽、曹班与安明轩已凑到他身边，他们相聊甚欢。三位公子虽衣着与长相不错，与乔公子比起来还是逊色不少，夏果更是看得入神。
[EN] Xia Guo felt relieved and focused her attention even more on Young Master Qiao. She turned her head to take a look at Young Master Qiao. Dongfang Chenze, Cao Ban, and An Mingxuan had already gathered around him, and they were chatting happily. Although the three young masters were well-dressed and good-looking, they were still inferior compared to Young Master Qiao. Xia Guo was even more entranced as she watched.
[ZH] 南双用筷子在夏果眼前晃晃：“喂，你老看我家公子干什么？”
[EN] Nan Shuang waved her chopsticks in front of Xia Guo's eyes: "Hey, why do you keep looking at my young master?"
[ZH] 夏果的脸立刻变红，转过头，夹口菜吃起来。
[EN] Xia Guo's face immediately turned red. She turned her head and picked up some vegetables to eat.
[ZH] 化雨笑道：“她的表现太正常，乔公子走到哪里都招姑娘们喜欢。”
[EN] Huayu laughed: "Her reaction is too normal. Wherever Young Master Qiao goes, he attracts the girls' admiration."
[ZH] 夏果见化雨的位置极佳，不用转身便能看到乔公子：“喂！你跟我换换位置。”
[EN] Xia Guo saw that Huayu had an excellent position where she could see Young Master Qiao without turning around: "Hey! Switch places with me."
[ZH] 化雨不乐意：“我才不跟你换。”
[EN] Huayu was unwilling: "I'm not switching with you."
[ZH] “你要是跟我换，我在我家小姐写废的手稿里给你留个好的。”
[EN] "If you switch with me, I'll save you a good one from the discarded manuscripts my young lady wrote."
[ZH] “这还差不多。”
[EN] "That's more like it."
[ZH] 化雨起身与夏果换了位置，夏果正好坐在南双对面。
[EN] Huayu got up and switched places with Xia Guo. Xia Guo happened to sit opposite Nan Shuang.
[ZH] 苦荞十分不解：“那边的菜都被化雨吃光，你坐那里岂不没得吃？把这盘菜给你端过去。”
[EN] Kuqiao was very puzzled: "The dishes over there have all been eaten by Huayu. If you sit there, won't you have nothing to eat? Let me bring this plate of food over to you."
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐留着吃吧，我不需要。”夏果拒绝完，又往乔文仙处瞧了一眼，向南双打听，“你家公子可定亲？”
[EN] "Young Lady Kuqiao, keep it for yourself. I don't need it." After refusing, Xia Guo glanced at Qiao Wenxian again and asked Nan Shuang: "Has your young master been betrothed?"
[ZH] “你问这干嘛？”
[EN] "Why are you asking?"
[ZH] “随便问问。”
[EN] "Just asking casually."
[ZH] “既然随便问问，我当然不能说。”
[EN] "Since you're just asking casually, of course I can't tell you."
[ZH] “不说算了。”
[EN] "If you won't tell me, forget it."
[ZH] 夏果继续朝乔文仙瞧去，面前的美食都没有乔公子有滋味。
[EN] Xia Guo continued to look at Qiao Wenxian. The delicious food in front of her was less interesting than Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 苗知瑜与幽栖居士在朱府边走边聊，终于将话题扯到乔文仙身上：“前日我听说乔家正在为乔公子选亲，以乔公子的人品和家世，乔家应该会给他选一个不错的姑娘。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu and Youqi Jushi walked and chatted in the Zhu residence. Finally, she brought the topic to Qiao Wenxian: "The other day, I heard that the Qiao family is selecting a bride for Young Master Qiao. With Young Master Qiao's character and family background, the Qiao family should choose a good young lady for him."
[ZH] 千里寻站住：“消息可确切？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped: "Is the news reliable?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜也站住，观察幽栖居士的表情：“千真万确，乔家已派出好几个媒婆搜集临安合适的姑娘，在我不知情的状况下，我父母也把我的画像给了媒婆。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu also stopped and observed Youqi Jushi's expression: "Absolutely true. The Qiao family has sent out several matchmakers to collect information on suitable young ladies in Lin'an. Without my knowledge, my parents also gave my picture to the matchmakers."
[ZH] 千里寻苦笑道：“真可笑，你跟文渊互相认识，还需要画像？”
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled bitterly: "How ridiculous. You and Wenyuan know each other. You still need a portrait?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜回道：“没办法，长辈们有长辈们的方式，幽栖居士可为乔公子感到高兴？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu replied: "There's no choice. The elders have their ways. Surely Youqi Jushi must be happy for Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 千里寻当然不高兴，她好不容易有个喜欢的人，还没正式开始恋爱，男方的家人就为他选亲，这不是给自己制造障碍又是什么？如若她没有婚姻，也可成为乔家选亲的对象，可现在……
[EN] Of course Qianli Xun wasn't happy. She finally had someone she liked, and before their romance had even officially started, the man's family was selecting a bride for him. Wasn't this just creating obstacles for herself? If she weren't married, she could also be a candidate for the Qiao family's bride selection. But now...
[ZH] 千里寻心情沉重，继续向前走。
[EN] Qianli Xun's heart was heavy as she continued walking forward.
[ZH] 苗知瑜紧跟上：“幽栖居士不高兴？难道你已喜欢上乔公子？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu hurried to keep up: "Youqi Jushi is unhappy? Could it be that you've already fallen for Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 千里寻想要承认，想起婚姻，又不敢承认，自己倒无所谓，只怕坏了文渊的名声，还需掩饰：“苗姑娘说笑，我与文渊互相视对方为知己，也仅在诗词方面。乔家为他选亲，我当然替他高兴，我只是替将来没选上的姑娘伤感，现在都能想象到，文渊成亲那日会有多少姑娘哭红眼睛，呵呵……”
[EN] Qianli Xun wanted to admit it, but remembering her marriage, she didn't dare. She didn't care about herself, but she was afraid of ruining Wenyuan's reputation. She still needed to cover up: "Young Lady Miao is joking. Wenyuan and I consider each other kindred spirits in poetry. The Qiao family is selecting a bride for him. Of course I'm happy for him. I'm just feeling sad for the young ladies who won't be chosen. I can imagine now how many young ladies will cry their eyes red on Wenyuan's wedding day. Hehe..."
[ZH] 苗知瑜有些许轻松，想起两人互看对方的眼神，她又充满担忧，继续试探：“可乔公子喜欢幽栖居士。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu felt somewhat relieved, but remembering the way the two looked at each other, she was filled with worry again and continued to probe: "But Young Master Qiao likes Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 千里寻努力保持冷静：“苗姑娘真会说笑……”
[EN] Qianli Xun tried to stay calm: "Young Lady Miao really knows how to joke..."
[ZH] “决不是说笑，大家都是东方公子诗会的常客，谁都能得看出乔公子的心思，再说乔公子并未向东方公子隐瞒此事。”
[EN] "I'm definitely not joking. Everyone who regularly attends Young Master Dongfang's poetry gatherings can see Young Master Qiao's feelings. Besides, Young Master Qiao hasn't hidden this from Young Master Dongfang."
[ZH] “我已嫁人，乔姑娘对我说这些是什么意思？”
[EN] "I'm already married. What do you mean by telling me these things, Young Lady Qiao?"
[ZH] “你正在与练兵官闹和离，和离后幽栖居士还可愿接受乔公子？”
[EN] "You're in the process of a mutual divorce with the Military Training Official. After the divorce, would Youqi Jushi be willing to accept Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 千里寻不知该如何回答苗知瑜的问题，她沉默着，仍旧向前走，希望有什么事能打断此话题。
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't know how to answer Miao Zhiyu's question. She remained silent and continued walking forward, hoping something would interrupt this topic.
[ZH] 苗知瑜见幽栖居士不言语，说出一段像是慨叹又像是提醒的话来：“我真替幽栖居士与乔公子惋惜，如若没有婚姻，你与乔公子在一起应该是段佳话，可你已婚，和离后想要嫁给乔公子，恐怕乔家不会同意……”
[EN] Seeing Youqi Jushi not speak, Miao Zhiyu said something that was like a sigh and also like a reminder: "I truly feel it's a pity for Youqi Jushi and Young Master Qiao. If there were no marriage, you and Young Master Qiao together would be a beautiful story. But you're married. After a divorce, if you want to marry Young Master Qiao, I'm afraid the Qiao family won't agree..."
[ZH] 苗知瑜话都说到这个份上，千里寻再不言语，岂不是任由她猜测和发问？
[EN] Miao Zhiyu had said this much. If Qianli Xun remained silent, wouldn't she be letting Miao Zhiyu guess and ask freely?
[ZH] 千里寻站住：“苗姑娘不必试探，我与文渊的关系只可能是两种。要么像朋友一样不谈男女之情，要么我与文渊相爱。如若是第一种，苗姑娘大可放心；如若是第二种，只要我想与他在一起，别人也阻挡不了，哪怕我和离过一次。目前我与文渊还是第一种关系，如若有些人在我耳边不断提醒文渊喜欢我，真担心我忍不住答应文渊，会不会出现第二种关系，很难说。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped: "Young Lady Miao doesn't need to probe. My relationship with Wenyuan can only be one of two kinds. Either we're like friends without romantic feelings, or Wenyuan and I are in love. If it's the first kind, Young Lady Miao can rest assured. If it's the second kind, as long as I want to be with him, no one can stop me, even if I've been divorced once. Currently, Wenyuan and I are still in the first kind of relationship. But if some people keep reminding me in my ear that Wenyuan likes me, I'm worried I won't be able to resist accepting Wenyuan. Whether the second kind of relationship will appear is hard to say."
[ZH] 苗知瑜赶快道歉：“幽栖居士不要误会，我与你和乔公子都是朋友，替你们担心，才说这么多……”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu quickly apologized: "Youqi Jushi, please don't misunderstand. I'm friends with both you and Young Master Qiao. I was worried about you, so I said so much..."
[ZH] 苗知瑜话未说完，传来陈浩宇的声音：“朱淑真，你我狭路相逢，是不是该说点儿什么？”
[EN] Before Miao Zhiyu could finish, Chen Haoyu's voice came: "Zhu Shuzhen, looks like we've run into each other. Shouldn't we say something?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇在宴会上与官员和文人才子没有共同语言，大家也懒得搭理他。他一个人无聊，随便吃几口，早早离开男宾区，到处逛逛，打算差不多时，跟姐姐陈文心打个招呼，好提前出朱府。没想到逛着逛着见朱淑真与苗知瑜迎面走来。他瞅瞅四周没有其他人，不如与朱淑真过过嘴瘾，能报多少仇就报多少，只要别激得她动拳头就行。
[EN] At the banquet, Chen Haoyu had nothing in common with the officials and literary talents. No one bothered to pay attention to him. He was bored alone, ate a few bites casually, and left the male guest area early. He wandered around, planning to say goodbye to his sister Chen Wenxin when it was about time so he could leave the Zhu residence early. He didn't expect to see Zhu Shuzhen and Miao Zhiyu walking toward him. He looked around and saw no one else. He might as well give Zhu Shuzhen a hard time with words and get some revenge, as long as he didn't provoke her into using her fists.
[ZH] 千里寻第一次感谢陈浩宇出现，终于不用听苗知瑜继续说下去：“你见了我不赶紧行礼滚开，竟然还敢跟我搭话，该不会是想找打？”
[EN] For the first time, Qianli Xun was grateful for Chen Haoyu's appearance. She finally didn't have to listen to Miao Zhiyu continue: "When you see me, shouldn't you hurry up and greet me then get lost? You actually dare to talk to me. Are you looking for a beating?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“我见这里没外人，想跟你说件事，你听了准高兴。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu smiled: "I see there's no one else here. I want to tell you something. You'll definitely be happy to hear it."
[ZH] 千里寻纳闷，陈浩宇葫芦里到底卖的是什么药？不如听听他说些什么，量他不敢造次：“说吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun was puzzled. What was Chen Haoyu up to? She might as well listen to what he had to say. She figured he wouldn't dare act recklessly: "Go ahead."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“你在竹林时，我到聚香楼吃饭，碰到林觉，不仅帮你收拾了他，还劝他尽快跟你和离……”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: "When you were in the bamboo forest, I went to Juxiang Lou to eat and ran into Lin Jue. Not only did I help you deal with him, I also persuaded him to divorce you as soon as possible..."
[ZH] 千里寻不大相信：“你有这么好？”
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't quite believe it: "Are you that good?"
[ZH] 陈浩继续说：“我这个人其实不错，只是你对我误解太深。你知道我还对林觉说了什么？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu continued: "I'm actually not a bad person. It's just that you misunderstand me too deeply. Do you know what else I said to Lin Jue?"
[ZH] “说了什么？”千里寻还是充满期待。
[EN] "What did you say?" Qianli Xun was still full of anticipation.
[ZH] 陈浩宇做好逃跑的准备，大声说：“我告诉林觉，你跟他和离是想嫁给本公子！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu prepared to run and shouted: "I told Lin Jue that you're divorcing him because you want to marry me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说完撒腿朝相反的方向跑去。
[EN] After saying this, Chen Haoyu took off running in the opposite direction.
[ZH] 千里寻被气坏，大喊道：“陈浩宇！你等着！老娘追上你！定将你大卸八块！”
[EN] Qianli Xun was furious and shouted: "Chen Haoyu! You wait! I'll catch you and chop you into pieces!"
[ZH] 她打算追上去，被苗知瑜一把拦住：“他看着挺怕你的，今日是宴会，不必跟他计较。”
[EN] She was about to chase after him, but Miao Zhiyu stopped her: "He looks quite afraid of you. Today is a banquet. No need to argue with him."
[ZH] 千里寻只好作罢。
[EN] Qianli Xun had no choice but to give up.
[ZH] 这一幕正好被申简辰注意到。他上完茅房，不想回宴饮之处，不想目睹文渊与幽栖居士眉目传情，更不想接受乔梦容时不时投来的眼神，决定到处走走。
[EN] This scene happened to be noticed by Shen Jianchen. After finishing in the latrine, he didn't want to go back to the banquet area. He didn't want to witness Wenyuan and Youqi Jushi exchanging flirtatious glances, and he didn't want to accept Qiao Mengrong's occasional glances either. He decided to walk around.
[ZH] 走着走着，瞧见朱淑真与苗知瑜，接着看到陈浩宇堵住她们。陈浩宇的作风他见识过，担心两位姑娘被欺负。靠近后，躲在一旁观察，一旦陈浩宇有所举动，方便保护。
[EN] As he walked, he saw Zhu Shuzhen and Miao Zhiyu, then saw Chen Haoyu block them. He had seen Chen Haoyu's behavior before and was worried the two young ladies would be bullied. After getting closer, he hid to the side to observe, ready to protect them if Chen Haoyu made any moves.
[ZH] 当听到朱淑真自称“老娘”，风格像极了千里寻，他不由偷偷将朱淑真瞧了一遍，自语道：“她会是千里寻吗？没想到这个时代也有如此有趣的灵魂。”
[EN] When he heard Zhu Shuzhen refer to herself as "this lady," the style was very similar to Qianli Xun. He couldn't help but secretly look Zhu Shuzhen over and said to himself: "Could she be Qianli Xun? I didn't expect this era to also have such a fascinating spirit."

#CH 57
[ZH] 申简辰认为自己的猜测很没道理，朱淑真与文渊眉来眼去他可是看在眼里，她见到喜欢的人娇羞的样子，根本不像会说出“除非猪能上树，老娘才会嫁给你”的千里寻。
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought his suspicion was ridiculous. He had seen how Zhu Shuzhen and Wenyuan exchanged flirtatious glances. The way she blushed shyly when she saw the man she liked was nothing like Qianli Xun, who would say "I'll marry you when pigs learn to climb trees."
[ZH] 申简辰叹口气，摇摇头，觉得自己实在可笑。心想，还是等白风的消息吧。白风因有任务在身，今日宴会没能带他来参加，回头到酒楼好好犒赏他一顿。
[EN] Shen Jianchen sighed, shook his head, and felt quite foolish. He thought it better to wait for Bai Feng's news. Bai Feng had a mission today, so he couldn't bring him to the banquet. He'd treat him to a good meal at the restaurant later as a reward.
[ZH] 申简辰选择一条偏僻的路向宴会处走去，朱府最大的优点是四通八达，哪条路都可以到达宴饮之处。
[EN] Shen Jianchen chose a secluded path toward the banquet area. The Zhu residence's greatest advantage was its connectivity—every path led to the banquet.
[ZH] 到达后，申简辰发现朱晞颜与各位官员依旧坐着欣赏歌舞表演。再扫视周围，有欣赏的，有聚在一处聊天的，也有像他一样转悠回来的。
[EN] Upon arrival, Shen Jianchen found Zhu Xiyan and the officials still seated, enjoying the song and dance performances. Looking around, some were watching, some were chatting in groups, and others were wandering back like him.
[ZH] 朱淑真与苗知喻也一起回来，各自坐回原位，她一坐下又将目光投向文渊。而陈浩宇跟朱家少夫人耳语几句后，迅速离开，看样子不会再回来。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen and Miao Zhiyu also returned together, each sitting back in their places. The moment she sat down, she cast her gaze toward Wenyuan again. Meanwhile, Chen Haoyu whispered a few words to the Young Madam of the Zhu family, then quickly left, looking like he wouldn't be coming back.
[ZH] 申简辰想起答应文渊帮朱淑真和离之事，他没有回男宾区，走到一位朱府的下人跟前，说了几句话，然后朝不远处的一棵紫薇花树走去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen remembered his promise to Wenyuan to help Zhu Shuzhen get a mutual divorce. Instead of returning to the male guest section, he walked over to a Zhu family servant, said a few words, then headed toward a crepe myrtle tree not far away.
[ZH] 朱府的下人找到林觉，与他耳语一番。
[EN] The Zhu family servant found Lin Jue and whispered to him.
[ZH] 林觉感到纳闷，起身朝下人指的方向瞧去，申简辰正站在花树下，被紫薇花树衬托得更加高大挺拔。
[EN] Lin Jue felt puzzled. He got up and looked in the direction the servant pointed. Shen Jianchen was standing under the flower tree, the crepe myrtle making him appear even taller and more upright.
[ZH] 林觉猜不出这位申公子为何找他，因怀疑他是皇子，便出了男宾区，向紫薇花树走去。到达跟前，恭敬地称呼一声：“申公子。”
[EN] Lin Jue couldn't guess why Young Master Shen was looking for him. Suspecting he was a prince, he left the male guest section and walked toward the crepe myrtle tree. Upon reaching him, he respectfully greeted: "Young Master Shen."
[ZH] 申简辰转过身：“你还是称呼我仪王吧。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around: "You might as well call me Prince Yi."
[ZH] 林觉吓得就要跪下，申简辰阻止道：“不必下跪，我不想在宴会上让大家知道我的身份，否则全围过来行礼，岂不麻烦？”
[EN] Lin Jue was so startled he was about to kneel. Shen Jianchen stopped him: “No need to kneel. I don't want everyone at the banquet to know my identity. Otherwise, they'd all gather around to pay respects—wouldn't that be troublesome？”
[ZH] 林觉继续站立，腰已弯下。他很是不解，早听人说仪王是个病秧子，眼前的人却十分健康，该不会是唬他？不管是真是假小心些不会有错。
[EN] Lin Jue remained standing, his waist already bent. He was quite confused. He had heard that Prince Yi was a sickly prince, yet the person before him looked very healthy. Could he be fooling him? Whether true or false, it wouldn't hurt to be careful.
[ZH] 申简辰说：“官家派我来临安担任节度使一职，本想先隐瞒一段时日，趁此机会到临安各处体察民情，现下却不得不提前暴露身份，以仪王的名头和节度使的身份要求练兵官答应一件事。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said: "His Majesty sent me to Lin'an to serve as Military Commissioner. I originally planned to keep my identity hidden for a while, taking the opportunity to observe the people's lives throughout Lin'an. But now I have to reveal my identity early, using the title of Prince Yi and the position of Military Commissioner to ask the Military Training Officer to agree to one thing."
[ZH] 听到这番话，林觉已确定他是皇子：“仪王……不，节度使……不，还是仪王，您请说，下官无不答应。”
[EN] Hearing this, Lin Jue was certain he was a prince: "Prince Yi... no, Military Commissioner... no, Prince Yi, please speak. Your subordinate will agree to anything."
[ZH] 劝说别人和离，申简辰总觉得难以启齿，自己毕竟是个王爷，怎么干起这种事来？已主动答应文渊，不好反悔，只好硬着头皮说：“我找你不为公事，是私事。那日在聚香楼相遇，你已知我与文渊的关系，也清楚他对幽栖居士的想法，既然你跟幽栖居士过不下去，不如放过她，也好让文渊与幽栖居士有情人终成眷属。”
[EN] Persuading someone to agree to a mutual divorce always felt awkward for Shen Jianchen. After all, he was a prince—how did he end up doing this? But having already promised Wenyuan, he couldn't back out. So he gritted his teeth and said: “I'm not here for official business, but personal matters. When we met at Juxiang Lou that day, you already knew my relationship with Wenyuan and understood his feelings for Youqi Jushi. Since you and Youqi Jushi can't get along, why not let her go? That way, Wenyuan and Youqi Jushi can finally be together as lovers should.”
[ZH] 原来仪王是为和离而来，听这话定是受乔文仙所托，更加证明乔文仙觊觎朱淑真不是一天两天。林觉心里虽气，毕竟面前的人是仪王，又是临安节度使，掌握着他的生杀大权，面子还是要给的。
[EN] So Prince Yi was here about the divorce. From his words, he must have been entrusted by Qiao Wenxian, which further proved that Qiao Wenxian had been coveting Zhu Shuzhen for quite some time. Though Lin Jue was angry inside, the person before him was Prince Yi, the Military Commissioner of Lin'an, who held power over his life and death. He still had to save face.
[ZH] “仪王不说，我也打算与朱淑真和离，今日宴会结束，就找岳父大人表明和离之心。不过，恐怕岳父大人不同意。”
[EN] "Even without Your Highness saying so, I was already planning to mutually divorce Zhu Shuzhen. After the banquet today, I'll speak to my father-in-law about it. But I'm afraid he won't agree."
[ZH] “无妨，我会亲自面见知府大人，劝说他多为女儿的前途和幸福考虑。”林觉如此顺利答应，申简辰反而觉得有愧，劝道，“和离后，找个适合自己的娘子，最好能与你有共同语言。”
[EN] "That's fine. I'll personally meet with the prefect and persuade him to consider his daughter's future and happiness more." Lin Jue agreed so easily that Shen Jianchen actually felt guilty. He advised: "After the divorce, find a wife who suits you, preferably one who shares common interests with you."
[ZH] “感谢仪王关怀。”
[EN] "Thank you for your concern, Your Highness."
[ZH] “去吧，宴会上不要对他人提起我的身份。”
[EN] "Go now. Don't mention my identity to anyone at the banquet."
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes, sir."
[ZH] 林觉拜别离开。
[EN] Lin Jue bowed and left.
[ZH] 皇家人已参与其中，对朱淑真放手在所难免，林觉心里再不舍，也必须付诸行动。朱淑真到乡下一个月，给他留出更多思考的时间，经过这段时日斟酌，不愿和离的态度已没有以前那么坚决。
[EN] With royalty now involved, letting go of Zhu Shuzhen was inevitable. No matter how reluctant Lin Jue felt, he had to take action. Zhu Shuzhen's month in the countryside had given him more time to think. After this period of consideration, his unwillingness to divorce was not as firm as before.
[ZH] 林觉叹口气，在原位坐下，向朱淑真的方向瞧去，她面带笑容，一边品尝丫鬟刚摆上的瓜果，一边看向男宾区。顺着她的视线，不是乔文仙又会是哪个？林觉在心里骂道：“不知羞耻！”
[EN] Lin Jue sighed, sat back in his place, and looked toward Zhu Shuzhen. She was smiling, tasting the melons and fruits the maid had just served, while looking toward the male guest section. Following her gaze—who else could it be but Qiao Wenxian? Lin Jue cursed in his heart: “Shameless！”
[ZH] 乔梦容一直观察哥哥，好不容易看到他的身影，又拐去紫薇花树下，与那人说完话，才回到男宾区。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong had been watching her brother all along. Finally catching sight of him, she saw him detour to the crepe myrtle tree, talk to someone, and only then return to the male guest section.
[ZH] 负责果蔬蜜饯之人，早已将时令的果子和蜜饯摆上。参加宴会的人都回来坐下，品尝起来。哥哥坐下后，只知与乔公子说话，不看她一眼，乔梦容很是失望。
[EN] Those in charge of fruits and candied sweets had already set out the seasonal fruits and preserves. Everyone at the banquet returned to their seats and began tasting them. After her brother sat down, he only talked with Young Master Qiao, not even glancing at her. Qiao Mengrong was quite disappointed.
[ZH] 木台上的歌舞已停止，朱晞颜走上前：“诸位，接下来请才子才女们尽情展示各自的才华，每人写下一首词。今年的主题是……”
[EN] The singing and dancing on the wooden stage had stopped. Zhu Xiyan stepped forward: "Everyone, next, let our talented young men and women showcase their literary skills. Each person will write a ci poem. This year's theme is..."
[ZH] 朱晞颜想了一会儿：“近日也没什么可入词的话题，我仔细想想还有半个月到七夕，不如以七夕为题，各写一首，从中选出最好的。我新招的歌伎和舞伎有临场编曲编舞的本事，由她们将最好的一首词现场表演给大家，如何？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan thought for a moment: "There's not much worth writing about these days. Let me think... in half a month it will be Qixi Festival. Why not make Qixi the theme? Each person writes one poem, and we'll select the best. My newly recruited female singers and dancers have the talent to compose music and choreography on the spot. They'll perform the best poem live for everyone. How does that sound?"
[ZH] 在场许多人无不高兴，表示赞同。
[EN] Many present were pleased and expressed their approval.
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续说：“笔墨已准备好，请诸位去长廊处，一展才华。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan continued: "Brush and ink are ready. Please proceed to the Long Corridor to showcase your talents."
[ZH] 想要展示才华之人都向长廊走去。
[EN] Those who wanted to showcase their talents headed toward the Long Corridor.
[ZH] 千里寻自然不能替朱淑真丢脸，也起身走向长廊。
[EN] Qianli Xun naturally couldn't let Zhu Shuzhen down, so she also got up and walked toward the Long Corridor.
[ZH] 苦荞准备上前伺候，被夏果拉住：“姐姐留着享用水果和蜜饯，我过去伺候小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao was about to go forward to attend, but Xiaguo held her back: "Sister, stay and enjoy the fruits and candied sweets. I'll go attend to Young Lady."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“不行，我留这里算什么？你我一同过去。”
[EN] Kuqiao said: "No, what would I be doing staying here? Let's go together."
[ZH] 其他丫鬟、小厮和书童也都跟过去，准备伺候各家的公子和小姐。
[EN] Other maids, young servants, and book boys also followed, ready to attend to the young masters and young ladies of each family.
[ZH] 苦荞与夏果来到小姐身边，走入长廊后，选好桌子，一个展纸，一个研磨。
[EN] Kuqiao and Xiaguo went to their young lady's side. After entering the Long Corridor, they chose a table—one spread the paper, the other ground the ink.
[ZH] 千里寻提起笔，心想：“朱淑真有一首《鹊桥仙·七夕》，正好借此机会展示一番。”只见她写下：
[EN] Qianli Xun picked up her brush, thinking: "Zhu Shuzhen has a poem 'Magpie Bridge Immortal · Qixi.' This is the perfect opportunity to showcase it." She wrote:
[ZH] 巧云妆晚，西风罢暑，小雨翻空月坠。牵牛织女几经秋，尚多少、离肠恨泪。
[ZH] 微凉入袂，幽欢生座，天上人间满意。何如暮暮与朝朝，更改却、年年岁岁。
[EN] *Light clouds adorn the evening, the west wind ends the heat,
[EN] Light rain falls from the sky as the moon sets.
[EN] The Cowherd and Weaver Girl have passed so many autumns—
[EN] How much sorrowful longing and tearful grief remains?
[EN] A cool breeze enters the sleeves, quiet joy fills the seats,
[EN] Heaven and earth, all is satisfied.
[EN] Why not be together morning and evening,
[EN] Instead of changing year after year?*
[ZH] 在小姐书写之时，夏果依然没有忘记用目光照顾乔公子，尽管只能看到背影，也足以令她着迷。
[EN] While her young lady was writing, Xiaguo still didn't forget to keep an eye on Young Master Qiao. Even though she could only see his back, it was enough to captivate her.
[ZH] 她想不通世上怎么会有像乔公子这等完美的男子，不仅有才华，家世也好，关键是人品不错。虽然夏果没有遇到能够说明乔公子人品之事，但她碰到过陈浩宇那样的人，两相对比，乔公子的人品肯定好。
[EN] She couldn't understand how there could be such a perfect man in the world as Young Master Qiao—not only talented and from a good family, but most importantly, of good character. Although Xiaguo hadn't encountered anything that specifically demonstrated Young Master Qiao's character, she had met someone like Chen Haoyu. Comparing the two, Young Master Qiao's character must be excellent.
[ZH] 乔公子刚好写完，放下笔，回过头朝小姐瞧来。夏果不敢再看下去，迅速收回眼神。
[EN] Young Master Qiao had just finished writing. He put down his brush and turned to look at their young lady. Xiaguo didn't dare keep watching and quickly withdrew her gaze.
[ZH] 小姐已写完，大部分字夏果认得，要感谢苦荞姐姐细心教授，可这些字组合在一起后她又不知所云。心想自己如何能配得上乔公子？不由失落起来。
[EN] Their young lady had finished writing. Xiaguo recognized most of the characters—thanks to Sister Kuqiao's careful teaching—but when combined, she couldn't understand the meaning. She thought about how she could never be worthy of Young Master Qiao and felt quite down.
[ZH] 乔文仙看一眼淑真的背影，心满意足地将写好的词递给一旁负责的丫鬟。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian glanced at Shuzhen's back, then contentedly handed his finished poem to the maid in charge nearby.
[ZH] 丫鬟接过，行完礼，送到知府大人等官员的面前。
[EN] The maid received it, bowed, and delivered it before the prefect and other officials.
[ZH] 所有人的词作被丫鬟们一一送到评判之处。
[EN] All the poems were delivered one by one by the maids to the judging area.
[ZH] 林子孝有幸被邀请其中，与诸位有身份之人负责评判优劣。
[EN] Lin Zixiao was honored to be invited among those of status to judge the merits of each poem.
[ZH] 年轻人中除林觉不懂诗词没有参加外，也只有申简辰还坐在席间。有皇子陪伴，林觉并不觉得有多丢人。
[EN] Among the young people, except for Lin Jue who didn't understand poetry and didn't participate, only Shen Jianchen remained seated. With a prince accompanying him, Lin Jue didn't feel too embarrassed.
[ZH] 才子才女们写完诗词纷纷回到原位。
[EN] The talented young men and women returned to their seats after finishing their poems.
[ZH] 千里寻刚坐下，陈文心便说：“这种场合，你的词总是位居榜首，我都不用猜，今日必定如此。不过，说句心里话，你可别生气。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had just sat down when Chen Wenxin said: “At occasions like this, your poems always rank first. I don't even need to guess—it will be the same today. But let me speak from the heart, and don't be angry.”
[ZH] “嫂嫂尽管说，我肯定不生气。”
[EN] "Sister-in-law, go ahead. I won't be angry."
[ZH] “你的诗词别人都说好，可我不喜欢，只因读了你的诗词，让人有太多感伤，也不知道你哪来那么多伤感？”
[EN] "Others say your poems are wonderful, but I don't like them. Reading them brings too much sorrow. I don't know where you get so much sadness from?"
[ZH] 千里寻很认同陈文心所说，朱淑真的诗词的确感伤有余境界不足，可惜她穿越而来，在这个时代能借着朱淑真的身体过千里寻的人生，却无法改变朱淑真的诗词，因为她的诗词已被历史和时间定格。
[EN] Qianli Xun quite agreed with Chen Wenxin. Zhu Shuzhen's poems were indeed full of sorrow but lacked depth. Unfortunately, having traveled through time, while she could live Qianli Xun's life in Zhu Shuzhen's body in this era, she couldn't change Zhu Shuzhen's poems—they were already fixed by history and time.
[ZH] “嫂嫂难道看不出？我是闺阁女子，面对的都是闺阁之事，遇到一段不幸的婚姻，又如何能写出高兴的诗词来？”
[EN] "Can't you see, Sister-in-law? I'm a sheltered young lady, dealing only with domestic matters. Having encountered an unhappy marriage, how could I write joyful poems?"
[ZH] 陈文心点头认可：“那倒也是。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin nodded in agreement: "That's true."
[ZH] 正如陈文心所说，朱淑真的词位居第一。当林子孝面对众人宣布幽栖居士的词最佳时，乔文仙笑道：“就知道是她！”
[EN] Just as Chen Wenxin had said, Zhu Shuzhen's poem ranked first. When Lin Zixiao announced to everyone that Youqi Jushi's poem was the best, Qiao Wenxian smiled: "I knew it would be her!"
[ZH] 申简辰很不满：“既然知道是她，直接让她一人写即可，为何还要这么多人陪衬？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was quite dissatisfied: "Since you knew it would be her, why not just have her write alone? Why need so many others as foils?"
[ZH] “我乐意陪衬。”
[EN] "I'm willing to be a foil."
[ZH] “你乐意别人不一定乐意。”
[EN] "You may be willing, but others might not."
[ZH] “别人我管不了，只要我乐意就行。”
[EN] "I can't control others. As long as I'm willing, that's enough."
[ZH] “真是没得救！”
[EN] "Truly hopeless!"
[ZH] “还有脸说我，你说的那个……有趣的灵魂，真是有趣得离谱！”
[EN] “You have the nerve to talk? That... fascinating spirit you mentioned—is really ridiculously fascinating！”
[ZH] 申简辰想起朱淑真自称“老娘”之事：“还别说，你看人的眼光不错，幽栖居士倒挺有趣。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen remembered how Zhu Shuzhen used "I" in such a fiery tone when scolding Chen Haoyu: "You know what? You have good taste in people. Youqi Jushi is quite an interesting character."
[ZH] 乔文仙担忧道：“什么意思？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian asked worriedly: "What do you mean?"
[ZH] “看你那样，好像我要跟你抢她似的，放心，我对她没兴趣。”申简辰解释道，“今日，我听她骂陈浩宇时自称'老娘'，便觉得她十分有趣。”
[EN] “Look at you, as if I'm going to steal her from you. Don't worry, I'm not interested in her.” Shen Jianchen explained: “Today, I heard her use 'I' in such a fiery tone when scolding Chen Haoyu—she's quite something.”
[ZH] “什么？她竟然口出粗言？她以前哪会如此？肯定是嫁给林觉后被逼成那样，不行，得赶快让她和离。”乔文仙抓住仪王的手，“你一定要帮我。”
[EN] "What? She actually used vulgar language? She would never have done that before. She must have been forced into it after marrying Lin Jue. No, we must hurry and get her divorced." Qiao Wenxian grabbed Prince Yi's hand: "You must help me."
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“我已帮了你一半儿，还剩一半儿。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen smiled: "I've already helped you halfway. Only half remains."
[ZH] 乔文仙高兴道：“什么意思？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian happily asked: "What do you mean?"
[ZH] 申简辰小声说：“我刚向林觉表明身份，提出要求，他已同意和离，再搞定知府大人，就万事大吉。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen whispered: "I just revealed my identity to Lin Jue and made the request. He's already agreed to the mutual divorce. Once we handle the prefect, everything will be settled."
[ZH] 乔文仙更是兴奋不已：“太好了！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was even more excited: "Excellent!"
[ZH] 他又向朱淑真瞧去，她正看向木台之上。乔文仙也向台上看去，歌伎迅速为幽栖居士的词谱好曲，拨弄琴弦展示着美妙的歌喉。一位舞伎依照旋律扭动腰肢，配合词的内容跳出惊艳的舞蹈。
[EN] He looked at Zhu Shuzhen again. She was looking at the wooden stage. Qiao Wenxian also looked toward the stage. The female singer had quickly composed music for Youqi Jushi's poem, plucking the strings and displaying her beautiful voice. A dancer swayed her waist to the melody, performing a stunning dance that matched the poem's content.
[ZH] 东方尘泽等人仔细品着词句。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze and others carefully savored the words.
[ZH] 曹班说：“幽栖居士的词依然写得凄美婉约。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "Youqi Jushi's poems remain beautifully sorrowful and graceful."
[ZH] 东方尘泽肯定道：“的确，符合她一贯的风格。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze affirmed: "Indeed, consistent with her usual style."
[ZH] 安明轩说：“自从在永泰茶楼见识她的厉害后，我一直在想，幽栖居士的诗词凄美婉约的风格与她那日的表现实在不符。”
[EN] An Mingxuan said: “Ever since witnessing her prowess at Yongtai Teahouse, I've been thinking—Youqi Jushi's sorrowful and graceful style really doesn't match her performance that day.”
[ZH] 曹班说：“我也疑惑过，后来想明白，是因为我们都太不了解幽栖居士。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "I was also puzzled, but later I realized it's because we all know too little about Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 东方尘泽点头认可：“有道理。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze nodded in agreement: "Makes sense."
[ZH] 琴弦声和歌声传入朱府佛堂。
[EN] The sound of strings and singing reached the Zhu family's Buddhist hall.
[ZH] 老祖宗正在拨弄佛珠，听到：“何如暮暮与朝朝，更改却、年年岁岁。”不由睁开双眼：“也不知自那日之后，朱家姑娘和离之事是否有些眉目？”
[EN] The Old Ancestor was fingering her prayer beads when she heard: "Why not be together morning and evening, instead of changing year after year." She couldn't help but open her eyes: "I wonder if there's been any progress on the Zhu family young lady's mutual divorce since that day?"
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷回道：“前日我出佛堂到朱府管家那里给老祖宗领月钱，正好遇到朱家公子，我多了句嘴，问起姑娘和离之事，他说还是老样子。”
[EN] Nanny Xiuzhen replied: "The day before yesterday, I left the Buddhist hall to collect the Old Ancestor's monthly allowance from the Zhu family steward. I happened to run into the Zhu family young master. I asked a bit about the young lady's divorce, and he said it's still the same as before."
[ZH] 老祖宗吩咐道：“拿纸笔来。”
[EN] The Old Ancestor ordered: "Bring paper and brush."
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷把纸笔摆好，老祖宗提笔写下一封信。
[EN] Nanny Xiuzhen set out the paper and brush. The Old Ancestor picked up the brush and wrote a letter.
[ZH] 写完后叮嘱道：“你把这封信交给朱家老爷。”
[EN] After finishing, she instructed: "Deliver this letter to the Zhu family master."
[ZH] 秀珍嬷嬷接过，走出佛堂，一路打听，顺着琴声和歌声找到宴饮之处，看到朱晞颜坐在席间欣赏歌舞，她来到男宾区，行礼道：“老爷，老祖宗给您写了封信，让我送来。”
[EN] Nanny Xiuzhen received it, walked out of the Buddhist hall, asked for directions along the way, followed the sound of strings and singing to the banquet area, and saw Zhu Xiyan seated enjoying the performance. She came to the male guest section, bowed, and said: "Master, the Old Ancestor wrote you a letter and asked me to deliver it."
[ZH] 朱晞颜听到是老祖宗的信，十分恭敬：“没想到老祖宗还记得我。”
[EN] Hearing it was a letter from the Old Ancestor, Zhu Xiyan was very respectful: "I didn't expect the Old Ancestor to still remember me."
[ZH] 他接过信后，秀珍嬷嬷又行礼离开。
[EN] After he received the letter, Nanny Xiuzhen bowed again and left.
[ZH] 朱晞颜打开信浏览一遍，面色变得凝重，向旁边的下人招手，吩咐他把公子叫来。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan opened the letter and read through it. His expression became grave. He beckoned to a servant nearby and instructed him to fetch the young master.
[ZH] 朱景元走来后，朱晞颜对儿子耳语几句，朱景元听后点头称“好”。
[EN] After Zhu Jingyuan came over, Zhu Xiyan whispered a few words to his son. Zhu Jingyuan nodded and said "good" after hearing them.
[ZH] 朱晞颜又对身边的官员说：“诸位继续欣赏歌舞，我身体偶感不适，回屋休息片刻。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan then said to the officials beside him: "Everyone, please continue enjoying the performance. I'm feeling a bit unwell and will return to my room to rest for a while."
[ZH] 官员们都说：“知府大人请便。”
[EN] The officials all said: "Please, Prefect."

#CH 58
[ZH] 女宾区内，向夫人第一个注意到朱晞颜离开，仔细观察老爷的去向，应该是回屋。本想宴会结束后，亲自将韦嬷嬷带到老爷面前，计划赶不上变化。
[EN] In the female guest section, Madam Xiang was the first to notice Zhu Xiyan leaving. She carefully observed her husband's direction—he was heading back to his room. She had planned to bring Nanny Wei to meet him after the banquet, but plans never go as expected.
[ZH] 宴会正在进行，老爷又离开，向夫人要在此处招呼，不便跟着去，她向大树下贴身下人聚集处招手。
[EN] The banquet was still going on, and her husband had left again. Madam Xiang needed to stay and entertain the guests, so she couldn't follow. She waved toward the group of personal servants gathered under the big tree.
[ZH] 银杏见夫人招手，立刻前来：“夫人有何吩咐？”
[EN] Yinxing saw Madam Xiang waving and immediately came over: "What are your orders, Madam?"
[ZH] 向夫人小声说：“你去告诉韦嬷嬷，老爷回屋了，正是好时机，让她此刻便去。”
[EN] Madam Xiang whispered: "Go tell Nanny Wei that the master has returned to his room. This is the perfect timing. Tell her to go now."
[ZH] 银杏回了声：“是。”
[EN] Yinxing replied: "Yes, Madam."
[ZH] 林觉也注意到岳父大人离开，他起身跟上去，想趁此机会找朱晞颜表明和离的态度。
[EN] Lin Jue also noticed his father-in-law leaving. He got up and followed, wanting to take this opportunity to express his attitude about the mutual divorce to Zhu Xiyan.
[ZH] 申简辰注意到，对文渊说：“机会来了，我去找知府大人替你寒暄几句，等我的好消息。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen noticed and said to Wenyuan: "The opportunity has come. I'll go find the prefect and put in a good word for you. Wait for my good news."
[ZH] 乔文仙当然乐意：“感激不尽！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was naturally delighted: "I'm extremely grateful!"
[ZH] 仪王离开座位后，乔文仙很想立刻把好消息告诉淑真，还是忍住，等有了结果她自然便知。
[EN] After Prince Yi left his seat, Qiao Wenxian really wanted to immediately tell Shuzhen the good news, but he held back. Once there was a result, she would naturally know.
[ZH] 千里寻接收到文渊的目光，心中又甜又美，如同眼前的蜜饯一样。她拿起一颗，微笑着。
[EN] Qianli Xun received Wenyuan's gaze. Her heart felt sweet and beautiful, just like the candied fruit before her. She picked one up and smiled.
[ZH] 乔文仙领会，也拿起一颗蜜饯。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian understood and also picked up a candied fruit.
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“文渊真是与我心有灵犀一点通，我希望与他的感情如同蜜饯一样甜甜蜜蜜，他也回应我甜甜蜜蜜，不如吃下，看他如何。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought: "Wenyuan and I are truly two hearts beating as one. I hope our feelings will be as sweet as this candied fruit, and he's responding with sweetness too. Let me eat it and see what he does."
[ZH] 千里寻将蜜饯放入口中，乔文仙也放入，两人嚼着蜜饯相视一笑，即刻明了对方的心意。
[EN] Qianli Xun put the candied fruit in her mouth. Qiao Wenxian did the same. They chewed the candied fruit, looked at each other, and smiled, instantly understanding each other's feelings.
[ZH] 千里寻第一次感受到与一位异性如此合拍的妙处，她为自己在另一个时代不想涉足爱情找出合理的解释：肯定是因为她的爱人在这个时代等着她，穿越而来，为的就是到一千年前与爱的人相遇相守。
[EN] For the first time, Qianli Xun felt the wonder of being so in sync with someone of the opposite sex. She found a reasonable explanation for why she didn't want to get involved in love in her previous era: it must be because her lover was waiting for her in this era. She traveled through time precisely to meet and be with the one she loved a thousand years ago.
[ZH] 此情此景被苗知瑜看在眼中，更加确定二人的关系已是幽栖居士讲的“第二种”，她既羡慕又为自己的爱得不到回应而感伤。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu saw this scene and became even more certain that the relationship between the two had reached the "second type" that Youqi Jushi had mentioned. She felt both envious and sad that her own love received no response.
[ZH] 苗知瑜的母亲秦夫人见女儿不高兴，问道：“你怎么了？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu's mother, Madam Qin, saw her daughter was unhappy and asked: "What's wrong?"
[ZH] “没什么。”苗知瑜回答完，转而问秦夫人，“母亲，乔家为乔公子选亲之事进展得如何？”
[EN] "Nothing." After answering, Miao Zhiyu turned to ask Madam Qin: "Mother, how is the progress of the Qiao family's bride selection for Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 秦夫人说：“听媒婆说，乔家仔细着呢，毕竟乔家只有乔公子一棵独苗，不可能这么快出结果。我还听媒婆说，你的条件在众多姑娘中是数一数二的。”
[EN] Madam Qin said: "According to the matchmaker, the Qiao family is being very careful. After all, Young Master Qiao is their only heir, so they can't produce results so quickly. I also heard from the matchmaker that your qualifications are among the best of all the young ladies."
[ZH] 苗知瑜苦笑道：“我看乔家还是不要再选，乔公子已有喜欢之人。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu smiled bitterly: "I think the Qiao family should stop searching. Young Master Qiao already has someone he likes."
[ZH] 秦夫人说：“你们这些年轻人懂什么，乔公子喜欢不重要，重要的是乔家的长辈能看上。”
[EN] Madam Qin said: "What do you young people know? It doesn't matter what Young Master Qiao likes. What matters is that the elders of the Qiao family approve."
[ZH] 苗知瑜向往自由的爱情，希望能找到一位与她互相倾慕之人。她喜欢乔公子，可乔公子心有所属，此时的她忽然觉得父母之命媒妁之言也没有什么不对。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu yearned for free love, hoping to find someone who mutually admired her. She liked Young Master Qiao, but Young Master Qiao had someone else in his heart. At this moment, she suddenly felt that there was nothing wrong with parents' orders and matchmakers' words.
[ZH] 哥哥又要离开，乔梦容起身尾随而去，跟在哥哥身后走了一会儿，见他已追上林觉。
[EN] Her brother was leaving again. Qiao Mengrong got up and followed, walking behind her brother for a while until she saw he had caught up with Lin Jue.
[ZH] 乔梦容开口喊道：“哥哥！”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong called out: "Brother!"
[ZH] 申简辰站住，转过身，走到乔梦容面前：“你怎么跟来？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stopped, turned around, and walked to Qiao Mengrong: "Why did you follow me?"
[ZH] “哥哥要去做什么？”
[EN] "What are you going to do, Brother?"
[ZH] 告诉梦容也无妨，申简辰照实说：“文渊喜欢幽栖居士，可幽栖居士还未和离，你瞧，那位就是她的夫君……”
[EN] There was no harm in telling Mengrong, so Shen Jianchen told the truth: "Wenyuan likes Youqi Jushi, but Youqi Jushi hasn't divorced yet. Look, that's her husband..."
[ZH] 乔梦容朝林觉看去。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong looked toward Lin Jue.
[ZH] 林觉深知能把仪王称为“哥哥”的姑娘，身份肯定不一般。听说仪王身边有位佳和郡主，对他照料有加，而且两人已有婚约，这位姑娘定是佳和郡主，林觉不敢怠慢。他张着嘴，露出笑容回礼。
[EN] Lin Jue knew very well that a young lady who could call Prince Yi "brother" must have an extraordinary status. He had heard that there was a Princess Jiahe by Prince Yi's side, who took good care of him, and the two were already engaged. This young lady must be Princess Jiahe. Lin Jue didn't dare to be negligent. He opened his mouth, showed a smile, and returned the greeting.
[ZH] “他在我的劝说下已同意和离，现在让他带我去见知府大人，再把知府大人说动，和离之事必成，文渊就能与幽栖居士在一起。”
[EN] "Under my persuasion, he has already agreed to the mutual divorce. Now I'm having him take me to meet the prefect. Once we convince the prefect, the mutual divorce will definitely happen, and Wenyuan can be with Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 原来是为此事，乔梦容终于放心：“有哥哥出马，必然成功。”
[EN] So it was for this matter. Qiao Mengrong finally felt relieved: "With you on the case, Brother, it will surely succeed."
[ZH] “那是当然，你回去安心坐着，等我回来。”
[EN] "Of course. Go back and sit comfortably. Wait for me to return."
[ZH] 申简辰嘱咐完，快走几步来到林觉身边，与他一起去找朱晞颜。
[EN] After giving his instructions, Shen Jianchen took a few quick steps to reach Lin Jue's side and went with him to find Zhu Xiyan.
[ZH] 望着哥哥的背影，乔梦容松口气，转身回到女宾区，继续欣赏节目。
[EN] Watching her brother's back, Qiao Mengrong breathed a sigh of relief, turned around, and returned to the female guest section to continue enjoying the performance.
[ZH] 朱晞颜回到屋内，坐在榻上，把老祖宗写的信打开再浏览一遍，上面写着两句话：“我同意朱家姑娘与姑爷和离，请朱府的当家人尽快促成。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan returned to his room and sat on the daybed. He opened the letter from the Old Ancestor and read it again. It contained two sentences: "I agree to the mutual divorce between the Zhu family's young lady and her husband. Please have the head of the Zhu family expedite the matter."
[ZH] 朱晞颜把信放在桌上，面色更加严肃。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan put the letter on the table, his expression even more serious.
[ZH] 老祖宗已发话，他作为晚辈必须慎重对待。按照朱家的家规，像老祖宗这等级别的长辈发话，朱晞颜只有遵从的份儿，可女儿的婚事是他经过慎重考虑后挑选的，岂能轻易同意和离？
[EN] The Old Ancestor had spoken. As a junior, he must treat it with caution. According to the Zhu family's rules, when an elder of the Old Ancestor's level spoke, Zhu Xiyan could only obey. But his daughter's marriage was something he had carefully considered and chosen. How could he easily agree to a mutual divorce?
[ZH] 林家也算临安大户，林觉大小也有职位，唯一的缺点是不懂诗词，可这个缺点在朱晞颜看来也算优点。女儿从小聪明伶俐，阅览群书，尤好诗词，被诗词影响得整日生活在不切实际的幻想中，喜欢像徐家公子那种只知空想之人。朱晞颜认为女儿更应该找一个实实在在过日子的，而林觉正是这样的人。
[EN] The Lin family was also a prominent household in Lin'an, and Lin Jue held a position of some sort. His only shortcoming was that he didn't understand poetry, but in Zhu Xiyan's view, this shortcoming was actually an advantage. His daughter had been clever and bright since childhood, well-read, especially fond of poetry. Influenced by poetry, she lived in unrealistic fantasies all day, liking the kind of person who only knew how to daydream, like Young Master Xu. Zhu Xiyan believed his daughter should find someone practical to live life with, and Lin Jue was exactly that kind of person.
[ZH] 最初，因朱徐两家交好，朱晞颜不觉得徐家公子有什么不对，想着以后年龄大些，自然会好。直到徐家搬走，徐公子另娶，朱晞颜既看到徐家的心思，同时也认识到徐家公子不只会空想，还是个没有主见的男子，没有与徐家结亲是不幸中的万幸。从那时起，朱晞颜把嫁女之事提上日程。
[EN] At first, because the Zhu and Xu families were on good terms, Zhu Xiyan didn't think there was anything wrong with Young Master Xu, thinking that when he got older, he would naturally improve. It wasn't until the Xu family moved away and Young Master Xu married someone else that Zhu Xiyan saw the Xu family's intentions and also realized that Young Master Xu was not just a daydreamer but also a man without opinions. Not marrying into the Xu family was a blessing in disguise. From that time on, Zhu Xiyan put the matter of marrying off his daughter on the agenda.
[ZH] 最先找的几家，女儿都不愿意，只因她心里装着徐公子，朱晞颜倒也没强求。拖着拖着，年纪越来越大，再拖下去，恐怕朱家家世再好，都不会有人再要。
[EN] The first few families they approached, his daughter was unwilling, just because she had Young Master Xu in her heart. Zhu Xiyan didn't force her. They delayed and delayed, and she got older and older. If they continued to delay, he was afraid that no matter how good the Zhu family's background was, no one would want her.
[ZH] 正好林家上门提亲，朱晞颜觉得林觉甚合他的心意，便与女儿说了此事，她仍然不同意。后来不断劝说，终于点头，没成想婚后日子过到这个份儿上。
[EN] Coincidentally, the Lin family came to propose marriage. Zhu Xiyan felt that Lin Jue suited him very well, so he told his daughter about it. She still didn't agree. Later, after continuous persuasion, she finally nodded. He never expected their married life would reach this point.
[ZH] 上次女儿回来，一拳将饭桌打裂，朱晞颜便意识到，现在的女儿已不是从前的女儿。
[EN] Last time his daughter came back, she smashed the dining table with one punch. Zhu Xiyan realized that his daughter now was not the same as before.
[ZH] 淑真从小做些什么他作为父亲最清楚不过，她从未练过任何功夫，哪有那么大的力气！定是在林家发生了什么，将她完全改变，此事又不能对外人提起。
[EN] He knew best what Shuzhen had done since childhood. She had never practiced any martial arts. How could she have such great strength! Something must have happened at the Lin family that completely changed her, but this was not something to mention to outsiders.
[ZH] 朱晞颜表面虽冷漠，却已从心里认下这个不一样的女儿。
[EN] Although Zhu Xiyan appeared indifferent on the surface, he had already accepted this different daughter in his heart.
[ZH] 想着以前的女儿跟姑爷过不下去，是因性情不相投，现在既然变了，与姑爷一样有些功夫，被气到时，说话也是粗声粗气，两人应该能找到共同话题。
[EN] He thought that the reason his previous daughter couldn't get along with her husband was because their temperaments didn't match. Now that she had changed, had some martial arts skills like her husband, and spoke roughly when angry, the two should be able to find common topics.
[ZH] 没想到，这个不一样的女儿如此有能耐，既能请动老祖宗出面，还能让老祖宗坚守想法。
[EN] He didn't expect that this different daughter was so capable—she could not only get the Old Ancestor to intervene but also make the Old Ancestor stick to her opinion.
[ZH] 朱晞颜正想着，下人进来：“老爷，韦嬷嬷求见。”
[EN] As Zhu Xiyan was thinking, a servant came in: "Master, Nanny Wei requests an audience."
[ZH] 银杏接到夫人吩咐后，一路小跑赶回夫人屋中。
[EN] After receiving Madam Xiang's instructions, Yinxing ran all the way back to Madam Xiang's room.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷早已享用完美食，靠在榻上小憩。她把韦嬷嬷唤醒，说了老爷回屋之事，告诉她此时正是找老爷的最佳时机。韦嬷嬷赶紧从榻上下来，匆忙赶往老爷的院子。
[EN] Nanny Wei had long finished enjoying the delicacies and was resting on the couch. Yinxing woke her up, told her that the master had returned to his room, and said this was the best time to find him. Nanny Wei quickly got down from the couch and hurried to the master's courtyard.
[ZH] 朱晞颜猜到韦嬷嬷定是为和离之事而来，这位嬷嬷最大的优点是对淑真忠心，一切替她想，替她忙。也因这品性，朱晞颜才放心将淑真交给她照顾。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan guessed that Nanny Wei must be here for the matter of mutual divorce. This nanny's greatest virtue was her loyalty to Shuzhen, thinking of everything for her and busy herself with her affairs. It was also because of this character that Zhu Xiyan felt at ease entrusting Shuzhen to her care.
[ZH] “请她进来。”
[EN] "Please let her in."
[ZH] 得到允许后，韦嬷嬷进入老爷房中：“拜见老爷。”
[EN] After receiving permission, Nanny Wei entered the master's room: "Greetings, Master."
[ZH] “坐。”
[EN] "Sit."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷挑椅子坐下。
[EN] Nanny Wei chose a chair and sat down.
[ZH] “韦嬷嬷是随淑真一起回来？”
[EN] "Did Nanny Wei return with Shuzhen?"
[ZH] “正是。我这次回来除看望老爷和夫人，还有一事要与老爷说。”
[EN] "Yes. Besides visiting the master and madam on this trip, I also have something to discuss with the master."
[ZH] “说吧。”
[EN] "Speak."
[ZH] 本来韦嬷嬷想了很多如何先把老爷哄高兴的话，到跟前，哄老爷高兴的话一句也说不出，干脆直截了当：“老爷把姑娘交给我，本出于信任，可我没把姑娘照顾好，让她时常遭受姑爷毒打……”
[EN] Originally, Nanny Wei had thought of many ways to first make the master happy, but when she got here, she couldn't say a single word to please him. She just got straight to the point: "The master entrusted the young lady to me out of trust, but I didn't take good care of her, letting her often suffer harsh beatings from the young master..."
[ZH] “毒打？不至于，夫妻吵架很正常，姑爷给我说过此事，再说淑真不与姑爷圆房，才有了后来的事，都是她自作孽。”
[EN] "Brutal beatings? That's an exaggeration. It's normal for couples to argue. The young master has told me about this. Besides, Shuzhen refused to consummate the marriage with the young master, which led to what happened later. It's all her own doing."
[ZH] “老爷怎能如此轻描淡写？您这样只会纵容姑爷……”
[EN] "How can the master downplay it so casually? You're only indulging the young master this way..."
[ZH] “纵容？不至于，现在姑爷未必能打得过淑真，你放一百个心。”
[EN] "Indulging? That's not it. Now the young master might not even be able to beat Shuzhen. You can rest assured."
[ZH] “看来老爷已知此事，我出去收了回租，姑娘的性情竟然大变样，还会功夫，姑爷确实打不过，我为姑娘感到开心，以后不至于被欺负。”
[EN] “It seems the master already knows about this. After I went out to collect rent, the young lady's temperament completely changed, and she even knows martial arts. The young master indeed can't beat her. I'm happy for the young lady—she won't be bullied in the future.”
[ZH] “所以不要再怨自己没把她照顾好，也不用再替她担心。”
[EN] "So don't blame yourself for not taking good care of her, and don't worry about her anymore."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不想再言其他，还是赶紧引入正题：“姑爷虽不敢再欺负姑娘，日子却也过不下去，姑娘一心想和离，希望老爷能出面促成和离之事。”
[EN] Nanny Wei didn't want to talk about other things and quickly got to the main topic: "Although the young master no longer dares to bully the young lady, they still can't live together. The young lady is set on a mutual divorce and hopes the master can intervene to make it happen."
[ZH] “嬷嬷只想顺着淑真的意思，你有没有想过，和离后淑真何去何从？她以后的人生该怎么办？”
[EN] "Nanny Wei only wants to follow Shuzhen's wishes. Have you thought about where Shuzhen will go after the divorce? What will she do with her future life?"
[ZH] “我已想好，和离后，若朱府愿意接纳姑娘，我们就住进朱府，若不愿意，我已替姑娘在外买好宅子，搬去住也挺好。”
[EN] "I've already thought it through. After the divorce, if the Zhu family is willing to accept the young lady, we'll move into the Zhu residence. If not, I've already bought a courtyard house outside for the young lady. Moving there would be fine too."
[ZH] “住的地方好解决，她以后的婚事怎么办？”
[EN] "Living arrangements are easy to solve. What about her future marriage?"
[ZH] “姑娘毕竟是老爷的千金，又才貌双全，再找户好人家不成问题，即使没人可嫁，大不了我陪姑娘一辈子，我老了，还有苦荞。总比跟看着不顺眼的人待在一个宅子里强。”
[EN] "After all, the young lady is the master's daughter, talented and beautiful. Finding a good family shouldn't be a problem. Even if there's no one to marry, at worst I'll accompany the young lady for life. When I'm old, there's still Kuqiao. It's better than staying in the same courtyard with someone you can't stand."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷已将以后的生活考虑好，朱晞颜既佩服又欣慰，可和离之事他还是不能轻易同意：“朱家的女儿要以朱家的名声为重，和离虽比被夫家所休听着好些，也有损名声，不仅会损坏淑真的名声，连带着朱家也会受影响。”
[EN] Nanny Wei had already planned for the future. Zhu Xiyan felt both admiration and comfort, but he still couldn't easily agree to the divorce: "A daughter of the Zhu family must prioritize the Zhu family's reputation. Although a mutual divorce sounds better than being cast out by the husband's family, it still damages reputation. It will not only damage Shuzhen's reputation but also affect the Zhu family."
[ZH] “老爷饱读诗书，又肩负临安重任，按说见识应在我这妇道人家之上，可为何我都知名利乃身外之物，老爷却不知？”
[EN] "The master is well-read and bears the heavy responsibility of Lin'an. By rights, your insight should be above mine, a mere woman. Yet why do I know that fame and profit are external things, while the master doesn't?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜正要说话，下人又进来：“老爷，姑爷求见。”
[EN] Just as Zhu Xiyan was about to speak, a servant came in again: "Master, the young master requests an audience."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“姑爷来的正是时候，不如与他当面将和离之事说定。”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "The young master has come at the perfect time. Why not settle the matter of mutual divorce with him face to face?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜也认为林觉来的正是时候，他向来不愿和离，正好与自己一起说服韦嬷嬷：“让他进来吧。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan also thought Lin Jue had come at the perfect time. He had always been unwilling to divorce, and it would be good to have him help persuade Nanny Wei: "Let him in."
[ZH] 林觉与申简辰早已到达门外，下人说老爷正在里面与人说话。申简辰决定在院中等待一会儿，林觉只能同意。
[EN] Lin Jue and Shen Jianchen had already arrived outside the door. The servant said the master was talking to someone inside. Shen Jianchen decided to wait in the courtyard for a while, and Lin Jue could only agree.
[ZH] 林觉不敢随便跟仪王搭话，恭敬地站着。申简辰与林觉无话可说，也保持沉默。
[EN] Lin Jue didn't dare to casually strike up a conversation with Prince Yi and stood respectfully. Shen Jianchen had nothing to say to Lin Jue and also remained silent.
[ZH] 二人等了一会儿，林觉认为差不多时，请示仪王后，才进屋拜见岳父大人。
[EN] The two waited for a while. When Lin Jue thought it was about time, he asked Prince Yi for permission before entering the room to greet his father-in-law.
[ZH] 林觉进入后，先向岳父与韦嬷嬷行礼，得到允许，在韦嬷嬷对面的椅子上坐下。
[EN] After entering, Lin Jue first bowed to his father-in-law and Nanny Wei. After receiving permission, he sat in the chair opposite Nanny Wei.
[ZH] 朱晞颜问：“不知贤婿前来所为何事？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan asked: "I wonder what brings my worthy son-in-law here?"
[ZH] 屁股还没坐热，林觉赶紧又起身，恭敬地说：“岳父大人，我前来是想向您说说与娘子和离之事。”
[EN] Before his bottom even warmed the seat, Lin Jue quickly got up again and said respectfully: "Father-in-law, I've come to discuss the matter of mutual divorce with my wife."
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“别紧张，坐下慢慢说。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said: "Don't be nervous. Sit down and take your time."
[ZH] 林觉又坐下，大胆说：“这段时日我想明白，我和淑真与其这么难受地过着，不如放对方自由，这样大家都开心，我也好重新娶一房适合我的娘子。”
[EN] Lin Jue sat down again and boldly said: "I've thought it through recently. Instead of living so unhappily, Shuzhen and I might as well set each other free. That way, everyone will be happy, and I can remarry a wife who suits me."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷笑道：“姑爷能这么想实在难得。”
[EN] Nanny Wei smiled: "It's truly rare for the young master to think this way."
[ZH] 林觉这么快改变主意，令朱晞颜感到意外：“姑爷的想法改变得如此快，不知是何缘故？”
[EN] Lin Jue changed his mind so quickly, which surprised Zhu Xiyan: "The young master's thoughts have changed so quickly. May I ask the reason?"
[ZH] 林觉心想，外面还站着位大人物，总不能让他一直站在那里，还是赶紧把人请进来，但也需先说说朱淑真的不是，发泄发泄才行。
[EN] Lin Jue thought to himself that there was still an important figure waiting outside. He couldn't let him stand there forever—he needed to hurry and bring him in. But he also needed to first speak ill of Zhu Shuzhen to vent a bit.
[ZH] “岳父大人有所不知，我本想跟娘子好好过下去，不管她对我怎样，我都不计较，即便她不跟我圆房，我也不嫌弃，大不了再纳一房妾室。可娘子说，我不同意和离，她就耗着我，我领进一个女人，她就打走一个，让我没有机会纳妾，考虑到林家香火，我只好同意和离。”
[EN] "Father-in-law, there's something you don't know. I originally wanted to live well with my wife, no matter how she treated me, I wouldn't mind. Even if she refused to consummate the marriage, I wouldn't mind. At worst, I'd take a concubine. But my wife said that if I didn't agree to the divorce, she would wear me down. If I brought in a woman, she would chase her away. She wouldn't give me a chance to take a concubine. Considering the Lin family's ancestral line, I had no choice but to agree to the divorce."
[ZH] 朱晞颜气道：“太不像话！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said angrily: "That's outrageous!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷反问道：“姑爷平时在老爷面前都是如此编排我家姑娘？”
[EN] Nanny Wei countered: "Does the young master usually badmouth our young lady like this in front of the master?"
[ZH] 林觉并未接韦嬷嬷的话。
[EN] Lin Jue didn't respond to Nanny Wei's words.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续说：“老爷不必生气，姑爷的话您只需信一半儿。”
[EN] Nanny Wei continued: "The master doesn't need to be angry. You only need to believe half of what the young master says."
[ZH] 林觉心里念着仪王，赶紧说：“岳父大人，参加宴会时，我确实想到要向您说明和离之事，可想法不是很坚决，有一人前来劝说，我不得不同意。”
[EN] Lin Jue was thinking about Prince Yi and quickly said: "Father-in-law, during the banquet, I did think about telling you about the divorce, but I wasn't very firm. Someone came to persuade me, and I had to agree."
[ZH] 林觉走到朱晞颜跟前，在他耳边小声嘀咕：“仪王正在外面等着见您。”
[EN] Lin Jue walked up to Zhu Xiyan and whispered in his ear: "Prince Yi is outside waiting to see you."
[ZH] 朱晞颜吓得立刻站起来：“韦嬷嬷，你从后门走吧，有贵客前来，和离之事稍后再议。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan was so startled that he immediately stood up: "Nanny Wei, leave through the back gate. An honored guest has come. We'll discuss the divorce later."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷也是见过大世面的人，猜到官府来了不一般的人物，迅速起身从后门出去。
[EN] Nanny Wei was also someone who had seen the world. She guessed that an extraordinary person from the government had come and quickly got up and left through the back gate.
[ZH] 朱晞颜整理好衣装，带着林觉来到院中。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan adjusted his clothes and took Lin Jue to the courtyard.

#CH 59
[ZH] 朱晞颜看到院中站着一人，背影尽显贵气，肯定是位王爷。只是想不通仪王长期病着，官家并不重视，怎么突然好了？还被任命为临安节度使？
[EN] Zhu Xiyan saw a person standing in the courtyard. His bearing exuded nobility—definitely a prince. He just couldn't figure out: Prince Yi had been ill for a long time, and the emperor didn't pay much attention to him. How did he suddenly recover? And why was he appointed Military Commissioner of Lin'an?
[ZH] 朱晞颜没有时间纠结心中的疑惑，与林觉上前拜道：“不知仪王驾到，有失远迎，还望恕罪。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan had no time to dwell on his doubts. He and Lin Jue stepped forward and bowed: "We didn't know Prince Yi was visiting. We didn't welcome you properly. Please forgive us."
[ZH] 申简辰转过身，被年长之人以礼相拜，甚觉荣耀：“临安风景美，我想多玩几日，故而没有公开身份，知府大人何罪之有？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around. Being respectfully greeted by an elder filled him with honor: "The scenery in Lin'an is beautiful. I wanted to stay a few more days, so I didn't reveal my identity. What offense could the prefect possibly have?"
[ZH] “宴会中没认出仪王就是罪过。”
[EN] "Not recognizing Prince Yi at the banquet is an offense."
[ZH] “知府大人没认出我，十分正常，在汴梁时，很多朝中大臣也没怎么见过我。”
[EN] "It's perfectly normal that the prefect didn't recognize me. When I was in Bianliang, many court officials hadn't seen me much either."
[ZH] 朱晞颜不知该如何搭话，只能说：“仪王，里边请。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan didn't know how to respond and could only say: "Prince Yi, please come inside."
[ZH] 申简辰走进屋内随便挑张椅子坐下。朱晞颜吩咐下人上茶，他与林觉依然弯腰站立。
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked into the room and casually picked a chair to sit. Zhu Xiyan ordered a servant to serve tea. He and Lin Jue remained standing respectfully.
[ZH] 下人上好茶后，申简辰说：“知府大人就坐吧，站着怪累的。”
[EN] After the servant served the tea, Shen Jianchen said: "Please sit down, Prefect. Standing must be tiring."
[ZH] 朱晞颜不敢坐下：“下官还是站着聆听仪王教诲。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan didn't dare sit: "Your subordinate will stand and listen to Prince Yi's instructions."
[ZH] 岳父大人不坐，林觉也不敢随便就坐，恭恭敬敬弯腰站立着。
[EN] Since the father-in-law didn't sit, Lin Jue didn't dare sit either. He stood respectfully with his waist bent.
[ZH] 申简辰不好再劝：“自从我疾病痊愈后，与高太尉比试蹴鞠，不小心赢了他，官家一高兴，给了我临安节度使的职位，不得已来此任职。我对临安不熟，以后还要多多仰仗知府大人。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn't press further: "Ever since I recovered from my illness, I competed in cuju with Grand Marshal Gao and unexpectedly won. The emperor was so pleased that he gave me the position of Military Commissioner of Lin'an. I had no choice but to take up the post here. I'm not familiar with Lin'an, so I'll have to rely on the prefect from now on."
[ZH] “仪王能来临安任职是临安之福，下官定会听从仪王差遣。”
[EN] "Prince Yi's appointment to Lin'an is a blessing for the city. Your subordinate will certainly follow your commands."
[ZH] “差遣谈不上，我初来乍到，需先熟悉环境，至于临安事务，知府大人以前如何现在依然如何。”
[EN] "Commands are too strong a word. I'm new here and need to get familiar with the environment first. As for Lin'an's affairs, the prefect should continue managing them just as before."
[ZH] 听仪王这话，显然不大愿意多插手临安事务，朱晞颜心里轻松许多：“下官记下，明日下官定携临安全体官员为仪王接风洗尘。”
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi's words, it was clear he didn't want to interfere much in Lin'an's affairs. Zhu Xiyan felt much more at ease: "Your subordinate takes note. Tomorrow, I'll definitely bring all of Lin'an's officials to host a welcome banquet for Prince Yi."
[ZH] 申简辰拒绝道：“不必，今日参加宴会，算是接风洗尘。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen declined: "No need. Attending today's banquet counts as the welcome."
[ZH] 朱晞颜依然客气道：“那怎么成？仪王来临安任职，官员们需隆重迎接才对。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan still insisted politely: "How can that be? With Prince Yi coming to Lin'an, the officials should give you a grand welcome."
[ZH] 想到北宋即将成为南宋，之后又是各种朝代更替，申简辰一点儿从政的欲望都没有。他来临安主要是找千里寻，至于其他，并不看重。
[EN] Thinking about how the Northern Song would soon become the Southern Song, followed by various dynastic changes, Shen Jianchen had no desire to engage in politics at all. He came to Lin'an mainly to find Qianli Xun. As for everything else, he didn't care much.
[ZH] “我向来喜欢低调，隆重迎接也免了，知府大人只需吩咐人在府衙给我留个办理公务的地儿即可，有时间我会过去坐坐。”
[EN] "I've always preferred to keep a low profile, so spare me the grand welcome. The prefect just needs to arrange an office for me at the prefecture office. I'll drop by when I have time."
[ZH] 朱晞颜又一阵开心，庆幸来了位闲散王爷，只要他不过分参与临安府衙之事，大权就不会旁落他人：“临安的军政要务以后还需仪王多多费心。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan was pleased again, relieved that a leisurely prince had come. As long as he didn't interfere too much in Lin'an's affairs, the power wouldn't fall into others' hands: "Lin'an's military and political affairs will need Prince Yi's attention in the future."
[ZH] “那是自然。”寒暄这么多，申简辰不想再寒暄下去，还是尽快表明来意，“我前来见知府大人，是有私事要与您谈，还是坐下说吧。”
[EN] "Of course." After so much small talk, Shen Jianchen didn't want to continue. He'd better get to the point: "I came to see the prefect about a personal matter. Let's sit down to discuss it."
[ZH] 仪王已是第二次请他坐下，朱晞颜不好再站着，赶紧坐下。再说仪王要谈私事，坐下才有谈私事的氛围。
[EN] Prince Yi had asked him to sit for the second time. Zhu Xiyan couldn't keep standing and quickly sat down. Besides, if Prince Yi wanted to discuss a personal matter, sitting down created the right atmosphere.
[ZH] 林觉见岳父大人坐下，也跟着坐下。
[EN] Seeing his father-in-law sit down, Lin Jue followed and sat as well.
[ZH] 朱晞颜问：“不知仪王要与下官谈什么私事？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan asked: "I wonder what personal matter Prince Yi wants to discuss with your subordinate?"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“也不是什么大事，林练兵官已知，不如让他告诉知府大人。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said: "It's not a big matter. Military Training Officer Lin already knows. Why not let him tell the prefect?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜扭头用疑惑的目光看向林觉：“贤婿请讲。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan turned and looked at Lin Jue with a puzzled gaze: "Please speak, son-in-law."
[ZH] 林觉说：“是我与淑真和离之事，仪王劝说我放了淑真，只因她与乔文仙两情相悦。”
[EN] Lin Jue said: "It's about my mutual divorce with Shuzhen. Prince Yi persuaded me to let Shuzhen go because she and Qiao Wenxian are mutually in love."
[ZH] 申简辰趁机说：“我已说服林练兵官，现在还想说服知府大人，希望你以幽栖居士的幸福为重，同意和离。”
