[EN] Shen Jianchen seized the opportunity: "I've already persuaded Military Training Officer Lin. Now I want to persuade the prefect. I hope you'll prioritize Youqi Jushi's happiness and agree to the mutual divorce."
[ZH] 朱晞颜是聪明人，迅速意识到仪王是帮乔文仙。不过，乔文仙与淑真两情相悦他倒是第一次听说。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan was smart and quickly realized that Prince Yi was helping Qiao Wenxian. However, this was the first time he heard that Qiao Wenxian and Shuzhen were mutually in love.
[ZH] 和离最大的障碍是女儿未来的婚姻，如果和离后婚姻无法解决，不仅影响女儿，也影响朱家的声誉。
[EN] The biggest obstacle to the mutual divorce was his daughter's future marriage. If the marriage couldn't be resolved after the divorce, it would not only affect his daughter but also the Zhu family's reputation.
[ZH] 既然她与乔文仙有情，又有仪王出面，乔家娶淑真应该没有多大问题。女儿的未来和朱家的声誉都能保住，他还有什么可说的。
[EN] Since she had feelings for Qiao Wenxian, and Prince Yi was intervening, the Qiao family marrying Shuzhen shouldn't be a problem. His daughter's future and the Zhu family's reputation could both be preserved. What more could he say?
[ZH] 朱晞颜瞬间释然许多，仪王的面子还是要给的。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan instantly felt much more at ease. He still had to give Prince Yi face.
[ZH] “下官本不同意贤婿与淑真和离，主要考虑一段婚姻来之不易。可他们的日子过得并不顺心，此事还惊动仪王，让您费心，仪王都发话，我没什么可反对的，同意便是。”
[EN] "I originally didn't agree to the mutual divorce between my son-in-law and Shuzhen, mainly because a marriage is not easily built. But their life together hasn't been happy. This matter has even disturbed Prince Yi and caused you to take the trouble. Since Prince Yi has spoken, I have nothing to object to. I agree."
[ZH] 朱晞颜说出“同意”二字，林觉一阵失落，这段婚姻来得快，去得也快。
[EN] When Zhu Xiyan said the word "agree," Lin Jue felt a surge of disappointment. This marriage came quickly and left just as fast.
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“婚姻就像一扇挂着锁的大门，要成功打开锁，必须用适合的钥匙。若把男子比为锁，女子便是钥匙，一把锁配一把钥匙，锁和钥匙不相配，门怎么都打不开。幽栖居士这把钥匙不适合林练兵官这把锁，分开才是正确的选择，练兵官不必伤感。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen smiled: "Marriage is like a big gate with a lock. To successfully open the lock, you need the right key. If a man is the lock, then a woman is the key. One lock matches one key. If the lock and key don't match, the door can never be opened. Youqi Jushi is a key that doesn't fit Military Training Officer Lin's lock. Separating is the right choice. There's no need to be sad."
[ZH] 两人齐声说：“受教了。”
[EN] The two said in unison: "We've learned something."
[ZH] 宴会结束后，参加宴会之人纷纷在朱府外乘坐马车回府。
[EN] After the banquet ended, the guests one by one took their carriages outside the Zhu residence to return home.
[ZH] 申简辰将朱晞颜同意和离之事告诉文渊，乔文仙高兴地说：“不行！我太兴奋！必须马上跳下去，直接跑回幽林别居！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen told Wenyuan that Zhu Xiyan had agreed to the mutual divorce. Qiao Wenxian happily said: "No way! I'm too excited! I have to jump down right now and run straight back to Youlin Biejü!"
[ZH] 他让南双停下马车，直接跳下，在大街上开心地跑着。
[EN] He had Nanshuang stop the carriage, jumped right down, and ran happily along the main street.
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐南双：“马车不要赶得太快，紧跟在文渊身后即可，等他跑累了，好上来休息。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen instructed Nanshuang: "Don't drive the carriage too fast. Just follow closely behind Wenyuan. When he's tired from running, he can come up and rest."
[ZH] 南双回道：“是。”
[EN] Nanshuang replied: "Yes, sir."
[ZH] 乔梦容从后面的马车里探出头，见乔公子在大街上兴奋地奔跑，笑道：“没想到乔公子这么大个人，也像个孩子似的，一高兴就跑起来。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong poked her head out from the carriage behind. Seeing Young Master Qiao running excitedly along the main street, she smiled: "I didn't expect Young Master Qiao, a grown man, to be like a child, running off the moment he's happy."
[ZH] 乔梦容将身体坐正，很快又变得伤感。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong sat up straight and soon became melancholy again.
[ZH] 乔公子高兴定是因为知道幽栖居士和离必然成功，他能因即将得到所爱之人兴奋成那样子。可哥哥呢？自身体恢复健康后，面对他们的感情从未兴奋过。
[EN] Young Master Qiao must be happy because he knew Youqi Jushi's mutual divorce would surely succeed. He could be that excited about soon getting the person he loved. But what about her brother? Ever since he recovered his health, he had never been excited about their relationship.
[ZH] 喜兰问道：“郡主，您怎么了？”
[EN] Xilan asked: "Princess, what's wrong?"
[ZH] 乔梦容沉默一会儿，想听听喜兰的看法：“喜兰，你说现在的哥哥到底喜不喜欢我？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong was silent for a moment, wanting to hear Xilan's opinion: "Xilan, do you think Brother really likes me now?"
[ZH] 喜兰心里虽怀疑，还是肯定道：“王爷当然喜欢郡主。”
[EN] Although Xilan had doubts in her heart, she affirmed: "The prince certainly likes the princess."
[ZH] “可我怎么越来越感觉不到哥哥对我的感情？”
[EN] "But why do I feel less and less of Brother's feelings for me?"
[ZH] “郡主不要瞎想，王爷病好后是有些变化，可王爷对郡主依然关怀照顾。”
[EN] "Princess, don't overthink it. The prince has changed somewhat after recovering, but he still cares for and looks after the princess."
[ZH] 关怀照顾不假，可哥哥对她更像是对待家人，而非爱人。
[EN] It was true that he cared and looked after her, but Brother treated her more like a family member than a lover.
[ZH] 她悄悄羡慕起幽栖居士，还未和离，就有人为她爱得疯狂，她与乔公子在宴会上眉目传情，乔梦容偶尔看到，也是歆羡不已。
[EN] She secretly envied Youqi Jushi. Even before the divorce was finalized, someone loved her madly. She and Young Master Qiao exchanged loving glances at the banquet. Qiao Mengrong occasionally saw it and was filled with envy.
[ZH] 为何哥哥不主动看她一眼？哥哥是不懂？还是根本不愿意？乔梦容宁可相信哥哥根本不懂。
[EN] Why didn't Brother take the initiative to look at her? Did Brother not understand? Or did he simply not want to? Qiao Mengrong preferred to believe that Brother simply didn't understand.

#CH 60
[ZH] 乔梦容想起哥哥病着时，有一次，身体稍微好些，要求丫鬟为他穿戴整齐，他要牵着她的手，在仪王府的花园里走走。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong remembered when her brother was ill. Once, when he was feeling a bit better, he had the maid dress him properly. He wanted to hold her hand and walk through the gardens of Prince Yi's mansion.
[ZH] 哥哥拉着她的手，步履艰难，却也努力走着，不让乔梦容和丫鬟搀扶，说他要好好体验恋爱的感觉。乔梦容既心疼哥哥，又不愿违背他的心愿，任凭他牵着手。
[EN] Her brother held her hand, his steps unsteady, yet he struggled forward, refusing to let Qiao Mengrong or the maid support him. He said he wanted to truly experience the feeling of being in love. Qiao Mengrong felt heartache for her brother but didn't want to go against his wishes, so she let him hold her hand.
[ZH] 他们好不容易走到王府内一大片种满薰衣草的园中，哥哥将她的手拽得更紧：“梦容，等我好了，一定要抱着你，在这片薰衣草中转无数个圈，告诉它们，我会爱你一生一世。”
[EN] They finally made it to a large garden in the mansion filled with lavender. Her brother squeezed her hand tighter: "Mengrong, when I'm well, I will definitely hold you and spin you around in countless circles in this lavender field. I'll tell every flower that I will love you for all eternity."
[ZH] 乔梦容幸福地笑着：“哥哥那样转我，岂不是会把我转晕？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong smiled happily: "If you spin me like that, Brother, won't you make me dizzy?"
[ZH] “晕了才好，倒在我怀里，我会永远抱着你。”
[EN] "Good. If you're dizzy, you'll fall into my arms, and I will hold you forever."
[ZH] 马车外传来乔文仙的声音：“累死我了！快停下来！我要上马车歇歇！”
[EN] Outside the carriage came Qiao Wenxian's voice: "I'm exhausted! Stop! I need to get back in the carriage and rest!"
[ZH] 乔梦容的回忆被乔公子的声音打断，外面已没有街市上嘈杂的人声，应该出了城。她轻轻叹口气，埋怨自己要求太多，只要哥哥身体健康比什么都重要。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's memories were interrupted by Young Master Qiao's voice. Outside, there were no longer the noisy sounds of the market streets. They must have left the city. She sighed softly, scolding herself for wanting too much. As long as her brother was healthy, that was more important than anything.
[ZH] 宴会结束后，千里寻并未回林府，她匆匆去找韦嬷嬷，想尽快了解韦嬷嬷有没有与父亲谈过。
[EN] After the banquet ended, Qianli Xun did not return to the Lin residence. She hurried to find Nanny Wei, wanting to know as soon as possible whether Nanny Wei had spoken with her father.
[ZH] 当她带着苦荞与夏果来到向夫人居住的院子时，韦嬷嬷正好从屋内出来，先批评夏果。
[EN] When she arrived at Madam Xiang's courtyard with Kuqiao and Xiaguo, Nanny Wei happened to be coming out of the room. She first criticized Xiaguo.
[ZH] “夏果，我让你在院中待着，你却跑到宴会上，如此不懂礼数，不适合待在姑娘身边，从哪里来回哪里去吧。”
[EN] "Xiaguo, I told you to stay in the courtyard, but you ran off to the banquet. You're so lacking in propriety that you're not fit to stay by the young lady's side. Go back to where you came from."
[ZH] 夏果立刻跪下：“嬷嬷恕罪，我在院中无聊，又担心小姐，才跑到宴会上，再说我也没犯什么大错，嬷嬷又何必针对我？”
[EN] Xiaguo immediately knelt down: "Nanny, please forgive me. I was bored in the courtyard and worried about the young lady, so I ran to the banquet. Besides, I didn't make any big mistake. Why must you single me out?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“针对你？你也配？如若你像苦荞一样是个安分的丫头，我也不说什么，可你天生不安分……”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "Single you out? You're not even worth it. If you were a well-behaved maid like Kuqiao, I wouldn't say anything. But you've always been unruly by nature..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷还未说完，千里寻劝道：“嬷嬷不必理会夏果，我还有重要的事问你。”
[EN] Before Nanny Wei could finish, Qianli Xun interceded: "Nanny, don't bother with Xiaguo. I have something important to ask you."
[ZH] 姑娘已发话，韦嬷嬷不好再批评下去：“罚你在门外跪上半个时辰，好好想想错在哪里。苦荞，你在旁边守着，姑娘由我来伺候。”
[EN] Since the young lady had spoken, Nanny Wei couldn't continue criticizing: "You're to kneel as punishment outside the gate for half a shichen. Think carefully about what you did wrong. Kuqiao, you watch over her. I'll attend to the young lady."
[ZH] 千里寻虽可怜夏果，可也不能驳了韦嬷嬷的面子，毕竟这位嬷嬷无论做任何事都是为她考虑。
[EN] Although Qianli Xun felt sorry for Xiaguo, she couldn't undermine Nanny Wei's authority. After all, this nanny always considered things for her sake, no matter what she did.
[ZH] 二人进屋后，千里寻问起韦嬷嬷见父亲之事，韦嬷嬷将具体过程一一对姑娘说了。
[EN] After the two entered the room, Qianli Xun asked Nanny Wei about meeting her father. Nanny Wei told the young lady the whole process in detail.
[ZH] 林觉已同意和离，千里寻当然高兴，可父亲的态度依然坚决，又让千里寻高兴不起来。
[EN] Lin Jue had already agreed to the mutual divorce. Of course, Qianli Xun was happy about that. But her father's attitude remained firm, which made Qianli Xun unhappy again.
[ZH] 银杏从外面进来：“小姐，韦嬷嬷，老爷请你们去厅堂。”
[EN] Yinxing came in from outside: "Young lady, Nanny Wei, the master invites you to the main hall."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷问：“是单叫我与姑娘，还是也有其他人？”
[EN] Nanny Wei asked: "Is it just Nanny Wei and the young lady, or are there others?"
[ZH] 银杏回道：“夫人已过去，大公子和少夫人也去了。”
[EN] Yinxing replied: "Madam has already gone. The eldest young master and the young madam have also gone."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷判断道：“看来有大事，不妨过去听听，趁人都在，我再与老爷提提和离之事。”
[EN] Nanny Wei judged: "It seems something big is happening. We might as well go listen. While everyone is there, I'll bring up the matter of the mutual divorce with the master again."
[ZH] 来到院中，韦嬷嬷看一眼跪在地上的夏果，没有说一句话，与姑娘往厅堂而去。
[EN] Coming to the courtyard, Nanny Wei glanced at Xiaguo kneeling on the ground. Without saying a word, she and the young lady headed toward the main hall.
[ZH] 夏果显得很委屈，见韦嬷嬷走远，问道：“苦荞姐姐，你说说，韦嬷嬷为何看我不顺眼？”
[EN] Xiaguo looked very wronged. Seeing Nanny Wei walk far away, she asked: "Sister Kuqiao, tell me, why does Nanny Wei dislike me so much?"
[ZH] “我哪里知道？”
[EN] "How would I know?"
[ZH] “她以前可这么对待过你？”
[EN] "Did she ever treat you like this before?"
[ZH] “那倒没有。”
[EN] "No, she didn't."
[ZH] “哼！她肯定是嫌我长得好看！我长得好看，难道有错吗？”
[EN] "Hmph! She must resent that I'm good-looking! Is it wrong that I'm good-looking?"
[ZH] “你别瞎想，肯定不是因为这个。”
[EN] "Don't think nonsense. It's definitely not because of that."
[ZH] “肯定是！她嫌我长得好看，小姐把我带出去，怕我抢了小姐的风头。小姐还没说什么，韦嬷嬷凭什么这么对待我？”
[EN] "It definitely is! She resents that I'm good-looking. The young lady took me out, afraid I'd steal the young lady's spotlight. The young lady hasn't said anything, so why is Nanny Wei treating me like this?"
[ZH] 苦荞劝道：“你别胡乱想，至于什么原因，我也不清楚，但我知道韦嬷嬷是个很厉害的人，一般连我们自己都还没发现的问题，她一眼就能看出，她罚你，肯定是你有问题。”
[EN] Kuqiao advised: "Don't think wildly. As for the reason, I'm not clear either. But I know Nanny Wei is a very sharp person. Usually, even problems we haven't discovered ourselves, she can see at a glance. If she punished you, there must be something wrong with you."
[ZH] 夏果更加不满：“苦荞姐姐什么意思？难道你伺候小姐从来都不会出错？”
[EN] Xiaguo became even more dissatisfied: "What do you mean, Sister Kuqiao? Have you never made a mistake while attending the young lady?"
[ZH] 苦荞想想说：“出错没出错我都不记得，不过韦嬷嬷的确从来没罚过我。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought and said: "I don't remember whether I made mistakes or not. But Nanny Wei has indeed never punished me."
[ZH] “哼！”夏果很不服气。
[EN] "Hmph!" Xiaguo was very unconvinced.
[ZH] 千里寻与韦嬷嬷来到厅堂时，其他人已坐在里面，向夫人示意她们尽快落座。
[EN] When Qianli Xun and Nanny Wei arrived at the main hall, the others were already seated inside. Madam Xiang signaled them to take their seats quickly.
[ZH] 朱晞颜坐在主位，一脸严肃，与宴会上的表现判若两人。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan sat in the seat of honor, his face serious, looking like two different people from his performance at the banquet.
[ZH] 人已到齐，朱晞颜咳嗽一声，开口说：“把你们叫来，有两件大事要说。第一件，今日宴会中来了一位贵人，不知你们可注意到？”
[EN] Everyone had arrived. Zhu Xiyan coughed once and began to speak: "I've called you all here to discuss two important matters. First, a noble guest came to today's banquet. Did you all notice?"
[ZH] 大家十分不解。
[EN] Everyone was very confused.
[ZH] 朱景元在府衙办差，很快明白父亲的意思：“爹，您说的贵人可是临安节度使？”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan worked at the prefecture office and quickly understood his father's meaning: "Father, is the noble guest you're referring to the Military Commissioner of Lin'an?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜点点头：“没错，是临安节度使，他今日出现在宴会，竟然无人知晓，你们可知道临安节度使是谁？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded: "That's right, the Military Commissioner of Lin'an. He appeared at the banquet today, yet no one knew. Do you all know who the Military Commissioner of Lin'an is?"
[ZH] 向夫人小心问：“是谁？”
[EN] Madam Xiang asked cautiously: "Who is it?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜回道：“官家的十三子，仪王。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan replied: "The thirteenth son of the emperor, Prince Yi."
[ZH] 大家被惊到，面面相觑。
[EN] Everyone was shocked and looked at each other.
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续说：“都仔细想想，在宴会中有没有说过分的话，做过分的事。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan continued: "Think carefully. Did any of you say anything excessive or do anything excessive at the banquet?"
[ZH] 千里寻问：“爹，你说的仪王到底是宴会中哪一位？我可一点儿印象都没有。”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked: "Father, who exactly is the Prince Yi you're talking about at the banquet? I don't have any impression at all."
[ZH] 向夫人问：“该不会是跟着乔公子来的那位申公子？”
[EN] Madam Xiang asked: "Could it be Young Master Shen who came with Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜点点头。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded.
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“原来他是仪王，还是临安节度使。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought to herself: "So he's Prince Yi, and also the Military Commissioner of Lin'an."
[ZH] 陈文心说：“仔细想想，我与婆婆，还有淑真说话做事均符合礼仪要求，应该不会得罪仪王。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin said: "Thinking carefully, Mother-in-law and I, along with Shuzhen, all spoke and acted according to propriety. We shouldn't have offended Prince Yi."
[ZH] 朱景元说：“儿子与仪王一直没打过照面，谈不上有什么差错。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan said: "I never came face to face with Prince Yi, so there's no question of any mistake."
[ZH] 朱晞颜点点头：“那便好，还有一位，大家以后也要谨慎对待，仪王身边有位姑娘，是佳和郡主，以后跟她说话也需恭敬，记住没？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan nodded: “That's good. There's another person you all must be cautious with in the future. There's a young lady by Prince Yi's side—she's Princess Jiahe. When speaking with her in the future, you must also be respectful. Remember？”
[ZH] 大家回道：“记下了。”
[EN] Everyone replied: "We remember."
[ZH] 千里寻回忆她与佳和郡主聊天儿的情景，没有什么不对。心想：“没想到乔姑娘是佳和郡主，跟我说话谦和有礼，一点儿没有郡主的架子。”
[EN] Qianli Xun recalled her conversation with Princess Jiahe. Nothing was wrong. She thought to herself: "I didn't expect Young Lady Qiao to be Princess Jiahe. She spoke to me so gently and politely, without any airs of a princess at all."
[ZH] 朱晞颜又咳嗽一声，看向女儿：“接下来该说第二件事。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan coughed again and looked at his daughter: "Now it's time to discuss the second matter."
[ZH] 接触到父亲的目光，千里寻纳闷，为何父亲看向她？难道第二件事与她有关？
[EN] Meeting her father's gaze, Qianli Xun was puzzled. Why was her father looking at her? Could the second matter be related to her?
[ZH] 果不其然，父亲唤她的名字：“淑真。”
[EN] Sure enough, her father called her name: "Shuzhen."
[ZH] 千里寻回道：“在。”
[EN] Qianli Xun replied: "Yes."
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“你既然跟林觉过不下去，和离就和离吧……”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said: "Since you can't get along with Lin Jue, then let's just have the mutual divorce..."
[ZH] 朱晞颜话还未说完，千里寻已忍不住兴奋激动的心情，起身抱住朱晞颜：“谢谢爹！你是天下最好的爹！”
[EN] Before Zhu Xiyan could finish speaking, Qianli Xun couldn't contain her excitement. She stood up and hugged Zhu Xiyan: "Thank you, Father! You are the best father in the world!"
[ZH] 女儿长这么大，第一次搂抱父亲，朱晞颜十分不适应：“回去坐好，听我把话说完。”
[EN] His daughter had grown up and was hugging her father for the first time. Zhu Xiyan was very uncomfortable: "Go back and sit properly. Let me finish speaking."
[ZH] 千里寻只得放开，坐回原位。其他人也都无不替她感到高兴。
[EN] Qianli Xun had to let go and sit back in her seat. Everyone else was also happy for her.
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续说：“景元，明日把族中所有长辈请到朱府，就说是我的意思，在这厅堂，给淑真把和离的事办了。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan continued: "Jingyuan, tomorrow invite all the clan elders to the Zhu residence. Say it's my wish. In this main hall, handle the mutual divorce matter for Shuzhen."
[ZH] 朱景元回道：“好的，爹。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan replied: "Yes, Father."
[ZH] 朱晞颜发话道：“今日就到这里，散了吧。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan announced: "That's all for today. Dismissed."
[ZH] 一家人目送朱晞颜离开，千里寻将每个人抱了一遍，表达开心之情。由此看来，竹林的确给她带来好运。
[EN] The family watched Zhu Xiyan leave. Qianli Xun hugged everyone, expressing her joy. It seemed the bamboo grove had indeed brought her good luck.

#CH 61
[ZH] 明天就要和离了，千里寻不用再回林府。向夫人邀请韦嬷嬷和她住在一起，两人年纪相仿，共同话题也多。千里寻则带着两个丫鬟回到了偏僻的园子。
[EN] Tomorrow was the day of the mutual divorce. Qianli Xun didn't need to return to the Lin residence anymore. Madam Xiang invited Nanny Wei to live with her—they were about the same age and had plenty to talk about. Qianli Xun, meanwhile, returned to the remote garden with her two maids.
[ZH] 宴会过后，朱府归于平静，但千里寻的内心却因为激动而难以平静。明天过后，她就可以过属于千里寻的生活了。
[EN] After the banquet, the Zhu residence returned to peace, but Qianli Xun's heart was too excited to settle. After tomorrow, she could finally live the life of Qianli Xun.
[ZH] “苦荞，拿酒来！”
[EN] "Kuqiao, bring me some wine!"
[ZH] 没有传来苦荞的声音，她才想起夏果跪了半个时辰，膝盖都跪红了，她吩咐苦荞去给夏果上药。
[EN] There was no response from Kuqiao. She remembered that Xiaguo had knelt for half a shichen, her knees red and raw. She had sent Kuqiao to apply medicine to Xiaguo's wounds.
[ZH] 既然没人拿酒，总得找个方式表达心情。
[EN] Since no one was bringing wine, she had to find another way to express her feelings.
[ZH] 朱淑真是女词人，千里寻自然想到写诗作词。她搜索记忆，脑子里全是些哀怨伤感的诗词，朱淑真似乎从来没有高兴过。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen was a female poet, so naturally Qianli Xun thought of writing poetry. She searched her memory, but her mind was filled with sorrowful, melancholic verses. Zhu Shuzhen never seemed to have been happy.
[ZH] 千里寻走到窗前，望着窗外的月亮。竹林就在月亮升起的方向，她不由得想起竹林，感谢它给她带来了好运。
[EN] Qianli Xun walked to the window and gazed at the moon outside. The bamboo grove was in the direction where the moon rose. She couldn't help but think of the bamboo grove, grateful for the good luck it had brought her.
[ZH] 这么好的竹林被陈浩宇抢走，千里寻心有不甘。以后一定要想办法把竹林要回来，在那里建一座属于自己的竹屋，与心爱的人住在一起，白头偕老。
[EN] Such a beautiful bamboo grove had been taken away by Chen Haoyu. Qianli Xun couldn't accept this. In the future, she had to find a way to get the bamboo grove back, build her own bamboo house there, live with the one she loved, and grow old together.
[ZH] 她自然而然想到文渊。明天过后，他们可以肆无忌惮地在一起享受爱情。千里寻的脸上不由自主地露出了笑容。
[EN] Naturally, she thought of Wenyuan. After tomorrow, they could enjoy their love together without restraint. A smile involuntarily appeared on Qianli Xun's face.
[ZH] 朱淑真的一首诗出现在脑海。这是少有的一首既有境界又不凄婉的诗作，与她此时的心境颇为相符。
[EN] One of Zhu Shuzhen's poems appeared in her mind. It was a rare poem—deep yet not sorrowful—quite fitting for her current mood.
[ZH] 于是走到书桌前坐下，将纸展开，提笔写下：
[EN] So she walked to the desk, sat down, spread out the paper, picked up her brush, and wrote:
[ZH] 百竿高节拂云齐，千亩谁人羡渭溪。
[ZH] 燕雀漫教来唧噪，虚心终待凤凰栖。
[ZH] （《对竹一绝》）
[EN] A hundred tall stalks, their noble joints brushing the clouds,
[EN] A thousand acres—who would envy the Wei River streams?
[EN] Let sparrows and swallows come and chatter noisily,
[EN] The humble bamboo awaits the phoenix to rest.
[EN] ("Quatrain on Bamboo")
[ZH] 写完后，千里寻轻声念着诗句，用心体会，自语道：“朱淑真，我已经替你等到了'凤凰'，他就是文渊。”
[EN] After finishing, Qianli Xun softly recited the poem, feeling it with her heart, and murmured to herself: "Zhu Shuzhen, I've already waited for the 'phoenix' on your behalf. He is Wenyuan."
[ZH] 刚放下笔，苦荞走了进来，手里拿着药瓶。见小姐在写诗，她凑到跟前看了一遍：“小姐的这首新诗跟以前写的不太一样。”
[EN] Just as she put down the brush, Kuqiao walked in, holding a medicine bottle. Seeing her young lady writing poetry, she leaned over to take a look: "This new poem of yours is quite different from your previous ones."
[ZH] 千里寻故意问：“你说说有什么不同？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked deliberately: "Tell me, what's different?"
[ZH] 苦荞走到一旁放下药瓶，说道：“小姐以前写的诗词，让人读了有种伤心的感觉，但这首没有。”
[EN] Kuqiao walked aside to put down the medicine bottle and said: "The poems Young Lady wrote before made people feel sad when reading them, but this one doesn't."
[ZH] 千里寻继续追问：“这首诗给你什么感觉？”
[EN] Qianli Xun continued to ask: "What feeling does this poem give you?"
[ZH] 苦荞想了想：“嗯……让我觉得小姐笔下的竹子很孤傲，燕雀在它身边叽叽喳喳叫，它根本看不上，只因为他在等待凤凰的到来。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought for a moment: "Hmm... it makes me feel that the bamboo in Young Lady's writing is very proud and aloof. The sparrows and swallows chirp noisily beside it, but it pays them no mind, for it awaits the phoenix's arrival."
[ZH] 千里寻赞叹道：“没想到你这丫头也学会品鉴诗词了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun praised: "I didn't expect you to have learned to appreciate poetry, girl."
[ZH] “我经常跟着小姐参加诗会，多少学了一些，不过都是些皮毛而已。”苦荞走到小姐面前，行礼道：“恭喜小姐，明天就要离开姑爷了。”
[EN] "I often follow Young Lady to poetry gatherings, so I've picked up a little, though it's just superficial knowledge." Kuqiao walked in front of her young lady, bowed, and said: "Congratulations, Young Lady. Tomorrow you'll be leaving the young master."
[ZH] “想到明天过后就能重获自由，我高兴得都不知道该做什么了，不如再喝点酒？”
[EN] "Thinking that I'll regain my freedom after tomorrow, I'm so happy I don't know what to do. How about having some more wine?"
[ZH] “还是不要了，今天在宴会上小姐已经喝了不少，再喝恐怕会伤身。”
[EN] "Better not. Young Lady has already drunk quite a lot at the banquet today. Drinking more might harm your health."
[ZH] 千里寻觉得苦荞说得有道理，便放弃了喝酒的念头：“夏果的伤严重吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought Kuqiao had a point, so she gave up the idea of drinking: "Is Xiaguo's injury serious?"
[ZH] “小姐不用担心，我已经给她膝盖上涂了药，很快就好了。”苦荞想起在宴会上见到申简辰的事，“小姐，今天在宴会上我见到了那位去竹林寻找千里寻的公子，只是没见到他身边的跟班。他看上去和乔公子关系不错。”
[EN] "Young Lady doesn't need to worry. I've already applied medicine to her knees. She'll be fine soon." Kuqiao remembered seeing Shen Jianchen at the banquet. "Young Lady, today at the banquet I saw the young master who went to the bamboo grove looking for Qianli Xun, but I didn't see his attendant. He seemed to have a good relationship with Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] “这个人很不一般，他是皇子，听我爹说是仪王。”
[EN] "This person is very extraordinary. He's a prince. I heard from my father that he's Prince Yi."
[ZH] “啊！是皇子？没想到官家的儿子也喜欢听说书。他该不会就是乔贵妃生的儿子吧？”
[EN] "Ah! A prince? I didn't expect a prince to enjoy listening to storytellers. Could he be the son born to Consort Qiao?"
[ZH] “我爹没提这件事，但看他跟文渊关系那么好，应该就是。”
[EN] "My father didn't mention that, but seeing how close he is with Wenyuan, he probably is."
[ZH] “既然是皇子，小姐不如向他承认话本是您写的。”
[EN] "Since he's a prince, why doesn't Young Lady admit to him that you wrote the storytelling scripts?"
[ZH] 千里寻想了想说：“还是算了，你家小姐我是江南女词人，让人知道我偷偷写话本，岂不是丢了你家小姐的人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought for a moment and said: "Forget it. Your young lady is a famous female poet from Jiangnan. If people found out I'd been secretly writing storytelling scripts, wouldn't that embarrass me?"
[ZH] “其实小姐写的话本很有意思，我每次拿给苦蕨的时候，都要先看一遍。比起您的诗词，我更喜欢话本。”
[EN] "Actually, the storytelling scripts Young Lady writes are very interesting. Every time I take them to Kujue, I read them through first. Compared to your poetry, I like the storytelling scripts better."
[ZH] “我写的故事当然不错，谁看了都会喜欢。”
[EN] "Of course the stories I write are good. Anyone who reads them will like them."
[ZH] “不如公开吧，说不定大家会夸小姐厉害，既会写诗词，又会写话本。”
[EN] "Why not make them public? Maybe everyone will praise Young Lady for being talented, able to write both poetry and storytelling scripts."
[ZH] 千里寻想要在这个陌生的时代留下属于自己的痕迹，这样才不会白白穿越一回：“还是算了，话本里的故事只属于千里寻，怎么能借着朱淑真的名字公开呢？”
[EN] Qianli Xun wanted to leave her own mark in this unfamiliar era, so that her time travel wouldn't be in vain: "Forget it. The stories in the storytelling scripts belong only to Qianli Xun. How could I publish them under Zhu Shuzhen's name?"
[ZH] 苦荞已经习惯了小姐时不时把自己看成是两个人：“小姐想怎样就怎样，我都能理解，我也会替小姐保密。”
[EN] Kuqiao was already used to her young lady occasionally treating herself as two different people: "Whatever Young Lady wants is fine. I understand, and I'll keep Young Lady's secret."
[ZH] “连夏果也不能告诉，听到没？”
[EN] "Don't even tell Xiaguo, understand?"
[ZH] “听到了。”苦荞充满担忧，“小姐，恐怕夏果已经知道了。在竹林的时候，有几次您写话本，她就在旁边伺候。”
[EN] "I understand." Kuqiao was full of worry. "Young Lady, I'm afraid Xiaguo already knows. When we were at the bamboo grove, several times when you were writing storytelling scripts, she was attending nearby."
[ZH] “放心，她认不了几个字，根本不知道我写的是什么。以后要谨慎了，因为你已经开始教她识字了。”
[EN] "Don't worry. She can barely read a few characters. She has no idea what I was writing. We need to be more careful in the future, though, since you've already started teaching her to read."
[ZH] “以后我会更加小心。”
[EN] "I'll be even more careful from now on."
[ZH] “最近事情多，没时间写话本。等闲下来再继续写，写好后，由你负责送到说话艺人那里。”
[EN] "Things have been busy lately, so there's no time to write storytelling scripts. When we have free time, I'll continue writing. After it's done, you'll be responsible for delivering it to the storyteller."
[ZH] “好的，小姐。”
[EN] "Yes, Young Lady."
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 朱家大厅里，朱林两家的人都已经到齐。有知府大人坐镇，林家人不敢多说一句话。林子孝依然代表林家长辈坐在主位，另一边坐着朱晞颜。
[EN] In the main hall of the Zhu residence, people from both the Zhu and Lin families had gathered. With the prefect presiding, the Lin family didn't dare say a word. Lin Zixiao still sat in the seat of honor representing the Lin family elders, with Zhu Xiyan sitting on the other side.
[ZH] 今天的千里寻只需要保持沉默，一切有人替她做主，不用再冲到前面争取自己的利益。
[EN] Today, Qianli Xun only needed to remain silent. Everything had someone to make decisions for her. She didn't need to rush to the front to fight for her own interests.
[ZH] 两家先商量财产分割。
[EN] The two families first discussed the division of property.
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“既然是和离，财产就按照规定划分吧，我朱家没有任何意见。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said: "Since this is a mutual divorce, the property should be divided according to the rules. The Zhu family has no objections."
[ZH] 闫媒婆说：“按照规定，练兵官和少夫人的财产应该对半分。但练兵官的财产没有少夫人带去的嫁妆多，如果对半分，少夫人岂不是吃亏了？”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan said: "According to the rules, the property of the military training officer and the young madam should be divided equally. But the military training officer's property isn't as much as the dowry the young madam brought. If it's divided equally, wouldn't the young madam be at a disadvantage?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“没关系。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said: "It doesn't matter."
[ZH] 知府大人在场，林觉哪敢分走嫁妆，赶紧说：“嫁妆我一分不要，都让淑真带走。既然是和离，大家好聚好散。”
[EN] With the prefect present, how could Lin Jue dare to take any of the dowry? He quickly said: "I don't want a single coin of the dowry. Let Shuzhen take it all with her. Since this is a mutual divorce, let's part on good terms."
[ZH] 千里寻看着林觉，他显得很失落。这一刻她才意识到，原来这家伙确实喜欢朱淑真，不愿意和离也确实是出于真情。但谁让他不懂得如何赢得女词人的心呢。仔细想想，婚姻走到这一步，不是谁对谁错能解释清楚的，关键是两个人不合适。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked at Lin Jue. He seemed very dejected. At this moment, she realized that this guy really did like Zhu Shuzhen, and his unwillingness to divorce was truly out of genuine feelings. But who told him he didn't know how to win a female poet's heart? Thinking carefully, how the marriage reached this point couldn't be explained by right or wrong. The key was that the two people weren't compatible.
[ZH] 林子孝说：“我同意林觉侄儿说的话。他没有福气留住幽栖居士，又怎么能分走嫁妆？孩子们分开，两家还要来往。林家也不希望因为财产的事和朱家产生隔阂。幽栖居士嫁到林家也没过上好日子。我做主，林觉侄儿的财产，凡是幽栖居士看上的，尽管拿去。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao said: “I agree with what my nephew Lin Jue said. He lacks the fortune to keep Youqi Jushi, so how can he divide the dowry? The children are separating, but the two families still need to maintain relations. The Lin family also doesn't want to create a rift with the Zhu family over property. Youqi Jushi didn't live a good life after marrying into the Lin family. I've made my decision—of my nephew Lin Jue's property, whatever Youqi Jushi wants, she can take it.”
[ZH] 林觉和林家长辈开始紧张，担心朱家真的要分走财产。
[EN] Lin Jue and the Lin family elders began to get nervous, worried that the Zhu family really would take away their property.
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“林家如此大度，朱家又怎么能不懂礼数乱占便宜？既然如此，林觉的财产我家女儿一分不要，只带走自家的嫁妆就行。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said: "The Lin family is so generous. How can the Zhu family be so rude as to take advantage? Since that's the case, my daughter won't take a single coin of Lin Jue's property. She'll only take her own dowry."
[ZH] 林家人和林觉都松了口气，纷纷说道：“就按知府大人说的办吧。”
[EN] The Lin family and Lin Jue all breathed a sigh of relief, saying one after another: "Let's do as the prefect says."
[ZH] 闫媒婆把和离的文书摆好：“既然两家已经商量好财产分割，就在和离的文书上签字并按手印吧。”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan laid out the divorce documents: "Since both families have agreed on the property division, please sign the divorce documents and press your fingerprints."
[ZH] 千里寻先起身，走到文书前，提起笔，写下朱淑真的名字时，在心里说道：“朱淑真，我已经替你搞定了离婚，你是不是和我一样高兴？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stood up first, walked to the documents, picked up the brush, and as she wrote Zhu Shuzhen's name, she said in her heart: "Zhu Shuzhen, I've already finalized the divorce for you. Are you as happy as I am?"
[ZH] 她用右手大拇指沾了印泥，在文书上按下了红红的指印。这一刻，她的心情怎么能只用“开心”两个字来形容？
[EN] She dipped her right thumb in the ink paste and pressed a red fingerprint on the document. At this moment, how could her feelings be described by just the word "happy"?
[ZH] 林觉第二个签字按手印。在按下指印的那一刻，他心有不甘。难道就这样放了朱淑真？一切已成定局，根本无法挽回。他觉得自己很奇怪，明明已经想好的事，到了真正和离的时候，心里竟然很不舒服。
[EN] Lin Jue was the second to sign and press his fingerprint. At the moment of pressing his fingerprint, he felt unwilling. Was he really going to let Zhu Shuzhen go just like this? Everything was settled, and there was no way to turn back. He felt strange. He had clearly made up his mind, but when it came time for the actual divorce, he felt strangely uncomfortable.
[ZH] 接着朱晞颜和林子孝也在文书上签字按手印。
[EN] Then Zhu Xiyan and Lin Zixiao also signed and pressed their fingerprints on the documents.
[ZH] 闫媒婆拿起文书，看了一遍：“好了，这婚就这么离了。以后各自安好。我要赶去官府送和离文书，就不在这里打扰了，你们两家再聊聊。”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan picked up the documents and looked them over: "Alright, the divorce is finalized. May you both be well in the future. I need to rush to the government office to deliver the divorce documents, so I won't disturb you any longer. You two families can chat some more."
[ZH] 众人看着闫媒婆离去的背影，每个人的心情大不相同。
[EN] Everyone watched Matchmaker Yan's departing figure, each person's feelings vastly different.

#CH 62
[ZH] 闫媒婆前脚刚走，林觉立刻跪在地上：“岳父大人……不，知府大人，我现在跟淑真离了，身边也没个喜欢的人，能不能向您讨要个人？”
[EN] Matchmaker Yan had just left when Lin Jue immediately dropped to his knees: "Father-in-law... no, Prefect. Now that Shuzhen and I have divorced, I don't have anyone I like by my side. Could I ask you for someone?"
[ZH] 林子孝批评道：“林觉侄儿，你这是做什么？想要媳妇儿，等回去后，林家长辈会帮你寻，怎么能在这里向知府大人讨要？”
[EN] Lin Zixiao scolded: "Nephew Lin Jue, what are you doing? If you want a wife, wait until we get back. The Lin family elders will help you find one. How can you ask the Prefect for someone right here?"
[ZH] 千里寻本来还有些怜悯林觉，他这么一跪，反而让她觉得讨厌，男儿膝下有黄金，这人说跪就跪，实在不像个男人。
[EN] Qianli Xun had felt some pity for Lin Jue, but seeing him kneel like this only turned her pity to disgust. A man's knees are precious as gold—yet this fellow dropped to the ground without a second thought. He really wasn't acting like a man.
[ZH] 朱晞颜倒没有生气，毕竟林觉对他一直毕恭毕敬，女儿跟他没处好，这位曾经的女婿与他倒是相处良好。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan wasn't angry. After all, Lin Jue had always been respectful to him. His daughter hadn't gotten along with him, but this former son-in-law had actually maintained a good relationship with him.
[ZH] “起来说，不必跪着。”
[EN] "Get up and speak. No need to kneel."
[ZH] 林觉坚持道：“知府大人不答应，我就不起来。”
[EN] Lin Jue insisted: "If the Prefect doesn't agree, I won't get up."
[ZH] 朱晞颜问：“那得看你要什么人，说出来，我衡量衡量才知要不要答应。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan asked: "That depends on who you're asking for. Tell me, and I'll weigh it before deciding whether to agree."
[ZH] 林觉说：“请知府大人劝说淑真，让她把身边的丫鬟夏果送给我当妾室。”
[EN] Lin Jue said: "Please persuade Shuzhen, Prefect. Let her give me her maid Xiaguo as a concubine."
[ZH] 朱晞颜笑道：“呵呵，不就是讨要个丫鬟，还需跪着？快起来，我同意就是。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan laughed: "Heh, it's just asking for a maid. Why do you need to kneel? Get up. I agree."
[ZH] 林觉起身谢道：“感谢知府大人，我想今日就把夏果带回去。”
[EN] Lin Jue got up and thanked him: "Thank you, Prefect. I'd like to take Xiaguo back with me today."
[ZH] 朱晞颜转向女儿：“淑真，你大方些，今日就让他把你身边的丫鬟带走。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan turned to his daughter: "Shuzhen, be generous. Let him take your maid away today."
[ZH] 千里寻为难道：“爹，在林家时，林觉提过同样的要求，可夏果不乐意，我又不能逼她，不如让林觉重找一个，临安的女子多的是，又何必只要夏果？对不对？练兵官？”
[EN] Qianli Xun said with difficulty: "Father, when we were at the Lin residence, Lin Jue made the same request. But Xiaguo didn't want to, and I couldn't force her. Why not let Lin Jue find someone else? There are plenty of women in Lin'an. Why insist on only Xiaguo? Right, Military Training Officer?"
[ZH] 林觉清楚，在座的所有人绝对不会把夏果当回事，他更加坚定地说：“我只要夏果。”
[EN] Lin Jue knew that no one present would take Xiaguo seriously. He said more firmly: "I only want Xiaguo."
[ZH] 朱晞颜说：“来人！”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan said: "Someone!"
[ZH] 进来一名下人：“老爷，有何吩咐？”
[EN] A servant came in: "Master, what are your orders?"
[ZH] “去把丫鬟夏果找来，就说我有话要问。”
[EN] "Go find the maid Xiaguo. Tell her I have questions for her."
[ZH] 下人出去后，韦嬷嬷悄悄说：“姑娘还是同意吧？也算给夏果找个好人家。”
[EN] After the servant left, Nanny Wei whispered: "Young lady, perhaps you should agree? It would be finding a good home for Xiaguo."
[ZH] 千里寻小声回道：“需尊重夏果的意思，看她来了，当着这么多人的面会是什么态度。”
[EN] Qianli Xun whispered back: "We need to respect Xiaguo's wishes. Let's see what her attitude is when she comes, in front of all these people."
[ZH] 夏果腿上的伤昨晚已无碍，正在园子跟苦荞学习认字，听到老爷唤她，急急匆匆跟着传话的下人向朱府厅堂而去。
[EN] Xiaguo's leg injury had healed last night. She was in the garden learning characters with Kuqiao when she heard the master was calling for her. She hurriedly followed the messenger servant toward the Zhu residence's main hall.
[ZH] 一路上，猜测不出是何缘故。她只知朱家老爷正在厅堂处理小姐与姑爷和离之事，唤她前去，难道是小姐需要人伺候？若是小姐唤她，为何传话的人非要说老爷唤她问话？
[EN] Along the way, she couldn't guess the reason. She only knew the Zhu family master was handling the young lady and young master's mutual divorce in the hall. If they were calling her, was it because the young lady needed someone to attend to her? If the young lady was calling, why did the messenger insist it was the master who wanted to question her?
[ZH] 夏果长这么大，从来没见过什么大官，昨日在宴会中，看过几眼那些官员，也看过老爷，远远观察，已觉十分威严，竟然要当面向她问话，夏果越想越感到紧张。
[EN] Xiaguo had never seen any high officials in her life. At yesterday's banquet, she had caught a few glimpses of those officials, and also seen the master from afar. Even from a distance, he seemed very imposing. Now he was going to question her in person. The more Xiaguo thought about it, the more nervous she became.
[ZH] 她带着疑惑和紧张感来到厅堂，一屋子人都看向她，她更加紧张，不由跪下，不敢说一句话。
[EN] She arrived at the hall filled with confusion and nervousness. Everyone in the room looked at her, making her even more nervous. She couldn't help but kneel, not daring to say a word.
[ZH] 朱晞颜仔细瞧夏果，立刻明白林觉为何非夏果不要，这丫鬟的身段与淑真有几分相似，模样也生得好。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan studied Xiaguo carefully and immediately understood why Lin Jue insisted on having Xiaguo. The maid's figure was somewhat similar to Shuzhen's, and she was also quite pretty.
[ZH] “你就是夏果？”
[EN] "You are Xiaguo?"
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes."
[ZH] “家住哪里？”
[EN] "Where is your home?"
[ZH] “乡下。”
[EN] "The countryside."
[ZH] “你既然给朱家姑娘当丫鬟，以后的亲事由朱家说了算，林练兵官看上你，是你的福气，我已答应将你许给他，今日你跟着他去林家吧，当个妾室，也不亏。”
[EN] "Since you serve as a maid for the Zhu family's young lady, your future marriage will be decided by the Zhu family. Military Training Officer Lin has taken a liking to you. It's your good fortune. I've already agreed to give you to him. Follow him to the Lin residence today. Being a concubine isn't a bad deal."
[ZH] 听到这话，夏果如同五雷轰顶，她心里刚有喜欢的人，就要被无缘无故许配给他人，她根本不乐意，何况还是去当妾室？
[EN] Hearing this, Xiaguo felt as if five thunderbolts had struck her down. She had just found someone she liked in her heart, and now she was being married off to someone else for no reason. She wanted no part of it—let alone becoming some man's concubine.
[ZH] 刚进厅堂时的紧张感完全消失，向小姐求救：“小姐，您是知道的，我不想跟着姑爷，想陪您一辈子。”
[EN] The nervousness she felt when first entering the hall completely disappeared. She pleaded with her young lady: "Young lady, you know I don't want to follow the young master. I want to stay with you for life."
[ZH] 千里寻对朱晞颜说：“爹，我看还是算了，夏果根本不乐意，我不想强人所难。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said to Zhu Xiyan: "Father, I think we should forget it. Xiaguo clearly doesn't want to. I don't want to force someone against their will."
[ZH] “就这么定了，让练兵官把她带走，以后不要让我在朱府再见到她。”
[EN] "It's settled. Let the Military Training Officer take her away. I don't want to see her at the Zhu residence ever again."
[ZH] 朱晞颜厉声说完，起身离开厅堂，朱家族人也都跟着离开。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan spoke sternly, then got up and left the hall. The Zhu family clan members all followed him out.
[ZH] 林子孝起身对林觉说：“侄儿，我们先走了，剩下的事你自己处理吧。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao got up and said to Lin Jue: "Nephew, we're leaving first. Handle the rest yourself."
[ZH] 林家的族人也都一一离去。
[EN] The Lin family clan members also left one by one.
[ZH] 向夫人劝道：“夏果，老爷都发话，你跟着练兵官走吧，林家也是好人家，亏不了你。”
[EN] Madam Xiang persuaded: "Xiaguo, the master has spoken. Follow the Military Training Officer. The Lin family is a good family. You won't be treated badly."
[ZH] 夏果哭道：“呜呜……！夫人，我不喜欢练兵官，我不想跟着他，更不想当他的妾室。呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo cried: "Wuwu... Madam, I don't like the Military Training Officer. I don't want to follow him, and I definitely don't want to be his concubine. Wuwu..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“错过练兵官，你以后不一定能找到比他更好的，还是乖乖跟着他去吧。”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "If you miss the Military Training Officer, you might not find someone better later. Just obediently follow him."
[ZH] 夏果哭着对小姐说：“小姐，呜呜……！您不是说不会把我送给姑爷吗？为何又变卦？难道您为了自己能和离，不顾及我的幸福！呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo cried to her young lady: "Young lady, wuwu... Didn't you say you wouldn't give me to the young master? Why have you changed your mind? Are you so eager to get your own divorce that you don't care about my happiness? Wuwu..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷厉声批评：“夏果！闭上你的嘴！你是丫鬟，哪有资格说姑娘的不是！”
[EN] Nanny Wei scolded sharply: "Xiaguo! Shut your mouth! You're just a maid. What right do you have to criticize the young lady?"
[ZH] 夏果不敢再言语，只顾伤心地哭着。
[EN] Xiaguo didn't dare speak again. She just continued crying sadly.
[ZH] 千里寻思忖该如何处理此事，思来想去，关键还在林觉。
[EN] Qianli Xun pondered how to handle this matter. After thinking it through, the key was still Lin Jue.
[ZH] 林觉安然坐着，已没有刚才的失落，反而一副得意的样子，千里寻很是不爽。
[EN] Lin Jue sat there at ease, all trace of dejection gone. Instead, he wore a thoroughly smug expression. Qianli Xun was seething inside.
[ZH] 向夫人在此，她不好展示泼妇的一面。
[EN] With Madam Xiang present, she couldn't show her shrewish side.
[ZH] “娘，您跟嬷嬷去休息吧，剩下的事，我来处理，放心，定会让大家满意。”
[EN] "Mother, you and Nanny go rest. I'll handle the rest. Don't worry, I'll make sure everyone is satisfied."
[ZH] 向夫人担心女儿，犹豫要不要离开。韦嬷嬷拉起她：“夫人，我们走吧，姑娘既说能处理好，您只管信她。”
[EN] Madam Xiang worried about her daughter, hesitating whether to leave. Nanny Wei pulled her up: "Madam, let's go. Since the young lady said she can handle it, just trust her."
[ZH] 向夫人才放心与韦嬷嬷离开。
[EN] Only then did Madam Xiang feel reassured enough to leave with Nanny Wei.
[ZH] 夏果跪在地上哭得更伤心：“呜呜……！姑爷为何老盯着我不放？”
[EN] Xiaguo knelt on the ground, crying even more sadly: "Wuwu... Why does the young master keep targeting me?"
[ZH] 千里寻强调道：“以后不要再称呼他姑爷，我已跟他和离。”
[EN] Qianli Xun emphasized: "Don't call him 'young master' anymore. I've already divorced him."
[ZH] 林觉站起来，绕着夏果转了一圈：“谁让你长得好看，又跟你家小姐身段有几分相似，我得不到你家小姐，把你放在身边也不错。朱淑真，知府大人都同意，你干脆些，让我把她带走。”
[EN] Lin Jue stood up and walked around Xiaguo in a circle: "Who told you to be pretty? Your figure is somewhat similar to your young lady's. Since I can't have your young lady, having you by my side isn't bad either. Zhu Shuzhen, even the Prefect has agreed. Just be straightforward and let me take her away."
[ZH] 林觉的话，千里寻听着极其不舒服，她更不情愿将夏果拱手相让。
[EN] Lin Jue's words made Qianli Xun extremely uncomfortable. She was even less willing to hand Xiaguo over.
[ZH] “林觉，今日你我和离本是一桩美事，你偏偏当着众人的面提出这等无理要求，你也看到，夏果不乐意。告诉你，我也不乐意，你要是知趣的话，赶紧离开朱府，否则别怪老娘不客气！”
[EN] "Lin Jue, our divorce today was supposed to be a clean break. Yet you insist on making such an unreasonable demand in front of everyone. You've seen that Xiaguo doesn't want to. Let me tell you, I don't want it either. If you know what's good for you, get out of the Zhu residence now. Otherwise, don't blame this lady for losing her temper!"
[ZH] 林觉强调道：“你爹，知府大人，都已同意，还说以后不想在朱府见到她，难道你没听见？”
[EN] Lin Jue emphasized: "Your father, the Prefect, has already agreed. He even said he doesn't want to see her at the Zhu residence anymore. Didn't you hear?"
[ZH] 千里寻也强调道：“我爹是我爹，我是我，夏果是我的丫鬟，又不是我爹身边的下人，她以后嫁给谁，老娘说了算！夏果，你起来，不必再跪着，跟我走！”
[EN] Qianli Xun emphasized right back: “My father is my father, and I am me. Xiaguo is my maid, not my father's servant. Who she marries from now on—I call the shots! Xiaguo, get up. No more kneeling. Come with me！”
[ZH] 夏果起身，擦干眼泪，跟着小姐离开朱府厅堂。
[EN] Xiaguo got up, wiped her tears, and followed her young lady out of the Zhu residence's main hall.
[ZH] 林觉对着朱淑真的背影骂道：“走着瞧！”
[EN] Lin Jue cursed at Zhu Shuzhen's retreating back: "We'll see about this!"
[ZH] 骂完将周围瞧一遍，没有一个人，才甩了甩袖子，放心离开。
[EN] After cursing, he looked around. No one was there. Only then did he flick his sleeves and leave with peace of mind.

#CH 63
[ZH] 回到园子，夏果担心地说：“小姐，老爷不愿在朱府见到我，我继续待在这里恐怕引起老爷不满。”
[EN] Back at the garden, Xiaguo said worriedly: "Young lady, the master doesn't want to see me at the Zhu residence. If I stay here, I'm afraid I'll anger him."
[ZH] 千里寻为难道：“要不我把你送回乡下？”
[EN] Qianli Xun said with difficulty: "Should I send you back to the countryside?"
[ZH] 夏果急了，她才不愿回去，倘若回去，恐怕往后再也见不到乔公子。
[EN] Xiaguo panicked. She didn't want to go back at all. If she returned, she'd probably never see Young Master Qiao again.
[ZH] 她哭着跪下：“不！小姐！我不能回乡下，那些地痞流氓如果见到我，还会告诉陈公子，没有小姐护着，我只有死路一条。”
[EN] She knelt down crying: "No! Young lady! I can't go back to the countryside. If those local thugs see me, they'll tell Young Master Chen. Without your protection, I'm as good as dead."
[ZH] “你起来吧，既然不想回去，继续跟着我。朱府你是待不下去，我爹已放话，需给他面子。不如这样，韦嬷嬷已给我在外买了一处宅子，你先过去，负责把宅子收拾收拾，说不定哪天我会搬过去住。”
[EN] “Get up. Since you don't want to go back, stay with me. You can't stay at the Zhu residence anymore. My father has made his position clear, and I need to respect that. How about this—Nanny Wei bought me a residence outside. You go there first and tidy it up. Maybe one day I'll move in.”
[ZH] 千里寻刚说完，韦嬷嬷走进园子，见夏果正要起身，不满道：“继续跪着！”
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun finished speaking, Nanny Wei walked into the garden. Seeing Xiaguo about to get up, she said displeased: "Stay on your knees!"
[ZH] 夏果只好再跪下。
[EN] Xiaguo had no choice but to kneel again.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷训道：“姑娘收你在身边是伺候她，可你给姑娘带来多少麻烦？她一个主子没享你几天福，尽忙着给你处理这些子事，要我看，姑娘还是放她回乡下吧。”
[EN] Nanny Wei scolded: "The young lady took you in to serve her, but how much trouble have you caused her? She's your mistress, yet she's barely had a few days of your service. She's been busy dealing with your problems. If you ask me, the young lady should just send you back to the countryside."
[ZH] 夏果哭道：“呜呜……！我也不想给姑娘添麻烦，我不找事，事却来找我，只怪我生得这模样，我这就拿剪刀在脸上划一道，往后也就清净！呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo cried: "Wuwu... I don't want to cause the young lady trouble either. I don't go looking for trouble, but trouble finds me. It's all because I was born looking like this. I'll take scissors and slash my face right now. Then everything will be peaceful! Wuwu..."
[ZH] 夏果哭着起身往屋里冲，准备找剪刀毁了容貌。
[EN] Xiaguo got up crying and rushed toward the room, ready to find scissors to ruin her looks.
[ZH] 千里寻一把抓住她：“行了！这样闹有什么意思？长得漂亮是好事，错只错在像林觉和陈浩宇那等好色之徒。你既跟着我，我有责任罩着你，回屋收拾收拾，准备去新宅子。”
[EN] Qianli Xun grabbed her: "Enough! What's the point of making such a scene? Being pretty is a good thing. The only fault lies with lustful men like Lin Jue and Chen Haoyu. Since you're following me, I have a responsibility to have your back. Go back to your room and pack. Get ready to go to the new residence."
[ZH] 被小姐拉住，夏果心里当然高兴，如果真的毁了容貌，以后可怎么见乔公子？她谢过小姐，回屋去收拾东西。
[EN] Being held back by her young lady, Xiaguo was naturally relieved. If she really ruined her looks, how could she ever face Young Master Qiao? She thanked her young lady and went back to pack her things.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“姑娘就是心善，你既然对夏果已有安顿，我也不多说什么，让她去新买的宅子待着倒是个办法，既不会让老爷看着碍眼，也好有人过去把宅子负责收拾一下。不过，老爷都答应的事，恐怕不容易糊弄过去。”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "The young lady is too kind-hearted. Since you've already made arrangements for Xiaguo, I won't say more. Letting her stay at the newly bought residence is a solution. It won't offend the master, and someone will be there to tidy up the place. However, since the master has already agreed, I'm afraid it won't be easy to fool him."
[ZH] “我说过，不想强人所难，既然夏果不乐意，我又怎能将她拱手送给林觉？让她去新宅子待着，我爹见不到她，也许会渐渐忘记此事。”
[EN] "I've said I don't want to force someone against their will. Since Xiaguo doesn't want to, how could I just hand her over to Lin Jue? Let her stay at the new residence. My father won't see her there, and maybe he'll gradually forget about this."
[ZH] “恐怕没那么容易。”
[EN] "I'm afraid it won't be that easy."
[ZH] “走一步看一步，我就不相信，林觉还能强抢民女？”
[EN] "We'll cross that bridge when we come to it. I don't believe Lin Jue would forcibly take a commoner's daughter."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见姑娘坚决，也就默认：“夫人说了，老爷允许姑娘继续住在朱府，若姑娘不愿意，也可搬出去。”
[EN] Seeing the young lady's determination, Nanny Wei acquiesced: "Madam said the master allows you to continue living at the Zhu residence. If you don't want to, you can move out."
[ZH] 千里寻从心底乐意住在朱府，只因她已把朱淑真的亲人当成自己的亲人。在先进的时代，她从未体验过有多个亲人在身边的感觉，既然在陌生的时代有了亲人，不如好好体验一下亲情。
[EN] Qianli Xun was genuinely happy to stay at the Zhu residence because she had come to treat Zhu Shuzhen's family as her own. In the modern era, she had never experienced having multiple family members around her. Since she now had family in this unfamiliar era, she might as well fully experience the warmth of family.
[ZH] “我想先在朱府住下，以后再说。”
[EN] "I want to stay at the Zhu residence for now. We'll see what happens later."
[ZH] “姑娘想怎样，我都没意见。”
[EN] "Whatever the young lady wants, I have no objections."
[ZH] 这时夏果拿着包袱出来行礼，韦嬷嬷叮嘱道：“你去夫人院中，找我身边的小厮，他认识去新宅的路，让他把你送过去，就说是我说的。新宅里有几个下人，你去后，安排他们每日把宅子收拾收拾，说不定哪天姑娘会过去住。”
[EN] Just then, Xiaguo came out with a bundle and bowed. Nanny Wei instructed: "Go to Madam's courtyard and find my page boy. He knows the way to the new residence. Have him take you there. Tell him I sent you. There are a few servants at the new residence. Once you're there, arrange for them to tidy up the place daily. The young lady might move in someday."
[ZH] 夏果继续行礼：“谢谢小姐，谢谢嬷嬷。”
[EN] Xiaguo bowed again: "Thank you, young lady. Thank you, Nanny."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷继续叮嘱：“记着小姐的恩情，以后不要做对不起小姐的事。”
[EN] Nanny Wei continued to instruct: "Remember the young lady's kindness. Don't do anything to betray her in the future."
[ZH] “嬷嬷放心，小姐是这个世上除了家人外对我最好的人，我绝不会做对不起她的事。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Nanny. The young lady is the best person to me in this world besides my family. I would never do anything to betray her."
[ZH] “知道就好，去吧。”
[EN] "Good that you know. Go now."
[ZH] 夏果离开后，韦嬷嬷又聊了一会儿才离开园子。
[EN] After Xiaguo left, Nanny Wei chatted for a while longer before leaving the garden.
[ZH] 剩下千里寻一人，没有任何事情打扰，心中对文渊的感情再次激荡着，她想尽快把和离的好消息告诉他。
[EN] Left alone, with nothing to disturb her, Qianli Xun's feelings for Wenyuan surged again. She wanted to tell him the good news about the divorce as soon as possible.
[ZH] 四下瞅瞅，没见苦荞的身影，这丫头怎么半天不见人影儿？
[EN] She looked around but didn't see Kuqiao. Where had that girl been all this time?
[ZH] 既找不到苦荞，干脆休息一会儿。千里寻坐到藤椅上，闭上眼睛，想着第一次见文渊的情景。
[EN] Since she couldn't find Kuqiao, she decided to rest for a while. Qianli Xun sat down on the rattan chair, closed her eyes, and thought about the first time she met Wenyuan.
[ZH] 绿桃从外面进来，带来许多丫鬟，大家手里拿着灯笼，一进园子，开始到处挂灯笼。
[EN] Lvtao came in from outside, bringing many maids. Everyone held lanterns in their hands. As soon as they entered the garden, they started hanging lanterns everywhere.
[ZH] 绿桃见小姐坐在藤椅上，双目微闭，一副悠闲的样子，上前行礼：“小姐，您上次回来时，让给园子多挂些灯笼，我一直忙，没抽出空来。小姐这回应该要在朱府常住，我今日带丫鬟过来把灯笼挂上。”
[EN] Lvtao saw her young lady sitting on the rattan chair with eyes half-closed, looking relaxed. She stepped forward and bowed: "Young lady, last time you came back, you asked to hang more lanterns in the garden. I've been busy and haven't had the time. Since you'll be living at the Zhu residence this time, I brought maids today to hang the lanterns."
[ZH] 千里寻继续微闭双目：“让你费心，你可见到苦荞？”
[EN] Qianli Xun kept her eyes half-closed: "Thank you for your thoughtfulness. Have you seen Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “我来的路上遇到苦荞，她说乔公子身边的南双正在府外等着，她出去瞧瞧。”
[EN] "I ran into Kuqiao on my way here. She said Nanshuang, who serves Young Master Qiao, is waiting outside the residence. She went out to check."
[ZH] 听到绿桃的话，千里寻睁开眼睛，露出笑容，肯定是文渊有话捎来。
[EN] Hearing Lvtao's words, Qianli Xun opened her eyes and smiled. Wenyuan must have sent a message.
[ZH] 灯笼很快挂完，绿桃拜别后带着下人离开。
[EN] The lanterns were soon hung. Lvtao took her leave and left with the servants.
[ZH] 千里寻又在藤椅上坐一会儿，猜测着文渊会捎来什么话。
[EN] Qianli Xun sat on the rattan chair a while longer, wondering what message Wenyuan had sent.
[ZH] 正猜测着，苦荞回来。她一跨进园子，千里寻充满期待地问：“文渊可有话传来？”
[EN] Just as she was guessing, Kuqiao returned. As soon as she stepped into the garden, Qianli Xun asked expectantly: "Did Wenyuan send a message?"
[ZH] 苦荞诧异道：“小姐，您真神了，怎么知道乔公子有话传来？”
[EN] Kuqiao said in surprise: "Young lady, you're amazing! How did you know Young Master Qiao sent a message?"
[ZH] 千里寻催道：“别问那么多，快说！”
[EN] Qianli Xun urged: "Don't ask so many questions. Just tell me!"
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“乔公子让南双传话，明日约小姐去游湖。”
[EN] Kuqiao replied: "Young Master Qiao asked Nanshuang to deliver a message. He's inviting you to go boating on the lake tomorrow."
[ZH] “什么时辰？在什么地方？”
[EN] "What time? Where?"
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐着急的样子，直想笑：“未时，西湖见。”
[EN] Seeing her young lady's anxious expression, Kuqiao wanted to laugh: “The hour of wei—between one and three in the afternoon—at West Lake.”
[ZH] 千里寻从藤椅上蹦起来，一把抱住苦荞：“苦荞，你知道吗？我爱上文渊！”
[EN] Qianli Xun bounced up from the rattan chair and hugged Kuqiao: "Kuqiao, you know what? I've fallen in love with Wenyuan!"
[ZH] “知道知道，我都知道，小姐早该如此。”
[EN] "I know, I know. I've known all along. The young lady should have done this sooner."
[ZH] 千里寻放开苦荞：“从今日起，我会有新的人生，都是竹林给我带来的好运。”
[EN] Qianli Xun let go of Kuqiao: "From today on, I'll have a new life. It's all thanks to the good luck the bamboo grove brought me."
[ZH] 苦荞提醒道：“还有系在柳树上的红绸。”
[EN] Kuqiao reminded her: "And the red silk tied on the willow tree."
[ZH] 在乡下一个月，千里寻满心满眼只有竹林。从竹林回来，又是遇到爱情，又是宴会，又是和离之事，搞得她将神柳差点忘记。苦荞的话提醒了她，也勾起异样的情愫。
[EN] During her month in the countryside, Qianli Xun's heart and mind were filled only with the bamboo grove. After returning from the bamboo grove, there was finding love, the banquet, and the divorce—she'd nearly forgotten about the Divine Willow. Kuqiao's words reminded her and stirred strange emotions.
[ZH] “随我去看看神柳。”
[EN] "Come with me to see the Divine Willow."
[ZH] 主仆二人走出朱府，乘坐马车，出了城，来到山脚下。
[EN] The mistress and maid left the Zhu residence, took a carriage, and went out of the city to the foot of the mountain.
[ZH] 下了马车后，她们绕着山下的路往神柳处走去。
[EN] After getting off the carriage, they walked along the path at the foot of the mountain toward the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 当池塘和神柳出现在眼前时，千里寻想起申简辰。他到底在哪里？如果他穿越而来，是否像自己一样遇到爱情？
[EN] When the pond and the Divine Willow appeared before her, Qianli Xun thought of Shen Jianchen. Where exactly was he? If he had traveled through time, had he also found love like she did?
[ZH] 千里寻望着挂在神柳高处的红绸，它随风飘动，像一面旗帜。如若申简辰来到神柳之下，会不会也像她一样激动？会不会抬头看到她绑在高处的红绸？
[EN] Qianli Xun looked at the red silk hung high on the Divine Willow. It fluttered in the wind like a banner, a silent testament to her hopes. If Shen Jianchen came to the Divine Willow, would he be as excited as she was? Would he look up and see the red silk she had tied high up?
[ZH] 千里寻梦到的爱人是申简辰，可现实却让她遇到文渊。如若申简辰此刻出现在她面前，问她：“千里寻，你愿不愿意嫁给我？”她该如何回答？
[EN] The lover Qianli Xun had dreamed of was Shen Jianchen, but reality had her meet Wenyuan. If Shen Jianchen appeared before her right now and asked, "Qianli Xun, would you be willing to marry me?" how should she answer?
[ZH] 很快，千里寻已有答案，她一定会说：“申简辰，你来晚了，如果你早来一步，我定会答应你。”
[EN] Soon, Qianli Xun already had her answer. She would definitely say: "Shen Jianchen, you're too late. If you had come a step earlier, I would have agreed."
[ZH] 身后传来一个声音：“幽栖居士怎么会在这里？”
[EN] A voice came from behind: "How is Youqi Jushi here?"

#CH 64
[ZH] 千里寻转过身，仪王出现在面前，她感到有些恍惚，为何此刻会对他产生莫名的熟悉感？
[EN] Qianli Xun turned around. Prince Yi appeared before her. She felt somewhat dazed—why did she feel this inexplicable sense of familiarity with him at this moment?
[ZH] 直到看见他身旁的乔姑娘，现在应该称她为佳和郡主，她手里拿着红绸，面带笑容盯着自己，千里寻才清醒过来。
[EN] Only when she saw the young lady Qiao beside him—now she should call her Princess Jiahe—holding red silk in her hand and watching her with a smile did Qianli Xun come to her senses.
[ZH] 看样子他们刚从后山下来，红绸应该是从尼姑庵求的。
[EN] Judging by appearances, they had just come down from the back mountain. The red silk was probably obtained from the nunnery.
[ZH] 千里寻屈膝行礼：“仪王、佳和郡主，没想到在这里也能碰到二位贵人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun curtsied and bowed: "Prince Yi, Princess Jiahe, I didn't expect to run into Your Highnesses here."
[ZH] 乔梦容面带微笑：“看来，你已知道我与哥哥的身份，知道也无妨，但不能因为我们的身份，你就生分，希望幽栖居士把我和哥哥当朋友一样看待。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong smiled: "It seems you already know my brother's and my identity. Knowing is fine, but you shouldn't keep a distance because of who we are. I hope Youqi Jushi will treat my brother and me as friends."
[ZH] 穿越而来有了家人，还缺朋友，两位贵人愿意屈尊与她做朋友，千里寻很是乐意。
[EN] Having gained family after traveling through time, she still lacked friends. The two nobles were willing to condescend to be her friends, and Qianli Xun was more than happy.
[ZH] “只要二位贵人不嫌弃，我当然愿意与你们成为朋友。”
[EN] "As long as Your Highnesses don't despise me, I'd naturally be willing to become friends with you."
[ZH] 乔梦容说：“当然不嫌弃，是不是哥哥？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong said: "Of course we don't despise you. Isn't that right, brother?"
[ZH] 申简辰回道：“梦容不嫌弃，我又怎会嫌弃？再说幽栖居士是文渊的好友，也就是我与梦容的好友。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen replied: "If Mengrong doesn't despise her, how could I? Besides, Youqi Jushi is Wenyuan's good friend, which makes her my and Mengrong's good friend too."
[ZH] 两位贵人平易近人，千里寻轻松许多，刚刚因地位悬殊产生的距离感荡然无存，反而羡慕起佳和郡主与仪王的感情。
[EN] The two nobles were approachable, and Qianli Xun relaxed considerably. The sense of distance just created by their vast difference in status vanished completely. Instead, she found herself envying the relationship between Princess Jiahe and Prince Yi.
[ZH] 仪王的话语中流露出对佳和郡主迁就之意，在千里寻看来，只有一个男人深爱着一个女人，才懂得处处迁就对方。
[EN] Prince Yi's words revealed an accommodating attitude toward Princess Jiahe. In Qianli Xun's view, only a man who deeply loves a woman knows how to accommodate her in every way.
[ZH] “你们是要在神柳上系红绸吗？”
[EN] "Are you going to tie red silk on the Divine Willow?"
[ZH] 乔梦容回道：“是的，今日哥哥无事，专程陪我到尼姑庵请了条红绸，上面写下我和哥哥的名字，现在我与哥哥要一起把红绸系在神柳的枝条上。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong replied: "Yes. Today my brother had nothing to do, so he specially accompanied me to the nunnery to get a red silk. We wrote our names on it, and now my brother and I are going to tie the red silk together on the branches of the Divine Willow."
[ZH] 申简辰心想，红绸上写着“赵朴”二字，他就当是替赵朴来挂红绸，希望赵朴在天有灵，到月老处走一趟，给他的妹妹重新牵一条婚姻的红线。
[EN] Shen Jianchen thought to himself: the red silk had "Zhao Pu" written on it. He would treat this as hanging the red silk on behalf of Zhao Pu. He hoped that if Zhao Pu had a spirit in heaven, he would visit the Matchmaker God and tie a new red thread of marriage for his sister.
[ZH] 千里寻目睹佳和郡主牵起仪王的手靠近神柳。
[EN] Qianli Xun watched Princess Jiahe take Prince Yi's hand and approach the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 喜兰先挑好一根柳枝，佳和郡主并不满意。仪王个高，用另一只手从较高处拉下一根。佳和郡主很满意，放开仪王的手，亲自将红绸在仪王拽下的柳枝上绑好，二人相视而笑。
[EN] Xilan first picked a willow branch, but Princess Jiahe wasn't satisfied. Prince Yi was tall, so he used his other hand to pull down a higher one. Princess Jiahe was very pleased. She let go of Prince Yi's hand and personally tied the red silk on the branch Prince Yi had pulled down. The two looked at each other and smiled.
[ZH] 千里寻叹道：“二位的感情真令人羡慕。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sighed: "Your relationship is truly enviable."
[ZH] 乔梦容今日很开心，哥哥如此配合，她似乎找回从前的感觉。听到幽栖居士的慨叹，回应道：“乔公子对幽栖居士的感情也令梦容羡慕不已。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong was very happy today. Her brother was so cooperative that she seemed to have recaptured the feelings of the past. Hearing Youqi Jushi's sigh, she responded: "Young Master Qiao's feelings for Youqi Jushi also make Mengrong extremely envious."
[ZH] 申简辰放开柳枝，那柳枝向上浮去，将红绸带到较高处，可离最高处的红绸还差得很远。
[EN] Shen Jianchen let go of the willow branch. The branch floated upward, taking the red silk to a higher position, but it was still far from the highest red silk.
[ZH] 乔梦容望向高处：“哥哥，为何有人将红绸挂得那么高？不知是什么人这么厉害？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong looked up: "Brother, why did someone hang the red silk so high? I wonder who is so capable?"
[ZH] 听到佳和郡主问题，苦荞心想：“当然是我家小姐，只有她有这等本事。”
[EN] Hearing Princess Jiahe's question, Kuqiao thought to herself: "Of course it's my young lady. Only she has such ability."
[ZH] 申简辰也瞧向高处：“估计是不想被人摘去，才挂那么高。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen also looked up: "Probably hung it that high so no one would take it down."
[ZH] 乔梦容担心起自己刚挂的红绸：“我们系的红绸会不会被别人拽走？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong worried about the red silk they had just hung: "Will someone pull away the red silk we tied?"
[ZH] 申简辰宽慰道：“应该不会，这么多红绸，也没见有人把它们拽走。你要是担心，可以每日过来检查一番，一旦被人拽走，大不了我再带你到尼姑庵求一条，下次绑得更高些。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen reassured her: "Probably not. There are so many red silks here, and I haven't seen anyone take them down. If you're worried, you can come check every day. If someone does take it down, at worst I'll take you back to the nunnery to get another one, and we'll tie it even higher next time."
[ZH] “像那条红绸一样高，可以吗？”
[EN] "As high as that red silk, is that okay?"
[ZH] “可以。”
[EN] "That's fine."
[ZH] 千里寻听着二人的对话，问道：“仪王难道会轻功？”
[EN] Listening to their conversation, Qianli Xun asked: "Does Prince Yi know qinggong?"
[ZH] 申简辰疑惑地问：“你怎么知道我会轻功？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked suspiciously: "How do you know I know qinggong?"
[ZH] 千里寻解释道：“能把红绸挂得那么高，不会轻功的人很难实现。”
[EN] Qianli Xun explained: "Only someone who knows qinggong could hang the red silk that high. It would be very difficult for someone without that skill."
[ZH] 申简辰稍有疑惑：“难道幽栖居士也会轻功？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was slightly puzzled: "Could it be that Youqi Jushi also knows qinggong?"
[ZH] 千里寻隐瞒道：“我……我……我一介女流，怎么可能会那玩意儿。”
[EN] Qianli Xun concealed the truth: "I... I... I'm just a woman. How could I possibly know that kind of thing?"
[ZH] 申简辰来到幽栖居士面前：“可我听说幽栖居士偷偷练习功夫，为了和离，宁可被练兵官打伤，都不还一拳。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen came before Youqi Jushi: "But I heard that Youqi Jushi secretly practiced martial arts. For the sake of mutual divorce, you'd rather be beaten by the Military Training Officer than throw a single punch in return."
[ZH] 千里寻猜想，仪王肯定是从东方尘泽等人口中得知，看来隐瞒不下去：“偷偷练过一些，只为防身，至于轻功，我可是一点儿都不会。”
[EN] Qianli Xun guessed that Prince Yi must have learned this from Dongfang Chenze and others. It seemed she couldn't hide it anymore: "I secretly practiced some, just for self-defense. As for qinggong, I don't know any of it at all."
[ZH] 申简辰来了兴致：“幽栖居士可否与我切磋一番？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen became interested: "Would Youqi Jushi spar with me?"
[ZH] 千里拳威力不小，只怕将仪王打趴下，即使不打趴下，赢了他，他面子上也挂不住。他是王爷，若败下阵来，难免以后会找麻烦，还是不比试得好。
[EN] Thousand Mile Fist was quite powerful. She was afraid she'd knock Prince Yi down. Even if she didn't knock him down, if she beat him, he'd lose face. He was a prince. If he lost, he'd inevitably cause trouble later. It was better not to spar at all.
[ZH] 再说仪王与文渊熟识，千里寻可不想让文渊知道她是一个能把男人打败的母老虎。文渊喜欢的应是像朱淑真那样又温柔又有才情的女子，故而，万万不可与仪王比试，不管输赢都都不可。
[EN] Besides, Prince Yi was familiar with Wenyuan. Qianli Xun didn't want Wenyuan to know she was a tigress who could beat men. Wenyuan should like gentle and talented women like Zhu Shuzhen. Therefore, she absolutely must not spar with Prince Yi, regardless of winning or losing.
[ZH] “还是不了，我是弱女子，又怎能比得过仪王？”
[EN] "Better not. I'm a weak woman. How could I possibly compete with Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 对方拒绝，申简辰不好再继续要求，想起醉云楼诗会一事，说道：“刚来临安时，我跟着文渊参加东方公子举行的诗会，拜读了幽栖居士的新词，一番品鉴下来，想着什么时候碰到幽栖居士，定要叮嘱一番。今日遇见，也算缘分，叮嘱的话还是要说的。”
[EN] Since she refused, Shen Jianchen couldn't press further. Remembering the poetry gathering at Drunken Cloud Tower, he said: "When I first came to Lin'an, I followed Wenyuan to attend the poetry gathering hosted by Young Master Dongfang. I read Youqi Jushi's new lyrics. After appreciating them, I thought about when I'd meet Youqi Jushi and definitely give some advice. Meeting you today is fate, so I still need to give that advice."
[ZH] “仪王请讲。”
[EN] "Please speak, Prince Yi."
[ZH] “以后写诗作词能不能不要写个人隐私？”
[EN] "In the future, when writing poems and lyrics, could you not write about personal privacy?"
[ZH] 千里寻愣住：“仪王的话是何意？”
[EN] Qianli Xun was stunned: "What does Prince Yi mean?"
[ZH] 申简辰解释道：“你把想要离开夫君的想法写到《断肠谜》中，被人品来品去，可觉得舒服？再说，以写诗作词的名头给别人提供八卦自己隐私的机会，似乎有欠妥当。被本朝人议论也就罢了，只怕幽栖居士美名远播，诗词会被后人一代又一代记住，你的隐私也会被一代又一代人议论，等你百年之后，在天之灵想要安息都难。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen explained: "You wrote your thoughts of wanting to leave your husband in 'Heartbreak Riddle.' People appreciate it and discuss it. Does that feel comfortable? Besides, using the name of writing poems and lyrics to give others the opportunity to gossip about your own privacy seems inappropriate. Being discussed by people of this dynasty is one thing, but I'm afraid Youqi Jushi's famous reputation will mean your poems and lyrics will be remembered by future generations, and your privacy will be discussed by generation after generation. After you pass away, even your spirit in heaven will find it hard to rest in peace."
[ZH] 仪王一番嘱咐不无道理，可千里寻依然要为朱淑真辩解几句。
[EN] Prince Yi's advice was not without reason, but Qianli Xun still wanted to say a few words in defense of Zhu Shuzhen.
[ZH] “感谢仪王叮嘱，淑真受教。淑真生在闺阁，面对的只有闺阁之情，略微懂些写诗作词的技巧，有情无处可宣泄，便用诗词表达。如若我能像男儿一样立身朝堂，或征战沙场，或许诗词的内容会有所不同。可惜我的天地和战场在闺阁，这不是我的错，是由时代决定的。别的我不强求，只要大家能从我的诗词中看出这个时代女子的不易，我便心满意足。”
[EN] “Thank you for your advice, Prince Yi. Shuzhen has learned from it. Shuzhen was born in the boudoir. All she faced were the emotions of the boudoir. She slightly understood the techniques of writing poems and lyrics. With emotions but nowhere to vent them, she expressed them through poetry. If I could stand in the court like a man, or fight on the battlefield, perhaps the content of my poems would be different. But unfortunately, my world and battlefield are in the boudoir. This is not my fault—it's determined by the times. I don't ask for anything else. As long as people can see from my poems the difficulties of women in this era, I'll be completely satisfied.”
[ZH] 朱淑真讲得至情至理，反而让申简辰觉得自己境界狭隘：“幽栖居士既如此认为，我也不必多说什么，希望你以后写出更多诗词，让后人看到本朝闺阁女子的不易。”
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen spoke with such heartfelt emotion and sound reasoning that Shen Jianchen instead felt his own perspective was narrow: "Since Youqi Jushi thinks this way, I don't need to say more. I hope you'll write more poems and lyrics in the future, so future generations can see the difficulties of boudoir women in our dynasty."
[ZH] 很快将仪王的观念转变过来，千里寻对自己感到满意：“那是自然。”
[EN] Having quickly changed Prince Yi's perspective, Qianli Xun felt satisfied with herself: "That's only natural."
[ZH] 乔梦容走过来邀请道：“幽栖居士要不要去幽林别居坐坐？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong walked over and invited: "Would Youqi Jushi like to visit Secluded Forest Villa?"
[ZH] “幽林别居？”
[EN] "Secluded Forest Villa?"
[ZH] 乔梦容指着不远处的树林：“就在那片树林里，我与哥哥住在里边，乔公子也在，幽林别居是乔家在城外的宅子。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong pointed to the woods not far away: "It's right in those woods. My brother and I live inside. Young Master Qiao is also there. Secluded Forest Villa is the Qiao family's residence outside the city."
[ZH] 原来她用轻功飞身神柳之上看到的府宅是乔家的。
[EN] So the residence she saw when she used qinggong to fly up the Divine Willow belonged to the Qiao family.
[ZH] 千里寻看看自己身上，出来也没有认真打扮过，决不能如此草率去见文渊。于是婉拒道：“还是不了，明日文渊约我去西湖，自然见到，若今日见了，明日又见，那多没意思。”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked at herself. She hadn't bothered to dress up properly when she came out. She absolutely couldn't see Wenyuan so carelessly. So she politely declined: "Better not. Wenyuan invited me to West Lake tomorrow. We'll naturally meet then. If we meet today and meet again tomorrow, that would be too boring."
[ZH] 乔梦容笑道：“有道理，改日幽栖居士也约乔公子一起到尼姑庵请条红绸挂在柳树上。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong laughed: "That makes sense. Another day, Youqi Jushi should invite Young Master Qiao to the nunnery together to get a red silk and hang it on the willow tree."
[ZH] “一定一定。”
[EN] "Definitely, definitely."
[ZH] 千里寻行完礼，目睹二位贵人离开神柳旁，沿着池塘走到对岸，身影消失在幽林中。
[EN] After Qianli Xun finished bowing, she watched the two nobles leave the Divine Willow, walk along the pond to the opposite shore, and their figures disappear into the secluded forest.
[ZH] 仪王刚刚出现时，千里寻产生错觉，误以为他是申简辰，此刻想来，甚觉可笑。他怎么可能是申简辰？肯定是她太想念申简辰的缘故，以至于见到有些类似的人，不由自主产生错觉。
[EN] When Prince Yi first appeared, Qianli Xun had an illusion and mistakenly thought he was Shen Jianchen. Thinking about it now, it was quite laughable. How could he be Shen Jianchen? It must be because she missed Shen Jianchen so much that when she saw someone somewhat similar, she couldn't help but have an illusion.
[ZH] 周围只剩下苦荞，千里寻才走到神柳跟前，伸开双臂，将神柳抱住，小声说：“神柳，我已有喜欢的人，喜欢一个人的感觉很不错，让人觉得整个世界都变得无比美好。即便如此，也希望你帮我找到申简辰，他对我来说就像……”
[EN] With only Kuqiao around, Qianli Xun walked up to the Divine Willow, spread her arms and embraced it. She whispered: “Divine Willow, I already have someone I like. The feeling of liking someone is wonderful—it makes you feel the whole world has become incomparably beautiful. Even so, I still hope you'll help me find Shen Jianchen. To me, he's like...”
[ZH] 千里寻不知如何表达她对申简辰的感觉，好像用任何语言都不恰当，只好无奈地放开神柳：“苦荞，我们回去吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't know how to express her feelings for Shen Jianchen. It seemed no language was appropriate, so she had no choice but to let go of the Divine Willow helplessly: "Kuqiao, let's go back."

#CH 65
[ZH] 申简辰刚踏进幽林别居，像是想起什么，对乔梦容说：“梦容，你先回屋，我突然想起件事，需出去一趟。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen had just stepped into Secluded Forest Villa when he seemed to remember something. He said to Qiao Mengrong: "Mengrong, go back to your room first. I suddenly remembered something and need to go out."
[ZH] “什么事？哥哥能否告诉我？”乔梦容一脸疑惑。
[EN] "What is it? Can you tell me, brother?" Qiao Mengrong looked puzzled.
[ZH] 申简辰犹豫片刻，他不愿将真实的想法表达出来：“白风还未回来，我去找找他。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen hesitated for a moment. He didn't want to express his true thoughts: "Bai Feng hasn't returned yet. I'll go look for him."
[ZH] 乔梦容鼓起勇气问出心中的疑惑：“哥哥为何听了一次说书，就要寻找写话本的人？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong gathered her courage to ask the question on her mind: "Why did you want to find the person who wrote the storytelling scripts after listening to just one storytelling session?"
[ZH] “没……没什么，只是想结识而已。”
[EN] "There's... there's nothing. I just want to meet him, that's all."
[ZH] “写话本的人难道就是哥哥占卜之人？就是那位叫千里寻的公子？”
[EN] "Is the person who wrote the storytelling scripts the one you divined about, brother? Is it that young master named Qianli Xun?"
[ZH] 申简辰不愿再解释下去：“我得赶紧走，不能再跟你聊。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn't want to explain further: "I need to leave quickly. I can't chat with you anymore."
[ZH] 哥哥迅速出了幽林别居。
[EN] Her brother quickly left Secluded Forest Villa.
[ZH] 乔梦容紧跟到门口，目睹哥哥坐上马车，又吩咐车夫快些，像有急事一般。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong followed closely to the gate and watched her brother get into the carriage. He told the coachman to hurry, as if he had urgent business.
[ZH] 乔梦容既疑惑又担心：“你说哥哥要去干什么？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong was both puzzled and worried: "What do you think brother is going to do?"
[ZH] 面对郡主的发问，喜兰感到为难：“王爷可能有急事。”
[EN] Faced with the princess's question, Xilan felt at a loss: "The prince probably has urgent business."
[ZH] “他连临安节度使的职位都不当回事，还能有什么急事？”
[EN] "He doesn't even care about the position of Military Commissioner of Lin'an. What urgent business could he possibly have?"
[ZH] 喜兰不知如何回答：“郡主还是回屋歇息吧，又是爬山又是挂红绸的，肯定累了。”
[EN] Xilan didn't know how to answer: "Princess, you should go back to your room and rest. You've been climbing mountains and hanging red silk. You must be tired."
[ZH] 提到红绸，乔梦容充满疑惑的脸上才露出笑容：“回去吧，哥哥是男子，总会有些不方便告诉女子的事。”
[EN] Mentioning the red silk brought a smile to Qiao Mengrong's puzzled face: "Let's go back. Brother is a man. There will always be things he can't conveniently tell women."
[ZH] 喜兰十分认可：“郡主能这样想就对了。”
[EN] Xilan strongly agreed: "That's the right way to think, Princess."
[ZH] 申简辰乘坐马车来到城门外，吩咐车夫将马车停在城外的一棵树下。他从马车上下来，望向天空，然后盯着通往神柳处的路。
[EN] Shen Jianchen took the carriage to outside the city gate and told the coachman to stop the carriage under a tree outside the city. He got off the carriage, looked up at the sky, then stared at the road leading to the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 不多时，看到一辆马车从远处而来，申简辰露出笑容。
[EN] Before long, he saw a carriage approaching from the distance. Shen Jianchen smiled.
[ZH] 等马车靠近后，他走上前：“幽栖居士，我有事请教，能否下车与我聊会儿？”
[EN] When the carriage got closer, he stepped forward: "Youqi Jushi, I have something to ask you. Could you get off the carriage and chat with me for a while?"
[ZH] 千里寻从车窗探出脑袋，见仪王拦住她的马车，甚是不解。对方是王爷，她岂有不听之理？便下了马车。
[EN] Qianli Xun poked her head out of the carriage window. Seeing Prince Yi blocking her carriage, she was quite confused. He was a prince—how could she not obey? So she got off the carriage.
[ZH] 千里寻向四处瞧了瞧，没见佳和郡主，显然仪王是独自前来拦截。她行礼道：“不知仪王找我何事？”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked around and didn't see Princess Jiahe. Obviously, Prince Yi had come alone to intercept her. She bowed and said: "I wonder what business Prince Yi has with me?"
[ZH] 申简辰指着城外的草地：“可否与我去那边聊？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen pointed to the grassland outside the city: "Could you come over there and chat with me?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，用目光阻止苦荞不要跟来。苦荞领会，站在原地等候。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded and used her gaze to stop Kuqiao from following. Kuqiao understood and stood in place to wait.
[ZH] 千里寻跟随仪王来到草地，草长得很茂盛，踩上去软软的，很舒服。
[EN] Qianli Xun followed Prince Yi to the grassland. The grass grew thick and lush. Stepping on it felt soft and comfortable.
[ZH] 仪王没有任何寒暄之语，直接问道：“我在竹林碰到你，说明你那时住在竹林，你那丫鬟的弟弟正好帮千里寻将书稿送给说话艺人，我想你肯定见过千里寻，你的丫鬟说他不知所踪，你可知他的去向？”
[EN] Prince Yi didn't bother with small talk. He asked directly: "I met you in the bamboo forest, which means you were living in the bamboo forest at that time. Your maid's younger brother happened to help Qianli Xun deliver his manuscripts to the storytellers. I think you must have met Qianli Xun. Your maid said he's nowhere to be found. Do you know where he went?"
[ZH] 原来是为此事，千里寻感到荣耀，被网暴的小说，在这个时代不仅没人说她抄袭，而且一位王爷为了挖出幕后的作者，真是煞费苦心，搞得千里寻都想把实情告诉他。不过，她还是忍住。
[EN] So it was for this matter. Qianli Xun felt honored. Her novel that had been attacked online in her time was not only not accused of plagiarism in this era, but a prince was going to great lengths to dig out the author behind the scenes. It made Qianli Xun almost want to tell him the truth. But she held back.
[ZH] “我确实在竹林见过千里寻，也看过他的手稿，他写的故事实在引人入胜，我读后佩服得五体投地，当下断定，千里寻写的故事肯定会流芳百世，仪王觉得呢？”
[EN] "I did meet Qianli Xun in the bamboo forest and I've seen his manuscripts. The stories he writes are truly captivating. After reading them, I was completely impressed. I immediately concluded that Qianli Xun's stories will definitely be remembered for generations. What do you think, Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 朱淑真只顾夸赞，并未回答问题，申简辰强调道：“你可知千里寻的去向？”
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen only kept praising him without answering the question. Shen Jianchen emphasized: "Do you know where Qianli Xun went?"
[ZH] 千里寻一番自我夸耀，没有得到对方回应，她并不满足，更不想错过听他人评论的好机会：“仪王先评评千里寻写的话本，我再告诉你他的去向。”
[EN] After Qianli Xun's self-praise, she didn't get a response from him. She wasn't satisfied and didn't want to miss this good opportunity to hear others' comments: "Prince Yi, first evaluate the storytelling scripts written by Qianli Xun, then I'll tell you where he went."
[ZH] 申简辰一阵高兴，看来朱淑真知道千里寻在哪里。既然她如此欣赏千里寻写的话本，不如说些好听的，将她哄高兴，肯定会告知实情。
[EN] Shen Jianchen was pleased. It seemed Zhu Shuzhen knew where Qianli Xun was. Since she appreciated Qianli Xun's storytelling scripts so much, he might as well say some nice things to make her happy, and she would definitely tell him the truth.
[ZH] “我在京城也听过说书，都是些陈词滥调，来到临安，听到千里寻写的故事，让人耳目一新，能否流芳百世不敢妄下定论，起码我喜欢听，我见其他人也喜欢听，只要大家喜欢说明他写得不错。”
[EN] "I've also listened to storytelling in the capital. It was all clichés. Coming to Lin'an and hearing the stories written by Qianli Xun was refreshing. I dare not make a hasty conclusion about whether they'll be remembered for generations, but at least I enjoy listening to them. I see others enjoy listening too. As long as everyone likes them, it shows he writes well."
[ZH] 一番夸耀，千里寻听着很受用，体会着“耳目一新”四个字，心里偷偷乐道：“嘿嘿，在千里寻生活的时代，这样的内容一点儿都不新鲜，没承想拿到宋朝来会让人耳目一新，实在有趣！”
[EN] After all the praise, Qianli Xun found it very pleasing. Savoring the words "a breath of fresh air," she secretly rejoiced: "Heh, in the era where Qianli Xun lives, this kind of content isn't fresh at all. I didn't expect it to be a breath of fresh air when brought to the Song Dynasty. How interesting!"
[ZH] “还请幽栖居士告诉我千里寻去了哪里？”
[EN] "Please tell me where Qianli Xun went, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 仪王的声音使得千里寻无法继续沉迷下去，要不要告诉他实情？千里寻犹豫着……
[EN] Prince Yi's voice snapped Qianli Xun out of her daydream. Should she tell him the truth? Qianli Xun hesitated...
[ZH] “幽栖居士赶紧告诉我千里寻去了哪里？”
[EN] "Please hurry and tell me where Qianli Xun went, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 仪王继续追问，不能再缄默不语：“千里寻啊……他是位公子，不是姑娘……”
[EN] Prince Yi continued to press. She couldn't stay silent anymore: "Qianli Xun... he's a young master, not a young lady..."
[ZH] 申简辰想要听到更关键的内容，急切地说：“我知道。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen wanted to hear more crucial information and said anxiously: "I know."
[ZH] “他离开竹林时，我们在一起喝茶来着，他好像说……好像说……”千里寻构思着如何编个去处，“对！他说神柳要带他离开，后来我就找不到他，该不会真的被神柳带走？今日想起他，我才去神柳旁看看，也没看出所以然来。”
[EN] "When he left the bamboo forest, we were having tea together. He seemed to say... he seemed to say..." Qianli Xun thought about how to make up a destination. "Right! He said the Divine Willow was going to take him away. After that, I couldn't find him. Could he really have been taken away by the Divine Willow? Thinking of him today, I went to check by the Divine Willow, but I didn't see anything unusual."
[ZH] 千里寻观察仪王，发现他显得很失落，内心更窃喜：“没想到我的铁杆粉丝竟然是位王爷，这'铁杆'的程度简直无人能比，我把他伤成那样，实在残忍，不如安慰安慰他。”
[EN] Qianli Xun observed Prince Yi and found he looked very disappointed. She felt even more secretly pleased: “I didn't expect my die-hard fan to actually be a prince. This level of 'die-hard' is simply unmatched. I hurt him so badly—that's really cruel. I might as well comfort him.”
[ZH] “仪王不必伤感，千里寻肯定是跟我开玩笑，我刚才将神柳研究半天，也没看出它有这能力，你没事多去勾栏瓦肆听听说书，如果再听到千里寻写的新故事，说明他并未离开。”
[EN] "Prince Yi, don't be sad. Qianli Xun must be joking with me. I just studied the Divine Willow for a long time and didn't see that it has this ability. When you have time, go to the entertainment quarters and listen to storytelling. If you hear new stories written by Qianli Xun, it means he hasn't left."
[ZH] 申简辰又伤感一会儿，才说道：“离开了也好，只是我……”他无法再说下去，千里寻若走了，留下他一人在这个时代还有什么意义。
[EN] Shen Jianchen felt sad for a while longer before saying: "It's good that he's left. It's just that I..." He couldn't continue. If Qianli Xun was gone, what meaning was there in leaving him alone in this era?
[ZH] 仪王话未说完，落寞地离开草地，向树下的马车走去。
[EN] Before Prince Yi finished his words, he left the grassland dejectedly and walked toward the carriage under the tree.
[ZH] 千里寻看着仪王的背影，并未多想，依然只把他当做铁杆粉丝而已，心想：“过几日，新的手稿写完，不如约仪王去勾栏瓦肆听听，让他高兴高兴，就这么定了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun watched Prince Yi's back and didn't think too much about it. She still just treated him as a die-hard fan and thought: "In a few days, when the new manuscript is finished, I might as well invite Prince Yi to the entertainment quarters to listen and cheer him up. That's settled then."
[ZH] 回幽林别居的路上，申简辰透过马车的窗户，望着远处神柳的影子，自语道：“千里寻，你若真回去，我也不想在陌生的时代待下去，不如现在就去找神柳，求它把我带回去。”
[EN] On the way back to Secluded Forest Villa, Shen Jianchen looked through the carriage window at the distant shadow of the Divine Willow and said to himself: "Qianli Xun, if you've really gone back, I don't want to stay in this unfamiliar era either. I might as well go find the Divine Willow now and beg it to take me back."
[ZH] 刚想吩咐车夫调转马头，乔梦容温柔而又充满期待的眼神浮入脑海，他不忍心就此离去：“还是回去帮赵朴安顿好梦容，再做打算。”
[EN] Just as he was about to tell the coachman to turn the carriage around, Qiao Mengrong's gentle and expectant eyes floated into his mind. He couldn't bear to leave just like that: "I should go back and help Zhao Pu settle Mengrong first, then make plans."
[ZH] 明日要约会，乔文仙很兴奋，打算邀请仪王带上佳和郡主一起游湖。
[EN] Tomorrow was the date. Qiao Wenxian was very excited and planned to invite Prince Yi to bring Princess Jiahe along for a boat ride on the lake.
[ZH] 白风不在，又未带南双，乔文仙走进主屋，坐到茶桌跟前，亲自动手做起茶来。
[EN] Bai Feng wasn't around, and he hadn't brought Shuang either. Qiao Wenxian walked into the main room, sat at the tea table, and personally started making tea.
[ZH] 刚做好茶，品了一口，仪王从外面进来，看上去一副失魂落魄的样子。
[EN] He had just finished making the tea and taken a sip when Prince Yi came in from outside, looking utterly devastated.
[ZH] 乔文仙放下茶盏：“你可回来，怎么看上去不高兴？我也给你做盏茶解解渴。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian put down his teacup: "You're back. Why do you look unhappy? Let me make you a cup of tea to quench your thirst."
[ZH] 申简辰强压住失落的情绪，挤出笑容：“你来了多久？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen forced down his disappointment and squeezed out a smile: "How long have you been here?"
[ZH] “做一盏茶的功夫。”乔文仙开始为仪王做茶，“你整日让白风出去办事，也没人伺候，不如让李管家再给你安排个下人，最好是位娇俏的丫鬟，以前你在仪王府时，身边尽是些丫鬟，病好后还真是转了性情。”
[EN] "Just enough time to make a cup of tea." Qiao Wenxian started making tea for Prince Yi: "You have Bai Feng go out on errands all day and have no one to attend to you. Why not have Butler Li arrange another servant for you? Preferably a pretty young maid. When you were at Prince Yi's Manor, you were surrounded by maids. After you recovered from your illness, you really changed your ways."
[ZH] 申简辰坐在椅子上，显得很落寞：“要那么多人伺候干什么？白风一人足矣，白风不在，我有手有脚，什么都能干，别人伺候，我还不习惯。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen sat in a chair, looking very lonely: "Why do I need so many people to attend to me? Bai Feng alone is enough. When Bai Feng isn't around, I have hands and feet and can do everything myself. I'm not used to others attending to me."
[ZH] “你的做派与皇子的身份极不相符。”
[EN] "Your behavior is completely inappropriate for a prince's status."
[ZH] “今日是皇子，明日也许就成为阶下囚，人千万不能被眼前的荣华富贵迷惑，都是假的。”
[EN] "Today I'm a prince, tomorrow I might become a prisoner. One must never be deceived by the wealth and honor right before one's eyes. It's all fake."
[ZH] “呸呸呸！怎么能说这么不吉利的话？你今日很不对劲，是不是与佳和郡主吵架了？”
[EN] "Tsk tsk tsk! How can you say such unlucky words? You're very off today. Did you quarrel with Princess Jiahe?"
[ZH] “梦容怎么可能与我吵架？她要是跟我吵一架倒好了。”
[EN] "How could Mengrong quarrel with me? It would be better if she did quarrel with me."
[ZH] “也对，佳和郡主温柔端庄，不可能跟仪王吵架。你到底怎么了？看着情绪不佳。”
[EN] "That's true. Princess Jiahe is gentle and dignified. She wouldn't quarrel with Prince Yi. What's wrong with you then? You look upset."
[ZH] 申简辰瞅一眼正在仔细做茶的文渊：“真想不通，一盏茶做得那么细致有什么意思？你继续，我到里屋躺会儿。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced at Wenyuan, who was carefully making tea: "I really don't understand. What's the point of making a cup of tea so meticulously? You continue. I'll go lie down in the inner room for a while."

#CH 66
[ZH] 乔文仙为仪王做好茶，端着茶盏来到里屋。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian made tea for Prince Yi and carried the teacup into the inner room.
[ZH] 仪王正躺在床上发呆，他上前做出恭敬有礼的样子，夹着嗓子说：“仪王殿下，奴婢已为您把茶做好，请享用。”
[EN] Prince Yi was lying on the bed, lost in thought. Wenxian put on an act of respectful courtesy, pinching his voice to say: "Your Highness Prince Yi, your servant has prepared the tea for you. Please enjoy."
[ZH] 文渊学丫鬟的声音，把申简辰逗乐，他“噗嗤”笑出声，坐起来：“小心我把你现在的样子告诉幽栖居士。”
[EN] Wenyuan imitating a maid's voice made Shen Jianchen laugh. He burst out laughing and sat up: "Careful, or I'll tell Youqi Jushi what you look like right now."
[ZH] 乔文仙把茶盏递到跟前：“还是别，我只是看你不高兴，逗个乐子，又何必告诉她？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian handed him the teacup: "Better not. I was just trying to cheer you up because you looked unhappy. Why tell her?"
[ZH] 申简辰接过茶盏，一口气喝下，又把茶盏递到文渊手中。
[EN] Shen Jianchen took the teacup, drank it in one gulp, and handed it back to Wenyuan.
[ZH] 乔文仙接过茶盏，批评道：“怎么能这么喝茶！要品，品才有滋味。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian took the teacup and chided: “How can you drink tea like that! You have to savor it—only then can you appreciate the flavor.”
[ZH] “不就是一盏茶吗？讲究那么多干嘛？”说着又要躺下。
[EN] "It's just a cup of tea. Why so particular?" He was about to lie down again.
[ZH] 乔文仙把茶盏随手放在一旁的桌上，迅速过去扶住仪王：“别躺了，跟你说个事。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian casually set the teacup on the nearby table and quickly went over to support Prince Yi: "Stop lying down. Let me tell you something."
[ZH] “什么事？”
[EN] "What is it?"
[ZH] “明日我与淑真相约一起游西湖，你来临安还没去西湖逛过，要不带上佳和郡主一起去？”
[EN] "Tomorrow Shuzhen and I have a date to tour West Lake. You haven't visited West Lake since coming to Lin'an. Why not bring Princess Jiahe along?"
[ZH] “你们两个约会，我和梦容去，难道要给你们当电灯泡？”
[EN] "You two are going on a date. If Mengrong and I go, wouldn't we be third wheels?"
[ZH] “电灯泡？什么东西？”
[EN] "A light bulb? What on earth is that?"
[ZH] 申简辰才想起这个时代哪有什么“电灯泡”，曾经说顺口的词语，借用到古代，就如同他看到古代文章一样不知所云。
[EN] Shen Jianchen then remembered—how could there be “light bulbs” in this era? Words he used casually became just as incomprehensible in ancient times as ancient texts were to him.
