[ZH] “意思就是你与幽栖居士约会，我与梦容去了会影响你们。”
[EN] “What I mean is—you and Youqi Jushi are going on a date. If Mengrong and I tag along, we'd be intruding on you two.”
[ZH] “怎么会？你和佳和郡主是一对儿，我跟淑真是一对儿，互不干扰。”
[EN] "How so? You and Princess Jiahe are a couple, Shuzhen and I are a couple. We wouldn't interfere with each other."
[ZH] 申简辰依旧拒绝：“还是算了，我明日有事。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen still refused: "Forget it. I have something to do tomorrow."
[ZH] “你能有什么事？作为临安节度使连府衙都懒得去，我真担心哪天官家治你的罪。”
[EN] "What could you possibly have to do? As the Military Commissioner of Lin'an, you can't even be bothered to go to the yamen. I'm worried the emperor will punish you one day."
[ZH] “放心吧，我在这里的情况只有临安知府上报官家才会知道，我不理临安政事，朱晞颜高兴着呢，他只会给官家说我的好话，我何罪之有？”
[EN] "Don't worry. The only way the emperor learns about me here is if the prefect of Lin'an reports to him. I don't involve myself in Lin'an's affairs, and Zhu Xiyan is happy about it. He'll only say good things about me to the emperor. What crime could I possibly have?"
[ZH] “感情你是走一步想好几步，厉害！既然没什么事，跟我去吧？”
[EN] "So you've thought several steps ahead. Impressive! Since you have nothing to do, come with me then?"
[ZH] “刚都说了，我有事，别缠着我，搞得好像你要跟我约会似的，小心幽栖居士嫉妒。”
[EN] "I just said I have something to do. Stop pestering me. It's like you want to go on a date with me. Careful, or Youqi Jushi will get jealous."
[ZH] 乔文仙放开手：“好吧，既然这次你不去，改日我再带你去游湖。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian let go: "Fine. Since you won't go this time, I'll take you boating another day."
[ZH] 乔文仙走后，申简辰继续躺下，因文渊在一旁叽叽喳喳，失落的情绪疏散不少，关注点由神柳转向其他，猛然想起幽栖居士说过的话：“你没事多去勾栏瓦肆听听说书，如果再听到千里寻写的新故事，说明他并未离开。”
[EN] After Wenyuan left, Shen Jianchen lay back down. Wenyuan's chatter had dispelled much of his gloom, shifting his focus away from the Divine Willow. He suddenly remembered what Youqi Jushi had said: "When you have time, go to the entertainment quarters and listen to storytelling. If you hear new stories written by Qianli Xun, it means he hasn't left."
[ZH] 申简辰自语道：“对，千里寻只是给幽栖居士说神柳要带他离开，并不代表他真的离开，要是神柳能带千里寻离开，为何每次我到神柳跟前，甚至还抱过它，它都没有任何反应，说不定千里寻并未回去，还是让白风继续在西三胡同守着。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said to himself: “Right. Qianli Xun only told Youqi Jushi that the Divine Willow was going to take him away—that doesn't mean he actually left. If the Divine Willow could take Qianli Xun away, then why does it show no reaction every time I go to it, even when I've embraced it? Maybe Qianli Xun hasn't gone back after all. I'll have Bai Feng keep watch at West Third Alley.”
[ZH] 申简辰再次充满希望。
[EN] Shen Jianchen was filled with hope once again.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 一大早起来，千里寻用过早饭，开始为第一次约会做准备。
[EN] Early in the morning, after breakfast, Qianli Xun began preparing for her first date.
[ZH] 苦荞先为小姐做头发，绾了小姐喜欢的发髻。
[EN] Kuqiao first did her young lady's hair, styling it in the bun she liked.
[ZH] 千里寻亲自挑发饰，既不能太素雅，也不能太浓艳，她挑了一朵海棠花，让苦荞替她装饰在发髻上。
[EN] Qianli Xun personally chose her hair ornament—neither too plain nor too gaudy. She picked a begonia flower and had Kuqiao place it in her hair.
[ZH] 苦荞又为小姐涂脂抹粉，描眉添妆。完毕后，千里寻照照镜子，很是满意，接着开始挑衣裳。
[EN] Then Kuqiao applied powder and rouge to her young lady's face, drew her eyebrows and finished her makeup. When done, Qianli Xun looked in the mirror and was very satisfied. Then she began choosing her outfit.
[ZH] 朱府的园子里本没几件衣裳，昨日和离结束后，朱淑真成婚时带到林家的丫鬟婆子全部回来，也把朱淑真所有的家当和衣物带回，搞得园子里的衣柜衣裳充足，够千里寻挑的。
[EN] The garden residence at the Zhu household didn't originally have many clothes. But after the mutual divorce was finalized yesterday, all the maids and servant women who had been brought to the Lin family when Zhu Shuzhen married returned, bringing back all of Zhu Shuzhen's belongings and clothes. Now the wardrobe was well-stocked—plenty for Qianli Xun to choose from.
[ZH] 她让苦荞将衣裳一件一件展示出来，苦荞便把小姐穿过的和没穿过的一件一件挂出来，几乎挂了个遍，小姐终于挑到一件满意的。
[EN] She had Kuqiao display the clothes one by one. Kuqiao hung up every piece her young lady had worn and hadn't worn—nearly all of them—until her young lady finally found one she was satisfied with.
[ZH] 是一身襦裙装，上襦为天蓝色，下裙为乳白色，再搭一条乳白的披帛，既显示出姣好的身材，又将皮肤衬托得更加白皙。
[EN] It was a ruqun outfit—the upper jacket in sky blue, the lower skirt in creamy white, paired with a creamy white silk drape. It showed off her figure beautifully and made her skin look even fairer.
[ZH] 穿好衣裳后，苦荞将铜镜拿在手中，让小姐瞧瞧哪里不好，她再重新为小姐装扮。
[EN] After getting dressed, Kuqiao held the bronze mirror so her young lady could check if anything needed fixing, and she would redo the styling.
[ZH] 千里寻瞧着镜中配好衣裳和饰品后的容颜，不由在心里夸赞，朱淑真的这副皮囊实在好看，她很满意地点点头。
[EN] Looking at her reflection—clothes and accessories all matched—Qianli Xun couldn't help but admire Zhu Shuzhen's beautiful appearance in her heart. She nodded with satisfaction.
[ZH] 离约会时间还早，千里寻走到院中，坐在藤椅上。今日的太阳仿佛照顾她的情绪，并不是很强烈，温和中多了一丝惬意，搞得千里寻都想把藤椅从树下搬到阳光下。想归想，她并未实施，感受着有一丝凉意的清晨。
[EN] It was still early before the date. Qianli Xun walked into the courtyard and sat in a rattan chair. Today's sun seemed to accommodate her mood—not too intense, gentle and pleasant. It made Qianli Xun want to move the rattan chair from under the tree into the sunlight. But she only thought about it and didn't act on it, enjoying the slightly cool morning air.
[ZH] “苦荞，是不是快立秋了？”
[EN] "Kuqiao, is the Beginning of Autumn coming soon?"
[ZH] 苦荞在心里推算着时间，脸上不由挂起笑容：“还有七八日才立秋，今年的七夕节在立秋之后。”
[EN] Kuqiao calculated the time in her head, a smile appearing on her face: "The Beginning of Autumn is still seven or eight days away. This year's Qixi Festival falls after the Beginning of Autumn."
[ZH] 千里寻想起宴会上朱晞颜因七夕快到，才让大家以七夕为题写诗作词：“怎么提到七夕你这么开心？”
[EN] Qianli Xun remembered that at the banquet, Zhu Xiyan had everyone write poems on the theme of Qixi because the festival was approaching: "Why are you so happy just mentioning Qixi?"
[ZH] “七夕是非常有趣的节日，到了这一天，可好玩了。”
[EN] "Qixi is a very fun festival. When the day comes, it's so much fun."
[ZH] “好玩？”千里寻难以想象，在她所处的现代，从未觉得七夕节有多好玩儿，还不如西方的情人节，“你倒是说说有什么好玩的？”
[EN] “Fun？” Qianli Xun found it hard to imagine. In her modern era, she had never found the Qixi Festival particularly fun—it wasn't even as exciting as Western Valentine's Day. “Tell me what's so fun about it？”
[ZH] “到了七夕，大家都会穿上漂亮衣裳，朱府允许下人出去逛街，街上好玩的可多了，回到朱府还要焚香跪拜，望月穿针。我今年都已想好，要在七夕节那日捉一只蜘蛛，把它放在盒子里，第二天看它结出的网是否又圆又正。”苦荞说得兴高采烈。
[EN] “On Qixi, everyone puts on beautiful clothes. The Zhu household lets the servants go out to enjoy the streets. There's so much fun to be had on the streets. When we return to the Zhu household, we burn incense, kneel and bow, then thread a needle under the moonlight. I've already planned for this year—I'll catch a spider on Qixi, put it in a box, and the next day check if the web it spins is round and even.” Kuqiao spoke with great excitement.
[ZH] 千里寻听着也开心：“果然有趣。”
[EN] Qianli Xun was also happy listening: "It does sound interesting."
[ZH] 心里牵挂约会一事，她闭上眼睛，耐心等待时间消逝，不知为何，总觉得今日时间过得很慢。
[EN] With her mind on the date, she closed her eyes and patiently waited for time to pass. For some reason, today felt like it was dragging on.
[ZH] 苦荞问：“小姐，要不要在朱府用了午饭再去？”
[EN] Kuqiao asked: "Young lady, would you like to have lunch at the Zhu household before going?"
[ZH] 千里寻懒得睁开眼睛：“你去给绿桃说一下，今日午饭我不想吃，不用给我端来。”
[EN] Qianli Xun was too lazy to open her eyes: "Go tell Lvtao I don't feel like eating lunch today. No need to bring me any."
[ZH] 苦荞很担心：“那怎么行？小姐的身体重要。”
[EN] Kuqiao was worried: "How can you do that? Your health is what matters, young lady."
[ZH] “好不容易装扮好，唇脂都涂了，一吃饭就没了，还是不吃的好。”
[EN] "I went through all that trouble getting dressed up. I've already applied my lip rouge. If I eat, it'll be ruined. Better to skip lunch."
[ZH] “没关系，唇脂没了，我再给小姐涂上。”
[EN] "It's fine. If the lip rouge wears off, I'll reapply it for you, young lady."
[ZH] “不行，现在涂得挺好看，若再涂估计涂不出这效果。”
[EN] "No way. It looks great right now. If I reapply, I probably won't get the same effect."
[ZH] 苦荞很无奈，这位小姐好像从来没约会过，竟然如此紧张，连午饭都不敢吃：“既如此，我去给绿桃姐姐说一声。”
[EN] Kuqiao was helpless. It was as if her young lady had never been on a date before—so nervous she didn't even dare eat lunch: “In that case, I'll go tell Sister Lvtao.”
[ZH] 苦荞出了园子，千里寻睁开眼睛，望望天上的太阳，希望它走得快些。可太阳根本体会不到千里寻焦急的心情。她很无奈，继续闭起眼睛想象着第一次约会的情景，边想边笑。
[EN] Kuqiao left the garden. Qianli Xun opened her eyes and looked up at the sun, wishing it would move faster. But the sun was indifferent to Qianli Xun's anxious heart. Helpless, she closed her eyes again and imagined her first date, smiling as she daydreamed.
[ZH] 苦荞回来，手里拿着个布袋：“小姐，我已给绿桃姐姐说了，她说要向夫人和韦嬷嬷报告后，才能确定要不要同意您不用午饭。”
[EN] Kuqiao came back with a cloth bag in her hand: "Young lady, I've told Sister Lvtao. She said she needs to report to Madam and Nanny Wei before confirming whether you can skip lunch."
[ZH] 千里寻睁开眼睛：“啊，还要报告长辈？”
[EN] Qianli Xun opened her eyes: "What? They need to report to the elders?"
[ZH] 苦荞点点头。
[EN] Kuqiao nodded.
[ZH] 千里寻立刻站起来：“现在赶紧走，否则一会儿我娘或者韦嬷嬷来了，岂不是被拦住？”
[EN] Qianli Xun immediately stood up: "Let's leave right now. Otherwise, if my mother or Nanny Wei comes, won't they stop us?"
[ZH] 小姐匆忙走出园子，苦荞只好跟上。
[EN] Her young lady hurried out of the garden, and Kuqiao had no choice but to follow.
[ZH] 两人来到朱府门外，上了马车，千里寻才注意到苦荞手中的布袋：“你拿个袋子做什么？”
[EN] The two arrived outside the Zhu household gate and got into the carriage. Only then did Qianli Xun notice the cloth bag in Kuqiao's hand: "Why are you carrying a bag?"
[ZH] 苦荞回道：“小姐不吃午饭，我怕您约会时会饿，装些糕点带上。”
[EN] Kuqiao replied: "You're skipping lunch, young lady. I was afraid you'd get hungry during your date, so I packed some pastries to bring along."
[ZH] 千里寻很是赞赏：“还是你会替我着想。”
[EN] Qianli Xun was very appreciative: "You really know how to think of me."
[ZH] 马车载着主仆二人前往西湖的方向。
[EN] The carriage carried the mistress and maid toward West Lake.

#CH 67
[ZH] 到达西湖后，千里寻直奔望景亭，并未看到文渊的身影。她来得太早，离未时还有一个时辰，按照千里寻所熟悉的计时方法，还应该有两个小时。
[EN] After arriving at West Lake, Qianli Xun headed straight for the Lake-viewing Pavilion, but didn't see Wenyuan anywhere. She had come too early. There was still one hour before the hour of wei. By Qianli Xun's familiar timekeeping method, there were still two hours.
[ZH] 足足两个小时，该怎么度过？不如与苦荞在西湖走走。她与文渊相约的观景亭离白堤较近，还是去白堤上逛逛，既欣赏美景，又可消磨时光。
[EN] A full two hours—how should she pass the time? She might as well take a walk around West Lake with Kuqiao. The pavilion where she and Wenyuan agreed to meet was close to Bai Causeway. She'd stroll along Bai Causeway, enjoying the scenery while killing time.
[ZH] 苦荞见小姐拐出望景亭，她快步跟上，总觉今日的小姐跟往常很不一样。往常大大咧咧，今日走起路来，尽显婉约之风，真有点失忆前的模样。
[EN] Kuqiao saw her young lady turn away from the Lake-viewing Pavilion and quickly caught up. She felt her young lady was very different today. Normally carefree and easygoing, today her walk showed a gentle grace—truly like her pre-amnesia self.
[ZH] 苦荞心里偷偷乐道：“还是乔公子厉害，一句话不说，让小姐回到失忆之前。”
[EN] Kuqiao secretly rejoiced: "Young Master Qiao is really something. Without saying a word, he's brought the young lady back to who she was before losing her memory."
[ZH] 千里寻踏上白堤，边走边欣赏西湖岸边的垂柳，不由想起两句诗，随口吟出：“最爱湖东行不足，绿杨阴里白沙堤。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped onto Bai Causeway, walking while admiring the weeping willows along West Lake's shore. Two lines of poetry came to mind, and she recited them aloud: "East of the lake, I never tire of wandering, along the white sand dyke where green willows cast their shade."
[ZH] 苦荞夸赞道：“小姐真厉害，才逛几步，就能做出这么美的诗句！”
[EN] Kuqiao praised: "Young lady, you're amazing! After just a few steps, you can compose such beautiful poetry!"
[ZH] 千里寻宛然一笑：“哪里是我写的？是唐代诗人白居易《钱塘湖春行》里的诗句。”
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled gently: "How could I have written this? These are lines from 'Spring Journey by Qiantang Lake' by the Tang Dynasty poet Bai Juyi."
[ZH] “小姐想起香山居士的诗倒也正常，据说这白堤与他有关。”
[EN] "It's normal for you to remember Xiangshan Jushi's poetry. It's said that Bai Causeway is related to him."
[ZH] “你还知道这些，很不错！”
[EN] "You know quite a lot. That's impressive!"
[ZH] 被小姐夸赞，苦荞很开心，话也变得多起来：“都是以前小姐告诉我的，我还知道苏堤，是我朝东坡居士所建，景色也不错，不如小姐逛完白堤再到苏堤逛逛。”
[EN] Praised by her young lady, Kuqiao was delighted and became more talkative: “You told me all this before. I also know about Su Causeway—it was built by Dongpo Jushi of our dynasty. The scenery there is nice too. Why not visit Su Causeway after finishing Bai Causeway？”
[ZH] “还是不了，走完白堤起码得半个时辰，再走回来又得半个时辰，只怕让文渊等得焦急，我已想好，只把白堤走上一半，就往回走，然后到望景亭旁边躲起来，我要亲眼目睹文渊焦虑等待的样子。”
[EN] “Better not. Walking the entire Bai Causeway would take at least an hour, and walking back another hour. I'm afraid Wenyuan would be anxious waiting. I've decided—I'll walk halfway along Bai Causeway, then turn back. I'll hide near the Lake-viewing Pavilion. I want to see with my own eyes how anxious Wenyuan gets waiting.”
[ZH] “小姐的主意不错，您一早起来为见乔公子精心打扮，又因见乔公子连午饭都不吃，还来得这么早，总得亲眼目睹他是否也会着急见您。”
[EN] "That's a good idea, young lady. You got up early this morning, dressed up carefully to meet Young Master Qiao, skipped lunch just to see him, and came so early. You should definitely see with your own eyes whether he'll be anxious to see you too."
[ZH] 千里寻转过头，盯着苦荞，这丫头什么时候变成她肚中的蛔虫，竟然知道得这么清楚。
[EN] Qianli Xun turned her head and stared at Kuqiao. When had this girl become a tapeworm in her stomach, knowing her thoughts so clearly?
[ZH] 苦荞以为小姐要批评她，赶紧承认错误：“我说错了，您千万不要批评我。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought her young lady was going to scold her and quickly admitted her mistake: "I said something wrong. Please don't scold me."
[ZH] 千里寻并未批评苦荞，而是笑道：“你讲得没错，我为何要批评你？”
[EN] Qianli Xun didn't scold Kuqiao. Instead, she laughed: "You said nothing wrong. Why would I scold you?"
[ZH] “我以为小姐嫌我多嘴。”
[EN] "I thought you'd find me too talkative."
[ZH] “你确实多嘴，我今日心情好，不与你计较。”
[EN] "You are too talkative, but I'm in a good mood today, so I won't hold it against you."
[ZH] 千里寻继续欣赏西湖美景，比美景更吸引人的则是时不时从她身边经过的情侣；湖面上也有成双成对的人荡着小船，在水中嬉戏，他们的脸上洋溢着爱情的喜悦。
[EN] Qianli Xun continued admiring the beautiful scenery of West Lake. More captivating than the scenery were the couples passing by from time to time. On the lake, pairs of people rowed small boats, playing in the water, their faces beaming with the joy of love.
[ZH] 以前的千里寻看到这些，心中总是不屑，还会嘲笑恋爱中的姑娘是一群恋爱脑残加智障。
[EN] The old Qianli Xun, upon seeing this, would always feel disdain and mock girls in love as a bunch of love-brained fools and idiots.
[ZH] 可现在她却羡慕起恋爱中的女子，她也想与文渊牵着手逛完白堤再逛苏堤，然后再划船嬉戏……
[EN] But now she found herself envying the girls in love. She also wanted to hold Wenyuan's hand, stroll along Bai Causeway then Su Causeway, and then row a boat and play in the water...
[ZH] 正想着，闻道一股烤肉的味道，千里寻向四处瞅瞅：“哪里来的烟火气？”
[EN] Just as she was thinking this, she caught the smell of roasting meat. Qianli Xun looked around: "Where is this smoke coming from?"
[ZH] 苦荞指着不远处：“小姐，您瞧，烟火气是从那边传来。”
[EN] Kuqiao pointed not far away: “Young lady, look—the smoke is coming from over there.”
[ZH] 千里寻望去，在湖岸边，白堤较宽阔之处，有一片天然的草地，周围栽种着四棵垂柳。树下聚集着一群人，看上去是一群富家公子，他们不知从哪里搬来四个圆形的火炉，正在那里烧烤，一股一股的烧烤味传来，搞得千里寻越发饥饿，仔细想想该是午饭时间。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked over. On the lakeshore, where Bai Causeway was wider, there was a natural patch of grassland surrounded by four weeping willows. Under the trees, a group of people had gathered—looks like a bunch of rich young masters. They had somehow brought four round stoves and were having a barbecue. Waves of roasting meat smell drifted over, making Qianli Xun even hungrier. Thinking carefully, it must be lunchtime.
[ZH] 千里寻忍住饥饿，批评道：“西湖可是5A级景区，这些人怎么敢在此处烧烤？难道就没人管吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun suppressed her hunger and criticized: "West Lake is a 5A-level scenic spot! How dare these people hold a barbecue here? Is no one in charge?"
[ZH] 苦荞疑惑道：“小姐，什么是5A级景区？”
[EN] Kuqiao asked puzzled: "Young lady, what is a 5A-level scenic spot?"
[ZH] “意思就是说西湖景色美，我们应该保护美景，而不应像那群人，在这里烧烤，破坏自然环境。”
[EN] "It means West Lake has beautiful scenery, and we should protect it, not be like that group, having a barbecue here and destroying the natural environment."
[ZH] 苦荞似乎听明白：“小姐说得很对！”
[EN] Kuqiao seemed to understand: "You're absolutely right, young lady!"
[ZH] 千里寻的公德意识被激起：“走，跟我上前批评批评那群人，让他们赶紧把火炉撤了……”
[EN] Qianli Xun's sense of public morality was aroused: "Come, let's go over and criticize that group. Tell them to pack up their stoves right away..."
[ZH] 苦荞拽住小姐：“还是别去，您难道没看到陈公子在那里？”
[EN] Kuqiao pulled her young lady back: "Better not go. Don't you see Young Master Chen is over there?"
[ZH] 千里寻定眼瞧去，果然是陈浩宇，他坐在一棵柳树下，一个小弟用盘子端着炙烤的肉块，另一个用长著给他喂，陈浩宇则一脸享受的样子。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked carefully—and sure enough, it was Chen Haoyu. He sat under a willow tree, one lackey holding a plate of roasted meat, another feeding him with long chopsticks, while Chen Haoyu wore an expression of pure enjoyment.
[ZH] “怎么哪里都能遇见他？”
[EN] "Why do I run into him everywhere?"
[ZH] 苦荞劝道：“小姐虽不怕陈公子，但今日是您与乔公子第一次约会，多一事不如少一事。”
[EN] Kuqiao advised: "Although you're not afraid of Young Master Chen, today is your first date with Young Master Qiao. Less trouble is better than more trouble."
[ZH] 想到约会，千里寻只好作罢：“算了，今日放他一马，等下次再碰见他做坏事，一并收拾！”
[EN] Thinking of the date, Qianli Xun had to give up: "Forget it. I'll let him off today. Next time I catch him doing something bad, I'll deal with him then!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇出现，搞得千里寻不想再继续逛下去，她打算沿原路返回。
[EN] Chen Haoyu's appearance made Qianli Xun not want to continue strolling. She decided to turn back the way she came.
[ZH] 返回的途中，迎面走来五位姑娘，苗知瑜正在其中，她一眼看到朱淑真，迎上去打招呼：“早知幽栖居士也来游湖，我应该约上你。”
[EN] On her way back, five young ladies walked toward her. Miao Zhiyu was among them. She spotted Zhu Shuzhen at first glance and went up to greet her: "If I'd known Youqi Jushi was also coming to tour the lake, I should have invited you."
[ZH] 千里寻心想，这一天怎么什么人都能遇上？苗知瑜倾慕文渊，千万不能让她知道她与文渊约会：“早知你来，我应该约上你，逛了一上午，我也乏了。苗姑娘是刚来吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought—how could she run into all sorts of people today? Miao Zhiyu admired Wenyuan. She absolutely couldn't let her know about the date with Wenyuan: “If I'd known you were coming, I should have invited you. I've been strolling all morning and I'm tired now. Have you just arrived, Young Lady Miao？”
[ZH] 苗知瑜看看身边的女友：“我与几位闺中密友相约在永泰茶楼喝茶，顺便在茶楼用了午饭，茶足饭饱后走一走，既可消食，又可欣赏美景。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu looked at the friends beside her: “I made an appointment with a few close friends to have tea at Yongtai Teahouse, and we also had lunch there. After tea and a full meal, we took a walk—it helps digest the food and lets us enjoy the beautiful scenery.”
[ZH] 千里寻感到疑惑：“永泰茶楼是喝茶的地儿，怎么也卖起饭来？”
[EN] Qianli Xun was puzzled: "Yongtai Teahouse is a place for tea. Why are they selling food now?"
[ZH] 一位闺秀解释道：“今日苗姑娘请客，她提前在聚香楼定了午饭，聚香楼的伙计按照约好的时间送到永泰茶楼，我们才得以享用。”
[EN] A young lady explained: "Today Young Lady Miao is treating us. She ordered lunch in advance from Juxiang Tower. The staff from Juxiang Tower delivered it to Yongtai Teahouse at the agreed time, so we could enjoy it."
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“原来宋代也有送外卖这档子事。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought: "So the Song Dynasty also has food delivery."
[ZH] 苗知瑜见朱淑真不言语，主动说：“我来时碰到东方公子他们在西湖北面的望景亭饮酒聚会，很奇怪，竟然没见到乔公子，幽栖居士可知道乔公子为何没来？”
[EN] Seeing Zhu Shuzhen stay silent, Miao Zhiyu proactively said: "On my way here, I ran into Young Master Dongfang and his group drinking and gathering at the Lake-viewing Pavilion on the north side of West Lake. Strangely, I didn't see Young Master Qiao at all. Does Youqi Jushi know why Young Master Qiao didn't come?"
[ZH] 千里寻本担心苗知瑜伤心，不愿将实情告知，可对方当着这么些人的面直接试探，她又何必为对方考虑？
[EN] Qianli Xun had been worried about hurting Miao Zhiyu's feelings and reluctant to tell the truth. But since the other party was probing directly in front of so many people, why should she consider her feelings?
[ZH] “当然知道，文渊今日与我有约，我早到了些，在白堤上逛逛，算算时间，他这会儿快到，你们逛，我要赶去与文渊相会，失陪。”
[EN] "Of course I know. Wenyuan and I have an appointment today. I arrived a bit early and strolled along Bai Causeway. Judging by the time, he should be arriving soon. You all enjoy your stroll. I need to hurry to meet Wenyuan. Excuse me."
[ZH] 千里寻说完，带着苦荞离开。
[EN] After speaking, Qianli Xun left with Kuqiao.
[ZH] 苗知瑜愣在那里，羡慕与悲伤之情袭上心头。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu stood there stunned, envy and sorrow overwhelming her.
[ZH] 一位闺秀议论道：“听说幽栖居士与练兵官刚和离，这么快跟乔公子在一起，看来以前的传言真实不虚，她和离会不会是因为乔公子？”
[EN] A young lady commented: "I heard Youqi Jushi just divorced the Military Training Officer. She's already with Young Master Qiao so quickly. It seems the rumors were true. Could her divorce have been because of Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] 另一位说：“我看是，否则怎会昨日和离，今日就相约逛西湖？唉，我们这些待字闺中的女子还眼巴巴等着父母之命媒妁之言，也不知得等到什么时候？幽栖居士不仅经历了婚姻，还把临安女子都倾慕的乔公子抢走，令我等自愧不如。”
[EN] Another said: "I think so too. Otherwise, how could she divorce yesterday and be invited to tour West Lake today? Sigh, we unmarried girls are still eagerly waiting for our parents' orders and the matchmaker's words. Who knows when that will be? Youqi Jushi has not only experienced marriage, but also snatched away Young Master Qiao, whom all the young ladies in Lin'an admire. We can't help but feel inferior."
[ZH] 苗知瑜不想再听下去：“幽栖居士的确令人羡慕，只可惜乔家正在为乔公子选亲，据我所知，乔家绝不会选离过婚的女子，好戏还在后头。我们还是走吧，不要辜负西湖美景。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu didn't want to listen anymore: "Youqi Jushi is indeed enviable. It's just that the Qiao family is currently choosing a bride for Young Master Qiao. As far as I know, the Qiao family will never choose a woman who has been divorced. The real show has yet to begin. Let's go. We shouldn't waste the beautiful scenery of West Lake."
[ZH] 苗知瑜的话既是说给他人听，也是宽慰自己。几位闺秀不再议论，继续欣赏美景。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu's words were meant for others to hear, but also to comfort herself. The young ladies stopped discussing and continued enjoying the scenery.
[ZH] 当她们经过陈浩宇等人烧烤之处时，苗知瑜一脸嫌弃，不予理会。其他几位闺秀也都快步向前走，担心被陈家公子注意到。
[EN] When they passed the spot where Chen Haoyu and his group were having a barbecue, Miao Zhiyu wore a look of disgust and ignored them. The other young ladies also walked quickly, worried about being noticed by Young Master Chen.
[ZH] 越担心什么，越来什么。跟陈浩宇一起烧烤的富家公子们瞧到几位大家闺秀，有的吹起口哨，有的调侃道：“几位美人！过来跟哥哥吃烧烤！肉刚烤好！香着呢！”
[EN] The more you worry about something, the more it happens. The rich young masters having a barbecue with Chen Haoyu spotted the young ladies. Some whistled, others teased: "Beautiful ladies! Come join us for barbecue! The meat is freshly roasted! It smells great!"
[ZH] 有位闺秀一边快走，一边轻声骂道：“纨绔子弟！”
[EN] One young lady walked quickly while cursing softly: "Playboys!"
[ZH] 两个富家公子准备上前去拉人，被陈浩宇阻止：“她们几个虽有几分姿色，人却无趣，况且她们爹的官职都在你们爹之上，小心她们回家告状，你们的爹吃不了兜着走。今日我们只管游玩，不问女色。一会儿吃完，再坐船玩儿。”
[EN] Two rich young masters were about to go over and grab them, but Chen Haoyu stopped them: “Those girls may have some looks, but they're boring. Besides, their fathers' ranks are all higher than yours. Be careful—if they go home and complain, your fathers will have a hard time. Today we just enjoy ourselves and don't bother with women. After we finish eating, we'll take a boat ride.”
[ZH] 陈公子发话，两位富家公子只得作罢，继续嬉皮笑脸，烟熏火燎地翻着正在炙烤的肉块。
[EN] With Young Master Chen speaking, the two rich young masters had to give up. They continued grinning, turning the roasting meat through the smoke and fire.

#CH 68
[ZH] 千里寻朝相约的望景亭而去，快要到达亭子时，看到文渊已站在亭中，一旁的南双手中提着食盒。
[EN] Qianli Xun headed toward the agreed-upon Wangjing Pavilion. As she approached, she saw Wenyuan already standing inside the pavilion, with Nanshuang holding a food carrier beside him.
[ZH] 食盒勾起腹内的馋虫，千里寻更感到饥饿，依然忍住，心里喜道：“没想到文渊也提前到了，还这么贴心，给我带来吃的。”
[EN] The food carrier stirred up her appetite, making Qianli Xun feel even hungrier. She suppressed it, thinking happily: "I didn't expect Wenyuan to arrive early too. How thoughtful of him to bring me food."
[ZH] 她想象着食盒中的美味，悄悄躲在离亭子不远的大石后面，石头不算太高，挡不住视线，不影响她观察文渊。
[EN] Imagining the delicacies inside the food carrier, she quietly hid behind a large rock not far from the pavilion. The rock wasn't too tall, so it didn't block her view or prevent her from observing Wenyuan.
[ZH] 苦荞边乐边跟着小姐躲起来。
[EN] Kuqiao followed her young lady with a smile, hiding along with her.
[ZH] 文渊背对着大石，从那背影能清晰地感受到他又兴奋又焦急又期待的心情。
[EN] Wenyuan had his back to the rock. From his posture, she could clearly sense his mixture of excitement, anxiety, and anticipation.
[ZH] 千里寻看了一会儿，实在坚持不下去，肚子咕咕叫，加之想要早些与文渊说话，便从大石后面现身，朝那背影唤了声：“文渊！”
[EN] After watching for a while, Qianli Xun couldn't hold out any longer. Her stomach grumbled, and she wanted to talk to Wenyuan sooner, so she stepped out from behind the rock and called out to his figure: "Wenyuan!"
[ZH] 乔文仙兴奋地转过身，上前拉起她的手：“淑真，没想到我们两个都早来了一些。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian turned around excitedly, stepped forward, and took her hand: "Shuzhen, I didn't expect both of us to arrive a bit early."
[ZH] 苦荞心想：“哪里是一些？我家小姐可是早到了一个时辰！”
[EN] Kuqiao thought to herself: "A bit early? My young lady arrived a whole two hours early!"
[ZH] 千里寻被文渊拉到亭中，乖乖坐在石桌跟前。
[EN] Qianli Xun was pulled into the pavilion by Wenyuan and obediently sat down at the stone table.
[ZH] “我怕你匆忙赶来，在家没吃饱，专门买来三种小吃，还热着呢。”乔文仙转向南双，“把小吃和桂花酿摆上。”
[EN] "I was worried you might rush here without eating properly at home, so I specially bought three kinds of snacks. They're still warm." Qiao Wenxian turned to Nanshuang: "Set out the snacks and osmanthus wine."
[ZH] 南双把食盒放在桌上，打开，将三盘小吃摆上，又拿出一瓶桂花酿，再摆上酒杯，还有两个瓷盘和筷子。
[EN] Nanshuang placed the food carrier on the table, opened it, and set out three plates of snacks. He then took out a bottle of osmanthus wine, along with wine cups, two porcelain plates, and chopsticks.
[ZH] 苦荞瞅一眼精美的食物，再看看手里一直拿的布袋，叹道：“糕点白带了，还是乔公子厉害。”
[EN] Kuqiao glanced at the exquisite food, then looked at the cloth bag in her hand, and sighed: "I brought pastries for nothing. Young Master Qiao is really something else."
[ZH] 面前的三盘小吃，千里寻觉得甚是好看，其中一道大约能猜出名字：“这道应是糯米藕，其它两道我不知名字，你能否告诉我？”
[EN] Looking at the three plates of snacks before her, Qianli Xun found them quite beautiful. She could roughly guess the name of one dish: "This should be glutinous rice lotus root. I don't know the names of the other two. Could you tell me?"
[ZH] 乔文仙想起淑真失忆之事，坐下耐心解释：“是桂花糯米藕，这道叫葱花肉，这道是刺毛肉圆。”
[EN] Remembering Shuzhen's amnesia, Qiao Wenxian sat down and patiently explained: "This is osmanthus glutinous rice lotus root. This one is called scallion pork, and this one is fuzzy meatball."
[ZH] “名字好，颜色也好，形状更好，味道肯定不错。”千里寻拿起筷子就要吃，想起花了心思的妆容，总得让文渊瞧瞧再说，她放下筷子，“文渊，你有没有觉得我今日有什么不同？”
[EN] "Great names, great colors, and even greater shapes. The taste must be wonderful too." Qianli Xun picked up her chopsticks, about to eat, but remembered her carefully applied makeup. She should let Wenyuan see it first. She put down her chopsticks: "Wenyuan, do you notice anything different about me today?"
[ZH] 乔文仙仔细瞧着朱淑真装扮娇美的容颜，他看得呆住……
[EN] Qiao Wenxian carefully looked at Zhu Shuzhen's beautifully adorned face. He was transfixed...
[ZH] 南双已将食盒收拾好，苦荞拉起他走到亭子外面。
[EN] Nanshuang had already tidied up the food carrier. Kuqiao pulled him outside the pavilion.
[ZH] 南双埋怨道：“苦荞姐姐为何要拉我出来？”
[EN] Nanshuang complained: "Why did you pull me out, Sister Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “没看到我家小姐跟你家公子那眼神，亲密着呢，你我还是躲远些，别影响他们。”
[EN] "Didn't you see the look between my young lady and your young master? They're being intimate. We should stay away and not disturb them."
[ZH] 南双这才醒悟过来：“还是苦荞姐姐心细。”
[EN] Only then did Nanshuang realize: "Sister Kuqiao is really thoughtful."
[ZH] 苦荞打开布袋：“我带的糕点与你家公子带的吃食比起来差远了，我家小姐肯定不会再稀罕，还是我们两个享用吧。”
[EN] Kuqiao opened the cloth bag: "The pastries I brought are nothing compared to what your young master brought. My young lady won't care about them anymore. Let's enjoy them ourselves."
[ZH] 南双把食盒放在地上，直接伸手从布袋里取一块糕点，咬了一口：“朱府的糕点实在好吃，多给我吃几块。”
[EN] Nanshuang put the food carrier on the ground and directly reached into the cloth bag to take a pastry. He took a bite: "The pastries from the Zhu residence are really delicious. Give me a few more."
[ZH] “放心，够你吃的。”苦荞边吃边瞧向亭子内，小姐与乔公子看上去真像一对神仙眷侣。
[EN] "Don't worry, there's enough for you to eat." Kuqiao ate while looking toward the pavilion. Her young lady and Young Master Qiao looked like a match made in heaven.
[ZH] 被文渊盯着，千里寻感到脸上发烫，她竟然不知不觉红了脸：“你为何盯着我看？”
[EN] Being stared at by Wenyuan made Qianli Xun feel her face burning. She had actually blushed without realizing it: "Why are you staring at me?"
[ZH] “因为你好看。”
[EN] "Because you're beautiful."
[ZH] “你有没有听过'女为己悦者容'？”
[EN] "Have you ever heard of 'a woman adorns herself for the one who delights in her'?"
[ZH] 乔文仙温柔地提醒道：“是'女为悦己者容'。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian gently corrected: "It's 'a woman adorns herself for the one who delights in her.'"
[ZH] “我给改了，'悦己'不如'己悦'，试想女子若不喜欢男子，又怎会为他梳妆打扮？文渊便是我心悦之人，故而，我一早起来梳妆打扮，只为见己悦之人。”
[EN] “I've changed it. 'Being delighted in' is not as good as 'delighting in someone.' Think about it—if a woman doesn't like a man, why would she dress up for him? Wenyuan is the one my heart delights in. So I got up early this morning to dress up, just to see the one who delights me.”
[ZH] “你怎样我都喜欢。”
[EN] "I like you no matter how you look."
[ZH] “难道我蓬头垢面，眼角带着眼屎，你也喜欢？”
[EN] "Even if I were unkempt and disheveled, with eye boogers in the corners of my eyes, you'd still like me?"
[ZH] 乔文仙被逗乐：“一来你绝不会蓬头垢面；二来，你即使不特意装扮依旧貌美；三来你我若成婚，每天清晨起床，你眼角的眼屎我定会看到，你既是我的人，那眼屎也是我的，我岂有嫌弃之理？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was amused: "For one thing, you'd never let yourself look unkempt. For another, you're beautiful even without trying. And for a third, if we were married, every morning when we wake up, I'd definitely see the eye boogers in the corners of your eyes. Since you're mine, even your eye boogers are mine. How could I possibly find them disgusting?"
[ZH] “没想到你这么会说情话，会不会哄过很多女孩子？”
[EN] "I didn't expect you to be so good at sweet talk. Have you coaxed many girls?"
[ZH] “我只心仪过你一人。”
[EN] "You're the only one I've ever been fond of."
[ZH] 千里寻更害羞，低下头，目光接触到美食，肚子又开始叫唤，既然自己什么样文渊都喜欢，又何必在乎涂得恰到好处的唇脂，还是开吃吧。
[EN] Qianli Xun became even more shy. She lowered her head. Her gaze met the food, and her stomach started grumbling again. Since Wenyuan liked her no matter how she looked, why worry about the perfectly applied lip rouge? Time to eat.
[ZH] 她拿起瓶子，给文渊和自己面前的杯子各倒些桂花酿：“你陪我一起吃。”
[EN] She picked up the bottle and poured some osmanthus wine into both Wenyuan's cup and her own: "Eat with me."
[ZH] 千里寻先拿起筷子吃了起来，边吃便夸赞：“不错！很好吃！”
[EN] Qianli Xun picked up her chopsticks and started eating, praising as she ate: "Delicious! Really good!"
[ZH] 乔文仙品着桂花酿，面带微笑，瞧着心爱的女子，她的吃相实在不像一位大家闺秀，可贵在真实、可爱。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian sipped his osmanthus wine with a smile, looking at his beloved. Her eating manner was truly unlike that of a refined young lady, but what was valuable was her authenticity and cuteness.
[ZH] “你怎么不吃？”千里寻边吃边问。
[EN] "Why aren't you eating?" Qianli Xun asked while eating.
[ZH] 乔文仙放下酒杯：“我吃过了，要是你吃不完，我会吃你剩下的。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian put down his wine cup: "I've already eaten. If you can't finish, I'll eat your leftovers."
[ZH] “你竟然愿意吃我剩下的？”
[EN] "You're actually willing to eat my leftovers?"
[ZH] 乔文仙没有回答，拿起筷子，夹了一个刺毛肉圆，送到她口边：“咬一大口。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian didn't answer. He picked up his chopsticks, picked up a fuzzy meatball, and brought it to her lips: "Take a big bite."
[ZH] 千里寻张开嘴巴，咬了大大一口，幸福地嚼着。
[EN] Qianli Xun opened her mouth, took a big bite, and chewed happily.
[ZH] 文渊的筷子中只夹着小小的一块儿，只见他毫不犹豫放入口中，津津有味的吃着。
[EN] Wenyuan only had a tiny piece on his chopsticks. Without hesitation, he put it in his mouth and ate with relish.
[ZH] 千里寻立刻断定，文渊是可与她相伴终生之人。她咽下口中的食物：“文渊，我想嫁给你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun immediately concluded that Wenyuan was the one she could spend her life with. She swallowed the food in her mouth: "Wenyuan, I want to marry you."
[ZH] 突如其来的表白和肯定，乔文仙喜出望外，他抬起一只手，抓住淑真纤细而又柔软的手指：“我此生非你不娶，今日回去，我就向爹娘表明，让他们到朱府提亲。”
[EN] The sudden confession and affirmation took Qiao Wenxian by surprise. He raised one hand and grasped Shuzhen's slender and soft fingers: "I will marry no one but you in this life. Today, when I return, I'll make it clear to my parents and have them come to the Zhu residence to propose."
[ZH] “我是不是有点太着急？”
[EN] "Am I being too hasty?"
[ZH] “不是你太着急，是我。你刚刚接受我，可我对你早已有情，又怎能不着急？我要趁你喜欢我，把你娶进门，让你成为我的人，省得整日担心你被别人抢走。”
[EN] “It's not you who's hasty—it's me. You've just accepted me, but I've had feelings for you for a long time. How could I not be hasty? I want to marry you while you like me, make you mine, so I don't have to worry all day about someone else stealing you away.”
[ZH] 千里寻担忧地问：“你的家人会不会嫌弃我离过一次婚？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked worriedly: "Will your family mind that I've been divorced once?"
[ZH] “家人嫌弃不重要，重要的是我不嫌弃，即使家人嫌弃，我也会想方设法说服他们。”
[EN] "It doesn't matter if my family minds. What matters is that I don't mind. Even if they do, I'll find a way to persuade them."
[ZH] 文渊的态度很肯定，千里寻自然对他充满信任：“文渊，有你真好，我以后不会觉得心无所依，也不会觉得不踏实，更不会觉得我与这个时代没有关系。”
[EN] Wenyuan's attitude was very certain. Qianli Xun naturally trusted him completely: "Wenyuan, it's so good to have you. From now on, I won't feel adrift, I won't feel unsettled, and I won't feel like I have no connection to this era."
[ZH] 乔文仙虽不大理解淑真为何会觉得她与这个时代没有关系，依然关切地说：“放心，我会永远陪在你身边。”
[EN] Although Qiao Wenxian didn't quite understand why Shuzhen felt she had no connection to this era, he still said with concern: "Don't worry, I'll always be by your side."
[ZH] 他们又深情对视了一会儿，千里寻才抽回手，继续享用美食。她把三盘食物吃得精光，没有给文渊留下吃剩食的机会，最后又喝了三杯桂花酿，才算真正享用完毕。
[EN] They gazed at each other affectionately for a while longer before Qianli Xun withdrew her hand and continued enjoying the food. She finished all three plates completely, not leaving Wenyuan any chance to eat the leftovers. Finally, she drank three more cups of osmanthus wine before truly finishing her meal.
[ZH] 乔文仙看到她嘴角沾有食物，拿出丝帕，为她轻轻擦去。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian saw food stuck at the corner of her mouth. He took out a silk handkerchief and gently wiped it away for her.
[ZH] 千里寻安心享受完文渊的照顾，建议道：“带我去坐船吧。”
[EN] After comfortably enjoying Wenyuan's care, Qianli Xun suggested: "Take me boating."
[ZH] 乔文仙点头答应。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian nodded in agreement.
[ZH] 糕点已吃完，苦荞提醒道：“他们吃完了，过去收拾吧。”
[EN] The pastries were finished. Kuqiao reminded: "They're done eating. Let's go clean up."
[ZH] 两个下人又进入亭中，将盘子、桂花酿等物品收拾到食盒中，目睹两位主人牵起对方的手，朝湖边租船的方向走去。
[EN] The two servants entered the pavilion again, collecting the plates, osmanthus wine, and other items into the food carrier. They watched their two masters take each other's hands and walk toward the lake to rent a boat.
[ZH] 他们租了一条不大不小的船只，上面有一个小房屋，可容一人休息。最惬意的莫过于外面放着圆形桌凳，可坐在那里边饮酒边赏美景。
[EN] They rented a medium-sized boat with a small cabin on it that could accommodate one person for rest. The most pleasant part was the round table and stools outside, where one could sit, drink, and enjoy the beautiful scenery.
[ZH] 乔文仙吩咐南双把剩下的桂花酿拿出来。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian instructed Nanshuang to bring out the remaining osmanthus wine.
[ZH] 南双照办，又摆上桂花酿和酒杯。
[EN] Nanshuang complied, setting out the osmanthus wine and wine cups again.
[ZH] 千里寻与乔文仙在桌旁坐下，南双与苦荞则坐在船尾。
[EN] Qianli Xun and Qiao Wenxian sat at the table, while Nanshuang and Kuqiao sat at the stern of the boat.
[ZH] 划船的人启动船只，在水的波动中，船只缓缓向水中行进，徜徉于水中的感觉十分美妙。
[EN] The boatman started the boat. Amid the rippling water, the vessel slowly moved forward into the lake. The feeling of drifting on the water was truly wonderful.
[ZH] 文渊将桂花酿给二人斟满，端起酒杯与心爱的女子互相碰杯后喝下。
[EN] Wenyuan filled both their cups with osmanthus wine. He raised his cup, clinked it with his beloved's cup, and they both drank.
[ZH] 此时，千里寻的内心比口中的桂花酿还要甜美。
[EN] At that moment, Qianli Xun's heart was even sweeter than the osmanthus wine in her mouth.

#CH 69
[ZH] 船只行到湖中心时，剩下的桂花酿已被二人喝完。
[EN] When the boat reached the center of the lake, the two had finished the remaining osmanthus wine.
[ZH] 千里寻拿起瓶子，继续往杯子里倒，只从瓶口掉下一滴。她放下瓶子：“桂花酿实在好喝，像我喝过的饮料。”
[EN] Qianli Xun picked up the bottle and tried to pour more into her cup, but only a single drop fell from the opening. She put down the bottle: “This osmanthus wine is so delicious—it reminds me of a soft drink I once had.”
[ZH] “饮料？是酒吗？我怎么没听说过？”乔文仙仔细搜索记忆，临安的美酒他再熟悉不过，在汴梁时，也品过不少汴梁的美酒，从未听过“饮料”这个酒名。
[EN] "A soft drink? Is it a kind of wine? I've never heard of it." Qiao Wenxian searched his memory carefully. He was intimately familiar with the fine wines of Lin'an, and had also tasted many wines from Bianliang, but he had never heard of a wine called "soft drink."
[ZH] 千里寻认真解释：“饮料不是酒，喝起来甜甜的，有水果的味道。”
[EN] Qianli Xun explained seriously: "A soft drink isn't wine. It tastes sweet, with a fruity flavor."
[ZH] 文渊对新鲜事物总是充满好奇：“不知哪里有？我可否品尝一下？”
[EN] Wenyuan was always curious about new things: "I wonder where I can find it? Could I taste it?"
[ZH] 千里寻瞎编道：“有一天我睡得很沉，做了一个神奇的梦，在梦中见到饮料，喝过一口，味道实在好喝，就记住了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun made something up: "One day I was sleeping deeply and had a magical dream. In the dream, I saw a soft drink and took a sip. It tasted so good that I remembered it."
[ZH] 文渊瞧着淑真像桃花一样微红的脸颊：“原来是黄粱一梦，饮料并非酒，可桂花酿是酒，喝多了会上头。”
[EN] Wenyuan looked at Shuzhen's peach-blossom-pink cheeks: “So it was just a golden millet dream—a fleeting illusion. A soft drink isn't wine, but osmanthus wine is. Drink too much, and it'll go to your head.”
[ZH] “难怪我觉得脸颊发热，原来桂花酿是酒，不过我脑子还很清晰，也不觉得晕，应该没有喝醉吧？”
[EN] “No wonder my face feels warm—so osmanthus wine is wine after all. But my mind is still clear, and I don't feel dizzy, so I shouldn't be drunk, right？”
[ZH] “你只是微醉，不会有事，即使真醉了，也不用担心，有我在你身边。”乔文仙靠近淑真，“你微醉的样子实在好看。”
[EN] “You're just slightly tipsy. You'll be fine. And even if you were really drunk, there's no need to worry—I'm right here with you.” Qiao Wenxian moved closer to Shuzhen. “You look really beautiful when you're tipsy.”
[ZH] 被文渊夸奖，千里寻既开心又害羞，在酒精的作用下站起来，望着周围茫茫的湖水：“湖水的味道真好闻！我感觉自己像是水中的仙子！”
[EN] Flattered by Wenyuan's compliment, Qianli Xun felt both happy and shy. Under the influence of alcohol, she stood up and gazed at the vast lake around them: "The lake smells so wonderful! I feel like a nymph dancing on the water!"
[ZH] 千里寻闭上眼睛，感受着湖面的微风拂过面颊，希望风将面颊上的红晕带走，不要让文渊看出她的娇羞。
[EN] Qianli Xun closed her eyes, feeling the gentle breeze from the lake brush against her cheeks. She hoped the wind would carry away the blush on her face so Wenyuan wouldn't notice her shyness.
[ZH] 她感受到一只手臂搂在腰间，她没有睁开眼，很自然地将头靠在对方的肩膀处，用心享受着文渊的臂膀带来的温暖。
[EN] She felt an arm wrap around her waist. Without opening her eyes, she naturally rested her head against his shoulder, savoring the warmth from Wenyuan's embrace.
[ZH] 苦荞用手把南双的眼睛捂住：“你还小，不能看。”
[EN] Kuqiao covered Nanshuang's eyes with her hand: "You're too young to watch this."
[ZH] 南双把苦荞的手挪开：“我已经十四了，一点都不小，苦荞姐姐比我大不了几岁，你看得我为何看不得？”
[EN] Nanshuang moved Kuqiao's hand away: “I'm already fourteen—not young at all. Sister Kuqiao, you're only a few years older than me. If you can watch, why can't I？”
[ZH] 苦荞不再管南双，他们一起瞧着这对情侣亲密的背影。
[EN] Kuqiao stopped bothering with Nanshuang. Together, they watched the intimate silhouette of the couple.
[ZH] 南双悄悄说：“苦荞姐姐，我家公子跟你家小姐真是郎才女貌。”
[EN] Nanshuang whispered: "Sister Kuqiao, your young lady and my young master are truly a talented man and a beautiful woman."
[ZH] 苦荞反对道：“不对，应该是女才郎貌，好像也不对，反正他们两个都是既有才又有貌，实在般配，我家小姐总算找对人。”
[EN] Kuqiao corrected: “No, it should be a talented woman and a handsome man. Hmm, that doesn't sound right either. Anyway, they're both talented and good-looking—a perfect match. My young lady has finally found the right person.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇等纨绔子弟乘坐一艘较大的船只行驶到湖中心，几人继续在船上烧烤，边说笑饮酒，边吃着炙烤的肉块。
[EN] Chen Haoyu and the other playboys were on a larger boat that had sailed to the center of the lake. They continued their barbecue on board, laughing, drinking, and eating grilled meat.
[ZH] 有人说：“你们瞧，那不是乔文仙吗？什么时候身边多了一位美娇娘？还那么亲密？真是少见！”
[EN] Someone said: “Look over there! Isn't that Qiao Wenxian? When did a beautiful lady appear by his side? And they're so intimate—how unusual！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇向不远处的船只瞧去：“哼！我当是谁！不就是我们陈家的亲家姑娘，没想到，他们两个搞到一起，也忒快了些。听说她昨日才与林觉和离，今日就跑到别的男人怀里，实在有失女德。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu glanced at the boat not far away: “Hmph! I thought it was someone! Isn't that the Zhu girl—our affinal kin? I didn't expect them to get together so fast. I heard she just divorced Lin Jue yesterday, and today she's already in another man's arms—shameless.”
[ZH] 有人问：“那女的难道是江南女词人朱淑真？”
[EN] Someone asked: "Could that woman be Zhu Shuzhen, the famous female poet from Jiangnan?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇很不屑：“除了她还能有谁？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu was dismissive: "Who else could it be?"
[ZH] 有人边吃炙烤的肉，边说：“陈公子看上去对这位亲家姑娘很不满。”
[EN] Someone said while eating grilled meat: "Young Master Chen seems quite dissatisfied with this affinal relative."
[ZH] “当然不满，她可是我的死对头。”陈浩宇心生一计，用手招来一位小弟，对着小弟的耳边悄悄说了几句。
[EN] “Of course I'm dissatisfied—she's my sworn enemy.” Chen Haoyu came up with a plan and beckoned one of his lackeys, whispering something in his ear.
[ZH] 那小弟走到四位划船的船工跟前，向他们传达了陈公子的意思。
[EN] The lackey went to the four boatmen and conveyed Young Master Chen's instructions.
[ZH] 只见船工开始用力划船，扭转大船的船头，向对方的船只撞去……
[EN] The boatmen began rowing hard, turning the bow of the large boat toward the other vessel and ramming into it...
[ZH] 千里寻睁开眼，仍旧沉浸在湖面美景和爱情之中，无意中用余光瞥见冲撞而来的大船，一把抱住文渊：“不好！”
[EN] Qianli Xun opened her eyes, still immersed in the lake's beauty and her romance. Out of the corner of her eye, she caught sight of the large boat charging toward them. She grabbed Wenyuan: "Oh no!"
[ZH] 乔文仙还未反应过来，大船已撞了上来，船身立刻颠簸起来，他与淑真一起掉进水里，还好二人识水性。
[EN] Before Qiao Wenxian could react, the large boat had crashed into them. The boat immediately lurched, and he and Shuzhen fell into the water. Fortunately, both could swim.
[ZH] 苦荞和南双坐着，问题不大，都担心自家小姐和公子。他们趴在船上，伸出手，去抓各自的主人，因船颠簸得厉害，怎么都无法抓住对方的手。
[EN] Kuqiao and Nanshuang, who were sitting, were fine, but they worried about their young lady and young master. They leaned over the boat, reaching out to grab their masters, but because the boat was rocking so violently, they couldn't grasp their hands.
[ZH] 船夫继续操纵船桨，用熟练的技巧努力控制颠簸的船只……
[EN] The boatman continued to work the oars, using skilled techniques to try to control the rocking boat...
[ZH] 船身未稳，二人只能先游到一起，准备待船稳后再爬上去。
[EN] With the boat still unsteady, the two could only swim together first, planning to climb back aboard once it stabilized.
[ZH] 传来一阵笑声：“哈哈哈……！”
[EN] A burst of laughter came over: "Hahaha..."
[ZH] 千里寻顺着笑声看去，不是陈浩宇又是哪个！
[EN] Qianli Xun followed the sound of laughter. Who else could it be but Chen Haoyu!
[ZH] 她大骂道：“陈浩宇！老娘今日没找你的事，你竟然主动欺负到我头上，待会儿老娘上去，看我怎么收拾你！”
[EN] She cursed loudly: “Chen Haoyu! I didn't come looking for trouble today, you hear me? Yet you dared to bully me! Just wait till I get over there—I'll teach you a lesson！”
[ZH] 乔文仙第一次见这样的淑真，既新奇又诧异。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had never seen Shuzhen like this before. He felt both intrigued and surprised.
[ZH] “哈哈……！都成落汤鸡，还嘴硬。”陈浩宇继续笑着，又向乔文仙说，“乔文仙，你放着临安那么多好姑娘不要，偏偏喜欢一个既离过婚，又是母老虎的女人，爱好实在独特，佩服佩服！”
[EN] “Hahaha...! You're both drowned rats, yet you still talk tough.” Chen Haoyu continued laughing, then turned to Qiao Wenxian: “Qiao Wenxian, with so many fine girls in Lin'an to choose from, you had to pick a woman who's both divorced and a tigress. Your taste is truly unique—I admire it！”
[ZH] 乔文仙虽觉得刚才的淑真与她所认识的不同，但不管淑真变成什么样，他都不介意。
[EN] Although Qiao Wenxian felt that Shuzhen just now was different from the one he knew, he didn't mind no matter what she became.
[ZH] “陈公子，请你不要污蔑淑真，她是有过一段婚姻，那又怎样？我喜欢的是她这个人，至于你说她是'母老虎'，我不以为然，淑真跟我在一起时，既温柔又可爱，根本不是你说的那样。我看她只有遇到像你这样的人，才会变成母老虎。”
[EN] “Young Master Chen, please don't slander Shuzhen. Yes, she's had a marriage before—so what? I like her for who she is. As for calling her a 'tigress,' I disagree. When Shuzhen is with me, she's both gentle and adorable—nothing like what you describe. I think she only turns into a tigress when she encounters someone like you.”
[ZH] 在水中，乔文仙转向淑真：“淑真，以后有我在你身边，不用再将自己伪装得那么凶，我会保护你。”
[EN] In the water, Qiao Wenxian turned to Shuzhen: "Shuzhen, from now on, with me by your side, you don't need to pretend to be so fierce. I'll protect you."
[ZH] 事实上千里寻并不需要文渊保护，可听到喜欢的人说关切她的话，感动在所难免：“文渊，有你真好。”
[EN] In fact, Qianli Xun didn't need Wenyuan's protection. But hearing words of concern from the one she loved, she couldn't help but be moved: "Wenyuan, it's so good to have you."
[ZH] 陈浩宇实在看不下去：“喂！你们两个要不要脸？一个是临安才子，一个是女词人，当着这么多人的面说那些肉麻的话，真是丢人！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu couldn't stand it anymore: “Hey! Do you two have any shame? One is Lin'an's talented scholar, the other a famous female poet, yet you're saying such lovey-dovey nonsense in front of so many people—how embarrassing！”
[ZH] 船已稳，在苦荞、南双和船工的帮助下，两人先后被拉上船只。
[EN] The boat had stabilized. With the help of Kuqiao, Nanshuang, and the boatmen, the two were pulled back aboard one after the other.
[ZH] 浑身上下已湿透，一大早的装扮算是全毁了，一切拜陈浩宇所赐。
[EN] They were soaked from head to toe. The careful dressing from early morning was completely ruined—all thanks to Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 对面的大船上，陈浩宇正在为他的杰作沾沾自喜，一群纨绔子弟都露出嘲笑的表情。
[EN] On the large boat opposite, Chen Haoyu was smugly pleased with his handiwork, while the group of playboys all wore mocking expressions.
[ZH] 千里寻气不打一处来：“陈浩宇！我两次碰到你光天化日之下欺负良家妇女，不要脸的是你，丢人的也是你！你等着，看我怎么收拾你！”
[EN] Qianli Xun was furious: “Chen Haoyu! Twice I've caught you bullying respectable women in broad daylight. The shameless one is you, the embarrassing one is you! Just you wait—I'll deal with you！”
[ZH] 她想要跳到对面的大船上。
[EN] She wanted to jump onto the large boat opposite.
[ZH] 乔文仙将她拦住：“离那船还有一段距离，恐怕跳不过去，你又掉到水里。不必跟他置气，我们把船划到那边，离他们远些。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stopped her: "There's still some distance to that boat. I'm afraid you won't make it and will fall into the water again. No need to get angry with him. Let's row our boat over there, away from them."
[ZH] 陈浩宇担心朱淑真跳过来打他一顿。按照他目测，两船之间的距离对朱淑真来讲没有任何阻碍，要不是乔文仙拦着，恐怕这会儿已被她三拳两脚打趴下，还是见好就收，不管怎么说今日算是把她搞得挺惨。于是大声命令：“赶紧把船划走！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu was worried that Zhu Shuzhen might jump over and beat him up. By his estimation, the distance between the two boats was no obstacle for Zhu Shuzhen. If Qiao Wenxian hadn't stopped her, he'd probably already be beaten down with three punches and two kicks by now. Better to take the win and run—after all, he'd made her pretty miserable today. So he shouted loudly: “Row the boat away quickly！”
[ZH] 船工卖力地划着，确定距离安全后，陈浩宇扯着嗓子喊道：“朱淑真！本公子今日总算赢你一回！哈哈哈……！”
[EN] The boatmen rowed hard. Once they confirmed a safe distance, Chen Haoyu shouted at the top of his lungs: "Zhu Shuzhen! This young master finally won one against you today! Hahaha..."
[ZH] 千里寻在心里骂道：“要不是顾及文渊，老娘今日肯定会跳到大船上，打得你跪地求饶！”
[EN] Qianli Xun cursed in her heart: "If it weren't for Wenyuan, I'd have jumped onto that big boat right now and beaten you until you begged for mercy on your knees!"
[ZH] 好好的一次乘船游湖，被陈浩宇搞得兴致全无。
[EN] What should have been a wonderful boat ride on the lake was completely ruined by Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 因为水凉，文渊打了个喷嚏：“阿嚏！”
[EN] Because the water was cold, Wenyuan sneezed: "Achoo!"
[ZH] “淑真，我们还是尽快靠岸，你我身上都湿漉漉的，需找个地方换件衣裳，北面有家裁缝铺，里面有成衣，我们过去现买一身换上。”
[EN] "Shuzhen, let's get to shore as soon as possible. We're both soaked and need to find a place to change clothes. There's a tailor's shop to the north that sells ready-made clothes. Let's go there and buy a set to change into."
[ZH] 千里寻无不同意，对船工说：“请两位师傅直接把船划到北岸，我们要在那里下船。”
[EN] Qianli Xun agreed without hesitation and said to the boatmen: "Please row the boat directly to the north shore. We'll disembark there."
[ZH] 船工调转船头，向西湖北岸滑去。
[EN] The boatmen turned the bow and glided toward the north shore of West Lake.

#CH 70
[ZH] 来到西湖北岸，下了船，南双请示道：“公子乘坐的马车离这里不远，我先过去把食盒放好，再到裁缝铺找您。”
[ZH] 乔文仙点头同意后，南双小跑着离开。
[ZH] 离裁缝铺还有一段路，乔文仙是男子，全身湿透，衣服贴在身上倒无伤大雅，可淑真是女子，令他看着心疼。
[ZH] 他四下瞅瞅，正好发现一位姑娘身上披着薄如羽翼的披风。
[ZH] “淑真，你在这里等会儿，我去去就来。”
[ZH] 千里寻还未点头，文渊已从她身边离开，朝一位姑娘走去。
[ZH] 千里寻感到伤心，小声嘀咕：“文渊看到美女舍我而去，难道他认识那位姑娘？”
[ZH] 苦荞也搞不懂乔公子要做什么。
[ZH] 只见文渊走到那姑娘跟前，先行礼，然后对那姑娘说着什么。又见那姑娘开始摇头，直到文渊指了指淑真，又指了指另一个方向，说了些话，那女子才点头，将披风取下，送到文渊手中。
[ZH] 看到这一幕，千里寻才恍然大悟，原来文渊是为她借披风。
[ZH] 文渊回来后，将披风为她披上，一股暖流涌入心头，她身上的冷意荡然无存。
[ZH] 文渊牵起她的手，他们穿过弯弯曲曲的小路，来到景区之外，一家裁缝铺出现在面前。
[ZH] 正要过去时，苗知瑜与闺中密友从裁缝铺刚好出来。
[ZH] 苗知瑜见乔公子拉着幽栖居士的手，二人甚是亲密，本想装作没看到，从一旁拐走，一位闺秀偏偏说：“你们瞧，那是乔公子与幽栖居士，手都拉到一起，看来二人在一起真实不虚。”
[ZH] 苗知瑜只好上前打招呼：“乔公子，幽栖居士，你们二人身上的衣裳怎么湿了？”
[ZH] 苗知瑜主动前来搭话，千里寻担心对方伤心，想要抽回手。
[ZH] 文渊却将她的手抓得更紧，向苗知瑜解释道：“我与淑真乘船赏湖景，不慎与其他船只相撞，我们两个就成了落汤鸡。”
[ZH] 千里寻接着解释：“听说这里有家裁缝铺，我们过来看看，买身成衣换上。”
[ZH] 苗知瑜强颜欢笑：“这家裁缝铺的布料质地不错，成衣的款式也好看，二位忙，知瑜还要与闺中密友继续逛。”
[ZH] “苗姑娘请便。”乔文仙继续牵着淑真向铺子内走去。
[EN] Stepping ashore on West Lake's northern bank, Nan Shuang bowed for instructions. "Your carriage is parked not far from here, Young Master. I'll go stow the food carrier first, then meet you at the tailor shop."
[ZH] 苗知瑜一阵心酸，面向乔文仙的背影，不由喊了一声：“乔公子！”
[EN] At Qiao Wenxian's nod, Nan Shuang jogged off.
[ZH] 乔文仙站住，回过头：“苗姑娘还有事？”
[EN] The tailor shop still lay a short walk away. As a man, having soaked clothes cling to his frame did not bother Qiao Wenxian, yet seeing Shuzhen drenched through made his heart ache. Scanning the bank, he spotted a young woman wearing a gossamer-thin cloak. "Wait here a moment, Shuzhen. I'll be right back."
[ZH] 千里寻也跟着站住，并未回头，她不想看到苗知瑜失望的表情，仅用耳朵仔细听着。
[EN] Before Qianli Xun could nod, Wenyuan walked away straight toward the stranger. A pinch of hurt pricked her chest as she muttered under her breath, "Sees a pretty girl and walks right off on me. Does he know her?" Kuqiao looked just as puzzled over what Young Master Qiao had in mind.
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“东方公子他们在不远处的亭子里聚会，乔公子一会儿可过去？”
[EN] Watching from afar, they saw Wenyuan bow to the woman and speak a few words. At first she shook her head, until Wenyuan gestured back toward Shuzhen, pointed down another path, and spoke again. Only then did the woman nod, unfasten her cloak, and hand it over. Understanding hit Qianli Xun at last: Wenyuan had gone to borrow a cloak for her.
[ZH] 乔文仙回道：“东方兄昨日已约过我，因我与淑真有约在先，已婉言拒绝，今日我只陪淑真，什么聚会都不参加。”
[EN] Returning, Wenyuan draped the cloak over her shoulders. Instant warmth flooded her chest, chasing away every trace of chill. Taking her hand, he led her along winding paths out past the lakeside grounds until a tailor shop came into view.
[ZH] 苗知瑜本就无事，不过是想多与乔文仙搭话而已，对方表明态度，反而令她无话可搭，行礼离开，边走边回头瞧着他们二人牵手进入裁缝铺内。她叹口气，不想说一句话，继续跟随闺中密友闲逛。
[EN] Just as they neared the door, Miao Zhiyu walked out of the tailor shop with a group of close female friends. Spotting Young Master Qiao holding Youqi Jushi's hand in plain sight, Miao Zhiyu meant to pretend she saw nothing and slip past, until one young lady spoke up. "Look! That's Young Master Qiao and Youqi Jushi, hand in hand! Guess those rumors about them being together are true after all!"
[ZH] 一位闺秀问：“知瑜，你该不会喜欢上乔公子？”
[EN] Cornered, Miao Zhiyu had no choice but to step up and greet them. "Young Master Qiao, Youqi Jushi, how did your clothes get drenched?"
[ZH] 苗知瑜没有回答，只当是默认。
[EN] Worried the sight might hurt Miao Zhiyu, Qianli Xun tried to slip her hand free. Instead, Wenyuan gripped her fingers tighter and explained smooth, "Shuzhen and I went boating on the lake and bumped into another craft by mishap, turning us both into drowned rats."
[ZH] 另一位闺秀说：“像乔公子那样的家世、人品和容貌，谁见了不喜欢？你们敢说自己不喜欢？”
[EN] Qianli Xun added, "Heard there's a tailor shop nearby, so we came over to pick out ready-made clothes to change into."
[ZH] 其他闺秀都承认道：“确实喜欢！”
[EN] Forcing a smile, Miao Zhiyu said, "Their fabrics are fine and the cuts look lovely. Go right ahead; Zhiyu still has shopping to do with her friends."
[ZH] 苗知瑜叹口气：“唉，只可惜，我们没有幽栖居士的福气，乔公子把幽栖居士的手拉得那么紧，仿佛怕她跑掉似的，让人看了羡慕。”
[EN] "Enjoy your outing, Lady Miao." Still holding Shuzhen's hand, Qiao Wenxian led her toward the shop door.
[ZH] 一位闺秀也叹道：“唉，我们几个只能寄希望于乔家选亲，要是有幸被选中，哪怕乔公子对我没那么好，只要在他身边，我也心满意足。”
[EN] A sharp pang gripped Miao Zhiyu. Gazing at his retreating back, she could not keep from calling out, "Young Master Qiao!"
[ZH] 这话说到苗知瑜的心里，就目前情形来看，她只能寄希望于此。
[EN] Halting, Qiao Wenxian glanced back over his shoulder. "Something else, Lady Miao?"
[ZH] 裁缝铺内，两人挑好成衣后，乔文仙向伙计买了两条干净的大帕子，给了苦荞一个。
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped beside him without turning around, unwilling to face Miao Zhiyu's downcast look, listening sharp instead.
[ZH] 苦荞拿着帕子带小姐进入铺子里间专供客人试衣裳的地方，把小姐身上的湿衣裳脱下，用帕子帮小姐把身上的水擦干，再换上新的衣裳，又把头发重新整理一番。
[EN] "Young Master Dongfang and the others are gathering at a pavilion not far off," Miao Zhiyu said. "Will you go over in a bit?"
[ZH] 当千里寻收拾完毕，从里间出来时，南双刚好回来，他负责伺候公子进里间换衣裳。
[EN] "Brother Dongfang invited me yesterday, and I already declined since Shuzhen and I had plans first," Qiao Wenxian answered clear. "Today I'm staying with Shuzhen alone. No gatherings for me."
[ZH] 走进一位姑娘，上前对千里寻说：“姑娘，可以把披风还给我吗？”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu had only wanted to keep him talking a moment longer; his blunt answer left nothing more to say. She curtsied and walked off, glancing back over her shoulder as the two stepped hand in hand inside the shop. Letting out a sigh, she fell mute as she wandered along beside her friends.
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“多谢姑娘。”
[EN] "Zhiyu, don't tell me you fell for Young Master Qiao?" one girl asked.
[ZH] 苦荞把披风送到姑娘手中，姑娘接过，夸道：“你的夫君对你可真好。”说完那姑娘拿着披风离开。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu stayed quiet, which said yes plain enough.
[ZH] 千里寻品味着“夫君”一词，跟林觉有婚姻时，这个词对她来讲是个累赘，时刻想卸掉，现在品味起来却别有一番滋味。
[EN] "With Young Master Qiao's lineage, character, and looks, who wouldn't like him?" another girl chimed in. "Dare any of you swear you don't?"
[ZH] 裁缝铺的伙计看一眼放在桌上的湿衣裳，主动建议：“姑娘，你们前来逛西湖，拿着湿衣裳实在不方便，不如放在我们铺子，我找人给您洗干净，再熨烫平整了，您派人来取即可。”
[EN] The others all confessed together, "We do!"
[ZH] 千里寻乐道：“没想到你们裁缝铺也承接洗衣店的业务。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu sighed again. "Pity none of us have Youqi Jushi's luck. Saw how tight Young Master Qiao held her hand, as if afraid she might vanish? Makes anyone envious."
[ZH] 伙计说：“这年头只要客人需要，又能赚银子，何乐而不为？”
[EN] Another young lady echoed with a sigh, "All we can pin our hopes on is the Qiao family's bride selection. If one of us gets chosen, even if Young Master Qiao doesn't dote on me quite that much, just staying by his side would be enough." Those words struck straight to Miao Zhiyu's heart; as things stood, that bride selection was her sole remaining hope.
[ZH] 千里寻示意苦荞将银子给了。
[EN] Inside the tailor shop, once both chose ready-made outfits, Qiao Wenxian bought two large clean cloths from the clerk and handed one to Kuqiao. Taking the cloth, Kuqiao escorted her mistress into the shop's inner fitting room, helped her peel off the wet robes, dried her skin, dressed her in crisp new garments, and smoothed her hair back in place.
[ZH] 苦荞拿出银子，递给伙计。
[EN] The moment Qianli Xun stepped back out into the front room, Nan Shuang arrived right on cue and escorted his master inside to change. Just then a young woman walked in and approached Qianli Xun. "Excuse me, miss. May I have my cloak back?"
[ZH] 伙计接过问道：“那位公子的也一起算吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled warm. "Thank you so much, miss."
[ZH] 苦荞说：“当然。”
[EN] Kuqiao handed the cloak back. Accepting it, the girl beamed, "Your husband treats you so well!" With that, she left carrying her cloak.
[ZH] 伙计随口说道：“看来你们是一家人。”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned the word "husband" over in her mind. Back when she was tied to Lin Jue, that word had felt like a dead weight she could not wait to shed; savoring it now brought an altogether different sweetness.
[ZH] “一家人”又让千里寻十分享受，她希望与文渊成为一家人，忍不住想象他们婚后的情景，相亲相爱，儿女双全……
[EN] Eyeing the damp garments on the table, the shop clerk offered, "Miss, touring West Lake lugging wet clothes is a hassle. Why not leave them at our shop? I'll have someone wash them clean and press them flat, and you can send a servant to pick them up."
[ZH] “淑真，你在想什么？”
[EN] Qianli Xun chuckled. "Didn't know tailor shops ran laundry service too."
[ZH] 文渊的声音将千里寻从想象中拉回现实。
[EN] "These days, if a customer needs it and there's silver to earn, why not?"
[ZH] 文渊已换完衣裳，在新衣裳的衬托下，五官显得更加立体，皮肤也愈加白皙。
[EN] At Qianli Xun's glance, Kuqiao counted out silver and handed it to the clerk. Taking the coin, the clerk asked, "Include that young master's wet clothes too?"
[ZH] 千里寻羡慕不已，不由伸手向文渊脸上摸去：“你的皮肤真好，得让多少女孩子嫉妒？”
[EN] "Of course," Kuqiao replied.
[ZH] 乔文仙抓住淑真的手：“你不必嫉妒，因为我是你的人。”
[EN] "Looks like you're all one family," the clerk remarked offhand.
[ZH] 直接的表白，令周围听到的人都不好意思起来。
[EN] "One family" warmed Qianli Xun straight through. Longing to build a real home with Wenyuan, she drifted into daydreams of married life: loving each other deep, a son and daughter running around their feet...
[ZH] 千里寻觉得文渊的话比美酒更令人陶醉，她在微醺的状态下，又被文渊牵着手，拉出裁缝铺。
[EN] "Shuzhen, what are you thinking about?"
[ZH] 一路上她只顾陶醉在“我是你的人”这句话中，时不时露出傻笑。
[EN] Wenyuan's voice pulled her back to earth. Freshly changed into new robes, his features looked even crisper, his complexion glowing pale and smooth. Envying skin like that, Qianli Xun reached right up to touch his cheek. "Your skin is so smooth. How many girls must envy you?"
[ZH] 文渊倒知趣，也不打扰她，一味地抓住她的手。
[EN] Catching her hand in his palm, Qiao Wenxian murmured, "No need to envy me, because I belong to you."
[ZH] 他们又来到湖边，默默地走着，享受着二人之间此时无声胜有声的默契感。
[EN] Such a blunt confession made everyone within earshot blush and look aside. To Qianli Xun, his words went straight to her head stronger than fine wine; floating in a happy daze, she let him clasp her hand and lead her back out of the tailor shop. All down the lane she stayed wrapped inside "I belong to you," breaking into silly grins every few steps, while Wenyuan read her mood well enough not to interrupt, keeping her hand locked in his.
[ZH] 走着走着，传来女子唱曲儿的声音，真是歌喉婉转，悦耳动听。
[EN] Reaching the lakeshore again, they strolled in comfortable hush, soaking in a bond where words felt needless. Along the walk, a woman's clear singing floated over the breeze, sweet and lyrical.
[ZH] 乔文仙终于说话：“这唱词正是你写的一首《菩萨蛮》。”
[EN] At last Qiao Wenxian spoke. "Those lyrics are your 'Pusaman.'"
[ZH] 千里寻仔细听那唱词，果然听到：“山亭水榭秋方半，凤帏寂寞无人伴。愁闷一番新，双蛾只旧颦。……”
[EN] Listening close, Qianli Xun caught the opening lines: "Hill pavilions and waterside halls stand at mid-autumn; lone behind phoenix curtains, no one keeps me company. Fresh sorrow wells anew, while my moth brows knit in the same old frown..."
[ZH] 二人不由循着声音走去。他们穿过杨柳小径，面前出现一大片荷叶，有些荷花已凋谢，有些正在开放，有些还是花骨朵。离荷叶不远处是座亭子，亭子里围着些人，中间坐着一位歌伎，一边抚琴一边唱：“起来临绣户，时有疏萤度。我谢月相怜，今宵不忍圆。……”
[EN] Drawn by the music, the two followed a willow-lined path until a vast spread of lotus leaves opened ahead. Some blossoms had faded, some stood in full bloom, and others remained tight buds. Just beyond rose a lakeside pavilion ringed with listeners, a singing girl seated at its center strumming a zither as she sang: "Rising to stand by the embroidered door, sparse fireflies drift past now and then. I thank the moon for pitying me—tonight it cannot bear to shine full." [TN: Translator's rendering]
[ZH] 千里寻叹道：“不知是谁谱的曲子，竟然如此好听。”
[EN] "Who set that to music?" Qianli Xun breathed in wonder. "What a lovely tune!"
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“还能是谁？当然是东方兄，他除了爱好诗词，也是谱曲高手，最喜欢为你的诗词谱曲，估计今儿谱了新曲子，才邀请大家来听。我们既然来了，不如过去打声招呼。”
[EN] "Who else? Brother Dongfang, of course," Qiao Wenxian smiled. "Beyond loving poetry, he's a master at composing melodies, and setting your lyrics to music is his favorite pastime. He must have written a new score today and invited everyone to hear it. Since we're right here, let's go say hello."
[ZH] 千里寻朝亭子望去，果然是东方尘泽他们，还有几位她没见过的公子。既然文渊已发话，她心甘情愿地点点头。
[EN] Glancing toward the pavilion, Qianli Xun spotted Dongfang Chenze's circle alongside several unfamiliar young gentlemen. Since Wenyuan suggested it, she nodded without hesitation.

#CH 71
[ZH] 千里寻与乔文仙走到亭子跟前时，歌伎正好唱完。
[EN] When Qianli Xun and Qiao Wenxian reached the pavilion, the female singer had just finished her song.
[ZH] 东方尘泽端起一杯酒送到歌伎面前：“想绒姑娘唱得不错，这杯酒是我赏你的。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze picked up a cup of wine and held it out to the singer: "Miss Xiangrong sang wonderfully. This cup is my reward to you."
[ZH] “谢谢东方公子赞赏。”想绒接过，一口喝下，将酒杯在众人面前亮亮。
[EN] "Thank you for your praise, Young Master Dongfang." Xiangrong took it, drank it in one gulp, and showed the empty cup to everyone.
[ZH] 东方尘泽夸道：“想绒姑娘好酒品，不如再来一杯？”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze praised: "Miss Xiangrong holds her liquor well. How about another cup?"
[ZH] 传来乔文仙的声音：“你们不要为难想绒姑娘，她要是喝醉，怎么给大家唱曲儿？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian's voice came over: "Don't put Miss Xiangrong in a difficult position. If she gets drunk, how will she sing for everyone?"
[ZH] 所有人向亭外瞧去，见乔文仙牵着幽栖居士的手，立刻什么都明白。
[EN] Everyone looked outside the pavilion and saw Qiao Wenxian holding the hand of the Youqi Jushi. They immediately understood everything.
[ZH] 东方尘泽上前说：“难怪乔兄昨日不答应我，原来是与佳人有约。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze stepped forward and said: "No wonder you declined my invitation yesterday, Brother Qiao. It turns out you had a date with a beauty."
[ZH] 当着一群公子的面，千里寻不好意思再被文渊牵着，她抽回手：“我与文渊被唱曲儿的声音吸引而来，没成想遇到你们。”
[EN] In front of the group of young men, Qianli Xun felt embarrassed to continue being held by Wenyuan. She withdrew her hand: "Wenyuan and I were drawn here by the sound of singing. We didn't expect to run into you all."
[ZH] 曹班说：“相请不如偶遇，留下来与我们一起听曲儿，聊聊人生，再聊聊天下大事。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "A chance meeting is better than a planned invitation. Stay and listen to songs with us, chat about life, and discuss national affairs."
[ZH] 乔文仙用目光向淑真请示。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian looked at Shuzhen for her permission.
[ZH] 千里寻明白文渊的心思，此时若不答应，岂不是当众驳了他的面子？
[EN] Qianli Xun understood Wenyuan's thoughts. If she didn't agree now, wouldn't she be embarrassing him in front of everyone?
[ZH] “既如此，我与文渊留下与诸位聊聊。”
[EN] "In that case, Wenyuan and I will stay and chat with you all."
[ZH] 安明轩说：“乔兄身边有幽栖居士这样知书达理的佳人，实在令我等羡慕。”
[EN] An Mingxuan said: "With a refined and accomplished beauty like the Youqi Jushi by his side, Brother Qiao is truly the envy of us all."
[ZH] 乔文仙只顾乐，并不回应。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian just smiled happily and didn't respond.
[ZH] 其他人听到面前这位女子是幽栖居士，一个个上前行礼：“幽栖居士，久闻大名，今日得以相见实乃荣幸！”
[EN] When the others heard that the woman before them was the Youqi Jushi, they came forward one by one to greet her: "Youqi Jushi, we've long heard of your fame. It's truly an honor to meet you today!"
[ZH] 想绒也走上前：“我刚才唱的正是幽栖居士的词，定是这词将你招来，想绒这厢有礼。”
[EN] Xiangrong also stepped forward: "The lyrics I just sang were written by the Youqi Jushi. It must have been these words that drew you here. Xiangrong pays her respects."
[ZH] 朱淑真受同时代人喜爱，千里寻倍感荣耀，她扶住想绒：“你我看着年纪相仿，以后就是朋友，哪有朋友拜朋友之礼？”
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen was beloved by her contemporaries, and Qianli Xun felt deeply honored. She helped Xiangrong up: "You and I look about the same age. From now on, we're friends. How can friends bow to friends?"
[ZH] 想容激动道：“幽栖居士愿意将我当朋友看待？”
[EN] Xiangrong said excitedly: "The Youqi Jushi is willing to treat me as a friend?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“当然。”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded: "Of course."
[ZH] 想绒双眼微红，看上去像要哭的样子。
[EN] Xiangrong's eyes turned slightly red, as if she were about to cry.
[ZH] 千里寻很是不解：“为何我把你当朋友，你却难过？”
[EN] Qianli Xun was puzzled: "Why do you look sad when I'm treating you as a friend?"
[ZH] 想绒解释道：“不是难过，是高兴。”
[EN] Xiangrong explained: “Not sad—happy.”
[ZH] 曹班说：“幽栖居士有所不知，想绒是花云楼的姑娘，从来没有大家闺秀愿与她做朋友。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "Youqi Jushi may not know this, but Xiangrong is a girl from the Huayun Tower. No refined young lady has ever been willing to be her friend."
[ZH] “花云楼是什么地方？”千里寻担心眼前这位女子是像春金巧那样的商妓。
[EN] "What kind of place is the Huayun Tower?" Qianli Xun worried that the woman before her might be a prostitute like Chun Jinqiao.
[ZH] 想绒赶紧解释：“幽栖居士放心，花云楼的姑娘卖艺不卖身，凭技艺讨口饭吃。”
[EN] Xiangrong quickly explained: "Please rest assured, Youqi Jushi. The girls at the Huayun Tower perform arts but not their bodies. We earn our rice by our skills."
[ZH] 千里寻总算放心：“职业不分贵贱，都是凭本事吃饭。”
[EN] Qianli Xun was finally relieved: “No profession is noble or base—we all earn our living by our skills.”
[ZH] 乔文仙被冷落在一旁，故意装作伤心的样子：“你们都围着幽栖居士，看来我是多余的，不如就此离去。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was left on the sidelines. He deliberately put on a sad expression: "You're all gathered around the Youqi Jushi. It seems I'm superfluous. I might as well leave."
[ZH] “乔兄别装了，幽栖居士在此，你肯走？好好坐着！”东方尘泽一把抓住乔文仙，把他按在石桌旁。
[EN] "Stop pretending, Brother Qiao. With the Youqi Jushi here, you'd actually leave? Sit down properly!" Dongfang Chenze grabbed Qiao Wenxian and pushed him down at the stone table.
[ZH] 想绒说：“能认识幽栖居士，我今儿高兴，把刚才的曲子再唱一遍，东方公子放心，接下来我唱的所有曲子不收取一分一毫。”
[EN] Xiangrong said: “I'm so happy to have met the Youqi Jushi today. I'll sing the song again. Don't worry, Young Master Dongfang—I won't charge a single coin for any of the songs I sing today.”
[ZH] 东方尘泽自然高兴：“那感情好，我们都是沾了幽栖居士的光。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze was naturally pleased: "That's wonderful. We're all riding on the Youqi Jushi's coattails."
[ZH] 接下来的时光，大家在亭中听曲儿、饮酒、观景，又畅聊人生……
[EN] For the rest of the time, everyone listened to songs, drank wine, enjoyed the scenery in the pavilion, and chatted freely about life...
[ZH] 千里寻看着这群年轻的公子，心想：“年轻真好，无忧无虑。他们每日这么开心地享受生活，却不知不久的将来这个时代会面临什么。”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked at this group of young men and thought: "How wonderful it is to be young and carefree. They enjoy life so happily every day, unaware of what this era will soon face."
[ZH] 想到不久要来临的时代变迁，千里寻更是感谢文渊出现在她穿越而来的人生中，以后不管发生什么，只要能与文渊在一起，什么都不重要。
[EN] Thinking of the era's upheaval that would soon come, Qianli Xun felt even more grateful that Wenyuan had appeared in her life after she transmigrated. No matter what happened in the future, as long as she could be with Wenyuan, nothing else mattered.
[ZH] 随着时间推移，聚会的人一个一个散去，因花云楼有时间规定，想绒姑娘也带着丫鬟离去，亭内只剩下千里寻和四位公子。
[EN] As time passed, the people at the gathering dispersed one by one. Because the Huayun Tower had curfew rules, Miss Xiangrong also left with her maid. Only Qianli Xun and the four young men remained in the pavilion.
[ZH] 四位公子凑在一处聊着天下大事，千里寻并不感兴趣。
[EN] The four young men gathered together to discuss affairs of the realm. Qianli Xun wasn't interested.
[ZH] 她看向四周，寻找苦荞的身影，原来她跟南双和化雨正坐在湖边的石头上聊天儿，他们有他们的世界，千里寻更不想打扰。
[EN] She looked around for Kuqiao and found her sitting on a rock by the lake, chatting with Nanshuang and Huayu. They had their own world, and Qianli Xun didn't want to disturb them.
[ZH] 她拿起石桌上的酒酿给自己倒上一杯，边品边听四位公子聚集在一旁聊天儿。
[EN] She picked up the sweet rice wine on the stone table and poured herself a cup, sipping it while listening to the four young men chatting nearby.
[ZH] 安明轩说：“你们可知大宋与金国结盟后，攻打辽国燕京，两次均以失败告终，听说金国打算派兵再次攻打辽国。”
[EN] An Mingxuan said: "Do you know that after the Great Song allied with the Jin Dynasty, we attacked Yanjing of the Liao Dynasty twice, and both times we failed? I've heard that the Jin Dynasty plans to send troops to attack the Liao Dynasty again."
[ZH] 曹班说：“我大宋泱泱大国竟然打不败一个辽国？难道燕云十六州要旁落他人？”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "Our Great Song, such a great and mighty nation, can't even defeat the Liao Dynasty? Are the Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun going to fall into others' hands?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“金国看似与大宋结盟，不过是因辽与大宋都是它潜在的对手，只能联合一个攻打另一个，等到它强大后绝对会反咬一口。我猜金国若打败辽国，绝对不会将燕云十六州拱手相让。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said: "The Jin Dynasty may seem allied with the Great Song, but only because both the Liao and the Song are its potential rivals. It must ally with one to attack the other. Once it grows strong enough, it will absolutely turn on us. I suspect that if the Jin defeats the Liao, it will never hand over the Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun to us willingly."
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“当时结盟时都已说定，燕云十六州要大宋自己打，可大宋军队节节败退，要拿回燕云十六州恐怕无望。唉，大宋表面繁华，其实内忧不断，外患难防。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said: "When the alliance was formed, it was agreed that the Great Song would have to take the Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun by itself. But our army has been suffering defeat after defeat. It's probably hopeless to recover the Sixteen Prefectures. Alas, the Great Song appears prosperous on the surface, but in reality, we face constant internal troubles and external threats that are hard to defend against."
[ZH] 曹班说：“说起内忧，百姓起义不断，去年方腊起义被剿灭后，今年宋江等人也被斩杀，听说有个叫贾进的也集结一帮人在相州作乱。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "Speaking of internal troubles, the people keep rising up in rebellion. After the Fang La Rebellion was suppressed last year, Song Jiang and his men were executed this year. I've heard that someone named Jia Jin has also gathered a group of people and is causing chaos in Xiangzhou."
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“你这消息已经晚了，我听说相州有位英雄，名叫岳飞，主动请缨，已将这股势力剿灭。所以大宋最大的敌人不是自己人，而是金人。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said: "Your news is outdated. I've heard that there's a hero in Xiangzhou named Yue Fei who volunteered to lead the troops and has already suppressed this force. So the Great Song's greatest enemy is not our own people, but the Jin."
[ZH] 听着他们的讨论，千里寻觉得好笑，有什么好讨论的？金军南下攻打大宋，历史书上都写着呢。她穿越成朱淑真，必然要亲历北宋灭亡，想到这里，又觉悲伤，不是为时代变迁，而是为人生变幻莫测，难以预料。
[EN] Listening to their discussion, Qianli Xun found it amusing. What was there to discuss? The Jin army's southern invasion of the Great Song was written in history books. Having transmigrated into Zhu Shuzhen, she would inevitably witness the fall of the Northern Song firsthand. Thinking of this, she felt sad—not for the era's changes, but for how unpredictable life is, how it can change in an instant.
[ZH] 一悲伤，酒又多喝了几杯，瞧着夕阳西下的湖面，才发现已出来许久。
[EN] Feeling sad, she drank a few more cups. Looking at the lake under the setting sun, she realized they had been out for a long time.
[ZH] 文渊与三位公子依然聊得火热，千里寻不愿打扰，还是继续饮酒等待，她从未像今日这般喜欢默默地等待一个人……
[EN] Wenyuan and the three young men were still chatting enthusiastically. Qianli Xun didn't want to disturb them, so she continued drinking and waiting. She had never liked silently waiting for someone as much as she did today...
[ZH] 申简辰一早派白风去西三胡同守着，他闲来无事，到府衙走一趟，在办理公务的屋子坐了一会儿，除几个官员前来攀关系寒暄外，林觉也过来一趟，请他有时间给临安士兵训话，还说仪王亲临，必然鼓舞士气。
[EN] Early that morning, Shen Jianchen had sent Baifeng to keep watch at West Third Alley. With nothing else to do, he visited the government office and sat in the room where official business was conducted. Aside from a few officials who came to curry favor and make small talk, Lin Jue also came by and asked him to give a speech to the Lin'an soldiers when he had time, saying that the Yi Wang's personal appearance would surely boost morale.
[ZH] 申简辰点点头，并未说出明确答应的话。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded but didn't give a clear answer.
[ZH] 林觉离开后，无人再来打扰，申简辰甚觉无聊。离开府衙，又在街上逛了一会儿，才乘坐马车回幽林別居。
[EN] After Lin Jue left, no one else disturbed him. Shen Jianchen felt quite bored. He left the government office, wandered the streets for a while, and then took a carriage back to the Youlin Villa.
[ZH] 路上他不由自主透过马车的窗户向神柳瞧去，夕阳下的神柳别具一番韵味，勾起他的兴致，又想去神柳下坐会儿。
[EN] On the way, he couldn't help looking out the carriage window at the Divine Willow. The Divine Willow under the setting sun had a unique charm that piqued his interest, and he wanted to go sit under it again.
[ZH] 当马车到达幽林别居门口时，他没有回屋，只身穿过幽林，向池塘边的神柳走去。
[EN] When the carriage arrived at the gate of the Youlin Villa, he didn't go inside. Instead, he walked alone through the Youlin forest toward the Divine Willow by the pond.

#CH 72
[ZH] 到达神柳处，夕阳已渐渐消失，天边一轮新月缓缓升起。天还不是很黑，月亮的身影不像在漆黑的夜空中那么清晰可见。
[EN] By the time they reached the Divine Willow, the setting sun had gradually faded, and a new moon was slowly rising on the horizon. It wasn't fully dark yet, so the moon's silhouette wasn't as clear as it would be on a pitch-black night.
[ZH] 申简辰依旧坐在石头上，靠着神柳粗壮的树干，在心里默默与神柳沟通。
[EN] Shen Jianchen still sat on the rock, leaning against the thick trunk of the Divine Willow, silently communicating with it in his heart.
[ZH] “如若你把千里寻已带回去，拜托把我也带回去吧；如若她还在临安，请你让我尽快找到她。”
[EN] "If you've already brought Qianli Xun back, please take me back too; if she's still in Lin'an, please let me find her as soon as possible."
[ZH] 神柳没有任何反应，申简辰闭上眼睛继续用心与它沟通。
[EN] The Divine Willow gave no response. Shen Jianchen closed his eyes and continued to reach out to it with all his heart.
[ZH] 传来一个声音：“阿弥陀佛。”
[EN] A voice came: "Amitabha."
[ZH] “难道是神柳的声音？”申简辰睁开双目，看到一位老尼站在神柳之下，面目慈祥，笑容可掬。
[EN] "Could it be the voice of the Divine Willow?" Shen Jianchen opened his eyes and saw an elderly nun standing beneath the Divine Willow, her face kind and her smile beaming.
[ZH] 原来不是神柳，申简辰失望之余起身打招呼：“阿弥陀佛，师傅可来自山上的尼姑庵？”
[EN] So it wasn't the Divine Willow after all. Disappointed, Shen Jianchen stood up and greeted her: "Amitabha. Master, do you come from the nunnery on the mountain?"
[ZH] 老尼正是净光法师：“正是，我经常见施主一个人坐在神柳之下，今日没忍住过来打声招呼。”
[EN] The elderly nun was precisely Master Jingguang: "Indeed. I often see you sitting alone beneath the Divine Willow. Today I couldn't resist coming over to say hello."
[ZH] 申简辰感到疑惑，他从未在神柳旁遇到过老尼，为何她会经常看到他？难道从山上能看到山下的情景？
[EN] Shen Jianchen felt puzzled. He had never encountered the elderly nun by the Divine Willow before. Why would she say she often saw him? Could she see what was happening at the foot of the mountain from up above?
[ZH] 申简辰向山上望去，山不算太高，但要从山上看到神柳还是有难度，除了树木遮盖外，从上而下的距离也为俯瞰带来困难。
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked up toward the mountain. It wasn't particularly high, but it would still be difficult to see the Divine Willow from the summit. Aside from the tree cover, the vertical distance made it hard to look down and spot anything.
[ZH] 净光法师仿佛猜中申简辰所想，笑着解释：“我好几次从后山下来，施主专注神柳，从未注意过我。”
[EN] Master Jingguang seemed to guess what Shen Jianchen was thinking. She smiled and explained: "I've come down from the back of the mountain several times. You were so focused on the Divine Willow that you never noticed me."
[ZH] “原来如此，请问师傅，神柳可是尼姑庵的？”申简辰想多了解神柳，毕竟与他穿越之前见到的神柳一模一样。
[EN] "I see. May I ask, Master, does the Divine Willow belong to the nunnery?" Shen Jianchen wanted to learn more about the Divine Willow, after all, it was exactly the same as the one he had seen before he transmigrated.
[ZH] 净光法师说：“神柳并非属于尼姑庵，我师傅圆寂前告诉我，这棵神柳已在此待了上千年，她还说神柳依旧有将近一千多年的寿命，试想到那时你我都已不在，而它还活着，也不知它以后见证的时代会是什么样。”
[EN] Master Jingguang said: “The Divine Willow doesn't belong to the nunnery. Before my master passed into nirvana, she told me it had been here for over a thousand years. She also said it still has nearly another thousand years of life ahead. Imagine—by then, you and I will be long gone, yet it will still be alive. Who knows what era it will witness.”
[ZH] 申简辰在心里推算着时间，越推算他越激动，这棵神柳正是他在现代社会看到的那棵，以前虽也这么认为，只是凭感觉，听老尼这么一说，才真正确定它正是带他来到这个时代的神柳。
[EN] Shen Jianchen calculated the timeline in his mind. The more he calculated, the more excited he became. This Divine Willow was exactly the one he had seen in modern society. He had thought so before, but only by instinct. Only after hearing the elderly nun speak did he truly confirm that this was the Divine Willow that had brought him to this era.
[ZH] 可令他不解的是这棵千年神柳为何在千年之前和千年之后处于不同的环境？也许所有的经历不过是一场梦而已。
[EN] What puzzled him, though, was why this thousand-year-old Divine Willow existed in such different environments a thousand years ago and a thousand years later. Perhaps all his experiences were nothing but a dream.
[ZH] “将近一千多年的时间，时代会不断更替，别的我不清楚，可以确定的是它会经历一个人人平等，科技发达的新时代。”
[EN] "Over the next thousand-plus years, eras will continue to change. I'm not clear on the details, but what's certain is that it will witness a new era of equality for all and advanced technology."
[ZH] 净光法师故作惊讶：“哦？施主经历过那样的时代？”
[EN] Master Jingguang feigned surprise: "Oh? Has the benefactor experienced such an era?"
[ZH] 不知为何，面前的老尼令申简辰感到莫名亲切，他不想对她隐瞒：“实不相瞒，我来自更先进的时代，是这棵神柳把我带到宋朝，让我成为别人。还有一位朋友，也被神柳带来，可我找不到她。之所以经常来这里，是想知道它到底把我的朋友带去哪里？”
[EN] For some reason, Shen Jianchen felt inexplicably close to the elderly nun. He didn't want to hide anything: “To tell you the truth, I come from a more advanced era. This Divine Willow brought me to the Song Dynasty and made me someone else. Another friend was also brought here, but I can't find her. That's why I come here so often—to find out where it brought her.”
[ZH] 净光法师抬头瞧一眼高处的红绸：“有缘自会相见，据我猜测，你已遇到她，只是不知她就是你要找的人。”
[EN] Master Jingguang glanced up at the red silk ribbon high above: “When the affinity is destined, you will meet. I suspect you have already encountered her—you simply do not realize she is the one you seek.”
[ZH] “什么？我已遇到他？他是谁？师傅怎会知道这些？”申简辰靠近净光法师，激动地问。
[EN] "What? I've already met her? Who is she? How does the Master know this?" Shen Jianchen stepped closer to Master Jingguang and asked excitedly.
[ZH] 净光法师露出慈祥的笑容：“你很快会知道。阿弥陀佛，施主，告辞。”
[EN] Master Jingguang revealed a kindly smile: "You'll know soon enough. Amitabha. Farewell, benefactor."
[ZH] 净光法师离开神柳，往后山的路走去，她年纪虽大，走起路来却十分矫健。
[EN] Master Jingguang left the Divine Willow and walked toward the path leading to the back of the mountain. Though elderly, her steps were swift and sure.
[ZH] 申简辰望着老尼的背景，直到那背影被山下的草木掩盖。
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched the elderly nun's figure until it was hidden by the trees and grass at the foot of the mountain.
[ZH] 他又开始寄情神柳，自语道：“刚才老尼说我已遇到千里寻，神柳，你告诉我谁是千里寻？文渊？东方尘泽？曹班？还是安明轩？唉，我怎么尽猜些男子，都是因说书艺人告诉我话本的内容是位叫千里寻的才子所写，我才会想到她穿越而来变成男子。会不会她依然是女子？若是女子，又会是谁？梦容？不可能。苗知瑜？也不像，难道是幽栖居士？……”
[EN] He turned back to the Divine Willow and murmured to himself: “The old nun just said I've already met Qianli Xun. Divine Willow, tell me—who is Qianli Xun? Wenyuan? Dongfang Chenze? Cao Ban? Or An Mingxuan? Sigh, why am I only guessing men? It's because the storyteller told me the story was written by a talented scholar named Qianli Xun, which made me think she transmigrated and became a man. Could she still be a woman? If she is, then who? Mengrong? Impossible. Miao Zhiyu? Doesn't seem like it. Could it be... the Youqi Jushi？”
[ZH] 申简辰想起她自称“老娘”的情景：“难道真的是她？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen remembered the scene where she called herself "old lady": "Could it really be her?"
[ZH] 天色已暗，弯弯的月亮升到高空，将有限的月光洒落在神柳的枝叶上，高处的红绸在月光下随风摆动，不知不觉被风解开，向下飘落……
[EN] The sky had darkened. The crescent moon had climbed high, casting its limited moonlight on the branches and leaves of the Divine Willow. The red silk ribbon high above swayed in the wind under the moonlight, unknowingly loosened by the breeze, and began to drift downward...
[ZH] 申简辰瞧着向下飘落的红绸，叹道：“挂那么高有什么用？旁人难以顺手取下，却容易被风刮走，还好我在这里，又会轻功，不妨接住，再帮忙挂上去。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched the red silk ribbon drifting down and sighed: "What's the point of hanging it so high? It's hard for others to reach, yet it's easily blown away by the wind. Luckily I'm here and I know qinggong. I might as well catch it and hang it back up."
[ZH] 申简辰伸手去接飘落下来的红绸，当红绸快要靠近他的手时，借着月光，似乎看到“千里寻”三个字。
[EN] Shen Jianchen reached out to catch the drifting red silk ribbon. As it neared his hand, in the moonlight, he seemed to see the three characters "Qianli Xun."
[ZH] 他迅速将红绸接在手中，又迅速展开，月光下“千里寻”三个字赫然入目。
[EN] He quickly caught the red silk ribbon in his hand and swiftly unfolded it. In the moonlight, the three characters "Qianli Xun" were plainly visible.
[ZH] 申简辰激动不已，再仔细瞧，又看到朱淑真的名字，那名字外面画着一个长形的方框。激动而又高兴的泪水滚落下来：“原来你穿越而来成为幽栖居士。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was overwhelmed with excitement. Looking more carefully, he also saw Zhu Shuzhen's name, with a rectangular box drawn around it. Tears of excitement and joy rolled down: "So you transmigrated and became the Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 申简辰将红绸揣入怀中，兴奋地奔跑着，越过池塘，穿过幽林，刚到别居门口，正好看到白风从西三胡同回来，还没等白风拜见，迅速说：“你来赶马车，跟我去趟西湖。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen tucked the red silk ribbon into his robe and ran excitedly, leaping over the pond and dashing through the Youlin forest. Just as he reached the gate of the villa, he happened to see Baifeng returning from West Third Alley. Before Baifeng could greet him, he quickly said: "Drive the carriage and come with me to West Lake."
[ZH] 白风问：“王爷，天都黑了，为何还要去西湖逛？”
[EN] Baifeng asked: "Your Highness, it's already dark. Why go to West Lake now?"
[ZH] “少废话！”申简辰刚要上马车，突然又想起什么，对门口的下人招手，“过来一下！”
[EN] "Less talk!" Shen Jianchen was about to board the carriage when he suddenly remembered something and beckoned to the servant at the gate: "Come here!"
[ZH] 下人走近后，申简辰问：“文渊可回来？”
[EN] After the servant approached, Shen Jianchen asked: "Has Wenyuan returned?"
[ZH] 下人回道：“乔公子还没回来。”
[EN] The servant replied: "Young Master Qiao hasn't returned yet."
[ZH] 申简辰急忙说：“白风！赶马车去西湖！要快些！”
[EN] Shen Jianchen urged: "Baifeng! Drive the carriage to West Lake! Hurry!"
[ZH] 白风不知所以，既然王爷吩咐，还是老实照做。
[EN] Baifeng didn't know what was going on, but since the prince had ordered it, he dutifully complied.
[ZH] 王爷已坐上马车，他也迅速跳上车，一阵尘土飞扬而过，马车疾驰在去西湖的路上。
[EN] The prince had already boarded the carriage. Baifeng quickly jumped up too. A cloud of dust rose as the carriage galloped along the road to West Lake.
[ZH] 幽林别居离西湖不是很远，大约两刻钟后终于到达。
[EN] The Youlin Villa wasn't far from West Lake. After about half an hour, they finally arrived.
[ZH] 申简辰迅速跳下马车，白风把马车安顿好，跟上王爷。这个时间，西湖周围倒是灯火通明，人还不少，大多是逛完要离开的人。
[EN] Shen Jianchen quickly jumped down from the carriage. Baifeng settled the horses and followed the prince. At this hour, the area around West Lake was lit up bright. There were still quite a few people around, mostly those finishing their stroll and heading home.
[ZH] 这么大的西湖，到底去哪里找？申简辰正在为难之际，瞧见苗知瑜与闺中密友正准备上马车离开。于是上前问道：“苗姑娘，你可知文渊在哪里？”
[EN] West Lake was so vast—where on earth should he look? Just as Shen Jianchen was at a loss, he spotted Miao Zhiyu and her close friends about to board their carriage and leave. He stepped forward and asked: “Miss Miao, do you know where Wenyuan is？”
[ZH] 苗知瑜已知对方是仪王，屈膝行礼：“仪王。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu already knew the other was Yi Wang. She curtsied: "Yi Wang."
[ZH] “不必多礼，快告诉我文渊在哪里？”申简辰显得很焦急。
[EN] "No need for formalities. Quick, tell me where Wenyuan is!" Shen Jianchen looked very anxious.
[ZH] 苗知瑜说：“我最后见他们是在湖的北面，仪王殿下可以去那里找找。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu said: "The last time I saw them was on the north side of the lake. Your Highness can try looking there."
[ZH] 传来一个声音：“仪王殿下，我知道乔文仙在哪里？”
[EN] A voice came: "Your Highness Yi Wang, I know where Qiao Wenxian is."
[ZH] 申简辰转过身，原来是陈浩宇，他的身边跟着些纨绔子弟。他们一起上前拜见：“仪王殿下。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around. It was Chen Haoyu, with a group of playboys at his side. They all stepped forward to greet him: "Your Highness Yi Wang."
[ZH] 苗知瑜很讨厌陈浩宇，赶紧拜道：“仪王殿下，知瑜先行一步。”
