[EN] Miao Zhiyu disliked Chen Haoyu greatly. She quickly curtsied: "Your Highness Yi Wang, Zhiyu will take her leave now."
[ZH] 申简辰对苗知瑜点点头，向陈浩宇问道：“陈公子见过文渊？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded to Miao Zhiyu and asked Chen Haoyu: "Has Young Master Chen seen Wenyuan?"
[ZH] “当然见过，他正在北边的望景亭跟一群才子在一起，朱淑真坐在那里很无聊，一个人喝闷酒。离这里不远，就在那边。”陈浩宇指着一个方向，“殿下顺着这个方向走，很快就能看到。”
[EN] "Of course I have. He's at the Wangjing Pavilion on the north side with a group of talented young men. Zhu Shuzhen is sitting there looking bored, drinking his sorrows away. It's not far from here, just that way." Chen Haoyu pointed in a direction. "Your Highness, just follow this way and you'll see it soon enough."
[ZH] “多谢！”申简辰谢完，迅速朝陈浩宇指的方向走去。
[EN] "Many thanks!" After thanking him, Shen Jianchen swiftly headed in the direction Chen Haoyu had pointed.
[ZH] 有人问：“陈公子，仪王该不会就是官家派来的节度使？”
[EN] Someone asked: "Young Master Chen, could Yi Wang be the Military Commissioner sent by the Emperor?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇回道：“听我姐说是这样。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu replied: "That's what I hear from my sister."
[ZH] 又有人说：“陈公子怎么见到仪王这么乖？还给他指路？”
[EN] Another person said: "Why is Young Master Chen so well-behaved when meeting Yi Wang? You even gave him directions?"
[ZH] “人家可是王爷，我再纨绔也不敢在一位皇子面前造次，你们以后也得恭敬些。行了，回去吧。”陈浩宇带着人也上了马车，离开西湖。
[EN] "He's a prince, alright? No matter how wild I am, I don't dare mess with a prince. You guys better show some respect from now on. Alright, let's head back." Chen Haoyu and his group boarded their carriage and left West Lake.
[ZH] 苗知瑜与一位闺秀同乘一辆马车，那闺秀惊讶地问：“那人竟然是仪王？听你刚才称呼他，我吓得都不敢下车行礼。”
[EN] Miao Zhiyu shared a carriage with a young lady. The young lady asked in surprise: "That person was actually Yi Wang? When I heard you address him just now, I was so scared I didn't even dare get off the carriage to greet him."
[ZH] “我也是听我爹说他是仪王，第一次见他时，根本不知道身份，还好没有唐突贵人。”
[EN] "I only learned from my father that he was Yi Wang. The first time I met him, I had no idea of his identity. Luckily, I didn't offend a noble."
[ZH] “以前听说仪王一直卧病在床，你刚才跟他说话时，我在马车上偷偷瞧了几眼，根本不像生过病的样子。”
[EN] "I used to hear that Yi Wang was always bedridden. When you were talking to him just now, I took a few secret glances from the carriage. He doesn't look like someone who's ever been sick at all."
[ZH] “病总有好的时候，肯定是治好了。”
[EN] "Illnesses can always be cured. He must have recovered."
[ZH] “也对。仪王病已好，又得官家重用，乔家有他这棵大树依靠，以后在临安恐怕大家更会把乔家当回事。”
[EN] "That's true. Now that Yi Wang has recovered and is favored by the Emperor, with such a powerful tree to lean on, everyone in Lin'an will surely take the Qiao family even more seriously from now on."
[ZH] 苗知瑜用微笑认可。
[EN] Miao Zhiyu acknowledged with a smile.
[ZH] 乔家本来祖上有人在朝中为官，后来一个为官做宰的都没有，后辈均是受到祖上福荫，生活依然可用“富贵”二字形容。直到大女儿被官家看上，成为贵妃，后又生下仪王，乔家又开始扬眉吐气。
[EN] The Qiao family originally had ancestors who served as officials in the court. Later, not a single one rose to high office. The younger generations relied on the blessings of their forebears, and their lives could still be described as "wealthy and noble." It wasn't until their eldest daughter caught the Emperor's eye and became a Noble Consort, and later gave birth to Yi Wang, that the Qiao family began to hold their heads high again.
[ZH] 再后来乔贵妃不在，传言说仪王身体病弱，活不了多久，一些人又不把乔家当回事。现在仪王健健康康地出现在大家面前，还被官家封为临安节度使，临安的官员包括知府大人在内都会给乔家面子。
[EN] Later, when Noble Consort Qiao passed away and rumors spread that Yi Wang was sickly and wouldn't live long, some people stopped taking the Qiao family seriously again. Now Yi Wang appeared before everyone in good health and had been appointed Military Commissioner of Lin'an by the Emperor. All the officials in Lin'an, including the prefect himself, would give face to the Qiao family.
[ZH] 想到这里，苗知瑜担心乔家会看在临安知府的面子上，不计较和离一事，接纳幽栖居士，那么她连一点希望都没有。
[EN] Thinking of this, Miao Zhiyu worried that the Qiao family, out of regard for the Lin'an prefect, might overlook the divorce and accept the Youqi Jushi. If that happened, she wouldn't have the slightest hope left.

#CH 73
[ZH] 白风跟随仪王匆忙向北边的观景亭而去，王爷焦急的神情搞得他没有心情欣赏西湖夜景。
[EN] Baifeng followed Yi Wang as they hurried toward the Wangjing Pavilion on the north side. The prince's anxious expression left him in no mood to enjoy the West Lake night scenery.
[ZH] 当他们来到离观景亭不远处时，白风先看到南双，他与在竹林里见到的丫鬟，好像叫苦荞，一起坐在亭子的长椅上聊天儿。还有一位，应该是南双经常提起的化雨。
[EN] When they reached a spot not far from the Wangjing Pavilion, Baifeng first spotted Nanshuang. He was sitting on a bench in the pavilion, chatting with the maid they had met in the bamboo forest—the one called Kuqiao, if he remembered correctly. There was another person with them, likely Huayu, whom Nanshuang often mentioned.
[ZH] 白风本想喊南双，见王爷盯着他们在竹林里见到的姑娘，眼神中充满复杂的情感，似乎是高兴，又似乎想要哭。白风不敢言语，默默陪伴，他猜测那姑娘应该是南双曾提到的幽栖居士。
[EN] Baifeng was about to call out to Nanshuang when he noticed the prince staring at the girl from the bamboo forest. His eyes were filled with complex emotions—seeming both happy and as if he wanted to cry. Baifeng dared not speak, but silently accompanied him. He guessed that the girl must be the Youqi Jushi whom Nanshuang had mentioned.
[ZH] 亭子里挂着几个灯笼，申简辰能清晰地看到千里寻，她已不是穿越前的样貌，但和穿越前一样美。他怨自己为何不早些认出她，若早些，她也许不会跑来跟文渊约会。
[EN] Several lanterns hung in the pavilion. Shen Jianchen could clearly see Qianli Xun. She no longer looked the same as before the transmigration, but she was just as beautiful. He blamed himself for not recognizing her sooner. If he had, perhaps she wouldn't have run off to date Wenyuan.
[ZH] 亭子内，四位公子依然聊得很开心。千里寻已将一壶酒酿喝完，加之喝过的桂花酿，醉意渐渐袭来，她觉得自己有种飘飘欲仙的感觉，不由诗意萌生。
[EN] Inside the pavilion, the four young men were still chatting happily. Qianli Xun had already finished a pot of sweet rice wine. Combined with the osmanthus wine she'd drunk earlier, the intoxication gradually crept over her. She felt a floating, almost heavenly sensation, and poetic inspiration stirred within her.
[ZH] 申简辰瞧了一会儿，终于鼓起勇气喊出：“千……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched for a while and finally summoned the courage to call out: "Qian..."
[ZH] 后面的还没喊出口，听到千里寻说：“你们不要再聊，我……我突然来了灵感，我……我要作词。”
[EN] Before he could finish, he heard Qianli Xun say: "Stop chatting, you all. I... I've suddenly gotten inspiration. I... I'm going to compose a poem."
[ZH] 她站起来，踉跄地向文渊走去。
[EN] She stood up and staggered toward Wenyuan.
[ZH] 其他人听到幽栖居士要作词，一个个兴致极佳，用心听着。
[EN] When the others heard that the Youqi Jushi was going to compose a poem, they were all in high spirits and listened attentively.
[ZH] 申简辰只好先作罢，站在不远处的黑暗之地，瞧着亭中那个拥有有趣灵魂的朱淑真。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had to give up for now. He stood in the darkness not far away, gazing at Zhu Shuzhen—the woman with the fascinating soul—in the pavilion.
[ZH] 乔文仙站起来扶住淑真：“你醉了，我带你回去吧？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stood up to support Shuzhen: "You're drunk. Should I take you home?"
[ZH] 千里寻盯着这个让她拥有爱情的男子，笑道：“我没醉，我要作词。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stared at the man who had given her love and smiled: "I'm not drunk. I'm going to compose a poem."
[ZH] 她推开文渊，踉跄着步子，瞧向月光下的湖水，口中吟道：“恼烟撩露，留我须臾住。”
[EN] She pushed Wenyuan away, staggered forward, gazed at the lake under the moonlight, and recited: "The mist and dew tease and tempt, asking me to stay a moment longer."
[ZH] 又转身看向文渊，他爱溺的眼神令她陶醉，上前牵起他的手：“携手藕花湖上路，一霎黄梅细雨。”
[EN] Then she turned back to look at Wenyuan. His loving, doting gaze intoxicated her. She stepped forward and took his hand: "Hand in hand along the lotus-lined lake path, a sudden drizzle in the plum rain season."
[ZH] 文渊朝她笑着，她把文渊推到亭子的长椅上，文渊只好坐下。
[EN] Wenyuan smiled at her. She pushed him onto the bench in the pavilion, and he had no choice but to sit down.
[ZH] “娇痴不怕人猜，”千里寻又吟出一句后，竟然坐到文渊的怀里，双手将文渊的脖子环绕住，脸靠在他的胸前，闭上双目，“和衣睡倒人怀。”
[EN] "In my charming folly, I fear no gossip," Qianli Xun recited another line. Then, to everyone's shock, she actually sat in Wenyuan's lap, wrapped her arms around his neck, rested her face against his chest, and closed her eyes: "Fully dressed, I fall asleep in his arms."
[ZH] 此情此景，申简辰无法再喊出“千里寻”三个字。他依然爱着她，可她却爱上了文渊。再次琢磨千里寻说过的话：“除非猪能上树，老娘才会嫁给你！”申简辰才觉出明显拒绝的意味来。
[EN] At this sight, Shen Jianchen could no longer bring himself to call out "Qianli Xun." He still loved her, but she had fallen in love with Wenyuan. He pondered again the words Qianli Xun had once said: "Only when pigs fly will I ever marry you!" Only now did Shen Jianchen truly grasp the meaning of her clear rejection.
[ZH] 她或许从未爱过他，让她知道他是申简辰，又能如何？再说她已爱上文渊，难道要让她再无情地拒绝他？申简辰越想越伤悲，叹口气，转身离开。
[EN] Perhaps she had never loved him. What would be the point of letting her know he was Shen Jianchen? Besides, she had already fallen in love with Wenyuan. Was he to make her reject him once more, without mercy? The more Shen Jianchen thought about it, the more heartbroken he became. He sighed and turned to leave.
[ZH] 白风跟上王爷，他实在搞不懂，王爷明明要找乔公子，他就在亭子里，为何急急匆匆而来，什么都没说，什么都没做，又要离开？
[EN] Baifeng followed the prince. He really couldn't understand—the prince had clearly come to find Young Master Qiao, who was right there in the pavilion. Why had they rushed over, said nothing, done nothing, and now were leaving?
[ZH] 亭子里，安明轩小声说：“还是走吧，幽栖居士都吟出'和衣睡倒人怀'，我们在这里不合适。”
[EN] In the pavilion, An Mingxuan whispered: "We should go. The Youqi Jushi has already recited 'fully dressed, I fall asleep in his arms.' It's not appropriate for us to be here."
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“词还未作完，就差两句，再等等。曹班，你记忆力好，用心记着。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said: “The poem isn't finished yet—just two more lines. Wait a bit longer. Cao Ban, you have a good memory. Pay attention and remember it.”
[ZH] 曹班说：“放心，都记着呢。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "Don't worry. I've got it all memorized."
[ZH] 苦荞惊得站起来，心想：“小姐怎么这么大胆？竟然当着众人的面坐到乔公子怀里！”
[EN] Kuqiao was so shocked she stood up, thinking: "How can Miss be so bold? She's actually sitting in Young Master Qiao's lap in front of everyone!"
[ZH] 南双和化雨瞪大眼睛互相瞅瞅，露出意味深长的笑容。
[EN] Nanshuang and Huayu widened their eyes and looked at each other, revealing knowing smiles.
[ZH] 乔文仙没想到淑真会当众坐到他的怀里，定是喝醉了才变得如此毫无顾忌。但乔文仙很开心，也用手搂住她，温柔地说：“淑真，你醉了。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hadn't expected Shuzhen to sit in his lap in public. She must have been drunk to become so unrestrained. But Qiao Wenxian was delighted. He wrapped his arms around her and said gently: "Shuzhen, you're drunk."
[ZH] 千里寻朦胧着醉眼，把脸从文渊的胸膛挪开，看着他：“文渊，我没醉，我想告诉你，我爱你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun, her eyes hazy with intoxication, moved her face from Wenyuan's chest and looked at him: “Wenyuan, I'm not drunk. I want to tell you—I love you.”
[ZH] 周围人又一阵尴尬。
[EN] Those around them felt another wave of awkwardness.
[ZH] 乔文仙小声在她耳边说：“我知道，你当着他们的面说，我会不好意思。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian whispered in her ear: "I know. But if you say it in front of them, I'll be embarrassed."
[ZH] 千里寻转头用醉眼瞧向三位公子：“是他们吗？没事，都是熟人，我还有两句……”
[EN] Qianli Xun turned her intoxicated gaze toward the three young men: "Them? It's fine. They're all familiar faces. I still have two more lines..."
[ZH] 三位公子立刻竖起耳朵，只听幽栖居士带着醉意念道：“最是分携时候，归来懒傍妆台。”
[EN] The three young men immediately perked up their ears as the Youqi Jushi, still half-drunk, recited: “Most painful is the moment of parting—returning home, too listless to approach the vanity.”
[ZH] 东方尘泽赶紧说：“成了！我们也该走，不要再打搅文渊与幽栖居士。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze hastily said: "Done! We should go now. Let's not disturb Wenyuan and the Youqi Jushi any longer."
[ZH] 三位公子迅速离开。
[EN] The three young men swiftly departed.
[ZH] 出了亭子，安明轩问曹班：“可把整首词记住？”
[EN] Once outside the pavilion, An Mingxuan asked Cao Ban: "Did you memorize the whole poem?"
[ZH] 曹班自信地说：“那还用问？”
[EN] Cao Ban replied confidently: "Is that even a question?"
[ZH] 乔文仙见三位公子离开，劝道：“大家都走了，我也送你回去吧？”
[EN] When Qiao Wenxian saw the three young men leave, he urged: "Everyone's gone. Should I take you home too?"
[ZH] 千里寻依然抱住文渊的脖子：“我爱你，我想嫁给你，想给你生很多很多孩子……”
[EN] Qianli Xun still held onto Wenyuan's neck: "I love you. I want to marry you. I want to give you many, many children..."
[ZH] 乔文仙把淑真抱起来悄悄在她耳边说：“我也爱你。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian lifted Shuzhen up and whispered in her ear: "I love you too."
[ZH] 千里寻听了很开心，安心在文渊怀中睡去。
[EN] Hearing this, Qianli Xun was overjoyed and peacefully fell asleep in Wenyuan's arms.
[ZH] 乔文仙一路抱着她向马车走去，虽有些吃力，他努力坚持着。到了马车跟前，将淑真抱上自己的马车，让她靠着他入睡。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian carried her all the way to the carriage. It was somewhat strenuous, but he persevered. When they reached it, he lifted Shuzhen inside and let her lean against him as she slept.
[ZH] 一路乔文仙的脸上一直挂着笑容，品着淑真刚刚的词句“和衣睡倒人怀”，自语道：“没想到你一个姑娘家能写出这样的句子？别人听了肯定会批评你，可我却喜欢，因为你是坐在我的怀里想到这句，是为我而作。”
[EN] A smile hung on Qiao Wenxian's face all the way. He savored Shuzhen's line—“fully dressed, I fall asleep in his arms”—and murmured: “I never expected a girl like you could write such a line. Others would criticize you, but I adore it—for you were in my arms when you thought of it. It was written for me.”
[ZH] 马车里只有他们二人，他瞧着怀中的淑真，她在酒酿的作用下，睡得很香。乔文仙忍不住在淑真的额头上轻轻吻了一下：“淑真，你放心，我定会与你白头偕老，一生一世陪着你。”
[EN] The two of them were alone in the carriage. He gazed at Shuzhen in his arms. Under the influence of the sweet rice wine, she slept soundly. Qiao Wenxian couldn't help but gently kiss her forehead: "Shuzhen, rest assured. I will grow old with you. I will stay by your side for a lifetime."
[ZH] 到达朱府侧门，乔文仙本想将淑真抱回朱府，苦荞阻止道：“乔公子送到这里即可，朱府大着呢，您抱着小姐肯定会累坏，朱家有轿子，小姐坐上，抬回去即可。”
[EN] When they reached the side gate of the Zhu residence, Qiao Wenxian was about to carry Shuzhen back inside. Kuqiao stopped him: "Young Master Qiao, you've brought her far enough. The Zhu residence is vast. Carrying Miss would surely exhaust you. The Zhu family has sedan chairs. Just have Miss sit in one, and they'll carry her back."
[ZH] 乔文仙又抱起淑真，等门口的下人把轿子抬出来，他小心翼翼将淑真在轿子里安顿好。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian lifted Shuzhen again. When the servants brought out the sedan chair, he carefully settled her inside.
[ZH] 苦荞与乔公子拜别：“天色已晚，乔公子也早些回去歇着。”
[EN] Kuqiao bid Young Master Qiao farewell: "It's late. Young Master Qiao should go home and rest early too."
[ZH] 乔文仙目送轿子进了朱府，才放心坐回马车。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian watched the sedan chair enter the Zhu residence before he felt at ease and returned to the carriage.
[ZH] 南双问：“公子是回乔府，还是去幽林别居？”
[EN] Nanshuang asked: "Should Young Master return to the Qiao residence or go to the Youlin Villa?"
[ZH] “还用问？”乔文仙的语气中带着批评。
[EN] "Is that even a question?" Qiao Wenxian's tone carried a hint of reproach.
[ZH] 南双建议道：“这么晚，公子要不别回幽林别居，朱府离乔府比较近。”
[EN] Nanshuang suggested: "It's so late. Perhaps Young Master shouldn't return to the Youlin Villa tonight. The Zhu residence is closer to the Qiao residence."
[ZH] 乔文仙今日过得很开心，他想立刻见到仪王，与他分享自己的快乐：“听你的还是听我的？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had been very happy today. He wanted to see Yi Wang immediately and share his joy: "Are we listening to you or to me?"
[ZH] “当然听公子的。”南双只好将马车向城外赶去。
[EN] "Of course we listen to Young Master." Nanshuang had no choice but to drive the carriage toward the outskirts of the city.
[ZH] 回到幽林别居，乔文仙没有回屋，直接奔向仪王的院子。
[EN] When they returned to the Youlin Villa, Qiao Wenxian didn't go to his own room. He headed straight for Yi Wang's courtyard.
[ZH] 在院门外，瞧到里面灯亮着，更是开心：“仪王这么贴心，知道我没回来，他也不睡。”
[EN] At the courtyard gate, he saw the light was still on inside, which made him even happier: "Yi Wang is so thoughtful. Knowing I hadn't returned, he stayed awake for me."
[ZH] 一进院门，大喊起来：“仪王！我回来了！”
[EN] As soon as he entered the courtyard gate, he shouted: "Yi Wang! I'm back!"
[ZH] 还没进屋，被白风堵在门口：“乔公子，王爷累了，刚睡下，您要有事，明日再说。”
[EN] Before he could enter the room, Baifeng blocked him at the door: "Young Master Qiao, the prince is tired. He just fell asleep. If you have something to discuss, please wait until tomorrow."
[ZH] 乔文仙又瞧向屋内：“灯不是还亮着吗？你别挡着，我只说两句。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian looked toward the room again: "Isn't the light still on? Don't block me. I just want to say a few words."
[ZH] 话音刚落，屋内的灯立刻被熄灭。
[EN] The moment he finished speaking, the light inside the room was immediately extinguished.
[ZH] “既如此，明日我再找他。”乔文仙并未多想，认为仪王肯定是累了，要不怎么听到他的声音还要把灯熄灭。
[EN] “In that case, I'll find him tomorrow.” Qiao Wenxian didn't think much of it. He assumed Yi Wang must be exhausted—otherwise, why would he put out the light upon hearing his voice?
[ZH] 文渊离开后，申简辰从床上坐起来，他根本无法入睡。之所以不愿见文渊，是怕他谈起与幽栖居士的约会，会令自己更加伤心。
[EN] After Wenyuan left, Shen Jianchen sat up from the bed. He couldn't fall asleep at all. The reason he didn't want to see Wenyuan was that he feared Wenyuan would talk about his date with the Youqi Jushi, which would only make him even more heartbroken.
[ZH] 申简辰摸着黑走到院子，开始练拳。白风本想睡去，王爷又起来，他必须陪着。
[EN] Shen Jianchen walked into the courtyard in the dark and began practicing his fist forms. Baifeng had been about to go to sleep, but the prince was up again. He had to accompany him.
[ZH] 王爷的拳法精妙，他在一旁看得入迷……
[EN] The prince's fist techniques were exquisite. Baifeng watched from the side, completely captivated...
[ZH] 大约半个时辰后，王爷终于停下来：“你去睡吧，不用陪我，我要单独出去走走。”
[EN] After about an hour, the prince finally stopped: "Go to sleep. You don't need to accompany me. I want to go out for a walk alone."
[ZH] 白风刚准备去睡，又被叫住。只听王爷说：“从明日起，不用再去西三胡同。”
[EN] Baifeng was just about to go to sleep when he was called back. He heard the prince say: "Starting tomorrow, there's no need to go to West Third Alley anymore."
[ZH] 这是白风近期听到的最开心的话：“遵命！”
[EN] This was the happiest news Baifeng had heard in a long time: "Understood!"
[ZH] 他目送王爷走出院落，想不通为何王爷明明睡不着却不愿见乔公子。既想不通，他也不愿多想，还是回屋睡大觉去。
[EN] He watched the prince leave the courtyard, unable to understand why the prince, though clearly unable to sleep, didn't want to see Young Master Qiao. Since he couldn't figure it out, he didn't want to think about it any longer. He went back to his room to get some proper sleep.

#CH 74
[ZH] 已接近凌晨，申简辰一天之内第二次来到神柳下。周围寂静得可怕，他毫无感觉，内心纠结对千里寻的情感，根本无法感受其他情绪。
[EN] It was nearly dawn. Shen Jianchen came to the Divine Willow for the second time that day. The surroundings were eerily silent, but he felt nothing. His heart was tangled with emotions for Qianli Xun—he simply couldn't feel anything else.
[ZH] 月亮早已变换位置，正好在神柳的枝头，显得寂静而又孤独，如同申简辰此刻的心情一般。
[EN] The moon had long since shifted position, now hanging right above the branches of the Divine Willow. It looked quiet and lonely, just like Shen Jianchen's mood at that moment.
[ZH] 他为寻找千里寻来到临安，好不容易找到，她却已倒在他人的怀抱。
[EN] He had come to Lin'an to find Qianli Xun. After all that trouble, she had already fallen into someone else's arms.
[ZH] 穿越前的千里寻因不愿涉足爱情屡屡拒绝他的表白，可为何她穿越而来这么快接受文渊的感情？
[EN] Before the transmigration, Qianli Xun had repeatedly rejected his confessions because she didn't want to get involved in love. But why, after transmigrating, did she accept Wenyuan's feelings so quickly?
[ZH] 申简辰自问着，也许是问题的力量太过强大，脱口而出：“她为何不等我？为何不寻找我？为何要轻易接受文渊的感情？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen asked himself. Perhaps the questions were too powerful, and they slipped out: "Why didn't she wait for me? Why didn't she look for me? Why did she so easily accept Wenyuan's feelings?"
[ZH] 神柳沉默依旧，没有给出任何答案。
[EN] The Divine Willow remained silent as ever, giving no answer.
[ZH] 申简辰望着神柳沉默半晌，为自己找到一个合理的解释。
[EN] Shen Jianchen gazed at the Divine Willow in silence for a long while, then found a reasonable explanation for himself.
[ZH] “也许她像我一样内心太孤单，一场地震一切化为泡影，好不容易在古代借助他人的身体活过来，想法必然会有所改变。”
[EN] "Maybe she's as lonely inside as I am. An earthquake turned everything to bubbles. She barely came back to life in ancient times, in someone else's body. Her way of thinking would naturally change."
[ZH] 又怎能不变？他申简辰再次活过来对人生的态度与穿越前大不相同，曾经追求的功名利禄在他眼中已如同草芥，现在只想与爱的人生活在一起，千里寻一定也是如此想法。
[EN] How could it not change? He, Shen Jianchen, had a completely different attitude toward life after coming back to life—so different from before the transmigration. The fame and fortune he once pursued now meant as much as grass to him. Now he just wanted to live with the one he loved. Qianli Xun must feel the same way.
[ZH] 想到这里，申简辰不再埋怨千里寻，反而埋怨自己没有及时出现在她身边。出现了，为何不早些认出她？真是造化弄人。
[EN] Thinking of this, Shen Jianchen stopped blaming Qianli Xun. Instead, he blamed himself for not appearing by her side sooner. He had appeared—so why hadn't he recognized her earlier? Truly, fate played tricks on people.
[ZH] “她已有文渊，他们看上去很爱对方，我该怎么办？”申简辰在心里自问着。
[EN] "She already has Wenyuan. They look like they love each other very much. What should I do?" Shen Jianchen asked himself in his heart.
[ZH] “哥哥！这么晚，你为何还要来这儿？”
[EN] "Brother! Why are you still coming here so late?"
[ZH] 是乔梦容的声音。
[EN] It was Qiao Mengrong's voice.
[ZH] 申简辰转过身，月光下，乔梦容显得更加柔弱。他心中想道：“难道我只能接受她的感情？可我并不爱梦容。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned around. Under the moonlight, Qiao Mengrong looked even more fragile. He thought to himself: "Can I only accept her feelings? But I don't love Mengrong."
[ZH] “哥哥，你怎么了？为何不说话？”
[EN] "Brother, what's wrong? Why aren't you saying anything?"
[ZH] “你怎么跑来？”
[EN] "Why did you come here?"
[ZH] “我睡不着，很难受，打算去找哥哥，瞧见哥哥往外走，便带着喜兰悄悄跟在后面。”
[EN] "I couldn't sleep. I felt terrible. I was going to find you, but I saw you leaving, so I brought Xilan and followed quietly behind."
[ZH] “明日我找个大夫给你瞧瞧……”
[EN] "Tomorrow I'll find a doctor to check on you..."
[ZH] “不用！大夫解决不了我的问题。”乔梦容强调道，“今日哥哥没来看我，我心中不安，才无法入睡，只要哥哥每日看看我，对我没有任何隐瞒，我定会安然无恙。”
[EN] "No need! A doctor can't solve my problem," Qiao Mengrong emphasized. "When you didn't come see me today, I felt uneasy and couldn't sleep. As long as you check on me every day and keep no secrets from me, I'll be perfectly fine."
[ZH] 申简辰想过代替赵朴为乔梦容负责，可找到千里寻，想要负责的想法越来越淡，哪怕她已爱上别人。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had thought about taking responsibility for Qiao Mengrong in Zhao Pu's place. But after finding Qianli Xun, that desire to take responsibility grew weaker and weaker—even though she had already fallen in love with someone else.
[ZH] “梦容，你不要再把我当成……”
[EN] "Mengrong, stop treating me as..."
[ZH] “哥哥不要说下去！”乔梦容很快打断，她能猜到哥哥要说什么，她根本不愿听。她走到哥哥跟前，抱住他，“不要再说，我爱的是现在的哥哥，不管你变成什么样，你都是我的哥哥。”
[EN] "Don't say it, Brother!" Qiao Mengrong quickly interrupted. She could guess what he was about to say, and she simply didn't want to hear it. She walked up to her brother and hugged him. "Don't say anymore. I love the brother you are now. No matter what you become, you're still my brother."
[ZH] 申简辰很希望自己能爱上梦容，也希望能把她搂在怀里。可双手抬起后，又不得不放下，若真的抱住梦容，他会觉得自己猪狗不如。
[EN] Shen Jianchen really wished he could fall in love with Mengrong. He also wished he could hold her in his arms. But after raising his hands, he had to lower them again. If he really hugged Mengrong, he would feel worse than a pig or a dog.
[ZH] “哥哥，我冷，你能抱抱我吗？”
[EN] "Brother, I'm cold. Can you hug me?"
[ZH] 梦容主动要求，并未改变申简辰的心意，他看到喜兰手里拿着披风：“喜兰，把披风给郡主披上。”
[EN] Mengrong's request didn't change Shen Jianchen's mind. He saw Xilan holding a cloak: "Xilan, drape the cloak over her ladyship."
[ZH] 喜兰捧着披风过来，准备为郡主披上。
[EN] Xilan came over with the cloak, ready to drape it over her ladyship.
[ZH] 乔梦容拒绝道：“我不要披风，我要哥哥抱着我，哥哥的怀抱最温暖。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong refused: "I don't want the cloak. I want you to hug me. Your arms are the warmest."
[ZH] 喜兰不知如何是好。
[EN] Xilan didn't know what to do.
[ZH] 申简辰哄道：“梦容，你听话，把披风披上。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen coaxed: "Mengrong, be good. Put on the cloak."
[ZH] 乔梦容没有说话，放开哥哥，迅速踮起脚尖，在哥哥的唇上吻了一下，害羞地转过身。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong said nothing. She let go of her brother, quickly stood on her tiptoes, kissed him on the lips, then shyly turned around.
[ZH] 喜兰立刻把披风给郡主披好，乔梦容红着脸带喜兰离开。今晚，她终于能睡个好觉。
[EN] Xilan immediately draped the cloak over her ladyship. Qiao Mengrong, her face red, led Xilan away. Tonight, she could finally get a good night's sleep.
[ZH] 突如其来的吻让申简辰有些懵，余温还留在他的唇上，并没有任何美好的感觉。
[EN] The sudden kiss left Shen Jianchen dazed. The lingering warmth remained on his lips, but it brought him no sense of beauty or joy.
[ZH] 申简辰的嘴角露出一丝无奈的笑容，难道他穿越而来的人生就要这么无奈地过下去？爱的人得不到，不爱的人又主动投怀送抱。
[EN] A helpless smile appeared at the corner of Shen Jianchen's mouth. Was his transmigrated life going to be lived like this—so helplessly? The one he loved, he couldn't have; the one he didn't love threw herself into his arms.
[ZH] 他该怎么办？申简辰找不到答案，还是将命运交给时间，他需要做的唯有遵循内心真实的感情。
[EN] What should he do? Shen Jianchen couldn't find an answer. He might as well leave his fate to time. All he needed to do was follow his true feelings.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 昨日酒喝得太多，沉沉地睡了一晚。窗外的鸟鸣声将千里寻吵醒，她睁开眼，习惯性地唤了声：“苦荞！”
[EN] She had drunk too much wine yesterday and slept soundly all night. The chirping of birds outside the window woke Qianli Xun. She opened her eyes and called out habitually: "Kuqiao!"
[ZH] 苦荞正在收拾屋子，听到小姐呼唤的声音，放下手中活计，来到床前，将床幔挂起。
[EN] Kuqiao was tidying the room. Hearing her miss's call, she put down her work, came to the bedside, and hung up the bed curtain.
[ZH] “我让厨房给小姐做了醒酒汤，温度刚刚好，您现在喝吗？”
[EN] "I asked the kitchen to make hangover soup for you, Miss. It's just the right temperature. Would you like to drink it now?"
[ZH] “睡了一晚上，醉意全消，不需要喝那玩意儿。”千里寻坐起来，还有些许眩晕，“把醒酒汤端来吧，头还有些沉，也不知我喝了多少酒。”
[EN] "After sleeping all night, I'm completely sober. No need for that stuff." Qianli Xun sat up, still feeling a bit dizzy. "Bring the hangover soup, though. My head still feels heavy. I don't even know how much I drank."
[ZH] 苦荞把醒酒汤端到小姐面前：“昨晚您把东方公子他们带去的酒都喝光了。”
[EN] Kuqiao brought the hangover soup to her miss: "Last night, you drank all the wine that Young Master Dongfang and the others brought."
[ZH] 千里寻接过醒酒汤埋怨道：“都喝光？你为什么不阻止我？任由你家小姐我当众丢人？”
[EN] Qianli Xun took the hangover soup and complained: "Drank it all? Why didn't you stop me? Just let your miss embarrass herself in public?"
[ZH] 苦荞委屈道：“我阻止了三次，您不听，嫌我多事，还说酒酿太好喝，根本不会醉。我最后一次阻止时，乔公子说您高兴，多喝些没关系，大不了他把您送回来，我只好作罢。”
[EN] Kuqiao said aggrievedly: “I tried to stop you three times, but you wouldn't listen. You said I was being nosy and that the sweet rice wine tasted so good it couldn't possibly make you drunk. When I tried to stop you the last time, Young Master Qiao said you were happy and it was fine to drink more—that at worst he'd bring you back home. So I had to give up.”
[ZH] 千里寻将醒酒汤喝光，还别说，醒酒汤刚下肚，顿时觉得神清气爽，她把碗递给苦荞，下地站起来，到窗前伸了伸懒腰：“昨晚是文渊送我回来的？”
[EN] Qianli Xun drank all the hangover soup. She had to admit—as soon as it went down, she felt refreshed. She handed the bowl to Kuqiao, got out of bed, stood up, and stretched by the window: “Did Wenyuan bring me back last night？”
[ZH] 苦荞把碗放好：“是的。”
[EN] Kuqiao put the bowl away: "Yes."
[ZH] 千里寻想起写过的网络小说，一般这种情况下，她都会让女主吐男主一身，心想：“难道昨晚，女主喝醉吐男主一身污秽的桥段，也发生在我身上？”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought of the web novels she had written. Usually in such situations, she would have the female lead throw up all over the male lead. She wondered: "Did last night's 'drunk female lead throws up all over male lead' scene happen to me too?"
[ZH] 她转过身，充满期待地问：“昨晚我喝多，什么都不记得，我该不会给文渊吐了一身？”
[EN] She turned around and asked expectantly: "I drank too much last night and don't remember anything. I didn't throw up all over Wenyuan, did I?"
[ZH] 苦荞摇摇头：“那倒没有。”
[EN] Kuqiao shook her head: "No, that didn't happen."
[ZH] 千里寻感到失望，现实总是没有想象出来的美好，如若她吐文渊一身，他又不嫌弃，那才叫一个美。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt disappointed. Reality was never as nice as imagination. If she had thrown up all over Wenyuan and he didn't mind, that would have been truly romantic.
[ZH] “这么平淡，难道没发生些什么？”
[EN] "So plain? Did nothing happen at all?"
[ZH] 苦荞说：“当然发生了，还是大事。”
[EN] Kuqiao said: "Of course something happened. Something big."
[ZH] “什么大事？说来听听。”千里寻兴致很浓，好像是在听别人的八卦一般。
[EN] "What big thing? Tell me." Qianli Xun was very interested, as if she were listening to gossip about someone else.
[ZH] “小姐可还记得昨晚您作诗之事？”
[EN] "Does Miss remember composing a poem last night?"
[ZH] “我竟然作诗了？”千里寻什么都不记得，她喝断片，只怕胡邹了些像诗又不像诗的白话句子，岂不是给朱淑真丢脸？担忧地问，“你可还记得我写的诗句？”
[EN] “I actually composed a poem？” Qianli Xun didn't remember anything. She'd blacked out. She was afraid she'd spouted some lines that were like poems but not really poems—wouldn't that embarrass Zhu Shuzhen? She asked worriedly, “Do you still remember the lines I wrote？”
[ZH] “有一句印象深刻。”
[EN] "One line left a deep impression."
[ZH] “念来听听。”
[EN] "Recite it for me."
[ZH] “您真的要听？”
[EN] "Do you really want to hear it?"
[ZH] 千里寻紧张地点点头。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded nervously.
[ZH] 只听苦荞念道：“和衣睡倒人怀。”
[EN] Kuqiao recited: "Fully dressed, I fall asleep in his arms."
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，原来她醉酒之后吟诵的是朱淑真的《清平乐·夏日游湖》，还好没给原主丢脸。
[EN] Qianli Xun breathed a sigh of relief. So after getting drunk, she had recited Zhu Shuzhen's "Qing Ping Yue: Summer Day on the Lake." At least she hadn't embarrassed the original owner.
[ZH] “你说我作诗，吓坏我，我是做了一首新词好不好！”
[EN] “You said I composed a poem—you scared me! I actually composed a new ci poem, okay！”
[ZH] “小姐以前告诉我词也是诗。”
[EN] "Miss told me before that ci is also poetry."
[ZH] “我不就作了一首新词，你怎么说发生了大事？”
[EN] "I just composed a new ci poem. Why do you say something big happened?"
[ZH] “还不是大事？您昨晚在亭子里把乔公子一把推倒，当众坐在他的怀里，才吟出'和衣睡倒人怀'，东方公子他们眼睛瞪得老大。您是大家闺秀，以后可得注意些，否则别人会议论您有失女德。”
[EN] "Isn't that a big deal? Last night in the pavilion, you pushed Young Master Qiao down and sat in his lap in public, then recited 'fully dressed, I fall asleep in his arms.' Young Master Dongfang and the others had their eyes wide open. You're a proper young lady. You should be more careful in the future, or people will talk about your scandalous behavior."
[ZH] 千里寻感到满意，两人之间终于发生浪漫之事：“我与文渊两情相悦，坐在他的怀里再正常不过。”
[EN] Qianli Xun felt satisfied. Something romantic had finally happened between them: "Wenyuan and I are in love. It's perfectly normal for me to sit in his lap."
[ZH] 苦荞强调道：“可您还没嫁给乔公子。”
[EN] Kuqiao emphasized: "But you haven't married Young Master Qiao yet."
[ZH] 千里寻肯定道：“我迟早会嫁给他。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said firmly: "I'll marry him sooner or later."
[ZH] 门外传来绿桃的声音：“小姐，夫人让您用过早饭后去她屋里一趟。”
[EN] Outside the door came Lvtiao's voice: "Miss, Madam wants you to come to her room after breakfast."
[ZH] 千里寻对外回道：“好的！知道了！”
[EN] Qianli Xun replied to the outside: "Alright! I understand!"
[ZH] 要去见长辈，需收拾一番，在苦荞的伺候下洗了脸，坐到铜镜前，仔细整理妆容。
[EN] To meet an elder, she needed to freshen up. With Kuqiao's help, she washed her face, sat in front of the bronze mirror, and carefully touched up her makeup.

#CH 75
[ZH] 千里寻收拾完，简单用过早饭，来到向夫人房中，韦嬷嬷也在。
[EN] After tidying up, Qianli Xun had a simple breakfast and went to Lady Xiang's room. Nanny Wei was also there.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷与向夫人虽是主仆，却胜似姐妹，回朱府这段时日，天天住在向夫人的院子。
[EN] Though Nanny Wei and Lady Xiang were mistress and servant, they were closer than sisters. During their time back at the Zhu residence, Nanny Wei stayed in Lady Xiang's courtyard every day.
[ZH] 千里寻进屋先拜见两位长辈，然后坐在椅子上不敢言语。她已猜到，长辈找她肯定是因游湖约会一事。
[EN] Qianli Xun entered the room and first paid her respects to the two elders, then sat on a chair, not daring to speak. She'd already guessed why the elders had summoned her—the lake outing.
[ZH] 果然，向夫人开口问：“你昨日连午饭都没吃，就跑出去，很晚才回来，有没有饿着？”
[EN] Sure enough, Lady Xiang asked: "You didn't even eat lunch yesterday, ran out, and came back very late. Were you hungry?"
[ZH] 千里寻想起文渊为她带的小吃，幸福感难以掩饰：“我在外吃了，根本没饿着。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought of the snacks Wenyuan had brought her, her happiness hard to hide: "I ate outside, I wasn't hungry at all."
[ZH] 向夫人说：“那便好，听绿桃说，你到西湖去见乔家公子，韦嬷嬷也告诉我，你对乔公子十分中意，可否给娘仔细说说？”
[EN] Lady Xiang said: "That's good then. Lvtiao told me you went to West Lake to see Young Master Qiao. Nanny Wei also told me you're very fond of Young Master Qiao. Can you tell Mother all about it?"
[ZH] 千里寻本就不想瞒着朱淑真的家人：“我失忆后第一次见到文渊，他向我表明想法，才知道他一直以来的心意。而我又将徐家公子忘得一干二净，自然对文渊一见……一见钟情，故而昨日与他相约一起游西湖。”
[EN] Qianli Xun never intended to hide anything from Zhu Shuzhen's family: "The first time I met Wenyuan after losing my memory, he expressed his feelings to me, and I learned of his long-held intentions. Since I had completely forgotten Young Master Xu, I naturally fell in love with Wenyuan at first...at first sight, which is why I agreed to go to West Lake with him yesterday."
[ZH] 向夫人点点头：“乔家公子在临安年轻一辈中算是出挑的，你爹也常夸赞他，只是……”
[EN] Lady Xiang nodded: "Young Master Qiao is indeed outstanding among the young generation in Lin'an. Your father often praises him. It's just..."
[ZH] 千里寻见向夫人停住，问道：“娘，只是什么？”
[EN] Seeing Lady Xiang pause, Qianli Xun asked: "Mother, just what?"
[ZH] 向夫人面有难色：“只是……我听说乔家已开始为乔公子选亲，好几个媒婆搜罗了不少大家闺秀的生辰八字，你当时有婚姻，乔家自然没有要你的，娘担心……”
[EN] Lady Xiang looked troubled: "It's just...I've heard that the Qiao family has already started selecting a bride for Young Master Qiao. Several matchmakers have collected the birth dates of many young ladies from good families. Since you were married at the time, the Qiao family naturally didn't consider you. Mother is worried..."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“夫人不必过分担忧，乔公子中意我们家姑娘，定会向他的父母提起。”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "Madam needn't worry too much. Since Young Master Qiao is fond of our young lady, he will surely mention it to his parents."
[ZH] 千里寻插嘴道：“文渊告诉我，今日就向家人提起此事，让他父母来朱家提亲。”
[EN] Qianli Xun interrupted: "Wenyuan told me he would mention this to his family today and have his parents come to the Zhu family to propose marriage."
[ZH] 向夫人点点头，继续忧虑道：“你毕竟有过婚姻，只怕乔家嫌弃。”
[EN] Lady Xiang nodded, continuing anxiously: "After all, you've been married before. I'm afraid the Qiao family might look down on you."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷接着说：“我们姑娘是临安知府的女儿，又是江南女词人，也是真正的大家闺秀，虽有过一次婚姻，却并未与练兵官圆房，依然清白，乔家没有嫌弃的道理。”
[EN] Nanny Wei continued: "Our young lady is the daughter of the Lin'an prefect, a famous female poet of Jiangnan, and a true young lady from a good family. Though she's been married once, she never consummated the marriage with the military training officer. She's still pure. The Qiao family has no reason to look down on her."
[ZH] 向夫人仍旧担忧：“话虽如此，乔家是清白人家，又是皇亲国戚，只有这么一个儿子，必然要求高，我就是怕淑真与乔公子的感情会是竹篮打水一场空。”
[EN] Lady Xiang remained worried: "Even so, the Qiao family is a respectable family and imperial relatives. They only have this one son, so their requirements must be high. I'm just afraid that Shuzhen's affection for Young Master Qiao will prove as futile as fetching water with a wicker basket."
[ZH] 千里寻起身，拉起向夫人的手：“娘，您别瞎想，趁女儿现在恢复自由之身，陪您好好逛逛，要不我们去西湖？”
[EN] Qianli Xun stood up and took Lady Xiang's hand: "Mother, don't overthink it. While your daughter is free now, let me take you out for a good stroll. How about we go to West Lake?"
[ZH] 向夫人笑道：“你昨日去了，今日又去，也不嫌烦？”
[EN] Lady Xiang laughed: "You went yesterday and want to go again today? Aren't you tired of it?"
[ZH] “不烦！不烦！”千里寻像撒娇一样，有娘的感觉真不错。
[EN] "Not tired at all! Not tired at all!" Qianli Xun acted playfully. It felt so nice to have a mother.
[ZH] 向夫人说：“我才不去西湖，又远人又多，我们家的花园也不错，你陪娘和韦嬷嬷在花园里逛逛得了。”
[EN] Lady Xiang said: "I'm not going to West Lake. It's far and crowded. Our family garden is nice enough. You can accompany Mother and Nanny Wei for a stroll in the garden."
[ZH] 千里寻满口答应，她搀着向夫人，韦嬷嬷伴随在一旁，三人在园子里边聊边逛。
[EN] Qianli Xun readily agreed. She supported Lady Xiang, with Nanny Wei accompanying them. The three strolled through the garden, chatting as they walked.
[ZH] 银杏与苦荞跟在后面伺候。
[EN] Yinxing and Kuqiao followed behind to attend them.
[ZH] 千里寻认为她的生活终于恢复正常，不管现在的生活是千里寻的还是朱淑真的，能够确定的是，无论生活发生什么，她都会用积极的态度面对，只要坚持净光法师说的四个字“正思正念”，她相信即使现在是朱淑真的命运，终有一天会走上属于千里寻的命运。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt her life had finally returned to normal. No matter whether this life belonged to Qianli Xun or Zhu Shuzhen, what was certain was that no matter what happened, she would face it with a positive attitude. As long as she held fast to Master Jingguang's four words—“Right Thought and Right Mindfulness”—she believed that even if she were bound by Zhu Shuzhen's fate today, she would one day reclaim the destiny that belonged to Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 正逛着，一个熟悉的身影从眼前闪过，千里寻在心里冷笑道：“真是阴魂不散！”
[EN] As they were strolling, a familiar figure flashed before her eyes. Qianli Xun sneered in her heart: "What a persistent nuisance!"
[ZH] “娘，陈浩宇为何来朱府？”
[EN] "Mother, why is Chen Haoyu at the Zhu residence?"
[ZH] 向夫人朝不远处瞧去，的确是陈家公子，他只身一人，看那样子是要离开。
[EN] Lady Xiang looked toward the distance. It was indeed Young Master Chen, alone and appearing to leave.
[ZH] “他跟你嫂嫂姐弟情深，经常来看她，应该是看完亲姐姐准备离开。”
[EN] "He's very close to your sister-in-law. He often comes to visit her. He's probably finished visiting his older sister and is preparing to leave."
[ZH] 千里寻盯着陈浩宇往出走的身影，不满道：“娘，您都不知道陈浩宇有多坏！昨日我与文渊乘船，遇到他，他故意用船撞我们，我与文渊都掉进水里，全身上下湿透，这仇我迟早会报！”
[EN] Qianli Xun stared at Chen Haoyu's departing figure and said discontentedly: "Mother, you don't know how bad Chen Haoyu is! Yesterday, Wenyuan and I were on a boat when we ran into him. He deliberately rammed our boat. Wenyuan and I both fell into the water and got soaked from head to toe. I'll get revenge for this sooner or later!"
[ZH] “唉！这孩子是淘气了些，你既然跟他处不来，以后离他远点儿。”向夫人说得很轻松。
[EN] "Sigh! That boy is a bit mischievous. Since you don't get along with him, just stay away from him in the future." Lady Xiang said it lightly.
[ZH] 千里寻诧异道：“娘，您怎么能认为他是淘气？他就是彻彻底底的坏人！”
[EN] Qianli Xun said in surprise: "Mother, how can you think he's just mischievous? He's thoroughly bad!"
[ZH] “坏人？不至于。”向夫人依然不信。
[EN] "Bad? That's going too far." Lady Xiang still didn't believe it.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“不信，您问嬷嬷，在竹林时，他是怎么欺负我的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun said: "If you don't believe me, ask Nanny Wei. Ask her how he bullied me at the bamboo forest."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷笑道：“夫人，陈家公子的确纨绔，姑娘从他手中救了夏果，他心中不服，买了竹林的田根，又找来打手，不仅把竹林拿走，还让打手跟姑娘打了一架，还好，现在的姑娘有些身手，倒把那打手打得要认姑娘当师傅，呵呵！我想起来都觉得好笑。”
[EN] Nanny Wei smiled: "Madam, Young Master Chen is indeed a dandy. After the young lady saved Xiaguo from him, he was unwilling to accept it. He bought the land rights to the bamboo forest and hired thugs. Not only did he take the bamboo forest, but he also had the thugs fight the young lady. Fortunately, the current young lady has some martial skills. She actually beat the thug so badly that he wanted to recognize her as his master. Hehe! It makes me laugh just thinking about it."
[ZH] 向夫人琢磨着“现在的姑娘”，她笑不出来：“竟然有这等事？看来陈家公子的确纨绔。不过，韦嬷嬷，你不觉得淑真现在会功夫，实在离奇？”
[EN] Lady Xiang pondered the words "the current young lady." She couldn't laugh: "There was such a thing? It seems Young Master Chen is indeed a dandy. But, Nanny Wei, don't you find it strange that Shuzhen now knows martial arts?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷看一眼姑娘，回道：“当然离奇，但我并不惊讶，不管姑娘变成什么样儿，只要她还在，其他并不重要。”
[EN] Nanny Wei glanced at the young lady and replied: "Of course it's strange, but I'm not surprised. No matter what the young lady has become, as long as she's still here, nothing else matters."
[ZH] 向夫人点点头：“你说的有道理。淑真，你与以前大不相同，但在娘的眼里，你还是以前的淑真。”
[EN] Lady Xiang nodded: "What you say makes sense. Shuzhen, you're very different from before, but in Mother's eyes, you're still the same Shuzhen."
[ZH] 千里寻很想对二位长辈说出她是谁，又来自哪里，又担心和盘托出，她们会难过，她不想看到亲人伤心的样子，故而将想要说出的话咽回肚内。
[EN] Qianli Xun very much wanted to tell the two elders who she was and where she came from, but worried that if she told everything, they would be sad. She didn't want to see her loved ones hurt, so she swallowed the words she wanted to say.
[ZH] “娘，不管我变成什么样，我都是您的女儿。”
[EN] "Mother, no matter what I've become, I'm still your daughter."
[ZH] 女儿充满情感的表达，向夫人感到无比幸福，不再计较现在的女儿和以前女儿的不同。
[EN] Hearing her daughter's emotional expression, Lady Xiang felt overwhelmingly happy and stopped caring about the differences between her current daughter and her former daughter.
[ZH] 千里寻发现她们说话间，陈浩宇已不见身影，她的心情瞬间变好：“我们继续逛吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun noticed that while they were talking, Chen Haoyu had disappeared. Her mood instantly improved: "Let's continue our stroll."
[ZH] 正走着，有丫鬟急急匆匆走来，挡住她们的去路：“夫人，姑爷……不对，林练兵官派人来问今日能不能把小姐身边的丫鬟夏果带回林府，林家的轿子已在外面候着。”
[EN] As they were walking, a maid came hurriedly and blocked their path: "Madam, the son-in-law...no, Military Training Officer Lin sent someone to ask if Xiaguo, the young lady's maid, can be brought back to the Lin residence today. The Lin family's sedan chair is waiting outside."
[ZH] 向夫人问女儿：“淑真，你说该怎么办？”
[EN] Lady Xiang asked her daughter: "Shuzhen, what should we do?"
[ZH] 千里寻向丫鬟吩咐：“你去给林家来的人说，夏果已不是我的丫鬟，人不在朱府，回乡下去了，林觉想要夏果，直接到乡下去找。”
[EN] Qianli Xun instructed the maid: "Go tell the people from the Lin family that Xiaguo is no longer my maid. She's not at the Zhu residence. She's gone back to the countryside. If Lin Jue wants Xiaguo, he can go look for her in the countryside directly."
[ZH] “是。”丫鬟离开，前去回话。
[EN] "Yes." The maid left to deliver the message.
[ZH] 向夫人担心道：“这样可以吗？”
[EN] Lady Xiang worried: "Is this alright?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“不管可不可以，也只能先这么回练兵官，实在不行时，再见机行事。”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "Whether it's alright or not, we can only reply to the military training officer this way for now. If it really doesn't work, we'll deal with it as the situation demands."
[ZH] 千里寻赞道：“还是嬷嬷最了解我。”
[EN] Qianli Xun praised: "Nanny Wei knows me best."
[ZH] 丫鬟来到朱府侧门，对林家来的下人说：“我家姑娘说了，夏果已不是朱家丫鬟，她回乡下了，林家若还想要夏果，尽可去乡下找。”
[EN] The maid came to the side gate of the Zhu residence and told the Lin family's servant: "Our young lady says that Xiaguo is no longer a Zhu family maid. She's gone back to the countryside. If the Lin family still wants Xiaguo, you can go look for her in the countryside."
[ZH] 朱府丫鬟说话的气势与别家不同，林府的下人也不敢多问。瞧着那丫鬟进了门，朱府的下人又把门重重关上。林府来的人叹口气，吩咐抬轿子的人原路返回。
[EN] The Zhu family maid's tone was different from other families. The Lin family's servant didn't dare ask more. Watching the maid enter the gate, the Zhu family's servant heavily closed the gate again. The person from the Lin family sighed and ordered the sedan chair bearers to return the way they came.
[ZH] 正要走，被陈浩宇拦住：“等等！”
[EN] Just as they were about to leave, Chen Haoyu stopped them: "Wait!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇今日探望姐姐陈文心，顺便从姐姐口中探些有关朱淑真之事，好寻找机会再伺机报复。
[EN] Today, Chen Haoyu visited his older sister Chen Wenxin and took the opportunity to probe her for information about Zhu Shuzhen, looking for a chance to retaliate.
[ZH] 他从侧门出来，林家的下人刚好抬着轿子过来。陈浩宇站在一旁听了一会儿，才知原委，心想：“我看上的美人儿，没能得手，竟然便宜了林觉这小子，都怪朱淑真，不如在此观察一会儿，看能不能找到报复朱淑真的机会。”
[EN] He came out from the side gate just as the Lin family's servants were bringing the sedan chair. Chen Haoyu stood to the side and listened for a while before learning the whole story. He thought: "The beauty I set my sights on, I couldn't get, and it turns out she's gone to that brat Lin Jue. It's all Zhu Shuzhen's fault. Might as well observe here for a while and see if I can find a chance to get revenge on Zhu Shuzhen."
[ZH] 林府的下人认识陈浩宇，知道他是纨绔子弟，不敢得罪：“陈公子有何指教？”
[EN] The Lin family's servants recognized Chen Haoyu. Knowing he was a dandy, they didn't dare offend him: "Does Young Master Chen have any advice?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“回去告诉林觉，朱淑真是骗他，夏果那丫头已被她藏起来，具体在哪里我不清楚，但人肯定还在城里。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu smiled: "Go back and tell Lin Jue that Zhu Shuzhen is lying to him. That maid Xiaguo has been hidden away by her. I don't know exactly where, but the person is definitely still in the city."
[ZH] 林府的下人不敢轻易相信陈浩宇，毕竟他是有名的纨绔子弟：“陈公子怎么如此清楚？”
[EN] The Lin family's servants didn't dare believe Chen Haoyu easily. After all, he was a famous dandy: "How does Young Master Chen know so clearly?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“我怎么可能不清楚？我刚从朱府出来，是我姐姐告诉我的，她说的能有假？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: "How could I not know? I just came out from the Zhu residence. My older sister told me. Could what she says be false?"
[ZH] 林府下人谢道：“感谢陈公子相告。”
[EN] The Lin family's servant thanked: "Thank you for telling us, Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇为自己的一次挑拨感到满意，他没有机会收拾朱淑真，给她制造些麻烦也不错。
[EN] Chen Haoyu was satisfied with his act of stirring up trouble. Since he had no chance to deal with Zhu Shuzhen himself, causing her some trouble was not bad either.
[ZH] 林家下人抬着空轿子回到林府，将情况给林觉说了，又将陈浩宇的原话告知。
[EN] The Lin family's servants carried the empty sedan chair back to the Lin residence, told Lin Jue the situation, and relayed Chen Haoyu's exact words.
[ZH] 林觉气道：“朱淑真，你为了一个丫鬟，不顾曾经的夫妻情分，别怪我不客气，你不想把夏果给我，我偏要把她弄到手，走着瞧！”
[EN] Lin Jue said angrily: "Zhu Shuzhen, for the sake of a mere servant, you've cast aside what little remained of our past kinship. Don't blame me for showing no mercy. You don't want to give me Xiaguo? I'm determined to get her. Just you wait and see!"

#CH 76
[ZH] 一大早，乔文仙起床后，就往仪王的院子里跑，还未向仪王分享快乐，他又怎能轻易放弃？
[EN] Early in the morning, after getting up, Qiao Wenxian ran straight to Prince Yi's courtyard. How could he give up so easily before sharing his happiness with Prince Yi?
[ZH] 一进院子，仪王正在练习拳法。
[EN] As soon as he entered the courtyard, he saw Prince Yi practicing boxing.
[ZH] 精妙的拳法使得乔文仙暂时忘记分享快乐，站在一旁陷入沉思：“这还是以前的仪王吗？除长得像，其他没有一点儿类似，他长年病着，哪有时间练习拳法？难道起死回生可以让人改变？真是世界之大，无奇不有。”
[EN] The exquisite boxing made Qiao Wenxian temporarily forget about sharing his happiness. He stood aside, lost in thought: “Is this truly the same Prince Yi as before? Aside from his appearance, there's not a single point of resemblance. He's been ill for years—when did he have time to practice boxing? Can being brought back to life actually change a person? The world is truly full of wonders.”
[ZH] 白风在一旁伺候王爷，见乔公子前来，行礼道：“乔公子。”
[EN] Baifeng was attending the prince nearby. Seeing Young Master Qiao arrive, he bowed and said: "Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 乔文仙问：“你为何今日没去西三胡同？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian asked: "Why didn't you go to Xisan Hutong today?"
[ZH] “王爷说以后不用再去。”
[EN] "The prince said there's no need to go anymore."
[ZH] “难道已找到千里寻？”
[EN] "Have you found Qianlixun already?"
[ZH] “还没有。”
[EN] "Not yet."
[ZH] 乔文仙心想，也许仪王突然醒悟，明白在另一个世界见到的人，怎么可能在这个世界找到？
[EN] Qiao Wenxian thought to himself: Perhaps Prince Yi has suddenly come to his senses, realizing that someone met in another world couldn't possibly be found in this one?
[ZH] 申简辰练完一套拳法，收了功，文渊前来的目的他再清楚不过，该面对的总要面对。
[EN] Shen Jianchen finished his set of boxing and gathered his qi. He knew all too well why Wenyuan had come. What must be faced has to be faced eventually.
[ZH] “今早我要在你这里用饭。”见仪王已练完，乔文仙上前拉住他，又对白风说，“叫上南双，到后厨把我和仪王的早饭端过来。”
[EN] "I'm having breakfast here this morning." Seeing that Prince Yi had finished practicing, Qiao Wenxian stepped forward and grabbed his arm, then said to Baifeng, "Get Nanshuang and bring our breakfast from the kitchen."
[ZH] “是。”白风跑出去找南双，他每日去西三胡同，都没机会跟南双在一起玩儿，从今日起终于有时间。
[EN] "Yes." Baifeng ran off to find Nanshuang. Every day he went to Xisan Hutong, he never had a chance to play with Nanshuang. Starting today, he finally had the time.
[ZH] “我一会儿还要去府衙，只怕不能陪你太久。”申简辰虽已做好听文渊炫耀感情的准备，还是不由自主说出此话。
[EN] "I still need to go to the yamen shortly. I'm afraid I can't keep you company for long." Though Shen Jianchen had prepared himself to listen to Wenyuan show off about his romance, he couldn't help but say this.
[ZH] 文渊放开仪王：“哦？你倒变得勤政起来，是好事。放心，我占用不了你多长时间，一顿早饭的功夫而已，一会儿你回府衙，我回乔府。”
[EN] Wenyuan let go of Prince Yi: “Oh? You've actually become diligent about governance. That's a good thing. Don't worry, I won't take up much of your time—just the duration of a breakfast. Later you go back to the yamen, and I'll return to the Qiao residence.”
[ZH] “哦，回去后代我向外公问好。”
[EN] "Oh, when you go back, send my regards to your grandfather."
[ZH] “那是自然，你知道我为何要回去？”
[EN] "Of course. Do you know why I'm going back?"
[ZH] “我怎么知道？”申简辰边说，边朝屋内走去。
[EN] "How would I know?" Shen Jianchen said as he walked toward the room.
[ZH] 乔文仙跟上，待仪王坐定后，他直接来到茶桌前，开始为二人做茶：“我这次回去是要告诉父亲和母亲，请他们到朱家提亲。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian followed. Once Prince Yi was seated, he went straight to the tea table and began preparing tea for the two of them: "This time I'm going back to tell my father and mother, asking them to go to the Zhu family to propose marriage."
[ZH] “提亲？会……会不会太急了些？”申简辰的内心像被针扎了一下，有种微痛之感，可表面依然平静。
[EN] "Propose marriage? Isn't...isn't it a bit too hasty?" Shen Jianchen felt as if his heart had been pricked by a needle, a slight pang of pain, yet his surface remained calm.
[ZH] 乔文仙专注做茶，并未观察仪王：“一点儿都不急，我喜欢淑真是几年前的事，现在她好不容易答应，我要趁热打铁，赶紧把她娶回乔府，耽搁下去，只怕有更优秀的人把她抢走。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian focused on making tea, not observing Prince Yi: "Not hasty at all. I've liked Shuzhen for several years. Now that she's finally agreed, I need to strike while the iron is hot and marry her back to the Qiao residence quickly. If I delay, I'm afraid someone better might steal her away."
[ZH] 申简辰瞧着文渊在茶盏中不断击拂，每一下都像是击在他心痛之处：“你这么不自信？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen watched Wenyuan whisking the tea continuously in the cup. Each stroke felt like a blow to his aching heart: "Are you so insecure?"
[ZH] “不是不自信，是淑真太优秀，喜欢她的人太多。她刚和离，一旦消息传出，估计上门提亲的必然不会少，所以，我得赶紧告知父母，让他们知道此事，去朱家把亲事定下来，最好今年成亲。”
[EN] “It's not insecurity—it's that Shuzhen is too outstanding, and too many people like her. She's just recently divorced. Once the news spreads, there will surely be no shortage of people coming to propose marriage. So I need to hurry and inform my parents, let them know about this, go to the Zhu family to settle the engagement, and preferably have the wedding this year.”
[ZH] “成亲？朱淑真可同意？”申简辰虽知道答案，还是不由问出口。
[EN] "Wedding? Has Zhu Shuzhen agreed?" Though Shen Jianchen already knew the answer, he couldn't help but ask.
[ZH] “她当然同意，昨日还说……”乔文仙不好意思说下去。
[EN] "Of course she agreed. Yesterday she even said..." Qiao Wenxian felt too embarrassed to continue.
[ZH] 申简辰忍着心痛，追问道：“还说什么？”
[EN] Enduring the heartache, Shen Jianchen pressed: "What else did she say?"
[ZH] 茶盏中已冒出丰富的茶沫，乔文仙端起来，给仪王放到面前，大胆说：“她还说想跟我生好多孩子。她当着众人的面说出这些话，搞得我很不好意思。”
[EN] Rich tea foam had already risen in the cup. Qiao Wenxian picked it up, placed it in front of Prince Yi, and boldly said: "She also said she wants to have many children with me. She said these things in front of everyone, which made me quite embarrassed."
[ZH] 瞧着文渊嘴上说着“不好意思”，却满脸享受的样子，申简辰端起茶盏又是一饮而尽。
[EN] Seeing Wenyuan say "embarrassed" while looking thoroughly pleased, Shen Jianchen picked up his tea cup and drained it in one gulp.
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“怎么又不品？有失王爷身份。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said: "Why aren't you tasting it again? That's not fitting for a prince."
[ZH] 白风与南双端着早饭进来，为二人摆好。早饭很简单，一碗七宝素粥，两个肉包子，外加一个茶叶蛋。摆好后，两人到院中去玩耍。
[EN] Baifeng and Nanshuang came in carrying breakfast and set it up for the two of them. Breakfast was simple—a bowl of Seven-treasure Vegetable Porridge, two meat buns, and a tea egg. After setting everything out, the two servants went out to the courtyard to play.
[ZH] 乔文仙坐下，开始享用早饭。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian sat down and began enjoying his breakfast.
[ZH] 申简辰吃了一口粥，试探地问道：“文渊，如若有人从你身边把幽栖居士抢走，你会如何？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen took a sip of porridge and asked tentatively: "Wenyuan, if someone were to snatch Youqi Jushi away from you, what would you do?"
[ZH] 乔文仙咬了一口包子，听到这话，赶紧咽下：“我……我就怕这种事情发生，才要尽快提亲。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian bit into a bun. Hearing this, he hurriedly swallowed: "I...I'm afraid of exactly that happening, which is why I need to propose marriage as soon as possible."
[ZH] 申简辰的问题与其说是试探，不如说正是他内心真实的想法。
[EN] Shen Jianchen's question was less a test than a reflection of his true feelings.
[ZH] 可就目前状况来看，他毫无希望。面前的饭食，已没有任何吸引力。
[EN] But given the current situation, he had no hope at all. The food before him had lost all appeal.
[ZH] “实在没胃口，你在此慢慢享用，我去趟府衙。”
[EN] "I really have no appetite. You enjoy your meal here. I'm heading to the yamen."
[ZH] 还没等乔文仙反应过来，仪王已起身走出屋外，喊了声：“白风！随我去府衙！”
[EN] Before Qiao Wenxian could react, Prince Yi had already risen and walked out of the room, calling out: "Baifeng! Come with me to the yamen!"
[ZH] 乔文仙叹道：“仪王好像有心事，他到底怎么了？难道与佳和郡主闹别扭？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian sighed: "Prince Yi seems troubled. What's wrong with him? Could he be having a spat with Princess Jiahe?"
[ZH] 乔文仙带着疑惑用完早饭，来到佳和郡主的闺房外。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian finished breakfast with confusion, then went to Princess Jiahe's boudoir.
[ZH] 南双向里说：“郡主，我家公子前来拜访。”
[EN] Nanshuang called inward: "Princess, Young Master Qiao has come to visit."
[ZH] 不一会儿，喜兰陪着郡主出来。
[EN] After a moment, Xilan accompanied the princess out.
[ZH] “乔公子找我何事？”
[EN] "What brings Young Master Qiao to see me?"
[ZH] “今日仪王看着心情不佳，我不知缘故，故而过来问问。”
[EN] "Prince Yi seems to be in a bad mood today. I don't know the reason, so I came to ask."
[ZH] 乔梦容想起昨晚她吻哥哥的一幕，难道因为那个吻，哥哥生气了？
[EN] Qiao Mengrong remembered kissing her brother last night. Could it be that her brother was angry because of that kiss?
[ZH] “感谢乔公子关心哥哥，他无妨，只是梦容做错事而已。听说乔公子昨日与幽栖居士约会，真是令人羡慕。”
[EN] “Thank you for caring about my brother, Young Master Qiao. He's fine—it's just that Mengrong did something wrong. I heard Young Master Qiao went on a date with Youqi Jushi yesterday. How enviable.”
[ZH] 乔文仙安慰道：“郡主怎会做错事？即使真做错，仪王也不会放在心上。郡主与仪王两情相悦，还有官家赐婚，令文渊羡慕不已。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian comforted her: “How could the princess do anything wrong? Even if you did, Prince Yi wouldn't take it to heart. The princess and Prince Yi share mutual affection, plus the Imperial Marriage Decree—it makes Wenyuan extremely envious.”
[ZH] 乔梦容体会着“两情相悦”，哥哥病着时，的确如此，现在她觉得“两情相悦”似乎离她越来越远。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong reflected on "mutual affection." When her brother was ill, that was indeed the case. Now she felt "mutual affection" seemed to be drifting further and further away from her.
[ZH] 乔文仙注意到佳和郡主有些不对劲，赶紧说：“我还有事，就不在此打扰。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian noticed something was off with Princess Jiahe and quickly said: "I still have matters to attend to, so I won't disturb you further."
[ZH] “乔公子慢走。”待乔文仙离开后，乔梦容问，“喜兰，你说哥哥会不会是生我的气？嫌我昨晚在神柳下亲了他？”
[EN] "Take care, Young Master Qiao." After Qiao Wenxian left, Qiao Mengrong asked: "Xilan, do you think my brother is angry with me? Disgusted that I kissed him under the Sacred Willow last night?"
[ZH] “郡主多虑，在奴婢看来，王爷高兴还来不及，怎么会生气？”
[EN] "The princess overthinks. In this servant's view, the prince would be delighted, not angry."
[ZH] 听了喜兰的解释，乔梦容心安许多。
[EN] Hearing Xilan's explanation, Qiao Mengrong felt much more at ease.
[ZH] “那你说说我与哥哥在外人眼中是两情相悦吗？”
[EN] "Then tell me, do outsiders see my brother and me as sharing mutual affection?"
[ZH] 喜兰虽已在王爷的眼中看不到喜爱郡主的光芒，依然回道：“当然，王爷只会与郡主两情相悦，再说有官家赐婚，王爷迟早会娶郡主。”
[EN] Though Xilan could no longer see the light of affection for the princess in the prince's eyes, she still replied: "Of course. The prince will only share mutual affection with the princess. Besides, with the Imperial Marriage Decree, the prince will marry the princess sooner or later."
[ZH] “官家赐婚，是啊，我还担心什么？哥哥迟早会娶我，可我为什么高兴不起来？是不是我要求太多？”
[EN] "The Imperial Marriage Decree, yes. What am I still worried about? My brother will marry me sooner or later. But why can't I feel happy? Am I asking for too much?"
[ZH] 郡主的问题，喜兰无法回答，只能沉默。
[EN] Xilan couldn't answer the princess's question and could only remain silent.
[ZH] 乔文仙离开幽林别居，乘坐马车往城里的乔府而去。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian left Youlin Bieju and took a carriage toward the Qiao residence in the city.
[ZH] 一进乔府，直接奔向父亲乔守仁居住的院子。
[EN] As soon as he entered the Qiao residence, he headed straight for his father Qiao Shouren's courtyard.
[ZH] 乔守仁正坐在院中品茶，旁边站着伺候的下人。见儿子回来，十分高兴，招呼道：“快过来坐，和我一起品茶。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren was sitting in the courtyard tasting tea, with servants standing by to attend him. Seeing his son return, he was very pleased and called out: "Come sit here and taste tea with me."
[ZH] 下人给乔文仙把茶倒满，他端起来品了一口：“父亲品的是九曲红梅。”
[EN] A servant filled Qiao Wenxian's cup. He picked it up and took a sip: "Father is drinking Jiuqu Hongmei."
[ZH] 乔守仁赞道：“你果然是我的儿子，懂茶，一品便知。临安人都爱喝西湖龙井，可我偏喜欢九曲红梅，只因它滋味甘醇，又暖胃，我胃不好，喝点它既解渴，又对身体有益。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren praised: “You're truly my son—you understand tea. One sip and you know. Everyone in Lin'an loves drinking West Lake Longjing, but I specifically prefer Jiuqu Hongmei because of its mellow flavor and stomach-warming properties. My stomach isn't good, so drinking a bit of it both quenches thirst and benefits my health.”
[ZH] 乔文仙问：“父亲的胃可好些？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian asked: "Is Father's stomach any better?"
[ZH] “好多了。仪王近期可好？”
[EN] "Much better. How has Prince Yi been lately?"
[ZH] “他是王爷，走到哪里别人都不敢慢待，自然不错。”
[EN] "He's a prince. Wherever he goes, no one dares to slight him. Naturally, he's doing well."
[ZH] “有你在别居陪伴仪王，我也可放一百个心。”
[EN] "With you at the villa accompanying Prince Yi, I can rest completely at ease."
[ZH] 寒暄得差不多，乔文仙想直接说出想法，又觉不好意思。
[EN] After enough small talk, Qiao Wenxian wanted to speak his mind directly but felt too embarrassed.
[ZH] 猛然想到去莱州时，听人说起德甫先生与易安居士的陈年旧事，忽然有了灵感。
[EN] He suddenly remembered hearing about the old story of Master Defu (Zhao Mingcheng) and Yian Jushi (Li Qingzhao) when he went to Laizhou, and inspiration struck.
[ZH] 德甫先生年轻时，为表露心迹，提醒他父亲向李家提亲，编出一梦，梦中有三句话，每一句包含着字谜，不如借来一用。
[EN] When Master Defu (Zhao Mingcheng) was young, to express his feelings and prompt his father to propose marriage to the Li family, he fabricated a dream containing three sentences, each holding a word riddle. Why not borrow this idea?
[ZH] “父亲，昨晚我做了一个梦，梦中有神人对我说：'文渊，你该成亲了。'我一想，也对，我已到成亲的年纪。就问神人：'你可知我会与谁成亲？'神人笑笑，说了三句话，儿子一时体味不出，父亲不妨帮我猜猜。”
[EN] “Father, last night I had a dream. In it, a divine being told me: 'Wenyuan, it's time for you to marry.' I thought about it—he's right. I've reached the age for marriage. So I asked the divine being: 'Do you know whom I'll marry?' The divine being smiled and said three sentences. I can't quite figure them out at the moment. Perhaps Father could help me guess.”
[ZH] 乔守仁正为儿子选亲一事揪心，听到是关于成亲的梦，立刻变得兴致勃勃：“说来听听。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren had been worrying about his son's marriage selection. Hearing it was a dream about marriage, he immediately became enthusiastic: "Tell me about it."
[ZH] 乔文仙见父亲已入套，很是满意，边说边瞧着父亲的表情：“神人说：言与司合，安上已脱，芝芙草拔。”
[EN] Seeing his father had taken the bait, Qiao Wenxian was quite satisfied. He spoke while watching his father's expression: "The divine being said: 'Speech combined with office; peace with its roof removed; herbs stripped of their green heads.'"

#CH 77
[ZH] 乔守仁听完，品了一会儿，皱起眉头：“神人真会故弄玄虚，到底说的是什么，为父一时品不出。”
[EN] After listening, Qiao Shouren pondered for a while, then frowned: "The divine being truly loves being cryptic. What exactly is being said? Your father can't figure it out at the moment."
[ZH] 乔文仙心想，也难怪，德甫先生的父亲是宰相，满腹才学，自然很快品出。自己的父亲虽也有学问，可才学疏浅，又如何能品出其中意味？不如给他提醒提醒：“父亲，我觉得这三句话里包含着字谜。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian thought to himself: No wonder. Master Defu's father was a prime minister, full of learning—naturally he could figure it out quickly. His own father, though educated, had shallower literary skills. How could he decipher the meaning? Better give him a hint: “Father, I think these three sentences contain word riddles.”
[ZH] “字谜？让我再品品。”乔守仁继续在心里念着三句话，还是毫无头绪，“是字谜吗？”
[EN] "Word riddles? Let me think again." Qiao Shouren continued reciting the three sentences in his heart, still with no clue. "Are they word riddles?"
[ZH] 乔文仙很无语，为何这么有趣的事放在自己身上就是不灵，干脆直接说：“据儿子猜'言与司合'是个'词'字，'安上已脱'是个'女'字，'芝芙草拔'是'之'和'夫'字，连起来就是'词女之夫'，儿子觉得神人是说我未来要娶之人是位女词人。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was speechless. Why was something so interesting so hard for him to figure out? He might as well just say it directly: "According to my guess, 'speech combined with office' forms the character 'ci' (word/poetry); 'peace with its roof removed' forms the character 'nv' (woman); 'herbs stripped of their green heads' gives 'zhi' and 'fu.' Put together, it reads 'Husband of the Poetess.' I believe the divine being is saying that the person I'm destined to marry is a female poet."
[ZH] “女词人。”乔守仁口中默默念着。
[EN] "A female poet." Qiao Shouren murmured silently.
[ZH] 钱氏已把选来的闺秀一一给乔守仁详细说过，其中没有女词人。在临安最有名的女词人除了朱家姑娘，还能有谁？
[EN] Lady Qian had already described in detail all the young ladies selected for marriage. Not one of them was a female poet. In Lin'an, who was the most famous female poet besides the Zhu family's daughter?
[ZH] 乔守仁早听到有关儿子与朱家姑娘的风言风语，本也想去朱家提亲，可后来朱家把女儿匆匆嫁给林家，乔守仁也就断了念想。
[EN] Qiao Shouren had long heard rumors about his son and the Zhu family's daughter. He had originally considered proposing marriage to the Zhu family, but when they hastily married their daughter to the Lin family, Qiao Shouren gave up the idea.
[ZH] 近几日朱家姑娘和离一事在临安富贵人家传开，乔守仁又怎能不知？儿子以梦为由，暗示想娶朱家姑娘，看来二人确实有情。可乔家就这么一个儿子，娶谁不好，偏偏要娶一个离过婚的女子，乔守仁是万万不能同意。
[EN] In recent days, the news of the Zhu family's daughter's divorce had spread among Lin'an's wealthy families. How could Qiao Shouren not know? His son used a dream as an excuse to hint at wanting to marry the Zhu family's daughter. It seemed the two truly had feelings for each other. But the Qiao family had only this one son. Why did he have to marry a divorced woman of all people? Qiao Shouren absolutely could not agree.
[ZH] “哦，女词人，你的婚姻大事要父母说了算，只是一个梦，别太当回事。”
[EN] “Oh, a female poet. Your marriage is a matter for your parents to decide. It's just a dream—don't take it too seriously.”
[ZH] 父亲显然是拒绝，既然话已说开，他心中也应知道自己想娶谁，不如更加直白地表明态度：“请父亲到朱家为儿子提亲，儿子这辈子非淑真不娶。”
[EN] His father was clearly refusing. Since the topic was already out in the open, and his father should know whom he wanted to marry, he might as well be more straightforward: "Please go to the Zhu family to propose marriage for me, Father. I will marry no one but Shuzhen in this lifetime."
[ZH] 乔守仁觉得儿子还是太年轻，不懂什么是婚姻：“千万不要说非谁不娶之类的话，小心到时食言。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren felt his son was still too young to understand what marriage was: "Never say things like 'I'll marry no one but her.' Be careful not to break your word later."
[ZH] “父亲难道不同意去朱家提亲？”
[EN] "Does Father disagree with going to the Zhu family to propose marriage?"
[ZH] 乔守仁叹口气：“唉，要是朱家姑娘没婚姻那回事，我当然会去，他们家姑娘也肯定是乔家的第一首选。可惜啊，嫁人不淑，还闹到和离，好好的姑娘身上有了污点，你是乔家的独苗，娶妻更应慎重，娶一个别人家不要的姑娘回来，岂不是给乔家门楣上抹黑？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren sighed: “Sigh, if the Zhu family's daughter hadn't been married before, of course I would go. Their daughter would definitely be the Qiao family's first choice. But alas, she married the wrong man and ended in divorce. A good girl now has a stain on her. You're the only son of the Qiao family. Choosing a wife requires even more caution. Marrying a girl that another family has cast aside—wouldn't that tarnish the Qiao family's honor？”
[ZH] 乔文仙急道：“淑真虽嫁过人，可她一身清白，若真没了清白，我也不嫌弃，还请父亲同意！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said anxiously: "Though Shuzhen has been married, she is completely pure. Even if she weren't, I still wouldn't mind. Please consent, Father!"
[ZH] “胡闹！”乔守仁批评完，又和颜悦色，“文渊啊，你看，朱家姑娘刚刚和离，就去提亲，也不合适，此事放放再说。”
[EN] "Nonsense!" After scolding, Qiao Shouren softened his tone: "Wenyuan, you see, the Zhu family's daughter just recently divorced. It's not appropriate to propose marriage right away. Let's put this matter aside for now."
[ZH] “父亲可是愿意去朱家提亲？”
[EN] "Is Father willing to go to the Zhu family to propose marriage?"
[ZH] 儿子再次逼问，乔守仁不想与儿子闹得太僵：“要不要去朱家提亲，我得和你母亲，还有你娘好好合计合计再说，毕竟是乔家的大事，总不能仓促决定。你跟我说了，就不用再亲自找她们，我跟她们说就行，你只管等消息。好好在别居陪仪王，不要到处乱跑，有事可以让南双回来传话。”
[EN] His son pressed again. Qiao Shouren didn't want to make things too tense: “Whether to go to the Zhu family to propose marriage—I need to discuss it carefully with your mother and your concubine. After all, it's a major matter for the Qiao family. We can't decide in haste. You've told me, so there's no need to seek them out yourself. I'll tell them. Just wait for news. Stay at the villa and accompany Prince Yi. Don't run around everywhere. If there's something, you can have Nanshuang come back to deliver a message.”
[ZH] 父亲的态度模棱两可，乔文仙充满担忧，他又不能逼迫父亲立即答应，不如让家人好好商量，过上几日再回来催问。便起身拜别父亲，继续回幽林别居。
[EN] His father's attitude was ambiguous, leaving Qiao Wenxian full of worry. He couldn't force his father to agree immediately. Better let the family discuss it properly and come back in a few days to press the matter. He rose, bid farewell to his father, and continued back to Youlin Bieju.
[ZH] 话说申简辰出了幽林别居，进了城，吩咐白风去朱府。
[EN] Meanwhile, after leaving Youlin Bieju, Shen Jianchen entered the city and ordered Baifeng to go to the Zhu residence.
[ZH] 白风不解，明明出门时说要去府衙，为何又要去朱府？王爷看上去不高兴，他不敢多问，乖乖扭转马头。
[EN] Baifeng was puzzled. When they left, the prince clearly said they were going to the yamen. Why were they going to the Zhu residence now? The prince looked unhappy, so he didn't dare ask more and obediently turned the horse around.
[ZH] 白风在临安城的几日，没事到处逛，已对路况了然于胸。尤其是大户人家住在哪里，他已基本摸清。主要是因为以前太穷，老寻摸着去大户人家当个下人，养成关注大户人家的习惯。成了王爷的贴身侍卫，还是改不了养成的臭毛病。
[EN] During his days in Lin'an, Baifeng had wandered around in his spare time and become thoroughly familiar with the roads. He especially knew where the wealthy families lived. Mainly because he used to be so poor, always looking for opportunities to become a servant in a rich household, he'd developed the habit of paying attention to where the big families lived. Even after becoming the prince's personal guard, he couldn't break this old habit.
[ZH] 马车很快来到朱府的侧门外，申简辰走下马车，对守在门口的下人说：“请告诉幽栖居士，有位申公子来访。”
[EN] The carriage soon arrived at the side gate of the Zhu residence. Shen Jianchen stepped down and told the servant guarding the gate: "Please inform Youqi Jushi that a Young Master Shen has come to visit."
[ZH] “请公子随我进去等。”
[EN] "Please come in and wait, Young Master."
[ZH] “不必，我就在府外，与你家姑娘说两句话就走。”
[EN] "No need. I'll just wait outside the residence. I only need to speak a few words with your young lady and then leave."
[ZH] 下人进入府内，寻找小姐的身影。
[EN] The servant entered the residence to look for the young lady.
[ZH] 千里寻与向夫人和韦嬷嬷刚刚逛完园子，正往回走。
[EN] Qianli Xun had just finished strolling through the garden with Lady Xiang and Nanny Wei and was heading back.
[ZH] 下人迎面走来，拜道：“小姐，门外有位申公子求见。”
[EN] The servant approached and bowed: "Young lady, a Young Master Shen is requesting an audience at the gate."
[ZH] “申公子？”千里寻想起宴会那日假借申公子之名的仪王，“娘，估计是仪王，我出去会会他。”
[EN] "Young Master Shen?" Qianli Xun remembered Prince Yi using that name at the banquet. "Mother, it's probably Prince Yi. I'll go out and meet him."
[ZH] “既然是仪王，不如把他请进家里来。”
[EN] "Since it's Prince Yi, why not invite him in?"
[ZH] “他既不愿借着仪王的名头来朱府，应该是不想进来，您与嬷嬷先回，我去去就来。”
[EN] "Since he doesn't want to come to the Zhu residence under Prince Yi's name, he probably doesn't want to come in. You and Nanny Wei go back first. I'll be right back."
[ZH] 申简辰一直盯着朱府的侧门，这是他知道朱淑真就是千里寻后，第一次正式见她，难免有些紧张。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had been staring at the side gate of the Zhu residence. This was the first time he would formally see her after learning that Zhu Shuzhen was Qianli Xun. He couldn't help but feel somewhat nervous.
[ZH] 白风不大明白，王爷为何不见朱晞颜，而是要见他的女儿？
[EN] Baifeng didn't quite understand why the prince wanted to see Zhu Xiyan's daughter instead of Zhu Xiyan himself.
[ZH] 当千里寻从朱府走出时，申简辰迎上去：“幽……幽栖居士。”
[EN] When Qianli Xun walked out of the Zhu residence, Shen Jianchen stepped forward: "You...Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 千里寻赶紧行礼：“仪王殿下，不知找我何事？”
[EN] Qianli Xun quickly bowed: "Your Highness Prince Yi, may I ask what brings you here?"
[ZH] 申简辰想要把他是申简辰之事说明，可想到她与文渊情意浓浓，还有她曾拒绝他的话，又变得犹豫不决。
[EN] Shen Jianchen wanted to reveal that he was truly Shen Jianchen, but thinking of her deep affection for Wenyuan and her previous rejection of him, he became hesitant again.
[ZH] 他从怀里掏出红绸：“昨晚，我去神柳下看风景，一条红绸飘落下来，我见上面写着幽栖居士的名字，还有'千里寻'三个字，今日过来，一是送红绸，二是想当面问问，幽栖居士可是写话本的千里寻？”
[EN] He took out the red silk from his robe: “Last night, I went to admire the scenery under the Sacred Willow. A red silk floated down. I saw Youqi Jushi's name written on it, along with the three characters 'Qianli Xun.' I came today for two reasons: first, to return the red silk; second, to ask in person—is Youqi Jushi the Qianli Xun who writes storytelling scripts？”
[ZH] 白风听到，才明白为何王爷不让他再去西三胡同守着，原来千里寻才子正是幽栖居士。
[EN] Hearing this, Baifeng finally understood why the prince no longer needed him to guard Xisan Hutong. It turned out that the talented Qianli Xun was actually Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 真是奇怪，一个姑娘家写个话本偏偏要装作才子，也不知是怎么想的，害自己一到临安整日待在西三胡同，错过好几次享用美食的机会。
[EN] How strange—a young lady writing storytelling scripts yet pretending to be a male scholar. Who knew what she was thinking? It had caused him to spend all his time at Xisan Hutong ever since arriving in Lin'an, missing several opportunities to enjoy fine food.
[ZH] 千里寻接过红绸，拽住仪王手臂，把他拉到一边。
[EN] Qianli Xun took the red silk, grabbed Prince Yi's arm, and pulled him to the side.
[ZH] 被心爱之人抓着手臂，申简辰的内心不由泛起美好的感觉。
[EN] Held by the arm by the one he loved, Shen Jianchen felt a warm flutter rise within him.
[ZH] 白风正要跟着过去，苦荞瞪着眼睛挡在他面前：“主人家说话，你还是好生在此候着。”
[EN] Baifeng was about to follow when Kuqiao blocked his path, glaring: "The young lady is speaking with our guest. You'd better stay here and wait."
[ZH] 白风虽有力气，却不会对女人动手，加之苦荞瞪他的那一刻，竟然发现这丫鬟的眼睛挺美，不由多看了几眼。
[EN] Though Baifeng was strong, he would never lay hands on a woman. Besides, the moment Kuqiao glared at him, he was surprised to find the maid's eyes quite beautiful and couldn't help but steal a few more glances.
[ZH] 苦荞气道：“谁让你这么盯着我看？”
[EN] Kuqiao said angrily: "Who told you to stare at me like that?"
[ZH] 白风说：“你啊，你瞪我不就是让我看你？难道想让我视而不见？”
[EN] Baifeng said: “You there—you glared at me, didn't you? That's basically asking me to look at you. Did you want me to turn a blind eye？”
[ZH] 苦荞被怼得无语，不再说什么。
[EN] Kuqiao was rendered speechless by the retort and said nothing more.
[ZH] 千里寻把仪王拽到一边，放手后，先行礼：“仪王不要介意，此事我不想让人知道，才把你拉到一边。”
[EN] Qianli Xun pulled Prince Yi to the side. After letting go, she bowed first: "Please don't take offense, Your Highness. I didn't want others to know about this matter, which is why I pulled you aside."
[ZH] 申简辰故意装作生气的样子：“你都把我拉过来，我介意又有何用？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen deliberately pretended to be angry: "You've already pulled me over. What use is it if I take offense?"
[ZH] 千里寻见仪王生气，想着怎么能把他哄高兴，他既喜欢听说书，不如从此处着手。
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi angry, Qianli Xun thought about how to coax him into a good mood. Since he enjoyed listening to storytelling, she might as well start from there.
[ZH] “仪王喜欢我写的故事，大不了我写好话本，先让苦荞送给仪王阅览，您看完后，我再让人送到说话艺人那里，您觉得如何？或者等我再写好新的故事，请仪王去听说书？”
[EN] "Since Your Highness enjoys the stories I write, at most I can have Kuqiao deliver the completed storytelling scripts to you for review first. After you've read them, I'll have someone send them to the professional storytellers. How does that sound? Or, once I've written new stories, I could invite Your Highness to come listen to the storytelling?"
[ZH] 千里寻哪曾哄过申简辰？这一世成为王爷，身份尊贵，竟然被美人所哄，申简辰甚是享受，再也装不下去，笑道：“幽栖居士可要说话算话，不仅手稿要给我看，还要请我听说书。”
[EN] When had Qianli Xun ever coaxed Shen Jianchen before? In this life, as a prince with noble status, he was actually being coaxed by a beauty. Shen Jianchen thoroughly enjoyed it and could no longer keep up the act. He smiled: "Youqi Jushi must keep your word. Not only will you show me the manuscripts, but you'll also invite me to listen to the storytelling."
[ZH] “没问题！也请仪王替我保密，不要把我写话本的事说出去。”
[EN] “No problem! But I also ask Your Highness to keep this secret—don't tell anyone that I write storytelling scripts.”
[ZH] “连文渊都不能说吗？”
[EN] "Not even Wenyuan?"
[ZH] “当然。”
[EN] "Of course."
[ZH] 申简辰很满意，还是问道：“你们互相喜欢对方，还需有所保留？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was quite satisfied but still asked: "You two like each other, yet you still need to keep things from each other?"
[ZH] “文渊喜欢诗词，我不想让他知道我偷偷写下里巴人之作，更不想破坏幽栖居士在他心中的形象。”
[EN] "Wenyuan loves poetry. I don't want him to know I secretly write such lowbrow works. I certainly don't want to ruin Youqi Jushi's image in his mind."
[ZH] “该不会因为文渊你才在话本上留下千里寻的名字？”
[EN] "Could it be that you left the name 'Qianli Xun' on the storytelling scripts because of Wenyuan?"
[ZH] “不是。”千里寻果断否认，她在竹林写话本时，文渊还未出现在她的人生中，“在我看来，这些故事只属于千里寻。”
[EN] "No." Qianli Xun denied it decisively. When she was writing storytelling scripts in the bamboo forest, Wenyuan hadn't yet appeared in her life. "In my view, these stories belong only to Qianli Xun."
[ZH] 申简辰明白过来，继续问：“红绸已给你，打算怎么处理？难道还要挂到神柳上？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen understood and continued: "The red silk has been returned to you. What do you plan to do with it? Are you going to hang it back on the Sacred Willow?"
[ZH] “既然掉落，说明红绸的任务已完成，再挂上去没有任何意义，我会把它收起来，保存好。”
[EN] "Since it fell, it means the red silk has completed its mission. Hanging it back up would be meaningless. I'll keep it and preserve it well."
[ZH] 申简辰瞅一眼千里寻手中的红绸，心想，它的任务难道是帮助他找到千里寻？如果是这样，足以证明他与千里寻之间依然有缘分：“红绸已送到，我还有事，就不在此打扰。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced at the red silk in Qianli Xun's hand and thought: Could its mission have been to help him find Qianli Xun? If so, that was enough proof that the bond between him and Qianli Xun still existed. "The red silk has been delivered. I still have matters to attend to, so I won't disturb you further."
[ZH] 千里寻行礼道：“仪王慢走。”
[EN] Qianli Xun bowed: "Take care, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰在马车上坐好后掀开帘子，探出脑袋：“幽栖居士，你自称'老娘'很有趣，以后不用绷着。”
[EN] After settling into the carriage, Shen Jianchen lifted the curtain and poked his head out: "Youqi Jushi, your habit of calling yourself 'lao niang' is quite amusing. You don't need to hold back from now on."
[ZH] 千里寻感到疑惑，她从未在仪王面前自称过“老娘”，他如何得知？再说她穿越成朱淑真，向来注意，只有碰到像林觉和陈浩宇那类人，才会忍不住爆粗口。
[EN] Qianli Xun felt puzzled. She'd never called herself "lao niang" in front of Prince Yi. How did he know? Besides, ever since transmigrating into Zhu Shuzhen, she'd always been careful. Only when encountering people like Lin Jue and Chen Haoyu would she blurt out coarse language.
[ZH] 千里寻把红绸递给苦荞：“帮我收好，不要让人看到。”
[EN] Qianli Xun handed the red silk to Kuqiao: "Help me keep this safe. Don't let anyone see it."
[ZH] 苦荞接过：“是，小姐。”
[EN] Kuqiao took it: "Yes, Young Lady."
[ZH] 边往府内走，千里寻边思索，不由问出口：“我在仪王面前自称过'老娘'？”
[EN] Walking back into the residence, Qianli Xun pondered and couldn't help asking aloud: "Did I ever call myself 'lao niang' in front of Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 苦荞想想说：“小姐见仪王恭恭敬敬，怎么会自称'老娘'？不过您在乔公子面前这样称呼过自己。”
[EN] Kuqiao thought for a moment and said: "Young Lady is always respectful when seeing Prince Yi. How could you call yourself 'lao niang'? But you did refer to yourself that way in front of Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] 千里寻想起昨日乘船游湖碰到陈浩宇时，的确如此自称过：“也对，应该是文渊告诉他的。”
[EN] Qianli Xun remembered yesterday, when boating on the lake and encountering Chen Haoyu, she had indeed referred to herself that way. "That's right. Wenyuan must have told him."
[ZH] 她对这个答案很满意，不再多想。
[EN] She was quite satisfied with this answer and didn't think about it further.

#CH 78
[ZH] 儿子走后，乔守仁吩咐下人：“去！把夫人和米姨娘请来，就说我有要事商量。”
[EN] After his son left, Qiao Shouren ordered a servant: "Go! Bring Madam Qian and Concubine Mi. Tell them I have important matters to discuss."
[ZH] 下人去了不多时，钱氏与米氏一起前来，跟乔守仁打过招呼后，各自落坐。
[EN] The servant didn't take long before returning with Madam Qian and Concubine Mi. After greeting Qiao Shouren, they each took their seats.
[ZH] 钱氏是主母，自然先发话：“老爷，不知唤我们二人前来有何要事？”
[EN] As the principal wife, Madam Qian naturally spoke first: "My lord, what important matters did you wish to discuss with us?"
[ZH] 乔守仁已命下人把茶具搬到屋内，继续品茶，品完一口说：“刚才文渊回来了一趟……”
[EN] Qiao Shouren had already ordered the servants to bring in the tea set. He continued tasting his tea, then spoke after a sip: "Wenyuan just came back for a visit..."
[ZH] 话还未说完，米氏急忙问：“儿子回来为何不来看我跟姐姐？”
[EN] Before he could finish, Concubine Mi asked anxiously: "Why didn't our son come to see Sister and me when he returned?"
[ZH] “急什么？听我把话说完。”乔守仁批评完，继续心平气和地说，“是我不让他去找你们，你们两个妇道人家，容易感情用事，他若见到你们，只怕会坏了大事。”
[EN] "Why the rush? Let me finish." After reprimanding her, Qiao Shouren continued calmly, "I was the one who told him not to see you. You two are emotional women. If he had seen you, I'm afraid it would have ruined the bigger plan."
[ZH] 钱氏不明所以：“不知文渊为何事回来？”
[EN] Madam Qian didn't understand: "What was Wenyuan's reason for returning?"
[ZH] “是为婚姻之事，他有中意的姑娘，回来请我们去女方家里提亲。”乔守仁显得很轻松。
[EN] "It's about marriage. He has a girl he likes and came back to ask us to propose marriage to her family." Qiao Shouren appeared quite relaxed.
[ZH] 钱氏更是不解：“这是好事，我与妹妹听了只会开心，老爷怎能说我们会坏事？”
[EN] Madam Qian was even more puzzled: "That's good news. Sister and I would only be happy to hear it. How could you say we'd ruin things?"
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“本是一件桩好事，不过文渊喜欢的人是朱家姑娘。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren said: "It would have been good news, except the girl Wenyuan likes is the Zhu family's daughter."
[ZH] 米氏问：“可是朱家刚刚和离的那个女词人？”
[EN] Concubine Mi asked: "The female poet who just divorced from the Zhu family?"
[ZH] 乔守仁点点头：“正是。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren nodded: "Exactly."
[ZH] 钱氏说：“文渊与朱家姑娘倒是志同道合，可惜那姑娘离过婚，怎么能配得上我家文渊？”
[EN] Madam Qian said: “Wenyuan and the Zhu family's daughter are indeed like-minded. It's a pity the girl has been divorced—how could she be worthy of our Wenyuan？”
[ZH] 米氏撇撇嘴：“当然配不上！我上次到裁缝铺做衣裳，碰到通判家的娘子，量完尺寸后，跟她聊了一会儿，从她口中得知朱家姑娘看着柔弱，其实厉害着呢，连陈家公子都打不过，估计林练兵官嫌弃她是母老虎，才不得不同意和离。”
[EN] Concubine Mi curled her lips: “Of course she's not worthy! Last time I went to the tailor shop for clothes, I ran into the magistrate's wife. After taking measurements, I chatted with her for a while. From her, I learned that the Zhu family's daughter looks weak but is actually quite fierce—even Young Master Chen couldn't beat her. I estimate Military Training Officer Lin had to agree to the divorce because he found her a tigress.”
[ZH] 钱氏问：“秦夫人怎么知道这些？”
[EN] Madam Qian asked: "How does Madam Qin know these things?"
[ZH] 米氏回道：“秦夫人的女儿苗知瑜经常跟朱家姑娘参加诗会，她自然晓得。”
[EN] Concubine Mi replied: "Madam Qin's daughter Miao Zhiyu often attends poetry gatherings with the Zhu family's daughter. Naturally, she knows."
[ZH] 钱氏叹道：“原来如此。老爷，我与妹妹正在给文渊选亲，还没个结果，他就提出这档子事，该如何是好？”
[EN] Madam Qian sighed: "I see. My lord, Sister and I are still selecting a bride for Wenyuan with no results yet, and now he brings up this matter. What should we do?"
[ZH] 乔守仁笑道：“看来你们两个与我想法一致，这事就好办。我们三人要统一口径，一旦文渊回来想要说服你们，坚决不能改变态度。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren smiled: "It seems you two share my thoughts. This makes things easier. The three of us must speak with one voice. Once Wenyuan returns to try persuading you, we must not change our stance."
[ZH] 米氏为难道：“老爷，要是文渊死缠硬磨，我怕自己拗不过他。”
[EN] Concubine Mi said hesitantly: "My lord, if Wenyuan pesters relentlessly, I'm afraid I won't be able to resist him."
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“拗不过也得坚持住，实在拗不过就拖着，告诉他婚事一年后再议。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren said: “Even if you can't resist, you must hold firm. If you truly can't resist, then delay—tell him the marriage will be discussed again in a year.”
[ZH] 米氏更加为难：“又拖一年？文渊也不小了。”
[EN] Concubine Mi was even more troubled: "Delay another year? Wenyuan isn't young anymore."
[ZH] 乔守仁说：“不拖还能怎样？难道要把朱家姑娘娶回来？表面上拖着，你们私下利用一年的时间好好给他物色个好姑娘。再说文渊正与朱家姑娘情浓，现在强行拒绝他，只怕他做出什么出格的事来，拖上一年，感情也就淡了，到那时，我们的话他也能听进去。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren said: "If we don't delay, what else can we do? Are we supposed to marry the Zhu family's daughter? On the surface, we delay. In private, you use the year to find him a good girl. Besides, Wenyuan is deeply in love with the Zhu family's daughter right now. If we bluntly reject him, I'm afraid he might do something reckless. If we delay for a year, his feelings will fade. By then, he'll be able to listen to reason."
[ZH] 钱氏十分赞同：“就按老爷说的办。”
[EN] Madam Qian agreed wholeheartedly: "We'll do as you say, my lord."
[ZH] 米氏也点头同意。
[EN] Concubine Mi also nodded in agreement.
[ZH] 申简辰离开朱府，吩咐白风将马车赶到聚香楼。白风以为王爷早饭没吃好，专程来此撮一顿。
[EN] Shen Jianchen left the Zhu residence and ordered Baifeng to drive the carriage to Juxiang Restaurant. Baifeng thought the prince hadn't eaten well at breakfast and had come specifically for a feast.
[ZH] 没想到，一进聚香楼，王爷选好包间，只让他点菜。白风才知道王爷是专程请他。
[EN] Unexpectedly, upon entering Juxiang Restaurant, the prince chose a private room and only asked him to order dishes. Only then did Baifeng realize the prince had invited him specifically.
[ZH] “王爷，您为何要请我吃饭？”
[EN] "My prince, why are you treating me to a meal?"
[ZH] “这段时间你天天往西三胡同跑，错过两次享用美食的机会，今日给你补回来，想吃什么随便点。”
[EN] "You've been running to Xisan Hutong every day lately, missing two chances to enjoy fine food. Today I'm making it up to you. Order whatever you want."
[ZH] 王爷如此慷慨，白风毫不客气，一下子点了七八道菜，还都是聚香楼的拿手好菜，当然少不了他爱吃的东坡肉。
[EN] With the prince being so generous, Baifeng didn't stand on ceremony. He ordered seven or eight dishes at once, all Juxiang Restaurant's specialties, including his favorite Dongpo Pork, of course.
[ZH] 点完菜后，伙计将茶水端上，又去忙活。
[EN] After ordering, the waiter brought tea and went back to work.
[ZH] 白风问：“王爷是怎么知道幽栖居士就是千里寻？”
[EN] Baifeng asked: "How did Your Highness discover that Youqi Jushi is Qianlixun?"
[ZH] “机缘巧合。”
[EN] "By chance."
[ZH] “幽栖居士真奇怪，为何要用别的名字，让王爷找得好苦。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi is so strange. Why use another name, making it so hard for Your Highness to find?"
[ZH] 申简辰叮嘱道：“幽栖居士是千里寻这件事，不能对他人提起。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen instructed: "The matter of Youqi Jushi being Qianlixun must not be mentioned to others."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “不要问原因，记住就行。”
[EN] "Don't ask why. Just remember."
[ZH] “对南双也不能提吗？”
[EN] "Not even to Nanshuang?"
[ZH] “当然，你和我知道即可。”
[EN] "Of course. Only you and I need to know."
[ZH] “这么神秘？”
[EN] "So mysterious?"
[ZH] “不是神秘，是我答应幽栖居士要这么做。”
[EN] "It's not about being mysterious. I promised Youqi Jushi I would do this."
[ZH] “好吧，王爷都答应她，我当然会守口如瓶。”
[EN] "Very well. Since Your Highness promised her, I'll keep my mouth shut."
[ZH] 今日见过千里寻，申简辰心情好了许多。哪怕她未向他流露出丝毫爱意，只与她说说话，他都会觉得人生充满灿烂的阳光。
[EN] After seeing Qianlixun today, Shen Jianchen's mood improved greatly. Even though she hadn't shown him the slightest affection, just talking with her made him feel life shone with brilliant light.
[ZH] 申简辰昨日些微想过，也许放弃对千里寻的情感，人生也不错，起码他还是王爷，身份尊贵，物质优渥，身边还有一位温柔漂亮的妹妹。可今日与千里寻见过一面，他更确定自己根本无法放下她。
[EN] Yesterday, Shen Jianchen had briefly considered that perhaps giving up his feelings for Qianlixun wouldn't be so bad. At least he was still a prince, with noble status, abundant wealth, and a gentle, beautiful sister by his side. But after seeing Qianlixun today, he was even more certain he could never let her go.
[ZH] 聚香楼的伙计把饭菜端上来后，不用王爷招呼，白风自觉地大嚼大咽起来。
[EN] After the Juxiang Restaurant waiter brought the dishes, without the prince even inviting him, Baifeng enthusiastically began devouring his food.
[ZH] 心情变好，食欲跟着变好，申简辰拿起筷子吃起来。他边吃边想，只要千里寻不嫁给文渊，他便一直等着。
[EN] In a better mood, his appetite improved too. Shen Jianchen picked up his chopsticks and began eating. As he ate, he thought: as long as Qianlixun didn't marry Wenyuan, he would keep waiting.
