[ZH] 想起与乔梦容的婚约，申简辰的刚好一些的心情又变得沉重。他放下筷子：“白风，问伙计要壶皇都春。”
[EN] Thinking of his engagement to Qiao Mengrong, Shen Jianchen's slightly improved mood grew heavy again. He put down his chopsticks: "Baifeng, ask the waiter for a pot of Capital Spring."
[ZH] 白风起身出了包间，过一会儿回来：“王爷，皇都春马上到。”然后继续坐下大嚼大咽。
[EN] Baifeng got up and left the private room. After a while, he returned: "My prince, the Capital Spring is coming right away." Then he sat back down and continued eating heartily.
[ZH] 不一会儿，伙计将酒壶和酒盅端来。白风不让伙计倒酒，他主动为王爷满上。
[EN] Soon, the waiter brought the wine pot and cups. Baifeng didn't let the waiter pour. He took the initiative to fill the prince's cup.
[ZH] 申简辰端起酒盅，一饮而尽，接着又满上，又喝下，一连饮了四盅。
[EN] Shen Jianchen picked up his cup, drained it in one gulp, then filled it again and drank it down. He drank four cups in succession.
[ZH] 第五盅时，被白风拦住：“王爷，您不能这样喝酒，会醉的。”
[EN] On the fifth cup, Baifeng stopped him: "My prince, you can't drink like this. You'll get drunk."
[ZH] 白风实在不解，王爷刚才还满脸笑容，为何这会儿又愁容满面，还想要借酒消愁。
[EN] Baifeng truly didn't understand. The prince had been smiling just moments ago. Why was his face now full of sorrow, wanting to drown his worries in alcohol?
[ZH] “吃你的，不用管我，我酒量好着呢。”申简辰说着，继续喝下第五盅。
[EN] "Keep eating. Don't worry about me. I can hold my liquor well." Shen Jianchen said this as he continued to drink the fifth cup.
[ZH] 想要再倒酒时，白风把酒壶挪开：“不行，您得听我的，吃会儿菜，过会儿再喝。”
[EN] When he tried to pour more wine, Baifeng moved the pot away: "No. You have to listen to me. Eat some food first, then drink later."
[ZH] 申简辰不再强求，放下酒盅：“白风，我问你，如果你爹娘给你定下一门亲，可你不喜欢那姑娘，你该怎么办？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stopped pressing. He put down his cup: "Baifeng, let me ask you. If your parents arranged a marriage for you, but you didn't like the girl, what would you do?"
[ZH] “在我身上不可能发生这种事，我娘在的时候听我的，现在她不在了，我的人生我做主。”白风显得很得意。
[EN] "That could never happen to me. When my mother was alive, she listened to me. Now she's gone. I'm the master of my own life." Baifeng looked quite proud.
[ZH] 白风得意的样子令申简辰羡慕，重申道：“我是说如果，假设你父母健在，发生这种事，你该如何做？”
[EN] Baifeng's proud expression made Shen Jianchen envious. He reiterated: “I'm saying if—suppose your parents were still alive and this happened, what would you do？”
[ZH] 从王爷渴求的眼神判断，今日必须给他个答复。看在王爷请客的份儿上，白风决定说说心里的想法。
[EN] Judging from the prince's eager gaze, he had to give an answer today. Considering the prince was treating him, Baifeng decided to share his thoughts.
[ZH] “既然不乐意父母定下的亲事，我会离家出走，跑得越远越好。过几年，姑娘等不下去，自然会嫁给别人，到时我再回去，什么问题都解决。”
[EN] "If I didn't want the marriage my parents arranged, I'd run away from home, as far as possible. After a few years, the girl wouldn't be able to wait and would naturally marry someone else. By then, I'd return, and the problem would be solved."
[ZH] 白风说的方法，申简辰不是没想过。对他来讲，逃跑根本解决不了问题，除圣旨限制外，还有佳和郡主为赵朴的付出，都像沉重的枷锁，令他无法轻松迈开双腿。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had considered the method Baifeng suggested. For him, running away couldn't solve the problem at all. Besides the imperial decree's restrictions, there was also Princess Jiahe's sacrifice for Zhao Pu. Both were like heavy shackles, preventing him from easily taking a step forward.
[ZH] “逃跑，哪有那么容易。”想到无缘无故多出的姻缘，申简辰又觉得自己没有资格再追求千里寻，“把酒给我，只有喝醉，我才不会如此难受。”
[EN] "Running away... it's never that simple." Thinking of the marriage that had been thrust upon him for no reason, Shen Jianchen felt he had no right to pursue Qianlixun. "Give me the wine. Only when I'm drunk won't I feel so miserable."
[ZH] 白风坚持不给，申简辰使出功夫。白风一只手拿着酒壶，另一只手拿着筷子应对。坚持了一会儿，实在抵挡不住，被王爷抓住手腕，一阵疼痛，筷子自然掉落在桌上，又从桌上弹落在地上。
[EN] Baifeng insisted on not giving it. Shen Jianchen used his martial arts. Baifeng held the wine pot in one hand and used chopsticks in the other to defend. After holding on for a while, he couldn't resist any longer. The prince grabbed his wrist, causing sharp pain. The chopsticks naturally fell onto the table, then bounced onto the floor.
[ZH] 王爷顺利从他另一只手中抢过酒壶，继续自斟自饮。
[EN] The prince successfully snatched the wine pot from his other hand and continued pouring and drinking by himself.
[ZH] 白风捡起筷子，用桌上的布子胡乱擦擦，仍旧吃起来。
[EN] Baifeng picked up the chopsticks, wiped them carelessly with the cloth on the table, and continued eating.
[ZH] 一壶酒不够，申简辰又要了两壶……
[EN] One pot of wine wasn't enough. Shen Jianchen ordered two more pots...
[ZH] 喝到第五壶时，醉意袭来，被白风扶上马车。
[EN] By the fifth pot, intoxication claimed him, and Baifeng helped him into the carriage.
[ZH] 白风自语道：“喝成这个样子，怎能去府衙？还是回幽林别居。”
[EN] Baifeng muttered to himself: "In this state, how can he go to the yamen? Better return to Youlin Villa."

#CH 79
[ZH] 回到幽林别居，白风一路将仪王扶着。
[EN] Back at Youlin Villa, Baifeng supported the prince all the way.
[ZH] 仪王倒不耍酒疯，只是一味傻笑，还爱说话：“嘿嘿……白风，我……给你说，我以前……可是公司……高管，管了很多……员工，而且……我长得也……帅，很多女孩子都……都喜欢我，嘿嘿……”
[EN] The prince didn't act wild from drunkenness. He just kept grinning foolishly and loved to talk: "Hehe... Baifeng, let me... tell you. I used to be... a senior executive... at a company. Managed lots of... employees. And... I was also... handsome. Many girls... all liked me. Hehe..."
[ZH] 什么“高管”，什么“公司”，白风根本听不懂，他边扶仪王，边与他搭话：“还是王爷厉害。”
[EN] What “senior executive，” what “company”—Baifeng didn't understand any of it. As he supported the prince, he chimed in: “Your Highness is truly amazing.”
[ZH] “嘿嘿……厉……厉害吧？嘿嘿……我告诉你……，虽然喜欢我的人很多，呃……可我就喜欢一个人，你知道……她叫什么吗？”
[EN] "Hehe... Amaz... amazing, right? Hehe... Let me tell you... even though many people like me, uh... I only like one person. Do you know... what her name is?"
[ZH] 刚想问王爷喜欢的人是谁，佳和郡主挡在面前：“白风，哥哥怎么喝成这样？”
[EN] Just as he was about to ask who the prince liked, Princess Jiahe blocked his path: "Baifeng, why is my brother so drunk?"
[ZH] 看到佳和郡主，白风心想：“我当然知道，王爷喜欢的人不就是佳和郡主吗？”赶紧回道：“王爷带我到聚香楼吃饭，那里的酒好喝，他多喝了几杯。”
[EN] Seeing Princess Jiahe, Baifeng thought: "Of course I know. The person the prince likes must be Princess Jiahe, right?" He quickly replied: "The prince took me to Juxiang Restaurant for a meal. The wine there is good, so he had a few extra cups."
[ZH] 乔梦容吩咐道：“快把哥哥扶回屋里，喜兰，快去后厨煮些醒酒汤来。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong ordered: "Quickly help my brother back to his room. Xilan, hurry to the kitchen and make some hangover soup."
[ZH] 喜兰领命而去。
[EN] Xilan followed the order and left.
[ZH] 申简辰用醉眼看到乔梦容，本来耷拉的脑袋更是耷拉下来，酒醉的他见到这位妹妹依然有种沉重之感，这种沉重感让他变得再次沉默，不再傻笑，也不愿多说一句。
[EN] Through drunken eyes, Shen Jianchen saw Qiao Mengrong. His already drooping head sank even lower. Drunk as he was, the sight of his sister still weighed on him. The heaviness silenced him again. He stopped grinning foolishly. He didn't want to say another word.
[ZH] 白风将王爷扶进屋内，再扶到床边，伺候他躺下。没多久，喜兰把醒酒汤端来。
[EN] Baifeng helped the prince into the room, then to the bedside, and attended to him lying down. Not long after, Xilan brought the hangover soup.
[ZH] 乔梦容让白风把哥哥扶起，她亲自用勺子一口一口喂他。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong had Baifeng prop her brother up. She personally fed him spoonful by spoonful.
[ZH] 申简辰虽酒醉，意识并不模糊，一切看在眼里。他不愿再让梦容喂下去，一把从她手里拿过碗，一口气喝下，把碗递给白风，然后倒头就睡。
[EN] Though drunk, Shen Jianchen's mind wasn't muddled. He saw everything. Not wanting Mengrong to continue feeding him, he suddenly took the bowl from her hands, drank it all in one gulp, handed the bowl to Baifeng, then dropped his head and slept.
[ZH] 乔梦容被哥哥突如其来的举动搞得伤感不已，哥哥明显不是担心她受累，而是不想让她继续照顾下去。她盯着哥哥的背影，感受到从未有过的拒绝。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong was deeply saddened by her brother's sudden action. Clearly, he wasn't worried about her getting tired—he didn't want her to continue caring for him. She stared at her brother's back, feeling a rejection she had never experienced before.
[ZH] 她起身走出屋，来到院中，想起哥哥刚才酒醉时向白风问出的问题：“可我就喜欢一个人，你知道……她叫什么吗？”
[EN] She got up, walked out of the room, and came to the courtyard. She remembered the question her brother had drunkenly asked Baifeng: "But I only like one person. Do you know... what her name is?"
[ZH] 乔梦容无法确定哥哥口中的“她”是否指的正是自己：“喜兰，你说哥哥喜欢人的是谁？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong couldn't be sure if the "her" her brother mentioned referred to herself: "Xilan, who do you think is the person my brother likes?"
[ZH] 喜兰说：“当然是郡主。”
[EN] Xilan said: "Of course it's you, Princess."
[ZH] 白风跟着出来，听到郡主的问题，也给出肯定的回答：“郡主放心，王爷刚才说的人必然是您。”
[EN] Baifeng followed her out. Hearing the princess's question, he also gave a definite answer: "Rest assured, Princess. The person the prince just mentioned must be you."
[ZH] 乔梦容才露出笑容，白风是哥哥的贴身侍卫，哥哥的行踪他最清楚不过，他说是，那便是。
[EN] Only then did Qiao Mengrong show a smile. Baifeng was her brother's personal guard. He knew her brother's whereabouts better than anyone. If he said so, then it must be true.
[ZH] “喜兰，一会儿把我屋里的糕点拿些给白风，算是我的奖赏。”
[EN] "Xilan, later bring some pastries from my room to Baifeng as my reward."
[ZH] 白风对吃的来者不拒：“谢郡主！”
[EN] Baifeng never refused food: "Thank you, Princess!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一早去朱府看过姐姐陈文心后，又在朱府门口挑拨了林觉与朱淑真，内心高兴，找来四五个小弟，吃吃喝喝后，去逛勾栏瓦肆。
[EN] Early that morning, after Chen Haoyu visited his sister Chen Wenxin at the Zhu residence, he stirred up trouble between Lin Jue and Zhu Shuzhen at the gate. Feeling pleased, he gathered four or five lackeys. After eating and drinking, they went to the pleasure quarters.
[ZH] 一路上逛的人见陈浩宇带人前来，都不敢冲撞，躲到一边。陈浩宇专挑有美女的地儿，什么唱曲的、跳舞的，统统要逛个遍。
[EN] People strolling along the way, seeing Chen Haoyu coming with his gang, didn't dare bump into him and stepped aside. Chen Haoyu specifically chose places with beauties—singing girls, dancing girls—he wanted to visit them all.
[ZH] 陈浩宇在跳舞的勾栏内，看到台上的美人儿，倒也呆住。
[EN] Inside the dancing quarter, Chen Haoyu saw the beauties on stage and was actually stunned.
[ZH] 一个小弟说：“陈公子，要不把那姑娘约出来见见？”
[EN] One lackey said: "Young Master Chen, why not invite that girl out for a meeting?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇没有说话，继续沉浸在舞伎扭动的腰肢上。
[EN] Chen Haoyu didn't speak. He continued immersed in the dancing girl's swaying waist.
[ZH] 另一个小弟说：“怎能光见见？怎么着也得陪陈公子一晚。”
[EN] Another lackey said: "Why just meet? She should at least keep Young Master Chen company for a night."
[ZH] 陈浩宇这才说话：“本公子近期有大事要办，为了能把大事办成功，我悄悄起誓，半年内不近女色，看看就好。”
[EN] Only then did Chen Haoyu speak: “I have important business to handle lately. To ensure success, I've secretly sworn an oath—no women for half a year. Just looking is fine.”
[ZH] 小弟问：“陈公子，您要办什么大事？以至于连美女都不放在眼里？”
[EN] The lackey asked: "Young Master Chen, what important business do you have? So important that you don't even care about beauties?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇勾勾手指，待小弟们靠近后，小声说：“我的大事就是对付朱淑真。本公子长这么大，还没吃过谁的亏，不把她扳倒誓不为人！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu beckoned with his finger. When the lackeys drew close, he whispered: "My important business is dealing with Zhu Shuzhen. I've never suffered a loss from anyone in my life. If I don't bring her down, I'm not a man!"
[ZH] 一个小弟轻声问：“扳倒她也不至于不近女色。”
[EN] One lackey asked softly: "Bringing her down doesn't require abstaining from women."
[ZH] “你们不懂，她那么厉害，想要扳倒哪有那么容易？需动点脑子外，还得靠运气。动脑子的事好说，运气就不那么容易。不知你们听说过没？进京赶考的学子为考取功名，赶考前后都不近女色，就是为了把运气搞好，好金榜题名，我也学学他们。”陈浩宇讲得十分郑重。
[EN] "You don't understand. She's so formidable. How can it be easy to bring her down? Besides using your brain, you also need luck. Using your brain is easy to talk about, but luck isn't so simple. Have you heard? Scholars going to the capital for the imperial exams abstain from women before and after the exams, just to build up good luck for their names to appear on the honor roll. I'm learning from them." Chen Haoyu spoke very solemnly.
[ZH] 又一个小弟说：“您又不进京赶考，何必戒色？”
[EN] Another lackey said: "But you're not going to the capital for exams. Why abstain from women?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇指着那小弟：“一听就知道你没文化，我虽不进京赶考，可这道理是一样的，都是想干成功一件事，都需要靠运气，就必须戒色，等我成功后，再开荤！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pointed at that lackey: "I can tell you're uneducated just by listening. Though I'm not going for exams, the principle is the same. When you want to succeed at something, you need luck. To get luck, you must abstain from women. After I succeed, then I'll end my abstinence!"
[ZH] 小弟们乐道：“祝陈公子早日开荤！”
[EN] The lackeys cheered: "We wish Young Master Chen breaks his vow soon!"
[ZH] 他们又进了几个勾栏逛了一圈儿，陈公子戒色，大家都觉得毫无兴致，从一个勾栏出来，准备回去。
[EN] They entered a few more pleasure quarters and looked around. But with Young Master Chen abstaining, everyone lost interest. They came out of one quarter and prepared to go back.
[ZH] 陈浩宇瞧见东方尘泽带着曹班和安明轩从面前经过，上前叫住他们：“东方尘泽，你们三个干什么去？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu saw Dongfang Chenze leading Cao Ban and An Mingxuan passing by. He stepped forward and called out: "Dongfang Chenze, where are you three going?"
[ZH] 三人才瞧到陈浩宇。
[EN] Only then did the three notice Chen Haoyu.
[ZH] 东方尘泽笑道：“原来是陈公子，上次你把醉云楼借我一用，还没机会当面感谢，今日就此谢过。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze smiled: "So it's Young Master Chen. Last time you lent me Zuiyun Tower, I haven't had the chance to thank you in person. Let me thank you now."
[ZH] “不必客气，你也帮了我。”陈浩宇显得很大度，“三位才子出现在这里，难道又要搞诗会？”
[EN] “No need to be polite. You also helped me.” Chen Haoyu appeared magnanimous. “The three talented gentlemen appearing here—are you holding another poetry gathering？”
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“是诗会没错，不过不是我组织的，是词坛前辈发起的，我等过来主要为展示幽栖居士的一首新词。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said: "It is a poetry gathering, but not organized by me. It was initiated by a senior in the ci poetry world. We're mainly here to present a new ci poem by Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 听到是朱淑真写的，陈浩宇兴致更浓，说不定能借此找到继续对付朱淑真的机会：“东方公子可否带我前去？”
[EN] Hearing it was written by Zhu Shuzhen, Chen Haoyu's interest grew even more. Perhaps he could find another opportunity to deal with Zhu Shuzhen: "Could Young Master Dongfang take me along?"
[ZH] 三人用惊讶的目光瞧着陈浩宇，再互相对视，他何时参加过这等活动？真是太阳打西边出来。
[EN] The three stared at Chen Haoyu in surprise, then glanced at each other. When had he ever attended such an event? That was a miracle.
[ZH] 东方尘泽不好拒绝：“带陈公子去没有问题，只是你带的这些人实在与诗会的氛围不搭，要不改日我举行诗会时再邀请你参加？”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze couldn't easily refuse: "Taking Young Master Chen is no problem. But the people you brought really don't fit the poetry gathering's atmosphere. How about another day when I hold a poetry gathering, I'll invite you?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇岂能放过这等机会？对身边的小弟吩咐道：“你们几个立刻从我身边滚开！”
[EN] How could Chen Haoyu let such an opportunity slip? He ordered his lackeys: "All of you, get away from me immediately!"
[ZH] 话音刚落，几个小弟迅速消失不见，速度之快令人惊讶。
[EN] As soon as the words left his mouth, the lackeys quickly vanished. Their speed was astonishing.
[ZH] 陈浩宇笑道：“现在应该可以了吧？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu smiled: "Now it should be fine, right?"
[ZH] 东方尘泽继续说：“可以是可以，不过陈公子去了可不能摆出往日的做派。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze continued: "It's fine now, but Young Master Chen must not show your usual attitude when you go."
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作诚心的样子：“放心，我去了一句话不说，坐在一旁听大家说，保证不给你们丢脸。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pretended to be sincere: "Don't worry. When I get there, I won't say a word. I'll sit to the side and listen to everyone. I guarantee I won't embarrass you."
[ZH] 三人又互相看看，都点点头。
[EN] The three looked at each other again and nodded.
[ZH] 陈浩宇跟着三位公子来到一个品茶的勾栏内，里面坐着的均是些文人，年纪大的约有六十多岁，小的也就十岁左右。
[EN] Chen Haoyu followed the three young masters to a tea-tasting quarter. Inside sat only literati—the oldest around sixty, the youngest about ten.
[ZH] 四人挑张茶桌坐下，伙计将茶壶与茶盏摆上，再上些茶点。
[EN] The four chose a tea table and sat down. The waiter brought the teapot and tea cups, then served some tea snacks.
[ZH] 此处已有人包场，能进来的都是受邀约之人。
[EN] The place had been reserved. Only those invited could enter.
[ZH] 先是旧词品鉴环节，此时大家正在品鉴东坡居士的《水调歌头》。
[EN] First was the classic ci appreciation segment. At the moment, everyone was appreciating Dongpo Jushi's "Water Melody."
[ZH] 三位公子进来得晚，根本插不上话，只能坐着边品茶边认真聆听，只等待举荐新词环节，好把幽栖居士的词拿出来。
[EN] The three young masters came late and couldn't interrupt. They could only sit, sipping tea and listening attentively, waiting for the new ci recommendation segment to present Youqi Jushi's poem.
[ZH] 陈浩宇一进这种环境，就觉得头皮发麻，全身上下起鸡皮疙瘩，实在不舒服。可他又不愿离去，干脆趴在茶桌上睡会儿。文人们品鉴诗词的声音如同催眠的香料，他不知不觉进入梦乡……
[EN] As soon as Chen Haoyu entered this environment, his scalp tingled. Goosebumps covered his whole body. He felt truly uncomfortable. But he didn't want to leave, so he simply lay on the tea table to nap. The literati's voices appreciating poetry were like hypnotic incense. Unaware, he drifted into dreams...

#CH 80
[ZH] 陈浩宇睡得很熟，还做了一个美梦。
[EN] Chen Haoyu slept soundly and had a wonderful dream.
[ZH] 梦中他变成武功高强之人，跟朱淑真打起来，几拳把她打倒在地，一只手抓住她胸前的衣裳，另一只手举起拳头：“朱淑真，你服不服？”
[EN] In the dream, he became a martial arts master. He fought Zhu Shuzhen, punched her to the ground, grabbed her clothes at the chest with one hand, and raised his fist with the other: "Zhu Shuzhen, do you admit defeat?"
[ZH] 朱淑真哭着祈求：“陈大爷！陈爷爷！你就饶了我吧！我以后再也不敢了！”……
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen cried and begged: "Master Chen! Grandfather Chen! Please spare me! I'll never dare again!"...
[ZH] 实在解气！陈浩宇在梦中不由大笑起来。
[EN] How satisfying! Chen Haoyu couldn't help but laugh out loud in his dream.
[ZH] 正当他笑得开心时，听到有声音唤他：“陈公子！陈公子！快醒醒！”
[EN] Just as he was laughing happily, he heard someone calling him: "Young Master Chen! Young Master Chen! Wake up quickly!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇睁开朦胧的双眼，发现是曹班唤他，原来刚刚经历的只是一场梦而已。
[EN] Chen Haoyu opened his blurry eyes and found it was Cao Ban calling him. It turned out what he just experienced was only a dream.
[ZH] 曹班说：“你刚才睡着，又是打呼噜，又是大笑，吵到大家，如果陈公子困倦，不如回家睡觉。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "While you were sleeping just now, you were snoring and laughing loudly, disturbing everyone. If Young Master Chen is sleepy, you might as well go home and sleep."
[ZH] 陈浩宇揉揉眼睛，四下瞧瞧，发现所有人盯着他看，不好意思道：“诸位别介意，只因大家讨论诗词太过吸引人，以至于我沉醉其中，不能自拔，不小心睡着。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu rubbed his eyes and looked around, finding everyone staring at him. He said embarrassedly: "Please don't mind, everyone. It's just that your discussion of poetry was so fascinating that I was immersed in it and couldn't extricate myself. I accidentally fell asleep."
[ZH] 安明轩凑到陈浩宇耳边：“陈公子没必要解释，大家都清楚怎么回事，还是回去吧，此处不适合你。”
[EN] An Mingxuan leaned close to Chen Haoyu's ear: "There's no need for Young Master Chen to explain. Everyone knows what happened. You might as well go back. This place isn't suitable for you."
[ZH] 还没听到朱淑真的新词，陈浩宇怎能离去？
[EN] Having not yet heard Zhu Shuzhen's new ci poem, how could Chen Haoyu leave?
[ZH] “放心，我不会再睡着。”
[EN] "Don't worry. I won't fall asleep again."
[ZH] 东方尘泽出来化解尴尬：“诸位！继续！继续！连陈公子都能来诗会，证明诗会影响甚广。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze stepped forward to ease the awkwardness: "Everyone! Continue! Continue! The fact that even Young Master Chen could attend the poetry gathering proves its wide influence."
[ZH] 林子孝也说：“盛公，东方公子说得对，我们还是继续吧，不用理会陈公子。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao also said: "Lord Sheng, Young Master Dongfang is right. Let's continue. No need to pay attention to Young Master Chen."
[ZH] 盛公是本次诗会的组织者，已近古稀之年，须发已白，人看着倒精神。
[EN] Lord Sheng was the organizer of this poetry gathering. He was nearly seventy years old, with white hair and beard, but he looked spirited.
[ZH] “刚才大家品鉴东坡居士的词太过投入，竟没注意到陈公子在此，要不是那呼噜和笑声盖过品鉴诗词的声音，我都不知道陈公子也会来参加诗会，呵呵……！正如东方公子所言，小老儿组织的诗会倒是蛮有吸引力，呵呵……！”
[EN] "Just now, everyone was so absorbed in appreciating Dongpo Jushi's ci that they didn't notice Young Master Chen was here. If not for the snoring and laughter drowning out the voices of poetry appreciation, I wouldn't have known Young Master Chen would attend the poetry gathering. Hehe...! As Young Master Dongfang said, this old man's poetry gathering is quite attractive. Hehe...!"
[ZH] 半夸赞半戏谑的话语，令陈浩宇很不爽，拿起一杯茶装作饮茶样子，想道：“有屁吸引力！要不是本公子需对付朱淑真，才懒得来这么无趣的地方！”
[EN] The half-praising, half-mocking words made Chen Haoyu very unhappy. He picked up a cup of tea and pretended to drink, thinking: "What attraction! If I didn't need to deal with Zhu Shuzhen, I wouldn't bother coming to such a boring place!"
[ZH] 盛公又说：“诸位，品鉴旧词的环节已结束，接下来进入新词举荐环节，若有人近期得到新词，请举荐上来，供大家品评鉴赏。”
[EN] Lord Sheng continued: "Everyone, the segment of appreciating classic ci has ended. Now we enter the new ci recommendation segment. If anyone has obtained new ci poems recently, please recommend them for everyone to appreciate."
[ZH] 陈浩宇精神倍增，对东方尘泽说：“你不是有朱淑真新写的词吗？赶紧拿出来！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's spirits soared. He said to Dongfang Chenze: "Don't you have Zhu Shuzhen's newly written ci? Bring it out quickly!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“前辈们在此，还是等会儿再说。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said: "The seniors are here. Let's wait a moment."
[ZH] 陈浩宇才不愿等下去，还没等其他人说话，直接喊道：“我要举荐幽栖居士的新词！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu didn't want to wait. Before anyone else could speak, he directly shouted: "I want to recommend Youqi Jushi's new ci!"
[ZH] 众人都朝他们看去。
[EN] Everyone looked toward them.
[ZH] 林子孝说：“难怪陈公子前来，原来是得了幽栖居士的新词，快拿出来供大家鉴赏一番。”
[EN] Lin Zixiao said: "No wonder Young Master Chen came. It turns out he obtained Youqi Jushi's new ci. Please bring it out for everyone to appreciate."
[ZH] 陈浩宇指向东方尘泽：“新词在他那里。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pointed at Dongfang Chenze: "The new ci is with him."
[ZH] 有人已将东坡居士的《水调歌头》取下。
[EN] Someone had already taken down Dongpo Jushi's "Water Melody."
[ZH] 既然陈浩宇已喊出，东方尘泽当仁不让，吩咐化雨把手中的卷轴挂在展示诗词的屏风之上。
[EN] Since Chen Haoyu had already shouted it out, Dongfang Chenze didn't decline. He ordered Huayu to hang the scroll in his hand on the screen for displaying ci poems.
[ZH] 东方尘泽起身走到众人中间：“词还新鲜着，是幽栖居士昨日游西湖所作，依'清平乐'词调。她只吟出词句，并未命名。我与曹班、安明轩两位公子悄悄记下，回去后，写在纸上，可苦于没有题目。经过仔细思索，现在还未立秋，依然是夏季，我等斗胆给幽栖居士的新词加上题目'夏日游湖'。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze stood up and walked to the middle of the crowd: "The ci is still fresh. It was written by Youqi Jushi yesterday while touring West Lake, following the 'Qingpingle' tune. She only recited the lines and didn't name it. The two of us, Cao Ban and An Mingxuan, quietly memorized it. After returning, we wrote it on paper, but we struggled with having no title. After careful thought, since the Beginning of Autumn hasn't arrived yet and it's still summer, we boldly added the title 'Summer Tour of the Lake' to Youqi Jushi's new ci."
[ZH] 所有人向幽栖居士的词看去，年纪最小的孩童先说话：“东方公子确定是幽栖居士昨日所作？”
[EN] Everyone looked at Youqi Jushi's ci. The youngest child spoke first: "Is Young Master Dongfang certain it was written by Youqi Jushi yesterday?"
[ZH] 没等东方尘泽说话，安明轩回道：“这还能有假？我们三人可是亲耳听到，亲眼见到。”
[EN] Before Dongfang Chenze could speak, An Mingxuan replied: "How could this be fake? The three of us heard it with our own ears and saw it with our own eyes."
[ZH] 孩童感到疑惑：“可昨日天气晴朗，为何词中却写'恼烟撩雾''一霎黄梅细雨'？难道昨日只有西湖周围天气不好？再说都快立秋，早已过黄梅时节，'一霎黄梅细雨'实在令人费解。”
[EN] The child felt puzzled: "But yesterday the weather was clear. Why does the ci write 'annoyed by mist, teasing fog' and 'a moment of plum rain drizzle'? Could it be that only the weather around West Lake was bad yesterday? Besides, the Beginning of Autumn is approaching, and the plum rain season has long passed. 'A moment of plum rain drizzle' is truly puzzling."
[ZH] 有人说：“我昨日游西湖，天气晴朗，并未落雨。”
[EN] Someone said: "I toured West Lake yesterday. The weather was clear, and it didn't rain."
[ZH] 陈浩宇听了，心里喜道：“昨日她落到水中，跟个落汤鸡似的，嘿嘿，心情如何能好？再晴朗的天气，在她眼中也是阴雨连绵，呵呵，我这仇报得实在好，可还不够！”
[EN] Hearing this, Chen Haoyu rejoiced inwardly: "Yesterday she fell into the water, looking like a drowned rat. Hehe, how could her mood be good? No matter how clear the weather, in her eyes it would be continuous rain. Hehe, my revenge was really well done, but it's not enough!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇一高兴，主动说：“你这小孩子一点儿都不懂写诗作词，这诗词不一定要按照实际情况来写，有一个词语叫什么来着？对！'虚写'，只要能把人内心的情感表达出来即可。”
[EN] When Chen Haoyu was happy, he voluntarily said: "You child don't understand writing poetry and ci at all. Poetry and ci don't necessarily have to be written according to actual circumstances. There's a term for it... what was it? Right! 'Imaginary description.' As long as it can express a person's inner emotions, that's enough."
[ZH] 陈浩宇竟然主动参与品鉴，令人感到新奇，他所说又不无道理，大家都点头认可。
[EN] Chen Haoyu actually voluntarily participated in appreciation, which everyone found novel. What he said wasn't without reason, so everyone nodded in agreement.
[ZH] 得到众人认可，陈浩宇更是兴致勃勃：“依我看，朱淑真……不，幽栖居士，之所以把晴朗的天气写成阴雨天，是因为她昨日不慎落水，心情不佳所致。”
[EN] Having gained everyone's approval, Chen Haoyu was even more enthusiastic: "In my opinion, Zhu Shuzhen... no, Youqi Jushi, wrote about clear weather as rainy days because she accidentally fell into the water yesterday and was in a bad mood."
[ZH] 三位公子顿觉十分丢人，其他人则露出嘲笑的表情，没有人愿接陈浩宇的话。
[EN] The three young masters immediately felt very embarrassed. Others showed mocking expressions. No one wanted to respond to Chen Haoyu's words.
[ZH] 只有孩童问：“陈公子确定昨日幽栖居士心情不好？”
[EN] Only the child asked: "Is Young Master Chen certain Youqi Jushi was in a bad mood yesterday?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇十分肯定：“那是当然，她落水，我亲眼所见，对！还有乔文仙，跟她一起落水，不相信，你们见到乔文仙，问他便知。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu was very certain: "Of course. I saw her fall into the water with my own eyes. Right! And Qiao Wenxian fell into the water with her. If you don't believe me, when you see Qiao Wenxian, ask him and you'll know."
[ZH] 曹班小声提醒道：“陈公子，别再说了，再说会更丢人。”
[EN] Cao Ban whispered a reminder: "Young Master Chen, don't say any more. The more you say, the more embarrassing it will be."
[ZH] 陈浩宇纳闷，他自觉没有任何问题，为何曹班会如此说？反正他也没其他可说的，不如老实坐下，听别人讲，再耐心等待对付朱淑真的机会。
[EN] Chen Haoyu was puzzled. He felt there was nothing wrong with what he said. Why would Cao Ban say that? Anyway, he had nothing else to say. He might as well sit down honestly, listen to others, and patiently wait for the opportunity to deal with Zhu Shuzhen.
[ZH] “诸位，我讲完了，大家继续。”
[EN] "Everyone, I've finished speaking. Please continue."
[ZH] 有人站起来：“我来说两句。陈公子讲虚写，自然有道理，可要说幽栖居士写这首词时心情不佳，则毫无道理可言。首句用一个'恼'字，似乎心情不佳，可依我看，她心情好得很，否则怎会有'携手藕花湖上路'，甚至有'和衣睡倒人怀'？”那人说完，露出戏谑的表情。
[EN] Someone stood up: "Let me say a few words. What Young Master Chen said about imaginary description naturally has reason, but to say Youqi Jushi was in a bad mood when writing this ci is completely unreasonable. The first line uses the character 'annoyed,' which seems to indicate a bad mood, but in my opinion, she was in a very good mood. Otherwise, how could there be 'hand in hand on the lake road among lotus flowers' and even 'sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed'?" After speaking, that person showed a teasing expression.
[ZH] 陈浩宇虽品错，并不脸红，心里骂道：“一群酸臭文人，会品几句诗词又能如何？本公子根本不稀罕！”
[EN] Though Chen Haoyu had appreciated incorrectly, he didn't blush. He cursed inwardly: "A group of pedantic literati. So what if you can appreciate a few lines of poetry? I don't care at all!"
[ZH] 听到“和衣睡倒人怀”，向卷轴瞧去，果然有，又在心里乐道：“朱淑真啊朱淑真，你虽有过婚姻，可也不必如此奔放，竟然不知羞耻，躺到乔文仙怀里，这么有趣的句子，我可得好好利用利用，不相信扳不倒你。”
[EN] Hearing "sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed," he looked at the scroll and indeed saw it. He rejoiced inwardly: "Zhu Shuzhen, oh Zhu Shuzhen, though you've been married, you don't need to be so unrestrained. You actually have no shame and lay in Qiao Wenxian's arms. Such an interesting line. I must make good use of it. I don't believe I can't bring you down."
[ZH] 有人说：“没想到幽栖居士能写出如此大胆的句子，实在令人汗颜，我看后都觉脸红，不知诸位如何看待？”
[EN] Someone said: "I didn't expect Youqi Jushi could write such bold lines. It's truly embarrassing. Even I feel blushing after reading it. I wonder what everyone thinks?"
[ZH] 又有人说：“实在有失女德！这种词，还是不要拿出来得好！”
[EN] Another person said: "This truly lacks feminine virtue! Such ci poems shouldn't be brought out!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇在一旁偷乐：“朱淑真啊朱淑真，今日我要亲眼见证大家怎么骂你！嘿嘿……！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu secretly rejoiced on the side: "Zhu Shuzhen, oh Zhu Shuzhen, today I will witness with my own eyes how everyone scolds you! Hehe...!"
[ZH] 东方尘泽见品鉴之人不怀好意，后悔将新词拿出。既已拿出绝不能让大家亵渎幽栖居士的新作。
[EN] Seeing the ill intentions of those appreciating, Dongfang Chenze regretted bringing out the new ci. Since it was already brought out, he absolutely couldn't let everyone profane Youqi Jushi's new work.
[ZH] “幽栖居士写这首词时，心情的确不错，我们三人可以作证，她因心情不错，还多饮了几杯酒，在醉酒的状态下吟出词句。词中塑造出一对青年男女，携手行于西湖，景美，人物的心情亦美。词句抒写的确大胆些，也只有如此，才能将闺阁女子对情感自由的渴望表达出来。诸位都是懂诗词之人，千万不要对诗词进行道德绑架。”
[EN] "When Youqi Jushi wrote this ci, her mood was indeed quite good. The three of us can testify. Because her mood was good, she even drank a few more cups of wine and recited the lines while in a drunken state. The ci portrays a young man and woman, hand in hand walking by West Lake. The scenery is beautiful, and the characters' moods are also beautiful. The expression of the lines is indeed somewhat bold, but only in this way can the longing of boudoir women for emotional freedom be expressed. Everyone here understands poetry and ci. Please don't impose moral judgments on the poetry and ci."
[ZH] 曹班希望将大家注意的点引到别处，他站起来：“六一居士《少年游》中有'恼烟撩雾，拚醉倚西风。'写的是与伴侣第一次游园时美好之情。幽栖居士活借活用，才有了'恼烟撩露'。六一居士是男子，又在朝中任职，忙的是天下大事，词句自然具有男儿情怀。幽栖居士是闺阁女子，眼界仅限于闺阁之情，将男女之情写于词中再正常不过。再说幽栖居士不是第一个将男女之情写在诗词中，《诗经》首篇'关雎'便是写男女之情，'窈窕淑女，君子好逑'成为千古名句；要说幽栖居士的词句大胆，依我看比不上《诗经•野有死麕》中的诗句：'有女怀春，吉士诱之。''和衣睡倒人怀'与此相比，根本不值一提。诸位，孔圣人可是常常与弟弟们讨论《诗经》，也没见他看到这样的句子有批判嫌弃之意，大家又何必心胸狭隘？还是以词论词为好。”
[EN] Cao Ban hoped to redirect everyone's attention elsewhere. He stood up: "In Liu Yi Jushi's 'Youthful Roaming,' there is 'annoyed by mist, teasing fog, drinking drunk leaning on the west wind.' It writes about the beautiful feelings of the first garden tour with a partner. Youqi Jushi borrowed and used it creatively, resulting in 'annoyed by mist, teasing dew.' Liu Yi Jushi was a man, serving in the court, busy with matters of the world. His lines naturally have a man's sentiments. Youqi Jushi is a boudoir woman, whose vision is limited to boudoir feelings. Writing about the feelings between men and women in ci is perfectly normal. Moreover, Youqi Jushi is not the first to write about feelings between men and women in poetry and ci. The first piece of the Classic of Poetry, 'Ospreys,' writes about feelings between men and women. 'A modest maiden is a fit wife for a gentleman' has become a famous line through the ages. If we say Youqi Jushi's lines are bold, in my opinion, they don't compare to the lines in the Classic of Poetry's 'A Dead Deer in the Wild': 'A maiden in spring, a gentleman courts her.' 'Sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed' compared to this is not worth mentioning at all. Everyone, Confucius often discussed the Classic of Poetry with his younger brothers, and he didn't show criticism or disgust when seeing such lines. Why should everyone be narrow-minded? It's better to discuss ci based on the ci itself."

#CH 81
[ZH] 安明轩岂能落后？幽栖居士的词是他们拿来，又怎能让大家诋毁？
[EN] How could An Mingxuan fall behind? Youqi Jushi's ci was brought by them—how could he let everyone slander it?
[ZH] 他站起来：“既然曹公子提到'以词论词'，我给大家带个头。东坡居士评价王右丞的山水诗'诗中有画'，我想借用来评价幽栖居士的词，真是'词中有画'，不过此画非彼画，王右丞诗中之画往往是一幅完整的山水画，而幽栖居士的画却是好几幅画面。第一幅是相爱之人驻足不前，一起欣赏着恼烟撩露之美景；第二幅是情人携手，却遇黄梅细雨；第三幅是躲雨时，四周无人，情人亲密无间，既纯真又美好……”
[EN] He stood up: “Since Young Master Cao mentioned 'discussing ci based on ci itself,' let me set an example for everyone. Dongpo Jushi evaluated Wang Youcheng's landscape poetry as 'having painting within the poetry.' I'd like to borrow this to evaluate Youqi Jushi's ci—it truly is 'having painting within the ci.' However, this painting is not that painting. The painting in Wang Youcheng's poetry is often a complete landscape painting, while Youqi Jushi's painting consists of several scenes. The first scene shows lovers pausing, admiring together the beautiful sight of mist teasing dew; the second scene shows lovers hand in hand, yet encountering plum rain drizzle; the third scene shows them sheltering from rain, with no one around, the lovers intimate and close, both innocent and beautiful...”
[ZH] 听到“既纯真又美好”，陈浩宇心里很不屑：“还纯真又美好，纯真个屁！美好你个头！我看朱淑真就是个行为不检点的女子！不过，嘿嘿，她越不检点，越有错处可让我抓，真是天助我也！”
[EN] Hearing "both innocent and beautiful," Chen Haoyu felt disdainful inwardly: "Innocent and beautiful, my ass! Beautiful your head! I think Zhu Shuzhen is just a woman with improper behavior! But, hehe, the more improper she is, the more mistakes I can catch. Heaven is truly helping me!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇想完，继续听安明轩吹嘘。
[EN] After Chen Haoyu finished thinking, he continued listening to An Mingxuan's praise.
[ZH] “第四幅是恋恋不舍分别后，女子回到闺房，回忆相约之情。如此充满意境的画面，令人赏心悦目！”
[EN] "The fourth scene shows the reluctant parting, the woman returning to her boudoir, recalling the feelings of their meeting. Such scenes full of artistic conception are pleasing to the eye and mind!"
[ZH] 有人被感染：“安公子一番解析，妙不可言，等我回去，定画出四幅画来。”
[EN] Someone was moved: "Young Master An's analysis is wonderfully indescribable. When I return home, I'll definitely paint four pictures."
[ZH] 安明轩拱手道：“有劳！”
[EN] An Mingxuan cupped his hands: "I leave it to you!"
[ZH] 安明轩讲完后，与东方尘泽、曹班回到桌前坐下。
[EN] After An Mingxuan finished speaking, he returned to the table with Dongfang Chenze and Cao Ban and sat down.
[ZH] 陈浩宇小声说：“你们几个又何必替朱淑真说话，我看她就是行为不检点！竟然还敢公开把不检点的行为写在词中，供大家鉴赏，肯定是脑子有问题！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu whispered: “Why do you guys speak up for Zhu Shuzhen? I think she's just improper! She actually dares to publicly write about improper behavior in her ci for everyone to appreciate—she must have something wrong with her brain！”
[ZH] 东方尘泽小声提醒：“你可是跟着我们三人进来，要站在我们这边。再说，陈公子还好意思说幽栖居士？你在茶楼调戏女茶师的事……”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze whispered a reminder: "You came in with the three of us, so you should be on our side. Besides, does Young Master Chen have the nerve to talk about Youqi Jushi? What about you harassing the female tea master at the tea house..."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“打住！打住！好汉不提当年勇！我是男子，朱淑真岂能跟我比？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: “Stop! Stop! A true hero doesn't brag about past exploits! I'm a man—how can Zhu Shuzhen compare to me？”
[ZH] 曹班强调道：“当年勇？就发生在这个夏季好不好！”
[EN] Cao Ban emphasized: "Past exploits? That happened this very summer!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇不再说话，也不敢拿出往日嚣张的气焰，毕竟诗会中没有自己的人，还是低调些，再听听，看还有没有人批判朱淑真，真希望多来几个。
[EN] Chen Haoyu stopped talking. He didn't dare show his usual arrogance either. After all, there was no one on his side at the poetry gathering. Better to keep a low profile and listen more, seeing if anyone else would criticize Zhu Shuzhen. He really hoped a few more would come forward.
[ZH] 盛公问道：“不知子孝有何见解？”
[EN] Lord Sheng asked: "I wonder what Zixiao's insights are?"
[ZH] 当幽栖居士的词被挂出时，林子孝已看出幽栖居士有了新恋情。她刚刚与林觉侄儿和离，就与别的男子一起游湖，说明婚姻存续期间心中已有他人。
[EN] When Youqi Jushi's ci was hung up, Lin Zixiao had already seen that Youqi Jushi had a new romance. She had just divorced his nephew Lin Jue, and was already touring the lake with another man—showing that during her marriage, her heart already belonged to someone else.
[ZH] 林子孝越想越不满，又不能发表不满的言论，一方面他确实喜欢幽栖居士的词；另一方面他得给知府大人面子。
[EN] The more Lin Zixiao thought about it, the more dissatisfied he became. But he couldn't voice his dissatisfaction. On one hand, he truly liked Youqi Jushi's ci; on the other hand, he had to give face to the prefect.
[ZH] 盛公主动提到他，林子孝不能不开口，他站起来：“既以词论词，我谈谈粗浅之见。幽栖居士的词往往胜在'细腻'二字，与她身为女子密不可分。'恼烟撩露'写出怀春少女复杂的心情，埋怨天公不作美，又沉浸在相约的美好情怀中。'一霎黄梅细雨'，就词本身来讲，交代时间，亦写明天气状况，更让词充满意境之美。能想象到这对青年男女遇到雨时焦急、愉快的复杂心境，还能想象到他们找地方避雨的场景，充满年少时活泼的气息。我读来也不由想起曾经的时光。”
[EN] Since Lord Sheng mentioned him, Lin Zixiao had to speak. He stood up: “Since we're discussing ci based on ci itself, let me share my humble views. Youqi Jushi's ci often excels in the word 'delicacy,' which is inseparable from her being a woman. 'Annoyed by mist, teased by dew' expresses the complex feelings of a young girl in spring, complaining that heaven doesn't provide fair weather, yet immersed in the beautiful sentiments of a rendezvous. 'A moment of plum rain drizzle'—in terms of the ci itself—establishes the time and also describes the weather condition, further filling the ci with artistic beauty. One can imagine the complex feelings of anxiety and joy when this young couple encounters rain, and also imagine the scene of them finding shelter, full of youthful vitality. Reading it, I can't help but recall my own past times.”
[ZH] 林子孝不愿再说下去，只因不想触及那句“和衣睡倒人怀”。幽栖居士嫁到林家，不愿与侄儿圆房，一和离就倒在别的男子怀里，他怎能有心情碰触下面的句子？还是坐下继续品茶。
[EN] Lin Zixiao didn't want to continue speaking, only because he didn't want to touch upon the line “sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed.” Youqi Jushi married into the Lin family, unwilling to consummate the marriage with his nephew, and upon divorce, immediately fell into another man's arms—how could he have the mood to touch upon the following lines? He sat back down and continued drinking tea.
[ZH] 陈浩宇见如此光景，担心大家不愿批判朱淑真，总得有人带头。诗词他是不懂，可批判人，谁不会！于是站起来，走到中间。
[EN] Seeing this situation, Chen Haoyu worried that no one wanted to criticize Zhu Shuzhen. Someone had to take the lead. He didn't understand poetry and ci, but criticizing people—who doesn't know how to do that! So he stood up and walked to the middle.
[ZH] “诸位，我也说两句。我爹常常教导我姐姐，'夫云妇德，不必才明绝异也'意思是说女子不必具有高超的才华……”
[EN] “Everyone, let me say a few words too. My father often taught my sister, 'A woman's virtue need not be exceptional in talent'—meaning a woman doesn't need to have extraordinary talent...”
[ZH] 曹班叹道：“陈浩宇竟然懂《女诫》中的句子，不容易！”
[EN] Cao Ban sighed: “Chen Haoyu actually understands a line from Admonitions for Women—that's not easy！”
[ZH] 安明轩说：“他都说了，是听他爹讲的，他爹整日教育他姐，听多了，不记住才怪。”
[EN] An Mingxuan said: "He already said it was from listening to his father. His father educates his sister all day long. Hearing it so much, it'd be strange if he didn't remember."
[ZH] 曹班说：“也对。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "That's true too."
[ZH] 东方尘泽提醒道：“你们两个别说了，他看样子是要批判幽栖居士。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze reminded: "You two stop talking. It looks like he's going to criticize Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] 两人也觉不对，都仔细听着。
[EN] The two also felt something was off and listened carefully.
[ZH] “幽栖居士有才华已经违背《女戒》所言，她还把才华表现在诗词中，就更不对！甚至比在座的才子都写得好，那就更更不对！写就写了吧，还写违背女德之事，那就更更更不对！'和衣睡倒人怀'，啧啧啧，这场景，得多不堪入目！”陈浩宇走到孩童面前，“你几岁了？”
[EN] “Youqi Jushi having talent already violates what Admonitions for Women says. She even displays her talent in poetry and ci—that's even more wrong! She even writes better than the talented gentlemen here—that's even more wrong! If she writes, then let her write, but she writes about matters that violate feminine virtue—that's even more wrong! 'Sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed'—tsk tsk tsk, this scene, how indecent it must be！” Chen Haoyu walked in front of the child. “How old are you？”
[ZH] 孩童回道：“九岁。”
[EN] The child replied: "Nine years old."
[ZH] 陈浩宇继续说道：“乖，回去吧，这里不适合你。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu continued: "Good boy, go back. This place isn't suitable for you."
[ZH] 孩童坚持说：“怎么不适合？我极会品鉴诗词，太爷爷才请我来。”
[EN] The child insisted: "Why isn't it suitable? I'm very good at appreciating poetry and ci. Great-grandfather invited me."
[ZH] 陈浩宇问：“你太爷爷是谁？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu asked: "Who is your great-grandfather?"
[ZH] 孩童指指盛公。
[EN] The child pointed at Lord Sheng.
[ZH] 盛公开口道：“陈公子若是讲完，请到一边就座。”
[EN] Lord Sheng spoke: "If Young Master Chen has finished speaking, please sit to the side."
[ZH] “我还没说完！”陈浩宇哪能放过批判朱淑真的好机会，担心他人打断，急忙说，“这么小的孩子，本抱着对诗词一腔热情前来参加诗会，却被朱淑真的'和衣睡倒人怀'毒害，幼小而又纯真的心灵受到伤害，实在可怜。”
[EN] “I haven't finished yet！” How could Chen Haoyu let go of such a good opportunity to criticize Zhu Shuzhen? Worried others would interrupt, he quickly said: “Such a young child, coming to the poetry gathering full of passion for poetry and ci, yet being poisoned by Zhu Shuzhen's 'sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed'—the young and innocent heart being hurt is truly pitiful.”
[ZH] 陈浩宇装作可怜孩童的样子，举起拳头：“我提议，我们一起，打倒朱淑真！打倒朱淑真！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pretended to be the pitiful child, raising his fist: “I propose we all together—down with Zhu Shuzhen! Down with Zhu Shuzhen！”
[ZH] 在场所有人都瞪大眼睛看着陈浩宇，觉得他十分奇怪。
[EN] Everyone present stared wide-eyed at Chen Haoyu, finding him very strange.
[ZH] 传来孩童稍显稚嫩的声音：“哥哥，我没有被毒害，幽栖居士说'和衣睡倒人怀'这很正常，我也经常穿衣裳睡倒在太爷爷怀里。”
[EN] The child's somewhat young voice came: "Brother, I haven't been poisoned. Youqi Jushi said 'sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed' is very normal. I also often sleep in my clothes in Great-grandfather's arms."
[ZH] 陈浩宇解释道：“孩子，你睡在你太爷爷怀里没有任何问题，朱淑真是女子，睡到一个男子怀里就是不道德。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu explained: "Child, you sleeping in your great-grandfather's arms has no problem at all. Zhu Shuzhen is a woman. Sleeping in a man's arms is immoral."
[ZH] 孩童又说：“哥哥，我是男孩子，青清是女孩子，昨日我与他一起玩儿，她还趴在我腿上睡着了，难道她也不道德吗？可我一点儿都不觉得。”
[EN] The child said again: "Brother, I'm a boy, and Qingqing is a girl. Yesterday I played with her, and she even fell asleep on my lap. Is she immoral too? But I don't feel that way at all."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“你们是孩子不必介意。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: "You're children, so it doesn't matter."
[ZH] “为何幽栖居士是大人就必须介意呢？还要用道德和不道德衡量？”孩童的问题，陈浩宇答不出来，又无话可说，只能回到座位。
[EN] "Why must it matter when Youqi Jushi is an adult? And why must it be measured by morality and immorality?" Faced with the child's question, Chen Haoyu couldn't answer. With nothing more to say, he could only return to his seat.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“陈公子，我们这一桌不欢迎你。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said: "Young Master Chen, you're not welcome at our table."
[ZH] 陈浩宇当众诋毁了朱淑真，又想到报复朱淑真的方法，心情大好，不愿跟东方尘泽他们计较：“本公子也逛够，打道回府，你们继续。”说完离开诗会。
[EN] Chen Haoyu had publicly slandered Zhu Shuzhen and thought of a way to get revenge on her. In a great mood, he didn't want to argue with Dongfang Chenze and the others: "I've had enough fun too. I'm heading home. You all continue." With that, he left the poetry gathering.
[ZH] 三人又听其他人品鉴完幽栖居士的新词后，跟着品鉴了一番别人举荐之作，才满意离开。
[EN] The three listened to others appreciate Youqi Jushi's new ci, then followed along appreciating works recommended by others, before leaving satisfied.
[ZH] 在勾栏瓦肆外分别时，东方尘泽担忧道：“今日大家品鉴幽栖居士的词，都有意避开'和衣睡倒人怀'，真担心我们三个办了件坏事。这首词一旦传开，怕是会坏了幽栖居士的名声。”
[EN] When parting outside the pleasure quarters, Dongfang Chenze worriedly said: "Today everyone appreciating Youqi Jushi's ci deliberately avoided 'sleeping in someone's arms fully clothed.' I'm really worried the three of us did something bad. Once this ci spreads, I'm afraid it will ruin Youqi Jushi's reputation."
[ZH] 曹班说：“我也担心。”
[EN] Cao Ban said: "I'm worried too."
[ZH] 安明轩提议：“不如改日我们找幽栖居士，把今日诗会之事与她说明，让她有个思想准备，顺便向她致歉。”
[EN] An Mingxuan proposed: "Why don't we find Youqi Jushi another day, explain today's poetry gathering to her, let her be mentally prepared, and apologize to her while we're at it."
[ZH] 东方尘泽点头认可：“就这么定了，明日给乔兄说一声，把幽栖居士约到乔家的幽林别居，他们二人再次相见，心情一好，幽栖居士也就不会太过怪罪我们。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze nodded in agreement: "It's settled then. Tomorrow we'll tell Brother Qiao and invite Youqi Jushi to the Qiao family's Youlin Villa. When the two of them meet again and are in a good mood, Youqi Jushi won't blame us too much."
[ZH] 三人商量完，乘坐马车各自归家。
[EN] After the three finished discussing, they took their carriages and returned home separately.

#CH 82
[ZH] 乔文仙离开乔府，并未直接回幽林别居，而是去街上闲逛。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian left the Qiao residence but didn't return directly to Youlin Villa. Instead, he went strolling on the streets.
[ZH] 乔守仁虽未明确拒绝提亲之事，可乔文仙能听得出，父亲对淑真不满，恐怕提亲之事会遇到阻碍。他边逛边回想父亲说过的话，以及说话时的表情，越想越担忧。
[EN] Though Qiao Shouren hadn't explicitly refused the marriage proposal, Qiao Wenxian could hear in his father's voice that he was dissatisfied with Shuzhen. He feared the proposal would meet obstacles. As he strolled, he recalled his father's words and the expressions on his face while speaking. The more he thought about it, the more worried he became.
[ZH] 好不容易等到淑真和离，又爱上他，本想着回家告知父母，他们会爽快答应，立刻到朱家提亲，谁承想事与愿违。
[EN] He had finally waited for Shuzhen to divorce and fall in love with him. He had thought that when he returned home to tell his parents, they would readily agree and immediately go to the Zhu family to propose. Who would have expected things to go against his wishes?
[ZH] 如若过几日他再回乔府，提起求亲之事，再被父亲明确拒绝，那该如何是好？乔文仙想到仪王，不如请他帮忙。他是王爷，由他出面，父亲多少会给些面子。
[EN] If in a few days he returned to the Qiao residence again and brought up the marriage proposal, only to be explicitly refused by his father once more, what should he do? Qiao Wenxian thought of the Prince of Yi. Why not ask him for help? He was a prince. If he stepped forward, his father would give some face.
[ZH] 乔文仙不再漫无目的逛下去，登上跟在身后的马车，返回幽林别居。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stopped wandering aimlessly. He boarded the carriage following behind him and returned to Youlin Villa.
[ZH] 一进别居大门，南双迎面走来：“公子出去也不带我，害我在别居一直担心您。”
[EN] As soon as he entered the villa's gate, Nanshuang came to meet him: "Young Master went out without taking me along, making me worry about you the whole time at the villa."
[ZH] “有什么好担心的，我是回乔府，自己的家。对了，仪王可在？”
[EN] "What's there to worry about? I was returning to the Qiao residence, my own home. By the way, is the Prince of Yi here?"
[ZH] “在是在，不过喝多了，正在屋里睡觉。”
[EN] "He is, but he drank too much and is sleeping in his room."
[ZH] “喝多了？为何？”
[EN] "Drank too much? Why?"
[ZH] “白风说他与仪王去聚香楼吃饭，那里的酒太好喝，王爷多贪了几杯。”
[EN] "Baifeng said he and the Prince of Yi went to Juxiang Restaurant for a meal. The wine there was so good that the prince had a few extra cups."
[ZH] “好吧，既如此，我先不打搅他。”
[EN] "Alright, in that case, I won't disturb him for now."
[ZH] 乔文仙向他居住的院子而去，到达院门口，瞧到木质高台，不如上去坐坐，极目远眺也许能够疏散胸怀：“南双，给我拿瓶桂花酿，我要坐在上面边赏景边品酒。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian headed toward the courtyard where he stayed. Reaching the courtyard gate, he saw the wooden platform. Why not go up and sit for a while? Gazing into the distance might help ease his mind: "Nanshuang, bring me a bottle of osmanthus wine. I want to sit up there, enjoy the view, and drink."
[ZH] 南双回屋取酒，乔文仙则登上木质高台，坐在桌前，眺望远方。视野变得开阔，可内心担忧的情绪并未得到疏散。
[EN] Nanshuang went back to get the wine. Qiao Wenxian climbed onto the wooden platform, sat at the table, and gazed into the distance. His field of vision broadened, but the worry in his heart didn't ease.
[ZH] 南双取了酒，也爬上高台，替公子满上，叮嘱道：“公子可别跟仪王一样喝得酩酊大醉。”
[EN] Nanshuang got the wine and also climbed onto the platform. He filled the prince's cup and reminded: "Young Master, don't get dead drunk like the Prince of Yi."
[ZH] “桂花酿没多少酒劲，醉不了。”
[EN] "Osmanthus wine doesn't have much kick. I won't get drunk."
[ZH] 乔文仙端起酒杯慢慢品着，与其说他欣赏景色，不如说是消磨时间，等待仪王酒醒。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian picked up his wine cup and slowly sipped. Rather than saying he was enjoying the scenery, it was more like he was killing time, waiting for the Prince of Yi to sober up.
[ZH] 当他喝完一瓶桂花酿后，天色渐渐暗下来，估计仪王这会儿应该已醒。
[EN] After he finished a bottle of osmanthus wine, the sky gradually darkened. He estimated the Prince of Yi should be awake by now.
[ZH] “南双，随我去见仪王。”
[EN] "Nanshuang, come with me to see the Prince of Yi."
[ZH] 两人走下高台，来到主屋的院外，佳和郡主竟然站在那里，看上去神色忧伤。
[EN] The two climbed down from the platform and came to the courtyard outside the main building. Princess Jiahe was actually standing there, looking melancholy.
[ZH] 乔文仙上前问：“郡主为何站在门口？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stepped forward and asked: "Why is the princess standing at the gate?"
[ZH] “我……我在等哥哥醒来。”乔梦容小心说道。
[EN] "I... I'm waiting for my brother to wake up." Qiao Mengrong said carefully.
[ZH] 乔文仙邀请道：“郡主随我进去吧？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian invited: "Would the princess like to come in with me?"
[ZH] “不，我怕哥哥见到我会不开心，因为我做错事。”原来乔梦容还是以为哥哥在生她的气，嫌她亲吻了他。
[EN] "No, I'm afraid my brother will be unhappy seeing me because I did something wrong." It turned out Qiao Mengrong still thought her brother was angry with her, disgusted that she had kissed him.
[ZH] 乔文仙见佳和郡主楚楚可怜的样子，生起怜悯之情：“郡主不如回去吧？天快黑了。”
[EN] Seeing Princess Jiahe's pitiful and delicate appearance, Qiao Wenxian felt pity: "Why doesn't the princess go back? It's getting dark."
[ZH] “乔公子不必管我，我要在此等哥哥醒来。”乔梦容依然坚持着。
[EN] "Young Master Qiao doesn't need to worry about me. I want to wait here for my brother to wake up." Qiao Mengrong still persisted.
[ZH] 乔文仙不再强求，行礼后，走进院内，直奔主屋。外厅没有人，便拐进里屋。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stopped insisting. After bowing, he walked into the courtyard and headed straight for the main building. No one was in the outer hall, so he turned into the inner room.
[ZH] 仪王躺在床上，两只眼睛睁得老大，显然酒已醒。
[EN] The Prince of Yi lay on the bed, his eyes wide open, obviously sober.
[ZH] 白风见南双进来，他站得无聊，过去拉上南双：“我们出去玩儿吧。”
[EN] Seeing Nanshuang come in, Baifeng, who was standing around bored, went over and pulled her: "Let's go out and play."
[ZH] 南双点头同意，他们拉着手出了主屋，在院中见到佳和郡主。
[EN] Nanshuang nodded in agreement. They held hands and left the main building, seeing Princess Jiahe in the courtyard.
[ZH] 乔梦容在乔文仙进屋后大胆走进院内，站在院中继续等着，喜兰为她放了把椅子，她却不愿坐下。
[EN] After Qiao Wenxian entered the room, Qiao Mengrong boldly walked into the courtyard. She stood in the middle of the courtyard continuing to wait. Xilan placed a chair for her, but she refused to sit.
[ZH] 乔梦容见到白风和南双，没等他们行礼，先问道：“白风，哥哥可醒？”
[EN] Seeing Baifeng and Nanshuang, Qiao Mengrong asked before they could bow: "Baifeng, is my brother awake?"
[ZH] 白风回道：“王爷早醒了，可他只想躺着，我也没辙。”
[EN] Baifeng replied: "The prince woke up long ago, but he just wants to lie down. I can't do anything about it."
[ZH] “哥哥可高兴？”
[EN] "Is my brother happy?"
[ZH] “嗯……王爷既没笑，也没哭，看着心情平静，应该不错。”
[EN] "Um... the prince neither laughed nor cried. He looks calm, so he should be fine."
[ZH] 乔梦容松口气，说明哥哥不再生她的气：“我问完了，你们去玩吧。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong relaxed, indicating her brother was no longer angry with her: "I've finished asking. You two go play."
[ZH] 白风与南双高高兴兴地跑了出去。
[EN] Baifeng and Nanshuang happily ran out.
[ZH] 屋内，乔文仙坐到床边：“既然酒已醒，赶紧起来，佳和郡主一直在外面站着，赶紧去哄哄她。”
[EN] Inside the room, Qiao Wenxian sat on the edge of the bed: "Since you're sober, get up quickly. Princess Jiahe has been standing outside all this time. Hurry up and coax her."
[ZH] 申简辰当然心疼妹妹，可这位妹妹给他无形中造成的重压远远超过心疼的感觉。他转过身，背对着文渊：“你帮我劝她回去休息。”
[EN] Of course Shen Jianchen cared for his sister, but the invisible pressure this sister caused him far exceeded the feeling of care. He turned around, his back to Wenyuan: "Help me persuade her to go back and rest."
[ZH] “我已劝过，一点用处没有。”乔文仙用手拉仪王，“起来吧，不要辜负外面的佳人，你去劝，她一准儿听。”
[EN] "I've already tried persuading her. It was no use at all." Qiao Wenxian pulled the Prince of Yi with his hand. "Get up. Don't let down the beauty outside. If you go persuade her, she'll definitely listen."
[ZH] 申简辰抽回手，依然背对着文渊。
[EN] Shen Jianchen pulled his hand back, still with his back to Wenyuan.
[ZH] 乔文仙脱掉鞋，也躺到床上：“你既然不愿起来，我陪你睡。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian took off his shoes and also lay on the bed: "Since you don't want to get up, I'll sleep with you."
[ZH] 申简辰依旧背对着文渊。
[EN] Shen Jianchen still had his back to Wenyuan.
[ZH] “你能不能答应我件事？”
[EN] "Can you promise me something?"
[ZH] “什么事？”
[EN] "What?"
[ZH] “过几天跟我回趟乔府，劝我父亲，让他去朱家提亲。”
[EN] "In a few days, come back to the Qiao residence with me and persuade my father to go to the Zhu family to propose."
[ZH] “自己的事情自己解决。”
[EN] "Solve your own problems yourself."
[ZH] “我已向父亲表明，他虽未明确拒绝，可我能感觉到，他根本不同意到朱家提亲。”
[EN] "I've already made it clear to my father. Though he hasn't explicitly refused, I can feel he doesn't agree at all with proposing to the Zhu family."
[ZH] 听到这话，申简辰竟然有一丝轻松，他转过身：“外公不同意，我去了又有何用？”
[EN] Hearing this, Shen Jianchen actually felt a trace of relief. He turned around: "If your grandfather doesn't agree, what's the use of me going?"
[ZH] “你是王爷，我爹绝不敢驳你的面子，就答应吧，帮帮我。”乔文仙继续央求。
[EN] "You're a prince. My father would never dare refuse your face. Just agree and help me." Qiao Wenxian continued to plead.
[ZH] 申简辰没有说话，又背对着文渊。
[EN] Shen Jianchen didn't speak and turned his back to Wenyuan again.
[ZH] 乔文仙从背后把仪王抱住：“你不答应我，我就抱着你，让佳和郡主嫉妒。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hugged the Prince of Yi from behind: "If you don't agree, I'll keep hugging you and make Princess Jiahe jealous."
[ZH] “想抱就抱吧，梦容才不会那么小心眼儿。”
[EN] "Hug away if you want. Mengrong won't be so petty."
[ZH] 申简辰并不理会，他很矛盾，要不要帮文渊？
[EN] Shen Jianchen ignored him. He was conflicted. Should he help Wenyuan?
[ZH] 帮了文渊，以后千里寻就是文渊的人，他申简辰可连一点儿希望也没有。
[EN] If he helped Wenyuan, in the future Qianlixun would be Wenyuan's person, and he, Shen Jianchen, would have not even a trace of hope.
[ZH] 不帮，文渊又是他最好的朋友，他也希望文渊能与相爱的人相守一生。
[EN] If he didn't help, Wenyuan was still his best friend, and he also hoped Wenyuan could spend his life with the person he loved.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 天黑后，朱府的园子内因挂满灯笼变得明亮许多。千里寻想不通朱淑真为何喜欢黑漆漆的院子，亮亮堂堂岂不美哉？
[EN] After dark, the garden of the Zhu residence became much brighter with lanterns hung everywhere. Qianlixun couldn't understand why Zhu Shuzhen liked such a dark courtyard. Wouldn't it be more beautiful if it were bright?
[ZH] 她在院子里走了几圈，意犹未尽，没想到小院儿在夜晚灯火的衬托下比白日更加美观。
[EN] She walked around the courtyard several times, still not satisfied. She didn't expect the small courtyard to be even more beautiful at night, set off by the lantern light, than during the day.
[ZH] 走累后，她才坐在藤椅上又欣赏一番。
[EN] After walking until tired, she sat in a rattan chair and appreciated it some more.
[ZH] 这么美的景，只有苦荞陪伴她欣赏。如若有爱人在身边，会更加完美。
[EN] With such beautiful scenery, only Kuqiao accompanied her to appreciate it. If her lover were by her side, it would be even more perfect.
[ZH] 想到这些，千里寻觉得朱淑真居住的园子太过冷清。
[EN] Thinking of this, Qianlixun felt the garden where Zhu Shuzhen lived was too lonely and cold.
[ZH] “苦荞，我想去幽林别居。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, I want to go to Youlin Villa."
[ZH] “天都黑了，小姐外出，夫人和韦嬷嬷肯定不同意。”
[EN] "It's already dark. If the young lady goes out, Madam and Nanny Wei will definitely not agree."
[ZH] “我偷偷溜出去，不让她们知道不就行了！”
[EN] "I'll sneak out secretly. If they don't know, isn't that fine?"
[ZH] “晚上出城会很危险。”
[EN] "Going out of the city at night will be very dangerous."
[ZH] “危险？在我眼里没有人比我更危险！”
[EN] "Dangerous? In my eyes, no one is more dangerous than me!"
[ZH] 也对，小姐重生后，变得厉害，谁都没怕过：“既如此，我随小姐去吧。”
[EN] That's true. After her young lady was reborn, she became formidable and feared no one: "In that case, I'll go with you, young lady."
[ZH] 千里寻夸道：“真乖！”
[EN] Qianlixun praised: "Good girl!"
[ZH] 主仆二人走出园子，悄悄溜出朱府，套了马车，直奔向城外。
[EN] The mistress and maid walked out of the garden, quietly sneaked out of the Zhu residence, hitched a carriage, and headed straight out of the city.
[ZH] ……
[EN] ...
[ZH] 天越来越黑，文渊竟然抱着仪王差点儿睡着，直到门外传来南双的声音：“公子，幽栖居士在别居外等您。”
[EN] The sky grew darker and darker. Wenyuan was actually hugging the Prince of Yi and nearly fell asleep, until Nanshuang's voice came from outside the door: "Young Master, Youqi Jushi is waiting for you outside the villa."
[ZH] 乔文仙立刻坐起来，他正想着淑真，没想到她就来了，实在合拍！乔文仙兴奋地穿上鞋子，跑出去。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian immediately sat up. He was just thinking of Shuzhen, and didn't expect she would come. They were really in sync! Qiao Wenxian excitedly put on his shoes and ran out.
[ZH] 到院中，见佳和郡主依然站着，顾不上问话，简单行礼后，匆忙离去。
[EN] Reaching the courtyard, he saw Princess Jiahe still standing there. Without time to ask, he gave a simple bow and hurriedly left.
[ZH] 南双想不通，明明有椅子，佳和郡主为何不坐着？他边想边跟着自家公子离开。
[EN] Nanshuang couldn't figure it out. There was clearly a chair, so why wouldn't Princess Jiahe sit? As he thought, he followed his young master out.
[ZH] 申简辰听到千里寻前来，也立刻起身，速度不比文渊差，紧跟文渊来到门口，发现下人已将院中的灯笼点着，倒也亮堂。乔梦容站在院中，腿脚轻微动着，显然已站了许久。
[EN] Hearing Qianlixun had come, Shen Jianchen also immediately got up, his speed no less than Wenyuan's. He followed closely behind Wenyuan to the gate and found the servants had already lit the lanterns in the courtyard, making it quite bright. Qiao Mengrong stood in the courtyard, her legs and feet moving slightly, obviously having stood for a long time.
[ZH] 申简辰不由站住：“梦容，你为何还站在这里？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen couldn't help but stop: "Mengrong, why are you still standing here?"
[ZH] 听到哥哥的声音，乔梦容露出笑容：“我在等哥哥醒来。”
[EN] Hearing her brother's voice, Qiao Mengrong showed a smile: "I was waiting for my brother to wake up."
[ZH] “为何不进屋？”
[EN] "Why didn't you come inside?"
[ZH] “我不敢，怕哥哥嫌弃我。”
[EN] "I didn't dare. I was afraid my brother would be disgusted with me."
[ZH] 乔梦容我见犹怜的样子，令申简辰感到心酸：“我怎么会嫌弃你？”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's appearance, so pitiful that even he felt sorry, made Shen Jianchen's heart ache: "How could I be disgusted with you?"
[ZH] “哥哥，我错了，不该在神柳下……”
[EN] "Brother, I was wrong. I shouldn't have under the divine willow..."
[ZH] 申简辰早已忘记被乔梦容强吻之事，原来她误解他心情不好是因为此事：“不，你没错，是哥哥的错。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen had long forgotten about being forcibly kissed by Qiao Mengrong. It turned out she misunderstood that his bad mood was because of this: "No, you weren't wrong. It was your brother's fault."
[ZH] “哥哥不再怨我？”
[EN] "Brother doesn't resent me anymore?"
[ZH] “我从来没怨过你。”
[EN] "I've never resented you."
[ZH] 乔梦容一身轻松，也因轻松站立不住，喜兰赶紧扶住郡主。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong felt completely relaxed, and from the relaxation, she couldn't even stand. Xilan quickly supported the princess.
[ZH] 申简辰吩咐道：“喜兰，扶郡主回屋休息。梦容，以后不许再这样。记住，哥哥永远不会生你的气。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen ordered: "Xilan, help the princess back to her room to rest. Mengrong, don't do this again in the future. Remember, your brother will never be angry with you."
[ZH] 乔梦容因高兴流下眼泪，拜别哥哥，在喜兰的搀扶下，向外走。因站的时间久，腿脚变麻，走起来酸得难受。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong shed tears from happiness. She bid farewell to her brother and, with Xilan's support, walked outward. Because she had stood for so long, her legs and feet had gone numb, and walking was painfully sore.
[ZH] 申简辰见状，上前一把抱起梦容：“我送你回屋。”
[EN] Seeing this, Shen Jianchen stepped forward and picked up Mengrong in his arms: "I'll take you back to your room."
[ZH] 突如其来的幸福，使得乔梦容没有时间思考，一味地感受着哥哥有力的臂膀和胸脯。她曾幻想过多次的情景终于实现，长久站立给身体带来的不适在这一刻荡然无存。
[EN] The sudden happiness left Qiao Mengrong with no time to think. She simply felt her brother's strong arms and chest. The scene she had fantasized about many times had finally come true. The discomfort her body had from standing for so long vanished in this moment.
[ZH] 申简辰将乔梦容抱回闺房，放到床上，说了句：“好好休息。”然后没有丝毫犹豫快步向别居的大门口走去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen carried Qiao Mengrong back to her boudoir, placed her on the bed, and said: "Rest well." Then, without the slightest hesitation, he walked quickly toward the villa's main gate.

#CH 83
[ZH] 乔文仙来到幽林别居外，见淑真站在马车前等他，夜色下她依然那么美好。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian arrived outside Youlin Villa and saw Shuzhen standing in front of the carriage waiting for him. Even in the night, she was still so beautiful.
[ZH] 乔文仙来到淑真面前，关切而又兴奋地问：“淑真，天都黑了，你怎么跑来？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian came to Shuzhen's side and asked with concern and excitement: "Shuzhen, it's already dark. Why did you come here?"
[ZH] 千里寻毫不掩饰自己的感情：“一日未见，甚是想念，忍不住过来看看你。”
[EN] Qianlixun didn't hide her feelings at all: "A day apart feels like an eternity. I couldn't help but come see you."
[ZH] 乔文仙抓住淑真的手：“我也想你。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian took Shuzhen's hand: "I miss you too."
[ZH] 苦荞与南双见二人亲密的样子，不好意思注视下去，都退到远处。
[EN] Kuqiao and Nanshuang, seeing the two being so intimate, felt embarrassed to keep watching and retreated to a distance.
[ZH] 两人执手互相对视，无须言语，便知对方心意。
[EN] The two held hands and gazed at each other. No words were needed to know each other's hearts.
[ZH] 申简辰到达别居门口时，他的腿脚无法挪动，明明猜到他们相见时会有亲密的举动，他却忍不住要跟着前来。
[EN] When Shen Jianchen arrived at the villa's gate, his legs couldn't move. He had clearly guessed they would be intimate when they met, yet he couldn't help but follow.
[ZH] 如果眼前的男子是别人，申简辰会一拳打过去，可偏偏是文渊。文渊是他来到这个时代认识的第一个朋友，他十分珍惜。
[EN] If the man before him were anyone else, Shen Jianchen would have thrown a punch. But it had to be Wenyuan. Wenyuan was the first friend he had made after coming to this era, and he cherished him greatly.
[ZH] 更令他伤心的是，千里寻喜欢着文渊，她看他的眼神与在另一个时代看他时完全不同。
[EN] What hurt him even more was that Qianlixun loved Wenyuan. The way she looked at him was completely different from how she had looked at him in the other era.
[ZH] 申简辰努力控制住情绪，不想让他们再这么亲密下去，脱口而出：“文渊，你不是想让我帮你吗？我答应。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen struggled to control his emotions. Not wanting them to continue being so intimate, he blurted out: "Wenyuan, didn't you want me to help you? I agree."
[ZH] 文渊果然放开爱人的手，转身跑到门口抱住仪王：“说话算话，不许骗我！”
[EN] Wenyuan, as expected, let go of his lover's hand, turned around, ran to the gate, and hugged the Prince of Yi: "A promise is a promise. No lying to me!"
[ZH] 申简辰看向千里寻，文渊放开她后，她显得失落。两人不再亲密，申简辰有些许快意，又后悔为何不假思索答应帮助文渊。话已出口，覆水难收。
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked at Qianlixun. After Wenyuan let go of her, she looked disappointed. The two were no longer intimate. Shen Jianchen felt a trace of satisfaction, but also regretted why he had agreed to help Wenyuan without thinking. The words were out. What's done cannot be undone.
[ZH] “你再抱着我，我可要反悔。”
[EN] "If you keep hugging me, I'll change my mind."
[ZH] “千万别，我与淑真的幸福全靠仪王您了。”乔文仙在仪王耳边说完，放开他。
[EN] "Please don't. My happiness with Shuzhen depends entirely on you, Your Highness." Qiao Wenxian whispered in the prince's ear, then let go.
[ZH] 千里寻上前行礼：“仪王，淑真这厢有礼。”
[EN] Qianlixun stepped forward and bowed: "Prince of Yi, Shuzhen pays her respects."
[ZH] “你见我不必如此多礼，像文渊一样，我才觉得舒服。”
[EN] "You don't need to be so formal with me. I only feel comfortable when you're like Wenyuan."
[ZH] “仪王为何不早说？害得我见您行了好几次礼。”
[EN] "Why didn't you say so earlier? You made me bow to you several times."
[ZH] “以前没想到，现在说也不晚。”申简辰有意靠近千里寻，“大晚上的，幽栖居士跑来见我家文渊，会不会显得太主动？姑娘家太主动似乎不大妥当。”
[EN] "I didn't think of it before. It's not too late to say it now." Shen Jianchen deliberately moved closer to Qianlixun. "Late at night, Youqi Jushi comes to see my Wenyuan. Won't that seem too forward? It doesn't seem proper for a young lady to be too forward."
[ZH] 千里寻的话还未出口，文渊主动说：“我喜欢淑真主动。”
[EN] Before Qianlixun could speak, Wenyuan volunteered: "I like Shuzhen being forward."
[ZH] 千里寻得意地看着仪王：“仪王刚说要帮文渊，是帮什么？”
[EN] Qianlixun looked at the Prince of Yi triumphantly: "You just said you'd help Wenyuan. Help with what?"
[ZH] 乔文仙怕淑真知道乔守仁没爽快答应求婚之事会难过，赶紧说：“是我与仪王之间的事，你不必挂怀。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian, afraid Shuzhen would be sad to know Qiao Shouren hadn't readily agreed to the proposal, quickly said: "It's something between me and the Prince of Yi. You don't need to worry about it."
[ZH] 千里寻装作生气的样子：“你们两个之间竟然有秘密，文渊，我嫉妒了，我觉得你跟仪王比跟我关系好。”
[EN] Qianlixun pretended to be angry: "You two actually have secrets between you. Wenyuan, I'm jealous. I feel you're closer to the Prince of Yi than to me."
[ZH] 乔文仙靠近淑真，着急道：“怎么会？别瞎想，我跟仪王除是亲人外，还是朋友，自然关系非他人能比，但与跟你的关系不一样，如果说仪王是我在这个世上最好的朋友，你就是我在这个世上最爱的人。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian moved closer to Shuzhen and said anxiously: "How could that be? Don't think nonsense. Besides being family, the Prince of Yi and I are also friends. Naturally, our relationship can't be compared to others, but it's different from my relationship with you. If the Prince of Yi is my best friend in this world, you are the person I love most in this world."
[ZH] 如若没有仪王在身边，千里寻真想抱住文渊。仪王一直盯着他们，令她难以下手。心想：“仪王怎么这么没眼色？没见人家与文渊情正浓，他却直勾勾盯着，让人难为情。”
[EN] If the Prince of Yi weren't around, Qianlixun would have loved to hug Wenyuan. The prince kept staring at them, making it hard for her to make a move. She thought: "Why is the Prince of Yi so clueless? Can't he see Wenyuan and I are in the middle of a tender moment? He keeps staring right at us, it's embarrassing."
[ZH] 文渊视他为亲人，申简辰十分感动，也让他感到羞愧，为何从文渊身边抢走千里寻的想法会如此强烈？他矛盾着。
[EN] Wenyuan regarded him as family. Shen Jianchen was deeply moved, but also ashamed. Why was the desire to take Qianlixun away from Wenyuan so strong? He was conflicted.
[ZH] “文渊，能否让我与幽栖居士单独聊聊？”
[EN] "Wenyuan, could you let me talk to Youqi Jushi alone?"
[ZH] 乔文仙显然不乐意：“为何？她可是来找我的。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian was obviously unwilling: "Why? She came to see me."
[ZH] “还想不想让我帮你？”
[EN] "Do you still want me to help you?"
[ZH] “当然想。”
[EN] "Of course I do."
[ZH] “既然想就听我的。”
[EN] "Then listen to me."
[ZH] 乔文仙见仪王语气坚定，心想，仪王已有佳和郡主，应该不会对淑真产生任何想法，既是为帮他，不如听他一回。他走到仪王身边，凑近后小声问：“我能不能在旁边待着？”
[EN] Seeing the prince's firm tone, Qiao Wenxian thought: The Prince of Yi already has Princess Jiahe. He shouldn't have any thoughts about Shuzhen. Since it's to help him, why not listen to him once. He walked to the prince's side, leaned close, and whispered: "Can I stay nearby?"
[ZH] “不行！”仪王声音虽不大，语气却坚定。
[EN] "No!" Though the prince's voice wasn't loud, his tone was firm.
[ZH] 乔文仙只好作罢：“那好，我在院子里等，你跟淑真说话温柔些，不要吓着她。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian had to give up: "Alright then. I'll wait in the courtyard. Be gentle when you talk to Shuzhen. Don't scare her."
[ZH] “她会害怕？”申简辰反问道，靠近文渊耳边小声说，“看来你一点儿都不了解她。”
[EN] "She'll be scared?" Shen Jianchen retorted, then leaned close to Wenyuan's ear and whispered, "It seems you don't understand her at all."
[ZH] “我当然了解。”乔文仙很自信，转向淑真，“我进去待会儿，一会儿我们再聊。”
[EN] "Of course I understand." Qiao Wenxian was very confident. He turned to Shuzhen. "I'll go in for a while. We'll talk later."
[ZH] 不等淑真答应，乔文仙已走进幽林别居。
[EN] Without waiting for Shuzhen's agreement, Qiao Wenxian had already walked into Youlin Villa.
[ZH] 南双跟上自家公子，到门口时，发现白风也跟来，一直在门口朝苦荞看。
[EN] Nanshuang followed his young master. At the gate, he found Baifeng had also followed and was staring at Kuqiao the whole time.
[ZH] “你盯着苦荞姐姐做什么？”
[EN] "Why are you staring at Sister Kuqiao?"
[ZH] 白风拉住南双：“她看着也不大，你怎么叫她姐姐？”
[EN] Baifeng pulled Nanshuang: "She doesn't look that old. Why do you call her sister?"
[ZH] 南双回道：“她比我大三岁，我当然称呼她姐姐。”
[EN] Nanshuang replied: "She's three years older than me. Of course I call her sister."
[ZH] “我比你大一岁，那她就比我大两岁，没想到她竟然比我大，不过大些也没关系。”白风像是自语。
[EN] "I'm one year older than you, so she's two years older than me. I didn't expect her to be older than me. But it's fine if she's a bit older." Baifeng seemed to be talking to himself.
[ZH] 南双听着没趣：“我去陪我家公子，懒得跟你说话。”
[EN] Nanshuang found it boring: "I'm going to keep my young master company. I can't be bothered to talk to you."
[ZH] 白风继续盯着苦荞，他从未对一个女孩子有过像现在一样奇怪的感觉，这感觉使得他只想盯着对方看，甚至忘记关注王爷对幽栖居士说些什么。
[EN] Baifeng continued staring at Kuqiao. He had never felt such a strange feeling toward a girl before. This feeling made him want to keep staring at her, even forgetting to pay attention to what the prince was saying to Youqi Jushi.
[ZH] 再说王爷拉起幽栖居士，走到离他与苦荞较远的地方讲话，显然是不想让他听到。他干脆走近苦荞，站在她身边，感受着与她待在一起的感觉。
[EN] Meanwhile, the prince took Youqi Jushi's hand and walked to a place far from him and Kuqiao to talk, obviously not wanting him to hear. He simply walked closer to Kuqiao, stood beside her, and enjoyed the feeling of being with her.
[ZH] 申简辰拉起千里寻的手臂，把她拽到一边，站定后，依然不舍得放开，直到千里寻要求道：“仪王，能不能放开我？”他才不得不放开。
[EN] Shen Jianchen took Qianlixun's arm and pulled her to the side. After they stopped, he still couldn't bear to let go, until Qianlixun requested: "Prince of Yi, could you let go of me?" Only then did he have to release her.
[ZH] “仪王为何拉我到这边？难道想把你与文渊的秘密告诉我？”
[EN] "Why did you pull me over here? Do you want to tell me the secret between you and Wenyuan?"
[ZH] 千里寻问完，发现仪王看她的眼神很不对劲，蕴含的感情并不比文渊差。心想：“怎么回事？他不是有佳和郡主吗？为何如此看我？该不会是因喜欢看我写的话本，偷偷喜欢上我？哎呀！不管是什么，我已有文渊，岂能接受他的感情？”
[EN] After Qianlixun asked, she noticed the prince's gaze was off — the emotion in it no less than Wenyuan's. She thought: “What's going on? Doesn't he have Princess Jiahe? Why look at me like that? Could he have fallen for me from reading my stories? No! I already have Wenyuan. I can't accept his feelings.”
[ZH] 千里寻向后退了一步，继续问：“仪王，不知你想对我说什么？”
[EN] Qianlixun stepped back and continued to ask: "Prince of Yi, what did you want to say to me?"
[ZH] 所爱之人有意与他保持距离，申简辰清醒许多，他收回眼神，抬起头，闭上眼睛，在心里叹口气，问道：“你确定想嫁给文渊？”
[EN] Seeing the person he loved deliberately keeping her distance from him, Shen Jianchen became much more clear-headed. He withdrew his gaze, raised his head, closed his eyes, sighed inwardly, and asked: "Are you sure you want to marry Wenyuan?"
[ZH] 千里寻毫不犹豫：“当然。”
[EN] Qianlixun didn't hesitate: "Of course."
[ZH] “如若乔家人不同意，你是否依然坚持？”
[EN] "If the Qiao family doesn't agree, will you still persist?"
[ZH] “那还用问！”
[EN] "Why even ask!"
[ZH] “若你坚持，乔家人依然不同意，你会怎么做？”
[EN] "If you persist but the Qiao family still doesn't agree, what will you do?"
[ZH] 千里寻沉默了一会儿，说道：“我会听文渊的，他若坚持，我便坚持，他若放弃，我……我不知道。”
[EN] Qianlixun was silent for a moment, then said: "I'll listen to Wenyuan. If he persists, I'll persist. If he gives up, I... I don't know."
[ZH] 申简辰对自己感到失望，穿越前不涉足爱情的她竟然在穿越后这么喜欢一个人，可悲的是这个人并不是他。
[EN] Shen Jianchen was disappointed in himself. Before transmigrating, she had never been interested in love. After transmigrating, she liked someone this much — and tragically, that person wasn't him.
[ZH] “你……你以前可喜欢过其他人？”
[EN] "Have... have you ever liked anyone else before?"
[ZH] 千里寻不明白，仪王为何要问出这个问题？她还是照实回答：“我失忆了，以前的事一点都记不起来，别人都说我喜欢过一位徐公子，可我连他长什么样都不记得。”
[EN] Qianlixun didn't understand. Why would the Prince of Yi ask this question? She still answered truthfully: "I have amnesia. I can't remember anything from before at all. Everyone says I once liked a Young Master Xu, but I can't even remember what he looked like."
[ZH] “我听说人失忆是因为有新的灵魂住进身体里，不知幽栖居士体内新的灵魂是否还记得曾喜欢过谁？”申简辰半真半假的话语无非是想知道千里寻有没有爱过他。
[EN] "I've heard that amnesia happens because a new soul has moved into the body. I wonder if the new soul in Youqi Jushi's body still remembers who she once liked?" Shen Jianchen's half-true, half-false words were simply to find out if Qianlixun had ever loved him.
[ZH] 千里寻不由自主想到申简辰，用疑惑的目光盯着仪王，难道他知道了什么？
[EN] Qianlixun couldn't help but think of Shen Jianchen. She stared at the Prince of Yi with a puzzled gaze. Could he know something?
[ZH] “这种说法我头一次听说。”
[EN] "This is the first time I've heard such a theory."
[ZH] 千里寻的回答没有说到申简辰心里，他叹口气：“唉，幽栖居士既然如此喜欢文渊，我定会努力成全。也希望你认真想一想，有没有曾经喜欢过其他人，我是说失忆后的你，如果有，一定要告诉我。”
[EN] Qianlixun's answer didn't reach Shen Jianchen's heart. He sighed: "Sigh, since Youqi Jushi likes Wenyuan so much, I will definitely try to help you succeed. I also hope you think carefully about whether you've ever liked anyone else. I mean, after your amnesia. If there is someone, you must tell me."
[ZH] 申简辰说完，转身离去。走到门口见白风未跟过来，大声喊道：“白风！还愣着干什么？”
[EN] After Shen Jianchen finished speaking, he turned and left. Walking to the gate, he saw Baifeng hadn't followed. He shouted loudly: "Baifeng! What are you standing there for?"
[ZH] 白风从怀里掏出一个泥人，塞进苦荞怀里：“送给你。”
[EN] Baifeng pulled out a clay figurine from inside his coat and pressed it into Kuqiao's arms: "This is for you."
[ZH] 苦荞接住，想要拒绝已不可能，白风早迅速跟随仪王进入幽林别居。
[EN] Kuqiao caught it. It was already impossible to refuse. Baifeng had already quickly followed the prince into Youlin Villa.
[ZH] 乔文仙见仪王进来， 拉住他：“淑真可走？”
[EN] Seeing the prince come in, Qiao Wenxian pulled him: "Can Shuzhen leave?"
[ZH] “还在外面。”申简辰快速走开开，他怕自己忍不住冲出去抱住千里寻，那样不仅会破坏他与文渊之间的感情，也会听到千里寻毫不犹豫拒绝的话语。
[EN] "She's still outside." Shen Jianchen quickly walked away. He was afraid he couldn't resist rushing out to hug Qianlixun. That would not only destroy the friendship between him and Wenyuan, but he would also hear Qianlixun's words of refusal without hesitation.
[ZH] 乔文仙再次来到淑真面前，他并未询问仪王说些什么，担心太晚，坚持要护送淑真回去。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian came to Shuzhen again. He didn't ask what the prince had said. Worried it was too late, he insisted on escorting Shuzhen back.
[ZH] 千里寻没有拒绝，她本就希望与文渊多待些时间。他们二人同坐在朱府的马车内，后面跟着乔家的马车，里面坐着苦荞与南双。
[EN] Qianlixun didn't refuse. She had originally hoped to spend more time with Wenyuan. The two sat together in the Zhu family's carriage, followed by the Qiao family's carriage behind, with Kuqiao and Nanshuang inside.
[ZH] 一路上，他搂着她，不用说任何话，只需互相用心感受对方的心意。
[EN] Along the way, he held her in his arms. No words needed to be spoken. They only needed to feel each other's hearts with their own.

#CH 84
[ZH] 靠着乔文仙的肩膀，千里寻觉出从未有过的踏实感，心里越踏实，困意越浓，渐渐睡去。
[EN] Leaning on Qiao Wenxian's shoulder, Qianlixun felt a sense of security she had never experienced before. The more secure she felt, the sleepier she became, and she gradually fell asleep.
[ZH] 乔文仙不敢再动，安静地坐着，连呼吸也变得谨慎，他怕自己的呼吸稍微用力些，会把淑真惊醒。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian didn't dare move. He sat quietly, even his breathing becoming cautious. He was afraid that if he breathed too hard, he would wake Shuzhen.
[ZH] 马车里除能听到车轮碾压道路的声音，便是乔文仙谨慎的呼吸声。
[EN] In the carriage, besides the sound of wheels rolling over the road, there was only Qiao Wenxian's cautious breathing.
[ZH] 乔家的马车跟在后面，苦荞与南双对面而坐。
[EN] The Qiao family's carriage followed behind. Kuqiao and Nanshuang sat opposite each other.
[ZH] 苦荞手中拿着泥人，心里一直嘀咕，仪王的贴身侍卫为何要给她送泥人？
[EN] Kuqiao held the clay figurine in her hand, wondering all along: Why would the prince's personal guard give her a clay figurine?
[ZH] “南双，仪王身边的侍卫叫什么？”
[EN] "Nanshuang, what's the name of the guard by the Prince of Yi's side?"
[ZH] “白风。”
[EN] "Baifeng."
[ZH] “白风。”苦荞重复着名字。
[EN] "Baifeng." Kuqiao repeated the name.
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐问白风做什么？”
[EN] "Why does Sister Kuqiao ask about Baifeng?"
[ZH] “他无缘无故送我一个泥人，让人摸不着头脑。”
[EN] "He gave me a clay figurine for no reason. It's confusing."
[ZH] “我就说苦荞姐姐手中的泥人怎么看着熟悉，原来是他送给你的。我问他要了好几次，他都舍不得，说是从汴梁带来的，小时候他娘给他买的。哼！白风太不够意思！我回去一定要找他好好问问，还想不想跟我做朋友！”
[EN] "No wonder the clay figurine in Sister Kuqiao's hand looked familiar. It turns out he gave it to you. I asked him for it several times, but he wouldn't part with it. He said he brought it from Bianliang, and his mother bought it for him when he was little. Hmph! Baifeng is too inconsiderate! When I go back, I'll definitely have a good talk with him about whether he still wants to be my friend!"
[ZH] 原来泥人这么重要，苦荞把泥人给南双：“这么重要的东西，你还是帮我还给他。”
[EN] So the clay figurine was that important. Kuqiao gave it to Nanshuang: "Since it's so important, help me return it to him."
[ZH] 南双接过，揣进怀里。
[EN] Nanshuang took it and tucked it into her bosom.
[ZH] 马车到达朱府侧门，苦荞与南双先下马车，等了一会儿，不见两位主人下车。他们不敢吱声，在车外继续耐心等待。
[EN] The carriage arrived at the side gate of the Zhu residence. Kuqiao and Nanshuang got off first. After waiting for a while, they didn't see their masters get off. They didn't dare make a sound and continued to wait patiently outside the carriage.
[ZH] 千里寻还未醒，睡得正香。乔文仙不愿打扰她，更确切地说，他正享受着心爱的女子靠着他进入梦乡的感觉。
[EN] Qianlixun hadn't woken up yet. She was sleeping soundly. Qiao Wenxian didn't want to disturb her. More precisely, he was enjoying the feeling of his beloved woman leaning on him as she drifted into sleep.
[ZH] 千里寻梦到地震的场景，梦到申简辰被神柳的力量带走的一刻，呼唤着她的名字：“千里寻！千里寻！”她竟然在梦中清晰地听到声音，也大声唤着：“申简辰！申简辰！”可申简辰在她面前消失不见。
[EN] Qianlixun dreamed of an earthquake. She dreamed of the moment Shen Jianchen was taken away by the power of the divine willow, calling her name: "Qianlixun! Qianlixun!" She actually heard the voice clearly in her dream and shouted back: "Shen Jianchen! Shen Jianchen!" But Shen Jianchen disappeared before her eyes.
[ZH] 千里寻唤着申简辰的名字醒来。
[EN] Qianlixun woke up calling Shen Jianchen's name.
[ZH] 乔文仙又把她搂在怀里：“是不是做噩梦了？别怕，有我在。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian held her in his arms again: "Did you have a nightmare? Don't be afraid. I'm here."
[ZH] 千里寻依偎在文渊怀中：“有文渊在，我当然不怕。”
[EN] Qianlixun nestled in Wenyuan's arms: "With Wenyuan here, of course I'm not afraid."
[ZH] “申简辰是谁？你为何在梦中唤着他的名字？”乔文仙想起仪王曾化名为申简辰。
[EN] "Who is Shen Jianchen? Why were you calling his name in your dream?" Qiao Wenxian remembered that the Prince of Yi had once used Shen Jianchen as a pseudonym.
[ZH] 穿越而来之事，又如何能向文渊说明？说了他也不会相信，恐怕他知道她并非真正的朱淑真而失望。好不容易遇到相爱之人，她不想轻易失去。
[EN] How could she explain to Wenyuan about transmigrating to this era? Even if she told him, he wouldn't believe it. She was afraid he would be disappointed to learn she wasn't the real Zhu Shuzhen. Having finally found someone she loved, she didn't want to lose him easily.
[ZH] “哦，是我在梦中见到的人，我梦到地震，他在地震中消失，我很害怕，不由自主喊出这个名字。”
[EN] "Oh, he's someone I saw in my dream. I dreamed of an earthquake, and he disappeared in it. I was so scared that I couldn't help but shout his name."
[ZH] 乔文仙认为一个是仪王的化名，一个是淑真梦中偶然梦到的名字，也许只是巧合而已，他并未多想。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian thought: one is the prince's pseudonym, the other is a name Shuzhen happened to dream of. Perhaps it was just a coincidence. He didn't think much of it.
[ZH] “原来如此，害我担心半天，还以为你心里装着别的男子。”
[EN] "I see. You worried me half to death. I thought you had another man in your heart."
[ZH] “怎么会？我心里只有文渊。”
[EN] "How could that be? There's only Wenyuan in my heart."
[ZH] 听到这话，乔文仙把淑真搂得更紧，生怕她被人抢走似的。
[EN] Hearing this, Qiao Wenxian held Shuzhen even tighter, as if afraid someone would snatch her away.
[ZH] 听到马车里传出声音，苦荞向里说：“小姐，太晚了，还是回去吧。”
[EN] Hearing voices from inside the carriage, Kuqiao said: "Young lady, it's too late. Let's go back."
[ZH] 两人很不情愿地走下马车，恋恋不舍地分开。
[EN] The two reluctantly got off the carriage and parted with reluctance.
[ZH] 乔文仙看着淑真走进朱府，身影消失在他面前，才又乘坐乔家的马车离开。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian watched Shuzhen walk into the Zhu residence. Only after her figure disappeared from his sight did he board the Qiao family's carriage and leave.
[ZH] 这天晚上，当千里寻享受爱情时，陈浩宇则在谋划着一桩大事。
[EN] That night, while Qianlixun was enjoying her love, Chen Haoyu was plotting a major scheme.
[ZH] 他派人找来临安城各处的小混混头目，聚集在陈家城东的一座宅子里。这座宅子无人居住，丫鬟下人应有尽有，里面的布置用具一切齐全，偶尔陈浩宇会带人在这里住一段时日。
[EN] He sent people to find the leaders of various petty thugs in Lin'an City and gathered them at a residence in the eastern part of the city owned by the Chen family. The residence was uninhabited but fully equipped with maids and servants. The furnishings and supplies were complete. Occasionally, Chen Haoyu would bring people to stay there for a period.
[ZH] 宅子的主院内，陈浩宇坐在椅子上，众人站在他面前。
[EN] In the main courtyard of the residence, Chen Haoyu sat in a chair with everyone standing before him.
[ZH] 一位小弟说：“陈公子把大家请来，有要事吩咐，现在请陈公子训话。”
[EN] One of the underlings said: "Young Master Chen has invited everyone here to give important instructions. Now please let Young Master Chen address us."
[ZH] 陈浩宇清了清嗓子：“请各位前来，是想让大家帮我整一个人……”
[EN] Chen Haoyu cleared his throat: "I've invited you all here because I want you to help me deal with someone..."
[ZH] 一个头目说：“陈公子尽管说，不管是杀人放火，还是打他一顿我们都敢干。”
[EN] One of the leaders said: "Just say it, Young Master Chen. Whether it's killing, arson, or beating someone up, we dare to do it all."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“不需要打人，更不需要杀人放火，只需各位帮我带领你们的弟兄到处传播谣言，一个月后让整个临安城，乃至乡下人都知道。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: "No need for beating, let alone killing or arson. I just need you all to lead your brothers and spread rumors everywhere. In a month, I want the entire Lin'an City, even the country folk, to know about it."
[ZH] 一个头目问：“不知是什么谣言？值得陈公子如此大费周折？”
[EN] One of the leaders asked: "What kind of rumors? Is it worth all this trouble, Young Master Chen?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇摆摆手。
[EN] Chen Haoyu waved his hand.
[ZH] 一个小弟将手中的纸展开，念道：“朱淑真是母老虎，逼得夫家和离，还红杏出墙，婚后与野男人暗送秋波，和离后竟然不知羞耻，当众睡在男人怀里，还写下'和衣睡倒人怀'，实在有失女德，这种人应该遭千夫所指，万人唾骂！”
[EN] One of the underlings unfolded the paper in his hand and read: "Zhu Shuzhen is a shrew who forced her husband's family into divorce. She had affairs, exchanging secret glances with strange men during her marriage. After the divorce, she shamelessly slept in a man's arms in public and even wrote 'sleeping in a man's arms fully clothed.' She has lost all feminine virtue. Such a person deserves to be pointed at by a thousand fingers and cursed by ten thousand people!"
[ZH] 小弟念完，收了纸张，问道：“各位，都记下没？”
[EN] After the underling finished reading, he put away the paper and asked: "Everyone, have you memorized it?"
[ZH] 几位混混头目，互相看看，很是不解。
[EN] The thug leaders looked at each other, quite confused.
[ZH] 有人问：“陈公子，陈家与朱家是亲家，这么诋毁朱家姑娘，会不会影响你们两家关系？”
[EN] Someone asked: "Young Master Chen, the Chen and Zhu families are in-laws. Slandering the Zhu family's daughter like this, won't it affect the relationship between your two families?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“本公子能让你们这么做，是经过深思熟虑。谣言是你们传的，跟本公子没有任何关系，跟陈家更没关系，等谣言传开，也跟你们没有任何关系，放心传吧。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: "I've thought this through carefully before asking you to do this. The rumors are spread by you, so they have nothing to do with me, and even less to do with the Chen family. Once the rumors spread, they'll have nothing to do with you either. Spread them with confidence."
[ZH] 一个头目赞道：“还是陈公子厉害！”
[EN] One of the leaders praised: "Young Master Chen is truly brilliant!"
[ZH] 另一个嬉皮笑脸问：“不知干这种事情，陈公子如何奖赏我们几个？”
[EN] Another asked with a grin: "For doing this kind of job, how will Young Master Chen reward us?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“我向来大方，难道你们不知？等事成之后，重重有赏，再带你们去春香楼逛逛。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu said: "I've always been generous. Don't you know that? After the job is done, there will be heavy rewards, and I'll take you to Chunxiang Brothel for a visit."
[ZH] 几个混混头目无不高兴：“陈公子放心，我等定将此事办得您满意，一个月后，定让朱家姑娘的丑事人尽皆知。”
[EN] The thug leaders were all pleased: "Rest assured, Young Master Chen. We'll definitely handle this to your satisfaction. In a month, we'll make sure the Zhu family daughter's scandal is known to everyone."
[ZH] 又一位头目为难道：“陈公子，谣言的内容太长，我等记不住，何况手下的兄弟？能不能再念念？让我们几个好好记记。”
[EN] Another leader said with difficulty: "Young Master Chen, the rumor content is too long. We can't memorize it, let alone the brothers under us. Could you read it again? Let us memorize it properly."
[ZH] “就知道你们几个笨，本公子都预备好了。”陈浩宇又挥挥手。只见两个小弟抬出一个木箱，放在地上，打开盖子，里面全是写好的纸张，“都在上面写着呢，你们只管拿，拿回去让你们手下的人都好好记记，实在记不住，大不了散播时，拿出来照着念。”
[EN] "I knew you idiots would have trouble. I've prepared everything." Chen Haoyu waved his hand again. Two underlings carried out a wooden box and placed it on the ground. Opening the lid, it was full of written papers. "It's all written here. Just take it. Have your people memorize it properly. If you really can't remember, just take it out and read it when spreading the rumors."
[ZH] 头目们夸道：“还是陈公子想得周到。”
[EN] The leaders praised: "Young Master Chen has thought of everything."
[ZH] 几位头目抬了木箱，向陈浩宇行礼后离开。
[EN] The leaders carried the wooden box, bowed to Chen Haoyu, and left.
[ZH] 这时一位小弟从外面回来，汇报道：“陈公子，练兵官此时正在春香楼。”
[EN] At this moment, an underling returned from outside and reported: "Young Master Chen, the training officer is at Chunxiang Brothel right now."
[ZH] 陈浩宇嘲笑道：“林觉和离后没人管束，去春香楼倒是勤快。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu mocked: "After Lin Jue's divorce, no one restrains him. He's been going to Chunxiang Brothel quite often."
[ZH] 小弟说：“公子说的不对，他没和离时，也挺勤快。”
[EN] The underling said: "You're wrong, Young Master. He was quite frequent even before the divorce."
[ZH] “勤快吗？我怎么没注意到？”

[ZH] “公子喜欢黄花大姑娘，基本没去过春香楼，可小的们常去，多次碰到练兵官，他还常常把春金巧带回林府。”
[EN] "Young Master likes young virgins and basically never goes to Chunxiang Brothel. But we go there often and have run into the training officer many times. He even often brings Chun Jinqiao back to the Lin residence."
