[ZH] “肯定生气，要不怎么会和离？”
[EN] "Definitely furious. Otherwise, why would they divorce?"
[ZH] 陈浩宇站起来：“跟我去春香楼会会林觉！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu stood up: "Come with me to Chunxiang Brothel to meet Lin Jue!"
[ZH] 春香楼是临安有名的商妓汇聚之地，一到晚上生意兴隆，有前来听曲儿看表演的，有寻找情感慰藉的，各色人等，来来往往。
[EN] Chunxiang Brothel was Lin'an's famous pleasure quarter, where courtesans gathered. At night, business thrived. Some came to listen to songs and watch performances, some sought emotional comfort, and all kinds of people came and went.

[EN] After the divorce, with no shrewish wife at home, Lin Jue could go wherever he wanted. He truly experienced the benefits of being unmarried.

[EN] The elders of the clan urged him to remarry quickly, but he was in no hurry. He wanted to win over Xiaguo first.

[EN] The reason he kept pursuing Xiaguo, besides her slight resemblance to Zhu Shuzhen, was more importantly that he didn't want Zhu Shuzhen to leave him so easily without any loss.
[ZH] 春金巧虽不是春香楼有名的商妓，也有几分姿色，她正在台上跟着几名商妓跳舞，作为她的老相好，林觉岂能不给面子？当然要好好饱饱眼福。
[EN] Although Chun Jinqiao wasn't a famous courtesan at Chunxiang Brothel, she had some charm. She was dancing on stage with several other courtesans. As her old flame, how could Lin Jue not show up? Of course he would enjoy the feast for his eyes.

[EN] Chen Haoyu brought his men into Chunxiang Brothel. An attendant came forward: "Young Master Chen honors us with his presence! This way, please!"

[EN] The madam of Chunxiang Brothel also came forward: "Oh my! Young Master Chen is a rare guest. Please, sit inside!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇被带到一个位置坐下，妈妈坐在他的旁边：“陈公子是来看节目，还是有要找的姑娘？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu was led to a seat. The madam sat beside him: "Is Young Master Chen here to watch the show, or is there a girl you're looking for?"

[EN] Chen Haoyu pointed at Lin Jue: "Neither. I'm here to find him."
[ZH] 妈妈看后，笑道：“既如此，陈公子忙，我就不在此打扰，有什么需要尽管给我说，无不满足。”
[EN] The madam looked and smiled: "In that case, Young Master Chen is busy. I won't disturb you. If you need anything, just let me know. I'll satisfy everything."

[ZH] 第二天，林觉从练兵署带上五六名士兵，直奔城东。
[EN] The next day, Lin Jue brought five or six soldiers from the Training Office and headed straight for the eastern part of the city.
[ZH] 虽说城东穷人居多，可也有富人在此建宅子，因而开辟出一处专属富人的区域。均是买了穷人原有居住之地，给他们一些银子，让他们搬走，再动工将原有的房子推倒，建成一幢幢供富人居住的宅子。
[EN] Though the eastern part of the city was mostly home to the poor, some rich people also built residences here. Thus, an area exclusively for the rich was opened up. They bought the land where the poor originally lived, gave them some silver to move away, then demolished the original houses and built residences for the rich.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷买的宅子就在其中，的确离陈家的宅子不远。
[EN] The residence Nanny Wei bought was among them, indeed not far from the Chen family's residence.
[ZH] 富人居住的区域很好找，林觉很快寻到，便到处找朱淑真买的宅子。昨日忘记问宅子的名称，他决定到陈家的宅子附近寻摸。
[EN] The rich area was easy to find. Lin Jue quickly located it and began looking for the residence Zhu Shuzhen bought. He forgot to ask the name of the residence yesterday, so he decided to search near the Chen family's residence.
[ZH] 陈家的宅子林觉还是清楚的，匾额上写着“永宁居”。他带人在永宁居附近寻摸着，功夫不负有心人，没寻多久，夏果从一个宅子出来。林觉一看，此宅名为“望月轩”。
[EN] Lin Jue was still familiar with the Chen family's residence. The plaque read “Yongning Residence.” He led his men to search near Yongning Residence. Hard work pays off. Before long, Xiaguo came out of a residence. Lin Jue looked — the residence was named “Wangyue Pavilion.”
[ZH] 夏果提着竹篮，准备去街上买菜，刚走出几步，一抬头，见林觉站在面前，五六名士兵堵在她身后。
[EN] Xiaguo carried a bamboo basket, preparing to go to the street to buy groceries. She had just taken a few steps when she looked up and saw Lin Jue standing in front of her, with five or six soldiers blocking behind her.
[ZH] “你……你要干什么？”夏果很害怕。
[EN] "What... what do you want to do?" Xiaguo was very scared.
[ZH] 林觉冷笑道：“干什么？你难道不知？知府大人已把你许给我，我现在就带你回林府。”
[EN] Lin Jue sneered: "What do I want to do? Don't you know? The prefect has already promised you to me. I'm taking you back to the Lin residence right now."
[ZH] “小姐若是知道会饶不了你！”
[EN] "If Young Lady finds out, she won't let you off!"
[ZH] “知道又如何？我把自己的小妾找回去，干她何事？”
[EN] "So what if she finds out? I'm bringing my own concubine back. What's it to her?"
[ZH] 夏果跪在地上：“请练兵官放过我吧？您人好，家世也不错，能找到比我好的，又何必揪住我不放？”
[EN] Xiaguo knelt on the ground: "Please let me go, Training Officer? You're a good person with a good family background. You can find someone better than me. Why insist on holding on to me?"
[ZH] “我就想要你，朱淑真越不同意，我越想要你，她若同意了，我反而觉得没趣。”
[EN] "I just want you. The more Zhu Shuzhen disagrees, the more I want you. If she agreed, I'd actually find it boring."
[ZH] 夏果认为林觉定是心里出现问题，今日是逃不脱魔掌，该如何是好？
[EN] Xiaguo thought Lin Jue must have something wrong with his mind. There was no escape today. What should she do?
[ZH] 还未想出好办法，听林觉向几位士兵命令道：“把她给我绑起来！送到林府！”
[EN] Before she could think of a good plan, she heard Lin Jue order the soldiers: "Tie her up! Take her to the Lin residence!"
[ZH] 两名士兵上前，掏出绳子，将夏果五花大绑，用布子塞了嘴巴，拽到马车上。
[EN] Two soldiers stepped forward, took out rope, trussed Xiaguo up, stuffed her mouth with cloth, and dragged her onto the carriage.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷安排在望月轩的一名小厮外出刚办完事回来，瞧见这一幕，赶紧躲在一边，等马车走后，急急忙忙跳上望月轩的马车，往朱府而去。
[EN] A servant boy arranged by Nanny Wei at Wangyue Pavilion had just returned from running errands outside. He saw this scene and quickly hid to the side. After the carriage left, he hastily jumped onto Wangyue Pavilion's carriage and headed for the Zhu residence.
[ZH] 千里寻用过早饭后，陪向夫人和韦嬷嬷闲聊，她很享受这种情意浓浓而又惬意的生活。
[EN] After breakfast, Qianlixun accompanied Madam Xiang and Nanny Wei in casual chat. She very much enjoyed this warm and comfortable life.
[ZH] 正聊着，银杏带小厮进来。
[EN] Just as they were chatting, Yinxing brought the servant boy in.
[ZH] 小厮急忙说：“姑娘，不好了，练兵官不知怎么知道夏果在望月轩，一大早带人把她绑走，我见状赶忙过来报信儿。”
[EN] The servant boy said hurriedly: "Young lady, it's bad. The Training Officer somehow found out Xiaguo is at Wangyue Pavilion. Early this morning, he brought people and tied her up. I saw this and hurried over to report."
[ZH] 千里寻“哗”地站起来：“欺人太甚！”说着就要去林家要人。
[EN] Qianlixun stood up abruptly: "They've gone too far!" Saying this, she was about to go to the Lin residence to demand Xiaguo back.

#CH 86
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷一把拉住姑娘：“犯不着为此生气，老爷本就将夏果许给练兵官，她既被绑走，也符合常理。”
[EN] Nanny Wei grabbed the young lady: "No need to get angry over this. The master already promised Xiaguo to the training officer. Since she was tied up and taken away, it's reasonable."
[ZH] 接着又批评小厮：“我当什么事，何必慌慌张张来报告姑娘？还不赶紧退下！”
[EN] Then she scolded the servant boy: "I thought it was something important. Why rush to report to the young lady in such a panic? Hurry up and leave!"
[ZH] 小厮本以为做了件好事，没承想被嬷嬷批评，赶紧退去。
[EN] The servant boy thought he had done a good deed, but didn't expect to be scolded by the nanny. He hurried away.
[ZH] 向夫人也说道：“嬷嬷说得对，不要管了，那是别人家的事，好不容易离开林家，又何必再上林家的门？如若你身边缺丫鬟，娘将银杏给你。”
[EN] Madam Xiang also said: "The nanny is right. Don't get involved. That's another family's business. You barely left the Lin family. Why go back to their door? If you need a maid, I'll give you Yinxing."
[ZH] “我身边不缺丫鬟，苦荞一人足矣。当时从陈浩宇手中救夏果，就是不想她被人欺负，现在却被林觉欺负，我岂能不管？”千里寻想起林觉要夏果主要因夏果的身段与她有几分相似，更觉厌恶，“不行！我得去看看，否则心里会不安。”
[EN] "I don't lack maids. Kuqiao is enough. When I saved Xiaguo from Chen Haoyu, it was because I didn't want her to be bullied. Now she's being bullied by Lin Jue. How can I not get involved?" Qianlixun remembered that Lin Jue wanted Xiaguo mainly because her figure was somewhat similar to hers, and felt even more disgusted. "No! I have to go see, or I won't be at ease."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷见阻拦不住，只好说：“既然姑娘想去，我陪你走一趟。”
[EN] Seeing she couldn't stop her, Nanny Wei had to say: "Since the young lady wants to go, I'll accompany you."
[ZH] 向夫人无奈地说：“罢了罢了，要去就去吧，你若把夏果带回来，还是赶紧把她打发走，放在身边净给你招惹麻烦。”
[EN] Madam Xiang said helplessly: "Fine, fine. If you want to go, go. But if you bring Xiaguo back, hurry up and send her away. Keeping her around will only cause you trouble."
[ZH] 千里寻在韦嬷嬷和苦荞的陪伴下，来到林府。
[EN] Accompanied by Nanny Wei and Kuqiao, Qianlixun arrived at the Lin residence.
[ZH] 下了马车，一进大门，不顾下人阻拦，轻车熟路往西院匆忙走去，半路上碰见春金巧迎面走来。
[EN] Getting off the carriage, she entered the main gate. Ignoring the servants' attempts to stop her, she hurried toward the west courtyard as if she knew the way well. Halfway there, she ran into Chun Jinqiao coming toward her.
[ZH] “吆！朱家姑娘来了，怎么？舍不得林府？”
[EN] "Oh! The Zhu family's young lady is here. What? Can't bear to leave the Lin residence?"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷不爽道：“哪有你问话的份儿？”
[EN] Nanny Wei said displeasedly: "What business is it of yours to ask?"
[ZH] 春金巧根本不把韦嬷嬷放在眼里：“我与你家姑娘说话，你搭什么茬？”
[EN] Chun Jinqiao didn't even bother to look at Nanny Wei: "I'm talking to your young lady. What are you butting in for?"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“你整日往林府跑，太阳都老高，才离开，也没见林觉娶你，甚至连个妾室都没混上，你不觉得很可悲吗？”
[EN] Qianlixun said: "You run to the Lin residence every day. The sun is already high when you leave. Lin Jue hasn't married you, or even made you a concubine. Don't you think that's pathetic?"
[ZH] 被戳到痛处，春金巧装作不在意的样子：“我不在乎这些，只要能跟练兵官在一起便好，他娶谁跟我都没关系。听说，他刚抓了一个丫鬟回来，准备纳她为妾，就关在你以前住的竹兰居。”
[EN] Hit where it hurt, Chun Jinqiao pretended not to care: "I don't care about that. As long as I can be with the training officer, it doesn't matter who he marries. I heard he just caught a maid and brought her back. He's planning to make her a concubine. She's locked up in the Zhulan Residence where you used to live."
[ZH] 春金巧说完，瞪了韦嬷嬷一眼，扭着腰肢离开。
[EN] After finishing, Chun Jinqiao glared at Nanny Wei and swayed her hips as she left.
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“装什么装，不在意又何必给我透露夏果关在哪里？”
[EN] Qianlixun laughed: "Why pretend? If you don't care, why tell me where Xiaguo is locked up?"
[ZH] 她们进入西院，直奔竹兰居。已有几个下人守在竹兰居门口，将千里寻挡住。
[EN] They entered the west courtyard and headed straight for Zhulan Residence. Several servants were already guarding the door, blocking Qianlixun.
[ZH] 千里寻说：“还敢拦老娘？怎么？想找打？”
[EN] Qianlixun said: "You dare block me? What? Looking for a beating?"
[ZH] 下人知道她的厉害，求道：“少夫人别为难我们几个，我们自知不是您的对手，可我们也得听主君的。”
[EN] The servants knew how formidable she was. They pleaded: "Young madam, don't make things difficult for us. We know we're no match for you, but we have to obey the master."
[ZH] 千里寻生气道：“不要再称呼我少夫人！既然知道老娘的厉害，还不赶紧闪开？否则我的拳头打的就是你们！”
[EN] Qianlixun said angrily: "Don't call me young madam anymore! Since you know how formidable I am, hurry up and get out of the way! Otherwise, my fists will be on you!"
[ZH] 几个下人乖乖让开。
[EN] The servants obediently stepped aside.
[ZH] 千里寻冲进去，见门紧紧关着，一脚踢开。
[EN] Qianlixun rushed in. Seeing the door tightly closed, she kicked it open.
[ZH] 夏果坐在地上，身上被绳子绑着，口里塞着布子，见小姐前来，努力挣扎，流下高兴而又伤心的泪水。
[EN] Xiaguo sat on the ground, tied up with rope, her mouth stuffed with cloth. Seeing her young lady come, she struggled hard, shedding tears of joy and sadness.
[ZH] 苦荞上前取掉夏果口中的布子，她“哇”地一声哭出声来：“呜呜……！我以为小姐以后不会再管我，呜呜……！小姐您终于来了！呜呜……！”
[EN] Kuqiao went forward and removed the cloth from Xiaguo's mouth. She burst into tears: "Wuwu...! I thought the young lady wouldn't care about me anymore. Wuwu...! Young lady, you finally came! Wuwu...!"
[ZH] 苦荞又帮夏果把身上的绳子解开。
[EN] Kuqiao helped Xiaguo untie the ropes from her body.
[ZH] 夏果跪在地上：“小姐救我！呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo knelt on the ground: "Young lady, save me! Wuwu...!"
[ZH] 千里寻生起怜悯之心：“放心，我这就带你走。”
[EN] Qianlixun felt pity: "Don't worry. I'll take you away now."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷在一旁不知该说些什么。
[EN] Nanny Wei stood aside, not knowing what to say.
[ZH] 门外传来林觉的声音：“想走，没那么容易！”
[EN] Lin Jue's voice came from outside the door: "Want to leave? It's not that easy!"
[ZH] 千里寻转过身，发现林觉带来许多士兵，他们已将院子团团围住。
[EN] Qianlixun turned around and found that Lin Jue had brought many soldiers. They had surrounded the courtyard.
[ZH] 林觉站在院中：“朱淑真，你现在已不是林府的人，跑到这里来抢我的人，我有很多理由可以把你抓起来！”
[EN] Lin Jue stood in the courtyard: "Zhu Shuzhen, you're no longer part of the Lin family. You come here to snatch my person. I have many reasons to arrest you!"
[ZH] 千里寻走出屋门：“林觉，你搞搞清楚，夏果是老娘的人，是你把老娘的人抢走，老娘还没找你算账，你却倒打一耙，真有你的。”
[EN] Qianlixun walked out of the room: "Lin Jue, get this straight. Xiaguo is my person. You snatched my person. I haven't settled accounts with you yet, and you turn around and accuse me. You've got some nerve."
[ZH] “知府大人已答应将她许给我，她就是我的人。”
[EN] "The prefect has already promised her to me. She's my person."
[ZH] “哼！有什么证据？我爹有没有给你签字按手印？有没有把夏果的卖身契给你？都没有！告诉你，夏果的卖身契在我手中，她是老娘的人，老娘没同意把她给你，你凭什么强抢民女？”
[EN] "Hmph! What proof? Did my father sign and give you a deed? Did he give you Xiaguo's deed of sale? No! Let me tell you, Xiaguo's deed of sale is in my hands. She's my person. I didn't agree to give her to you. What right do you have to forcibly take a civilian woman?"
[ZH] 林觉被怼得哑口无言：“那日，知府大人红口白牙允诺我，你也在场，难道想反悔？”
[EN] Lin Jue was rendered speechless: "That day, the prefect promised me in plain words. You were there too. Are you trying to go back on your word?"
[ZH] “告诉你，老娘那日什么都没听到，何谈反悔？”千里寻瞅瞅围着的士兵，“你今日竟然把临安士兵带来私用，等我回去在我爹面前告你一状，我爹虽不管临安军政，但他与新来的节度使关系要好，绝对会将此事报告节度使大人。也不怕告诉你，我跟节度使的关系也不错，他要是知道你私用士兵强抢民女，定然不会饶恕！”
[EN] "Let me tell you, I didn't hear anything that day. How can I go back on my word?" Qianlixun looked at the surrounding soldiers. "You actually brought Lin'an soldiers here for personal use. Wait until I go back and report you to my father. My father doesn't manage Lin'an's military affairs, but he has a good relationship with the new military commissioner. He will definitely report this to the commissioner. I'm not afraid to tell you, I also have a good relationship with the commissioner. If he knows you're using soldiers for personal affairs and forcibly taking a civilian woman, he definitely won't forgive you!"
[ZH] 林觉还真害怕起来：“此等小事，又何必对知府大人提起，更不必打扰节度使大人。”
[EN] Lin Jue actually became afraid: "This is such a small matter. Why mention it to the prefect? Even less need to disturb the military commissioner."
[ZH] 千里寻见对方害怕，带着众人打算离开。
[EN] Seeing the other party was afraid, Qianlixun led the group and prepared to leave.
[ZH] 林觉又堵住：“你可以走，把夏果留下。”
[EN] Lin Jue blocked them again: "You can leave, but leave Xiaguo behind."
[ZH] “老娘的话你是不是没听明白？夏果是我的人，你抢是没用的，不如好好想想重新娶妻之事，不要盯着老娘的人不放，再有下次，老娘的拳头绝不轻饶。”千里寻又看看周围的士兵，“即使你带这么多人来，老娘打他们也不在话下。”
[EN] "Didn't you understand what I said? Xiaguo is my person. It's no use you snatching her. You'd better think about remarrying. Don't keep eyeing my person. If there's a next time, my fists won't let you off easily." Qianlixun looked at the surrounding soldiers again. "Even if you bring so many people, I can still beat them."
[ZH] 朱淑真用节度使吓唬他，真的动起拳头来，又打不过，林觉只能让开，眼睁睁看着朱淑真将夏果从眼前带走，既不舍，又憋屈。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen used the military commissioner to scare him. If it came to fists, he couldn't beat her. Lin Jue could only step aside and watch helplessly as Zhu Shuzhen took Xiaguo away from right in front of him. He was reluctant but also frustrated.
[ZH] 夏果坐在马车里一直哭，苦荞哄她半天不管用，干脆不再理会。
[EN] Xiaguo kept crying in the carriage. Kuqiao tried to comfort her for a long time but it didn't work, so she just ignored her.
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷很不满：“哭什么哭！遇到姑娘这样的主子，你就偷着乐吧，要不是姑娘，你早都被陈公子糟蹋了。”
[EN] Nanny Wei was very dissatisfied: "What are you crying for? You should be happy to have a young lady like this as your master. If it weren't for the young lady, you would have been ruined by Young Master Chen long ago."
[ZH] 夏果哭得更厉害：“我怎么这么命苦！呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo cried even harder: "Why is my fate so bitter? Wuwu...!"
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷说：“这女子，有些人长得好是好事，有些人长得好不见得是好事。”
[EN] Nanny Wei said: "This girl, for some people being good-looking is a good thing. For others, being good-looking isn't necessarily a good thing."
[ZH] 夏果继续哭着：“我也不想长这样，可爹娘生下我就这样，难道是我的错？呜呜……！”
[EN] Xiaguo continued crying: "I didn't want to look like this either. But my parents gave birth to me looking like this. Is it my fault? Wuwu...!"
[ZH] 千里寻说：“我看林觉对你还未死心，他必然会找我爹，你还是先别回朱府，继续住在新宅子，以后尽量别出来，采买之事吩咐其他人即可。”
[EN] Qianlixun said: "I think Lin Jue hasn't given up on you. He will definitely look for my father. You'd better not return to the Zhu residence for now. Continue living in the new residence. Try not to go out in the future. For shopping, just have others do it."
[ZH] 夏果哭着点点头。
[EN] Xiaguo nodded while crying.
[ZH] 先把夏果送回望月轩，马车继续回往朱府。
[EN] First, they sent Xiaguo back to Wangyue Pavilion. The carriage continued back to the Zhu residence.
[ZH] 到达朱府门口，刚走下马车，南双迎面走来：“真是巧，我刚到，就在门口碰到幽栖居士。”
[EN] Arriving at the Zhu residence gate, they had just gotten off the carriage when Nanshuang came toward them: "What a coincidence. I just arrived and ran into Youqi Jushi at the gate."
[ZH] 南双前来，肯定是文渊有事要转达，千里寻自然开心：“文渊找我有事？”
[EN] Nanshuang coming here definitely meant Wenyuan had something to convey. Qianlixun was naturally happy: "Does Wenyuan have something for me?"
[ZH] “我家公子邀请姑娘明日去幽林别居，东方公子、曹公子和安公子都过去。”
[EN] "Young Master invites the young lady to Youlin Villa tomorrow. Young Master Dongfang, Young Master Cao, and Young Master An will all be there."
[ZH] “难道又要举行诗会？”
[EN] "Are they holding another poetry gathering?"
[ZH] “是不是诗会我不大清楚，反正公子让我前来传话，邀请姑娘前去。”
[EN] "I'm not sure if it's a poetry gathering. Young Master just asked me to come and deliver the message, inviting the young lady."
[ZH] “告诉文渊，明日不见不散。”
[EN] "Tell Wenyuan, I'll be there tomorrow, rain or shine."
[ZH] 南双说完，行了礼，目送三人往朱府走，趁其他人不注意，上前拉住苦荞，使了个眼色。
[EN] After Nanshuang finished speaking, he bowed and watched the three walk toward the Zhu residence. Taking advantage of the others' inattention, he went forward, pulled Kuqiao, and gave her a look.
[ZH] 苦荞领会，悄悄跟着他走到侧门的一边，问道：“有什么事？”
[EN] Kuqiao understood and quietly followed him to one side of the side gate. She asked: "What's the matter?"
[ZH] “泥人的事。”
[EN] "It's about the clay figurine."
[ZH] “怎么了？”
[EN] "What about it?"
[ZH] “我把泥人帮你还给白风，他看上去一点儿都不高兴，我怀疑他喜欢上你。”
[EN] "I helped you return the clay figurine to Baifeng. He didn't look happy at all. I suspect he likes you."
[ZH] “什么？喜欢我？”
[EN] "What? Likes me?"
[ZH] 南双点点头：“就像我家公子喜欢你家姑娘一样。”
[EN] Nanshuang nodded: "Just like my young master likes your young lady."
[ZH] 苦荞一阵脸红：“别胡说，他怎么可能喜欢我？我才刚和他认识。”
[EN] Kuqiao blushed: "Don't talk nonsense. How could he like me? I just met him."
[ZH] “苦荞姐姐难道没听过一个词？”
[EN] "Does Sister Kuqiao not know a word?"
[ZH] “什么？”
[EN] "What?"
[ZH] “一见钟情。”
[EN] "Love at first sight."
[ZH] “你年纪不大，懂得倒挺多，赶紧回去吧，小姐一会儿找不到我，会着急。”
[EN] "You're not old, but you know a lot. Hurry back. If the young lady can't find me in a while, she'll worry."
[ZH] 苦荞红着脸进了朱府，追上小姐与韦嬷嬷。
[EN] Kuqiao entered the Zhu residence with a red face and caught up with the young lady and Nanny Wei.
[ZH] 千里寻早已注意到苦荞被南双拉走，又瞧她的脸色通红，问道：“你的脸怎么那么红？南双对你说了什么？”
[EN] Qianlixun had already noticed Kuqiao being pulled away by Nanshuang. Seeing her red face, she asked: "Why is your face so red? What did Nanshuang say to you?"
[ZH] 苦荞不好意思道：“没说什么，我脸红是因追小姐走得太快，热的。”
[EN] Kuqiao said embarrassedly: "Nothing. My face is red because I walked too fast chasing the young lady. It's from the heat."
[ZH] 韦嬷嬷的心思不在苦荞身上，还在纠结夏果之事：“姑娘，要是练兵官找到老爷，说起今日之事，恐怕老爷会生气，姑娘有没有想到应对之策？”
[EN] Nanny Wei's mind wasn't on Kuqiao. She was still worried about Xiaguo's matter: "Young lady, if the training officer finds the master and talks about today's matter, I'm afraid the master will be angry. Have you thought of a solution?"
[ZH] 千里寻想想说：“嬷嬷放心，一路上我已想好，我爹只是口头答应，他完全可以否认，只要他否认，此事便万事大吉。”
[EN] Qianlixun thought and said: "Nanny, don't worry. I've already thought it through on the way. My father only promised verbally. He can completely deny it. As long as he denies it, everything will be fine."
[ZH] “可老爷是知府，一诺千金，况且当时两家的族人都在，岂可随意否认？”
[EN] "But the master is the prefect. A promise is worth a thousand gold. Besides, both families' clansmen were there at the time. How can he deny it at will?"
[ZH] “难就难在这里，不想了，走一步看一步，不行我就去求节度使大人帮忙，我看林觉即使敢得罪我爹，也不敢得罪临安节度使。”
[EN] "That's the difficult part. Forget it. We'll take it one step at a time. If it doesn't work, I'll go ask the military commissioner for help. I think even if Lin Jue dares to offend my father, he won't dare offend the Lin'an military commissioner."
[ZH] “只怕节度使大人不愿理会这等小事。”
[EN] "I'm afraid the military commissioner won't want to bother with such a small matter."
[ZH] 千里寻不能确定仪王是否答应帮她，不管他能否答应，明日去幽林别居还是要试上一试。
[EN] Qianlixun wasn't sure if the Prince of Yi would agree to help her. Whether he could agree or not, she still had to try tomorrow when she went to Youlin Villa.

#CH 87
[ZH] 朱晞颜从府衙回来，一直黑着脸，派儿子朱景元把淑真找来。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan returned from the prefect's office with a dark expression. He sent his son Zhu Jingyuan to fetch Shuzhen.
[ZH] 当朱景元来到小院时，千里寻正在写话本。
[EN] When Zhu Jingyuan arrived at the small courtyard, Qianlixun was writing story scripts.
[ZH] 有了仪王这等忠实粉丝，创作起来更有动力，用下笔如有神形容一点儿都不为过。
[EN] With such a loyal fan as the Prince of Yi, she felt more motivated to create. Saying her pen moved as if by magic was no exaggeration at all.
[ZH] 尽管是把以前写过的内容再写一遍，可小说不比诗，太长，不可能记住每句话。还好人物和重要情节都记得，只需重新组织语言，按照话本的格式要求来写即可。
[EN] Even though she was rewriting content she had written before, novels were different from poems — they were too long to remember every sentence. Fortunately, she still remembered the characters and important plot points. She only needed to reorganize the language and write according to the format requirements of story scripts.
[ZH] 正写得投入，哥哥前来。自从和离后，哥哥整日出去忙，都没时间与他一起聊天儿。她也没见过嫂嫂和莱宝，朱府太大，大得一家子人有时都难以碰面。
[EN] She was deeply engrossed in writing when her brother came. Ever since the divorce, her brother had been busy going out all day, with no time to chat with her. She hadn't seen her sister-in-law or Laibao either. The Zhu residence was so large that sometimes even family members couldn't meet.
[ZH] 千里寻撂下笔，上前主动抓起哥哥的手：“哥哥，我还说什么时候去你的院子找你和嫂嫂聊天儿，顺便看看莱宝，没想到，你倒来了。”
[EN] Qianlixun put down her brush, stepped forward, and took her brother's hand: "Brother, I was just wondering when to visit your courtyard to chat with you and sister-in-law, and see Laibao. I didn't expect you to come here."
[ZH] 朱景元开玩笑道：“你哪有时间看我们？你现在心里眼里只有乔文仙，忙着跟他约会，别以为我不知道。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan joked: “When do you have time for us? Now your heart and eyes are only on Qiao Wenxian. You're busy dating him — don't think I don't know.”
[ZH] “哥哥别打趣我，要不现在我就跟你去看嫂嫂和莱宝？”说着就要拉朱景元出园门。
[EN] "Don't tease me, brother. How about I go with you now to see sister-in-law and Laibao?" With that, she tried to pull Zhu Jingyuan out of the garden gate.
[ZH] 朱景元拽住妹妹：“现在不行，爹让我过来找你。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan held his sister back: "Not now. Father asked me to find you."
[ZH] “爹找我？”千里寻猜测，定是林觉恶人先告状，又到知府大人面前哭诉，“爹找我什么事？”
[EN] "Father wants to see me?" Qianlixun guessed that Lin Jue must have filed a complaint first, crying to the prefect. "What does Father want with me?"
[ZH] “我不知。”朱景元摇摇头，“不过，看着不是好事，爹的脸黑着呢！”
[EN] "I don't know." Zhu Jingyuan shook his head. "But it doesn't look good. Father's face is dark!"
[ZH] “那我还是不去了，哥哥只管对爹说我不在。”千里寻坐回桌前，提笔又要写。
[EN] "Then I'd better not go. Just tell Father I'm not here." Qianlixun sat back at the desk, picked up her brush, and was about to write again.
[ZH] 朱景元过去把她手中的笔取下，放好，因担心父亲批评妹妹，并未注意纸上写的内容：“逃避解决不了问题，还是去吧。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan went over, took the brush from her hand, and put it away. Worried that Father would scold his sister, he didn't pay attention to what was written on the paper: "Running away won't solve the problem. You'd better go."
[ZH] 千里寻把写好的纸张迅速反扣过来：“好吧，我听哥哥的。”
[EN] Qianlixun quickly turned the written paper face down: "Alright, I'll listen to you, brother."
[ZH] 反扣纸的动作反而引起朱景元好奇：“写的是什么？还怕哥哥看到？该不会是写给乔文仙的情书？”
[EN] The act of turning the paper over aroused Zhu Jingyuan's curiosity: "What are you writing? Afraid your brother will see it? It's not a love letter to Qiao Wenxian, is it?"
[ZH] “哥哥别瞎猜，我们走吧。”
[EN] "Don't guess wildly, brother. Let's go."
[ZH] 千里寻拉起朱景元往外走，苦荞拿丝帕将纸张盖好，快走几步跟上小姐。
[EN] Qianlixun pulled Zhu Jingyuan and walked out. Kuqiao covered the paper with a silk handkerchief and hurried a few steps to catch up with her young lady.
[ZH] 朱晞颜已换上居家的便装，坐在榻上品茶，下人进来报告：“老爷，小姐和公子在外面等候。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan had changed into casual home clothes and was sitting on a couch tasting tea. A servant came in to report: "Master, the young lady and young master are waiting outside."
[ZH] “让他们进来吧。”朱晞颜放下茶盏。
[EN] "Let them in." Zhu Xiyan put down his tea cup.
[ZH] 千里寻跟哥哥一前一后进入，两人拜见过父亲，得到允许，坐在一旁的椅子上。
[EN] Qianlixun followed her brother in, one behind the other. The two paid their respects to their father and, with his permission, sat on chairs to the side.
[ZH] 朱晞颜问：“你可知我找你来所为何事？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan asked: "Do you know why I called you here?"
[ZH] “女儿不知。”
[EN] "Your daughter doesn't know."
[ZH] “不知？好好想想今日你都做了哪些出格的事！”
[EN] "Don't know? Think carefully about what outrageous things you've done today!"
[ZH] “女儿今日做事均是依理而行，哪做过什么出格之事？”
[EN] "Your daughter acted according to reason today. What outrageous things have I done?"
[ZH] 只听“啪”的一声，朱晞颜拍了一下榻上的木桌，没吓着淑真，反而把自己的手拍得生疼。朱晞颜忍着疼，厉声说：“你冲进林家把林觉的小妾抢走，还有脸说依理而行？”
[EN] With a loud slap, Zhu Xiyan hit the wooden table on the couch. It didn't scare Shuzhen — instead, it made his hand hurt. Enduring the pain, Zhu Xiyan said sternly: “You rushed into the Lin residence and snatched Lin Jue's concubine. And you still have the nerve to say you acted according to reason？”
[ZH] 朱景元被父亲吓住，也替妹妹担忧。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan was frightened by his father and also worried about his sister.
[ZH] 千里寻根本不怕：“爹，您怎么那么喜欢听林觉一面之词？您是口头允诺过把夏果送给林觉当妾，可夏果的卖身契在我手中，她以后跟谁，由我说了算，夏果不愿意，我更不愿意，林觉就是强抢民女！您都不知道，我到林府时，夏果被五花大绑，嘴巴里还塞了块布，这是好人能做出来的事吗？他那样对待夏果，我怎么敢把夏果许给他？”
[EN] Qianlixun wasn't afraid at all: “Father, why do you always listen to one side of the story from Lin Jue? You did verbally promise to give Xiaguo to Lin Jue as a concubine, but Xiaguo's deed of sale is in my hands. Who she follows from now on is up to me. Xiaguo doesn't want to, and I don't want to either. Lin Jue is abducting an innocent woman! You don't even know — when I arrived at the Lin residence, Xiaguo was trussed up with rope, her mouth stuffed with cloth. Is that something a good person would do? The way he treated Xiaguo, how could I dare to give Xiaguo to him？”
[ZH] 朱晞颜继续严厉地说道：“你对你的丫鬟好，我不拦着，可那日和离时，我已当众人的面答应林觉把夏果给他，难道你想让我食言不成？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan continued sternly: "I won't stop you from being good to your maid. But that day during the divorce, I promised Lin Jue in front of everyone that I would give Xiaguo to him. Are you trying to make me break my word?"
[ZH] “爹，您说，夏果的幸福重要还是您的面子重要？”
[EN] “Father, tell me — is Xiaguo's happiness more important, or is your face more important？”
[ZH] 朱晞颜又被气到：“你问这话是什么意思？难道我作为一家之主，临安知府，在你眼里还不如一个丫鬟？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan was angered again: "What do you mean by that question? Am I, as the head of the family and the prefect of Lin'an, worth less than a maid in your eyes?"
[ZH] 朱景元见状帮忙解释：“爹，淑真不是这个意思，她就是太善良，不愿见身边的丫鬟受委屈。”
[EN] Seeing this, Zhu Jingyuan helped explain: "Father, Shuzhen doesn't mean that. She's just too kind and doesn't want to see her maid wronged."
[ZH] “难道我这个爹在她眼中连个丫鬟都不如？为了一个丫鬟顶撞我，还想让我食言！我……我……”朱晞颜抓起茶盏扔在地上。
[EN] "Am I, her father, worth less than a maid in her eyes? For the sake of a maid, she talks back to me and wants me to break my word! I... I..." Zhu Xiyan grabbed his tea cup and threw it on the ground.
[ZH] 只见茶盏被摔碎，茶水洒在地上，一旁的丫鬟吓得不敢上前收拾。
[EN] The tea cup shattered, tea splashing on the ground. The maids nearby were so frightened they didn't dare step forward to clean it up.
[ZH] 千里寻见状，倒也不怕。小时候，父亲一生气，她总会抱着父亲的脖子，再亲亲他的脸蛋，他会立刻气焰全消，不如也这么对付朱淑真的爹。
[EN] Seeing this, Qianlixun wasn't afraid. When she was little, whenever her father got angry, she would hug his neck and kiss his cheek. His anger would vanish immediately. She might as well try the same thing with Zhu Shuzhen's father.
[ZH] 她起身，走到朱晞颜跟前，抱住他，撒娇道：“爹，您这么生气干嘛？人家不就是说错话，爹当然重要，爹的面子和朱家的面子都重要。”说着在朱晞颜脸上亲了一口。
[EN] She stood up, walked to Zhu Xiyan, hugged him, and coaxed: "Father, why are you so angry? I just said the wrong thing. Of course Father is important. Father's face and the Zhu family's face are both important." With that, she kissed Zhu Xiyan on the cheek.
[ZH] 朱晞颜从未被女儿如此对待过，很不习惯，在心中对自己说：“她不是以前的淑真。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan had never been treated this way by his daughter before. He felt very unaccustomed and told himself: "She's not the Shuzhen from before."
[ZH] 不管如何，女儿突如其来亲密的举动，令朱晞颜怒气顿消：“从哪里学来这一套，还不赶紧坐回去。”
[EN] Regardless, his daughter's sudden intimate gesture made Zhu Xiyan's anger vanish on the spot: "Where did you learn this? Hurry up and sit back down."
[ZH] “不嘛，爹不生气了，我就坐回去。”千里寻继续抱着朱晞颜。
[EN] "No, I'll sit back down only when Father isn't angry anymore." Qianlixun continued hugging Zhu Xiyan.
[ZH] 朱晞颜装作不高兴的样子：“你放开我，我就不生气。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan pretended to be unhappy: "Let go of me, and I won't be angry."
[ZH] 千里寻放开朱晞颜，扭头向一旁的哥哥挤了挤眼睛。
[EN] Qianlixun let go of Zhu Xiyan and turned to wink at her brother to the side.
[ZH] 朱景元正在想着妹妹的变化，同时佩服变化后的妹妹实在不一般，竟然使出以柔克刚的本事，哪个男人能招架得住？何况还是亲生父亲。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan was thinking about his sister's change and admiring how extraordinary the changed sister was. She actually used the technique of overcoming the hard with the soft. What man could resist that? Especially her own father.
[ZH] 接收到妹妹的眼神信息，朱景元立刻说：“爹，我们可是一家人，您不能向着外人说话，林觉以前是朱家女婿，现在跟我们没什么关系，您没必要为他跟自己的亲生女儿置气。”
[EN] Receiving his sister's signal, Zhu Jingyuan immediately said: "Father, we're a family. You can't side with outsiders. Lin Jue used to be the Zhu family's son-in-law, but now he has nothing to do with us. There's no need for you to be angry with your own daughter for his sake."
[ZH] 千里寻瞅着父亲看似不高兴，但已舒展许多的表情说道：“哥哥说的很对，且不说林觉不再是朱家的女婿，就他那些做派，虽算不上坏人，也不是什么好东西，您又何必向着他。应该向着女儿说话，您要是有个头疼脑热的，伺候床前的可是我与哥哥，林觉才不会理这些。”
[EN] Looking at her father's expression, which seemed unhappy but had relaxed considerably, Qianlixun said: “Brother is right. Not to mention Lin Jue is no longer the Zhu family's son-in-law — just his behavior, though not exactly a bad person, isn't anything good either. Why side with him? You should side with your daughter. If you fall ill, it's your daughter and son who will take care of you by your bedside. Lin Jue won't care about any of that.”
[ZH] “哼！我要是卧床不起，可指望不上你。”朱晞颜的语气缓和许多。
[EN] "Hmph! If I'm bedridden, I can't count on you." Zhu Xiyan's tone had softened considerably.
[ZH] 千里寻强调道：“怎么指望不上？我可是您的亲女儿，我定会尽心尽力照顾爹。”
[EN] Qianlixun emphasized: "How can you not count on me? I'm your own daughter. I'll definitely take care of Father with all my heart."
[ZH] 朱晞颜终于露出少有的笑容：“可我毕竟当众答应林觉，你让我如何自处？”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan finally showed a rare smile: "But after all, I promised Lin Jue in public. How do you expect me to save face?"
[ZH] 父亲露出笑容，千里寻来了精神，脑袋变得更加灵活：“爹不用担心，女儿已替您想好。林觉再找您，您在他面前想怎么骂女儿都行，回到家该干嘛干嘛，余下来的事交给女儿处理。不仅保住您知府大人一诺千金的形象，也不至于搞得我们父女之间关系紧张。”
[EN] Seeing her father smile, Qianlixun became spirited, her mind even more agile: "Father doesn't need to worry. Your daughter has already thought it through. When Lin Jue comes to you again, you can scold your daughter however you want in front of him. When you get home, just go about your business. Leave the rest to me. This way, not only will you maintain your image as a prefect whose promise is worth a thousand gold, but it also won't cause tension between us father and daughter."
[ZH] 朱晞颜想了想，批评道：“就你鬼主意多，去吧，别在这里烦我，看见你就来气。”
[EN] Zhu Xiyan thought for a moment and scolded: "You're full of tricky ideas. Go, don't bother me here. Just seeing you makes me angry."
[ZH] 千里寻还想问爹到底同意不同意，被朱景元拉住：“爹，我跟妹妹走了，您好好休息。”
[EN] Qianlixun still wanted to ask if Father agreed or not, but Zhu Jingyuan pulled her: "Father, my sister and I are leaving. Get some good rest."
[ZH] 被哥哥拉着，千里寻边往出走边问：“爹，您到底同意不同意？”
[EN] Being pulled by her brother, Qianlixun asked as she walked out: "Father, do you agree or not?"
[ZH] 朱晞颜根本未理会。
[EN] Zhu Xiyan didn't pay her any attention.
[ZH] 离开父亲的院子，千里寻甩开哥哥的手：“哥，你拉我干什么？父亲还没答应。”
[EN] Leaving her father's courtyard, Qianlixun shook off her brother's hand: "Brother, why did you pull me? Father hasn't agreed yet."
[ZH] “妹妹刚才挺聪明，怎么这会儿又傻了？爹已答应，不用再问。”
[EN] "You were so smart just now, why are you being silly now? Father has already agreed. No need to ask again."
[ZH] “什么？爹答应了？可他并没说出答应的话，只顾批评我。”
[EN] "What? Father agreed? But he didn't say anything agreeing. He just kept scolding me."
[ZH] “你说的主意，显然是让爹跟你一起欺骗林觉，爹是何等人物？怎能跟你干那勾当？干也不能直说，他不再发怒，让你走，不是答应又是什么？”
[EN] “The idea you proposed is obviously to have Father deceive Lin Jue together with you. What kind of person is Father? How could he engage in such tricks with you? Even if he does it, he can't say it directly. He stopped being angry and let you leave — isn't that agreement？”
[ZH] 千里寻仔细想想，好像是这么回事：“难怪这官不是人人当得，脑子又复杂，说话又隐晦，把这一套都用到亲女儿身上，还好有你这么聪明的哥哥，否则我今日非逼爹给我说句明白话不可。”
[EN] Qianlixun thought carefully and realized it seemed to be the case: "No wonder not everyone can be an official. Their minds are so complex, their words so implicit. They use all this on their own daughter. Fortunately, I have such a smart brother. Otherwise, I would have forced Father to give me a clear answer today."
[ZH] 朱景元说：“你哥哥我虽不是官，可经常跟当官的打交道，见得多，自然明白。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan said: "Though your brother isn't an official, I often deal with officials. I've seen a lot, so naturally I understand."
[ZH] 见父亲之事算是有惊无险，顺利过关，千里寻一高兴，不想回小院：“哥哥，我要去你的院子，跟嫂嫂聊聊天儿，再看看莱宝。”
[EN] Seeing that the matter with Father had passed safely without incident, Qianlixun was happy and didn't want to return to the small courtyard: "Brother, I want to go to your courtyard, chat with sister-in-law, and see Laibao."
[ZH] “想去就跟我走。”
[EN] "If you want to go, come with me."
[ZH] “哥哥，稍等会儿。”千里寻把苦荞拉到一旁，小声说，“你不用跟着，回去把我写的新书稿送到说话艺人那里。”
[EN] "Brother, wait a moment." Qianlixun pulled Kuqiao aside and whispered: "You don't need to follow. Go back and deliver the new story scripts I wrote to the storyteller."
[ZH] 苦荞点头答应着，等小姐跟公子离开，她才往小院走去。
[EN] Kuqiao nodded in agreement. After her young lady and young master left, she headed toward the small courtyard.

#CH 88
[ZH] 来到哥哥居住的院子，一进院门，听到莱宝咿咿呀呀的声音，原来陈文心正与丫鬟在院中逗莱宝。莱宝坐在小木床上一边流着哈喇子，一边笑。
[EN] Arriving at her brother's courtyard, as soon as she entered the gate, she heard Laibao babbling. It turned out Chen Wenxin was playing with the baby in the courtyard with a maid. Laibao sat in a small wooden crib, drooling and laughing at the same time.
[ZH] 丫鬟用巾帕给莱宝擦着嘴角的口水，另一只手摇着拨浪鼓，叮叮咚咚。
[EN] The maid wiped the drool from the corners of Laibao's mouth with a handkerchief, while her other hand shook a pellet drum — ding-ding, dong-dong.
[ZH] 陈文心见淑真前来，迎上去：“我还以为你自和离后瞧不上我与你哥，不愿来我们的院子。”
[EN] Seeing Shuzhen come, Chen Wenxin welcomed her: "I thought after the divorce, you'd look down on your brother and me and wouldn't want to visit our courtyard."
[ZH] 千里寻打趣道：“嫂嫂这话说得奇怪，和离与我瞧不上你与哥哥有什么关系？”
[EN] Qianlixun teased: "Sister-in-law, that's a strange thing to say. What does the divorce have to do with looking down on you and brother?"
[ZH] 陈文心解释道：“你和离不了需要用我们，才会来找我与你哥帮忙，现在顺利和离，用不上我们两个，自然不会来。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin explained: "When you couldn't get a divorce, you needed us, so you'd come to your brother and me for help. Now that you've successfully divorced, you don't need us anymore, so naturally you won't come."
[ZH] 千里寻想起苦荞曾说这位嫂嫂嘴是刻薄了些，果然今日说话让人不舒服。
[EN] Qianlixun remembered Kuqiao once saying this sister-in-law had a sharp tongue. Sure enough, her words today made people uncomfortable.
[ZH] 不过千里寻觉得嫂嫂人并不坏，也就不与她计较：“我这不是来了？说明嫂嫂太多心。即使我不来，也是因有事绊住，绝不是嫂嫂说的那样。”
[EN] But Qianlixun felt her sister-in-law wasn't a bad person, so she didn't take it to heart: "Here I am now, aren't I? That shows you're overthinking, sister-in-law. Even if I hadn't come, it would be because something kept me busy, not what you said."
[ZH] “不是便好。”陈文心拉起淑真，“来，跟我坐到那边，聊会儿天儿。”
[EN] "Good then." Chen Wenxin took Shuzhen's hand. "Come, sit over there with me and chat for a while."
[ZH] 她们来到对面的树下，那里摆着桌椅和茶点，两人笑着坐下。
[EN] They went to a tree on the opposite side, where a table, chairs, and tea snacks were set out. The two sat down smiling.
[ZH] “听说你前日与乔家公子一起逛西湖。”
[EN] "I heard you toured West Lake with Young Master Qiao the other day."
[ZH] “嫂嫂是听娘说的？”
[EN] "Did you hear it from Mother?"
[ZH] “婆婆倒没告诉我，我是听浩宇讲的。”
[EN] "Mother-in-law didn't tell me. I heard it from Haoyu."
[ZH] 千里寻不再言语，听到陈浩宇的名字她都会觉得浑身不舒服。
[EN] Qianlixun fell silent. Just hearing Chen Haoyu's name made her feel uncomfortable all over.
[ZH] 朱景元逗完莱宝说：“文心，我进屋陪会儿小舅子，你跟淑真在这里聊。”
[EN] After playing with Laibao, Zhu Jingyuan said: "Wenxin, I'll go inside and keep my brother-in-law company for a while. You and Shuzhen can chat here."
[ZH] 陈文心很纳闷：“真是神了，我还没说，你怎么知道浩宇来了？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin was puzzled: “It's uncanny! I haven't said anything — how do you know Haoyu is here？”
[ZH] 朱景元笑道：“还用你说？他上次来时，说要给莱宝送一副银镯子，我一早走时还没见，现在却戴着，不是他来又能是谁？”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan laughed: “Do I need you to tell me? Last time he came, he said he'd give Laibao a silver bracelet. When I left this morning, it wasn't there yet. Now it's on — if it wasn't him, who else could it be？”
[ZH] 陈文心继续疑惑：“说不准他走了呢？”
[EN] Chen Wenxin remained puzzled: "Maybe he's already left?"
[ZH] 朱景元说：“他每次这个事后来，都要到屋里观赏我养的几条金鱼，不观赏一会儿绝不走。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan said: "Every time he comes for this, he always goes inside to admire the goldfish I raise. He won't leave until he's admired them for a while."
[ZH] “你倒了解他，他的确来了，在屋里看你养的金鱼。”陈文心说完，面向淑真，“我们别理他们，聊我们的。”
[EN] "You sure know him well. He is here, admiring your goldfish inside." Chen Wenxin finished, then turned to Shuzhen. "Let's ignore them and chat about our things."
[ZH] 听到陈浩宇在此，千里寻皱起眉头，想道：“老娘今日心情挺好，陈浩宇偏偏又出现，搞得老娘想要冲进去把他揪出来打一顿，报落水之仇！”
[EN] Hearing that Chen Haoyu was here, Qianlixun frowned and thought: “I was in a good mood today, and Chen Haoyu just had to show up. It makes me want to storm in, drag him out, and give him a beating — revenge for falling in the water！”
[ZH] “妹妹，你怎么了？看上去不高兴，该不会我刚才说的话伤到你，你正生我的气？”
[EN] "Sister, what's wrong? You look unhappy. Did my words just now hurt you? Are you angry with me?"
[ZH] “没……怎么会生嫂嫂的气？是生你弟弟的气。我逛西湖时，他故意撞我乘坐的船，我没站稳，落入水中，全身湿透，跟个落汤鸡似的。”
[EN] “No... how could I be angry with sister-in-law? I'm angry with your brother. When I was touring West Lake, he deliberately rammed my boat. I lost my balance and fell into the water, soaked from head to toe — like a drowned rat.”
[ZH] “这孩子，太顽劣了些，改日我回娘家，给父亲好好说说，教训教训他。”
[EN] "That boy is too wild. Someday when I visit my parents, I'll have a serious talk with Father and teach him a lesson."
[ZH] “嫂嫂怎能认为他是顽劣？我两次见他欺负良家妇女，我身边的丫鬟夏果就是从你那弟弟手中救出的。”
[EN] "How can you call it just wild behavior? Twice I've seen him bully decent women. My maid Xiaguo was rescued right from your brother's hands."
[ZH] “太不像话！我爹娘见他那样也是又训又骂，可又不能时时刻刻看着他，加之奶奶护佑，我们也没办法，不过他对我这个姐姐倒是没得说。”
[EN] "That's outrageous! My parents scold and yell at him for it too, but we can't watch him every moment. Besides, with Grandmother protecting him, there's nothing we can do. Though he's never done anything wrong to me as his sister."
[ZH] “嫂嫂也是女子，若嫂嫂在外面被登徒子欺负，你会做何感想？”
[EN] "Sister-in-law, you're a woman too. If you were bullied by some lecher out there, how would you feel?"
[ZH] “我杀人的心都有。”
[EN] "I'd want to kill someone."
[ZH] “你那弟弟在外面可是登徒子，还欺负茶楼的女茶师，当众亲人家。”
[EN] "Your brother is a lecher out there. He even bullied the tea house's female tea master and kissed her in public."
[ZH] “真是过分！看我不收拾他！”陈文心站起来，走到屋门口，大声喊道，“陈浩宇！给我滚出来！”
[EN] "That's going too far! Just wait till I get my hands on him!" Chen Wenxin stood up, walked to the door, and shouted loudly: "Chen Haoyu! Get your butt out here!"
[ZH] 陈浩宇正在屋内跟姐夫看鱼，并不知朱淑真也在院中，听到姐姐的喊声，立刻走出来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu was inside admiring fish with his brother-in-law. He didn't know Zhu Shuzhen was also in the courtyard. Hearing his sister's shout, he immediately walked out.
[ZH] “姐，你喊我就喊呗，那么大声干什么？小心吓到莱宝。”一眼瞧见朱淑真，她坐在椅子上露出得意的笑容。
[EN] "Sister, if you want me, just call. Why shout so loud? You might scare Laibao." Then he spotted Zhu Shuzhen. She sat in a chair with a smug smile.
[ZH] 陈文心上前拧住陈浩宇的耳朵：“老实交代，是不是又欺负良家妇女？小心我告诉爹，让他拿大棒子打你！”
[EN] Chen Wenxin went up and twisted Chen Haoyu's ear: “Confess honestly — did you bully decent women again? Watch out, or I'll tell Father and have him beat you with a big stick！”
[ZH] 陈浩宇被姐姐拧住耳朵，疼得直叫唤：“哎吆！哎吆！姐，你怎么能听信他人之言，误会你亲弟弟？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu's ear was twisted by his sister. He yelped in pain: "Ow! Ow! Sister, how can you listen to others and misunderstand your own brother?"
[ZH] “误会？你在外做些什么？以为我不知？我是心疼你，不愿批评你，你竟然敢欺负淑真，还撞得她落水，现在当着我的面，给她道歉！”
[EN] "Misunderstand? What do you do out there? Think I don't know? I spare you criticism out of love, and you dare bully Shuzhen and even knock her into the water? Now apologize to her in front of me!"
[ZH] “哎吆！我给她道歉？不可能！”
[EN] "Ow! Apologize to her? Impossible!"
[ZH] 朱景元也跟着出来，看到这一幕，上前劝道：“他还是个孩子，顽劣些很正常，长大就好，快放开手，耳朵都拧红了。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan followed out. Seeing this, he stepped forward to mediate: “He's still a kid. Being wild is normal. He'll grow out of it. Let go — his ear's already red.”
[ZH] 陈文心也不忍心把弟弟的耳朵拧得时间太长，被夫君一劝，立刻放手：“我不拧你耳朵可以，你必须给淑真道歉。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin couldn't bear to twist her brother's ear for too long either. With her husband's persuasion, she immediately let go: "I'll stop twisting your ear, but you must apologize to Shuzhen."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一百个不乐意，念在姐夫替他说话，装作道歉的样子，走到朱淑真跟前：“朱淑真，对不起，那日我不该把你撞到湖里，让你跟乔文仙成了落汤鸡。不过你得感谢我，要不是我，你跟乔文仙约会多没意思，就当我给你们平淡的约会添加点作料，以后想起来才有滋味。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu was extremely unwilling, but since his brother-in-law had spoken up for him, he pretended to apologize and walked to Zhu Shuzhen: “Zhu Shuzhen, sorry. That day I shouldn't have knocked you into the lake and turned you and Qiao Wenxian into drowned rats. But you should thank me. Without me, your date with Qiao Wenxian would've been boring. Think of it as adding some spice to your plain date — so you'll have something to remember.”
[ZH] 对方的道歉显然诚心不足，千里寻站起来，骂道：“有滋味个头！老娘好好的约会被你破坏，以后回忆起来还要想起你这个讨厌鬼，今日我看在哥哥和嫂嫂的面上不跟你计较，下次再让我碰到你，决不饶恕！”
[EN] The apology was clearly half-hearted. Qianlixun stood up and cursed: "Spice, my foot! You ruined my perfectly good date. Now every time I remember it, I'll have to think of you, you pest. Today I'll let it go for brother's and sister-in-law's sake, but next time I run into you, I won't forgive you!"
[ZH] 朱景元批评道：“淑真，你是姑娘家，怎能说话如此粗鲁？注意些。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan criticized: "Shuzhen, you're a young lady. How can you speak so rudely? Be more careful."
[ZH] 陈浩宇更加得意：“听到没？你是姑娘家，不要这么粗鲁，连我姐夫都看不下去，赶紧回你的闺房好好照照镜子，看看自己还有没有大家闺秀的样子。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu became even more smug: “Hear that? You're a young lady. Don't be so rude. Even my brother-in-law can't stand it. Hurry back to your boudoir and look in the mirror — see if you still look like a proper lady.”
[ZH] 见淑真已火冒三丈，陈文心厉声道：“陈浩宇！你给我闭嘴，赶紧走！以后没有淑真允许，不准来朱府！听到没？”
[EN] Seeing Shuzhen fuming, Chen Wenxin said sharply: "Chen Haoyu! Shut up and get out! From now on, you're not allowed in the Zhu residence without Shuzhen's permission! Got it?"
[ZH] 朱淑真生气，陈浩宇更是幸灾乐祸：“有本事打我呀！告诉你，过不了多久，我送你一份大礼！”说完陈浩宇迅速跑掉。
[EN] Zhu Shuzhen was angry. Chen Haoyu was even more gleeful: "Hit me if you dare! Let me tell you, before long, I'll send you a big gift!" With that, Chen Haoyu ran off quickly.
[ZH] 千里寻攥紧拳头要追上去，被陈文心拉住：“别跟他一般见识，我看你们两个是八字不合，以后还是少见面为好。”
[EN] Qianlixun clenched her fists and wanted to chase after him, but Chen Wenxin held her back: "Don't stoop to his level. I think you two have incompatible horoscopes. Better to see each other less in the future."
[ZH] 千里寻松开拳头，平复心情：“嫂嫂，要不是看在你的面子上，今日我定打得陈浩宇满地找牙！”
[EN] Qianlixun loosened her fists and calmed down: "Sister-in-law, if it weren't for your sake, I'd have beaten Chen Haoyu so hard he'd be picking his teeth off the ground today!"
[ZH] 陈文心说：“淑真，你跟以前完全不一样，以前你说话哪是这风格？在朱府连我都不大理会，何时理过浩宇？没想到现在竟与他结下梁子，也不知是好是坏。”
[EN] Chen Wenxin said: “Shuzhen, you're completely different from before. Your speaking style was never like this. When you were in the Zhu residence, you barely even acknowledged me — when did you ever pay attention to Haoyu? I never expected you'd end up feuding with him now. I don't know if that's good or bad.”
[ZH] 朱景元问：“淑真，你怎么会和浩宇如此合不来？以前你们见对方可是互不招惹。”
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan asked: "Shuzhen, why don't you get along with Haoyu at all? Before, when you two met, you didn't bother each other."
[ZH] “什么互不招惹？在我印象中，他们两个以前几乎未曾单独照面过，即使见了，淑真那个高傲劲儿，何时把浩宇放在眼里？浩宇还在我面前说朱家有个冰山美人，一走到你跟前就觉得冷冰冰的，只想打寒战。呵呵……！”陈文心的笑声化解了千里寻内心的不满。
[EN] “What 'didn't bother each other'? In my memory, you two barely met alone. Even when you did, with Shuzhen's proud attitude, when did she ever take Haoyu seriously? Haoyu once told me there's an ice beauty in the Zhu family — just being near you makes him feel cold and gives him the shivers. Hehe...” Chen Wenxin's laughter dissolved Qianlixun's inner dissatisfaction.
[ZH] 本来想与嫂嫂好好聊聊家常或是女子之间的事情，被陈浩宇破坏，她觉得陈浩宇就是甩都甩不掉的苍蝇屎，总是坏她的好事。好不容易稍有美满的人生，偏偏要出现一位时不时制造麻烦之人。
[EN] She had wanted to chat about daily life or women's matters with her sister-in-law, but Chen Haoyu ruined it. She felt Chen Haoyu was like a fly speck she couldn't shake off — always ruining her good moments. Just when life was becoming somewhat happy, someone who kept causing trouble had to appear.
[ZH] 如果陈浩宇欺负女茶师时，千里寻未出手；如果他欺负夏果时，千里寻也未出手，也许就不会有后来的事情和今日状况。
[EN] If Qianlixun hadn't stepped in when Chen Haoyu bullied the tea master; if she hadn't stepped in when he bullied Xiaguo — maybe none of this would have happened.
[ZH] 可遇到陈浩宇欺负人，千里寻若不出手，她良心难安。看来净光法师说的没错，人的命运是由自己的行为决定。
[EN] But when she saw Chen Haoyu bullying people, if she didn't step in, her conscience wouldn't let her rest. It seemed Master Jingguang was right — a person's fate is determined by their own actions.
[ZH] 千里寻无心再与嫂嫂聊下去，到小木床前逗逗莱宝：“莱宝，姑姑来看你，你长大了要像爷爷一样当个大官，千万不要像你舅舅。”
[EN] Qianlixun was no longer in the mood to chat with her sister-in-law. She went to the small wooden crib to play with Laibao: “Laibao, Auntie came to see you. When you grow up, be a big official like Grandpa — whatever you do, don't be like your uncle.”
[ZH] 莱宝“咯咯”笑了两声，似乎是听懂后的应答。
[EN] Laibao giggled twice, as if he understood and was responding.
[ZH] 千里寻夸道：“莱宝真乖！”
[EN] Qianlixun praised: "Laibao is such a good baby!"
[ZH] 朱景元与陈文心对视后，露出无奈的笑容。
[EN] Zhu Jingyuan and Chen Wenxin exchanged glances and showed helpless smiles.
[ZH] 逗完莱宝，千里寻辞别哥嫂，回小院的路上，想着陈浩宇说过的话：“过不了多久，我送你一份大礼！”
[EN] After playing with Laibao, Qianlixun said goodbye to her brother and sister-in-law. On the way back to the small courtyard, she thought about what Chen Haoyu had said: "Before long, I'll send you a big gift!"
[ZH] 到底是什么意思？她想不明白，决定以后还是小心些，这家伙绝对没安好心。
[EN] What did it mean? She couldn't figure it out. She decided to be more careful from now on — that guy definitely had no good intentions.

#CH 89
[ZH] 苦荞拿着新手稿，来到西三胡同。她没来过此处，幸好苦蕨早已将地址说明。她很容易找到说话艺人的家，一只手提着个布袋，另一只手敲了敲门。
[EN] Kuqiao, holding the new manuscript, arrived at West Three Alley. She had never been here before, but fortunately Kujue had already explained the address. She easily found the storyteller's house. Carrying a cloth bag in one hand, she knocked on the door with the other.
[ZH] 不一会儿，门从里打开，说话艺人探出脑袋：“姑娘找谁？”
[EN] After a moment, the door opened from inside. The storyteller poked his head out: "Who are you looking for, miss?"
[ZH] “我来送千里寻公子的书稿。”
[EN] "I'm here to deliver Young Master Qianlixun's manuscript."
[ZH] 说话艺人放心将门开大些：“我正想着新书稿怎么还不送来，您就来了，真是及时，千里寻公子写的话本大家都喜欢听，您回去告诉他没事多写点儿。”
[EN] The storyteller felt relieved and opened the door wider: "I was just wondering why the new manuscript hadn't arrived yet, and here you are. Perfect timing. Everyone loves the stories Young Master Qianlixun writes. Tell him to write more when he's free."
[ZH] 苦荞从袋子里取出一沓纸：“先给你送这些，后续还有，我家小……我家公子很会编故事，放心，少不了。”
[EN] Kuqiao took out a stack of papers from the bag: "Here's the first batch. There's more to come. My young... my young master is great at crafting stories. Don't worry, there'll be plenty."
[ZH] 说话艺人接过手稿：“你家公子真的不打算从我这里分走银子？”
[EN] The storyteller took the manuscript: "Your young master really doesn't want to take any silver from me?"
[ZH] 苦荞强调道：“我家公子不缺你那几个钱，她写话本纯粹是个人喜好。”
[EN] Kuqiao emphasized: "My young master doesn't need your money. She writes stories purely for personal enjoyment."
[ZH] 说话艺人笑道：“这等好事真希望多遇些。”
[EN] The storyteller laughed: "I wish I could meet more such good things."
[ZH] “我家公子说了，若大家喜欢听，辛苦你多多推荐给其他说话艺人，让全临安城的人都听她写的话本，我家公子的名字才会被更多人知道。”
[EN] "My young master said, if everyone enjoys listening, please recommend it to other storytellers, so that everyone in Lin'an can hear the stories she writes. Only then will my young master's name be known by more people."
[ZH] “好说好说，那是自然。”说话艺人无不同意，若将书稿转让出去，岂不是还可赚一笔？欢喜之余邀请道，“请姑娘进屋喝盏茶再走。”
[EN] "Of course, of course. Naturally." The storyteller agreed to everything. If he passed the manuscript to others, wouldn't he make even more money? In his joy, he invited: "Please come in for a cup of tea before you go, miss."
[ZH] “不了，我还等着回去向公子交差。”
[EN] "No thank you. I still need to go back and report to my young master."
[ZH] 说话艺人恭敬地说：“姑娘慢走。”
[EN] The storyteller said respectfully: "Take care on your way, miss."
[ZH] 苦荞往胡同外走去，马车正停在胡同口。她上了马车，准备吩咐车夫回朱府，白风“蹭”地钻了进来。
[EN] Kuqiao walked out of the alley. The carriage was parked at the alley entrance. She got on the carriage and was about to tell the coachman to return to the Zhu residence when Baifeng suddenly jumped in.
[ZH] 苦荞急了：“你怎么跑进来？”
[EN] Kuqiao was anxious: "Why did you jump in?"
[ZH] “咱两个也算认识，捎我一段路。”
[EN] "We know each other, right? Give me a ride."
[ZH] “我跟你不顺路，我要回朱府，你是出城，赶紧下去！”
[EN] "We're not going the same way. I'm going back to the Zhu residence. You're leaving the city. Hurry up and get out!"
[ZH] “我又不是坏人，你何必赶我？我会功夫，坏人来了，还能保护你。再说我是仪王的贴身侍卫，你个丫鬟岂敢得罪我？”
[EN] "I'm not a bad guy. Why chase me away? I know martial arts. If bad guys come, I can protect you. Besides, I'm the Prince of Yi's personal guard. How dare you, a maid, offend me?"
[ZH] 苦荞不敢再赶白风下车，态度也缓和许多：“你怎么知道我在马车上？”
[EN] Kuqiao didn't dare chase Baifeng out anymore. Her attitude softened: "How did you know I was on the carriage?"
[ZH] 白风掏出泥人：“你把泥人还给我，我不高兴，又不好意思到朱府找你，就在街上瞎逛，不由自主来到西三胡同，也可能是我以前老来这里，走习惯了，结果瞧见你，我便躲在一旁等你。”
[EN] Baifeng took out the clay figurine: "You returned the clay figurine to me. I was unhappy, but too embarrassed to look for you at the Zhu residence. So I wandered the streets and ended up at West Three Alley. Maybe I used to come here so often I walked here out of habit. Then I saw you, so I hid to the side and waited."
[ZH] 苦荞盯着泥人，想起南双的话，脸不由红起来：“听说泥人是你娘给的，你一直很珍惜，为何要送给我？”
[EN] Kuqiao stared at the clay figurine. Remembering Nanshuang's words, her face blushed: "I heard the clay figurine was given by your mother, and you've always treasured it. Why give it to me?"
[ZH] 白风大胆表白：“正因为珍惜，才想把它送给喜欢的人。”
[EN] Baifeng boldly confessed: "Precisely because I treasure it, I want to give it to the person I like."
[ZH] 苦荞更害羞，低下头，不敢看白风。第一次有人对她表白，她有些不知所措。
[EN] Kuqiao was even more embarrassed. She lowered her head, not daring to look at Baifeng. It was the first time someone had confessed to her. She didn't know what to do.
[ZH] “今日，你不收下泥人，我就不走，赖在朱家马车里。”
[EN] "Today, if you don't take the clay figurine, I won't leave. I'll stay in the Zhu family's carriage."
[ZH] “你怎么这么无赖！”
[EN] "How can you be so shameless!"
[ZH] “必须得无赖，否则你怎么会答应我？”
[EN] "I have to be shameless. Otherwise, how would you agree to me?"
[ZH] “你看着比我小，我喜欢比我大些的。”
[EN] "You look younger than me. I like someone older."
[ZH] “我都不嫌你大，你却嫌我小？什么道理！再说我才比你小两岁，年龄不是问题，重要的是你喜不喜欢我。反正你必须答应我，我都想好了，你要是不答应，我就天天缠着你。”
[EN] "I don't mind you being older. Why do you mind me being younger? What kind of logic is that! Besides, I'm only two years younger than you. Age isn't the problem. What matters is whether you like me. Anyway, you have to agree to me. I've thought it through. If you don't agree, I'll pester you every day."
[ZH] 苦荞继续低着头：“你是仪王的贴身侍卫，要伺候王爷，哪有时间天天缠我？”
[EN] Kuqiao kept her head down: "You're the Prince of Yi's personal guard. You have to serve the prince. When would you have time to pester me every day?"
[ZH] “我家王爷人可好了，从来不限制我，我想去哪里他都允许。我要是告诉他我喜欢你，他肯定会找幽栖居士为我提亲。”
[EN] "My prince is very kind. He never restricts me. He allows me to go wherever I want. If I tell him I like you, he'll definitely ask Youqi Jushi to propose marriage for me."
[ZH] 苦荞一把抓过泥人：“你真麻烦，我……我还是收下，你不准让别人知道我收了你的泥人。”
[EN] Kuqiao snatched the clay figurine: "You're so troublesome. I... I'll take it. But you can't let anyone know I took your clay figurine."
[ZH] “为什么？”白风很是不解。
[EN] "Why?" Baifeng was very confused.
[ZH] 苦荞红着脸催道：“没有为什么，你赶紧下去吧。”
[EN] Kuqiao blushed and urged: "No reason. Just hurry up and get out."
[ZH] “我马上下车，反正你收下我的泥人，证明你也喜欢我，既然你害羞不想让别人知道，我先听你的，等我觉得差不多时，会亲自请王爷帮我提亲。”
[EN] "I'll get off the carriage now. Anyway, you took my clay figurine, which proves you like me too. Since you're shy and don't want others to know, I'll listen to you for now. When I think it's about right, I'll personally ask the prince to propose marriage for me."
[ZH] “别胡说！仪王还未成亲，什么时候轮到你。”
[EN] "Don't talk nonsense! The Prince of Yi hasn't married yet. When is it your turn?"
[ZH] “那就等我家王爷成亲后我再提，回见！对了，明日我们还能相见。”白风迅速跳下马车，又从马车的窗户向里提醒，“回去后，想我时，多看看泥人。”
[EN] "Then I'll ask after my prince gets married. See you later! Oh right, we can meet again tomorrow." Baifeng quickly jumped off the carriage and reminded her through the window: "After you go back, when you miss me, look at the clay figurine more."
[ZH] 苦荞更加害羞，对车夫喊道：“还不快赶车！”
[EN] Kuqiao was even more embarrassed. She shouted to the coachman: "Hurry up and drive!"
[ZH] 马车迅速启动，很快把白风甩在后面。
[EN] The carriage started quickly, soon leaving Baifeng behind.
[ZH] 苦荞坐在车里自语道：“白风怎么说话这么直白？都不担心我骂他。我……我刚才为何不骂他？竟然爱听他说那些话，难道我也喜欢他？”她看着手中的泥人，小心抚摸着。
[EN] Kuqiao sat in the carriage and muttered to herself: "Why is Baifeng so straightforward? He's not afraid I'll scold him. Why... why didn't I scold him just now? I actually liked hearing him say those things. Could it be that I like him too?" She looked at the clay figurine in her hands and carefully stroked it.
[ZH] 回到朱府小院，小姐正坐在院子的藤椅上发呆。苦荞怕小姐发现手中的泥人，赶紧塞进布袋里。
[EN] Returning to the Zhu residence's small courtyard, she found her young lady sitting in a rattan chair in the yard, lost in thought. Afraid her young lady would discover the clay figurine, Kuqiao quickly stuffed it into the cloth bag.
[ZH] “小姐，书稿已送到。”
[EN] "Young lady, the manuscript has been delivered."
[ZH] “哦，你回来了，送到便好。”千里寻头靠在椅背上，望着夕阳西下后的天空，并未发现苦荞的动作。
[EN] "Oh, you're back. As long as it's delivered, that's fine." Qianlixun leaned her head against the chair back, gazing at the sky after sunset. She didn't notice Kuqiao's actions.
[ZH] “小姐要没事，我先去忙。”
[EN] "If there's nothing else, young lady, I'll go tend to my duties."
[ZH] “你去忙吧。”
[EN] "Go ahead."
[ZH] 苦荞庆幸没被小姐发现，她回到丫鬟房中，找来一个木盒，把泥人从布袋里取出，小心放在木盒里，回想着白风在马车上说的话，不由露出笑容。
[EN] Kuqiao was relieved she hadn't been discovered. She returned to the maids' room, found a wooden box, took the clay figurine out of the cloth bag, carefully placed it in the wooden box, and recalled what Baifeng had said in the carriage. She couldn't help but smile.
[ZH] “苦荞！你出来一下，与我聊会儿！”
[EN] "Kuqiao! Come out for a moment and chat with me!"
[ZH] 外面传来小姐的喊声，苦荞收好木盒，来到小姐面前：“小姐想和我聊什么？”
[EN] Her young lady's voice came from outside. Kuqiao put away the wooden box and came to her young lady: "What does young lady want to chat about?"
[ZH] “我今日去哥哥院中，你猜我碰到谁？”
[EN] "I went to my brother's courtyard today. Guess who I ran into?"
[ZH] “谁？”
[EN] "Who?"
[ZH] “阴魂不散的人——陈浩宇。”
[EN] “The person who won't leave me alone — Chen Haoyu.”
[ZH] “啊，又碰到陈公子？”
[EN] "Ah, you ran into Young Master Chen again?"
[ZH] “你说说，他该不会是我命中克星？只要碰见他准没好事。”
[EN] "Tell me, could he be my nemesis? Whenever I see him, nothing good happens."
[ZH] “我也不好说。”
[EN] "I can't say for sure."
[ZH] “那你说说，以前的我跟陈浩宇是怎么相处的？”尽管哥嫂已说过一些，千里寻还想听苦荞如何说。
[EN] "Then tell me, how did I get along with Chen Haoyu before?" Even though her brother and sister-in-law had already said something, Qianlixun still wanted to hear what Kuqiao would say.
[ZH] “嗯……，相处谈不上，小姐每次见到陈公子都是有外人在的时候，您从来没理过他，他也从来没招惹过您。”苦荞又想了想，“好像不对，你们说过一次话……”
[EN] "Well... I wouldn't say you got along. Every time you saw Young Master Chen, there were other people around. You never acknowledged him, and he never bothered you either." Kuqiao thought for a moment. "Wait, that's not right. You two did talk once..."
[ZH] 千里寻急切地问：“我跟他说了什么？或者他跟我说了什么？”
[EN] Qianlixun asked eagerly: "What did I say to him? Or what did he say to me?"
[ZH] 苦荞回想了一会儿：“时间太久，我记得不大清楚，只记得是大公子成亲时，他来参加喜宴，多喝点酒，在朱府瞎逛，来到小院，见门开着，借着酒劲闯进来。您当时刚从酒宴回到小院，因酒席间有人不小心把汤洒在您身上，我陪您回来换衣裳，正换着陈公子闯进来，那种情况下，您最多骂他流氓，应该不会说别的。”
[EN] Kuqiao recalled for a moment: "It was so long ago, I don't remember clearly. I only remember it was when the eldest young master got married. He came to the wedding banquet, drank a bit too much, wandered around the Zhu residence, and came to the small courtyard. Seeing the door open, he barged in, emboldened by alcohol. You had just returned from the banquet. Someone accidentally spilled soup on you, so I accompanied you back to change clothes. Just as you were changing, Young Master Chen barged in. In that situation, you probably just called him a hooligan. You wouldn't have said anything else."
[ZH] “什么？我换衣裳时被他看见？”千里寻从藤椅上坐起来，很是担心。
[EN] "What? He saw me changing clothes?" Qianlixun sat up from the rattan chair, very worried.
[ZH] “小姐不用担心，您只是换外衣。当时陈公子态度还可以，很快离开，看那样子他应是不知道小院儿是您住的地儿，好奇才闯进来。”
[EN] "Young lady, don't worry. You were only changing your outer clothes. Young Master Chen's attitude was okay at the time. He left quickly. It seemed he didn't know the small courtyard was where you lived. He probably barged in out of curiosity."
[ZH] 千里寻松口气，多亏没被陈浩宇看光，否则她跳湖的心都有。
[EN] Qianlixun breathed a sigh of relief. It was fortunate Chen Haoyu didn't see everything. Otherwise, she would have wanted to jump into a lake.
[ZH] 听苦荞这么说，朱淑真以前跟陈浩确实没什么往来，由此看来，陈浩宇只跟千里寻有关。想到这些，千里寻又不由感到高兴，说明她正在一步一步摆脱朱淑真的命运。可如何能让千里寻的命运摆脱陈浩宇这等人？她感到为难。
[EN] Hearing Kuqiao say this, it seemed Zhu Shuzhen really had nothing to do with Chen Haoyu before. It seemed Chen Haoyu was only connected to Qianlixun. Thinking of this, Qianlixun felt happy. It meant she was gradually escaping Zhu Shuzhen's fate. But how could she escape someone like Chen Haoyu in Qianlixun's fate? She felt troubled.
[ZH] “苦荞，你会不会认为我在茶楼帮助女茶师是错误的？解救夏果也是错误的？”
[EN] "Kuqiao, do you think I was wrong to help the tea master at the tea house? Was it also wrong to rescue Xiaguo?"
[ZH] “我一点儿都不这么认为，我如果像小姐一样有本事，也会这么做，坏人就该受到惩罚。”
[EN] "I don't think so at all. If I were as capable as you, young lady, I would do the same. Bad people deserve punishment."
[ZH] “你也认为陈浩宇是坏人？”
[EN] "You also think Chen Haoyu is a bad person?"
[ZH] “他做的那些事，只有坏人才做得出。”
[EN] “The things he does — only a bad person would do them.”
[ZH] “也只有你理解我，可我哥哥嫂嫂却只说他顽劣。”
[EN] "Only you understand me. But my brother and sister-in-law just say he's wild."
[ZH] 苦荞不好评判主人，只能说：“小姐怎么想，我就怎么想。”
[EN] Kuqiao didn't feel comfortable judging her masters. She could only say: "Whatever you think, young lady, I think the same."
[ZH] 千里寻笑笑，继续靠在藤椅上。明日立秋，她感到一丝凉意袭来，过不了多久，栅栏里的菊花会竞相开放。
[EN] Qianlixun smiled and leaned back on the rattan chair. Tomorrow was the Beginning of Autumn. She felt a hint of coolness. Soon, the chrysanthemums in the fence would bloom in competition.
[ZH] 千里寻决定，以后少跟陈浩宇碰面，能躲他多远便躲多远。
[EN] Qianlixun decided to avoid Chen Haoyu as much as possible in the future, staying as far away from him as she could.
[ZH] “苦荞，你给绿桃和其他丫鬟交代一下，以后多在朱府注意些，要是碰到陈浩宇来，给我报告一声，我好避开，省得与他再碰面。”
[EN] "Kuqiao, tell Lvtao and the other maids to pay more attention around the Zhu residence from now on. If Chen Haoyu comes, report to me so I can avoid him and not run into him again."
[ZH] “我记下了，这就去给绿桃姐姐和其他丫鬟嘱咐一声。”
[EN] "I'll remember. I'll go tell Sister Lvtao and the other maids right now."
[ZH] 看着苦荞走出小院的背影，千里寻叹道：“希望从今往后陈浩宇不要出现在我穿越而来的命运中。”
[EN] Watching Kuqiao's back as she walked out of the small courtyard, Qianlixun sighed: "I hope Chen Haoyu won't appear in the fate I've transmigrated into from now on."

#CH 90
[ZH] 按照约定时间到达幽林别居，乔文仙带领南双在门口迎接，没等淑真下马车，他已来到车前，伸出手，等待去扶她。
[EN] Arriving at Youlin Villa at the agreed time, Qiao Wenxian led Nanshuang to welcome them at the gate. Before Shuzhen could get off the carriage, he had already come to the front of the carriage, extended his hand, and waited to help her down.
[ZH] 千里寻推开马车的门，探出身，瞧到文渊伸出的手，会心一笑，自然而然扶上去，以优雅的姿态走下马车。
[EN] Qianlixun pushed open the door, leaned out, saw Wenyuan's outstretched hand, and smiled knowingly. She took his hand naturally and stepped down gracefully.
[ZH] 文渊不舍得放开，将她的手拉得更紧。千里寻当然不会主动甩开，她想时时刻刻被文渊牵着。
[EN] Wenyuan was reluctant to let go and held her hand even tighter. Qianlixun certainly wouldn't pull away on her own — she wanted to be held by Wenyuan every moment.
[ZH] “大家都到了吗？”
[EN] "Has everyone arrived?"
[ZH] “都已到齐，就差你。”
[EN] "Everyone's here. Only you were missing."
[ZH] “没想到大家这么积极，难道又是诗会？”
[EN] "I didn't expect everyone to be so enthusiastic. Is it another poetry gathering?"
[ZH] “今日不是，是东方尘泽他们想来幽林别居玩儿，让我必须叫上你。”
[EN] "Not today. Dongfang Chenze and the others wanted to come to Youlin Villa to have fun, and they made me bring you along."
[ZH] 千里寻想起苗知瑜：“可邀请苗姑娘？”
[EN] Qianlixun thought of Miao Zhiyu: "Was Young Lady Miao invited?"
[ZH] “苗姑娘只喜欢参加诗会，像玩乐这种事情，不必邀请她，邀请她，她也不会来。”
[EN] "Young Lady Miao only likes attending poetry gatherings. For things like having fun, there's no need to invite her. Even if invited, she wouldn't come."
[ZH] 千里寻又想起苗知瑜试探她的话：“那可不一定，要是文渊你出面邀请，我想她必来。”
[EN] Qianlixun remembered Miao Zhiyu's probing words: "That's not certain. If you, Wenyuan, were to invite her personally, I think she would definitely come."
[ZH] 乔文仙听出些醋意，靠近淑真耳边：“怎么？你吃醋了？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian detected a hint of jealousy and leaned close to Shuzhen's ear: "What? Are you jealous?"
[ZH] 千里寻用另一只手将文渊的脑袋推向一边：“别胡说。”
[EN] Qianlixun pushed Wenyuan's head aside with her other hand: "Don't talk nonsense."
[ZH] 能被心爱的女子吃醋，乔文仙心里美滋滋的，把淑真的手抓得更紧，拉着她向别居里面走去。
[EN] Being able to make the woman he loved jealous made Qiao Wenxian feel delighted. He held Shuzhen's hand even tighter and led her into the villa.
[ZH] 千里寻从文渊握紧着她手的力度中感受到对方的心意，更是甜在心头。
[EN] From the strength of Wenyuan's grip, Qianlixun could feel his affection, which made her heart even sweeter.
[ZH] 苦荞与南双跟在后面，她看到乔公子与小姐亲密的样子，很是羡慕，想起昨日白风表白的话，问道：“南双，怎么没见白风？”
[EN] Kuqiao and Nanshuang followed behind. Seeing Young Master Qiao and her young lady so close, Kuqiao felt a pang of envy. Remembering Baifeng's confession yesterday, she asked: "Nanshuang, where's Baifeng? Why haven't I seen him?"
[ZH] “白风在陪仪王。”
[EN] "Baifeng is accompanying the Prince of Yi."
[ZH] “一会儿他跟仪王也来吗？”
[EN] "Will he come with the prince later?"
[ZH] “当然，还有佳和郡主。”
[EN] "Of course. And Princess Jiahe too."
[ZH] 苦荞会心一笑。
[EN] Kuqiao smiled understandingly.
[ZH] 南双察觉，好奇地问：“苦荞姐姐怎么这么关心白风？你不会也喜欢上他？”
[EN] Nanshuang noticed and asked curiously: "Why is Sister Kuqiao so concerned about Baifeng? You're not falling for him too, are you?"
[ZH] 苦荞不好意思承认：“你个小孩子家，懂什么？净瞎说！”
[EN] Kuqiao was too embarrassed to admit it: "You're just a kid! What do you know? Stop making things up!"
[ZH] 来到乔文仙居住的小院外，千里寻被木质高台吸引：“这么高，怎么建造的？我要上去瞧瞧。”
[EN] Arriving outside the small courtyard where Qiao Wenxian lived, Qianlixun was attracted by a wooden platform: "It's so high. How was it built? I want to go up and take a look."
[ZH] “我带你上去。”乔文仙拉着淑真，一步一步登上高台。
[EN] "I'll take you up." Qiao Wenxian led Shuzhen by the hand and climbed the platform step by step.
[ZH] 到了顶端，当整个幽林尽收眼底时，千里寻叹道：“太美了！我们竟然站立在密林之上！文渊，你天天待在这么美的地方，真令人羡慕。”
[EN] Reaching the top, when the entire Youlin Forest came into view, Qianlixun sighed: "It's so beautiful! We're actually standing above the dense forest! Wenyuan, you stay in such a beautiful place every day. How enviable."
[ZH] 乔文仙深情地看着淑真：“你不用羡慕，只要你嫁给我，也可以天天待在这里。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian looked at Shuzhen affectionately: "No need to feel that way. As long as you marry me, you can stay here every day too."
[ZH] 高台上只有他们两人，这么好的氛围，这么美的景，这么美的人，乔文仙忍不住想要亲吻淑真……
[EN] Only the two of them were on the platform. With such a wonderful atmosphere, such beautiful scenery, and such a beautiful person, Qiao Wenxian couldn't help but want to kiss Shuzhen...
[ZH] 千里寻能感受到文渊靠近她的鼻息，她竟然紧张起来，却也期待享受她的初吻，余光却瞥见仪王正站在木质高台下向上张望。
[EN] Qianlixun could feel Wenyuan's breath approaching her. She grew nervous, yet also looked forward to her first kiss. Then out of the corner of her eye, she caught sight of the Prince of Yi. He stood below the wooden platform, looking up at them.
[ZH] 他又出现的不是时候！第二次坏她的好事。被他盯着，千里寻没有心情享受文渊即将送上来的香吻。她迅速转过身：“文渊，没想到从这里竟然能看到远处的神柳。”
[EN] He appeared at the wrong time again! Ruining her good moment for the second time. Being watched by him, Qianlixun was in no mood to enjoy the sweet kiss Wenyuan was about to give her. She quickly turned around: "Wenyuan, I didn't expect we could see the Divine Willow in the distance from here."
[ZH] 没有亲到心爱的人，乔文仙并不伤心，刚才淑真转身时已抽回手，他又上前抓起她的手：“不准轻易放开我。”
[EN] Not having kissed the one he loved, Qiao Wenxian wasn't sad. Shuzhen had already pulled her hand away when she turned around just now. He stepped forward and grabbed her hand again: "You're not allowed to let go of me easily."
[ZH] 千里寻小声说：“你没见仪王在下面看着我们，被他盯着，我怎么好意思跟你……”
[EN] Qianlixun whispered: "Don't you see the Prince of Yi watching us from below? With him staring, how can I be at ease with you..."
[ZH] 乔文仙故意问：“跟我什么？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian deliberately asked: "With me what?"
[ZH] “跟你那个。”
[EN] "With you... that."
[ZH] “哪个？”
[EN] "What?"
[ZH] “你就别问了，还是把仪王打发走再说。”
[EN] "Just stop asking. Let's get the Prince of Yi to leave first."
[ZH] “你怕什么？苦荞和南双也在下面。”
[EN] "What are you afraid of? Kuqiao and Nanshuang are also below."
[ZH] “怎么能一样？苦荞跟南双多懂事，能不看就不看，可仪王直勾勾地盯着，像看热闹似的，真没修养，都不知道回避一下。”
[EN] “How can that be the same? Kuqiao and Nanshuang are sensible — they won't look if they don't have to. But the Prince of Yi stares straight at us, like watching a show. He really has no manners. He doesn't even know to look away.”
[ZH] “别这样说仪王，他人很好，我很多事情都需他帮忙，就连你和离之事，也是他出面说服林觉和知府大人，否则和离哪有那么顺利？”
[EN] “Don't talk about the Prince of Yi like that. He's a very good person. I need his help with many things. Even your divorce — he was the one who persuaded Lin Jue and the prefect. Otherwise, how could the divorce have gone so smoothly？”
[ZH] 千里寻一直以为是老天开眼，加之韦嬷嬷出面，她爹朱晞颜才大发慈悲，没想到是仪王帮了她：“什么？我能和离跟他有关？”
[EN] Qianlixun had always thought it was heaven's will, and with Nanny Wei stepping in, her father Zhu Xiyan had shown mercy. She never expected the Prince of Yi had helped her: "What? My divorce had something to do with him?"
[ZH] 乔文仙将仪王在宴会上帮忙之事简单说明，千里寻才恍然大悟。她又向仪王看去，他还在抬头向他们仰望。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian briefly explained how the Prince of Yi had helped at the banquet. Qianlixun suddenly understood. She looked at the Prince of Yi again — he was still looking up at them.
[ZH] “文渊，随我下去感谢仪王。”
[EN] "Wenyuan, come down with me and thank the Prince of Yi."
[ZH] 千里寻抽回手，第一个向木台下走去。
[EN] Qianlixun pulled her hand back and was the first to walk down the wooden platform.
[ZH] 木台很高，走起来还需时间，申简辰一直注视着千里寻款款而下，等她来到他身边，又款款行礼：“拜见仪王殿下。”
[EN] The wooden platform was high, and it took time to descend. Shen Jianchen had been watching Qianlixun walk down gracefully. When she reached his side, she curtsied elegantly: "Greetings, Your Highness the Prince of Yi."
[ZH] 申简辰昨日已知文渊会邀请千里寻前来，他今日更不会去府衙，一直在屋里等着。估计时间差不多时，带着白风来到文渊居住的院子，结果看到千里寻登上木质高台。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had known yesterday that Wenyuan would invite Qianlixun to come. Today, he certainly wouldn't go to the prefect's office. He had been waiting in his room. When he estimated the time was about right, he brought Baifeng to the courtyard where Wenyuan lived, only to see Qianlixun climbing the wooden platform.
[ZH] 千里寻与文渊亲密的样子令他揪心，但又忍不住不看。千里寻转过身，他才松一口气，还好没亲到一起。他心里生起对文渊的嫉妒之情，可理智并不会让他因嫉妒做出傻事。
[EN] The intimacy between Qianlixun and Wenyuan made his heart ache, but he couldn't help watching. When Qianlixun turned around, he finally breathed a sigh of relief — at least they hadn't kissed. Jealousy toward Wenyuan rose in his heart, but reason wouldn't let him do something foolish out of jealousy.
[ZH] 千里寻走下台阶，朝着自己走来，申简辰幻想着她会扑进他的怀里，却听到她既有礼貌又充满距离感的问候之语。
[EN] As Qianlixun walked down the steps toward him, Shen Jianchen fantasized that she would throw herself into his arms. Instead, he heard her polite yet distant greeting.
[ZH] “我说过，幽栖居士见我不必多礼。”
[EN] "I've said before, Youqi Jushi doesn't need to be so formal when seeing me."
[ZH] “今日的礼我一定要行，哪怕仪王让我跪下，我也绝无二话。”
[EN] "Today I must show my respect. Even if the Prince of Yi asked me to kneel, I wouldn't say a word of complaint."
[ZH] “为何如此说？”
[EN] "Why say that?"
[ZH] “刚刚文渊告诉我，和离之事多亏仪王帮忙，否则不会如此顺利。”
[EN] "Wenyuan just told me that the divorce went so smoothly thanks to the Prince of Yi's help. Otherwise, it wouldn't have been so smooth."
[ZH] “举手之劳，何足挂齿。”
[EN] "A mere lift of the hand. Not worth mentioning."
[ZH] “对仪王来讲是举手之劳，可对我来讲却困难重重。要不我还是跪下给仪王您磕三个响头。”
[EN] "For the Prince of Yi, it was a mere lift of the hand. But for me, it was full of difficulties. How about I kneel and kowtow three times to the Prince of Yi?"
[ZH] 千里寻说着就要下跪。
[EN] As Qianlixun spoke, she was about to kneel.
[ZH] 申简辰上前一把扶住她：“我不需要你这样，如果你想谢我，就给我一个答案。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped forward and supported her: "I don't need you to do that. If you want to thank me, just give me an answer."
[ZH] 千里寻露出疑惑的表情：“什么答案？”
[EN] Qianlixun showed a confused expression: "What answer?"
[ZH] 申简辰提醒道：“我上次问过你一个问题，你还没给我答案。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen reminded her: "I asked you a question last time. You haven't given me the answer yet."
[ZH] 千里寻完全想不起来：“什么问题？”
[EN] Qianlixun couldn't remember at all: "What question?"
[ZH] 申简辰放开千里寻，他有些生气。
[EN] Shen Jianchen let go of Qianlixun. He was somewhat angry.
[ZH] “幽栖居士还是好好想想我的问题，再想好答案，我随时恭候。”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi should think carefully about my question and prepare the answer. I'll be waiting at any time."
[ZH] 仪王看上去不高兴，千里寻不敢多言，目送仪王走进文渊居住的院子。
[EN] The Prince of Yi looked unhappy. Qianlixun didn't dare say more and watched as the Prince of Yi walked into the courtyard where Wenyuan lived.
[ZH] 白风跟着仪王过来，一眼看到苦荞，凑到跟前，有主人在，两人只用目光互相交流。
[EN] Baifeng followed the Prince of Yi over. At a glance, he saw Kuqiao. He came close. With their masters present, the two could only communicate with their eyes.
[ZH] 南双见他们眉来眼去，甚是不满，又不敢说话。等仪王进入院内，白风才不得已离开。
[EN] Seeing them exchanging glances, Nanshuang was very dissatisfied but didn't dare speak. Only after the Prince of Yi entered the courtyard did Baifeng reluctantly leave.
[ZH] 乔文仙不明所以：“淑真，仪王何时问过你问题？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian didn't understand: "Shuzhen, when did the Prince of Yi ask you a question?"
[ZH] “我不记得。”千里寻在脑中回忆着与仪王接触过的情景，“哦！我想起来，那晚我来幽林别居，应该是那时仪王问我的，可问的是什么，我一点儿印象都没有。”
[EN] "I don't remember." Qianlixun recalled in her mind the scenes of her interactions with the Prince of Yi. "Oh! I remember. That night I came to Youlin Villa. It must have been then that the Prince of Yi asked me. But what he asked, I don't recall at all."
[ZH] “没事，不必为此费神，仪王是帮你，也是帮我，有我谢他，你不必挂心。”
[EN] "It's fine. Don't trouble yourself over it. The Prince of Yi was helping you, and also helping me. With me thanking him, you don't need to worry."
[ZH] “真的可以吗？”
[EN] "Is that really okay?"
[ZH] “当然，你放轻松，有我在，一切交给我。”
[EN] "Of course. Relax. With me here, leave everything to me."
[ZH] 本来想着欠了仪王一个大大的人情，该如何偿还，文渊如此说，千里寻轻松许多。可接着她又开始为难，今日来此，关于夏果一事还想求仪王帮忙，这下可让她如何开得了口。
[EN] She had been thinking about how to repay the great favor she owed the Prince of Yi. With Wenyuan saying this, Qianlixun felt much more relaxed. But then she became troubled again. She had come here today wanting to ask the Prince of Yi for help regarding Xiaguo. Now how could she bring herself to open her mouth?
[ZH] 正为难，瞧见佳和郡主带丫鬟走来。千里寻丢下文渊，又去向佳和郡主行礼，想着跟她搞好关系，说不定还能再麻烦仪王帮忙处理夏果之事。
[EN] Just as she was troubled, she saw Princess Jiahe approaching with her maid. Qianlixun left Wenyuan behind and went to greet Princess Jiahe, thinking that getting along well with her might allow her to trouble the Prince of Yi again to help with Xiaguo's matter.
[ZH] “拜见佳和郡主。”
[EN] "Greetings, Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 乔梦容牵起她的手：“幽栖居士今日能来，我甚是高兴，比乔公子还要高兴。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong took her hand: "I'm very happy that Youqi Jushi could come today. Even happier than Young Master Qiao."
[ZH] “为何？”
[EN] "Why?"
[ZH] “他们都是男子，就我一个女子多没趣，有你在，我才不会觉得孤单。”
[EN] "They're all men. It's not interesting being the only woman. With you here, I won't feel lonely."
[ZH] 淑真的手被佳和郡主牵住，乔文仙无从下手，只能无奈地跟在她们身后。
[EN] With Shuzhen's hand held by Princess Jiahe, Qiao Wenxian had no way to intervene and could only follow helplessly behind them.
[ZH] 文渊居住的院子并不大，里面圈着四棵树木，分布在道路两边。顺着道路直走便进入屋内。屋子的外厅挺大，已摆好桌椅。东方尘泽、曹班和安明轩三人都坐在右边，左边显然是给其他三位留的。
[EN] The courtyard where Wenyuan lived wasn't very large. Inside, four trees were enclosed, distributed on both sides of the path. Following the path straight led into the house. The outer hall of the house was quite large, with tables and chairs already set up. Dongfang Chenze, Cao Ban, and An Mingxuan all sat on the right. The left was obviously reserved for the other three.
[ZH] 正位坐着仪王，从千里寻进来那一刻，他的目光一直停留在她身上。
[EN] The Prince of Yi sat in the main seat. From the moment Qianlixun entered, his gaze had been fixed on her.
[ZH] 因千里寻与佳和郡主牵着手，两人离得很近，旁人都以为仪王正在注视郡主。连佳和郡主本人也如此认为，心里不由美滋滋的。
[EN] Because Qianlixun and Princess Jiahe were holding hands and standing very close, others thought the Prince of Yi was gazing at the princess. Even Princess Jiahe herself thought so, feeling delighted in her heart.
[ZH] 佳和郡主坐在靠近仪王的桌边，中间坐着千里寻，文渊坐在她的身旁。
[EN] Princess Jiahe sat at the table near the Prince of Yi. Qianlixun sat in the middle, with Wenyuan sitting beside her.
[ZH] 待千里寻坐定后，申简辰不好再看向她，主要担心文渊会疑心，更担心会伤到佳和郡主，便收回目光。
[EN] After Qianlixun was seated, Shen Jianchen didn't dare look at her anymore, mainly worried that Wenyuan would be suspicious, and even more worried about hurting Princess Jiahe. So he withdrew his gaze.
[ZH] 幽林别居的下人们进入，把酒菜摆好，又给每人将酒杯满上，才退出去。
[EN] The servants of Youlin Villa entered, set up the wine and dishes, filled everyone's cups, and then withdrew.
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“仪王，东方兄把自家的舞伎带来，我们不妨先欣赏一段舞蹈。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said: "Your Highness, Brother Dongfang brought his own dancers. Why don't we enjoy a dance first?"
[ZH] 申简辰点点头。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded.
[ZH] 东方尘泽看看身后的化雨，化雨明白自家公子的意思，迅速出去安排舞伎表演之事。
[EN] Dongfang Chenze looked at Huayu behind him. Huayu understood his master's intention and quickly went out to arrange the dance performance.

#CH 91
[ZH] 东方家的舞伎很快进入，舞动起来。大家边吃边欣赏，各自带的下人站在一旁伺候，等主人享用完，他们方可享用美食。
[EN] The Dongfang family's dancers soon entered and began their dance. Everyone ate as they watched the performance, while the servants they had brought stood off to the side waiting on them; only after their masters finished would they get to enjoy the feast.
[ZH] 今日的美食对白风毫无吸引力，他站在仪王身旁一直盯着苦荞。
[EN] Today's spread held no appeal for Bai Feng at all. Standing beside Prince Yi, he kept his eyes fixed on Kuqiao.
[ZH] 申简辰见有东坡肉，知道白风爱吃，不用看就猜到他此时哈喇子会流得多长。于是夹了一块，放进小瓷碗中，端起来，递到旁边。
[EN] Spotting Dongpo pork on the table, Shen Jianchen knew how much Bai Feng loved it. He did not even need to look to guess how long Bai Feng's drool must be by now. He picked up a piece with his chopsticks, placed it in a small porcelain bowl, lifted it, and held it out to the side.
[ZH] 结果半天没人接，申简辰扭过头，发现白风并未注意东坡肉。心中疑惑，这家伙怎么对东坡肉不感兴趣？难道是吃腻了？
[EN] When no one took it after a long moment, Shen Jianchen turned his head and saw that Bai Feng had paid no attention to the pork at all. Puzzled, he wondered why the guy had zero interest in Dongpo pork. Had he eaten too much of it and grown sick of it?
[ZH] 再仔细瞧，白风的脸上挂着笑容，眼睛直勾勾盯着一个方向。
[EN] Taking a closer look, he saw a smile hanging on Bai Feng's face, his eyes locked straight in one direction.
[ZH] 申简辰收回小瓷碗，在桌上放好，顺着白风盯的方向瞧去，原来他有了喜欢的人。
[EN] Shen Jianchen pulled the small porcelain bowl back, set it on the table, and followed Bai Feng's gaze. So Bai Feng had someone he liked now.
[ZH] 申简辰露出会心的笑容，心想：“我喜欢千里寻，你喜欢她的丫鬟，倒也有趣。”
[EN] A knowing smile curved Shen Jianchen's lips. "I like Qianli Xun, and you like her maid," he thought. "That's quite amusing."
[ZH] 申简辰对苦荞并不感兴趣，既已看向千里寻的方向，他忍不住不看她。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had no interest in Kuqiao. Now that he was already looking in Qianli Xun's direction, he could not stop himself from looking at her.
[ZH] 她除欣赏舞蹈享用美食外，只顾与文渊对饮，或是交流目光，他们看上去很亲昵。千里寻的脸上挂着申简辰从未见过的笑容，能够感受到她此刻很幸福。
[EN] Between watching the dance and enjoying the food, she had eyes only for Wenyuan, sharing cups of wine and trading glances. They looked so close. A smile Shen Jianchen had never seen before lit up Qianli Xun's face, and he could feel how happy she was in this moment.
[ZH] “哥哥。”传来乔梦容的声音。
[EN] "Brother," came Qiao Mengrong's voice.
[ZH] 申简辰看向梦容，她正向他举杯，面带笑容。
[EN] Shen Jianchen looked over at Mengrong. She was raising her wine cup toward him with a smile.
[ZH] 他端起桌上的酒杯，做出与梦容碰杯的动作，然后饮下。他不敢再注视千里寻，担心自己忍不住会上前质问她：“你为什么要爱上文渊？为什么不等我？”
[EN] He picked up his cup from the table, mimed clinking cups with Mengrong, and drank it down. He dared not keep watching Qianli Xun, afraid he might lose control, march right over, and demand of her: "Why did you have to fall in love with Wenyuan? Why didn't you wait for me?"
[ZH] 三位公子一直观察幽栖居士的表情，她见到乔兄心情果然不错，三人互相交换眼神。
[EN] The three young masters had been watching Youqi Jushi's expression the whole time. Sure enough, seeing Brother Qiao had put her in high spirits, and the three exchanged a glance.
[ZH] 待舞伎跳完后退出，东方尘泽为自己斟满酒，端起酒杯：“幽栖居士，我们三人今日来幽林别居，除拜见仪王，找乔兄乐一乐，还有一件重要的事。”
[EN] Once the dancers finished and withdrew, Dongfang Chenze filled his cup and raised it. "Youqi Jushi, besides paying our respects to Prince Yi and having a good time with Brother Qiao, the three of us came to Youlin Retreat today for another important matter."
[ZH] 千里寻不明白：“什么重要的事？为何要专门对我提起？”
[EN] Qianli Xun was puzzled. "What important matter? Why bring it up to me in particular?"
[ZH] 另两位公子也端起酒杯。
[EN] The other two young masters raised their cups as well.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“我们三人要向幽栖居士致歉！”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said, "The three of us owe Youqi Jushi an apology!"
[ZH] 三位公子一起说：“还望幽栖居士海涵！”
[EN] All three spoke in unison: "We hope Youqi Jushi will be magnanimous and forgive us!"
[ZH] 他们说完一起将酒饮下。
[EN] With that, they drained their cups together.
[ZH] 千里寻更是丈二和尚摸不着头脑：“为何要向我致歉？我不记得你们做过得罪我的事？”
[EN] Qianli Xun was even more baffled than before. "Why apologize to me? I don't recall you doing anything to offend me."
[ZH] 东方尘泽放下酒杯：“幽栖居士可还记得游西湖那晚，你酒醉后在望景亭作的新词？”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze set down his cup. "Does Youqi Jushi still remember the night we went boating on West Lake, when you got drunk and composed a new ci lyric at Wangjing Pavilion?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“当然记得，有什么不对吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. "Of course I remember. Is something wrong with it?"
[ZH] “我们三人把你的新词记下，词牌名我等尽知，可没有标题，便商量着拟了一个适合的标题……”
[EN] "The three of us wrote down your new ci. We all knew the tune title, of course, but since it had no title of its own, we talked it over and came up with a fitting one..."
[ZH] “什么标题？”千里寻担心，这首词已被后人所知，他们三个拟的标题难道是其他？
[EN] "What title?" Qianli Xun felt a pang of worry. Later generations already knew this ci lyric; could the three of them have given it some other title?
[ZH] “夏日游湖。”
[EN] "'Summer Excursion on the Lake.'"
[ZH] 千里寻宛然一笑，历史不会被任意改变，冥冥中三位公子也用“夏日游湖”四个字命名：“标题不错！你们做了件好事，为何要道歉？”
[EN] Qianli Xun broke into a gentle smile. History could not be altered on a whim; as if guided by fate, the three young masters had named it with those exact four characters, "Summer Excursion on the Lake." "It's a great title! You did a good thing, so why apologize?"
[ZH] “我们三人最喜传播幽栖居士的诗词，好不容易得了一首新词，自然会拿出去宣扬。可大家品鉴时，有人对‘和衣睡倒人怀’颇具微词，我等担心这首词一旦传开，会对幽栖居士不利。事后很是后悔将词拿出来供大家品鉴，可已拿出，他人也已知，一切无法挽回。幽栖居士若听到对你不利的见解，还请不要怪罪。”
[EN] "Nothing delights the three of us more than spreading Youqi Jushi's poetry, so once we got our hands on a new ci, we couldn't wait to share it. Yet while people were appraising it, some took issue with the line 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed.' We fear that once this lyric spreads, it could harm Youqi Jushi's reputation. Afterward we regretted putting it out for everyone to critique, but once it was out, others already knew it, and there's no taking it back. If unfavorable comments ever reach your ears, Youqi Jushi, please don't hold it against us."
[ZH] 千里寻想不通，那晚她酒醉后为何会吟出朱淑真的《清平乐·夏日游湖》？仔细想来，定是那日太过开心，又是游西湖，又是约会，自然而然会想起这首词。
[EN] Qianli Xun still could not figure out why she had recited Zhu Shuzhen's "Qingping Yue: Summer Excursion on the Lake" when she got drunk that night. Thinking back on it, she must have been too happy that day, boating on West Lake and going on a date, so the lyric had just come to mind on its own.
[ZH] 既然吟出，不怕被他人传诵，没必要在乎那么多。再说一千年后的历史已记住这首词，大家都已对它作出公正的评价，也没有人对朱淑真进行过任何批判。
[EN] Now that she had recited it, she was not afraid of people passing it around; there was no need to fret so much. Besides, a thousand years later, history still remembered this ci. Everyone had given it a fair appraisal, and nobody had condemned Zhu Shuzhen for it.
