[ZH] “我当什么事，不就是吟了一句‘和衣睡倒人怀’？我既然敢吟出，不怕被大家知道，当时你们在跟前，应该看到我并未做任何出格之事。至于我坐在文渊怀里，也无伤大雅，我与文渊的关系你们尽知。放心，即使被人非议，也绝不会怪罪你们。你们宣扬我的词本是为我好，只是欣赏诗词之人境界参差不齐，什么声音都有，你们三人怎能将罪过往自己身上揽？再说，到目前为止，我可是什么声音都没听到，呵呵……”
[EN] "I thought it was something serious! All I did was recite 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed.' If I dared say it aloud, I'm not afraid of people knowing. You were all right there at the time, so you saw with your own eyes I didn't do anything improper. As for sitting in Wenyuan's lap, what's the harm? You all know what Wenyuan and I are to each other. Relax. Even if people gossip, I'd never blame you. You spread my ci out of goodwill. People who read poetry are all over the map in taste, so of course you'll hear every sort of chatter. Why take the blame on yourselves? Besides, up to now, I haven't heard a single peep of criticism, haha..."
[ZH] 三位公子终于释怀。
[EN] At last, a weight lifted from the three young masters.
[ZH] 曹班说：“幽栖居士能如此想，我们终于可以放心。”
[EN] Cao Ban said, "Hearing Youqi Jushi see it that way puts our minds at ease—now we can rest easy."
[ZH] 乔文仙面向淑贞：“我很喜欢《夏日游湖》，会一辈子记着，如果有人玷污它，就是玷污我，我绝不会坐视不理。有我在，你什么都不用担心。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian turned to face Shuzhen. "I love 'Summer Excursion on the Lake,' and I'll remember it for the rest of my life. Anyone who slanders it slanders me, and I won't sit by and let that happen. As long as I'm here, you have nothing to worry about."
[ZH] 乔文仙的话如同春雨般滋润着她的心田，千里寻扭头看向文渊，他的眼神如此温暖，她感到自己快要被溶化。如果她的身边一直有像文渊这样的人时刻关怀着她，也许她根本不会继承千里拳。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian's words nourished her heart like spring rain. Qianli Xun turned to look at Wenyuan; his gaze was so warm she felt ready to melt. If she had always had someone like Wenyuan by her side, watching over her at every turn, perhaps she would never have inherited Qianli Fist at all.
[ZH] 母亲去世后，父亲虽也爱她，但父亲从来只会告诉她，女孩子要自立自强，要自己撑起一片天，男人根本靠不住。这种观念一直支配着她，不仅心甘情愿继承了千里拳，也努力成为网络小说家，一心扑在事业上，从不愿涉足恋爱和婚姻。
[EN] After her mother passed away, her father loved her too, yet all he ever told her was that a girl had to stand on her own two feet and hold up her own sky, because men could never be trusted. That mindset had guided her whole life. Not only had she taken up Qianli Fist of her own free will, she had worked hard to become a web novelist, pouring her whole heart into her career and refusing to touch romance or marriage.
[ZH] 这一刻，千里寻体会到有一个人在身边，对她说温暖的话，不仅暖了她的内心，也让她更不用担心可能会到来的非议，只因她的人生中已经有一个人能够与共同面对一切。
[EN] In this moment, Qianli Xun felt what it was like to have someone beside her offering gentle comfort. It did not just warm her heart; it freed her from fearing any gossip that might come, because now she had someone in her life to face everything with her.
[ZH] 正当千里寻沉浸在文渊温暖的目光中时，传来仪王的声音。
[EN] Just as Qianli Xun lost herself in Wenyuan's warm gaze, Prince Yi's voice cut in.
[ZH] “所以，还是我说的话，幽栖居士以后不要再把个人之事写在诗词里，搞得人尽皆知。你跟文渊在一起，你们两个知道即可，不要老在别人面前炫耀，还写在诗词里。可惜啊，你连文渊的名字都没提，谁知道你是倒在文渊的怀里？还是别人的怀里？只怕被人非议在所难免。”
[EN] "Which brings me back to what I said before. From now on, Youqi Jushi should stop putting private affairs into poetry for the whole world to see. If you and Wenyuan are together, it's enough that the two of you know. Stop parading it in front of others, let alone writing it into verse. What a pity: you didn't even mention Wenyuan's name. Who's to know whether it was Wenyuan's arms you fell into, or some other man's? I'm afraid gossip is inevitable."
[ZH] 申简辰着实不想看二人含情脉脉的样子，语气中多少带有批判的情绪。
[EN] Shen Jianchen could not stand watching the two of them trade tender gazes, and a distinct edge of criticism crept into his tone.
[ZH] 千里寻听后很不满，因说话的人是仪王，她不敢反驳，重要的是她有求于人，只能忍住情绪：“感谢仪王教诲……”本想解释两句，怕说出的话惹仪王不快，还是咽回肚内。
[EN] Qianli Xun bristled at that, yet since the speaker was Prince Yi, she dared not talk back. More important, she needed a favor from him, so she had to swallow her annoyance. "Thank you for your guidance, Prince Yi..." She had half a mind to explain herself, then thought better of risking his displeasure and bit the words back.
[ZH] 千里寻不再盯着文渊，用筷子夹盘子里的菜，似乎想要把菜夹得粉碎，以此来发泄不满。
[EN] Qianli Xun stopped looking at Wenyuan and stabbed at the dishes on her plate with her chopsticks, taking out her frustration as if she meant to crush the food to bits.
[ZH] 申简辰很开心，只要她不跟文渊对视，他就开心，笑着端起酒杯，慢慢品着酒。
[EN] Shen Jianchen felt delighted. As long as she was not locking eyes with Wenyuan, he was happy. Smiling, he lifted his wine cup and took a slow, savoring sip.
[ZH] 乔梦容看出幽栖居士听到哥哥的话后十分不快，怕她心里埋怨哥哥，于是说道：“我读过不少幽栖居士的诗词，都能写进我的心里，《夏日游湖》肯定也是如此，方才东方公子说的那句‘和衣睡倒人怀’，能听得出幽栖居士乃性情中人，活得率真而不虚伪，也没有闺阁女子的扭捏作态，梦容既欣赏又钦佩。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong saw how displeased Youqi Jushi looked after Prince Yi's remarks. Worried she might hold resentment toward him, she spoke up. "I've read many of Youqi Jushi's poems and ci, and every one of them speaks straight to my heart. 'Summer Excursion on the Lake' is bound to be the same. From the line Young Master Dongfang just quoted, 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed,' anyone can tell Youqi Jushi is a woman of true feeling, living with candor and without pretense, free of the coy affectations of sheltered ladies of the inner chambers. You have both Mengrong's appreciation and admiration."
[ZH] 佳和郡主就是比仪王会说话，千里寻放下筷子，露出真诚的笑容：“感谢佳和郡主赏识。”
[EN] Princess Jiahe sure knew how to talk so much better than Prince Yi. Qianli Xun set down her chopsticks and offered a genuine smile. "Thank you for your appreciation, Princess Jiahe."
[ZH] 说完向仪王看了一眼。
[EN] With that, she shot a glance at Prince Yi.
[ZH] “她终于主动看向我。”申简辰很开心，哪怕她投来的目光中看不到任何爱意，他也开心。
[EN] "She looked at me on her own at last." Shen Jianchen felt overjoyed; even if there was not a trace of affection in her gaze, he was happy all the same.
[ZH] 千里寻赶快收回目光，只因仪王露出一副极其享受的样子。
[EN] Qianli Xun tore her eyes away in a hurry, seeing Prince Yi wear an expression of sheer bliss.
[ZH] 她想起在幽林别居外，仪王看她的眼神，心里不由打了个寒战，想道：“仪王真够花心！佳和郡主这么好，还不知足？竟然对我有意思，要不是看在你是王爷，又帮过我，我又有求于你，老娘绝对会当着众人批判你是个花心大萝卜！”
[EN] Remembering the look Prince Yi had given her outside Youlin Retreat, she felt a chill run down her spine. "What a fickle flirt Prince Yi is!" she thought. "Princess Jiahe is this wonderful and he's still not satisfied? He even has ideas about me? If you weren't a prince, hadn't helped me before, and I didn't need a favor from you right now, I'd call you out right in front of everybody for the total playboy you are!"
[ZH] 转念一想，要真的批判仪王，他完全可以否认，毕竟他没说过任何话，只是眼神流露而已。
[EN] On second thought, if she really called him out, Prince Yi could deny the whole thing. After all, he had not said a word; it had only been in his eyes.
[ZH] 安明轩见大家安静，主动说：“幽栖居士，我有一事不明，还请解惑。”
[EN] Seeing everyone fall quiet, An Mingxuan spoke up. "Youqi Jushi, there is one thing I don't quite understand. Might I ask you to clear it up for me?"
[ZH] “请说。”千里寻认为安明轩的问题提得太及时，省得大家注意到仪王在看她，她更担心文渊发现，否则他会伤心。文渊伤心，她也不会开心。
[EN] "Please go ahead." Qianli Xun thought An Mingxuan's question had come at the best possible moment. It kept anyone from noticing Prince Yi staring at her; above all, she worried Wenyuan might catch on and be hurt. If Wenyuan felt sad, she would not be happy either.

#CH 92
[ZH] 安明轩说：“幽栖居士与乔兄游湖那日，明明天气晴朗，可你的词中为何要写‘恼烟撩露’，还有，早已过黄梅时节，又为何要写‘一霎黄梅细雨’？”
[EN] An Mingxuan said, "On the day Youqi Jushi and Brother Qiao went boating on the lake, the sky was clear and bright. Why, then, did your ci say 'vexing mist and teasing dew,' and since the plum rain season is long past, why write 'a passing spell of plum rain drizzle' [TN: Translator's rendering]?"
[ZH] 为何要写？千里寻哪里知道！估计朱淑真与相爱之人约会时，恰逢黄梅时节，天气不佳，又下起黄梅细雨。而她千里寻只是吟出朱淑真的词表达自己的情感而已，该如何给安公子一个合理的解释？
[EN] Why write that? How on earth would Qianli Xun know! Most likely, when the real Zhu Shuzhen went on a date with the man she loved, it had been the plum rain season, with dreary weather and plum rain drizzle falling. Qianli Xun had just recited Zhu Shuzhen's ci to voice her own feelings. How was she supposed to give Young Master An a plausible explanation?
[ZH] 正当她仔细思索之际，文渊开口说：“不知你们可否记得，淑真未有婚姻之时，我们也曾相约游西湖，正值黄梅时节，天气不佳。我记得当时天空乌云堆积，空气潮湿，偶尔会刮来凉风，后来下起雨，大家出门没带雨伞，你们三个拉着我到亭子里避雨，而徐公子则牵着淑真朝另一个方向而去，我看着他们离去的背影甚是失落……”
[EN] Just as she was racking her brains, Wenyuan spoke up. "I wonder if you all remember: before Shuzhen was married, we once went on an outing to West Lake together right in the middle of the plum rain season, when the weather was miserable. I remember dark clouds piling up overhead, damp air, and the odd gust of cool wind. Then it started to rain, and none of us had brought umbrellas. The three of you dragged me into a pavilion for shelter, while Young Master Xu took Shuzhen by the hand and led her off the other way. Watching their retreating backs left me feeling so downcast..."
[ZH] 讲到“失落”二字，乔文仙脸上还真露出失落的表情。
[EN] At the word "downcast," a look of genuine dejection crossed Qiao Wenxian's face.
[ZH] 文渊提到徐公子，千里寻担心他会伤心。文渊果然伤心，千里寻温柔地安慰道：“你又何必提起往事？我一点儿印象都没有。”
[EN] Hearing Wenyuan bring up Young Master Xu, Qianli Xun worried it would upset him. Sure enough, sadness crept over his face, and she offered him gentle comfort. "Why dig up old history? I don't remember a thing about it."
[ZH] 乔文仙很快露出笑容：“我没事。据我猜测，你吟出‘恼烟撩露’‘一霎黄梅细雨’是为了纪念那日的时光……”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian's smile returned in an instant. "I'm fine. If I had to guess, you recited 'vexing mist and teasing dew' and 'a passing spell of plum rain drizzle' to commemorate that day..."
[ZH] “根本不是这样！写词嘛，岂可全部为真？依我看，只需情感真挚，其余均可依想象而得。我之所以写黄梅时节，无非是想用懊恼的天气反衬女子喜悦的心情。”千里寻根本不愿认可文渊所说，再说朱淑真以往的经历与她没有任何关系，“各位需记住两点，其一，词的内容不一定完全实写，虚写非常必要；其二，这首词一旦被我作出，已不属于我，应属于品鉴它的人，大家没必要从中研究我的生活和情感，而是要从中品鉴出属于自己的情感。”
[EN] "That's not it at all! When writing a ci, how could every detail be literal fact? In my view, as long as the feeling is sincere, everything else can come from the imagination. I wrote of the plum rain season just to use the vexing weather as a foil for the young woman's joy." Qianli Xun refused to go along with Wenyuan's guess; besides, Zhu Shuzhen's past had nothing to do with her. "Keep two things in mind, everyone. First, a ci lyric doesn't have to be all literal fact; imagined description is essential. Second, once I compose a ci, it no longer belongs to me. It belongs to those who read and savor it. There's no need to dissect my private life and feelings through it; instead, find your own feelings within it."
[ZH] 淑真否认词中的青年男女是她与徐公子，乔文仙很快释然，看向她：“你说得对，魏晋时期陆机在《文赋》中提到‘诗缘情而绮靡’，情是诗词的根本，我们只需体味情，体味情景交融的意境之美，至于从诗词中窥探写诗作词之人的生活，则是品鉴中的小技，即使能从诗词中窥探出诗人的经历，那也只是我们看到的而已，不一定与诗人所见所闻完全相符，只因诗词中多多少少存在虚写。”
[EN] Hearing Shuzhen deny that the young couple in the ci were her and Young Master Xu, Qiao Wenxian felt immediate relief and looked at her. "You're right. During the Wei and Jin periods, Lu Ji wrote in his Rhapsody on Literature, 'Lyric poetry springs from feelings and is exquisitely ornate' [TN: Translated by David R. Knechtges]. Feeling is the very root of poetry and ci. All we need to do is savor the emotion and the beauty of scene and feeling blending into one. As for prying into a poet's private life through their verse, that is just a petty trick of literary criticism. Even if we catch glimpses of the poet's experiences, that is only what we ourselves perceive. It need not match what the poet saw or heard, because poetry and ci always contain some measure of imagined description."
[ZH] 得到文渊认可，千里寻更开心。
[EN] Winning Wenyuan's agreement made Qianli Xun even happier.
[ZH] 东方尘泽说：“受教了，是我等狭隘，偏偏要囿于那日游湖的天气状况。”
[EN] Dongfang Chenze said, "Thank you for enlightening us. How narrow-minded of us to fixate on the weather from that day on the lake."
[ZH] 申简辰听出，千里寻的话语无非是说给他听，不如说些高兴的，哄哄她：“幽栖居士和文渊的一番言论令我茅塞顿开，以后我不会再纠结幽栖居士是否在词中抒写个人生活。不过我要批评在座各位，你们上次品鉴幽栖居士的词，用的正是文渊说的‘小技’，就连文渊也如此，以后不能再这样。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen could tell Qianli Xun's words had been aimed straight at him. He might as well say something pleasant to coax her into a better mood. "What Youqi Jushi and Wenyuan just said has opened my eyes. From now on, I won't harp on whether Youqi Jushi writes about her personal life in her ci. Still, I have to scold everyone here: the last time you all analyzed Youqi Jushi's verse, you used the exact 'petty trick' Wenyuan just mentioned. Even Wenyuan himself did. Don't do that again."
[ZH] 文渊与三位公子拱手齐声说：“我等谨遵仪王教诲。”
[EN] Wenyuan and the three young masters cupped their hands in salute and answered in unison: "We shall heed Prince Yi's guidance."
[ZH] 千里寻不得不转向仪王：“感谢仪王理解。”
[EN] Qianli Xun had no choice but to turn toward Prince Yi. "Thank you for understanding, Prince Yi."
[ZH] 申简辰心里乐道：“怎么样？我厉害吧，又让你面向我，省得你老是看向文渊。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen crowed to himself: "How about that? Pretty clever of me, right? Got you facing me again, so you can't spend all day staring at Wenyuan."
[ZH] 宴饮结束后，下人们去用饭，申简辰单独回到主院。
[EN] Once the banquet ended, the servants went off to eat, and Shen Jianchen returned to the main courtyard alone.
[ZH] 三位公子第一次来幽林别居，想到处转转，乔文仙自然陪伴左右，有乔文仙的地方必然有千里寻。
[EN] Since it was the three young masters' first visit to Youlin Retreat, they wanted to stroll around and see the grounds. Qiao Wenxian went along as their host, and wherever Qiao Wenxian went, Qianli Xun was sure to follow.
[ZH] 哥哥不在，乔梦容不想陪他们走动，再说幽栖居士眼里只有乔公子，她去打扰实在不妥，喜兰去用饭，她便只身一人回到居住的院子。
[EN] With Prince Yi gone, Qiao Mengrong had no desire to tag along on their walk. Besides, Youqi Jushi had eyes only for Young Master Qiao, so intruding on them would be awkward. With Xilan off eating, Mengrong returned to her own courtyard alone.
[ZH] 千里寻陪文渊和三位公子在幽林别居转了一会儿，想起夏果之事，还是尽快求仪王帮忙：“文渊，你陪三位公子，我去拜见仪王，求他给我帮个小忙。”
[EN] After strolling around Youlin Retreat with Wenyuan and the three young masters for a while, Qianli Xun remembered Xiaguo's trouble. Better to ask Prince Yi for help as soon as possible. "Wenyuan, stay with the three young masters. I'm going to pay a call on Prince Yi and ask him for a small favor."
[ZH] “什么忙？”
[EN] "What favor?"
[ZH] “林觉看上我身边的丫鬟夏果，可夏果不同意，我爹又当着众人的面口头答应了此事……”
[EN] "Lin Jue has set his sights on my maid Xiaguo, but Xiaguo doesn't want any part of it. Yet my father gave his spoken word in front of everyone..."
[ZH] “既然林觉看上夏果，不如给他，夏果能进林家，对她来讲也算是个好去处。”
[EN] "If Lin Jue has taken a fancy to Xiaguo, why not let him have her? Entering the Lin family would be a decent outcome for her."
[ZH] “关键我不乐意，你不知道，林觉要夏果是因为夏果的身段与我有几分相似，想起来都觉得恶心，再说夏果不同意，我总不能强人所难。”
[EN] "The point is, I'm not having it. You don't know—Lin Jue only wants Xiaguo because her figure looks a bit like mine. Just thinking about it makes my stomach turn. Besides, Xiaguo said no. I can't force her against her will."
[ZH] “好，我听你的。”
[EN] "All right, I'll follow your lead."
[ZH] “你说我去求仪王，他会答应帮我吗？”
[EN] "Tell me, if I go ask Prince Yi, will he agree to help me?"
[ZH] “仪王跟其他王爷不一样，人好心好，最喜帮人，你去跟他说，他肯定答应。我要陪他们三人，你先去说，如若不成，我再缠着仪王让他答应此事。”
[EN] "Prince Yi isn't like other princes. He's kind and warmhearted, and he loves helping people out. Go ask him; he's bound to say yes. I need to stay with those three, so go ahead first. If it doesn't work, I'll pester Prince Yi myself until he agrees."
[ZH] “缠他？”
[EN] "Pester him?"
[ZH] “我与仪王关系要好，他最经不起我纠缠，绝对会一口应允。”
[EN] "Prince Yi and I are close. He can never hold out when I pester him. He'll say yes on the spot."
[ZH] 千里寻难以想象，一位王爷被一位才子纠缠的情景。她瞧一眼走在前面的三位公子：“那我去了。”
[EN] Qianli Xun could not imagine a refined scholar pestering a royal prince. She glanced at the three young masters walking ahead. "All right, I'm off."
[ZH] 乔文仙指着一个方向：“仪王住在那边的院子，你从这条路过去，若找不到方向，问问经过的下人。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian pointed in one direction. "Prince Yi stays in the courtyard over that way. Follow this path, and if you lose your way, just ask any servant passing by."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，与文渊分别后，顺着他指的方向走去。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded, parted ways with Wenyuan, and headed down the path he had indicated.
[ZH] 幽林别居不大，找起来很容易，千里寻仅向一位丫鬟问了路，便找到仪王居住的院子。应该是主院，不管是院子还是房屋都比文渊居住的地方大许多。
[EN] Youlin Retreat was not large, so finding it was easy. After asking just one maid for directions, Qianli Xun located Prince Yi's courtyard. It had to be the main courtyard; both the courtyard and the buildings were far larger than where Wenyuan stayed.
[ZH] 院门开着，千里寻小心走进，空空的院子见不到一个下人。
[EN] The courtyard gate stood open. Qianli Xun stepped inside with care; not a single servant was in sight across the empty yard.
[ZH] 她小声自语道：“我写的小说里，王爷身边绝对会有许多伺候的人，难道现实不是如此？还是仪王不喜欢被人伺候？”
[EN] She muttered under her breath, "In the novels I write, a prince always has a whole crowd of people waiting on him. Doesn't reality work like that? Or does Prince Yi just hate being waited on?"
[ZH] “幽栖居士小声嘀咕什么？”申简辰在屋内待得无聊，又想见千里寻，决定出去找文渊他们。走到门口，竟然在院中看到她，甚是欣喜，却依然拿出王爷的款儿来。
[EN] "What is Youqi Jushi muttering about under her breath?" Bored sitting indoors and eager to see Qianli Xun again, Shen Jianchen had decided to head out and find Wenyuan and the others. Reaching the doorway, he was delighted to spot her right there in the courtyard, yet he still put on his princely airs.
[ZH] 千里寻上前行礼：“拜见仪王。”
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped forward and bowed. "I pay my respects to Prince Yi."
[ZH] “又行礼？”
[EN] "Bowing again?"
[ZH] “您是王爷，我是草民，草民见王爷礼数还是要有的。”
[EN] "You are a prince, and I am a commoner. A commoner must still observe proper etiquette before a prince."
[ZH] “你为何不跟文渊在一起？却偷偷摸摸来我的院子？”
[EN] "Why aren't you with Wenyuan, instead of sneaking into my courtyard?"
[ZH] “偷偷摸摸？”千里寻心里很不爽，仪王怎么这么不会说话？算了，还是态度好点儿，毕竟有求于人：“仪王用偷偷摸摸，令淑真惶恐，我可是光明正大来拜见您。”
[EN] "Sneaking?" Qianli Xun's temper flared. How could Prince Yi have such a terrible way with words? Never mind, better keep her tone polite; after all, she needed a favor from him. "Hearing Prince Yi call it 'sneaking' makes Shuzhen uneasy. I came to call on you fair and square, in broad daylight."
[ZH] “你该不会是想起我的问题，赶来给我一个答案？”
[EN] "Don't tell me you remembered my question and hurried over to give me an answer?"
[ZH] 关于问题，千里寻还未想起，当时仪王说那问题时，她根本没用心听，事后也忘记：“仪王说的问题，我需谨慎思考，才能给您一个满意的答复，我此时来找您，有事相求。”
[EN] As for that question, Qianli Xun still had no memory of it. She had not paid much attention when Prince Yi asked it, and it had slipped her mind afterward. "Prince Yi's question requires careful thought before I can give you a satisfactory answer. I came looking for you now because I have a favor to ask."
[ZH] “有事相求？”申简辰嘴角露出笑容，千里寻何时求过他？真是新媳妇上轿头一回，他很喜欢被她麻烦的感觉，走到她跟前，“什么事？”
[EN] "A favor to ask?" A smile tugged at the corner of Shen Jianchen's mouth. When had Qianli Xun ever asked him for a favor? This was a true first, like a new bride climbing into her wedding sedan. He loved the feeling of her coming to trouble him. Walking up to her, he asked, "What is it?"
[ZH] 千里寻抬头与仪王四目相对，她感到诧异，怎么回事？为何会有一股电流闯进她的心里？千里寻赶紧垂下眼帘，心里骂道：“花心大萝卜！”
[EN] Qianli Xun looked up, and her eyes met Prince Yi's. A jolt of shock ran through her. What was going on? Why did an electric current just shoot straight into her heart? She dropped her gaze at once and cursed him in her mind: "Total playboy!"
[ZH] 嘴上却说：“仪王您身份尊贵，临安城所有人都听您的，连我爹也听您的，草民有一件小事，您只需动动嘴巴，便可解决。”
[EN] Aloud, however, she said, "Prince Yi, you hold an exalted rank. Everyone in Lin'an listens to you; even my father does. This humble commoner has a small matter you can settle with a single word."
[ZH] “到底什么事？”千里寻躲开他的眼神，说明她已明白他的心意，申简辰感到满意。
[EN] "Well, what is it?" That Qianli Xun had ducked away from his gaze meant she had caught on to his feelings, and Shen Jianchen felt satisfied.
[ZH] “林觉与我和离后，偏偏看上我身边的丫鬟夏果，夏果不同意，重要的是我也不同意，可我爹当众答应此事，不好帮我驳了林觉，而林觉总是以此给我制造麻烦……”
[EN] "After Lin Jue and I went through our mutual divorce, he had to go and set his eyes on my maid Xiaguo. Xiaguo refuses to go, and more important, I refuse too. But my father agreed to it in front of everyone, so he can't very well turn Lin Jue down for me, and Lin Jue keeps using it to stir up trouble..."
[ZH] “你想让我帮你说服林觉放了夏果，夏果的事情既可解决，你爹的颜面也保住？”
[EN] "So you want me to persuade Lin Jue to let Xiaguo go, solving Xiaguo's problem while saving your father's face?"
[ZH] “仪王果然聪明，是这样。”
[EN] "Prince Yi is sharp as ever. That's right."
[ZH] “哼！你倒是挺会盘算。”
[EN] "Hmph! You sure know how to work every angle."
[ZH] 千里寻很担心仪王不会答应：“您要是不答应，我就跪下给您磕头。”
[EN] Afraid Prince Yi might refuse, Qianli Xun said, "If you won't agree, I'll drop to my knees and kowtow to you right now."
[ZH] “应该让你的丫鬟夏果来给我磕头。念在你第一次求我，我答应你。”
[EN] "It's your maid Xiaguo who ought to come kowtow to me. Since this is the first time you've asked me for anything, I agree."
[ZH] 仪王如此爽快，令千里寻意外，礼数依然不能少：“多谢仪王！”
[EN] Prince Yi's ready agreement caught Qianli Xun off guard, though she still did not skimp on etiquette. "Many thanks, Prince Yi!"
[ZH] 申简辰上前扶住千里寻：“你能求我办事，我很开心。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped forward to steady Qianli Xun by the arms. "I'm glad you came to me for help."
[ZH] 仪王的语气温柔，加之扶着她的动作，还有她不敢接触的眼神，搞得千里寻心慌意乱：“王爷忙，我还有事。”
[EN] His gentle tone, the touch of his hands steadying her, and that gaze she dared not meet left Qianli Xun's heart racing in flustered confusion. "I'll leave Your Highness to your business. I have things to attend to."
[ZH] ﻿她仓皇逃走。
[EN] She fled in a panic.
[ZH] 申简辰望着千里寻逃出小院的背影，笑了一声：“呵呵，没想到，你竟然会害羞。”
[EN] Watching Qianli Xun race out of the courtyard, Shen Jianchen let out a chuckle. "Heh. Who would've thought you could get shy?"
[ZH] 千里寻逃出主院，见四下无人，大胆骂道：“别以为你是王爷，就可以用言语调戏老娘！要不是有求于你，老娘才不管你是谁，对我动歪心思，一拳打得你脑袋开花！”
[EN] Once out of the main courtyard, Qianli Xun checked that no one was around and let loose a bold tirade. "Don't think just because you're a prince you can flirt with me like that! If I didn't need a favor from you, I wouldn't care who you are. Get any crooked ideas about me, and one punch will split your skull wide open!"

#CH 93
[ZH] 千里寻刚自语完，听到佳和郡主的声音：“幽栖居士怎么一个人在哥哥的院外？”
[EN] The words had no sooner left Qianli Xun's mouth than Princess Jiahe's voice rang out: "Why is Youqi Jushi standing alone outside Brother's courtyard?"
[ZH] 千里寻担心自言自语的内容被郡主听到：“佳和郡主有没有听到我刚才说的话？”
[EN] Worried the princess might have overheard her muttering, Qianli Xun asked, "Did Princess Jiahe hear what I just said?"
[ZH] “你刚才说话了吗？我什么都没听到，再说只有你一个人，难道你是自说自话？”
[EN] "Were you speaking just now? I didn't hear a thing. Besides, you're all alone. Were you talking to yourself?"
[ZH] 看来对方没听到，千里寻总算放心：“我刚才是自语来着，感叹仪王真是老百姓的好王爷，我求他办事，他竟然一口答应，令我感动不已。”
[EN] Seeing she had not heard, Qianli Xun let out a quiet breath of relief. "I was talking to myself, yes, just marveling at how good Prince Yi is to the common folk—a true prince of the people. I came to ask him a favor, and he agreed on the spot. I'm moved beyond words."
[ZH] “原来如此，幽栖居士既然没跟乔公子一起，不如陪我在园子里走走？”
[EN] "I see. Since Youqi Jushi isn't with Young Master Qiao right now, how about taking a walk with me through the garden?"
[ZH] 佳和郡主邀请，千里寻不好拒绝，不如跟她聊聊，顺便提醒她仪王是个靠不住的男人：“淑真不胜荣幸。”
[EN] With Princess Jiahe extending an invitation, Qianli Xun could not very well refuse. She might as well chat with her and drop a hint that Prince Yi was not a man to be trusted. "Shuzhen would be honored."
[ZH] 乔梦容本想找哥哥，有幸碰到幽栖居士，两人又十分投缘，加之她早想结交一位闺中密友，有事没事可与密友畅聊心事，她认为幽栖居士是绝佳人选。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong had meant to look for Prince Yi, only to run into Youqi Jushi instead. The two of them hit it off so well, and she had long wished for a close female confidante with whom she could share her heart whenever she pleased. To her mind, Youqi Jushi was the ideal choice.
[ZH] 两个女子行走在别居的小径，别居内尽是些高大的树木，立秋时节更显凉爽。
[EN] The two young women strolled along the retreat's paths. Tall trees lined the grounds, making the air all the cooler now that the Beginning of Autumn had arrived.
[ZH] 行了没几步，千里寻问：“听说郡主与仪王有婚约，还是官家赐婚？”
[EN] After only a few steps, Qianli Xun asked, "I heard you and Prince Yi are engaged, Princess, and by His Majesty's own imperial betrothal?"
[ZH] 乔梦容点点头：“我与哥哥青梅竹马，一起长大，官家感念哥哥从小失去母妃，我一直在他身边照料，才赐婚，其实官家不赐婚，我也非哥哥不嫁。”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong nodded. "Brother and I grew up together as childhood sweethearts. His Majesty was touched that I stayed by his side looking after him ever since he lost his mother, the Consort, as a child, so he granted us the marriage decree. Truth be told, even without His Majesty's decree, I would never marry anyone but Brother."
[ZH] 佳和郡主如此痴情，更令千里寻为她不值：“郡主有没有想过，男人一旦有钱又有地位，身边不会只有一个女人。我朝官员好养侍妾，不瞒你说，我爹养的那些歌伎舞伎，名义上是跳舞唱歌的，其实你懂的。仪王是王爷，身份更尊贵，恐怕也会受此风气影响，难以专情……”
[EN] Princess Jiahe's deep devotion made Qianli Xun feel even worse for her. "Have you ever considered, Princess, that once a man has wealth and status, he never stops at just one woman? Officials in our dynasty love keeping concubines. To be honest with you, the singing girls and dancing girls my father keeps are hired to sing and dance in name, though you know how that goes. Prince Yi is a royal prince of even higher rank. I'm afraid he might pick up the same habits and struggle to stay faithful..."
[ZH] 乔梦容赶紧解释：“哥哥不是那样的人，他病着时，心里眼里只有我……”
[EN] Qiao Mengrong hurried to defend him. "Brother isn't like that! Back when he was ill, he had room in his heart and eyes for no one but me..."
[ZH] “好了呢？”
[EN] "And now that he's recovered?"
[ZH] “好了……好了也是如此。”乔梦容不愿承认哥哥起死回生后面对她时的变化。
[EN] "Now... now it's just the same." Qiao Mengrong could not bring herself to admit how Prince Yi had changed toward her since being brought back to life.
[ZH] 千里寻心想：“可怜郡主如此痴情，却不知她的哥哥是个花心大萝卜，喜欢到处留情，连我这种名花有主之人都不放过，实在龌龊！”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought to herself, "Poor Princess, so head over heels for him, with no idea that 'Brother' of hers is a total playboy who flirts with every skirt he sees. He won't even spare a taken woman like me! Disgusting!"
[ZH] 她继续对郡主说：“郡主跟仪王有官家赐婚，必然要嫁给他。看在我与郡主十分投缘的份上，我给你出个主意，保准你以后的日子会好过些。”
[EN] She kept talking to the princess. "Since you and Prince Yi have an imperial betrothal from His Majesty, you're bound to marry him. Because you and I get along so well, let me give you some advice. I guarantee it'll make your life ahead much easier."
[ZH] “幽栖居士请说。”
[EN] "Please speak, Youqi Jushi."
[ZH] “你将来肯定是仪王府的当家主母，以后要把主母该有的权利抓在手中，还要多攒钱，趁仪王没纳其他女子时，想方设法从他身上多捞些钱财，最好是田产铺子之类，让他转到你名下。然后再跟他生个儿子，奠定你的地位。等那些乱七八糟的女人进来后，你也不要管那么多，你管得越多，他越烦，你不管，他还觉得你懂事，然后利用他对你的好印象，再多捞些钱财，钱财总比人靠得住。”
[EN] "You're bound to become the mistress of Prince Yi's Manor one day. Keep a tight grip on every bit of authority due to the primary wife, and stash away plenty of money. Before Prince Yi takes any other women, find every way you can to squeeze wealth out of him—landed estates and shops are best—and get them put in your name. Then bear him a son to lock down your standing. Once those messy other women start moving in, don't bother policing him too hard. The more you nag, the more annoyed he'll get. Leave him alone, and he'll think you're sensible and gracious, then use his goodwill to grab even more wealth. Money is always more reliable than a man."
[ZH] 千里寻说了许多，乔梦容听着很懵，还是解释道：“我不缺钱财，除官家赐给我郡主的身份时，顺带给的赏赐之物，加之仪王府的一半财产，够我花好几辈子的，我只想跟哥哥白头偕老。”
[EN] Qianli Xun rattled off all this advice while Qiao Mengrong listened in a daze, before explaining, "I'm not short on wealth. Between the gifts His Majesty bestowed when he granted me the title of Princess and half the property of Prince Yi's Manor, I have enough to spend for several lifetimes. All I want is to grow old together with Brother."
[ZH] 千里寻继续说：“既然你不缺钱，以后嫁给仪王就从心态这一块努力，把心态放好，不管他以后纳多少女子，你都不要当回事，也不要把仪王看得太重，否则受伤的只有自己。”
[EN] Qianli Xun pressed on. "Since you don't need money, then once you marry Prince Yi, work on your mindset instead. Keep a cool head. No matter how many women he takes in later, don't let it get to you, and don't invest your whole heart in Prince Yi, or you'll be the only one getting hurt."
[ZH] “幽栖居士放心，哥哥曾说过，他只娶我一人，身边不会再有其他女子。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Youqi Jushi. Brother promised he will marry only me and never keep another woman by his side."
[ZH] 佳和郡主如此信任仪王，更令千里寻觉得仪王就是个渣男，一边欺骗温柔的妹妹，一边对其他女子放电：“唉，反正你记住我的话，这世上最靠不住的就是男人说过的话，你要多留个心眼儿。”
[EN] Seeing Princess Jiahe place such blind trust in Prince Yi convinced Qianli Xun all the more that he was a total scumbag, stringing along such a sweet, gentle girl with one hand while sending sparks at other women with the other. "Sigh. Just remember what I told you anyway. The least trustworthy thing in this world is a man's promise. Keep your wits about you."
[ZH] 乔梦容不认可幽栖居士所言，依然充满感激，幽栖居士所说都是为她考虑。第一次有人跟她畅聊男女之情，她倍感亲切，笑道：“幽栖居士可相信乔公子说过的话？”
[EN] Even though Qiao Mengrong did not agree with what Youqi Jushi said, she still felt grateful; she knew Youqi Jushi had her best interests at heart. For the first time, someone had shared such a frank heart-to-heart with her about matters between men and women, and she felt a warm closeness to her. Smiling, she asked, "Does Youqi Jushi believe what Young Master Qiao says?"
[ZH] “我……我当然相信，文渊跟别的男人不一样！”千里寻发现她一味担心佳和郡主，忘记了文渊。
[EN] "I... of course I believe him! Wenyuan isn't like other men!" Qianli Xun realized she had been so busy worrying about Princess Jiahe that she had forgotten about Wenyuan.
[ZH] “在我眼中，哥哥跟其他男人也不一样。”佳和郡主同样肯定着自己的哥哥。
[EN] "In my eyes, Brother isn't like other men either." Princess Jiahe stood by Prince Yi with the same certainty.
[ZH] 千里寻只觉恋爱中的女子智商均为零，佳和郡主如此，她是否也会如此？很快她否认自己的智商为零的想法。文渊是这个世界上最完美的男子，无懈可击，她很幸运，碰上他，还与他相爱。
[EN] Qianli Xun couldn't help thinking that women in love all had zero IQ. Princess Jiahe sure fit the bill—wait, could she be the same? In a heartbeat, she dismissed the thought that her own IQ had dropped to zero. Wenyuan was the most flawless man in the world, without a single blemish. How lucky she was to have met him and fallen in love with him.
[ZH] 游玩结束后，三位公子纷纷乘坐马车离开。乔文仙让淑真等一会儿，南双去帮她取衣裳。
[EN] Once the outing wrapped up, the three young masters boarded their carriages and departed one after another. Qiao Wenxian asked Shuzhen to wait a moment while Nan Shuang fetched her clothes.
[ZH] 原来是游湖那日放在裁缝铺的衣裳，千里寻差点儿忘记。
[EN] It turned out to be the garments left at the tailor shop on the day of their lake excursion; Qianli Xun had almost forgotten about them.
[ZH] 不一会儿，南双手中托着盒子走出大门，将盒子送到苦荞手中。
[EN] Before long, Nan Shuang came out through the main gate holding a box and handed it over to Kuqiao.
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“南双去裁缝铺取衣裳时，给你一同取回，我本想亲自送到朱府，正好你来，顺便带回去。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said, "When Nan Shuang went to the tailor shop to pick up the clothes, he brought yours back too. I'd meant to deliver them to Zhu Manor myself, so it's lucky you came; you can take them home with you now."
[ZH] “让文渊费心了。”
[EN] "I've put you to too much trouble, Wenyuan."
[ZH] “以后不许对我说这么客气的话。对了，你求仪王办事，他可答应？”
[EN] "Stop being so polite with me from now on. Oh, by the way, did Prince Yi agree to the favor you asked?"
[ZH] 千里寻点点头：“仪王倒是爽快，不过……”
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. "Prince Yi agreed without hesitation, except..."
[ZH] “不过什么？”
[EN] "Except what?"
[ZH] 千里寻大胆问道：“文渊，你真的认为仪王人很好吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun worked up her nerve and asked, "Wenyuan, do you really think Prince Yi is a good person?"
[ZH] “不然呢？他可是我最好的朋友。”
[EN] "Why wouldn't I? He's my best friend."
[ZH] “你既如此认为，我也不多说什么，反正我对他印象不好。”
[EN] "If that's what you think, I won't say any more. Anyway, I don't have a good impression of him."
[ZH] “你对他肯定有误会，没关系，以后接触时间长了，自然会清楚。”
[EN] "You must have misunderstood him. No matter—once you spend more time around him, you'll see for yourself."
[ZH] 千里寻不知该说什么，文渊一直拽着她的手，她不能立刻离开，也舍不得离开。
[EN] Qianli Xun did not know what to say. Wenyuan kept holding her hand; she could not leave right away, nor could she bear to.
[ZH] “淑真，快到七夕节，我想在七夕节那日约你一起逛街。”
[EN] "Shuzhen, Qixi Festival is almost here. I'd like to take you out strolling through the streets on Qixi."
[ZH] “好啊好啊！这么好的节日我怎么能一个人度过？一定要跟文渊在一起。”
[EN] "Yes, yes! How could I spend such a wonderful festival all alone? Of course I have to be with you, Wenyuan."
[ZH] “我想把仪王和佳和郡主邀约上，我们四个一起。”
[EN] "I was thinking of inviting Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe along, so the four of us can go together."
[ZH] “啊，还有他们。”
[EN] "Oh... them too?"
[ZH] “怎么？你不愿意？”
[EN] "What's wrong? You don't want to?"
[ZH] “我只想跟你在一起。”
[EN] "I just want to be with you."
[ZH] “本来我也是这么想的，可仪王非要跟着，没办法，他是仪王，又帮我，也帮你，不好拒绝。你不用担心，有佳和郡主在，他们是一对儿，根本不会影响我们。”
[EN] "I felt the same way at first, but Prince Yi insisted on tagging along. What can I do? He's Prince Yi, and he's helping both me and you, so it's hard to turn him down. Don't worry—Princess Jiahe will be there too. They're a couple, so they won't get in our way at all."
[ZH] 仪王真是个渣男加电灯泡，总是破坏她的好事，连七夕节都不放过。文渊提到帮忙之事，千里寻不好再说什么，只能答应让仪王同去，就当是感谢他。
[EN] Prince Yi really was both a scumbag and a third wheel, always spoiling her good times, not even sparing Qixi Festival! Still, since Wenyuan brought up the favors he was doing them, Qianli Xun could not object further and had to agree to let Prince Yi come along, treating it as a way to thank him.
[ZH] 想起感谢一事，不由说道：“仪王很喜欢听书，不如到那日，我请仪王和佳和郡主去勾栏瓦肆听书，感谢他给我帮忙。”
[EN] Thinking of thanking him, she spoke up: "Prince Yi loves listening to storytellers. Why don't I treat Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe to a storytelling session at the Goulan Wasi that day to thank him for helping me?"
[ZH] “主意不错，他确实喜欢听，尤其喜欢听一位叫千里寻的才子写的故事，也不知什么时候临安突然出现一位写话本的才子？”
[EN] "Great idea. He does love it—above all, he loves stories written by some gifted writer named Qianli Xun. No idea when a talented huaben author like that popped up out of nowhere in Lin'an."
[ZH] 千里寻有意问道：“他现在很出名吗？”
[EN] Qianli Xun probed on purpose: "Is he famous right now?"
[ZH] “出不出名不知道，听仪王提起过。”
[EN] "I wouldn't know if he's famous; I only heard Prince Yi mention him."
[ZH] “你说他未来的名气会不会盖过文渊你？”
[EN] "Do you think his fame might eclipse yours someday, Wenyuan?"
[ZH] “那不至于，大家的喜好不一样，他以写话本为主，我则是专攻诗词品鉴，互不相扰。”
[EN] "I doubt it. People have different tastes. He focuses on writing huaben stories, while I specialize in poetry and ci criticism. Our paths don't cross."
[ZH] “文渊喜不喜欢看话本？”
[EN] "Do you like reading huaben stories, Wenyuan?"
[ZH] “我从不看话本，除非你写话本，我才会看，可你曾在我面前说，最瞧不上下里巴人之作，所以我从不会看话本，偶尔没事听听，消遣一下。”
[EN] "I never read huaben—unless you wrote one, then I'd read it. But you once told me yourself that you look down on lowbrow works more than anything, so I never read them. Now and then I'll listen to a storyteller when I have spare time, just to pass the hours."
[ZH] 原来文渊是受朱淑真影响，所谓的知己不过是文渊单方面的付出而已。
[EN] So Wenyuan had been shaped by Zhu Shuzhen's tastes all along; this supposed meeting of soulmates had just been Wenyuan giving everything from his side.
[ZH] “可我现在认为，下里巴人之作也许会在未来得到主流人士的重视，就像词在唐代并不被大家重视，经过柳三变、东坡居士等人的努力，现已登上大雅之堂。以后，我不会再瞧不上落魄文人写的话本。”
[EN] "Now, though, I think lowbrow works might one day win respect from the mainstream, just like ci poetry got little respect back in the Tang dynasty, yet through the efforts of Liu Sanbian, Dongpo Jushi, and others, it has now ascended to the halls of refined literature. From now on, I won't look down on huaben stories written by down-and-out scholars anymore."
[ZH] “既然你态度改变，我当然会顺着你。”
[EN] "Since you've had a change of heart, I'll follow your lead, of course."
[ZH] 两人依依不舍，苦荞见小姐还没有要走的意思，提醒道：“小姐，该回去了。”
[EN] Neither wanted to part. Seeing her mistress show no sign of leaving, Kuqiao stepped in to remind her: "Miss, it's time to head back."
[ZH] 千里寻只好与文渊分别，上了马车。
[EN] Qianli Xun had no choice but to bid Wenyuan farewell and climb into the carriage.
[ZH] ﻿苦荞手捧盒子，要登上马车时，发现白风来到大门口，向她张望。
[EN] Holding the box in her arms, Kuqiao was about to board the carriage when she spotted Bai Feng walking up to the main gate, peering out toward her.
[ZH] 苦荞心中埋怨：“我都要走，你才来。”接着后悔为何不晚些催促小姐，搞得自己想多看白风几眼都没时间。
[EN] Kuqiao grumbled to herself: "I'm just about to leave and now you show up." Then she kicked herself for not waiting a bit longer to hurry her mistress, leaving no time to steal a few more glances at Bai Feng.
[ZH] “苦荞，你怎么不上马车？”千里寻从车窗探出脑袋。
[EN] "Kuqiao, why aren't you getting in?" Qianli Xun poked her head out of the carriage window.
[ZH] 苦荞向白风瞧了一眼，走上去。
[EN] Kuqiao cast one more glance at Bai Feng and climbed aboard.
[ZH] 千里寻又对文渊笑道：“七夕节见。”
[EN] Qianli Xun smiled at Wenyuan once more. "See you on Qixi Festival."
[ZH] “下次见你时，我会带给你一个大大的好消息。”乔文仙与白风站在原地，目送马车渐行渐远。
[EN] "Next time I see you, I'll bring you a huge piece of good news." Qiao Wenxian and Bai Feng stood where they were, watching the carriage roll farther and farther into the distance.

#CH 94
[ZH] 淑真乘坐的马车已消失在视线中，乔文仙转过身，发现白风站在门口向远处张望，他并未多想。
[EN] Once Shuzhen's carriage had vanished from sight, Qiao Wenxian turned around and saw Bai Feng standing by the gate, gazing off into the distance. He thought nothing of it.
[ZH] “仪王在做什么？”
[EN] "What is Prince Yi doing?"
[ZH] 乔公子问话，白风不好再继续张望：“王爷在屋里待着。”
[EN] With Young Master Qiao asking, Bai Feng could not keep craning his neck. "His Highness is inside his room."
[ZH] “随我一起去找仪王。”
[EN] "Come with me to see Prince Yi."
[ZH] 白风又朝远处瞧一眼，才跟乔文仙进入别居。他们来到主院的屋内，仪王正坐在椅子上发呆，时不时露出笑容。
[EN] Bai Feng stole one last glance down the road before following Qiao Wenxian back into the retreat. When they entered the chamber in the main courtyard, Prince Yi was sitting in a chair in a daze, breaking into a smile every now and then.
[ZH] 乔文仙靠近后，用手在仪王眼前晃了晃，仪王竟然没有任何反应。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stepped closer and waved a hand in front of Prince Yi's eyes, yet Prince Yi did not react at all.
[ZH] 乔文仙扭头向白风询问：“仪王怎么了？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian turned his head to ask Bai Feng, "What's wrong with Prince Yi?"
[ZH] 白风摇摇头。
[EN] Bai Feng shook his head.
[ZH] 申简辰正沉浸着回味千里寻前来求他办事的过程，尤其是他扶住她，她仓皇逃跑的情景。猛然发觉有人站在面前，才回过神来：“哦，文渊，你怎么来了？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen was lost in savoring the memory of Qianli Xun coming to ask him for a favor, above all the moment he steadied her and she bolted in a panic. Startled to notice someone standing right in front of him, he snapped back to his senses. "Oh, Wenyuan. What brings you here?"
[ZH] 乔文仙挑椅子坐下：“仪王在想什么？这么专注？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian picked a chair and sat down. "What was Prince Yi thinking about so hard?"
[ZH] “没什么。”
[EN] "Nothing."
[ZH] 申简辰面对文渊很自然萌生出愧疚之情，要是文渊知道他们喜欢同一个人，不知他会作何感想？
[EN] Facing Wenyuan, Shen Jianchen felt a rush of guilt rise in his chest. If Wenyuan ever found out they were in love with the same woman, what would he think?
[ZH] 很快，申简辰为自己找到充分的理由，文渊喜欢的是朱淑真，他喜欢的是千里寻。
[EN] Before long, Shen Jianchen came up with a neat excuse for himself: Wenyuan loved Zhu Shuzhen, while he loved Qianli Xun.
[ZH] 可又仔细一想，现在的朱淑真就是千里寻，千里寻就是朱淑真，很难分得清楚，就像分不清申简辰是赵朴，还是赵朴是申简辰一样。
[EN] Yet thinking it through, the Zhu Shuzhen standing here today was Qianli Xun, and Qianli Xun was Zhu Shuzhen. There was no telling them apart, just as he could no longer tell whether Shen Jianchen was Zhao Pu, or Zhao Pu was Shen Jianchen.
[ZH] 乔文仙说：“我想请仪王殿下明日随我回趟乔府。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian said, "I'd like to ask Your Highness to come back to Qiao Manor with me tomorrow."
[ZH] “回乔府？”申简辰猜到，文渊是想请他说服乔家长辈到朱府提亲。
[EN] "Back to Qiao Manor?" Shen Jianchen guessed at once that Wenyuan wanted him to persuade the Qiao family elders to send a marriage proposal to Zhu Manor.
[ZH] 乔文仙用恳切的语气说：“我想与淑真早些成亲，希望仪王帮我说服父亲。”
[EN] In an earnest tone, Qiao Wenxian said, "I want to marry Shuzhen soon, and I'm hoping Prince Yi will help me convince my father."
[ZH] 从内心来讲，申简辰很不乐意，可现实决定他不得不同意。一来他已主动答应文渊要帮他；二来他与千里寻根本不可能，莫说官家赐婚挡在面前，千里寻对文渊痴情的劲头，也足以使申简辰望而却步。
[EN] Deep down, Shen Jianchen hated the idea, yet reality left him no choice but to agree. For one thing, he had already volunteered to help Wenyuan. For another, he and Qianli Xun had no chance anyway. Never mind His Majesty's marriage decree standing in the way, Qianli Xun's head-over-heels devotion to Wenyuan was enough to stop Shen Jianchen in his tracks.
[ZH] 可要千里寻要成为别人的妻子，申简辰根本不愿意。该如何是好？他矛盾着。
[EN] Still, the thought of Qianli Xun becoming another man's wife was more than Shen Jianchen could bear. What was he supposed to do? He sat torn in two.
[ZH] 乔文仙担忧道：“仪王该不会想反悔？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian looked worried. "Prince Yi isn't thinking of going back on his word, is he?"
[ZH] 申简辰很想说出反悔的话，想到与梦容的婚约，已到喉咙的话又被痛苦地咽下。
[EN] Shen Jianchen longed to take it back, yet the thought of his engagement to Mengrong caught the words in his throat, and he forced them back down with an ache in his chest.
[ZH] 有段无法违背的婚约限制，他又如何能给千里寻带来幸福？不如成全她与文渊，只要她开心快乐便好。
[EN] Bound by an engagement he could not break, how could he ever bring Qianli Xun happiness? Better to help her and Wenyuan be together. As long as she was happy, that would be enough.
[ZH] “说出去的话，岂能收回？我答应你，明日随你回乔府。”
[EN] "How could I go back on words I've already spoken? You have my word—I'll go back to Qiao Manor with you tomorrow."
[ZH] 乔文仙兴奋地起身抱住仪王：“就知道你最好！”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian shot to his feet in excitement and threw his arms around Prince Yi. "I knew you were the best!"
[ZH] 申简辰高兴不起来，为自己没有资格从文渊身边抢走千里寻而伤感。
[EN] Shen Jianchen could not muster a shred of cheer, heavy with sorrow that he had no right to take Qianli Xun away from Wenyuan.
[ZH] “行了行了，别抱着我，你跟幽栖居士都来求我办事，我堂堂王爷，竟然成为你们两人的办事员，被你们差遣，实在有失身份，我决定，以后不管你们求我办什么事，一概不答应。”
[EN] "All right, all right, let go of me! Both you and Youqi Jushi come running to me for favors. Here I am, a dignified royal prince, reduced to your personal errand boy to be ordered around. It's beneath my station! I've made up my mind: from now on, no matter what favors you two ask of me, the answer is a flat no."
[ZH] 乔文仙放开仪王，笑道：“放心，此事之后，我不会再麻烦你。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian let go of Prince Yi with a laugh. "Don't worry. After this, I won't bother you again."
[ZH] “可是你说的，千万别食言！”申简辰努力挤出笑容。
[EN] "You said it—don't you dare break your word!" Shen Jianchen forced a smile onto his face.
[ZH] 乔文仙仔细观察仪王的表情：“你看着心情不佳，怎么回事？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian studied Prince Yi's face. "You look out of sorts. What's going on?"
[ZH] “你跟幽栖居士都来求我办事，让我好烦，心情怎么可能好？”申简辰起身，将文渊往出推，“不要再问，赶紧回你的屋，让我安静安静。”
[EN] "Both you and Youqi Jushi keep pestering me for favors. It's driving me nuts. How could I be in a good mood?" Shen Jianchen stood up and pushed Wenyuan toward the door. "Quit asking questions, get back to your own room, and give me some peace and quiet."
[ZH] 乔文仙被推出门外，拱手道：“谢过仪王，七夕那日，淑真想请你听说书，应该是千里寻写的话本，你肯定喜欢。”
[EN] Shoved out the door, Qiao Wenxian cupped his hands in salute. "My thanks, Prince Yi. On Qixi Festival, Shuzhen wants to treat you to a storytelling show. It ought to be one of Qianli Xun's huaben stories—you're sure to love it."
[ZH] 又有机会见到她，申简辰充满期待：“告诉她不许反悔。”
[EN] Another chance to see her filled Shen Jianchen with eager anticipation. "Tell her she's not allowed to back out."
[ZH] “淑真说话一向不会食言。”乔文仙又面露疑惑，“有一事我不明白，你为何不让白风再去找千里寻？难道仪王已经找到他？”
[EN] "Shuzhen never breaks her word." A puzzled look crossed Qiao Wenxian's face. "There's one thing I don't understand, though. Why did you stop having Bai Feng search for Qianli Xun? Has Prince Yi found him already?"
[ZH] 申简辰答应过千里寻要保密，当然不会告诉文渊。这个秘密是他与千里寻唯一共同拥有的，岂能与他人分享？
[EN] Shen Jianchen had promised Qianli Xun to keep it a secret, so of course he would not tell Wenyuan. This secret was the only thing he and Qianli Xun shared. How could he share it with anyone else?
[ZH] “都说了，别烦我，怎么还这么聒噪？”
[EN] "I already told you to leave me alone! Why are you still jabbering?"
[ZH] 仪王已答应帮忙，没必要再缠着他，既然他想静一静，还是不要继续打扰下去。乔文仙笑了笑，转身出了主院。
[EN] Prince Yi had already agreed to help, so there was no need to keep pestering him. If he wanted some peace and quiet, better to leave him be. Qiao Wenxian smiled, turned, and left the main courtyard.
[ZH] 文渊走后，申简辰走到院中，站在千里寻求他帮忙时站过的地方，似乎那里还保留着她身上的气息，他闭上眼睛用心闻了一会儿……
[EN] After Wenyuan left, Shen Jianchen walked out into the courtyard and stood on the exact spot where Qianli Xun had stood when she asked for his help. As if her scent still lingered there, he closed his eyes and breathed it in for a quiet moment...
[ZH] 闻够后，睁开眼，见白风站在他身旁，似乎有话要说。
[EN] Once he had his fill, he opened his eyes and found Bai Feng standing beside him, looking as if he had something to say.
[ZH] “你有事？”
[EN] "You need something?"
[ZH] “王爷，我喜欢苦荞？”
[EN] "Your Highness, I... I like Kuqiao?"
[ZH] “幽栖居士的贴身丫鬟？”
[EN] "Youqi Jushi's personal maid?"
[ZH] “是的，王爷能不能帮我提亲？”
[EN] "Yes. Could Your Highness ask for her hand on my behalf?"
[ZH] 申简辰批评道：“一日之内已有两人来求我，你又来？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen chided him: "Two people have already come begging favors of me today, and now you too?"
[ZH] “我担心幽栖居士把苦荞许给别人。”白风显得很委屈。
[EN] "I'm afraid Youqi Jushi might marry Kuqiao off to someone else." Bai Feng looked downright aggrieved.
[ZH] 申简辰安慰道：“现在提亲尚早，等时机成熟我自会帮你。你也不必担心，幽栖居士是开明之人，许配身边的丫鬟，必然会征求丫鬟的意见。我观察苦荞那丫鬟对你也有情，自然不会旁落他人。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen reassured him. "It's still too soon to ask for her hand. Once the time is right, I'll handle it for you. And don't fret—Youqi Jushi is open-minded. She'd never marry off her own maid without asking what the girl wants. From what I've seen, that maid Kuqiao has feelings for you too, so she won't end up with anyone else."
[ZH] 白风央求道：“王爷一定要答应我，等您跟郡主成亲后，就帮我提亲。”
[EN] Bai Feng pleaded, "You have to promise me, Your Highness. As soon as you and the Princess are married, you'll ask for her hand on my behalf."
[ZH] 提到与梦容的婚事，申简辰又露出愁容，叹口气：“唉，人生之事十有八九不如意。好，我答应你。”
[EN] At the mention of his marriage to Mengrong, gloom settled back over Shen Jianchen's face. He let out a sigh. "Ah, eight or nine things out of ten in life never go the way we want. Fine, you have my promise."
[ZH] 白风想不通，人生之事不如意与答应为他提亲有什么关系？只要王爷答应，他便高兴：“多谢王爷！”
[EN] Bai Feng could not fathom what life's disappointments had to do with asking for Kuqiao's hand for him, though as long as His Highness agreed, he was thrilled. "Thank you, Your Highness!"
[ZH] “随我去看看神柳。”申简辰想要到神柳跟前静一静，思考些事情。
[EN] "Come with me to see the Divine Willow." Shen Jianchen wanted to sit in peace before the Divine Willow and think things through.
[ZH] “是。”
[EN] "Yes, Your Highness."
[ZH] 去往神柳路上，白风一直在想，王爷为何总是喜欢到神柳下坐？他看不出那棵粗壮的柳树有什么特别之处，除满树挂些乱七八糟的红绸，实在普通不过，哪怕生长了千年，也不过是一棵树而已。
[EN] On the way to the Divine Willow, Bai Feng kept wondering why His Highness always loved sitting beneath it. He could not see anything special about that thick-trunked willow. Aside from a messy tangle of red silk ribbons hanging all over its branches, it looked plain as dirt. Even if it had stood for a thousand years, it was still just a tree.
[ZH] 来到神柳之下，申简辰又坐在石头上，靠着树干。凉意比之前明显许多，毕竟是立秋时节。
[EN] Arriving beneath the Divine Willow, Shen Jianchen sat back down on the boulder and leaned against the trunk. The chill in the air felt much sharper than before; it was the Beginning of Autumn, after all.
[ZH] 秋天刚到，神柳的叶子还未出现发黄的迹象。能够想象到，过不了多久，一树的绿叶必然枯黄凋零。
[EN] Autumn had only just arrived, so the Divine Willow's leaves showed no sign of yellowing yet. Still, he could picture how, before long, the whole canopy of green would wither, turn yellow, and fall away.
[ZH] 他对千里寻的爱如果也能像叶子一样枯黄凋零那该多好，他就不会这么痛苦，会心甘情愿接受新的命运给他安排的一切。
[EN] If only his love for Qianli Xun could wither and fall away like those leaves! Then he would not hurt like this; he could accept everything this new destiny had arranged for him with a willing heart.
[ZH] 恰恰相反，越是得不到千里寻，对她的爱越强烈，越发难以放下，还要忍着痛帮助文渊，他的心情有几人能体会得到！
[EN] Quite the opposite: the more out of reach Qianli Xun remained, the fiercer his love for her burned, and the harder it became to let go. On top of that, he had to swallow the pain and help Wenyuan win her. How many people could ever fathom what he was going through!
[ZH] 申简辰默默与神柳沟通：“神柳，你帮我找到千里寻，可她却爱上文渊，我又有婚姻牵绊，可我依然爱着她，比以往任何时候都爱，我该怎么办？是想尽办法让她爱上我，与我在一起，还是就此放手，各自安好？如果是第一种，请你掉下一片叶子，如果是第二种，请你掉下两片叶子。”
[EN] In silence, Shen Jianchen spoke to the Divine Willow in his mind: "Divine Willow, you helped me find Qianli Xun, yet she fell in love with Wenyuan, and I'm tied down by an engagement. Even so, I still love her, more than I ever have. What should I do? Should I try every means I have to make her love me and be with me, or should I let go right here and wish each other well? If it's the first, please drop one leaf. If it's the second, please drop two."
[ZH] 申简辰伸出手掌，期待着有叶片落入他的掌心……
[EN] Shen Jianchen held out his open palm, waiting in hope for a leaf to drift into his hand...
[ZH] 白风在不远处，见仪王伸出手掌，甚觉奇怪，小声自问：“王爷不会脑子有问题吧？为何要伸出手掌？难道想让神柳落下金元宝？可神柳不是摇钱树，王爷也不缺钱，到底是怎么回事？”
[EN] Standing not far off, Bai Feng found it bizarre to see Prince Yi holding out his palm and muttered to himself, "Has His Highness lost his mind? Why is he sticking his hand out? Does he expect the Divine Willow to drop gold ingots? The Divine Willow isn't a money tree, and His Highness isn't short on cash either. What on earth is going on?"
[ZH] 白风又观察了一会儿，发现仪王的手臂伸得酸困，打算抽回时，一片柳树的叶子飘落下来，不偏不倚掉落在他的手掌中。
[EN] Bai Feng watched a while longer. Just as Prince Yi's arm grew sore from holding it out and he was about to pull it back, a single willow leaf drifted down and landed smack in the middle of his palm.
[ZH] 只见仪王兴奋地站起来，大声说道：“一片！是一片！看来你也支持我努力与她在一起！太好了！”
[EN] Prince Yi sprang to his feet in delight and shouted, "One! Just one! So you're on my side too, telling me to fight to be with her! Wonderful!"
[ZH] 申简辰捏起那片柳树的叶子，仔细端详，像是捡到宝贝一般。
[EN] Shen Jianchen pinched the willow leaf between his fingers and studied it from every angle, as if he had just found a priceless treasure.
[ZH] 这时，白风看到又落下一片叶子，刚好掉在仪王的肩头：“王爷，又落下一片，在您肩膀上。”
[EN] Just then, Bai Feng saw another leaf drift down and land right on Prince Yi's shoulder. "Your Highness, another one fell—right on your shoulder."
[ZH] 白风的声音像一把威力强大的扫帚，将申简辰刚刚到来的兴奋一扫而光，他不愿承认：“你胡说！只有一片，哪有第二片！”
[EN] Bai Feng's voice acted like a mighty broom, sweeping away the burst of joy that had just reached Shen Jianchen. Refusing to accept it, he snapped, "Nonsense! There was only one! What second leaf?!"
[ZH] 白风靠近仪王，从他的肩头捏起另一片叶子，送到面前：“呶，王爷自己看。”
[EN] Bai Feng stepped over, plucked the second leaf off his master's shoulder, and held it out in front of him. "Here, look for yourself, Your Highness."
[ZH] 申简辰盯着不想看到的第二片叶子，很不情愿地从白风指间捏走，心情变得无比沉重。
[EN] Staring at the second leaf he had prayed not to see, Shen Jianchen pinched it from Bai Feng's fingers in bitter reluctance, a crushing weight sinking over his chest.
[ZH] ﻿“放手……放手……原来你劝我放手……”申简辰自语着，显得很痛苦。
[EN] "Let go... let go... So you're telling me to let go..." Shen Jianchen murmured to himself, raw pain etched across his face.
[ZH] 白风被吓坏，不就是多了一片柳树的叶子，仪王为何像要哭的样子，眼睛还红红的？
[EN] Bai Feng froze in alarm. It was just one extra willow leaf; why did Prince Yi look on the verge of tears, with both eyes rimmed red?
[ZH] “王爷，我是不是做错了？”
[EN] "Your Highness, did I do something wrong?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有说话，踉跄着步子往回走，两片柳树的叶子从他的手中飘落在树下，他边走边自语：“好吧，你既让我放下，我就放下，各自安好。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said nothing. He stumbled back along the path as the two willow leaves slipped from his fingers and drifted down beneath the tree. Walking on, he murmured to himself, "Fine. Since you tell me to let go, I'll let go. May we each find our own peace."
[ZH] 白风跟上去，不敢说任何话。
[EN] Bai Feng trailed after him, not daring to utter a single word.

#CH 95
[ZH] 再次回到乔府，乔文仙身边有仪王陪伴，父亲乔守仁恭敬地接待了仪王，还亲自上茶桌为仪王做茶。
[EN] Returning to Qiao Manor once more, Qiao Wenxian had Prince Yi at his side. His father, Qiao Shouren, received Prince Yi with full deference and even stepped up to the tea table himself to prepare whisked tea for the prince.
[ZH] 因两片柳叶影响，昨晚申简辰未睡好，精神不佳，可他已将一切理顺，必须接受落下两片柳叶的现实。
[EN] Shaken by those two willow leaves, Shen Jianchen had spent a restless night and felt low on energy, yet he had sorted everything out in his head. He had to accept the reality of those two falling willow leaves.
[ZH] “文渊，你肯定没把仪王照顾好。”乔守仁一边做茶一边批评。
[EN] "Wenyuan, you must not have taken proper care of Prince Yi," Qiao Shouren scolded as he prepared the tea.
[ZH] 乔文仙急了：“父亲说的哪里话，我对仪王好的不能再好，也经常亲自给他做茶。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian hurried to protest. "What are you talking about, Father? I couldn't treat Prince Yi any better. I even prepare tea for him myself all the time."
[ZH] “为何仪王看上去有些憔悴？”乔守仁不依不饶。
[EN] "Then why does Prince Yi look a bit worn out?" Qiao Shouren pressed on.
[ZH] 乔文仙看向仪王：“一早起来，我也觉纳闷，昨日还好好的，为何今日显得憔悴？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian looked at Prince Yi. "I was puzzled myself this morning. You were fine yesterday; why do you look so haggard today?"
[ZH] 申简辰解释道：“外公别责怪文渊，是我昨晚没休息好。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen explained, "Don't blame Wenyuan, Grandfather. I just didn't rest well last night."
[ZH] 乔文仙问：“仪王向来睡眠质量好，为何偏偏昨晚失眠？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian asked, "Prince Yi always sleeps like a log. Why suffer insomnia last night of all nights?"
[ZH] 申简辰随口回道：“想着公务之事。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen tossed off a quick answer: "I had official business on my mind."
[ZH] 乔文仙疑惑：“仪王何时关心过公务？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian blinked in disbelief. "Since when has Prince Yi cared about official business?"
[ZH] 乔守仁又批评道：“文渊，怎么跟仪王说话？仪王作为临安节度使，官职在临安知府之上，操心的事情自然多。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren rebuked him again. "Wenyuan, is that any way to speak to Prince Yi? As Military Commissioner of Lin'an, Prince Yi outranks the Prefect of Lin'an. Of course he has plenty on his plate."
[ZH] 乔文仙笑着不言语。
[EN] Qiao Wenxian smiled and kept quiet.
[ZH] 申简辰并不避讳：“外公，文渊讲得没错。在我没来之前，临安的军务政务运营顺畅，何须我一来就插手？所以，我一概不理会，官员们该如何便如何。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen made no attempt to hide it. "Grandfather, Wenyuan is right. Before I got here, Lin'an's military and civil affairs were running without a hitch. Why should I stick my nose in the moment I arrive? So I stay out of all of it and let the officials carry on as they always have."
[ZH] 乔守仁边做茶边说：“仪王不插手有不插手的好处，只怕官家不满。官家派仪王担任临安节度使一职，定是大有深意。”
[EN] As he prepared the tea, Qiao Shouren said, "Staying hands-off has its merits, yet I fear His Majesty might be displeased. His Majesty must have had deep intentions in appointing Prince Yi as Military Commissioner of Lin'an."
[ZH] 申简辰笑道：“外公想多了，只因我把官家吹捧得好，蹴鞠又赢了高太尉，官家一高兴才给我安排临安节度使一职，何来深意？至于官家不满，外公大可放心，只要临安的官员们高兴，绝不会让官家知道我的真实状况，他又何来不满？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen laughed. "You're overthinking it, Grandfather. I only got the post because I flattered His Majesty well and beat Grand Commandant Gao at cuju, so His Majesty put me in charge as Military Commissioner of Lin'an in a fit of good humor. What deep intentions could there be? As for displeasing His Majesty, put your mind at ease. As long as Lin'an's officials are happy, they'll never let word of what I'm really up to reach His Majesty. How could he be displeased?"
[ZH] “说得也是。”乔守仁已把茶做好，吩咐旁边的丫鬟给仪王捧上，接着又给文渊做。
[EN] "Fair enough." Having finished preparing the tea, Qiao Shouren instructed a maid beside him to serve it to Prince Yi, then began making a bowl for Wenyuan.
[ZH] “父亲，还是我来。”乔文仙走到茶桌前，“儿子岂敢麻烦您亲自做茶，您去陪仪王聊天儿，他今日有重要的事情与您说。”
[EN] "Let me do that, Father." Qiao Wenxian stepped up to the tea table. "How could your son trouble you to make tea for me? Go sit and chat with Prince Yi. He has something important to discuss with you today."
[ZH] 乔守仁笑着起身，把茶桌让给文渊。
[EN] Qiao Shouren rose with a smile, yielding the tea table to Wenyuan.
[ZH] 仪王坐在正位，他自然挑左边下位坐了。丫鬟见状，将泡好的九曲红梅为他摆上。
[EN] Prince Yi sat in the seat of honor, so Qiao Shouren took the lower seat to the left as a matter of course. Seeing this, a maid set a cup of steeped Jiuqu Hongmei tea before her master.
[ZH] 申简辰品着外公亲自做的茶，想着如何开口说服他同意到朱家提亲。
[EN] Sipping the tea his grandfather had prepared with his own hands, Shen Jianchen mulled over how to broach the subject and convince him to propose marriage to the Zhu family.
[ZH] 乔守仁问：“我做的茶如何？仪王可喜欢？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren asked, "How is the tea I made? Does it suit Prince Yi's taste?"
[ZH] “口感细腻，比文渊做得好。”
[EN] "Smooth and delicate on the palate, better than Wenyuan's."
[ZH] “仪王喜欢喝就好。”
[EN] "I'm glad Prince Yi enjoys it."
[ZH] “其实我昨晚没休息好，不是因为想着公务之事，方才是随口说说而已。”申简辰观察乔守仁。
[EN] "Truth be told, I didn't miss sleep last night over official business. I just said that off the cuff." Shen Jianchen studied Qiao Shouren's face.
[ZH] 乔守仁问：“不知因何事仪王未休息好？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren asked, "May I ask what kept Prince Yi from resting well?"
[ZH] “昨晚之所以没休息好，是因为文渊求我办事，幽栖居士也求我办事，我一直在想如何帮他们，搞得一晚上辗转反侧，白天才显得憔悴许多。”
[EN] "I couldn't sleep last night because Wenyuan came asking for my help, and Youqi Jushi came asking for my help too. I tossed and turned all night trying to figure out how to help them both, which is why I look so worn out today."
[ZH] 听到“幽栖居士”的称谓，乔守仁猜到朱家姑娘定是去过幽林别居，只怕与文渊感情越来越深。
[EN] Hearing the name "Youqi Jushi," Qiao Shouren guessed the Zhu family's daughter must have visited Youlin Retreat, and he feared her bond with Wenyuan was growing deeper by the day.
[ZH] “年轻人不懂事，还望仪王见谅。也不要把他们的事太当回事，都是些小事，仪王应该多操心临安大事。”
[EN] "Young folk don't know their manners; please forgive them, Prince Yi. Don't take their trifles to heart either. Those are all minor matters. Prince Yi ought to focus on Lin'an's grand affairs instead."
[ZH] “外公错了，难道文渊的婚姻大事不是大事？”
[EN] "You're mistaken, Grandfather. Isn't Wenyuan's marriage a grand affair?"
[ZH] 乔守仁立刻明白，仪王今日是为文渊的亲事而来。
[EN] Qiao Shouren understood in an instant: Prince Yi had come today on behalf of Wenyuan's marriage arrangement.
[ZH] “文渊的婚姻大事只是乔家的大事，跟临安大事比起来，不值一提。”
[EN] "Wenyuan's marriage is only a big matter for the Qiao family. Compared with the grand affairs of Lin'an, it isn't worth a mention."
[ZH] “外公此言差异，乔家视我为亲人，我便是乔家一分子，文渊的婚事对我来讲也是大事。”
[EN] "I have to disagree, Grandfather. The Qiao family treats me as one of their own, so I'm a member of the Qiao family too. That makes Wenyuan's marriage a major affair to me as well."
[ZH] 仪王说他是乔家一分子，说得乔守仁十分受用：“感谢仪王关怀文渊。”
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi call himself a member of the Qiao family pleased Qiao Shouren to no end. "Thank you for caring so much about Wenyuan, Prince Yi."
[ZH] “外公不必对我客气。”申简辰直接问，“文渊喜欢朱家姑娘，外公已知，不知您是什么态度？”
[EN] "No need to stand on ceremony with me, Grandfather." Shen Jianchen cut straight to the point: "You already know Wenyuan is in love with the Zhu family's daughter. Where do you stand on it?"
[ZH] 乔守仁沉默了一会儿，回道：“不瞒仪王，我对朱家姑娘不甚满意，她和离过一次，而文渊从未涉足婚姻，娶她岂不是亏大发？传出去大家也会笑话乔家。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren fell silent for a moment before answering. "To be blunt with Prince Yi, I'm not too thrilled with Miss Zhu. She has been through a mutual divorce, whereas Wenyuan has never been married. Wouldn't marrying her be a huge loss for him? Once word gets out, everyone will make a laughingstock of the Qiao family."
[ZH] 乔文仙急了，放下茶筅，已无心做茶：“父亲，您又何必在乎世俗眼光，我与淑真互相有情才最重要。”
[EN] Panic hit Qiao Wenxian. He set down the tea whisk, losing all heart for making tea. "Father, why care about what the world thinks? What matters most is that Shuzhen and I love each other."
[ZH] “情算个什么玩意儿？到我这个年纪你就会明白，年轻时的风花雪月都是扯淡！你还是现实些。”乔守仁顾不上仪王坐在自家厅堂，更加严厉地批评儿子。
[EN] "What is love worth anyway?! When you reach my age, you'll see all that youthful romance is utter nonsense! Be realistic." Forgetting that Prince Yi sat right there in his main hall, Qiao Shouren tore into his son with even harsher sternness.
[ZH] 外公的态度，让申简辰感到些许快意。
[EN] His grandfather's attitude gave Shen Jianchen a flicker of secret satisfaction.
[ZH] 他认为自己一定是被分裂成两个人。一个告诉他放了千里寻，成全她与文渊；一个却不愿意，想要霸占千里寻。只要他稍微放松警惕，两片柳叶带来的理智会随时被瓦解。
[EN] He felt sure he had split into two different people. One told him to let Qianli Xun go and help her find happiness with Wenyuan; the other refused, wanting to keep Qianli Xun all to himself. The instant he let his guard down, the clear-headed resolve those two willow leaves had given him threatened to crumble to dust.
[ZH] 文渊投来求助的目光，申简辰努力将理智的墙壁筑牢，在内心不断告诉自己：只要千里寻幸福就好。
[EN] Wenyuan shot him a pleading glance. Shen Jianchen worked hard to shore up the walls of his reason, telling himself over and over in his heart: as long as Qianli Xun is happy, that's all that matters.
[ZH] “不瞒外公，朱家姑娘能和离，我还是起了些作用。前前后后的事情也有所了解，怪只怪朱晞颜挑女婿的眼光太差。朱家姑娘婚后之事外公应该也听说过，她与林觉和离是唯一的出路，这不是她的错。”
[EN] "To be honest with you, Grandfather, I played a hand in helping Miss Zhu obtain her mutual divorce, so I know the whole story from start to finish. The only one to blame is Zhu Xiyan for having awful judgment when picking a son-in-law. You must have heard what Miss Zhu went through after her wedding. Divorcing Lin Jue by mutual consent was her only way out; none of it was her fault."
[ZH] “话虽如此，临安这么多大家闺秀，文渊又何必娶一个离过婚的？要是文渊有个哥哥弟弟的，我也不会太过在乎，可乔家就他一棵独苗，一家人正在为他选亲，到最后娶个和离的女子，传出去会被人嘲笑。”
[EN] "Even so, Lin'an is full of young ladies from fine families. Why must Wenyuan marry a divorced woman? If Wenyuan had an older or younger brother, I wouldn't care quite so much. But he's the sole heir of the Qiao line! The whole household has been busy choosing a bride for him, and if he ends up marrying a divorced woman, people will laugh us out of town."
[ZH] “外公此言差矣，日子是过给自己看，不是让别人看，一味顾及他人，岂不是活得很累？朱家姑娘才德品貌绝佳，若嫁到乔府，上可孝敬公婆，下可相夫教子，亦可将乔府打理得井井有条，这么好的儿媳妇打着灯笼都难找。”
[EN] "There you're wrong, Grandfather. You live your life for yourself, not as a show for outsiders. Living every day just to please outsiders—isn't that an exhausting way to live? Miss Zhu is unmatched in talent, virtue, character, and looks. If she marries into Qiao Manor, she'll show filial devotion to her in-laws, stand by her husband and raise their children well, and keep the entire Qiao household running in spotless order. A daughter-in-law this fine is hard to find even with a lantern."
[ZH] 仪王将淑真说得这么好，文渊很开心，又拿起茶筅继续做茶，再添两次水，击拂击拂也就好了。他边击拂，边听父亲言语。
[EN] Hearing Prince Yi sing Shuzhen's praises so high cheered Wenyuan right up. He picked up the tea whisk again to finish making the tea: two more pours of hot water, a round of brisk whisking, and it would be ready. Whisking away, he listened for his father's response.
[ZH] “朱家姑娘是不错，若没有嫁过人，她绝对是乔家首选，只可惜……”
[EN] "Miss Zhu is fine enough. Had she never been married before, she'd be the Qiao family's first choice without question. What a pity..."
[ZH] 乔守仁唯一过不了的槛儿还是和离一事，申简辰想起千里寻曾给他讲过关于刘娥的故事。
[EN] The one hurdle Qiao Shouren still could not get past was the mutual divorce. Just then, Shen Jianchen remembered the story Qianli Xun had once told him about Liu E.
[ZH] “外公可知章献明肃皇后？”
[EN] "Grandfather, have you heard of Empress Zhangxian Mingsu?"
[ZH] “当然知道。”
[EN] "Of course I know of her."
[ZH] “她也和离过，真宗皇帝不曾嫌弃，将她接入宫中，后成为皇后，甚至母仪天下，仁宗时又垂帘听政，政绩卓著，为仁宗盛世打下坚实基础。帝王都不在意女子和离，外公又何必纠结于此。”
[EN] "She went through a mutual divorce too, yet Emperor Zhenzong never looked down on her for it. He brought her into the palace, and she rose to become empress, the paragon of maternal virtue across the empire. During Emperor Renzong's reign, she ruled as regent from behind the curtain with an illustrious record of governance, laying a solid foundation for the golden age of Renzong's rule. If even an emperor didn't mind a divorced woman, why should Grandfather get hung up on it?"
[ZH] 仪王搬出章献明肃皇后，乔守仁不敢再以和离说事，继续强调便是对皇家不尊重：“仪王所言甚是，和离一事，我可以不计较，可听说朱家姑娘是……是母老虎，林觉才愿意和离。”
[EN] With Prince Yi invoking Empress Zhangxian Mingsu, Qiao Shouren dared not make an issue of the divorce anymore; pressing it further would show disrespect to the imperial family. "Prince Yi makes a sound point. I can look past the mutual divorce. Still, I've heard Miss Zhu is... is a tigress, and that's the only reason Lin Jue agreed to part ways."
[ZH] 乔文仙已将茶做好，依然坐在茶桌旁。心里更加佩服仪王，他竟然能想到搬出真宗时皇后刘娥之事，直接将父亲击退一大步。
[EN] Having finished preparing the tea, Qiao Wenxian stayed seated by the tea table, admiring Prince Yi even more. Who would have thought to bring up Emperor Zhenzong's Empress Liu E and push his father back such a huge step in one stroke?
[ZH] 可听到父亲说淑真是母老虎，他又急了，放下茶盏，正要强辩，见仪王向他使来阻止的眼色，便不再言语。
[EN] Yet hearing his father call Shuzhen a tigress rattled him again. He set down the tea bowl, ready to argue back, when he caught Prince Yi shooting him a warning glance to hold his tongue, so he kept quiet.
[ZH] 外公提到“母老虎”，申简辰在心里笑道：“朱淑真被人打，只能忍着，千里寻哪有被人打的份儿？不揍林觉才不符合她的风格，传出母老虎的名声倒也正常。”
[EN] At his grandfather's mention of "tigress," Shen Jianchen chuckled to himself. "The old Zhu Shuzhen could only grit her teeth and take a beating, but since when would Qianli Xun ever let anyone lay a hand on her? Not beating Lin Jue up would be out of character for her. No wonder she earned a name as a tigress."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“母老虎的说法有些过，我见过朱家姑娘，长得倒也温婉可人，怎么看也不像母老虎。之所以传出这名声，据我估计是不堪忍受林觉家暴，做出的反抗而已。外公试想，若文渊娶一位柔弱的女子，乔家这么大的家业，她岂能帮助文渊独当一面？”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said, "Calling her a tigress goes too far. I've met Miss Zhu. She looks gentle and lovely, nothing like a tigress at all. If that rumor got around, I'd wager she just couldn't stand Lin Jue's domestic abuse anymore and fought back. Think about it, Grandfather: if Wenyuan married some fragile, timid girl, how could she ever stand on her own and help him manage an estate as big as the Qiao family's?"
[ZH] 乔守仁依然不松口：“我听说她在外连陈家公子都打的过，简直就是个悍妇！以后嫁给文渊，他还能有好日子过？”
[EN] Qiao Shouren still would not budge. "I heard she even beat Young Master Chen in a fight out in public! She's an outright shrew! If she marries Wenyuan, will he ever know a day of peace?"
[ZH] 劝说到这种程度，申简辰清楚，他只要再继续劝说下去，外公必然答应，也就意味着将千里寻彻底拱手让给文渊。
[EN] Having argued this far, Shen Jianchen knew that if he pushed just a little more, his grandfather was bound to give in, which meant handing Qianli Xun over to Wenyuan for good.
[ZH] 申简辰开始犹豫要不要说出心里已想好的话……
[EN] Shen Jianchen began to hesitate over whether to speak the words already formed in his mind...

#CH 96
[ZH] 乔守仁见仪王不言语，以为他也不愿乔家的儿媳妇是个悍妇，不由心中窃喜。
[EN] Seeing Prince Yi fall silent, Qiao Shouren assumed the prince had no wish to see a shrew become the Qiao family's daughter-in-law either, and secret glee warmed his chest.
[ZH] 乔文仙急了，放下茶盏：“仪王，你怎么不说话？”
[EN] Panic seized Qiao Wenxian. He set down his tea bowl. "Prince Yi, why aren't you saying anything?"
[ZH] 申简辰看着文渊渴求的眼神，想道：“若千里寻在此，肯定也会用同样渴求的目光看着我，我爱她，却无法得到她，不如成全。”
[EN] Looking into Wenyuan's pleading eyes, Shen Jianchen thought, "If Qianli Xun were here, she'd be looking at me with that exact same longing. I love her, yet I can't have her. Better to give them my blessing."
[ZH] 申简辰定定心神，努力说道：“陈家公子的做派和名声，外公应该也听说过。朱家姑娘教训他，一来，代替哥嫂教训晚辈；二来，肯定是陈家公子做出该打之事，朱家姑娘才会出手。说明朱家姑娘坚持正义，不惧邪恶，如若是我遇到，定然也要管一管。能打得过陈家公子的姑娘，更应是文渊娶妻的不二人选。外公试想，乔家产业不少，文渊手无缚鸡之力，若再娶一位柔弱的当家主母，以后如何撑得起这份家业？还望外公仔细斟酌。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen steadied his mind and forced himself to speak. "Grandfather must have heard of Young Master Chen's conduct and reputation. When Miss Zhu taught him a lesson, first, she was disciplining a junior on behalf of her brother and sister-in-law; second, Young Master Chen must have done something that begged for a beating before she lifted a hand. That proves Miss Zhu stands up for justice and fears no bully. Had I been there, I would have stepped in too. A young woman who can beat Young Master Chen in a fight is all the more the one and only match for Wenyuan. Think about it, Grandfather: the Qiao family holds vast estates, and Wenyuan hasn't the strength to truss up a chicken. If he marries some frail mistress of the household on top of that, how will they ever hold this estate together in the future? Please give it careful thought, Grandfather."
[ZH] 乔守仁想起曾派人去乡下收租，遇到地痞流氓之事，收租的下人回来，不仅租没收到，下人的腿也被打得残废。
[EN] Qiao Shouren recalled a time he had sent servants to the countryside to collect rent, only for them to run into local thugs. The rent collector came back empty-handed, his leg beaten until it was crippled.
[ZH] 再瞧瞧正在品茶的儿子，虽不是仪王所说的“手无缚鸡之力”，但要让他一人撑起这份偌大的家业，的确辛苦，如若娶个厉害的女子也不错。
[EN] Glancing back at his son sipping tea, he admitted that while Wenyuan might not be quite "too weak to truss up a chicken" as Prince Yi put it, carrying such a massive family enterprise alone would indeed be a heavy burden. Marrying a tough, capable woman might not be a bad idea after all.
[ZH] 再说仪王苦口婆心，亲自前来，说的没有多少道理也就罢了，可偏偏至情至理，再不同意，岂不是不给仪王面子？
[EN] Besides, Prince Yi had come in person and argued with such earnest conviction. If his words had been hollow, that would be one thing, yet every point made complete sense. Turning him down now would be a slap in Prince Yi's face.
[ZH] “仪王说得不无道理，我无话可说，同意便是。”
[EN] "Prince Yi makes a compelling case. I have no further objection. I agree."
[ZH] 乔文仙一口喝完剩下的茶，还未放盏，急切地问：“父亲！您同意到朱府提亲？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian gulped down the rest of his tea and asked before even setting the bowl down, "Father! You agree to send a marriage proposal to Zhu Manor?"
[ZH] 乔守仁点点头：“我同意了，不过，你先别急，朱家姑娘和离不久，现在上门提亲不甚合适，等过上一两个月，我再带上你母亲去朱府拜见知府大人。”
[EN] Qiao Shouren nodded. "I agree. Hold your horses, though. Miss Zhu only went through her mutual divorce a short while ago, so calling on them to propose right now would look unseemly. Give it a month or two, and then I'll take your mother to Zhu Manor to pay a call on the Prefect."
[ZH] 文渊起身，向父亲和仪王行礼：“感谢仪王！感谢父亲！”
[EN] Wenyuan rose and bowed to both his father and Prince Yi. "Thank you, Prince Yi! Thank you, Father!"
[ZH] 申简辰却高兴不起来，好不容易找到千里寻，刚找到没多久，还未告诉她仪王正是申简辰，就要轻易失去。
[EN] Shen Jianchen, however, could feel no joy. After all the trouble it took to find Qianli Xun, he had just found her, had not even told her that Prince Yi was Shen Jianchen, and now he was about to lose her just like that.
[ZH] 离开乔府，申简辰想起千里寻拜托之事，他不打算跟文渊回幽林别居，两人在街头分开。
[EN] Leaving Qiao Manor, Shen Jianchen remembered the favor Qianli Xun had asked of him. With no plans to go back to Youlin Retreat alongside Wenyuan, he parted ways with him on the street.
[ZH] 今日有车夫赶车，用不上白风，他陪王爷坐在马车里。
[EN] Since a coachman was driving today, Bai Feng was not needed up front and rode inside the carriage with His Highness.
[ZH] 见王爷一副失落的样子，白风不敢打扰，静静坐在一旁，心里想着苦荞。想到在朱府的马车里向苦荞表白之事，觉得自己既大胆又好笑，不由笑出声来。
[EN] Seeing how downcast His Highness looked, Bai Feng dared not disturb him. He sat in silence at his side, thinking of Kuqiao. Recalling how he had confessed his feelings to Kuqiao inside Zhu Manor's carriage, he found himself both bold and ridiculous, and a laugh slipped out before he could stop it.
[ZH] 听到笑声，申简辰才注意白风：“你笑什么？”
[EN] The laugh drew Shen Jianchen's attention to Bai Feng. "What are you laughing about?"
[ZH] “没……没什么。”白风不好意思说出。
[EN] "N-nothing." Bai Feng felt too embarrassed to say.
[ZH] “刚才你跟南双去玩了？”
[EN] "Did you go hang out with Nan Shuang just now?"
[ZH] “他带我在乔府转了转。”
[EN] "He showed me around Qiao Manor."
[ZH] “你饿不饿？”
[EN] "Are you hungry?"
[ZH] “是有些饿，王爷为何不在乔府吃过饭再走？我看乔府今日做了东坡肉。”
[EN] "A bit. Why didn't Your Highness stay for lunch at Qiao Manor before leaving? I saw they made Dongpo pork today."
[ZH] 申简辰努力挤出笑容：“我以为你现在只对幽栖居士身边的丫鬟感兴趣，竟然还想着东坡肉。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen forced a smile. "I thought you only had eyes for Youqi Jushi's maid now. Didn't expect you to still be thinking about Dongpo pork."
[ZH] “两个我都感兴趣。”
[EN] "I'm interested in both."
[ZH] “待会儿办完事，带你下馆子。”
[EN] "Once I wrap up my errand, I'll take you out to eat."
[ZH] “好啊好啊！”白风很开心，又问道，“王爷，什么时候幽栖居士还去幽林别居？”
[EN] "Yes, yes!" Delighted, Bai Feng asked, "Your Highness, when is Youqi Jushi coming back to Youlin Retreat?"
[ZH] “这个嘛……我不大清楚。”申简辰故意逗白风，希望逗逗他，自己心里就不会那么难受。
[EN] "Well... hard to say." Shen Jianchen teased Bai Feng on purpose, hoping a little banter might ease the ache in his own chest.
[ZH] “唉，我现在真想和南双换换，乔公子与幽栖居士约会，他就能见到苦荞，可我却没这福气。”
[EN] "Sigh. Right now I'd love to trade places with Nan Shuang. Whenever Young Master Qiao goes out with Youqi Jushi, he gets to see Kuqiao, while I have no such luck."
[ZH] 申简辰装作开心的样子：“要不赶明儿你跟南双换换？”
[EN] Putting on a cheerful front, Shen Jianchen said, "How about we swap you with Nan Shuang tomorrow?"
[ZH] “那怎么行！我是王爷的贴身侍卫，还要跟王爷学拳法，刚才不过是感慨一下。”
[EN] "No way! I'm Your Highness's personal bodyguard, and I still have to learn martial arts from you. I was just sighing over my luck."
[ZH] “快到七夕节，到时文渊会约幽栖居士逛街，我也去，你就能见到想见的人。”
[EN] "Qixi Festival is almost here. Wenyuan is taking Youqi Jushi out to stroll the streets, and I'm going too, so you'll get to see the person you've been missing."
[ZH] 白风开始扳手指算日子，笑道：“还有五日。”
[EN] Bai Feng counted the days on his fingers and broke into a grin. "Five days left."
[ZH] 马车来到练兵署。
[EN] The carriage rolled up to the Military Training Office.
[ZH] 练兵署位于城西，由林觉主管，他的上面还有统领、统制、副都统制，诸军都统制，最后是节度使大人。
[EN] Located in the west of the city, the Military Training Office fell under Lin Jue's charge. Above him stood the Commandant, the Division Commander, the Vice Commander-in-Chief, the Commander-in-Chief of All Forces, and at the very top, the Lord Military Commissioner.
[ZH] 林觉之所以敢越级直接找节度使大人，无非是因和离一事与他攀上关系，再走得近些，其他官员也可对他忌惮几分。如此想来，与朱淑真和离倒是因祸得福。
[EN] The only reason Lin Jue dared bypass the chain of command to approach the Military Commissioner himself was the tie he had forged through his mutual divorce. Growing closer to him would also make other officials think twice before crossing Lin Jue. Looked at that way, divorcing Zhu Shuzhen had turned out to be a blessing in disguise.
[ZH] 申简辰走下马车，眼前是练兵署的大门，谈不上气派倒也庄严，上面写着“练兵署”三个大字。
[EN] Shen Jianchen stepped down from the carriage. Before him stood the gate of the Military Training Office, not grand yet solemn enough, with three large characters reading "Military Training Office" inscribed above.
[ZH] 白风上前对门口站的士兵说：“节度使大人来找林练兵官，去告诉他出来迎接。”
[EN] Bai Feng walked up to the soldier stationed at the gate. "The Lord Military Commissioner is here to see Training Officer Lin. Go tell him to come out and receive His Lordship."
[ZH] 士兵听到是节度使大人，立刻拜道：“节度使大人稍候，小的这就去报告练兵官。”
[EN] Hearing it was the Military Commissioner, the soldier dropped into a bow at once. "Please wait a moment, Your Lordship. I will report to the Training Officer right away."
[ZH] 士兵去后没过多久，林觉匆忙出来：“不知大人前来，望恕罪！”
[EN] Moments after the soldier hurried off, Lin Jue rushed out. "I did not know Your Lordship was coming! Please forgive my failure to greet you!"
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我也是偶然想起你上次请我给士兵训话，便过来看看。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said, "I just happened to recall how you invited me to address the troops last time, so I thought I'd drop by for a look."
[ZH] 林觉更觉荣耀，他随口一说，仪王竟然记在心里，攀上这棵大树，以后可平步青云。
[EN] Lin Jue felt even more honored. He had only tossed out a casual invitation, yet Prince Yi had taken it to heart. With a patron this mighty behind him, his rise through the ranks was assured.
[ZH] 统领的位置刚刚空缺下来，林觉有心爬上去。本想求朱晞颜帮忙，可因夏果一事，昨日他明显故意躲着他。仪王前来，正好给林觉送来一个好机会，定要好好表现一番。
[EN] The post of Commandant had just fallen vacant, and Lin Jue had his heart set on climbing into it. He had meant to ask Zhu Xiyan for help, yet over the whole Xiaguo affair, Zhu Xiyan had gone out of his way to dodge him yesterday. Prince Yi's arrival handed Lin Jue a golden opportunity; he had to put on a stellar show.
[ZH] “节度使大人，里面请，进去喝杯茶，我再带您去校场。”
[EN] "Please come inside, Lord Military Commissioner. Have a cup of tea first, and then I'll escort you to the drill ground."
[ZH] “喝茶就不必了，直接去校场，看完士兵，我还有事要与练兵官说。”
[EN] "Skip the tea. Take me straight to the drill ground. After I see the troops, I have something to discuss with you."
[ZH] 林觉内心更加窃喜，仪王有事与他说，说明已把他当成自己人：“好，我这就带节度使大人去校场，这边请。”
[EN] Secret delight surged in Lin Jue's chest. If Prince Yi had business to discuss with him, that meant the prince already viewed him as one of his own. "Very well, I'll take Your Lordship straight to the drill ground. This way, please."
[ZH] 校场离练兵署几步之遥，到达之后，林觉将仪王请上验兵台，吩咐校尉将士兵聚集而来。
[EN] The drill ground lay only a few steps from the Military Training Office. Upon arriving, Lin Jue invited Prince Yi up onto the reviewing platform and ordered a captain to assemble the soldiers.
[ZH] 临安的士兵真不少，黑压压一片。
[EN] Lin'an had quite a force: a dense, dark sea of soldiers stretching across the ground.
[ZH] 林觉先向仪王行礼：“节度使大人，我先给士兵们打个招呼，您再训话。”
[EN] Lin Jue bowed to Prince Yi first. "Lord Military Commissioner, let me address the troops first, and then you can give your speech."
[ZH] 申简辰点点头。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded.
[ZH] 只见林觉上前一步，大声说道：“诸位！你们肩负重任，保护临安百姓的安全，官家尽知，派仪王前来担任节度使一职，足以体现官家对大家十分关怀，现在有请节度使大人给大家训话！”
[EN] Lin Jue stepped forward and boomed, "Men! You shoulder a heavy duty, guarding the safety of Lin'an's people. His Majesty knows this well. Sending Prince Yi to serve as our Military Commissioner shows just how much His Majesty cares for every one of you. Now, let us welcome the Lord Military Commissioner to address the ranks!"
[ZH] 林觉退后，申简辰只能上前一步。想到昏庸的官家，他什么话都不愿说，可面对一张张充满期待的面孔，他内心的热情还是被点燃。
[EN] Lin Jue stepped back, leaving Shen Jianchen no choice but to step forward. Thinking of that incompetent emperor drained him of any desire to speak, yet facing row after row of expectant faces lit a spark of fire inside him.
[ZH] “诸位，我是赵朴，官家的十三子仪王，也是临安上任不久的节度使。从今日起，大家要记住，你们既是临安的士兵，也是大宋的士兵。有你们在，大宋终有一日不用再与金人联合攻打辽国，我们靠自己的实力便可夺取燕云十六州！”
[EN] "Men, I am Zhao Pu, Prince Yi, thirteenth son of His Majesty, and Lin'an's new Military Commissioner. From this day on, remember this: you are not just soldiers of Lin'an—you are soldiers of the Great Song! With men like you, the day will come when the Song no longer needs to ally with the Jin to strike the Liao. By our own strength alone, we will reclaim the Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun!"
[ZH] “燕云十六州！燕云十六州！燕云十六州！”
[EN] "The Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun! The Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun! The Sixteen Prefectures of Yanyun!"
[ZH] 申简辰清楚历史不可逆转，金宋联合是步臭棋，可他总不能在庄重的场合说些丧气话。鼓励的话语已说，还需说些警示之语。
[EN] Shen Jianchen knew history could not be undone, and the Song-Jin alliance was a disastrous blunder, yet he could not pour cold water on a solemn gathering like this. Now that he had stirred their spirits, he needed to leave them with a word of warning as well.
[ZH] “金人表面与大宋联合，实则心怀鬼胎，我们要时刻警惕，不要被金人迷惑，用其所用，防其所防。只有不断提高征战沙场的能力，方可保家卫国！”
[EN] "On the surface, the Jin ally with the Great Song, yet beneath it they harbor sinister designs. We must stay on our guard at every moment and never let the Jin fool us: use them where they serve us, and guard against them where we must. Only by sharpening our prowess on the battlefield can we protect our homes and defend our nation!"
[ZH] “保家卫国！保家卫国！保家卫国！”
[EN] "Protect our homes and defend our nation! Protect our homes and defend our nation! Protect our homes and defend our nation!"
[ZH] 申简辰说完，退后一步。
[EN] Finished speaking, Shen Jianchen took a step back.
[ZH] 林觉又行礼，向前一步：“大家都必须把节度使的话牢牢记在心里，听到没！”
[EN] Lin Jue bowed again and stepped forward. "Carve every word of the Military Commissioner's speech into your hearts—do you hear me?!"
[ZH] “听到！听到！听到！”
[EN] "Yes, sir! Yes, sir! Yes, sir!"
[ZH] “好！继续操练！”
[EN] "Good! Back to your drills!"
[ZH] 士兵们又开始操练。
[EN] The soldiers resumed their drills.
[ZH] 申简辰在士兵的操练声中，下了验兵台，走出校场。
[EN] Amid the clamor of the drilling troops, Shen Jianchen stepped down from the reviewing platform and walked out of the drill ground.
[ZH] 诸军都统制、副都统制和统制三位大人迎面走来。
[EN] Three senior officers came striding toward him: the Commander-in-Chief of All Forces, the Vice Commander-in-Chief, and the Division Commander.
[ZH] 申简辰见过他们，心想自己本因有私事要办，不得已来校场一趟，没想到这么快走漏风声，引得他们纷纷而来。
[EN] Shen Jianchen had met them before. He had only come to the drill ground because he had private business to settle; who would have guessed word would leak so fast and bring all three rushing over?
[ZH] 三位官员匆忙迎上来，鞠躬行礼。
[EN] The three officers hurried up to greet him, bowing low.
[ZH] 诸军都统制带头说：“节度使大人前来，应该让人先打声招呼，我等好提前迎接您，也不至于怠慢了贵人。”
[EN] The Commander-in-Chief of All Forces spoke first. "When the Lord Military Commissioner pays a visit, you ought to send word ahead so we can come welcome you in advance and avoid neglecting so honored a guest."
[ZH] 申简辰说：“我也是偶然起意，来练兵署走走，谁知也将你们几个惊动。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen said, "I just dropped by the Military Training Office on a whim. I didn't mean to stir up all three of you."
[ZH] 诸军都统制说：“练兵官派人告知我等，我们才匆忙赶到，大人若不嫌弃，可到我等办理公务之处坐坐，那里舒服些，站在这里太辛苦。”
[EN] The Commander-in-Chief said, "The Training Officer sent a runner to notify us, so we hurried right over. If Your Lordship doesn't mind, why not step into our headquarters for a seat? It's far more comfortable than standing out here."
[ZH] 申简辰拒绝道：“不了，我今日找练兵官有些私事要谈，改日再到各位办公之地坐坐。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen turned them down. "No need. I have some private matters to discuss with the Training Officer today. I'll pay a visit to your headquarters another day."
[ZH] 林觉感到面上有光，就凭仪王这句话，明日三位统制定会找他谈话，看来成为统领是板上钉钉了。
[EN] Lin Jue beamed with pride. On the strength of that single remark from Prince Yi, the three senior commanders were bound to summon him tomorrow. The Commandant's post was as good as in the bag.
[ZH] 果不其然，三位统制互相看看，心领神会。
[EN] Sure enough, the three commanders exchanged a knowing look.
[ZH] 诸军都统制又说：“既然仪王找练兵官有事，我等也不敢多打搅，只是有一事需向大人禀明，统领的位置暂时空缺，我们三人都有意推举林练兵官，不知大人意下如何？”
[EN] The Commander-in-Chief added, "Since Prince Yi has business with the Training Officer, we won't intrude any further. There is just one matter we must report to Your Lordship: the post of Commandant is vacant at the moment, and all three of us intend to recommend Training Officer Lin. What are Your Lordship's thoughts?"
[ZH] 刚猜是明日，没想到这么快！林觉喜出望外，与仪王走得近还是好处多多。
[EN] He had just guessed tomorrow; he never dreamed it would happen this fast! Overjoyed, Lin Jue thought how rewarding it was to stay close to Prince Yi.
[ZH] 申简辰瞅着林觉高兴的样子，心想：“没想到我这一来，倒帮他升了官儿，也好，一会儿劝他放弃夏果也好说些。”
[EN] Eyeing Lin Jue's delight, Shen Jianchen thought, "Didn't expect my visit to land him a promotion. Fine by me—that'll make it all the easier to talk him into giving up Xiaguo in a moment."
[ZH] 他对三位大人说：“一切由你们定夺。”
[EN] He told the three officers, "I leave it all to your discretion."
[ZH] 诸军都统制又带头说：“下官谨遵节度使大人的安排，您忙，我等退下。”
[EN] The Commander-in-Chief led the reply once more: "We shall abide by the Lord Military Commissioner's decision. We won't keep you from your business. We take our leave."
[ZH] 三人退去后，申简辰对林觉说：“我的贴身侍卫喜欢吃聚香楼的饭菜，也到饭点儿，林练兵官能否陪我一起去？”
