[EN] After the three withdrew, Shen Jianchen turned to Lin Jue. "My personal bodyguard loves the food at Juxiang Tower, and it's just about mealtime. Care to join me, Training Officer Lin?"
[ZH] 这等好事林觉岂能拒绝？满口应下：“能陪仪王用饭，下官不胜荣幸！”
[EN] How could Lin Jue turn down an honor like that? He agreed without missing a beat. "Dining with Prince Yi is the highest honor this humble officer could ask for!"

#CH 97
[ZH] 来到聚香楼，挑好包间。林觉坚持要请客，仪王又坚持不同意，他只好作罢。
[EN] Arriving at Juxiang Tower, they chose a private room. Lin Jue insisted on paying the bill, yet Prince Yi stood firm in refusing, leaving him no choice but to give in.
[ZH] 白风被仪王允许同坐一桌，他当然开心。仪王是有身份的人，吃起饭来绝对不会狼吞虎咽，林练兵官家里不缺钱，也不会抢着吃。今日这一桌子菜，他绝对能享用个够。
[EN] Allowed by Prince Yi to sit at the same table, Bai Feng was thrilled. Prince Yi was a man of status who would never wolf down his food, and Training Officer Lin came from money and wouldn't fight over dishes either. Today, Bai Feng could feast on this entire spread to his heart's content.
[ZH] 聚香楼的伙计把酒菜摆好后，有林练兵官在，白风连给仪王倒酒的程序都省了，只顾大口吃起来，在王爷面前，他从来不必扭捏。
[EN] Once the waiter at Juxiang Tower laid out the wine and dishes, Training Officer Lin's presence saved Bai Feng even the chore of pouring wine for Prince Yi. He dug straight in with big mouthfuls; he never had to stand on ceremony in front of His Highness.
[ZH] 林觉端起酒杯：“仪王今日帮下官，下官十分感激，连饮三杯，您随意。”
[EN] Lin Jue raised his wine cup. "I owe Prince Yi a huge debt of gratitude for helping me today. Let me drink three cups in a row; please drink at your leisure."
[ZH] 林觉一口气将酒喝下，又斟一杯，又饮下，第三杯时，被仪王阻止。
[EN] Lin Jue tossed back the first cup in one gulp, poured a second, and downed that too. As he reached for a third, Prince Yi stopped him.
[ZH] “练兵官酒量再好，也不能如此饮酒，你醉了，我怎么与你说事？再说我也没帮你什么忙。”申简辰装作毫不知情的样子。
[EN] "No matter how well you hold your liquor, Training Officer, you can't drink like that. If you get drunk, how am I supposed to talk business with you? Besides, I didn't do anything to help you." Shen Jianchen played dumb.
[ZH] 林觉放下酒杯：“仪王来练兵署就是帮我，又请我吃饭，也是帮我，没这些，三位大人怎么可能提拔我？”
[EN] Lin Jue set down his cup. "Prince Yi coming to the Military Training Office helped me, and inviting me to dine with you helped me too. Without that, how would those three senior officers ever promote me?"
[ZH] 申简辰佯装忽然明白的样子：“原来如此，我还以为他们已提前安排好，只是向我汇报一下。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen put on a look of sudden realization. "So that's how it is. Here I thought they had it all arranged beforehand and were just reporting it to me."
[ZH] “并非如此，有好几个人盯着统领的位置，三位统制都想将自己的人安插进来，若不是因仪王出现，又对我好，他们根本不会主动提拔我。”
[EN] "Not at all. Half a dozen men had their eyes on the Commandant's post, and each of the three commanders wanted to slip one of his own people in. If Prince Yi hadn't shown up and treated me with such favor, they would never have promoted me on their own."
[ZH] 申简辰边吃菜，边听林觉说，简单的话语中透露出临安官员的腐 败。有一个善于享受，又重用奸臣的官家，又怎能不腐 败？大宋的根正在逐渐腐烂，枝叶又能好到哪里去？
[EN] Eating as he listened to Lin Jue, Shen Jianchen caught the rot of Lin'an's officialdom in those few casual sentences. With an emperor who lived for pleasure and leaned on treacherous officials, how could the bureaucracy be anything but corrupt? When the very roots of the Great Song were rotting away, how healthy could its branches and leaves be?
[ZH] “没想到竟然是无心插柳柳成荫，既然我给练兵官带来益处，也请练兵官答应我件事。”
[EN] "Who would have guessed I'd stumble into doing you a good turn without even trying? Since I've brought you some good fortune, Training Officer, I'll ask you to grant me one favor in return."
[ZH] “仪王请说，上刀山，下火海我绝无二话。”
[EN] "Say the word, Prince Yi. I'd climb a mountain of blades or plunge into a sea of fire without a second thought."
[ZH] “别说得那么严重，是件小事。听说……你看上幽栖居士身边的丫鬟夏果，可有此事？”
[EN] "Don't make it sound so dramatic. It's a minor matter. I heard... you've taken a fancy to Youqi Jushi's maid Xiaguo. Is that true?"
[ZH] 原来仪王是为夏果而来，一个丫鬟之事竟然惊动仪王，定是受朱晞颜所托，难怪他昨日躲着自己。
[EN] So Prince Yi had come over Xiaguo! For a single maid to draw Prince Yi's attention, Zhu Xiyan must have put him up to it. No wonder the old man had dodged him yesterday.
[ZH] 林觉不敢隐瞒，再说也隐瞒不了：“是有此事，我与朱淑真和离那日，当众表明要纳夏果为妾，知府大人也当众允诺，可朱淑真与那丫鬟都不愿意。仪王您说，为何我的感情生活如此不顺？”
[EN] Lin Jue dared not hide it, nor could he. "It's true. On the day Zhu Shuzhen and I went through our mutual divorce, I stated in front of everyone that I wanted to take Xiaguo as a concubine, and the Prefect gave his word in public too. Yet both Zhu Shuzhen and the maid refuse. Tell me, Prince Yi, why is my love life always so full of snags?"
[ZH] “之所以不顺，是因你总盯着不合适或是不属于你的人，才会搞得如此波折……”申简辰想到自己，他之所以痛苦，正是因为他喜欢上无论穿越前还是穿越后都无法得到的人。
[EN] "It's full of snags because you keep fixating on people who aren't right for you, or who were never yours to begin with..." Shen Jianchen thought of himself. The whole reason he was in pain was that he had fallen for someone he could not have, neither before crossing over nor after.
[ZH] “仪王的话，一语惊醒梦中人，以后我应改变想法。”
[EN] "Prince Yi's words have woken me straight from a dream. From now on, I need to change my mindset."
[ZH] “如此想就对了，放了夏果吧，谁让夏果的主人是幽栖居士，你既然与她和离，就不要再与她扯上任何关系，好好过属于自己的生活，既让自己舒服，别人也舒服。”
[EN] "Now you're talking sense. Let Xiaguo go. After all, Xiaguo's mistress is Youqi Jushi. Now that you've divorced her, stop tangling yourself up in her affairs and go live a good life of your own. That way, you're at peace and everyone else is at peace too."
[ZH] 仪王的话，林觉必然要听：“谨遵义王嘱咐，我……我放了夏果。”
[EN] Lin Jue had no choice but to heed Prince Yi's words. "I will follow Prince Yi's counsel. I... I'll let Xiaguo go."
[ZH] “这就对了，来，吃菜。练兵官如此给我面子，以后你就是我的人。”
[EN] "That's the spirit. Come on, eat up. Since you've done me this honor, Training Officer, from today on, you're one of my men."
[ZH] 林觉更加兴奋，失去一个夏果，却赢来仪王把他当作自己人，一点都不亏。真是情场失意，官场反而得意，老天从来公平。
[EN] Thrill surged through Lin Jue. Giving up one Xiaguo to have Prince Yi count him as one of his own? Not a loss at all. Unlucky in love, lucky in officialdom; Heaven was fair after all.
[ZH] 对男人来讲，官场最重要，至于情场，林觉连朱淑真都舍了，一个夏果更是舍得轻松。男人只要有钱有地位，什么女人没有？今日他真正从心里与朱淑真断得一干二净，决定以后不再纠缠于她。
[EN] To a man, his career mattered most. As for romance, Lin Jue had already given up Zhu Shuzhen; letting go of a single Xiaguo was effortless. Once a man had wealth and rank, what kind of woman couldn't he have? Today, he made a clean break with Zhu Shuzhen in his heart and vowed never to pester her again.
[ZH] 一日之内，成功说服两人，申简辰毫无成功的喜悦。与林觉分别后只能回幽林别居，一旦回去，又要面对文渊兴奋的样子，他没有了回去的欲望。
[EN] Having persuaded two people in a single day, Shen Jianchen felt not a drop of triumph. After parting from Lin Jue, there was nowhere to go but Youlin Retreat, yet heading back meant facing Wenyuan's beaming excitement, and he had no desire to go home.
[ZH] 吩咐车夫将马车赶到山下，然后让车夫从大道先回。申简辰则带领白风顺着山下的路，向神柳的方向走去。
[EN] He told the coachman to drive to the foot of the hill, then sent him back ahead along the main road. With Bai Feng in tow, Shen Jianchen followed the trail at the hill's base toward the Divine Willow.
[ZH] 白风边走边问：“王爷为何又去神柳那里？”
[EN] Walking along, Bai Feng asked, "Why is Your Highness heading to the Divine Willow again?"
[ZH] 申简辰没有回答，只顾向前走。
[EN] Shen Jianchen did not answer, just kept walking ahead.
[ZH] 白风不敢再问，仪王不说话，定是有心事。他想不通，仪王不爱插手军政，又有钱，又有地位，身边还有佳和郡主，既没有可烦心的事，也什么都不缺，还有什么不满足的？为何会不高兴？
[EN] Bai Feng dared not ask again. When Prince Yi went quiet, something was weighing on his mind. Bai Feng could not figure it out: Prince Yi had no interest in meddling with military or civil affairs, had wealth and rank, and had Princess Jiahe by his side. With nothing to fret over and nothing lacking, what could he be dissatisfied with? Why was he unhappy?
[ZH] 到达神柳跟前时，有四五个女尼正在树下解红绸，她们已取下一大半。
[EN] When they reached the Divine Willow, four or five Buddhist nuns stood beneath it untying the red silk ribbons; more than half had already been taken down.
[ZH] 申简辰很好奇，主动上前问：“小师傅，为何要将红绸取下？”
[EN] Curious, Shen Jianchen stepped forward and asked, "Venerable Sister, why are you taking down the red silk ribbons?"
[ZH] 一位女尼取下一根红绸，放开柳枝，将红绸放进地上的竹筐里。
[EN] One nun untied a ribbon, let the willow branch spring back, and placed the strip of silk into a bamboo basket on the ground.
[ZH] “阿弥陀佛，施主有所不知，红绸太多，会给神柳增加负担，住持吩咐我们每半年取下，拿回尼姑庵焚烧，焚烧时，住持会亲自诵读经文。”
[EN] "Amitabha. Benefactor may not know this, but too many red ribbons weigh down the Divine Willow. The Abbess instructs us to take them down every half year and bring them back to the nunnery to burn, while the Abbess recites sutras over them herself."
[ZH] “原来如此，不过，挂红绸的人看到，岂不伤心？”申简辰已将千里寻的红绸送还本人，可乔梦容的红绸还挂在上面，她要是知道被取下，肯定会难过。
[EN] "I see. Still, won't the people who hung those ribbons feel heartbroken if they find out?" Shen Jianchen had already returned Qianli Xun's ribbon to her, yet Qiao Mengrong's was still up on the tree. If she learned it had been taken down, she was bound to be upset.
[ZH] 女尼说：“施主放心，神柳有灵性，一旦接触红绸，便已将所挂之人的心愿记下，之后看到的红绸不过是个形式而已。”
[EN] The nun replied, "Rest assured, Benefactor. The Divine Willow has a spirit of its own. The instant a ribbon touches its branches, it commits the wish of the one who hung it to memory. Leaving the silk behind afterward is just a formality."
[ZH] 白风认为女尼肯定是骗人，一棵树哪有那么神奇！
[EN] Bai Feng figured the nun was spinning a yarn. How could a tree be that magical!
[ZH] “感谢小师傅告知。”申简辰谢过后，心想，如若梦容问起，也好给她一个满意的答案，她才不至于太过伤心。
[EN] "Thank you for telling me, Venerable Sister." After thanking her, Shen Jianchen thought that if Mengrong ever asked, at least he now had a comforting answer to give her so she would not be too sad.
[ZH] 几位师傅都是女子，个头没有男子高，稍微高些的她们根本够不着。
[EN] All the nuns were women, shorter than men, and could not reach the ribbons tied higher up.
[ZH] 一位女尼说：“李老头怎么还不来？他个子高，高处的都需他来取。”
[EN] One nun said, "Why isn't Old Man Li here yet? He's tall; we need him to reach the high ones."
[ZH] 另一位说：“李老头家里有人生病，一时脱不开身。”
[EN] Another replied, "Someone in Old Man Li's family fell ill, so he can't get away right now."
[ZH] 申简辰见此情景，说道：“白风，与我一起给几位小师傅帮忙。”
[EN] Seeing their predicament, Shen Jianchen said, "Bai Feng, come help these Venerable Sisters with me."
[ZH] “是，公子。”白风现在已经完全分得清，什么情况下该称呼王爷为公子。
[EN] "Yes, Young Master." By now, Bai Feng knew down to a tee when he ought to call His Highness "Young Master."
[ZH] 几位女尼感激不尽。
[EN] The nuns could not thank them enough.
[ZH] 有了两名男子帮忙，高处的红绸不用再担心。很快红绸被尽数取下，整整两大竹筐，女尼们谢过后，抬起竹筐离开。
[EN] With two men lending a hand, the high ribbons were no longer a problem. Before long, every last ribbon was down, filling two whole bamboo baskets. After offering their thanks, the nuns lifted the baskets and left.
[ZH] 没有了红绸，申简辰更加确定眼前的柳树正是穿越前他与千里寻看到的神柳。
[EN] Stripped of the red ribbons, the willow before him convinced Shen Jianchen more than ever that this was the very Divine Willow he and Qianli Xun had seen before crossing over.
[ZH] 神柳依旧，人却已不是从前的模样，他有了乔梦容，千里寻有了文渊，一切都已被命运安排妥当，他又何必执着……
[EN] The Divine Willow stood unchanged, yet the people beneath it were no longer who they had been. He had Qiao Mengrong; Qianli Xun had Wenyuan. Fate had laid everything out in neat order. Why keep clinging on...
[ZH] “哥哥，你怎么在这里？”
[EN] "Brother, what are you doing here?"
[ZH] 身后传来乔梦容的声音，申简辰转过身，看到梦容惊讶的样子。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong's voice came from behind him. Shen Jianchen turned around to find Mengrong wearing a surprised expression.
[ZH] “应该是我问你，你怎么跑到这里来？”
[EN] "I should be asking you. What brought you all the way out here?"
[ZH] “哥哥今日一直不在，我很无聊，就来哥哥喜欢的神柳下走走，没想到在此遇到哥哥。听乔公子说，哥哥去找练兵官帮幽栖居士解决难题。”不等哥哥搭话，乔梦容急切地问，“柳树上的红绸呢？去了哪里？”
[EN] "Brother was gone all day, and I got bored, so I came to take a walk beneath the Divine Willow you like so much. I never expected to run into you here! Young Master Qiao told me Brother went to see the Training Officer to settle Youqi Jushi's trouble." Before he could reply, Qiao Mengrong asked in alarm, "Where are the red ribbons on the willow? What happened to them?"
[ZH] 申简辰照实说：“你来之前，红绸已被尼姑庵的小师傅全部取下，拿回尼姑庵焚烧。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen told her the truth. "Before you arrived, the nuns from the nunnery took every ribbon down to carry back and burn."
[ZH] “什么？我与哥哥一起挂上去的，还写着我们的名字，就这么被烧掉。”乔梦容果然伤心起来。
[EN] "What? The ones Brother and I hung together, with both our names written on them—just burned like that?" Sure enough, Qiao Mengrong's face fell.
[ZH] 申简辰安慰道：“你不必伤感，小师傅说了，挂上红绸的那一刻，神柳已将你的心意记下，红绸只是个形式而已。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen comforted her. "No need to feel sad. The nun told me that the moment you tie on a ribbon, the Divine Willow commits your wish to memory. The ribbon itself is just a formality."
[ZH] “哥哥该不会是哄我？”
[EN] "Brother isn't just making that up to coax me?"
[ZH] “小师傅说的，不信，你问白风，他也亲耳听到。”
[EN] "The nun said it herself. If you don't believe me, ask Bai Feng. He heard it with his own ears."
[ZH] 乔梦容看向白风，眼神中充满询问。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong looked to Bai Feng, her eyes full of doubt.
[ZH] 白风回道：“女尼的确是这么说的，王爷没有欺骗郡主。”
[EN] Bai Feng replied, "That's what the nun said, word for word. His Highness isn't fooling you, Princess."
[ZH] 乔梦容露出笑容：“只要神柳记住我与哥哥的心愿，有没有红绸不重要。”
[EN] A smile lit up Qiao Mengrong's face. "As long as the Divine Willow remembers Brother's wish and mine, whether the ribbon stays up doesn't matter."
[ZH] 白风心想，女尼估计是欺骗王爷，王爷又来欺骗郡主，唉！人有时喜欢听谎言，还美得不行。
[EN] Bai Feng figured the nun had fooled His Highness, and now His Highness was fooling the Princess. Sigh! Sometimes people just loved hearing sweet lies, and even beamed over them.
[ZH] “哥哥，我们回去吧？”
[EN] "Brother, shall we head back?"
[ZH] “你才刚来。”
[EN] "You only just got here."
[ZH] “我来是因为心里想着哥哥，既然看到哥哥，没必要再看神柳。”
[EN] "I came because I was thinking of Brother. Now that I've found you, there's no need to look at the Divine Willow anymore."
[ZH] 申简辰点头同意，他发觉今日面对梦容，心情不再像以前一样沉重。只因千里寻即将有她的归宿，而他也想好要接受命运的安排。
[EN] Shen Jianchen nodded in agreement. He realized that facing Mengrong today no longer weighed so heavy on his heart. Qianli Xun was about to find her own happy home, and he had made up his mind to accept what fate had laid out for him.
[ZH] 可他心里依然爱着千里寻，无法轻易将感情放下，只能一点一点把这份感情压到心底。
[EN] Yet in his heart he still loved Qianli Xun. Letting go was no easy thing; all he could do was press those feelings down to the bottom of his heart, bit by bit.
[ZH] 与乔梦容穿过池塘，快要走进幽林时，申简辰突然停下来：“梦容，明年中秋节，哥哥会娶你过门。”
[EN] As they crossed by the pond and approached the secluded grove, Shen Jianchen came to a halt. "Mengrong, at next year's Mid-Autumn Festival, I'll marry you and bring you home as my bride."
[ZH] 尽管乔梦容一直都知道，可这是哥哥起死回生后第一次主动说出要娶她的话，她喜出望外，不由上前抱住哥哥。
[EN] Though Qiao Mengrong had always known it, this was the first time since being brought back to life that Prince Yi had spoken of marrying her on his own. Overwhelmed with joy, she stepped forward and threw her arms around him.
[ZH] “我今日实在开心！”
[EN] "I'm so happy today!"
[ZH] 申简辰想要说服自己也抱住怀中的妹妹，可抬起的手怎么都无法落下……
[EN] Shen Jianchen tried to make himself wrap his arms around the girl pressed against his chest, yet his raised hands refused to come down...
[ZH] 喜兰和白风都转过身，为两位主人感到高兴。
[EN] Both Xilan and Bai Feng turned their backs to give them privacy, happy for their two masters.
[ZH] 这时，池塘里传出青蛙的叫声：“呱呱呱……”
[EN] Just then, frogs began croaking from the pond: "Ribbit, ribbit, ribbit..."
[ZH] 申简辰想起与千里寻对决的那晚，青蛙也是这么叫的。
[EN] Shen Jianchen remembered the night of his duel with Qianli Xun. The frogs had been croaking in that exact same way.

#CH 98
[ZH] 七夕节这日，朱府的下人一早起来，开始忙碌，只等着忙完去逛街。逛完街快天黑时，再赶回来安排乞巧宴。
[EN] On Qixi Festival, the servants of Zhu Manor rose at dawn and got straight to work, eager to finish their chores and head out to roam the streets. Once dusk closed in, they would hurry back to set up the Qiqiao Banquet, the Qixi feast for praying for skill.
[ZH] 有的拿来小木板，敷上泥土，将粟米的种子埋进泥土里；有的用各种瓜雕刻出不同花样，花纹精细巧妙；有的用油面蜜糖制作果食，花样百出，形态各异；有的把已长得长长的绿豆芽，用红蓝丝线捆绑起来，放进盘子里。
[EN] Some fetched small wooden boards, spread soil over them, and planted millet seeds; some carved fine, clever patterns into melons of every kind; some shaped all manner of pastries from flour, oil, and honey into endless designs and forms; and others bundled tall mung bean sprouts with red and blue silk threads and arranged them on plates.
[ZH] 还有一部分下人在朱家最大的庭院内张灯结彩，安排焚香桌案，排列摩睺罗、花瓜、美酒、笔墨纸砚、针线等。
[EN] Still other servants hung lanterns and silk festoons in Zhu Manor's largest courtyard, set up incense tables, and laid out Mohouluo clay dolls, carved flower-melons, fine wine, the Four Treasures of the Study, needles and thread, and more.
[ZH] 一切安排完毕后，下人们无论男女都换上新衣裳，三三两两相邀去逛街。
[EN] Once everything stood ready, the servants—men and women alike—changed into new clothes and headed out in twos and threes to stroll the streets.
[ZH] 绿桃换上新衣裳，约苦荞一起去街上玩儿，被苦荞拒绝，说她要陪小姐。苦荞本想跟丫鬟们去，可要是去了，就见不到白风，七夕节还是要跟喜欢的人在一起。
[EN] Dressed in her new outfit, Lutao invited Kuqiao to go out and have fun on the streets, only for Kuqiao to turn her down, saying she had to attend her mistress. Kuqiao had half wanted to go with the other maids, yet going with them meant missing Bai Feng, and Qixi Festival was meant to be spent with the one you liked.
[ZH] 因仪王帮忙，林觉不再纠缠夏果。夏果也大胆从望月轩回来，她更不愿离开小姐，只因跟着小姐才能见到乔公子。
[EN] Thanks to Prince Yi's help, Lin Jue no longer pestered Xiaguo. Xiaguo had ventured back from Wangyue Pavilion, and she was even less willing to leave her mistress's side, because staying with her mistress was the only way she could see Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 两个丫鬟各有心思，千里寻也不好多说什么，想跟着就跟着吧。
[EN] Each of the two maids had her own private motive, and Qianli Xun saw no point saying much. If they wanted to tag along, let them.
[ZH] 三人换上新做的衣裳，来到朱府门外，上了马车。昨日南双来过，说明相约的时间和地点，此时出发到达临安城内的七星桥，不早也不晚。
[EN] Changing into their fresh, custom-made outfits, the three walked out of Zhu Manor and boarded the carriage. Nan Shuang had stopped by yesterday with the meeting time and place; setting off now would bring them to Seven Star Bridge in Lin'an right on time, neither early nor late.
[ZH] 千里寻叮嘱道：“夏果，今日仪王也来，你见到他，一定要好好磕几个头。”
[EN] Qianli Xun reminded her, "Xiaguo, Prince Yi will be there today too. The moment you see him, make sure you give him a few proper kowtows."
[ZH] “小姐不用担心，见到仪王，我二话不说，跪下磕头。”
[EN] "Don't worry, Miss. As soon as I see Prince Yi, I'll drop to my knees and kowtow without a word."
[ZH] “这就对了。”
[EN] "Good."
[ZH] 苦荞心里想着白风，一直不言语。
[EN] With Bai Feng on her mind, Kuqiao stayed quiet the whole way.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“你今儿怎么这么安静？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked, "Why are you so quiet today?"
[ZH] 苦荞掩饰道：“我一直都很安静，难道小姐才发现？”
[EN] Kuqiao tried to cover it up. "I'm always quiet. Is Miss just noticing now?"
[ZH] 千里寻研究苦荞的表情：“不对，你今儿安静的样子跟往常不同，老实招来，是不是有喜欢的人？该不会是南双吧？我见你跟他走得很近，呵呵……！”
[EN] Qianli Xun studied Kuqiao's face. "No way. Your quiet today isn't like your usual quiet. Fess up: is there someone you like? Don't tell me it's Nan Shuang! I've seen you hanging around him a lot, haha...!"
[ZH] 苦荞红着脸：“小姐就喜欢拿我开玩笑，南双还是个孩子，我怎么可能喜欢他？跟他走得近，也是因为小姐总见乔公子，我不得已才跟他熟络起来。”
[EN] Kuqiao flushed crimson. "Miss just loves teasing me! Nan Shuang is still a kid. How could I like him? The only reason I got close to him is because Miss sees Young Master Qiao all the time, so I couldn't help getting friendly with him."
[ZH] 千里寻仔细一想，苦荞说得有道理，便不再逗她。
[EN] Thinking it over, Qianli Xun figured Kuqiao had a point and stopped teasing her.
[ZH] 苦荞提到乔公子，夏果心里产生异样的感觉。
[EN] Kuqiao's mention of Young Master Qiao stirred a strange ache inside Xiaguo.
[ZH] 她在望月轩这段时日，小姐与乔公子进展迅速，她不敢奢求乔公子会喜欢她，只要跟在小姐身边，能见到乔公子，夏果便心满意足。如若以后小姐嫁给乔公子，日日见到他，说不定还有近身伺候的机会。
[EN] During her stay at Wangyue Pavilion, her mistress and Young Master Qiao had grown close in leaps and bounds. Xiaguo dared not dream of Young Master Qiao liking her back; as long as she stayed by her mistress and got to see him, she would be content. And if her mistress married Young Master Qiao someday, seeing him every day might even bring her the chance to wait on him up close.
[ZH] 她听说过，临安富贵人家有丫鬟被纳为妾室的先例。夏果不愿当林觉的妾室，可若乔公子愿意，她会心甘情愿当他的妾室。
[EN] She had heard of plenty of wealthy households in Lin'an taking a mistress's maid as a concubine. Xiaguo wanted no part of being Lin Jue's concubine, yet if Young Master Qiao ever wanted her, she would become his concubine with all her heart.
[ZH] 通往七星桥的路不适合乘坐马车，她们早早下来，步行往七星桥走去。街上人来人往，都身着新衣，满面喜色，跟过年似的。
[EN] Since the lane leading to Seven Star Bridge was too narrow for a carriage, they stepped out early and continued on foot. Crowds streamed past in both directions, everyone dressed in new clothes and beaming with cheer, as festive as New Year's Day.
[ZH] 千里寻感慨，普通的七夕节被古人过得喜气洋洋，热闹非凡。
[EN] Qianli Xun marveled at how these ancient folk turned an ordinary Qixi Festival into such a jubilant, bustling spectacle.
[ZH] 主仆三人穿过人流，来到七星桥旁。千里寻向周围张望一番，不见文渊的人影。
[EN] Weaving through the crowd, the mistress and her two maids reached Seven Star Bridge. Qianli Xun scanned the area, yet saw no sign of Wenyuan.
[ZH] 正要埋怨，苦荞指向一旁：“小姐，乔公子在那里，正向我们招手。”
[EN] Just as she was about to grumble, Kuqiao pointed off to the side. "Miss, Young Master Qiao is over there! He's waving at us!"
[ZH] 千里寻瞧去，果然是文渊，他坐在茶肆里，挥动手臂。千里寻露出笑容，也挥挥手，带着丫鬟向茶肆走去。
[EN] Qianli Xun looked over, and sure enough, there sat Wenyuan inside a teahouse, waving his arm. Breaking into a smile, she waved back and led her maids toward the teahouse.
[ZH] 这家茶肆与别处不同，呈半露天状，搭着精美的顶棚，坐在棚下喝茶，四周一览无余。
[EN] This teahouse differed from most, built half open to the air beneath an exquisite awning; sitting under its shade over tea gave an unobstructed view all around.
[ZH] 千里寻清楚地看到仪王与佳和郡主也坐在桌旁，她上前先拜见两位贵人，特意未说出称呼之语，主要担心他们逛街不愿被人知道身份。
[EN] Qianli Xun spotted Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe sitting at the table too. Stepping forward, she bowed in greeting to the two noble guests, careful not to utter their titles aloud in case they preferred to keep their identities hidden while strolling the streets.
[ZH] 夏果二话不说，立刻跪下磕头：“多谢王爷！多谢王爷！”
[EN] Without a second thought, Xiaguo dropped to her knees and kowtowed at once. "Thank you, Your Highness! Thank you, Your Highness!"
[ZH] 申简辰皱起眉头：“哪来的王爷？今日只有申公子。”
[EN] Shen Jianchen knitted his brows. "What 'Highness'? Today there is only Young Master Shen."
[ZH] “多谢申公子！多谢申公子！”
[EN] "Thank you, Young Master Shen! Thank you, Young Master Shen!"
[ZH] “你只管谢你家小姐，至于我，得你家小姐来谢。”申简辰故意如此说，顺便向千里寻看了一眼，她今日打扮得甚是美艳，他忍不住不心动。
[EN] "Just thank your mistress. As for me, it's your mistress who owes me thanks." Shen Jianchen spoke those words on purpose, shooting Qianli Xun a glance. Dressed up today, she looked stunning, and his heart could not help racing.
[ZH] 仪王明显话中有话，眼神异常。千里寻心想：“又调戏老娘！”
[EN] Prince Yi's double meaning was plain as day, and that look in his eyes was anything but innocent. "Flirting with me again!" Qianli Xun thought.
[ZH] 再瞅瞅佳和郡主，她竟然还在笑，难道听不出仪王的话语中略带调戏？还有那眼神！郡主傻乎乎的，也不知品味一下。
[EN] Glancing over at Princess Jiahe, she saw the princess still smiling away. Couldn't she hear the flirtation laced into Prince Yi's tone? And that gaze! The princess was way too naive. Couldn't she read the room at all?
[ZH] 再观察文渊，也是一副高兴的样子，竟然对仪王毫无戒备。
[EN] Looking at Wenyuan, he looked just as cheerful, without a shred of guard up against Prince Yi.
[ZH] “夏果，申公子既然说了，你起来吧，不必跪着。”千里寻在文渊身边坐下，“申公子放心，今日我就感谢您。”
[EN] "Xiaguo, since Young Master Shen said so, get up. No need to stay kneeling." Qianli Xun took a seat beside Wenyuan. "Rest assured, Young Master Shen, I will thank you today."
[ZH] “你打算怎么谢我？”尽管已知，申简辰还想听千里寻亲口说出；尽管打算放下对她的感情，可见到她，还是不由自主想要与她多说话。
[EN] "And how do you plan to thank me?" Even though he already knew, Shen Jianchen wanted to hear Qianli Xun say it herself. Even though he meant to let go of his feelings for her, the moment he saw her, he could not stop himself from wanting to draw out every word with her.
[ZH] 千里寻问：“难道文渊没告诉您？”
[EN] Qianli Xun asked, "Didn't Wenyuan tell you?"
[ZH] “他说归他说，我想听幽栖居士亲口说。”
[EN] "What he says is his business. I want to hear it from Youqi Jushi's own lips."
[ZH] 千里寻心里仍旧不爽：“竟然敢光明正大继续调戏？老娘现在就让你死了这份心思。”
[EN] Irritation flared in Qianli Xun's chest. "Still got the nerve to flirt right out in the open? Watch me kill that wishful thinking right now!"
[ZH] 千里寻将头靠在文渊的肩头：“文渊完全可以代表我，他说跟我说没有任何区别。”
[EN] Qianli Xun rested her head against Wenyuan's shoulder. "Wenyuan can speak for me anytime. Him saying it is no different from me saying it."
[ZH] 乔梦容露出羡慕的表情，她也想把头搭在哥哥的肩膀。哥哥表情严肃，垂下眼帘，搞得她不敢这么做，不由叹道：“幽栖居士与乔公子真令人羡慕。”
[EN] Envy flickered across Qiao Mengrong's face; she wished she could lean her head on Prince Yi's shoulder too. Yet his expression turned stern and his eyes dropped, leaving her too timid to try. A sigh escaped her lips: "Youqi Jushi and Young Master Qiao really make people envious."
[ZH] 乔文仙小声说：“佳和郡主与仪王也令人羡慕。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian murmured, "Princess Jiahe and Prince Yi make people just as envious."
[ZH] 申简辰不愿面对二人亲密的样子，拉起乔梦容的手：“梦容，我们去逛街。”
[EN] Unable to stomach watching the two of them act so cozy, Shen Jianchen took Qiao Mengrong by the hand. "Mengrong, let's go stroll the streets."
[ZH] 哥哥主动拉她的手，乔梦容无比幸福，像只听话的羔羊，跟着哥哥离开茶肆。
[EN] With Prince Yi reaching for her hand on his own, bliss washed over Qiao Mengrong. Like a docile lamb, she followed him out of the teahouse.
[ZH] 千里寻放开文渊，喜道：“就该这样，有那么好的妹妹，要珍惜，不要吃着碗里的，还瞧着锅里。”
[EN] Qianli Xun sat up from Wenyuan's shoulder in delight. "That's how it should be! When you've got such a sweet girl, cherish her—don't eat from your own bowl while eyeing the pot."
[ZH] 乔文仙不解：“什么意思？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian blinked in confusion. "What do you mean?"
[ZH] “没什么，我们也去逛吧！”千里寻不便告诉文渊，她牵起乔文仙的手，也离开茶肆。
[EN] "Nothing. Let's go stroll too!" Unable to explain to Wenyuan, Qianli Xun took Qiao Wenxian's hand and led him out of the teahouse as well.
[ZH] 喜兰早已跟上郡主。
[EN] Xilan had already hurried after the princess.
[ZH] 南双一直观察苦荞与白风，更确定他们两个关系不一般。
[EN] Watching Kuqiao and Bai Feng the whole time, Nan Shuang grew all the more certain there was something special between those two.
[ZH] 白风舍不得苦荞，仪王要离开，他必须跟着，只得舍苦荞而去。
[EN] Bai Feng hated tearing himself away from Kuqiao, yet with Prince Yi leaving, he had to follow and could only leave her behind.
[ZH] 夏果顾不上别人，一双眼睛围着乔公子转。
[EN] Xiaguo paid no attention to anyone else; her eyes revolved around Young Master Qiao alone.
[ZH] 主人们起身要走，苦荞、夏果和南双都跟上。
[EN] When their masters rose to leave, Kuqiao, Xiaguo, and Nan Shuang all followed behind.
[ZH] 苦荞走得最快，赶上白风。
[EN] Kuqiao walked fastest of all and soon caught up to Bai Feng.
[ZH] 千里寻懒得管她，今日本就是放假，她想如何便如何。
[EN] Qianli Xun did not bother reining her in; today was a holiday anyway, so let her do as she pleased.
[ZH] 夏果则一直贴身跟着自家小姐，如同贴身跟着乔公子一般。
[EN] Xiaguo, meanwhile, stuck close to her mistress's heels, which felt just like staying right beside Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 跟前是成双成对的人，周围走过的也是成双成对之人，尤其是白风跟苦荞暗生情愫，搞得南双心里直痒，思忖着是喜兰好还是夏果好。
[EN] Ahead of him walked couples in pairs, and all around him passersby strolled in pairs too; above all, watching Bai Feng and Kuqiao blossom with quiet affection made Nan Shuang's heart itch. He weighed his options: was Xilan better, or Xiaguo?
[ZH] 很快，他想明白，喜兰是郡主的丫鬟，他根本够不着，还是夏果吧，再说夏果比喜兰生得好看。
[EN] Before long he sorted it out: Xilan was the princess's maid, way out of his league. Better to go with Xiaguo. Besides, Xiaguo was prettier than Xilan anyway.
[ZH] 南双拉住夏果：“夏果姐姐，你走慢些，我有话对你讲。”
[EN] Nan Shuang tugged Xiaguo's sleeve. "Sister Xiaguo, slow down. I have something to tell you."
[ZH] 夏果盯着乔公子的背影，不耐烦道：“有什么话？赶紧说。”
[EN] Eyes glued to Young Master Qiao's back, Xiaguo snapped with impatience, "What is it? Spit it out."
[ZH] 南双害羞道：“夏果姐姐陪我过七夕节吧？”
[EN] A shy blush crept up Nan Shuang's cheeks. "Sister Xiaguo, spend Qixi Festival with me, won't you?"
[ZH] 夏果反问道：“这不正陪着你吗？”
[EN] Xiaguo shot back, "Aren't I walking with you right now?"
[ZH] 南双解释道：“我的意思是，你跟我能不能像白风跟苦荞姐姐一样互相喜欢？”
[EN] Nan Shuang explained, "What I mean is, could you and I like each other the way Bai Feng and Sister Kuqiao do?"
[ZH] “你个小孩子，瞎想什么呢？还是想着怎么好好伺候你家公子吧。”夏果说完，甩下南双，追上二位主人，继续站在小姐身旁，观察乔公子。
[EN] "You little kid, what nonsense is running through your head? Worry about waiting on your master instead!" With that, Xiaguo shook Nan Shuang off, caught up to their two masters, and resumed walking beside her mistress while watching Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 南双也赶上去，一脸不高兴，望着前面白风与苦荞高兴的样子，十分羡慕。
[EN] Nan Shuang hurried after her with a sulky scowl, gazing with deep envy at how happy Bai Feng and Kuqiao looked up ahead.
[ZH] 千里寻牵着文渊的手，跟在仪王与佳和郡主身后。注意到仪王放开佳和郡主的手时，她心中的失落不比乔梦容少。
[EN] Holding Wenyuan's hand, Qianli Xun walked behind Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe. When she noticed Prince Yi let go of Princess Jiahe's hand, the pang of disappointment in her chest was no smaller than Qiao Mengrong's.
[ZH] 千里寻从心底希望仪王能全心全意对待佳和郡主，希望他们幸福地生活在一起。她认为只有充满美好希望的祝福，才是感谢仪王的最佳方式。
[EN] From the bottom of her heart, Qianli Xun hoped Prince Yi would devote himself to Princess Jiahe with all his heart and that the two of them would share a happy life together. To her mind, offering a blessing filled with sincere goodwill was the finest way to thank Prince Yi.

#CH 99
[ZH] 身边经过几对情侣，女子的手中都拿着两朵荷花的花骨朵，像是长在一根枝干上。
[EN] Several couples passed by, each young woman holding two lotus buds that looked as though they grew from a single stem.
[ZH] 千里寻好奇地问：“文渊，那是什么？”
[EN] Curiosity sparked in Qianli Xun's eyes. "Wenyuan, what is that?"
[ZH] 乔文仙笑道：“你失忆后真是什么都不记得，那叫双头莲，寓意吉祥。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian laughed. "After losing your memory, you really don't remember a thing! That's a twin-headed lotus. It's a symbol of good fortune."
[ZH] “我也想要！”千里寻并不缺银子，可她想让文渊买给自己。
[EN] "I want one too!" Qianli Xun was not short on silver, yet she wanted Wenyuan to buy it for her.
[ZH] 文渊拉着她来到一个摊位，售卖的全是七夕节之物，双头莲最多。文渊买了一枝，送给她。
[EN] Wenyuan pulled her over to a street stall packed with Qixi Festival wares, twin-headed lotuses most of all. He bought a stem and handed it to her.
[ZH] 千里寻拿在手中，甚是新奇，仔细观察，原来是用两个花骨朵做成的，并非真正的双头莲。
[EN] Holding it in her hand as a novel keepsake, Qianli Xun gave it a closer look and saw it had been fashioned by joining two lotus buds together, not a real twin-headed lotus at all.
[ZH] 申简辰转头看到千里寻手中拿着双头莲，他不知名头，走到经过的摊位旁，也给乔梦容买了一枝。
[EN] Turning his head, Shen Jianchen spotted the twin-headed lotus in Qianli Xun's hand. Without knowing what it was called, he stepped up to a passing stall and bought one for Qiao Mengrong as well.
[ZH] 乔梦容接过，拿在手中，甚是激动。她与哥哥第一次在外面过七夕节，哥哥也是第一次送她双头莲，她竟然热泪盈眶：“哥哥，你知道双头莲寓意着什么？”
[EN] Taking it into her hands, Qiao Mengrong felt a rush of emotion. This was her first time spending Qixi Festival out in the city with Prince Yi, and his first time giving her a twin-headed lotus. Tears welled in her eyes. "Brother, do you know what a twin-headed lotus stands for?"
[ZH] 申简辰摇摇头，乔梦容的表现又让他感到一丝压力。他见千里寻拿着一个，根本没有机会买给她，只能送给梦容。
[EN] Shen Jianchen shook his head, a fresh weight of pressure settling on him at Qiao Mengrong's reaction. He had seen Qianli Xun holding one and knew he had no chance of buying one for her, so he could only give it to Mengrong instead.
[ZH] “双头莲有永结同心之意。”
[EN] "A twin-headed lotus means two hearts bound together forever."
[ZH] “哦，永结同心。”
[EN] "Oh. Two hearts bound together forever."
[ZH] “我会把哥哥送我的双头莲好好珍藏。”
[EN] "I'll treasure the twin-headed lotus Brother gave me with all my care."
[ZH] “他们迟早会枯萎。”
[EN] "It's bound to wilt sooner or later."
[ZH] “枯萎了，我也要珍藏起来。”
[EN] "Even after it wilts, I'll still keep it safe."
[ZH] 申简辰又瞧向千里寻，她笑得那么灿烂，估计早已忘记申简辰，他又何必再执着于她。
[EN] Shen Jianchen glanced back at Qianli Xun. She was smiling so bright; no doubt she had long since forgotten Shen Jianchen, so why keep clinging to her?
[ZH] “没事，枯了就扔掉，哥哥再送你新鲜的。”
[EN] "No need. When it wilts, toss it out, and I'll buy you a fresh one."
[ZH] 乔梦容听得出来，哥哥的意思再明显不过，就是要与她白头偕老。她露出幸福的笑容，继续陪哥哥逛街。
[EN] Qiao Mengrong caught his meaning plain as day: he meant to grow old together with her. A radiant smile broke across her face as she continued strolling the streets beside him.
[ZH] 千里寻边走，边闻着双头莲上的香气，双眼也顾盼着周围的人和售卖之物。看着看着，最讨厌的人进入她的视线。她扭过头，装作没看到。
[EN] Walking along, Qianli Xun breathed in the scent of her twin-headed lotus while taking in the crowds and street stalls around her. As she looked about, the person she loathed most drifted into view. She turned her head away and pretended not to see him.
[ZH] 陈浩宇早已瞧见她，带着几个小弟，有意走近：“我与亲家姑娘真是有缘，逛个街都能遇上。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu had already spotted her and strolled right over on purpose with a handful of lackeys at his heels. "What fate brings my dear in-law and me together! Even out strolling the streets, we bump into each other."
[ZH] “陈浩宇，知趣的话离我远点儿，小心我揍你！”千里寻将双头莲送到夏果手中，举起拳头，吓唬道。
[EN] "Chen Haoyu, if you know what's good for you, stay back before I beat you up!" Qianli Xun thrust her twin-headed lotus into Xiaguo's hands and raised her fist in warning.
[ZH] “本公子只是过来打个招呼，你又何必把自己搞得跟母夜叉似的？”陈浩宇拍拍乔文仙的肩膀，“乔文仙，真替你担心，以后若娶了她，天天得过苦日子。”
[EN] "I only came over to say hello. Why act like a ferocious she-demon?" Chen Haoyu clapped Qiao Wenxian on the shoulder. "Qiao Wenxian, I feel sorry for you. If you marry her someday, every day of your life is going to be misery."
[ZH] 千里寻气道：“再不走，我可动手了！”
[EN] Qianli Xun snapped in anger, "One more second and I'm swinging!"
[ZH] 乔文仙劝道：“淑真，没必要跟他动气，他就是过过嘴瘾。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian stepped in to soothe her. "Shuzhen, no need to lose your temper with him. He's just running his mouth."
[ZH] 陈浩宇说：“还是乔文仙了解我，我的嘴瘾已过，走了，不打扰二位。”陈浩宇带人乖乖离开。
[EN] Chen Haoyu said, "Qiao Wenxian knows me best! I've had my fun running my mouth, so I'm off. Won't disturb the two of you any longer." With that, he led his lackeys away without a fuss.
[ZH] 千里寻松开拳头，伸出手掌，等夏果将双头莲送到她手中。
[EN] Qianli Xun unclenched her fist and held out her palm, waiting for Xiaguo to hand back the twin-headed lotus.
[ZH] 夏果犹豫了一会儿，才搁到小姐手中。双头莲是乔公子买的，她十分不舍。
[EN] Xiaguo hesitated for a beat before placing it back in her mistress's hand. Young Master Qiao had bought that twin-headed lotus, and she hated letting go of it.
[ZH] 千里寻接过双头莲后问道：“你也喜欢双头莲？”
[EN] Taking back the twin-headed lotus, Qianli Xun asked, "Do you like twin-headed lotuses too?"
[ZH] 夏果点点头。
[EN] Xiaguo nodded.
[ZH] “这玩意儿我送你不合适，只能等以后你有了喜欢的人，让他送你。”
[EN] "It wouldn't be right for me to buy you one of these. You'll just have to wait until you find someone you like and let him buy one for you."
[ZH] 千里寻注意到苦荞手中拿了一枝，才明白原来苦荞喜欢上白风，难怪两人一直在一起有说有笑。
[EN] Noticing a stem in Kuqiao's hand up ahead, Qianli Xun realized Kuqiao had fallen for Bai Feng; no wonder the two of them had been walking side by side laughing and chatting the whole time.
[ZH] 南双说：“如果幽栖居士同意，我可以给夏果姐姐买一个。”
[EN] Nan Shuang piped up, "If Youqi Jushi approves, I can buy one for Sister Xiaguo."
[ZH] 千里寻笑道：“我同意不同意不重要，重要的是夏果得同意。”
[EN] Qianli Xun laughed. "Whether I approve doesn't matter. What matters is whether Xiaguo agrees."
[ZH] 只听夏果说：“我才不要你买！”
[EN] Xiaguo cut in at once: "I don't want you buying me anything!"
[ZH] 千里寻笑着批评道：“你不同意，也别说得那么直接，南双听了会难过。”
[EN] Qianli Xun chided her with a laugh, "Even if you don't want it, don't be so blunt. You'll hurt Nan Shuang's feelings."
[ZH] 果然南双生气地走开，赶到前面去找苦荞和白风。
[EN] Sure enough, Nan Shuang stormed off in a huff and hurried ahead to join Kuqiao and Bai Feng.
[ZH] 乔文仙笑道：“我家南双长大了，情窦初开。夏果，你要不要考虑一下？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian chuckled. "Look at my Nan Shuang, all grown up and tasting his first crush. Xiaguo, won't you consider him?"
[ZH] 被喜欢之人劝说，夏果感到心酸，说明乔公子心里根本没有她。
[EN] Hearing the man she loved play matchmaker for her stung Xiaguo's heart; it proved Young Master Qiao did not have her in his heart at all.
[ZH] “乔公子不用劝，我已有喜欢之人……”
[EN] "No need to persuade me, Young Master Qiao. There's already someone I like..."
[ZH] 千里寻高兴地问：“是谁？”
[EN] Qianli Xun lit up with delight. "Who is it?"
[ZH] “我喜欢人家，人家未必喜欢我，还是不说了，说出去丢人。”夏果低下头，不愿再说。
[EN] "I like him, though he might not like me back. Better not say; it would only be embarrassing." Xiaguo lowered her head and refused to say another word.
[ZH] 夏果不想透露，千里寻也不好继续追问。
[EN] Since Xiaguo did not want to tell, Qianli Xun could not press her further.
[ZH] 乔文仙想起请仪王听说书之事：“淑真，前面就是桥头瓦。”
[EN] Remembering their plan to treat Prince Yi to a storytelling show, Qiao Wenxian said, "Shuzhen, Qiaotou Wa is just up ahead."
[ZH] “这么快到了，我还想与你多逛会儿。”
[EN] "We're here already? I still wanted to stroll around with you a bit longer."
[ZH] “等听完说书，我再陪你逛。”
[EN] "After the storytelling, I'll stroll with you some more."
[ZH] “好吧。”
[EN] "All right."
[ZH] 他们快走几步，赶上仪王与嘉和郡主。
[EN] Quickening their pace, they caught up to Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe.
[ZH] 主人们允许下人不必跟着，苦荞与白风更是兴奋，喜兰也想自由逛街，南双自告奋勇给喜兰当向导。只剩夏果还跟着小姐，谁让她喜欢的人一直在小姐身边。
[EN] Their masters gave the servants leave to go off on their own. Kuqiao and Bai Feng were over the moon, Xilan wanted to wander the streets on her own too, and Nan Shuang jumped at the chance to act as Xilan's guide. Only Xiaguo stayed behind with her mistress; after all, the man she loved stayed right by her mistress's side.
[ZH] 众位小弟跟随陈浩宇逛街甚觉无聊，有人说：“陈公子，七夕节一点儿意思都没有，还不如去春香楼逛逛。”
[EN] Meanwhile, Chen Haoyu's lackeys were bored stiff trailing him through the streets. One of them spoke up: "Young Master Chen, this Qixi Festival is a total bore. We'd be better off heading over to Chunxiang Brothel."
[ZH] 陈浩宇叹口气：“唉，我也知道没意思，可我想出来听听谣言散播的效果。今日街上人最多，你们有没有听到有人议论朱淑真？”
[EN] Chen Haoyu let out a sigh. "I know it's boring, all right? I only came out to gauge how well my rumors are spreading. Today's got the biggest crowds on the streets. Have any of you heard people talking about Zhu Shuzhen?"
[ZH] 小弟们摇摇头：“没听到。”
[EN] The lackeys shook their heads. "Not a word."
[ZH] “怎么这么慢？本公子都快等不及。你！”陈浩宇指着一位小弟，“去告诉散播谣言的人，让他们加大马力，使劲儿散播，本公子给他们再多加银两。”
[EN] "Why is it taking so long? I'm losing my patience! You!" Chen Haoyu pointed at one of his lackeys. "Go tell the rumor-mongers to pick up the pace and spread it twice as hard. I'll pay them extra taels of silver."
[ZH] “是！”那小弟领命离开。
[EN] "Yes, sir!" The lackey took his orders and scurried off.
[ZH] 一位小弟说：“陈公子也不要太着急，谣言靠口耳相传，必然慢些，再说才散播了不到十日，等一个月后，您再看效果。”
[EN] Another lackey said, "Don't get too impatient, Young Master Chen. Rumors travel by word of mouth, so they're bound to take time. Besides, it's been less than ten days. Give it a month, then see what happens."
[ZH] “说得也是，不能着急。不过本公子不能不急，你们刚才也看到，朱淑真见到本公子一副横眉怒视的样子，看着就来气，不好好修理修理她，我这心里着实不舒服。”陈浩宇摸着自己的胸口，装作难受的样子。
[EN] "Fair point, no use rushing. Still, how can I not be impatient? You all saw it just now: the way Zhu Shuzhen glared daggers at me made my blood boil. Until I knock her down a few pegs, I've got a knot sitting right here." Chen Haoyu rubbed his chest in mock agony.
[ZH] 一位小弟说：“我们几个心里也不舒服，满大街都是成双成对的人，陈公子和我们都是光棍儿，与今日的气氛实在不搭，还不如去春香楼。”
[EN] One lackey said, "We've got knots in our chests too! The whole street is packed with couples, while you and the rest of us are all bachelors. We stick out like sore thumbs in this crowd. We really ought to hit Chunxiang Brothel."
[ZH] 陈浩宇掏出一袋银子，扔到一人怀中：“你们想去就去吧，本公子还未到开荤之日，绝不越雷池一步。”
[EN] Chen Haoyu pulled out a pouch of silver and tossed it into one man's arms. "Go on if you want. My vow of abstinence isn't up yet, and I won't step a single inch over that line."
[ZH] “谢陈公子！”小弟们拿上银子，满脸堆笑，朝春香楼走去。
[EN] "Thank you, Young Master Chen!" Clutching the silver, the lackeys grinned from ear to ear and headed off toward Chunxiang Brothel.
[ZH] 剩下陈浩宇一人，瞧瞧街上来来往往的情侣，忍不住想，什么时候也给身边找个可心的人。
[EN] Left all alone, Chen Haoyu watched the couples drifting up and down the street and could not help wondering when he might find someone who suited his heart too.
[ZH] 陈浩宇玩儿过的女人不少，要说能走进他心里的，暂时还没有。
[EN] Chen Haoyu had dallied with plenty of women, yet not a single one had found her way into his heart so far.
[ZH] 也许是玩的女人太多，很难对哪个女子产生像乔文仙，以及街上那些不知道女人为何物的男人所产生的情感。
[EN] Perhaps he had played around with too many women to ever feel for one the way Qiao Wenxian did, or the way these men on the street did—men who knew nothing of what women were like.
[ZH] 有时他还挺羡慕那些无知的男人，还能用朦朦胧胧的眼睛看女人，还能产生说不清道不明的情感，而他至今也不知情为何物。
[EN] At times he even envied those naive men, who could still look at women through misty, starry eyes and feel emotions beyond words, whereas to this day he still had no idea what love was.
[ZH] 他曾想过，如果哪个女子能让他产生感情，他一定把她娶回去，从此改邪归正。可他觉得自己的想法只不过是天方夜谭。
[EN] He had thought before that if any woman ever stirred real feelings in him, he would marry her and mend his ways for good. Yet he figured that notion was nothing more than a pipe dream.
[ZH] 不知不觉走进一家普通茶馆，这种茶馆他向来不进，之所以破天荒进来，无非是想听听谣言传得如何。
[EN] Before he knew it, he had wandered into an ordinary teahouse, the sort of place he never set foot in. Breaking his own habit today had only one purpose: to listen in and hear how the rumors were spreading.
[ZH] 陈浩宇挑位置坐下，随便要了壶茶，把茶盏倒满，用耳朵仔细听着。茶馆里人来人往，更多的是聊家常，聊情感之人，听了半天也没听出丝毫关于谣言的内容来。
[EN] Chen Haoyu picked a seat, ordered a pot of tea at random, filled his cup, and strained his ears. Customers came and went, chatting for the most part of household trivia and romance; after listening for ages, he caught not a single word of his rumors.
[ZH] 两对青年男女进来，刚好被伙计安排在陈浩宇旁边的桌子坐下，他本想走，听到一个女子问：“你们最近有没有听到幽栖居士写的一首词？”
[EN] Two young couples walked in, and the waiter happened to seat them at the table right beside Chen Haoyu. He was just about to leave when one of the young women asked, "Have you heard the ci lyric Youqi Jushi wrote the other day?"
[ZH] 终于有人议论朱淑真，陈浩宇绝不会错过，竖起耳朵仔细听。
[EN] At last, someone was talking about Zhu Shuzhen! Not about to miss a word, Chen Haoyu pricked up his ears.
[ZH] 一个男子问：“什么词？”
[EN] One of the men asked, "Which ci?"
[ZH] 女子回道：“清平乐·夏日游湖。”
[EN] The young woman answered, "'Qingping Yue: Summer Excursion on the Lake.'"
[ZH] 另一个男子说：“听过，其中有一句甚是大胆。”
[EN] The other man said, "I've heard it. One line in it is bold beyond belief."
[ZH] 另一女子说：“是啊，她怎么这么大胆，竟然敢写出‘和衣睡倒人怀’？我读来甚觉得脸红。”
[EN] The second woman chimed in, "It really is! How could she be so brazen as to write 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed'? Just reading it makes me blush."
[ZH] 陈浩宇一阵喜悦，心里想：“哼！我的谣言还未传出，你写的词都已传开，这就叫自作孽不可活！嘿嘿……”
[EN] A wave of glee washed over Chen Haoyu. "Hmph!" he thought. "Before my rumors even make the rounds, your own ci has already spread far and wide. You reap what you sow, all right! Heheh..."
[ZH] 女子又说：“近期我还听到关于幽栖居士的传言，说她是母老虎，什么红杏出墙，暗送秋波，还说她应该被千夫所指，万人唾骂，实在难听。”
[EN] The first woman went on, "I've also heard ugly rumors about Youqi Jushi these past days—calling her a tigress, saying she had affairs and traded secret glances with men, even saying she deserves to be pointed at by a thousand fingers and spat on by ten thousand. It's awful to hear."
[ZH] 男子怀疑道：“不会是造谣吧？”
[EN] One of the men looked skeptical. "Could someone be making all that up?"
[ZH] 另一个女子说：“无风不起浪，能写出‘和衣睡倒人怀’，估计什么都做得出来。”
[EN] The second woman said, "There's no smoke without fire. Someone who can write 'falling asleep in his arms, fully dressed' is capable of just about anything."
[ZH] 其他三人点头认可。
[EN] The other three nodded in agreement.
[ZH] 陈浩宇更是窃喜：“朱淑真，等着瞧吧，过不了多久，满城都是你的风言风语，你的拳头再厉害，也不知道该打向谁？哈哈哈哈……！”
[EN] Chen Haoyu gloated all the more to himself: "Just you wait, Zhu Shuzhen! Before long, the whole city will be buzzing with scandals about you. No matter how tough your fists are, who're you going to punch when it's everyone talking? Hahaha...!"

#CH 100
[ZH] 来到桥头瓦，他们向说书的勾栏走去。
[EN] Reaching Qiaotou Wa, they headed toward the Goulan storytelling theater.
[ZH] 快要到时，申简辰突然停下：“文渊，我能否跟幽栖居士单独说句话？”
[EN] Just before they reached the entrance, Shen Jianchen came to a stop. "Wenyuan, may I have a quick word in private with Youqi Jushi?"
[ZH] 文渊满口答应：“说多少句都行。”
[EN] Wenyuan agreed without hesitation. "Say as many words as you like."
[ZH] 千里寻根本不愿意：“申公子有什么话，就在这里说吧。”
[EN] Qianli Xun wanted no part of it. "Whatever Young Master Shen has to say, say it right here."
[ZH] 申简辰说出三个字：“千里寻……”
[EN] Shen Jianchen spoke three syllables: "Qianli Xun..."
[ZH] 千里寻立刻走到一边，她可不想让仪王道出她就是千里寻的秘密。申简辰露出得意的笑容，跟在她身后。
[EN] Qianli Xun stepped aside at once; the last thing she wanted was for Prince Yi to spill her secret that she was Qianli Xun. A smug smile crossed Shen Jianchen's face as he followed behind her.
[ZH] 乔梦容很疑惑，哥哥与幽栖居士有什么话不能当着众人面说？
[EN] Qiao Mengrong felt bewildered. What could Prince Yi have to say to Youqi Jushi that couldn't be spoken in front of everyone?
[ZH] “乔公子，他们要说什么？为何避开我们？”
[EN] "Young Master Qiao, what are they going to talk about? Why step away from us?"
[ZH] “乔姑娘不用担心，申公子自有道理，你我不必过问。”
[EN] "No need to worry, Young Lady Qiao. Young Master Shen has his reasons. There's no need for us to pry."
[ZH] 乔文仙如此放心，乔梦容也打消疑虑，只顾观察他们，猜测可能说的话。
[EN] Seeing Qiao Wenxian so at ease put Qiao Mengrong's doubts to rest, though she kept watching them and guessing what they might be saying.
[ZH] 小姐不在身边，只有夏果一人，她有意向乔公子靠近一些。她看着他，可他的眼中只有小姐。
[EN] With her mistress stepped away, Xiaguo stood alone and edged a little closer to Young Master Qiao on purpose. She gazed at him, yet his eyes held only her mistress.
[ZH] 确定安全后，申简辰问道：“你不是说手稿写好后，要先送给我看，为何没送？却直接请我来听说书？”
[EN] Once sure they were out of earshot, Shen Jianchen asked, "Didn't you promise to send me the manuscript to read first as soon as you finished it? Why didn't you, and instead brought me straight to a storytelling show?"
[ZH] 千里寻觉得仪王长得人高马大，心眼儿还挺小：“这等小事申公子就别计较。”
[EN] Qianli Xun thought that for a tall, broad-shouldered man, Prince Yi sure had a petty streak. "Don't sweat a tiny thing like that, Young Master Shen."
[ZH] “怎么能算小事？说明你是个出尔反尔的人，在你心里根本没把我当回事。”
[EN] "How is that a tiny thing? It shows you go back on your word—and that I don't mean a thing to you."
[ZH] 千里寻急了，她不敢将嗓门放大，压着声音说：“本来我是想送到幽林别居，可你也知，文渊住在幽林别居，很容易被他发现，于是，我临时改变主意。您是仪王，又两次帮我，我再怎么着，也不会不把您放在眼里，我心里可是时时刻刻希望仪王您跟佳和郡主和和美美。”
[EN] Qianli Xun hurried to explain, keeping her voice low so no one would overhear. "I did plan to send it to Youlin Retreat, only as you know, Wenyuan stays there too, so he'd have caught on in no time. That's why I changed my mind at the last minute. You're Prince Yi, and you've helped me twice. No matter what, I would never brush you off. Every single moment, I'm wishing for you and Princess Jiahe to live in sweet harmony."
[ZH] 听到这话，申简辰有种想说出真实身份的冲动。周围吵吵闹闹，人流涌动，文渊与梦容又一直盯着，氛围实在不搭，还是等等再说。
[EN] Hearing that stirred an urge in Shen Jianchen to reveal who he truly was. Yet with the noisy crowd surging past and Wenyuan and Mengrong staring straight at them, the setting was all wrong; better to wait.
[ZH] “你真的希望我跟梦容好？”
[EN] "Do you really want things to go well between Mengrong and me?"
[ZH] “那还能有假？佳和郡主是好姑娘，您要懂得珍惜，千万不要因为您身份尊贵，又有钱就辜负她。”
[EN] "Could I be any more sincere? Princess Jiahe is a wonderful girl. Know how to treasure her, and whatever you do, don't let her down just because you have high rank and money."
[ZH] 申简辰喜欢千里寻的原因之一，便是因她善良，总是为他人考虑。为了能多跟千里寻待着，又故意问：“我若辜负呢？……”
[EN] One of the reasons Shen Jianchen loved Qianli Xun was her kind heart and the way she always thought of others. Wanting to linger beside her a little longer, he asked on purpose, "And what if I do let her down?..."
[ZH] “我已视佳和郡主为闺中密友，你若敢辜负她，我可不忌惮你的身份，该为她出头，绝不当缩头乌龟。”
[EN] "I already consider Princess Jiahe my close confidante. If you ever dare let her down, I won't care a bit about your rank. I'll stand up for her and never shrink back like a coward."
[ZH] 千里寻的话说得坚决，申简辰既为乔梦容感动，又为自己难过：“梦容有你这样的朋友是她的幸运，可我……”
[EN] The steel in Qianli Xun's words touched Shen Jianchen for Qiao Mengrong's sake even as it broke his own heart. "Mengrong is lucky to have a friend like you, yet I..."
[ZH] 千里寻不喜欢藏着掖着，不如趁此机会将话挑明：“你这个人其实还不错，挺热心，跟我想象中的王爷不一样，……你再稍微注意一下会更好……”
[EN] Never one to beat around the bush, Qianli Xun seized the chance to lay it all out in the open. "To be honest, you're a pretty decent guy—warmhearted, not at all like the princes I used to imagine... You'd be even better if you just watched yourself a little..."
[ZH] “注意什么？”申简辰不解。
[EN] "Watch myself how?" Shen Jianchen asked in puzzlement.
[ZH] “不要随意用语言和您那双眼睛调戏美女，不是所有美女都会像我一样定力十足。您长得也不赖，身份地位又高，总是对美女放电，难免招惹到花花草草，会伤佳和郡主的心。”
[EN] "Stop flirting with pretty women left and right with your smooth talk and those eyes of yours. Not every beauty has my kind of self-control! You're good-looking and hold high rank; if you keep tossing sparks at pretty girls, you're bound to stir up messy entanglements and break Princess Jiahe's heart."
[ZH] 申简辰这才明白，时机不对，再对的事情都是错的，他面对千里寻不由自主表现出的爱意，竟然被她认为是调戏，真是极大的讽刺。
[EN] It dawned on Shen Jianchen at last: when the timing is wrong, even the truest thing turns wrong. The love he could not stop showing whenever he faced Qianli Xun had been misread by her as shameless flirting. What bitter irony.
[ZH] “今日不谈此事，若幽栖居士想听我解释，明日酉时来神柳下，我会将实情告知。”
[EN] "We won't talk about this today. If Youqi Jushi wants my explanation, come beneath the Divine Willow tomorrow at dusk, at the Hour of the Rooster, and I'll tell you the whole truth."
[ZH] 申简辰说完，继续回到佳和郡主身边，拉起她朝说书的勾栏走去。
[EN] With that, Shen Jianchen walked back to Princess Jiahe's side, took her hand, and led her toward the Goulan theater.
[ZH] “哼，说到痛处就跑，明日就明日，正好趁其他人不在，好好教育教育你。”千里寻想，最近只顾着谈恋爱，几乎忘记神柳，不如趁此机会去看看它。
[EN] "Hmph, running off the second I hit a nerve! Fine, tomorrow it is. I'll use the chance while nobody else is around to give you a proper lecture." Qianli Xun realized she had been so caught up in romance these days that she had all but forgotten the Divine Willow; she might as well take the chance to go see it.
[ZH] 乔文仙微笑着走来，拉起她的手：“在想什么？”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian walked up with a smile and took her hand. "What are you thinking about?"
[ZH] “没什么。他们都进去了，我们快些跟上。”
[EN] "Nothing. They've already gone inside. Let's catch up."
[ZH] 今日说书的勾栏内，人真不少，仪王与佳和郡主已挑桌子坐下。乔文仙挑了一个离仪王近些的位置，两人在桌子的两侧坐下后，有伙计前来斟茶，摆蜜饯。
[EN] The storytelling theater was packed today. Prince Yi and Princess Jiahe had already taken a table, so Qiao Wenxian picked one close to theirs. Once he and Qianli Xun sat down on either side of the table, a waiter came over to pour tea and set out candied preserves.
[ZH] 千里寻担心夏果站着太累，吩咐伙计搬张椅子，让夏果坐在她身边，把有蜜饯的盘子放在她的手中：“这盘你端着吃。”
[EN] Worried Xiaguo would grow tired standing, Qianli Xun had the waiter bring over a chair so Xiaguo could sit beside her, then placed a dish of candied preserves in her hands. "Hold this plate and snack on these."
[ZH] “小姐和乔公子怎么办？”
[EN] "What about Miss and Young Master Qiao?"
[ZH] “桌上还有，放心吃，不够再拿。”
[EN] "There's more on the table. Go ahead and eat. We'll order more if we run out."
[ZH] 夏果很感激小姐照顾，她偷偷喜欢乔公子，小姐知道肯定不高兴。可她忍不住不喜欢，又时不时偷偷瞧他一眼。
[EN] Xiaguo felt touched by her mistress's kindness. She knew her mistress would be upset if she ever found out about her secret crush on Young Master Qiao, yet she could not stop her heart from beating for him, stealing shy glances his way every now and then.
[ZH] 台上说话艺人在说三国历史的动荡起伏，正讲到关云长刮骨疗毒的故事，听的人都被吸引住……
[EN] Up on stage, a storyteller was recounting the turbulent drama of the Three Kingdoms, right in the middle of the tale of Guan Yunchang having the poison scraped from his bone, holding the whole audience spellbound...
[ZH] 千里寻没见过接她手稿的说话艺人，也不知对方何时会表演她写的故事。她向四周瞅瞅，朝伙计招招手。
[EN] Qianli Xun had never met the storyteller who took her manuscript and had no idea when he would perform her tale. Looking around, she waved a waiter over.
[ZH] 伙计过来：“姑娘有何吩咐？”
[EN] The waiter hurried up. "How may I help you, Young Lady?"
[ZH] “听说有位叫千里寻的，话本写得不错，他的话本常常在你们勾栏内表演，不知今日何时开始？”
[EN] "I heard there's a writer named Qianli Xun whose huaben stories are quite good, and they're often performed here at your theater. Any idea when today's session starts?"
[ZH] “看来姑娘进门时没注意，外面的牌子上都写着呢，等台上的艺人把三国的历史说完，下一位就说千里寻写的故事。”
[EN] "Young Lady must not have noticed the signboard outside when you came in. It's all written right there. As soon as the performer on stage wraps up the Three Kingdoms, the next storyteller is up with Qianli Xun's tale."
[ZH] “千里寻在你们这里很出名吗？”
[EN] "Is Qianli Xun really that famous around here?"
[ZH] “只闻其名，不知其人，他的故事写得不错，大家都喜欢听。”
[EN] "Everyone knows the name, though nobody knows the man. He writes great stories, and the crowd loves hearing them."
[ZH] 千里寻很满意：“你去忙吧，有需要再叫你。”
[EN] Qianli Xun beamed with satisfaction. "Go on with your work. I'll call if we need anything."
[ZH] 千里寻看过好几遍三国演义的电视剧，每次看到华佗为关云长刮骨疗毒的情节时，她的心都会提到嗓子眼儿。
[EN] Qianli Xun had watched the "Romance of the Three Kingdoms" television drama several times, and every single time she reached the scene where Hua Tuo scraped the poison from Guan Yunchang's bone, her heart leaped into her throat.
[ZH] 她从小练习千里拳，倒也练出抗打的本领，可如果有人要给她刮骨疗毒，连麻药都不打，那是万万使不得，她认为自己还没被毒药毒死，肯定会被疼死。
[EN] Training in Qianli Fist since childhood had made her tough enough to take a beating, yet letting someone scrape poison off her bone without even a drop of anesthesia? Not in a million years! She figured she would die of the pain long before the poison ever got her.
[ZH] 今日在穿越而来的时代，听到说话艺人说起这段，内心对关云长又一次生起敬佩之情。
[EN] Hearing a storyteller perform the passage here in this bygone era she had crossed into filled her with a fresh wave of awe for Guan Yunchang.
[ZH] 乔文仙见淑真听得认真，本不想打扰，想起父亲答应提亲之事还未告知她，此刻时机最佳，不如趁此说了，让她高兴高兴。
[EN] Seeing how absorbed Shuzhen was in the tale, Qiao Wenxian had not meant to interrupt. Then he remembered he still had not told her his father had agreed to propose marriage—what better moment than right now to put a smile on her face?
[ZH] 乔文仙靠近小声说：“淑真，我父亲已答应一两个月后到朱府提亲。”
[EN] Leaning in close, Qiao Wenxian whispered, "Shuzhen, my father has agreed to come to Zhu Manor in a month or two to propose marriage."
[ZH] 千里寻上一秒还沉浸在体验关云长刮骨的疼痛中，听到文渊的话，痛苦全无，立刻露出笑脸，兴奋地看着文渊：“难道这就是你要告诉我的好消息？”
[EN] One second earlier, Qianli Xun had been wincing along with Guan Yunchang's bone-scraping agony; the moment Wenyuan spoke, every trace of pain vanished. A brilliant grin spread across her face as she looked at him in thrill: "Don't tell me this is the good news you promised to bring me?"
[ZH] 乔文仙点点头：“是的。”
[EN] Qiao Wenxian nodded. "It is."
[ZH] “真是一个大大的好消息。”千里寻又装作伤心的样子，“一两个月，这么晚，如果是明天那该多好。”
[EN] "That really is a huge piece of good news!" Qianli Xun then put on a mock pout. "A month or two? That's so far away! If only it were tomorrow."
[ZH] “没想到，你这么着急嫁给我。”
[EN] "Who knew you were in such a hurry to marry me."
[ZH] “难道你不着急娶我？”
[EN] "Aren't you in a hurry to marry me too?"
[ZH] “我当然着急，可父亲说，你刚和离，现在提亲不合适，让我等等，你耐心些，一两个月很快就过去，我答应你，多催催我父亲。”
[EN] "Of course I am! Only my father said since your mutual divorce was so recent, proposing right away wouldn't be proper, so he told me to wait. Be patient—a month or two will fly right by. I promise I'll keep nudging my father."
[ZH] 千里寻点点头，近期真是好事连连，不仅夏果的事情解决，她与文渊的婚事也快有眉目，在街上碰到陈浩宇带来的不快很快被忘记。
[EN] Qianli Xun nodded. Good news had been rolling in one piece after another these days: not only was Xiaguo's trouble settled, her own marriage to Wenyuan was taking shape too. The sour mood from running into Chen Haoyu on the street faded from her mind in no time.
[ZH] 夏果听到乔家要到朱府提亲，也替小姐高兴，更替自己高兴，只有小姐嫁到乔府，她才能离乔公子更近。
[EN] Hearing that the Qiao family would come to Zhu Manor to propose made Xiaguo happy for her mistress, and even happier for herself. Only once her mistress married into Qiao Manor could she draw closer to Young Master Qiao.
[ZH] 三国的故事终于讲完，下一位说话艺人走上台，正是接千里寻话本之人，啜了一口茶后，把扇子和茶壶放在桌上，开口说道：
[EN] At last the Three Kingdoms tale drew to a close. The next storyteller stepped onto the stage—the very man who had taken Qianli Xun's huaben manuscript. Taking a sip of tea, he set his fan and teapot on the table and began:
[ZH] “诸位，上次讲到红香与玄英相遇相知又相爱，可他们之间的爱情得不到祝福，只因花神与凤凰有仇，他们若想在一起，必须从轮回崖跳下，脱离仙体，成为人，方可永远在一起。可成为人后，他们又会面临一大难题……”
[EN] "Ladies and gentlemen! Last time we left off with Hongxiang and Xuanying meeting, coming to know each other's hearts, and falling in love. Yet their love could receive no blessing, because a blood feud divided the Flower Deity and the Phoenix. To be together, they had to leap from Reincarnation Cliff, shed their immortal bodies, and become mortals so they could remain together forever. Yet once they became mortal, a grave trial awaited them..."
[ZH] 有人问：“什么难题？”
[EN] Someone in the crowd called out, "What trial?"
[ZH] 说话艺人回道：“若为人后，互不相识，要想相遇只能靠缘分。”
[EN] The storyteller replied, "Once reborn as mortals, they would no longer recognize each other; finding one another again would rest on fate alone."
[ZH] 又有人叹道：“可悲可叹，赶紧讲吧，我们都等着听呢！”
[EN] Another listener sighed, "How tragic! Hurry up and tell us—we're all on the edge of our seats!"
[ZH] 刚才说三国故事时，没见有人搭话，一讲自己写的故事，就有人搭话。千里寻很开心，说明大家真的爱听。
[EN] Nobody had chimed in during the Three Kingdoms tale just now, yet the instant her own story started, listeners were calling out. Qianli Xun glowed with pride; it proved people truly loved listening to it.
[ZH] 只听说话艺人讲道：
[EN] The storyteller launched into the tale:
[ZH] 话说红香与玄英相约来到轮回崖，望着深不可测的崖底，玄英从身上拔下一根凤凰的羽毛，说道：“你拿着它，成为人后，带着它来找我。”红香用仙力变出一片金色的花瓣，送到玄英手中：“这是我送给你的，记着我，你我到人间再续前缘。”
[EN] By mutual vow, Hongxiang and Xuanying met at Reincarnation Cliff. Gazing down into the bottomless abyss, Xuanying plucked a single phoenix feather from his own body and said, "Take this. Once you are reborn as a mortal, bring it with you and come find me." With her immortal power, Hongxiang conjured a golden petal and pressed it into Xuanying's hand: "This is my gift to you. Remember me, and let us renew our destined bond in the mortal world."
[ZH] 他们都把对方给的信物用神力藏在身上，牵着手，从轮回崖跳下。跳下的过程中，他们被一股无形的力量分开，无法看到对方，不知在黑暗中飘了多久，终于来到人间。
[EN] Using their immortal power, each concealed the other's keepsake upon their body. Hand in hand, they leaped from Reincarnation Cliff. As they fell, an unseen force tore them apart until neither could see the other. After drifting through the darkness for who knew how long, they reached the mortal world at last.
[ZH] 玄英成为武烨城主的儿子，名叫武玄英，出生时，胸前有一胎记，呈花瓣形；红香出生在山村里一个姓华的人家，名叫华红香，出生时手臂上有一胎记，外形像凤凰的羽毛。他们都已忘记前尘往事……
[EN] Xuanying was reborn as the son of the Lord of Wuye City and named Wu Xuanying, bearing a petal-shaped birthmark upon his chest; Hongxiang was born to a mountain-village family surnamed Hua and named Hua Hongxiang, bearing a birthmark shaped like a phoenix feather upon her arm. Both had forgotten every trace of their past lives...
[ZH] 此故事申简辰再熟悉不过，并不觉得什么。乔梦容倒是感慨不已，本来相爱的两个人，就这样被分开，再次见面也不知要经历多少千难万险。
[EN] Shen Jianchen knew this story inside and out, so it stirred nothing new in him. Qiao Mengrong, however, felt swept up in emotion: two people in love, torn apart just like that—who knew how many trials and hardships they would have to endure before meeting again?
